Catalog number : M00117
Uniform title: āgamakalpalatā
Main title: āgamakalpalatā
Author : yadunātha
Alternate name : yadunāthacakravartti
Manuscript : ngmcp a145/3
Notes:
Transcribed by the staff of Muktabodha under the supervision of Mark S. G. Dyczkowski.


Revision 0: Sept. 16, 2008

Publication country : United States
########################################################################### 


 oṃ namaḥ śrīgaṇeśāye namaḥ |

 śrītripurasundaryai namaḥ |


oṃ yat pādapaṅkajayugaṃ hṛdiśaṃ niveśya viśvaṃ sṛjatya vikalaṃ 
khalupadmajanmā |

viṣṇuśca pālayati saṃharate maheśastaṃ devamādipuruṣaṃ satataṃ natosmi || 1-
1 ||

na tvā girīśaṃ girijāṃ gaṇeśaṃ vāṇīmunindraiḥ stutapādapallavam |

vilokyaśāstraṃ yadunāthaśarmaṇā vitanyatejyā gamakasya vallī || 1-2 ||

tathā ||

prapañcasārasiddhāntaṃ śāradātilakaṃ [+lakaṃ] tathā |

sapariskaramālokya tathā sārasamuccaya || 1-3 ||

yatnena dīpikāṃ datvā tathā ca laghudīpikām | 

tathā pūjā pradīpaṃ ca puraścaraṇacandrikām || 1-4 ||

mantradarpaṇasiddhāntaṃ mantradeva prakāśikām |

mantranetraṃ ca mantrārthaṃ śrīrāmārcanacandrikām || 1-5 ||

mantramuktāvalīratnāvalī jñānārṇavādikam |

tatraṃ sanatkumārasya nāradī ca catuḥśatīm || 1-6 ||

pūrṇayāgaṃ śomaśaṃbhumataṃ cāgastyasaṃhitām |

saṃhitāṃ vaiṣṇavī tadvat tva sāgarasahitām || 1-7 ||

saṃhitāṃ kapilaproktaṃ śrīmantravarasaṃhitām |

tantraṃ śrībhuvaneśvaryā na ya siddhāntamādarāt || 1-8 ||

kalpaṃ śrīnavadurgāyāstatraṃ sanmohanātmakam |

hayagrīvamataṃ tadva tathā vijñānabhāratam || 1-9 ||

śuklaṃ ca mantrarājaṃ ca tāṃ dṛṣṭvā ca punaḥ punaḥ |

sarveṣāṃ saṃhitāṃ caiva tathā vai piṅgalāmṛtam || 1-10 ||

vāmakeśvaratantra ca samastaṃ rāmadīkṣitam |

1b) vijñāneśvarasiddhāntaṃ sāra śrīsaṃhitāḥ kramāt || 1-11 ||

vijñānalalitāṃ pāṃcarātrī nārāyaṇīmapi |

vaśiṣṭhasaṃhitāṃ gārgyasaṃhitāṃ bhairavaṃ tathā || 1-12 ||

divyasārasvataṃ yiccandrikāṃ kāmikamādarāt |

homāvarottaraṃ granthaṃ pratiṣṭhā sārasaṃgraham || 1-13 ||

saṃhitāṃ vāyavīṃ tadvat saṃhitāṃ brahmaṇastathā |

kṛśānoḥ saṃhitāṃ brahmaviṣṇurudrādijāmalam || 1-14 ||

tathaiva yāmaloddhāraṃ matsyasūktamapi kramāt |

pārvatī harasaṃvādaṃ vārāhītantramādarāt || 1-15 ||

tathaiva yoginītantraṃ kramaśastripurārṇave |

īśānasaṃhitāṃ tadva siddhāntaṃ viśvakarmaṇaḥ || 1-16 ||

tantra śrīdakṣiṇāmūrte vaiśampāyena saṃhitām |

kulacūḍāmaṇiṃ tadva bhāvacūḍāmaṇiṃ tathāḥ || 1-17 ||

kulārṇavaṃ kulaśreṣṭhaṃ haṃsapārāyaṇaṃ tathā |

purāṇaṃ vividhaṃ tadvat smṛtiśāstrāṇibhāratam || 1-18 ||

jyotiḥ śāstrāṇi cālokya sakalāṃ paddhatiṃ kramāt |

anye ya saṃgrahāḥ santi tān dṛṣṭvā ca punaḥ punaḥ || 1-19 ||

sarveṣāṃ sāramuddhṛtye yadunāthena dhīmatā |

vidhānaṃ mantrayantrāṇāṃ dīkṣādīnāṃ prapaṃcitam || 1-20 ||

jñānānusārato jñeyaṃ vibhāvya ca muhumuhuḥ |

nānātantrasthamantrāṇāṃ jñānaṃ jhatiṭidullabham || 1-21 ||

atasteṣāṃ prabodhāya vicāryātiprayatnataḥ |

jñānāyamaṃdabuddhīnāṃ samāsena pracakṣate || 1-22 ||

paraṃ karapuṭe naiva prārthyate sudhiyomayā |

nyunātiriktatā cātra śobhanīyā mahāśayaiḥ || 1-23 ||

2a) tathā dīkṣitamartyānāṃ yad doṣaṃ kathayāmi tat |

yadu jñātvā na vā nityaṃ dīkṣāṃ kurcaṃ tu sattamaḥ || 1-24 ||



agastyasaṃhitāyām ||



adīkṣitānāṃ matyānāṃ doṣaṃ śṛṇvantu paṇḍitāḥ | 

annaviṣṭhāsamaṃ tasya jalaṃ mutrasamaṃ tathā || 1-25 ||

adikṣitaṃ kṛtaṃ śrārddha śrārddha cādīkṣitasya vā |

gṛhītvā pitarasteṣāṃ narake ca sudāruṇam || 1-26 ||

patantya va na saṃdeho yāvad indrā caturdaśa |

sahasrairupacāraistu bhaktiyukto yajed yadi || 1-2 ||

tathāpya dīkṣitasyārcāṃ devā gṛhnanti naiva ca |

karmākhilaṃ? tathā yasmāt tasmād adikṣitaḥ paśuḥ || 1-28 ||

tasmād adīkṣitaṃ dṛṣṭvā kicit kāryaṃ na sādhayet |

dīkṣā grahaṇakāle tu pāparāśiḥ parāyate || 1-29 ||

nirvāṇapadasaṃyukto vahi gacchati tat paraḥ |

tadā deva pitṛṇāṃ tu kāryādo bhavati svayam || 1-30 ||

anyathā yat kṛtaṃ karma hutaṃ nasmanihavyavat |

sarvāśrameṣu dīkṣāyāṃ nityatvaṃ paricakṣyate || 1-31 ||

anīśvarasya mantrasya nāsti trātā yathā bhuvi |

tathā dīkṣā vihīnasya nehasvāmī paratra ca || 1-32 ||

na tapobhirjayaihomai vividhairvāpi sādhanaiḥ |

na kriyā hi kadācit tu dīkṣāhīnasya siddhyati || 1-33 ||


kiṃcid iti śeṣaḥ ||


dīkṣāmūlaṃ japasarvaṃ dīkṣimūlaṃ paraṃ tapaḥ |

2b) dīkṣāmāśritya nivasat yatra kutrāśrame va san || 1-34 ||

adīkṣitaśca maraṇe rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet |

tasmād dīkṣāṃ prayatnena sadā kuryācca tāntrikāt || 1-35 ||

kalpe dṛṣṭvā tu mantraṃ yai yo gṛhnāti narodhamā |

manvantarasahasreṇa niṣkṛti naiva jāyate || 1-36 ||

dīkṣitānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ adhikārotraniścitam |

ataḥ prathamato dīkṣāṃ pravakṣyāmi viśeṣataḥ || 1-37 ||


tathā ca ||


vaiśampāyena saṃhitāyāṃ śaunakaṃ praticyā savākyam |

divyaṃ jñānaṃ yato dadyāt kuryāt pāpakṣayaṃ yataḥ || 1-38 ||

ato dīkṣeti sā proktā deśikaistantravedibhiḥ |

yāvan na prāpyate dīkṣā puruṣārthaphalapradā || 1-39 ||

sarvasiddhikarī dīkṣā sarvasaṃpatpradāyinī |

pratibhāsvaravarṇāni kāntipustidharāṇi ca || 1-40 ||

mohanastaṃbhanākṛṣṭi māraṇeccāṭanāni ca |

vaśīkāraṃ ca kavitā vuddhiśca dhāraṇāyutā || 1-41 ||

tāraṇaṃ ca samudrasya vahneḥ śaityaṃ raṇe jayen |

vipiṇe cābhayaṃ caiva śokaduḥkhāpahaṃ tathā || 1-42 ||

dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇi dadātyeṣā naśaṃsayaḥ |

asādhyasādhanī dīkṣā adaivadevakāriṇī || 1-43 ||

yebhyasanti parāṃ dīkṣāṃ sarvakāmadughā bhuvi | 

te vrajanti paraṃ sthānaṃ gurvārādhanatparaḥ || 1-44 ||



iti śrī-āgamakalpalatāyāṃ prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 




2a) athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi lakṣaṇaṃ guruśiṣyayoḥ |


śrīmantramuktāvalyām ||


gakāraḥ siddhidaḥ prokto rephaḥ pāpasya dāhakaḥ || 2-1 ||

ukāro viṣṇurityuktoḥ tritayātmā guruḥ paraḥ |

gaṇeśotrāgnināyukto viṣṇunā ca samanvitaḥ || 2-2 ||

varṇadvayātmakomantracaturvargaphalapradaḥ |


tathā ca rudrayāmale ||


avadātātvaye śuddhaḥ śādhitācāra tatparaḥ || 2-3 ||

āśramīvedavitkrodharahitaḥ sarvaśāstravit |

śraddhāvāna nasūyaśca priyavān priyadarśanaḥ || 2-4 ||

śuciḥ suveśastaruṇa sarvabhūtahite rataḥ |

śrīmānanurddhatamatibhadrābhadravimarśakaḥ || 2-5 ||

prājñaḥ svakṛtadhīḥ śāntaḥ kṛtajñāḥ śiṣyavatsaraḥ |

nigrahānugrahe śaktaḥ kṣamāmantraparāyaṇaḥ || 2-6 ||

uhāmohaprakārajñaḥ sarvabhūtakṛpālayaḥ |

ityādi lakṣaṇaiyuktoḥ guruḥ syād (?) garimadyutiḥ || 2-7 ||


śrīśāradātilake |


mātṛtaḥ pitṛtaḥ śuddha śuddhabhāvojitendriyaḥ |

sarvāgamānāṃ sārajñaḥ sarvaśāstrārthatattvavit || 2-8 ||

paropakāranirato japapūjādi tatparaḥ |

amoghavacanaḥ śānto vedavedārthapāragaḥ || 2-9 ||

yogamārgānusaṃdhāyī devatā hṛdayaṃ gamaḥ |

3b) saṃhārāddhāraṇe śaktodāntaśca brāhmaṇottamaḥ || 2-10 ||

mantrāṇā ca rahasyajño mantravit sādhakottamaḥ |

udāraḥ satyavādī ca gṛhastho gururucyate || 2-11 ||

ityādi guṇasaṃpanno gururāgamasammataḥ |


devyāgameśra | śivavākyam || 2-||


dāntaḥ śāntaḥ kulīnaśca vinītaḥ śuddhaveśavān || 2-12 ||

śuddhācāraḥ śupratiṣṭhaḥ śucirdakṣaśuvuddhimān |

āśramodhyānaniṣṭhaśca mantratantraviśāradaḥ || 2-13 ||

nigrahānugrahe śakto gururityabhidhīyate |


tathā coktaṃ sārasaṃgrahe ||


gṛhītamantrasvyaktavyaḥ sādhakena sadaiva hi || 2-14 ||

arigehasthito mantro hyanucāryaśca yo manuḥ |

sa,mskārarahito mantro vidhidīkṣāvivarjitaḥ || 2-15 ||

tyaktvā sarvaprayatnena punargrāhyaṃ yathāvidhi |


brahmavaivartta ||


piturdīkṣāyate dīkṣā dīkṣā ca vanavāsinaḥ || 2-16 ||

viviktasya ca yā dīkṣā na sā kalpā na dāyinī |


śrīskandapurāṇe ||


sādhyaṃ sādhyaṃ ca yan mantramaryariṃ vā maheśvari || 2-17 ||

svapnalabdhopi yo mantra tan mantraṃ yatnatastyajet |

nīcavarṇā gṛhītaṃ ya tathā vaiṣāna sādapi || 2-18 ||

viviktasya yateścaiva pitudīkṣā vivarjayet |


vṛhamatsyasūkte ||


pitumantraṃ na gṛhnīyāt tathā mātā mahāda *? || 2-19 ||

kaniṣṭhāt sodarādvāpi vairipakṣāśritāstathā |

pramādādyā tathājñayā pitṛdīkṣāṃ samācaret || 2-20 ||

4a) prāyaścittaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā punaḥ dīkṣāṃ samācaret |

pitudīkṣāyatidīkṣā dīkṣā ca vanavāsinaḥ || 2-21 ||

anāśramīṇāṃ yā dīkṣā sā dīkṣā dhananāsinī |


taduktaṃ tathā ca śrīnārāyaṇītantre ||


piturmantraṃ na gṛhnīyāt tathā śaivāśritasya ca || 2-22 ||

kadācidapi gṛhnāti punaḥ saskāramarhati |

ityādi vacanādebhyo mantraṃ na gṛhagni (?)yādityarthaḥ || 2-23 ||


tathā ca ||


nirvīryaṃ ca pitumantraṃ śaive śākte na duṣyati |


iti kaulikamantraṃ dīkṣā param ||


yoginītantre śaktividyāmadhikṛtya dīkṣā niṣedhāt | mahātīrthe uparāge sati 
sarvatra na doṣaḥ |

sādhu pṛṣṭha tvayā brahman vakṣyāmi sakalaṃ tava || 2-24 ||

brahmaṇā kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ vaśiṣṭhasya mahātmana |

vaśiṣṭhopi suputrāya matpitre dattavātsvayam || 2-25 ||

prasannaḥ hṛdayaḥ svaccha pitāmekaruṇānidhe |

kurukṣatre mahātīrthe sūryaparvaṇi dakṣavān || 2-26 ||


ityādi vaiśamyāyanasaṃhitāyām || śaunakaṃ prati śrīvyāsavākyāt ||
viṣṇumantramapi pitu gṛhnīyā iti bhāvaḥ || prāyaścittaṃ tvayutagāyatrījapaḥ 
sarvatra tathā darśanāt |

yoginītantre ||


nirvīryaṃ ca piturmantraṃ tathā mātāmahasya ca |

svapnalabdhaṃ striyā dattaṃ saṃskāreṇaiva śuddhyati || 2-27 ||

svapnalabdhe ca kalaśe guroḥ prāṇaṃ niveśayet |

4b) caṭapatre kuṃkumeṇa likhitvā grahaṇaṃ śubham || 2-28 ||

tataḥ saphalamāpnoti anyathā viphalaṃ bhavet |


idaṃ tu sadgurorabhāve tat satve tasmādekaṃ mantraṃ gṛhnīyāditi 
svapne tu nakālaniyamaḥ svapne tu niyamo nahiti nāradavacanāt |


varjanīyaṃ guruṃ vajya mantriṇāṃhitakāmyayā || 2-29 ||


vṛhanmatsyasūkte ||


aputro mṛtaputraśca kuṣṭī ca vāmanastathā |

kunakhīśyāvadantaśca strījitopyadhikāṅgakaḥ || 2-30 ||

hīnāṅgaḥ kapaṭīrogī vahvāśīvahujalpakaḥ |

etairdoṣaivihīnoyaḥ saguruḥ śiṣṭhasammataḥ || 2-31 ||

athedānī granthavaryaśiṣyalakṣaṇamucyate |


tathā ca rudrayāmale ||


rājñicāmābhyajodoṣaḥ patnīpāpaṃ svabharttari || 2-32 ||

tathā śiṣyojitaṃ pāpaṃ guruṃ prāpnoti niścitam |

ato vicārye yatnena śiṣyalakṣaṇamucyate || 2-33 ||


tathā ca śāradātilake ||


śiṣyaḥ kulīnaḥ suddhātmā puruṣārthaparāyaṇaḥ |

adhītavedaḥ kuśalo dūramukuṭamanobhavaḥ || 2-34 ||

hitaiṣī prāṇiṇāṃ nityamāstikona tu nāstikaḥ |

svadharmanirato bhaktyā pitṛmātṛhitodyataḥ || 2-35 ||

vāṅmanaḥ kāyavasubhiguruśuśruṣaṇe rataḥ |

etā dṛśa guṇopetaḥ śiṣyo bhavati nāparaḥ || 2-36 ||


tathā ca yāmale ||


śiṣyaḥ śuddhasanājñāni puruṣārthaparāyaṇaḥ |

adhītaśāstratattvajño hi taidvīprāṇināṃ sadā || 2-37 ||

nāstikācārarahita āstikaśca jitendriyaḥ |

5a) sudharmanirataḥ śuddhaḥ pitṛmātṛhite rataḥ || 2-38 ||

śuśruṣugurubhaktaśca jijñāsuśraddhayānvitaḥ |

kāmakrodhādirahito niviṣṭomantrapūjana || 2-39 ||

vāṅmanaḥ kāyavasubhi guruśuśruṣaṇerataḥ |

sarvātmanātitīṣuśca bhavārddhe bhavaduḥkha kṛt || 2-40 ||

brāhmaṇo dharmamokṣārthī kāmārthī vigataḥ spṛhaḥ |

kiṃ vodhanārthamokṣāthī niskāmaścārthavā dvijaḥ || 2-41 ||

mātṛvaṅgurupatnīṣu bhrātṛvat tat suteṣvapi | 

devavuddhyā sadā teṣu bhaktyā pūjanamācaret || 2-42 ||

etesu daivānukaṃpyo varjyānanyānivodhatī |

idānīṃ varjyaśiṣyānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ kathayāmi ca || 2-43 ||


pākhaṇḍāṅkuśe ||


alsāmalināḥ kuddho dāṃbhikāḥ kṛpaṇāḥ khalāḥ |

daridrā rogiṇaḥ kliṣṭā rāgiṇobhogalālasāḥ || 2-44 ||

asrūyāmatsaragrastāḥ śaṭhāḥ paruṣavādinaḥ |

anyāyopārjitadhanāḥ paradārarataśca ya || 2-45 ||

viduṣāṃ vairiṇaścaiva ajñāpaṇḍitamāninaḥ |

bhraṣṭavratāśca ye kecit piśunāḥ kaṣṭalālasāḥ || 2-46 ||

vahvāśinaḥ kruraceṣṭā durātmānaśca ninditāḥ |

ityāvamādayopyanye pāpiṣṭā puruṣādhamāḥ || 2-47 ||

evaṃ bhūtāḥ parityājyāḥ śiṣyatvenopakalpayet |

adya te upakalperan devatā krodhabhājanāḥ || 2-48 ||

bhavanatihadaridrāste putradāravivarjitāḥ |

nārakāścaiva dehānte tiryatkaṃ prabhavanti te || 2-49 ||

5b) kṣudrā pralobhitāstebhyo ninditebhyoti śanti ya |

vinaśyate ca sakalaṃ śaikatośālivijavat || 2-50 ||


tathā ca sārasaṃgrahe ||


śaṭhaḥ krūraśca mūrkhaśca cumbakobhaktivarjitaḥ |

atisāhasikaścaiva dūṣito deva nindakaḥ || 2-51 ||

kulanindā paraścaiva paradāratatastathā |

etairdoṣai vihīno yaḥ śaśiṣyaḥ śiṣṭasammataḥ || 2-52 ||

nadeyaṃ paraśiṣyeṣu paricaryā rateṣvapi |


brahmavaivattem ||


prātikulyaṃ guronityaṃ manasāpina kārayet || 2-53 ||

śadāśivadhiyā nityaṃ ekagrāmaṇivāsinam |

guruṃ vande taveddūre manasā vandanaṃ caret || 2-54 ||

prātaḥ śirasiśuklebje dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ gurum |

varābhayaṃ karaṃ śāntaṃ smaret tannāmapūrvakam || 2-55 ||

oṃ ajñānatimirāndhasya jñānāñjanasalākayā |

cakṣurunmīlitaṃ yena tasmai śrīgurave namaḥ || 2-56 ||

brahmāviṣṇuśivādīnāṃ pūjayet saphalaṃ bhavet |

tatphalaṃ samavāpnoti pratyahaṃ vaṃdanaguroḥ || 2-57 ||

gurubrahmā guruviṣṇu gurudevo maheśvaraḥ |

santuṣṭastu guruyasmai tasmai tuṣṭaṃ jagatraya || 2-58 ||

śive raṣṭe gurustātā gurauraṣṭe na kaścanaḥ |

avidyo vā savidyo vā gurureva tu daivatam || 2-59 ||

amārgasthothamārgastho gurureva parā gatiḥ |

yathāsiddharasasparśā tāmraṃ bhavati kāṃcanam || 2-60 ||

sannidhāne guroreva śiṣyaḥ śivamayo bhavet |

bhavārṇavatariḥsāstā gurureva ca saccāgatiḥ || 2-61 ||

6a) gurau tiṣṭhati yastvanye māsanaṃ pūjayejjaḍaḥ |

sadurgatimavāpnoti pūjā sā viphalā bhavet || 2-62 ||

praṇipatya guruṃ paścān mohād vipratipadyate |

yugakoṭisahasrāṇi narake pacyate tathā || 2-63 ||

ye gurvājñāṃ prakurvanti pāpiṣṭhāḥ puruṣādhamāḥ |

na teṣāṃ narakakleśanistārostikathaṃ ca na || 2-64 ||

yaiḥ śiṣyaiḥ śaśvadārādhyā guravojyavamānitāḥ |

putramitrakaratrādi saṃpatyaḥ pracyutāhite || 2-65 ||

adhikṣipya guruṃ mohāt paruṣaṃ pravadanti ya |

śukaratvaṃ bhavatyeva teṣāṃ janmagateṣvapi || 2-66 ||

ye gurudrohiṇo mūḍhaḥ satataṃ pāpakāriṇaḥ |

teṣāṃ tu yāvat sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ syān na saṃśayaḥ || 2-67 ||

yeṣāṃ bhaktigurau sthairyā muktisteṣāṃ kare sthitā |

śuśruṣayā dhanairdhānyaiścailai vākyaiḥ śubhāvahaiḥ || 2-68 ||

vaśaṃ kuryād guruṃ dhīraḥ svacchaṃdahṛdayena vai |

yasya nāsti gurau bhakti jñānaṃ cāpi tathāvidham || 2-69 ||

na tasya jāyate siddhiḥ kalpakoṭiśatairapi |

gurau bhaktiḥ sadā kāryā guruśceddoṣa (?) sayutaḥ || 2-70 ||

mahāpātakayukto vā gururdevaḥ sadāśivaḥ (-śiḥ) |


tathā ca devyāgame ||


guraśeryāsanaṃ yānapādukāṃ caiva pīṭhakam || 2-71 ||

snānodakaṃ tathā cchāyāṃ laṃghayen na kadācana |

guroragre pṛthak pūjā mauhṛtyaṃ ca vivarjayet || 2-72 ||

dīkṣā vyākhyā prabhutvaṃ ca guroragre parityajet |

gurumantraṃ devatānāmaikyaṃ sabhāvayed dhiyā || 2-73 ||

vijñānasyāt prameyasya nirguṇasyāśarīriṇaḥ |

sāṣakānāṃ hitārthāya brahmaṇorūpakalpanā || 2-74 ||

santuṣṭo hi guru yatra tatra tuṣṭo jagatpati |

deve ruṣṭe guruṣṭātā gurauraṣṭe na kaścana || 2-75 ||

gurordehaṃ vyapāśritya mantradevena tiṣṭhati |

utpādaka brahmadātrorgarīyān brahmadaḥ pitā || 2-76 ||

tasmān manyatasatataṃ piturapyadhikaṃ gurum |

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena gururmānyodhanādibhiḥ || 2-77 ||

yat prasādātparaṃ sthānaṃ prāpyate bhuvidullabham |

gandhaimālyaidhūpadravyai saṃpūjya gurumagrataḥ || 2-78 ||

tato mantraṃ ca gṛhnīyād vastreṇācchādyamastakam |

daśavaṃśastathā mātu śaśurasya tathā daśa || 2-79 ||

pitṛṇāmekapaṃcāśa svargaṃ gacchati niścitam |

naṃdati pitaraḥ sarve putre mantrārthasākṣiṇī || 2-80 ||

gṛhnanti svargabhogāśca svarge vāsaḥ suniścayaḥ |

vaiṣavavaiṣavaṃ grāhyaṃ śaive śaivaṃ ca śāktike || 2-81 ||

kalauśaktipradhānaṃ ca brāhmaṇādibhireva ca |

naśūdrasyādhikārosti purāṇe nigamātmake || 2-82 ||

śūdraśiṣye manorjñānaṃ utkarṣaṃ na hi sarvathā |

lobhārthai yadi tat kuryād viṣṇumantraṃ vi naiva hi || 2-83 ||

praṇavādyaṃ na dātavyaṃ mantraṃ śūdrāyasarvathā |

ātmamantraṃ guromantraṃ mantraṃ cājapasaṃjñakam || 2-84 ||

ātmakaulikamantraṃ ca naiva dadyāt kadācana |

gopālasya manurdeyo maheśasyāpi pādaje || 2-85 ||

tat patnyāṃ cāpi sūryasya gaṇeśasya maṇostathā |

7a) eṣāṃ dīkṣādhikārisyāt anyathā pāpabhāgbhavet || 2-86 ||


taduktaṃ śrītantrarāje ||


pustake likhitā vidyā yena sundarijapyate |

na jāyate tasya siddhi hānireva prajāyate || 2-87 ||


ityāgamakalpalatāyāṃ dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ || 2 ||




guroścānmaviśuddhyarthaṃ śiṣyāṃstu pariśodhayet |

abdaikena bhaved yogyo brāhmaṇobdadvayānnṛpaḥ || 3-1 ||

vaiśyo vaśaistribhiḥ śūdraścatubhirvatsaraissadā |

śiṣyanāpi tathā kuryāt svakīyābhiṣṭasiddhaye || 3-2 ||

ādau nṛpaśca sevyaśca jñeyaścābhīṣṭamicchatā |


gururiti śeṣa ||

tathā ca gautamīya ||


śubhehni snānamācarya namatkuryād gaṇeśvaram |

sūryaṃ śivaṃ hariṃ durgāṃ vāṇī ca kamalālayām || 3-3 ||

mātaraṃ pitaraṃ natvā brāhmaṇān pariṣya ca |

phalaṃ puṣpaṃ ca maṅgalyaṃ gṛhītvā bhaktisaṃyutaḥ || 3-4 ||

puruṣārthasamāvāptyai saśiṣyogurumāśrayet |

ātmavijñāpanaṃ kṛtvā pitṛmātṛkulānvitam || 3-5 ||

gurutveṇaiva saṃvodhya praṇamed daṃḍavad bhuvi |

guroragre samutthāya puṭāṃjalikarasthitaḥ || 3-6 ||

vicāraya mahāvāho maddhitaṃ mantravaryakam |

gururvicārayet paścād eya mantrāśca yatnataḥ || 3-7 ||


evaṃ ca ||


śvatālalāśikoṣṭānāṃ anukulaṃ bhajen manun |


evam ||


jha na mantraṃ na gṛhnīyād akulaṃ ca tathaiva ca || 3-8 ||


tyādivacanāccakra vicārasyāvaśyakatvāt prathamaṃ tannirūpyate |


7b) atha vakṣyavicāraṃ ca cakrāṇāṃ siddhihetave |

ādau kulākulaṃ cakraṃ makathākhyaṃ dvitīyakam || 3-9 ||

tṛtīyaṃ jha ṇa dhanākhyaṃ caturthaṃ vargasaṃjñakam |

paṃcamaṃ rāśicakraṃ syāt ṣaṣṭhaṃ nakṣatracakrakam || 3-10 ||

saptamaṃ gaṇacakraṃ syād aṣṭamamakadaṃ smṛtam |

navamaṃ mudditaṃ cakraṃ daśamaṃ nādikāhvayam || 3-11 ||


kramenāha ||

tathā ca śāradātirake ||

kāraṇāt pañcabhūtānāṃ udbhūtā mātṛkā yataḥ |

ato bhūtātmakā varṇāḥ paṃcapaṃcavibhāgataḥ || 3-12 ||

vāyvagnibhūjalākāśāḥ paṃcāśallipayaḥ kramāt |

paṃcahrasvācyaṃ ca dīrghā vidvantāṃ saṃdhisaṃbhavāḥ || 3-13 ||

paṃcāśaḥ kādayaḥ ṣakṣalasahāntā samīritāḥ |

svanāmādyakṣarāṇāṃ tu mitraṃ vā svakulaṃ varam || 3-14 ||


paṃcapaṃceti daśavarṇa ityarthaḥ ||

vāyvagnītyādi ||

8a) tat kulaṃ tasya tat proktaṃ mevamanyaṣulakṣayet || 3-16 ||


saṃdhiḥ saṃbhavati | 

e ai o au ekatro bhayāvasthāne tvakulam |

mitramiti parasparamantram ||

āgneyasyāṃ hasāṃ śatruraṃbhaśastaijalaṃ smṛtam |

uktaṃ kulokulaṃ cakraṃ samastapuruṣārthadam || 3-20 ||


iti kulākulacakram ||

tadā dakṣiṇadīrghāṃ dadyād rekhādvayaṃ vudha |

8b) tataḥ ṣoḍaśakoṣṭhaṃ ca jāyate cakravaryakam || 3-23 ||

ādikṣātān likhed varṇān krameṇānena mantravit |


piṅgalāmṛte ||


idvagnirudranavanetrayugārkadikṣu ṛtvaṣṭaṣoḍaśacaturddaśabhītikeṣu |

pātālapaṃcadaśavahni himāṃśukoṣṭhe varṇāllikhellipi bhavān kramaśastu 
dhīmān || 3-24 ||

nāmādvekṣaramārabhya yāvanmantrādyamakṣaram | catubhiḥ koṣṭhairekaikamiti 
koṣṭhacatuṣṭayam || punaḥ koṣṭhakakoṣṭheṣu savyate | nāmna āditaḥ |

siddhaḥ sādhyaḥ susiddhoriḥ kramā jñeyo vikṣaṇaiḥ |

siddhaḥ siddhyati kālena sādhyastu japahomataḥ || 3-25 ||

susiddho grahaṇādeva ripumūlaṃ nikṛntati |

siddhārṇā vāṃdhavā jñeyāḥ sādhyā (snehā) sevakāḥ smṛtāḥ || 3-26 ||

susiddhaḥ poṣakaḥ proktāḥ śatravoghātaka smṛtaḥ |

jayena vaṃdhuḥ siddhaḥ syāt sevakodhikasevayā || 3-27 ||

puṣṇāti poṣakobhīṣṭaṃ ghātakonāśayeddhruvam |

siddhasiddho yathoktaśca dviguṇaḥ siddhasādhyakaḥ || 3-28 ||

siddhaḥ sasiddhorddhajapāt siddhā mirhanti vāndhavān | (?)

sādhyasiddho dviguṇakaḥ sādhyasādhyo nirarthakaḥ || 3-29 ||

sādhya susiddhorddhajapāt sādhyārihanti gotrajān |

9a) susiddhasiddhorddhajapārtasādhyo dviguṇādhikaḥ || 3-30 ||

grahaṇāt tat susiddhastu susiddhoriḥ svagotrahā |

arisiddhaḥ sutaṃ hanyād arisādhyastu kanyakān || 3-31 ||

patnimarīsusiddhastu aryarimūlaghātakaḥ |


iti akathahacakram ||


atha ṛṇadhanaṃ vakṣya cakraṃ varṇāṅkasaṃyutam || 3-32 ||


taduktaṃ tantrarāje ||


kathaṃ ṛṇitvaṃ mantrāṇāṃ sādhakānāṃ tathaiva ca |

pūrvajanmakṛtābhyāsājjapādasyā phalāpti kṛt || 3-33 ||

pāpe naṣṭe phalā vāpti kāle dehakṣayā dṛśī |

mantraḥ saṃprāptimātreṇa prāk putaḥ siddhaye bhavet || 3-34 ||

siddhamantrā guroravdhā mantrāyaḥ siddhibhāṅna (?)raḥ |

lakṣmī mahāsarvapūjyo mantrabhogamavāptavān || 3-35 ||

samantrasya ritijñoyo bhajanaṃ tasya pūrvagam |

tasmād ṛṇaviśuddhistu kāryā sarvaistu sarvastu sarvadā || 3-36 ||


tathā vāgamakalpadrume ||


pūrvāparaṃ dvādaśaṃkhyasūtraṃ yā māttarasthaṃ ca tathā catuṣkam |

dvau dvau svarau paṃcasukoṣṭhakeṣu śeṣānsvarān ṣaṭsuṣadekamekam || 3-37 ||


kāderhaśeṣānvilīkhet tatorṇān ekaikamekādaśasusthaleṣuḥ |


indrarkṣanetraravipaṃdaśartu vedavahnyā yudhāṣṭanavabhiguṇitāṃśca 
sādhyān | 

digbhūgiriśrūti gajāgnimuniṣu vedaṣaḍvahnibhiśca guṇitānaghasādha 
(kāṃ)kān || 3-38 ||

9b) indrādibhiḥ gajahṛtai gaṇitaiḥ svakoṣṭhaiḥ sādhyaṃ va sādhakalipiṃ ca 
digādibhiśca |

nāmārddhalādakathavāṅgajabhuktaśeṣaṃ jñātvobhayoradhikaśeṣamṛṇaṃ 
dhanaṃ syāt || 3-39 ||


asyārtha ||

yasmin koṣṭhe sādhyasādhakayoraṃkaṃ dṛśyate |

tat koṣṭhasthitāṅkaṃ gṛhasaṃkhyākaiḥ pūrayitvāṣṭabhirbhāgamāharet |

sarvatraharaṇamaṣṭabhireva hārakā bhāvāt saptaparyantā va sthānaṃ 
ṣaṭkālakāla iti vasanadvayaṃ siddhāṅkapradarśakamiti niṣkarṣaḥ |

prakārāntaramāha ||

tathā ca sundarirahasya vṛttau ||


pūrvāparāya taṃ lekhyaṃ rekhādvādaśakaṃ kramāt |

krameṇaitrajāyante koṣṭhānyekādaśakramāt || 3-40 ||

teṣu varṇaṃ llikhen mantrī svarādikramayogataḥ |

dvau dvau svarau paṃcasukoṣṭhakeṣu śeṣān svarān ṣatsukhadekamekam |

kādahaśeṣānvilikhet tatorṇānekaikamekādaśamātrakeṣu || 3-41 ||

sādhyākānvilikhedūrdhve sādhakākānadholikhe |


indrarkṣanetrādi |


mantrī yadyadhikā *? syāt tadā mantraṃ japet sudhīḥ || 3-42 ||

samepi ca japenmantrī najapet tu riṇādhike || 3-43 ||

guṇite tu kṛtāṅkāṇāṃ yaccheṣaṃ jāyate sphuṭam |

tadaṃkaṃ kathayāmyatra dvādaśāṅgārasaṃsthitam || 3-44 ||

ṣaṭkālakālaviyadagni samudraveda khākāśaśūnyadahanāḥ khaludevatārṇāḥ

10) yugmadvipaṃ ca viyadaṃ varayuk śaśāṅkavyomāvdhivedaśaśanaḥ 
khalusādhakārṇāḥ || 3-45 ||


anya prakālamāha |


koṣṭhānāṃ kādaśānye va vede na pūritāni ca |

ākārādihakārāntaṃ likhe koṣṭheṣu tattvavit || 3-46 ||

prathamaṃ paṃcakoṣṭheṣu hrasvadīrghakrameṇa tu |

dvayaṃ dvayaṃ likhe tatra vicārakhalusādhakaḥ || 3-47 ||

śaṣeṣvaikaikaśovarṇān kramatastu likhet svadhīḥ |


ṣaṭkālakāletyādi ||

asyārtha ||

sādhyavarṇān svaravyaṃjanarūpeṇa pṛthak kṛtān ṣaṭkālodyaṃ kaiguṇitān 
kṛtvā |

tathā sādhakanāmākṣarān yugmādyaiḥ aṃkairguṇayitvāṣṭasaṃkhyābhi-
hṛtvā ubhayośca sādhyasādhakayoḥ śeṣasyādhikarūpasya ṛṇaṃ dhanaṃ 
jñātvā mantraṃ dadyāt |

mantraścettanī bhavati tadā śubhadāyako bhavati dhanīcenna tathā |

indrarkṣanetrebhyādināmāddhalādivacanaṃ tu viṣṇuviṣayaṃ 
śrīrāmārccanacandrikā dhṛtatvāt |

prakārāntaramāha ||

śrītantrarāje ||


nāmādvekṣaramārabhya yāvanmantrādimaṃ bhavet || 3-48 ||

tridhā kṛtvā suraihutvā tadunyadviparītakam |


asyārthaḥ ||

sādhakanādyakṣarato gaṇayā pāvanmantrādimaṃ bhavet |

tridhā kṛtvā suraihutvākṣaraṃ tat saṃkhyaṃ tridhā kṛtvā saptabhihutvādhikaṃ 
ṛṇaṃ śeśe uddhva siddhāṅkamāha |


10b) ṣaṭkālakālaviyadagni samudravedakhākāśaśūnyadahanāḥ 
khalusādhyavarṇā |


sādhya devatā ||

atha siddhāṅkamāha ||


yugmadvipaṃ ca vidāvarayuk śaśāṅkaśūnyāvdhivedaśaśinaḥ 
khalusādhakārṇāḥ || 3-49 ||

indrarkṣanetra iti navabhirguṇitāṃśca sādhyān |

digrūgiśrūti triguṇitānaghasādhakāṅkān || 3-50 ||


ityartha ||

ṣadhanaṃ syāt |

tadanyaditi |

saptaguṇaṃ kṛtvā tribhirharet |


sādhyanāmadviguṇitaṃ sādhakena samanvitam |

aṣṭabhiśca hare ccheṣaṃ tadanyaviparītakam || 3-51 ||


asyārtha ||

sādhyanāma svaravyaṃjanabhedena saṃyojyāṣṭabhirhutvā ṛṇaṃ dhaṇaṃ 
jñeyaṃ tadunyadapi |

11a) sādhakānāmādyakṣarān svaravyaṃjanabhedena dviguṇīkṛtya 
sādhyakṣareṇa svaravyaṃjanabhedena saṃyojyāstabhirhutvā ṛṣaṇaṃ jñeyamiti 
ṛṇadhanacakram |


vargacakraṃ pravakṣyāmi jyotirāgasammatam |

mantrāṇāmariśodhārthaṃ nāmādvekṣarapūrvakam || 3-52 ||

aṣṭaukoṣṭhān samāpādya vargānaṣṭau likhet tataḥ |

pakṣimājjārapaṃcāsya gurusarpāṣudantinaḥ || 3-53 ||

meṣakramādakārādi vasuvargāśritā ime |

sādhyasādhakayo prītirjñātavyā jātitaḥ sadā || 3-54 ||

pakṣimārjārayo vairaṃ vairaṃ kurkkuṭapakṣiṇeḥ |

vairaṃ pakṣibhujaṃgānāṃ śāstravaṃ gajasiṃhayoḥ || 3-55 ||

meṣakurkkurayo vairaṃ prītiyuktyā vicārayet |

evaṃ cakravaraṃ jñātvā gṛhīyān mantramuttamam || 3-56 ||


prakārāntaramāha |

vārāhe tantre ||


mājjālakurkkulavyālaiḥ pakṣiṇāṃ vairitā bhavet |

mārjārasya sunākhubhyāṃ siṃhasya dantinā sadā || 3-57 ||

kurkkurasya khagābhyāṃ tu kharākhubhyāṃ ca bhoginaḥ |

undurasyau tu sapābhyāṃ hariṇāsahadantinaḥ || 3-58 ||

bhujaṃgasiṃhayo maittrī meṣakayostathā |

lokādanyatra vijñayaṃ vyavahāraprayogataḥ || 3-59 ||


iti vargacakram |


idānīṃ tu pravakṣyāmi rāśicakraṃ viśeṣataḥ |

sūtradvayaṃ pūrvaparāyataṃ tanmadhyadvayaṃ yāmyakuverabhedāt |

11b) ekaikamīśānaniśācare tu hutāśavāyvo vilikhet tatorṇān || 3-60 ||

vedāgnivahniyugalaśravaṇākṣisaṃkhyān 
paṃcaṣuvāṇaśarapaṃcacatuṣṭayāntān |

mekhāditaḥ pravilikhet sakalāṃstu varṇān kanyā gatān pravilikhedatha 
sādivarṇān || 3-61 ||


tathā ca vṛhatmatsyasūkte ||


vedādi brahmayugaladvivāṇavāneṣu vāṇeṣupayodhasaṃkhyān |

meṣādi koṭheṣvabhilikhyavarṇā ṣaḍeva śādinyuvatau likhitvā || 3-62 ||


tathā ca sārasaṃgrahe |


caturbhiyādibhiḥ sārddhaṃ syāt kṣakārastu mīnaga iti |


śāradāyām ||


vālaṃ gaureṣuraṃ śoṇaṃ śamīśobhetirāśiṣu || 3-63 ||

krameṇabheditā varṇāḥ kanyāyāṃ sādaya sthitāḥ |


tathā ca varāhasaṃhitāyām ||


japtuḥ svarāśitovarkṣyāḥ ṣaṣṭāṣṭaniṣphaṇāḥ śubhāḥ || 3-64 ||

ekadvitrīcatuḥpaṃcasaptākṣadaśarudragāḥ |

* * * naṃ * * vaṃdhu putraśattru kalattrakāḥ || 3-65 ||

vaiṣṇave mate tu śatrusthāne vaṃdhuriti pāṭha |

12a) maraṇaṃ dharmakarmāyadha |

tatroyamupadeśa ||

pūrvapaścimāyate lekhā dvayaṃ dakṣiṇottarāyataṃ rekhādvayam | 

īśānādikoṇeṣu rekhācatuṣṭayaṃ tatkoṣṭheṣu paṃcāśadvarṇāmekhādito 
vakṣyamāṇakrameṇalikhya saḥ |

vapūrvāṣāḍhāmānuṣaḥ |

bha urttarakhāḍhāmānuṣaḥ |

maḥ śravaṇadevaḥ |

ya dhaniṣṭhā rākṣasaḥ |

sa śatabhiṣā mānuṣaḥ |

va śa pūrvabhadrapadā mānuṣaḥ |

ṣa sa ha uttarabhadrapadā mānuṣaḥ |

laṃ aṃ aḥ lakṣaḥ revatī devaḥ |

tṛtīyapaṃcamasaptamānivarjanīyāninakṣatrāṇi ||

svanakṣattrā devagaṇanīyam |

svanakṣattrā jñāna svanāmākṣarasaṃvaṃdhi nakṣatrā devanīyam |

prādakṣiṇya na gaṇayet sādhakākṣarataḥ sudhīriti ||

tathā ca jyotiṣe ||

hastasvātimṛgāśvinī harigurupauṣṇānurādhāditi ścā *?rohicottarābharaṇī 
pūrvānibhānitrayam |

jyeṣṭhā śleṣaviśāṣamūlavaruṇā vesvagnicittā maghāḥ kathyante 
munibhiryathākramamimede vānarā rākṣasāḥ |

jyotiṣe ||

kaṭapayavargabhaveriha piṇḍa atyairakṣarairaṃkāḥ ne ṅe śūnyaṃ jñeyaṃ 
tathā svarairakṣarairevam |

piṇḍe saṃyukte akṣare ||

śāradātilake ||


prāpalobhāt paṭuṃ prājyaṃ rudrasyātriruruḥ kakhet || 3-66 ||


12b) a ā i ī meṣa | u ū ṛ vṛṣe | 

ṛ ḷ ḹ mithune | e ai karkaṭe | 

o au siṃha | aṃ aḥ śa ṣa sa ha kanyāyām | 

ka kha ga gha ṅa tulāyām | ca cha ja jha ña vṛścike | 

ṭa ṭha ḍa ḍha ṇa dhanuṣi | ta tha da dha na makare | 

pa pha ba bha ma kuṃbha | ya la ra va mīna | 


ragnādhanamityādi krameṇa gananīyaṃ mīne kṣakāramiti kecit | 

caturbhiryādibhiḥ sārdhaṃ syāt kṣakārastu mīna ka iti vacanāt || 3-67 ||


vārāhī tantre | 


ekothavā pyatha paṃcamotha navamo rāśeḥ svakānvāndhavo rāśeḥ syād 
daśamo dvitīyasahitaṣaṣṭho bhavet sevakaḥ | 

rudrāgnisvarasaṃkhyayā nigadito mantraḥ svayaṃ poṣakaḥ syāccedaṣṭamavatsara 
śrutimite mantraḥ svayaṃ ghātakaḥ || 3-68 ||


lagnādhanantrā tṛśatruputramitrakaratrakāmaraṇaṃ dharmakāmāya vyayā 
dvādaśarāśayaḥ |

atra rāśicakre caturthasthānaṃ śatrurna tu vaṃdhuḥ | 

naśatruriti kaścit tacitnaniyam | iti rāśicakram ||

atha nakṣatracakraṃ ṛṣyaśuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi śiṣyānāṃ hitakāmyayā |


pūrvāparāyatā rekhā daśadeyā prayatnataḥ |

udagdakṣiṇadīrghāśca rekhā deyā catuṣṭayam || 3-69 ||

navatritayasaṃyuktāḥ koṣṭhāṃ nā śaptaviṃśatiḥ |

jāyate ca tathā kurmā likhedvarṇāstu teṣu ca || 3-70 ||

vakṣyaika trisvirūpā (?) vanibhujaśaśiyugyugma bhūyugmapakṣā 
yugmaikadvitrirūpa avaniśaśidyaikapakṣāgnivedā |

 (citra)

13a) aśvinyādikrameṇākṣaramiha gaditaṃ śeṣabhe viṃdusargau 
japtunakṣatrabhedād atha parigaṇayejjanmasaṃpatkrameṇa || 3-71 ||

janmasampad vipatkṣamaṃ pratyarisādhakā vudha |

maitraṃ paramamaitraṃ ca janmādīri punaḥ punaḥ || 3-72 ||

gaṇaya tu kramādeva phalaṃ nāmānurupataḥ |

janmarkṣa tu bhaveddhāniḥ saṃpattiḥ syācca saṃpadiḥ || 3-73 ||

vipantāḥ ravipantiḥ syā kṣame maṃgalamādiśet |

pratyarauśat tu vṛddhiḥ syāt sādhakasiddhisādhanam || 3-74 ||

vadharkṣa ca vadhojñeyo mitrarkṣamitravandhanam |

atimitre bhavet saukhyaṃ tārācakramiti smṛtam || 3-75 ||

vipadvadhapratyariśca parityājyāmaṇīṣibhiḥ |

nijanāmādhikaṃ varṇaṃ dāmāyaḥ purataḥ priya || 3-76 ||

nāmādyakṣaraparyantaṃ gaṇanīyaśca sūribhiḥ |


ityājñātarāśiviṣayam ||

jñāte tu tadeva || 3-siddhārthamāha ||

pūrvāparāyatāṃ rekhā datvāddakṣiṇottarāyatāṃ ca rekhā catuṣṭayaṃ 
dattvā śaṣṭaviṃśatikoṣṭhe pravakṣyamāṇakrameṇa paṃcāśadvarṇā likhet |

tadyathā |

a ā aśvinī devagaṇaḥ |

ī bharaṇī mānuṣaḥ | ī u ū kṛttikā rākṣasaḥ | 

ṛ ṝ ḷ ḹ rohiṇī mānuṣaḥ | e mṛgaśirāḥ devaḥ | 

ai ādrā mānuṣaḥ | o au punarvasu devaḥ | 

kaḥ puṣya devaḥ | kha aśleṣā rākṣasaḥ | 

gha ṅa maghā rākṣasaḥ | ya pūrvāphālguṇī mānuṣaḥ | 

jha ñaḥ hastaḥ devaḥ | ṭa ṭha citrā rākṣasaḥ |

da svāti devaḥ | ḍha ṇa viśākhā rākṣasaḥ | 

ta tha da anurādhā devaḥ | dha jyeṣṭhā rākṣasaḥ | 

na pa pha mūlaṃ rākṣasaḥ (rā * * ?) | raḥ |

13 b) lokalopapaṭuḥ prāyaḥ khalaugho bheṣu bheditaḥ || 3-77 ||

varṇāḥ kramāt svarāntyau tu revatyaṃ śagato tadā |


gavākṣatantre |


prāyaṃ somaṃ guṇaṃ vedaṃ rūpaṃ rūpaṃ yamaṃ śaśi || 3-78 ||

prāyaṃ prāyaṃ tathā caṃdraṃ triprāyaṃ somayugmakam |

guṇaikaḥ guṇatriḥsomayamānte somakhaṃ guṇāḥ || 3-79 ||

vedaceti nyasedekaṃ tat saṃkhyaṃ ca nyasellipiḥ |

svanāmādyakṣarasya nakṣatreṇa mantrādyakṣarasya vicāraḥ || 3-80 ||

rasāṣṭanavabhadrāṇi yugmayugmaprabhedatāḥ |

itarāṇi nabhadrāṇi jñātavyaṃ mantrakovidaiḥ || 3-81 ||


tathā ca tantracūḍāmaṇau ||


ārabhyajanmanakṣatraṃ tantratārāvasānikam |


yugmaṃ tāvaṭvayaṃ catuṣṭayamityādi ||

iti nakṣatracakram ||

mantraṃ vakṣyakanamākhyaṃ sasiddhaṃ sarvasiddhidam || 3-82 ||
