14a) akārādikṣakārāntān vilikhet kramataḥ sudhīḥ |

ādikoṣṭhe yathā varṇā gāyate ka *? māḥ kramāt || 3-84 ||

vilikhye gaṇaye japtu nāmādyakṣarapūrvakam |

siddhisādhyasusiddhāni krameṇaiva punaḥ punaḥ || 3-85 ||

yadi mantro na sobhante vicāreṇa kathaṃ ca na |

tadā niṣedhamāśritya gṛhaṇīyān mantramuttamam || 3-86 ||


anyacca ||


akārādikṣakārāntaṃ dakṣamārgakrameṇa tu |

meṣāditāpi nīrātaṃ gaṇayet kramataḥ sudhīḥ || 3-87 ||

japtvā svanāmato mantrī yāvanmantrādyamakṣaram |

siddhasādhyasusidhāri krameṇaiva punaḥ punaḥ || 3-88 ||


iti ṣaḍamantracakram ||

idaṃ tu gopālaviṣayem ||

uktaṃ pāṣāṃdāṅkuśe ||


uditākhyaṃ mahācakraṃ kathayāmisamāsataḥ |

ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vilikheccakraṃ samarekhaṃ manoharam || 3-89 ||

pūrvādivilikhe varṇā nādikṣāntāṃ nyaṭhā kramāt |

udite samadudiṣṭā dvitīye saṃpadāṃ kṣayaḥ || 3-90 ||

tṛtīya ca dhṛtiṃ viṃdyāccaturthe vaṃdhuviṣṇavaḥ |

paṃcame saṃsayātmā syāt ṣaṣṭhe sarvavināśakaḥ || 3-91 ||


 (citram)

14b) svanāmādyakṣarādi mantrādyakṣaraparyantaṃ gaṇanīyam |

atha gaṇacakram ||

yogiṇītantre ||


pūrvottaratrayaṃ paścā bharaṇyādrārtharohiṇī |

imānimānuṣyānyāhu nakṣatrāṇi maṇiṣiṇaḥ || 3-92 ||

jyaṣṭhāśatabhiṣaḍmūla dhaniṣṭhāśleṣakṛttikā |

citrāmaghāviśākhāḥ syu tārārākṣasadevatāḥ || 3-93 ||

aśvinīrevatīpuṣya svātihastapunarvasuḥ |

anurādhāmṛgaśilāḥ śravaṇād eva tālakaḥ || 3-94 ||


idaṃ cakraṃ nakṣatracakrāntargataṃ te naivāsya caritārthatvāt |

pṛthagupādānaṃ tu saṃsadāyānurodhāt ||

 (citram)

iti gaṇacakram || atha nāḍīcakraṃ || 

tathā ca tantre ||


ekanāḍī sthitā yasya gurumantrāśca devatāḥ |

tatra dveṣorujaṃ mṛtyuṃ krameṇaphalamādiśet || 3-95 ||

nakṣatramekaṃ yadi bhinnarāśirnadaṃyatī tatra śubhaṃ labhetām |

abhinna ṛṣyaṃ yadi caikarāśi tadā śubhaṃ syān munayo vadanti || 3-96 ||


tathā ca jyotiṣe || 3-sarpākoroghaṭanaviśeṣaḥ ||

15a) kṛrttikamukhayamadaiva ------------------------------------- taśeṣaḥ |

pṛṣṭhesu tahatanmadhyarogaḥ | 

rekhā bhinne yadudvāho vahudhaṃ na sutavadati varāhaḥ |

etad vacanasyān mulatvād vivāhaprakaraṇīyatvān neṣvantarbhāva iti ||

tathāpi saṃpradāyānurodhāllikhitam |

iti nāḍīcakram |


ādau kulākulaṃ cakraṃ nakathākhyaṃ dvitīyakam |

tṛtīyaṃ ṛṇadhanākhyaṃ caturthaṃ vargasaṃjñakam || 3-97 ||

pañcamaṃ rāśicakraṃ syāt ṣaṣṭhaṃ nakṣatracakrakam |

saptamaṃ gaṇacakraṃ syād aṣṭamakaḍema smṛtam || 3-98 ||

navamaṃ muditaṃ cakraṃ daśamaṃ nādikāṅkaṭām |

uktaṃ nānāvidhaṃ cakraṃ nānātantrasya darśanāt || 3-99 ||


vārāhītantre |


tārācakraṃ rāśicakraṃ nāmacakraṃ tathaiva ca |

atra ce ccheṣṭamantrāṇāṃ nānyacakraṃ vicārayet || 3-100 ||


athāpavāda ||

tathā coktaṃ magnipurāṇe |


haṃsasyāṣṭākṣarasyāpi tathā paṃcākṣarasya ca |

napuṃsakasya mantrasya siddhādīnneva śodhayet || 3-101 ||

mantravidyāvibhāgena trividhāmantrajātayaḥ |

mantrāḥ puṃ devatā jñeyā vidyāstrīdaiva smṛtā || 3-102 ||

puṃ strī napuṃsakātmāno mantrāḥ sarvasamīritāḥ |

hu/ matrā hu/ phadantaḥ syurdvithāntāḥ syustrīyo matāḥ || 3-103 ||


15b) napuṃsakānamontāḥ syu bhidyante manavastriyo dhāśestrāstrevidhā 
mantrā vaśyasāmtyabhicārake |


vicāryaniyataṃ mantrī gṛhanīyāt sarvasiddhaye |

mantraṃ vinā naiva siddhiḥ kutrāpi jāyate nṛṇām || 3-104 ||


yāmale ||


nṛsiṃhārkavarāhāṇāṃ prāsādapraṇavasya ca |

piṃḍitākṣaramantrāṇāṃ yuktākṣaramantraṇām || 3-105 ||


tathā coktaṃ sārasaṃhitāyām ||


tathaikārakūte ca mālāmantre tathaiva ca |


nityānāṃ haṃsamantrāṇāṃ siddhādīniti |

tathā ca gaṇeśavimarṣiṇyām ||


ekākṣare tathā kūṭe mālāmantre trivījake || 3-106 ||


svapnalabdhe striyādatta siddhādīniti |


viṃśatyarṇādhikāmantrā mālāmantraprakīrtitā |


śrīdevīpurāṇe ||


kārikāyāṃ ca tārāyāṃ kūllūkāyāṃ trikūṭake || 3-107 ||

tathaikākṣarakūṭe ca śivapaṃcākṣare tathā |

ṣaḍakṣare tu tasyaiva viṣṇoraṣṭākṣarepi ca || 3-108 ||

dviṣṇodvādaśavarṇe ca rāmacandrathatārake |

vaiṣṇaveṣu ca bhairavyāṃ sundarī mantravaryake || 3-109 ||

sārasvatī ca vālāyāṃ siddhādīnnaiva śodhayet |

vaiṣṇaveṣu ca mantreṣu kecidīcchanti paṇḍitā || 3-110 ||


vastutastu ||

idaṃ prasaṃsāparam |


anyathā duradṛṣṭava śādṛsāmantrasya prāptyā |


paśrārādhanā * * (?) t ||


atha sā saphalāṃ (?) vakṣya yāmaloktakrameṇa tu || 3-111 ||

yajñātvā mānavā nityaṃ prāpnuyurmāsajaṃ phalam |

16a) vaiśāṣe tu subhā dīkṣā vipulāṃ śriyamādiśet || 3-112 ||

eṣṭha mṛtyupradā caiva ākhāḍhasusaṃpadaḥ |

śrāvaṇe ratnahāni syā bhādre duḥkhapradānatā || 3-113 ||

āśvinī ratnasiddhiḥ syā kārttike jñānasiddhidā |

mārgaśirṣe sukhāvāptiḥ pauṣajñānavināśinī || 3-114 ||

medhā vṛddhikarī māghe phālguṇe sarvavaśyakṛta |

caitre pīḍā pradā caiva malamāsaṃ vivarjayet || 3-115 ||


caitre tu māsaviṣayaṃ gautamītantroktāt |

tathā ca ||

madhumāse bhaveddīkṣā yatyādi vacanān nānyatraniṣedaḥ ||


dīkṣākālaṃ vakṣyāmi sādhakānāṃ sukhāvaham |


devīpurāṇe ||


śaratkāle ca vaiśāṣe dīkṣā śreṣṭaphalapradā || 3-116 ||

phālguṇe mārgaśīrṣe ca jyeṣṭhe dīkṣā tu madhyamāḥ |

āṣāḍhe māghamāse ca kanisā prakīrtitā || 3-117 ||

niṃditā śrāvaṇe pauṣe tathā bhādrapadeṣu ca |

niṃditeṣvapi māseṣu dīkṣoktā grahaṇe śubhā || 3-118 ||

viṣayepyeya na dvaṃdve saṃkrāntyā madanotsava |

dīkṣākāryāṣṭakā kāle pavitre guruparvaṇi || 3-119 ||


guruparvāha ||

vasiṣṭhasaṃhitāyām ||


ṣaṣṭhe bhādrapade māsi iṣe kṛṣṇā caturddaśe |

kārttike navamīśuklā tṛtīyāmārgaśirṣake || 3-120 ||

pauṣasya navamīśuklā māghe caiva caturthikāḥ | 

phālguṇe na * mī śuklā caitrakāmatrayodaśī || 3-121 ||

16b) vaiśākhe cākṣayā caiva jyeṣṭhe daśaharā tathā |

āṣāḍhe paṃcame śuklā śrāvaṇe kṛṣṇapaṃcamī || 3-122 ||

etāni guruparvāṇi tīrthānyāhumaṇīṣiṇaḥ |


śrīmantramuktāvalyām |


śuklapakṣaprakurvīta kṛṣṇe vā daiśikottamaḥ || 3-123 ||

śukle sarvasamṛddhiḥ syāt kṛṣṇe madhyamato bhavet |


rājamārttaṃdeya ||


gurvarkabhārgapauśuddhau dīkṣāridrakāriṇī || 3-124 ||

śubhehniśubhalagne ca dīkṣaiśvaryapradāyinī |


rudrayāmale ||


śuklapakṣe śubhādīkṣā kṛ.qṇe tyāpaṃcamāditāt || 3-125 ||

kṣīṇendau vratadīkṣāṃ ca na kuryāt matravittamaḥ |

śaratkāle yugādyāyāṃ grahaṇe caṃdrasūryayoḥ || 3-126 ||

ārādhane ca durgāyāḥ kuryāddīkṣāṃ prasannadhīḥ |


vrahmavaivartte ||


saṃkrāntyāṃ grahaṇadvandve dīkṣāṃ kuryāt sadā vudhaḥ || 3-127 ||

sūryagrahaṇakālasya samānā nāsti bhūtale |

atra yadyat kṛtaṃ karma anantaphaladaṃ bhavet || 3-128 ||

navāratithimāsādya śodhanaṃ sūryaparvaṇi |

sūryagrahaṇakāle ca ravisaṃkramaṇe tathā || 3-129 ||

atra lagnādi śuddhiṃ ca na kuryāt mantravittamaḥ |


vṛhamatsyasūkte ||


grahaṇe ca tathā tīrthe nāstikālasya niścayaḥ || 3-130 ||

santīrtherkkavidhaugrāse dantadhāvanaparvaṇi |

mantradīkṣā prakurvāṇo māsadīkṣānnaśodhayat || 3-131 ||

jyeṣṭhasya śukladvādaśyāṃ tantuparvābhidhīyate |

17a) caitrasya śukladvādaśyāṃ damanaṃ parikīrttitam || 3-132 ||

śaśidinakarayograhaṇe janmaṇiśisyasya makarasaṃkrāntyām |

karuṇā samaya ca guro naikṣī tarkṣādidīkṣāyām || 3-133 ||

somagrahe viṣṇumantre sūryaśāktaṃ na cācaret |

athavānyaprakāreṇa dīkṣāgrahaṇamuttamam || 3-134 ||

śiṣyānā huyaguruṇā kṛpayā dīyate yadā |

tadā lagnādikaṃ kiṃcin na vicāryaṃ kathaṃ ca na || 3-135 ||

sarve vārāgrahāḥ sarve nakṣatrāṇi ca rāśayaḥ |

yasmin na hani santuṣṭo guruḥ sarveśubhāvahāḥ || 3-136 ||

santuṣṭe hi gurau yatra santuṣṭaḥ sarvadevatāḥ |

yadaivedyā tadā dīkṣā gurorājñānurūpataḥ || 3-137 ||

nakāraniyamastatra na deśaniyamastathā |

gurusantoṣaye bhaktyā svayameva tathā bhavet || 3-138 ||


atha tithiphalam 


atha tithiphalaṃ vakṣya yāmaloktakrameṇa tu |

pratipatsaṃskṛtā dīkṣā jñānanāśakarīmatā || 3-139 ||

dvitīyāyāṃ bhavajñānaṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ śuci bhavet |

caturthyāṃ dhananāśaḥ syā paṃcamyāṃ vuddhivarddhanam || 3-140 ||

ṣaṣṭyāṃ jñānakṣayaṃ saukhyaṃ labhate saptamī dine |

aṣṭamyāṃ buddhināśaḥ syān navamyāṃ maraṇaṃ bhavet || 3-141 ||

daśamyāṃ rājasaukhyaṃ ca ekādaśyāṃ śuci bhavet |

17b) dvādaśyāṃ sarvasiddhiḥ syā trayodaśyāṃ daridratā || 3-142 ||

tiryagyonicaturddaśyāṃ krimimāsāvasānake |

pakṣā te dharmasiddhiḥ syā datvādhyāyaṃ vivarjayet || 3-143 ||

saṃdhyā garjitanirghoṣa bhūkaṃpot kānipātine |

trirātraṃ varjjayen mantrī śrutyuktānapivarjayet || 3-144 ||


tathā ca vaśiṣṭhasaṃhitāyām ||


dvādaśyāṃ mapi karttavyaṃ trayodaśyāṃ mathāpi vā |

trayodaśividhānaṃ ya tad viṣṇuviṣayaṃ tathā || 3-145 ||


dvādaśyāmapi rāmārccanacaṃdrikā dhṛtavacanāt |

trayodaśī ca deśamī praśastā sarvakāmadeti sanatkumārasaṃhitā 
dhṛtavacanādhṛtitacintyam ||


vālānāṃ ca phalaṃ vakṣya sarvatantrānusārata |

yavau gurau tathā some karttavyaṃ budhaśukrayo || 3-46 ||

ravivāre bhaved vittaṃ somaśānti bhaven tila |

āyuraṃgārakohanti tatra dīkṣāṃ vivarjayet || 3-147 ||

budhai saundraryamāpnoti yaśohāniḥ śanaścare |

nakṣatrāṇāṃ phalaṃ vakṣya da?kṣoktānāṃ samāsataḥ || 3-148 ||

kṛtvā dīkṣāṃ tu tatraiva sadasatphalamāpnuyāt |


tathā ca jñānamālāyām ||

aśvinī rohiṇī svāti viśākhā hastabheṣu ca jyeṣṭhāttaratrayekṣevaṃ kuryān 
mantrabhiṣecana |


rohiṇī śravaṇād rāva dhaniṣṭhā cottarātrayam || 3-149 ||

puṣyā śatabhiṣaka caiva dīkṣādiṣu prakīrttitā |


18a) tathā ca yāmale ||


aśvinyā sukhamāpnoti bharaṇyāṃ maraṇadhruvam || 3-150 ||

kṛrttikāyāṃ bhavedduḥkhī rohinyāṃ vāhyati bhavet |

mṛgaśirṣe sukhā vāpti rādrāyāṃ vandhunāśanam || 3-151 ||

punarvaśaudhanāḥ dhyasyāt pusyaśatruvināśanam |

aśleṣāyāṃ bhaven mṛtyu maghā yā duḥkhamocanam || 3-152 ||

saundaryaṃ pūrvaphalguṇyāṃ prāpnoti ca na śaṃsayaḥ |

jñānaṃ cottaraphālguṇyāṃ prāpnoti ca na śaṃsayaḥ || 3-153 ||

jñānaṃ cottaraphālguṇyāṃ haste caiva valī bhavet |

citrāyāṃ jñānasiddhiḥ syāt snātauśatruvināśinī || 3-154 ||

viśākhāyāṃ bhavet saukhyaṃ anurādhā va vuddhidā |

jyeṣṭhāyāṃ sutahāniḥ syāt mūlāyāṃ kīrtivandhanam || 3-155 ||

pūrvakhāḍhottarākhāḍhe bhavatāṃ kīrttidāyake |

śravaṇe bhavedduḥkhī dhanīṣṭhāyāṃ daridratā || 3-156 ||

vuddhiḥ śatabhikhāyāṃ ca pūrvabhadrā sukhaḥ pradāḥ |

saukhyaṃ cottarabhadrāyāṃ revatyāṃ kīrttivarddhanam || 3-157 ||

dīkṣāyogaṃ pravakṣyāmi rudrayāmaladeśinam |

śuklapakṣe śubhaḥ kṛṣṇo daśamyantaḥ śubhāvahaḥ || 3-158 ||

vyatipāto vaidhṛtiścaḥ dīkṣāyogasukhāvahaḥ |

śakutyādi ca śubhadaṃ viṣṭiṃ yatnena varjayet || 3-159 ||


vyatipātalakṣaṇamāha ||


śravaṇāśvidhaniṣṭhādrā nāgadaivatamastake |

yadyamāravicāreṇa vyatipāta sa ucyate || 3-160 ||

18b) nāgadaivatamaśleṣā mastake prathamācare |

kanyāyāṃ yadi vā mātā mekhabhūmijabhānujau || 3-161 ||

tāmaśī śanivāreṇa saptamī kuṃbhabhāskare |

sūryagrahaṇataistulyo vyatipātoghanāśana || 3-162 ||

dīkṣālagnaṃ pravakṣyāmi rudrayāmaladeśitam |

vṛṣe siṃhe ca kanyāyāṃ dhanumīnākṣalagnake || 3-163 ||

caṃdratārānukūle ca dadyā dīkṣāṃ sadā guruḥ |

sthilaragne viṣṇumantre śivamantre calaṃ śubham || 3-164 ||

visku?bhāvagataṃ lagnaṃ śaktimantreṣu śasyate |

niṣiddhamāseṣu tu tad viśeṣo munibhedataḥ || 3-165 ||

ṣaṣṭharāṃ bhādrapade iṣe tathā kṛṣṇacaturdaśī |

kārttike navamīśuklā mārge śuklātṛtīyakā || 3-166 ||

pauṣe ca navamī śuklā māghe śuklacaturthikā |


trayodaśī iti pāṭha ||


vaiśākhe cākṣayā caiva jyaṣṭhe daśaharā tithi || 3-167 ||

āṣāḍhe paṃcamīśuklā śrāvaṇe śuklapaṃcamī |

etāni devaparvāṇi tīrthakoṭiphalaṃ bhavet || 3-168 ||


matātaraṃ ca |


caitre trayodaśī śuklā vaiśākhaikādaśīsitā |

jyeṣṭhe caturdaśī kṛṣṇā ākhāḍhe nāgapañcamī || 3-169 ||

śrāvaṇaikādaśī bhādre rohiṇī sahitāstamī |

āśvine ca mahāpuṇyā mahāṣṭamyapyabhīṣṭadā || 3-170 ||

kārttike navamīśuklā mārgaśīrṣe tathā sitā |

ṣaṣṭhīcaturdaśīpauṣe māghepyakādaśī sitā || 3-171 ||

19a) phālguṇe ca śitā ṣaṣṭhi ceti kālapinirṇayaḥ |


yoginītantre ||


ayaṇe grahaṇe caiva viṣave caṃdrasūryayoḥ || 3-172 ||

ravisaṃkrānti divase yugādyāyasureśvari |

manvantarāsu sarvāsu mahāpūjādite tathā || 3-173 ||

caturthī paṃcamī caiva caturdaśyāṣṭamī tathā |


tithayaḥ śubhadāḥ proktā ityādivacanācca caturdaśyaṣṭamīti śaktiviṣayam |


caturthī tithigaṇeśaviṣayaṃ tat tat kalpoktatvāt || 3-174 ||

ninditeṣvapi kāleṣu dīkṣoktāgrahaṇe śubhā |

sūryagrahaṇakālesya samānonāsti bhūtale || 3-175 ||

tatra yadya kṛtaṃ sarvamanantaphaladaṃ bhavet |

ravisaṃkramaṇe caiva sūryasya grahaṇe tathā || 3-176 ||

tatra lagnādikaṃ kiṃcit tavicārya kathaṃ ca na |


evaṃ candragrahaṇepi tathā ||


amāvāsyā somavāle bhaumavāle caturdaśī || 3-177 ||

saptamī ravivāre ca sūrye parvaśataiḥ samā |


vārāhītantre ||


śukrostoyadivā vṛddho gurvādibhyo bhaved yadi || 3-178 ||

meṣavṛṣṭikasiṃheṣu tadā doṣo na vidyate |

dikṣāsthānaṃ pravakṣyāmi nānātantrānusārata || 3-179 ||

gośālāyāṃ gurogehe devāgāre ca kānane |

ṣuṇyatīrthe tathādyāne nadītīre ca svāśrame || 3-180 ||

dhātrī viśvasamīpe tu parvatāgre guhāśu ca |

gaṃgāyāṃ tat kṛtaṃ sarvaṃ koṭi (ko) koṭi guṇaṃ bhavet || 3-181 ||

19b) atha bhakṣyaṃ pravakṣyāmi mantriṇāṃ sarvasiddhidam |


tathā coktaṃ śārdātilake ||


bhakṣyaṃ haviṣyaṃ śākādi vihitaṃ ca phalaṃ payaḥ || 3-182 ||

mūlasaktu yavotpanno bhakṣyāṇyetānimantriṇām |


tathā coktaṃ sārasaṃgrahe ||


dīkṣāṃ kṛtvā yadā mantrī hyupavāsaṃ samācaret || 3-183 ||

tasya deva sadāruṣṭaḥ śāpaṃ patati mūrddhani |

tadā gṛhītā nopoṣya dupavāsaḥ śriyaṃ haret || 3-184 ||

tasyādannaṃ samagnīyād dhaviṣyānnena śā kari |


ityāgamakalpalatāyāṃ tṛtīyaḥ pallavaḥ || || 3 || ||




tasmāt sadgurumāśritya hyāgamoktena vatmanā |

dīkṣāṃ kuryu prayatnena sādhakā siddhiḥ kāṃkṣiṇaḥ || 4-1 ||

prasaṃgādāgamasya prasaṃsā liṣyate |

nityapūjāṃ vinivartya daṃḍavat puṇamedgurum || 4-2 ||

tataśca praṇamed bhānuṃ mātaraṃ pitaraṃ tathā |

tatobhilaṣitāṃ vidyā mabhyase gurusannidhau || 4-3 ||

yadā yaṃ sarvaśāstrārtha jñāteti ca jagurvudhāḥ |

tadā lokajayārthaṃ ca jñānaśāstraṃ samabhyaset || 4-4 ||

na jñānena vinā mukti jñānabhaktivinā na hi |

bhaktistu bhagavadbhakta saṃgena parijāyate || 4-5 ||

vahunijñānaśāstrāṇi santī vakṣyati maṇḍale |

yasya jñāni manojñāni tāni tāni samabhyaset || 4-6 ||

ekena muninā proktāḥ prakārā vahavastathā |

kiṃ tasya kimatathye vā nāsatyavikarā narāḥ || 4-7 ||

20a) te nāgamaṃ jñānasāstramabhyaset prayataḥ sudhīḥ |

āgame sarvaśāstrāṇi hyāgame sarvadevatāḥ || 4-8 ||

āgame sakalaṃ puṇyaṃ āgame kiṃ na labhyate |

agame sakalaṃ jñānaṃ vidyāḥ sarvā pratiṣṭhitāḥ || 4-9 ||

āgame yanna labhyata brahmanne tannalabhyate |

āgataṃ śivavaktrā tu gataṃ tu girijāmukham || 4-10 ||

malatrayavināśāya tasmāddāgama ucyate |

śarīrotpatti jīvaṃ ca māyāśālīlakarmaṇām || 4-11 ||

jñānaṃ tenāvṛtaṃ viddhirbhavavaṃdhapravarttakam |

śarīrotpādako viṣṇustadbhaktyāstannikṛntati || 4-12 ||

jñānārthaṃ bhaktiyogasya yatnenāgamamabhyaset |

tasmādīkṣāṃ prakurvītahyāgamoktena vatmanā || 4-13 ||

atha dīkṣā vidhiṃ vakṣya mantriṇāṃhitakāmyayā |

vinā tayātalabhyata sarvamantraphalaṃ yataḥ || 4-14 ||

caturvidhā samudiṣṭā kriyāvatyādi bhedataḥ |

kriyāvatī varṇamayī kalātmāvedamapyapi || 4-15 ||

tatra kriyāvatī dīkṣā vahutvānna nigadyate |

punarvedamayī yoga gamyāśiṣyapravodhinī || 4-16 ||

atha kalāvatī dīkṣā vidhānaṃ cābhidhīyate |


tathā ca śāradātilake ||


daiśikovidhivansnātvā kṛtvā pūrvāhnikīḥ kriyā || 4-17 ||

yā yā dalaṃ kṛto maunī yāgārthe yāgamaṃḍapam |

tato bhūmitale śuddhe tuṣāṅkāravivarjite || 4-18 ||

puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu maṃḍapaṃ racayecchubham |

20b) ṣaṃcabhiḥ saptabhihaste navabhirvāmitāntaram || 4-19 ||

ṣoḍaśaḥ staṃbhayaṃ yuktaṃ catvārasteṣu madhyagāḥ |

asta hasta samucchrāyāḥ saṃsthāpya dvādasābhitaḥ || 4-20 ||

paṃcahastapramāṇāste nicchidrā ṛjavaḥ śubhāḥ |

tat paṃcamāṇaṃ nisvanen madinyāṃ tantravittamaḥ || 4-21 ||


tat pacamāṇaṃ teṣāṃ staṃbhānāṃ paṃcamāśaṃ mityarthaḥ |


nārakeradalairvaṃśeḥ sādhaye tat samaṃ tataḥ |

dvareṣu toraṇānisyuḥ kramāt kṣīramahīruhān || 4-22 ||

taṃbhocchrāyaḥ smṛtasteṣāṃ saptahastaiḥ pṛthak pṛthak |


teṣāṃ toraṇākam ||


daśāṅgulapramāṇena tatparīṇāha īritaḥ |

tiryakaphalakamānaṃ syāt staṃbhānāmardhamātrataḥ || 4-23 ||


tiryakaphalakamānaṃ syād iti staṃbhayo parikāṣṭhasyati viśeṣaḥ ||

taṃbhānāmardhamāna iti sārddhaṃ trayamiti śeṣaḥ ||


śūlānvikalpayen madhyatoraṇe hastamānataḥ |

dikṣudhvajanivadhnīyā lokapālasamaprabhān || 4-24 ||


tathā coktaṃ śāradāyām ||


indramagniṃ yamaṃ lakṣo varuṇaṃ pavanaṃ vidhum |

īśānaṃ yannagādhīśaṃ ata ūrdhvaṃ pitāmaham || 4-25 ||

pīto rakto sito dhūmraḥ śukle dhūmraśitā vubhau |

gauroruṇaḥ kramādeta varṇataḥ parikīrttitāḥ || 4-26 ||


kapiśakapilanīlaśyāmalaśvetadhūmrāmalaśucisitavaktrā varṇato 
vāsavādyāḥ |

21a) kramadīpikāyām ||


citā na darbhamālādyairalaṃkurvītamaṇḍapam |

tat tribhāgamiti kṣattre rattrimātrasamucchritām || 4-27 ||

caturaḥśrāṃ tato vedimaṃḍalāya prakalpayet |


taduktaṃ gautamīyām ||


vediṃ saṃcayate mantri catuśrī yathāvidhi || 4-28 ||

śuklamṛtsnā ca yā bhūmi brāhmī sā parikīrttitā |

kṣatriyā raktamṛtsnā ca haridvaiśyā prakīrttitā || 4-29 ||

kṛṣṇā bhūmirbhavacchidrā caturddhā parikīrttitā |

brāhmī sarvārthasiddhiḥ syāt kṣatriyā rājyadā matā || 4-30 ||

dhanadhānyakarī vaiśyā śūdrā tu ninditā mune |

prāgeva dīkṣā divasān maṇḍapaṃ kārayet sudhīḥ || 4-31 ||

caturviṃśat paṃgusāḍhyaṃ hastaṃ tantravidoviduḥ |

kartudakṣiṇahastasya madhyamāṅguliparvarṇaḥ || 4-32 ||

madhyasya dīrghamānena mātāṅgulamudīritam |

yavānāntaṃdulailekaṃ aṃgulaṃ cāṣṭabhi bhavet || 4-33 ||

adīrghayojitairhastaścaturviṃśāṅgulaibhavet |

astabhistai yavai jyaṣṭhaṃ madhyamaṃ saptabhiryavaiḥ || 4-34 ||

kaniṣṭhaṃ ṣaḍbhiruddiṣṭaṃ hastaṃ ca munisattama |


sarvatobhadravacanaprakāramāha ||


caturaśre catuṣkoṣṭhe karṇasūtrasamanvita |

catuṣvapi ca koṣṭheṣu koṇasūtracatuṣṭayam || 4-35 ||

madhya madhya yathā matsyā bhave yuḥ patayet tathā |

pūrvāparāyate dve dve mantrīyāmottarāyate || 4-36 ||

21b) pātayet teṣu matsyaṣu samasūtracatuṣṭayam |

pūrvavat koṇakoṣṭheṣu karmasūtrāṇi pātayet || 4-37 ||

tadvabhūteṣu matyeṣu dadyātsūtracatuṣṭayam |

tataḥ koṣṭheṣu matsyeṣu teṣu sūtrāṇi pātayet || 4-38 ||

yāvaghaṭadvayaṃ mantrī ṣaṭpaṃcāśatpadānyapi |

tāvartte naiva vidhinā tatra satrāṇi pātayet || 4-39 ||

ṣaṭtriṃśatāpadairmadhya likhet padmaṃ sulakṣaṇam |

vahiḥ paṃktyā bhavet pīṭhaṃ paktiyugmena vīthikāḥ || 4-40 ||

dvāraśobhopaśobhā srā naśiṣṭhā parikalpayet |

śāstroktavidhinā mantrī tataḥ padmaṃ samālikhet || 4-41 ||

padmakṣatrasya santyajya dvādaśāṃśaṃ vahiḥ sudhīḥ |

tanmadhyaṃ vibhajedvṛttaistribhiḥ samavibhāgataḥ || 4-42 ||

ādyaṃ syāt kaṇikāṣṭhānaṃ kesarāṇāṃ dvitīyakam |

tṛtīyaṃ tatra yantrāṇā muktāśena dalāgrakam || 4-43 ||

vāhyavṛttāntalālasya mānaṃyadvidhinā sudhīḥ |

vidhāyakeśarāgreṣu paritordhvaniśākarāna || 4-44 ||

likhitvā saṃdhisaṃsthāne tatra sūtrāṇi pātaye |

dalāgrāṇāṃ ca yanmānaṃ tanmānaṃ vṛttamālikhet || 4-45 ||

tadantalālaṃ tanmadhya sūtrasyā bhayataḥ sudhīḥ |

ālikhed vāhyahastena dalāgrāṇi samantataḥ || 4-46 ||

dalamūleṣu yagaṇaḥ keśalāli prakalpayet |

etat sādhāraṇaṃ proktaṃ paṃkajaṃ tatra vedibhiḥ || 4-47 || 

padātitrīṇipādārtha pīṭhikoṇeṣu mārjayet |

22a) avaśiṣṭhaiḥ padaividvān gātrāṇi parikalpayet || 4-48 ||

yadā nivīthisaṃskāre mājarotpaktibhedataḥ |

dikṣudvārāṇi recayed dvicatuṣkoṣṭhakaistataḥ || 4-49 ||

padaistribhirathaikena śobhāḥ syu dvārapārśvayoḥ |

upaśobhāḥ syurakena tribhiḥ koṣṭhairaṇantaraṃḥ || 4-50 ||

avaśiṣṭhaiḥ padaiḥ ṣadbhiḥ koṇāṃ nāsyāccatuṣṭayam | 

raṃjayet pacabhirvarṇairṣaḍatvaṃ tanmanoharam || 4-51 ||

pītaṃ haridrācūrṇaḥ syāt sitaṃ taṃ dūrasaṃbhavam |

kusuṃbhacūrṇamaruṇaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dagdhayulokajam || 4-52 ||

vilvādipatrakaṃ śyāmaṃ mityuktaṃ varṇapaṃkajam |

aṃguloghedavistārāḥ semā lekhāḥ sitāḥ śubhāḥ || 4-53 ||

karṇikā pītavarṇena keśarāṇyaruṇe na ca |

śubhravarṇe ca patrāṇi tat saṃdhīn śāmalena ca || 4-54 ||

ujasāraṃ jayen mantrī javāpītairakarṇikā |

kesarāḥ raktapītāḥ syuraruṇānidalānyapi || 4-55 ||

saṃdhayeḥ kṛṣṇavarṇā syuḥ pītenāpyasite na vā |

raṃjayet pīṭhagātrāṇi pādāsyuraruṇaḥ prabhāḥ || 4-56 ||

tātrāṇi tasya śubhrāṇi vīṭhīṣu ca taśrīsvepi |

ālikhet kalpalatikādalapuṣpasamanvitāḥ || 4-57 ||

varṇainānāvidhaiścitrāḥ sarvadṛṣṭimanoharāḥ |

tārāṇiśvetavarṇāni śobhāraktāḥ samīritāḥ || 4-58 ||

22b) upaśobhāḥ pītavarṇāḥ keśānyasitabhāni ca |

tisrorekhā vahiḥ kāryāḥ sitaraktāḥ sitāḥ kramāt || 4-59 ||

maṃḍalaṃ sarvatobhadrāmetat sādhāraṇaṃ smṛtam |

śiṣyopi tatra vā gatya śuciḥ pūrvamupoṣitaḥ || 4-60 ||

saṃkalpaṃ ca tataḥ kuryāt svastivācanapūrvakam |

paścācchiṣyo guruṃ tatra vṛṇuyābhaktisaṃyutaṃḥ || 4-61 ||

śaktyāguruṃ bhūṣayad vai vastrālaṃkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ |

gobhūhiraṇyavastrādyaiḥ kuṃḍalādibhirujjvalaiḥ || 4-62 ||

anyairābharaṇaiścaiva tathā vittamavaṃcayet |

guruṃ saṃpūjayed bhaktyā śiṣyosau śubha cesa || 4-63 ||

daṃbhāhaṃkāramānaṃ ca guroragre vivarjayet |

kṛtāñjalipluto bhūtvā prīnayedgurumagrataḥ || 4-64 ||


athāto bhagavan paramātmasvarūpin paramakāruṇika guro sakalapuruṣāya 
sādhanamantradīkṣābhiṣekapūrvakaṃ sāṃprasādaya ||


ityuktvāthaciraṃ tiṣṭhed vinayī dakṣiṇe guroḥ |

sahasraṃ prajapet tatra gāyatrīmājñayā guroḥ || 4-65 ||

vakṣyamāṇakrame nātra saṃkuryān mantramuttamam |


guroriti śeṣa ||


saṃskṛtya daśadhāmantra tataḥ pūjāṃ samārabhe || 4-66 ||

pādau prakṣālayed gatvā tato maṃḍapasannidhīm |

ācamya vidhinā samyak sāmānyārghaṃ vidhāya ca || 4-67 ||

astreṇa pātraṃ saṃprokṣya hṛnmantreṇaprapūrayet |

23a) tīrthamāvāhyagaṃdhādīn nikṣipye praṇavena tu || 4-68 ||

dhenumudrāṃ darśayecca sāmānyārghaṃ udāhṛtaḥ |

dvāramantrāṃ vubhiḥ prokṣya dvārapūjāṃ samācaret || 4-69 ||

urddhvāduvanakevighnaṃ malālakṣmīṃ salasvatīm |

tato dakṣiṇaśākhāyāṃ vighnaṃ kṣatraiśamanyataḥ || 4-70 ||

tato pārśvayuge gaṃgā yumune puṣpavāribhiḥ |

dehatyāmarccayedastraṃ pratidvāramiti kramāt || 4-71 ||

kiṃcit pṛśatvāmaśākhāṃ dehalīlaṃghayedgurum |

aṃgaṃ saṃkocayannantaḥ praviśedakṣiṇād triṇā || 4-72 ||

anantaraṃ daiśikendrā divyadṛṣṭyāvalokanāt |

divyānutsārayed vighnād aṣṭābhiścāntarikṣagān || 4-73 ||

pārirghātaistribhibhaumā nitivighnān nivārayet |

sirddhārthākṣatasaṃyuktaṃ nārācāstradhiyā vudhaḥ || 4-74 ||


apasarpaṃ tu yad bhūtā ye bhūtā vighnakartārastenaśyantu śivājñayāḥ ||


ityante straṃ samurccārya maṃḍapāntaḥ parikṣipet |

naiṛtyāṃ diśivāstvīśaṃ brahmāṇaṃ ca samarcayet || 4-75 ||

paṃcagarvyārghato yābhyāṃ prokṣayed yāgamaṃḍapam |

vīkṣaṇaṃ mulamantreṇa śareṇaprokṣaṇaṃ matam || 4-76 ||

te naiva tāḍanaṃ daṃbhai vamaṇāt yukṣaṇaṃ matam |

caṃḍanāgurukapurai dhupairyadantaraṃ sudhīḥ || 4-77 ||

vikirānvikirā tatra saptajaptvā śarānunā |

rājā caṃdanasiddhārtha bhasmadurvākuśākṣataḥ || 4-78 ||

23b) vikirā iti saṃdiṣṭāḥ sarvaṃ vighnaughanāśana (ṃ)ḥ |

vikīryavikirānyaṃ ca mārjanaṃ kārayet sudhīḥ || 4-79 ||

atra japtena darbhānāṃ muṣṭimāṇārjayercatām |

īśasya diśivardhanyā āsanāya prakalpayet || 4-80 ||


vadhirnī vikirādiṣṭā tṛdevatā ||

tasya āsanāya varddhinīmīśānakonakoṇe vistaro parivadanā |

iti yasyā prasiddhi ||


puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu brahmaṇān paritoṣya ca |

ukteṣu maṃḍaleṣvekaṃ vedikāyāṃ samālikhet || 4-81 ||

merupṛṣṭha-ṛṣiḥ chandaḥ pṛthivyāḥ sutalaṃ matam |

devatā kūrma ākhyātaḥ saṃsmare dṛṣṭipūrvakam || 4-82 ||

viniyogo hi mantrasya āsane parikīrtitāḥ |

vṛści tvayā dhṛtā loke devi tvaṃ viṣṇunā dhṛtā || 4-83 ||

tvaṃ ca dhāraya māṃ nityaṃ pavitraṃ kuru cāsanam |

iti mantraṃ japanpīṭhe prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ || 4-84 ||

svastikādikrameṇaiva ṛjukāyo viśedbudhaḥ |

sthāpayed dakṣiṇe bhāge pūjādravyāṇidaiśikaḥ || 4-85 ||

suvāsitāṃ vusaṃpūrṇaṃ savyakuṃbhaṃ suśobhitam |

prakṣālanāya karayeḥ pṛṣṭhe pātraṃ nidhāpayet || 4-86 ||

ghṛtaprajvālitā dvīpān sthāpayet paritaḥ śubhān |

dagdhanaṃ cāmaraṃ chatraṃ tātta vṛttaṃ manoharam || 4-87 ||

24a) kṛtāñjliṃ pluto bhūtvā vāmadakṣiṇapārśvayoḥ |

natvā guruṃ gaṇeśānaṃ bhūtaśuddhiṃ samācaret || 4-88 ||


yāmale ||


tathā puṭāṃjali bhūtvā vāme gurutrayaṃ jayet |

dakṣiṇe ca gaṇeśānaṃ bhūtaśuddhiṃ samācaret || 4-89 ||


yāmale ||


tathā puṭāṃjali bhūtvā vāme gurutrayaṃ jayet |

dakṣiṇe ca gaṇeśānaṃ murddhnidevaṃ vibhāvayet || 4-90 ||

karaśuddhiṃ samāpātya kuryāntālatrayaṃ tataḥ |

urddhvārdhvamantramantreṇa digdhaṃdhamapi deśikam || 4-91 ||

tena saṃjanitaṃ tejo rakṣāṃ kuryā samantataḥ |

bhūtaśuddhiṃ tataḥ kuryād daiśikaḥ sthiramānasaḥ || 4-92 ||


tathā coktaṃ sāradāyām ||


śarīrakārabhūtānāṃ bhūtānāṃ yad viśodhanam |

advayad brahmasaṃyogā bhūtaśuddhiriyaṃ mata || 4-93 ||

suṣumanā vasmaṇātmānaṃ paramātmaniyojayet |

yogayuktena vidhinā cinmantreṇasamāhitaḥ || 4-94 ||


cinmantreṇa haṃsa iti mantreṇa ||


kāraṇe sarvabhūtānāṃ tattvānyeṣi vicintayet |

vījabhāvena līnāni vyutkramātyaramātmani || 4-95 ||

tataḥ saṃśoṣayed dehe vāyuvījena vāyunā |

vahnibījena te ṇaiva saṃdahet sakalaṃ tanum || 4-96 ||

viśleṣayaṃ tadā doṣā na mṛte nāmṛtāṃbhasā |

āpādyaplāvayad dehaṃ āpādatalamastakam || 4-97 ||


vāyuvījena yamiti vījena vahnivījaṃ amṛtamamṛtavījaṃ va mityarthaḥ || 


24b) ātmalīnāni tattvāni svasthānaṃ prāpayet tataḥ |

ātmānaṃ hṛdayāṃ bhoja mānayet paramātmanaḥ || 4-98 ||

manunā haṃsadevasya kuryān nyāsādikāṃ tataḥ |


haṃsadeva haṃsa iti svarūpam ||


ṛṣicchaṃdo devatāni nyasen mantrasya mantravit || 4-99 ||

ātmano mūddhni vadane hṛdaye ca tathā kramāt |


tathā vṛhamatsyasūkte ||


ṛṣinyāso mūddhnideśe chaṃdastu mukhapaṃkaje || 4-100 || 

devatā hṛdaye caiva vījaṃ tu guhyadeśake |

śaktiṃ ca pādayoścaiva sarvāṅge kilakaṃ nyaset || 4-101 ||


ṛṣistu ||


maheśvaramukhā jñātvā yaḥ sākṣāt tapasāmanum |

saṃsādhayati śuddhātmā satasya ṛṣirīritaḥ || 4-102 ||

gurutvān mastake vāsya nyāsasya parikīrtitaḥ |

sarveṣāṃ mantratatvānāṃ chādanācchada ucyate || 4-103 ||

akṣaratvāt paratvā ca mukhe chaṃdaḥ samīritaḥ |

ṛṣiḥ chaṃdo parijñānān namantraḥ phalabhāgbhavet || 4-104 ||

daurvartyaṃ yāti mantrāṇāṃ vinijogamajānatām |

iti ṛṣyādi nyāsāvaśyakatvaṃ siddhamiti bhāva || 4-105 ||


ṛṣyādināha ||

mantracūḍāmaṇau ||


upāsakān mahādevi śṛṇu vaiḥ kamanāsvayam |

manuścaṃdraḥ kuveraśca manmathastadanantaram || 4-106 ||

lopāmudrā manurnadiḥ śakraḥ skaṃdhaśivastathā |

krodhabhaṭṭālakaścaiva paṃcamī parikīrtitā || 4-107 ||

dūrvāsā vyāsa pūjyo ca vaśiṣṭhaśca parāśaraḥ |

25a) aurvo vahniryamaścaiva nai-ṛto varuṇastathā || 4-108 ||

vāyuviṣṇuḥ svayaṃbhuśca bhairavā gaṇapastathā |

aniruddho bharadvājo dakṣiṇāmūrtireva ca || 4-109 ||

vaṭuko gaṇapaścaiva lakṣmī gaṃgā sarasvatī |

dhātrī śivaḥ prasastaśca (?) unmattakulabhairava || 4-110 ||

dhātrī dharitrī kṣatrapālo hanumāśca dakṣo garuḍa eva ca |

śukadevaśca prahlādo rāmo rāvaṇameva ca || 4-111 ||

kaśyapo kaussakuṃbhau ca jamadagni bhṛgustathā |

vṛhaspati yaduśreṣṭho dattātreyo yudhiṣṭhiraḥ || 4-112 ||

ajuno bhīmasenaśca droṇācāryā jayadratha |

duryodhanastathā kuntī sīto ca rukmiṇī tathā || 4-113 ||

satyabhāmā dropadī ca urvaśī ca tilottamā |

puṣpadanto mahābuddho vāṇakālaśca maṃḍaraḥ || 4-114 ||

kailāsa ikṣusiṃdhuśca udadhī himavāṃstathā |

yādavaśca mahāvīraḥ kalpavṛkṣaḥ sasādhakaḥ || 4-115 ||

mahāvidyā prasādena svasvakāma samanvitaḥ |


mahāvidyā māha ||

tathā ca muṇḍamālātantre ||


kālī tārā mahāvidyā ṣoḍaśī bhuvaneśvarī || 4-116 ||

bhairavī cchinnamastā ca vidyā dhūmāvatīstathā |

vagalā siddhavidyā ca mātaṃgī kamalātmikā || 4-117 ||

etā daśamahāvidyā mahādevi prakīrtitāḥ |

vidhāyamūlamantreṇa prāṇāyāmaṃ yathā vidhi || 4-118 ||


prāṇāyāmasya lakṣaṇamāha ||

tripurāsārasamucaya ||


25b) prāṇo vāyuśarīrastha āyāmastasya nigrahaḥ | 

prāṇāyāma iti prokto dvividhaḥ kathyate hi saḥ || 4-119 ||


sa garbho nivigarbhaśca ||


sagarbho mantrajāpe nigarbhā mātrayā bhavet |

agastye saṃhitāyāṃ tat kathitaṃ kuṃbhajena ca || 4-120 ||


mātrā ca ||


nalinī pattrasaṃhatyāṃ śūkṣmasūvyagrabhedhane |

dale dale ca yeḥ kālaḥ sakālo kālavācakaḥ || 4-121 ||

lavaistutiśca triṃśadbhiḥ kalāntā va tuṭi viduḥ |

kāṣṭha tāvat kalā jñeyāstāvat kāṣṭhāni meṣakaḥ || 4-122 ||

svāṅgulisphoṭatulyaśca mātrāstā daśabhiḥ smṛtā |

kālena yāvatāsvīyo hastaṃ (ḥ) svaṃjānumaṇḍalam || 4-123 ||

paryatimātrā mātasyāḥ svakīyasvāsatulyayā |


athavā majānumīti mātrā ca ||


vāmajānunihastasya bhramaṇaṃ yāvatā bhavet || 4-124 ||

kālena mātrā sā jñeyā | munibhirṣvedapāragai |


yāmale ||


prāṇāyāṃ tataḥ kuryād vāgbhavena samāhitaḥ || 4-125 ||

dakṣāṅguṣṭhena dakṣaṃ ca ghoṇaṃ saṃpīḍyamantravit |

idayā purayed vāyuṃ mātrā ṣoḍaśabhiḥ kramāt || 4-126 ||

aṃguṣṭhā nāmikābhyāṃ ca dhṛtvā nāsādvayaṃ tataḥ |

tataḥ stum kumbhayed vāyuṃ pūrakānāṃ caturguṇaiḥ || 4-127 ||

anāmayā tathā vāma nāmāṃ dhṛtvā tu dakṣiṇe |

dvātriṃśadbhistu mātrābhirvāyośca recanaṃ caret || 4-128 ||

26a) svacittaṃ mūlacakre tu nidhāyāsūtsamabhyaset |

tato recanamārgena prāṇavāyu prapūrayet || 4-129 ||

punaḥ ṣoḍaśamātrābhiḥ kuṃbhakaṃ ca samārabhet |

tato recanamārgena prāṇavāyuṃ prapūrayet || 4-130 ||

punaḥ ṣoḍaśamātrābhiḥ kuṃbhakaṃ ca samācaret |

catuḥṣaṣṭhitamaimantrī nāsāputau vidhṛtya ca || 4-131 ||

punaścare ca yed vāyuṃ dvātriṃśan mātrayā vudhaḥ |

vāmeṇaivapi ved vāyuṃ mātrābhiḥ ṣoḍaśaḥ kramāt || 4-132 ||

nāsāputau nipīḍyātha pūrakasya caturguṇaiḥ |

dakṣiṇe recayed vāyuṃ dvātriṃśanmātrayā vudhaḥ || 4-133 ||

vāmeṇapāṇiṃ dakṣiṇe recanamiti tat prathamaprāṇāyāmam |

yāmāṃbhi prāṇāyāmāvṛttyā tu tatada saṃbhavati || 4-134 ||

prāṇāyāmavidhiṃ samyak kathitaṃ yatnato mayā |

adhamomadhyamāścaiva prāṇāyāmastathottamaḥ || 4-135 || 

adhamojanaye dharmaṃ kaṃpo bhavati madhyame |

uttame khecarīṃ siddhiṃ prāpnuyān nātra saṃśayaḥ || 4-136 ||

prāṇāyāmaiḥ kṛtaiḥ śaśvanityaṃ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ sudhīḥ |

dainaṃ dinaṃ ca yat pāpaṃ tat sarvaṃ naśyati dhruvam || 4-137 ||

kaniṣṭhānāmikāṃguṣṭhairyan nāsāpuṭadhāraṇam |

prāṇāyāmaḥ savijñeyastarjanīmadhyame vinā || 4-138 ||

prāṇāyāmeṇa pāpāṇi nasvantyaṃ yana saṃśayaḥ |

prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryāt pratyahaṃ mūlamantrataḥ || 4-139 ||

japātau ca japāte ca sādhakaḥ siddhikāṃkṣayā |

26b) vidanyā mātṛkā nyāsaṃ mantraṃ nyāsādanantaram || 4-140 ||

aṃguṣṭhādiṣvaṃguleṣu nyasedaṃgaiḥ sajātibhiḥ |

jātayaśca namaḥ svāhā vaṣaṭ huṃ vaṣaṭ phaṭkarāḥ || 4-141 ||


evaṃ ca ||


jātiṣaṭkamiti proktaṃ hṛdādyastāntasaṃyutam |

astraṃ tu talayon yasya kuyāttāratrayādikam || 4-142 ||

diśaste naiva vahnīyācchotikābhiḥ samāhitaḥ |

hṛdayādiṣu vinyasya daṃgamantrāstataḥ sudhīḥ || 4-143 ||

hṛdayāye namaḥ pūrvaṃ śirase vahnivallabhā |

śikhāyai ca ṣaḍityuktaṃ kavacāya huṃ mīritam || 4-144 ||


netratrayāya vauṣaṭ syāya phaḍiti kramāt |

vahnivallabhā svāhā || 


ṣaḍaṃgamantrāṇityuktvā ṣaḍaṃgeṣu niyojayet |


anuktā ṣaḍaṃgasya ||


paṃcāṅgāni mano yasya tatra netramanuṃ tyajet || 4-145 ||

aṃgahīnasya mantrasya svenaivāṅgāni kalpayet |


svena ca mūlamantreṇaiva ||


tat tat kalpoktavidhinā nyāsātanyān samācaret || 4-146 ||

kalpayed ātmano dehe pīṭhaṃ dharmādibhiḥ kramāt |

aṃsoruyugmayorvidvān prādakṣiṇye va deśikaḥ || 4-147 ||

dharmaṃ jñānaṃ savairājñaṃ aiśvaryaṃ vinyasetkramāt | 

dharmādayaḥ smṛtāḥ pādāḥ pīṭhagātrāṇi vā pare || 4-148 ||

mukhapāśvanābhipārśve ṣvadharmādīn prakalpayet |

anantaṃ hṛdaye padmasasmin sūryandupāvākā || 4-149 ||

27a) e mu sve svakalānyasyan nāmādyakṣarapūrvikāḥ |

akārādyaṃ nyaset sūrya oṃkārādyaṃ niśākaram || 4-150 ||

sakārādyaṃ nyased vahni kalāmaṇḍalasaṃyutam |

sattvādīstrīguṇā nyasya * * * * * * * * || 4-151 ||

satvaṃ rajastama iti yasya bhedāḥ samīritāḥ |

ātmānamantarātmānaṃ paramātmānamatra tu || 4-152 ||

ādivījatrayaṃ nyasya śaktivījapurassaram |


ā/ ātmane namaḥ ||

aṃ antarātmane namaḥ ||

iti nyāsakramaḥ ||

hrī/kārātmane nama iti ca ||


jñānātmānaṃ pravinyasya nyaset pīṭhamanuṃ tataḥ || 4-153 ||

evaṃ dehamaye pīṭhe cintayediṣṭadevatām |

mudrā pradarśyavidhivadarghasthāpanamācaret || 4-154 ||

śaṃkhamustāṃ vubhuḥ prokṣya vāmato vahnimaṇḍale |


vahnimaṇḍale trikoṇe ||


sādhāraṃ sthāpaye vidvān vindustutaṃ sudhāmayaiḥ || 4-155 ||

sirasthacaṃndrāt yat sutayā vasudhā tanmayairjalaiḥ |


mūlamanunā ||


toyaiḥ sugandhipuṣpādyaiḥ pūraye tu yathā vidhi || 4-156 ||

ādhāraṃ pāvakaṃ śaṃkhaṃ sūryatoyaṃ sudhākaram |

smared vahnyarkacaṃdrāṇāṃ kalpāntāste ṣvanukramāt || 4-157 ||

mulamantranyaset spṛṣṭvā nyaset tasyāṅgamantrakam |

hṛnmantreṇābhisaṃpūjya hastābhyāṃ chādayen nṛpaḥ || 4-158 ||

japed vidyāṃ yathā nyāyaṃ daiśiko devatādhiyā |

astramantreṇasaṃrakṣya kavacenāvaguṃṭha ca || 4-159 ||

dhenumudrā samāpādya vodhayet tat svamudrayā |

27b) dakṣiṇe prokṣaṇī pātramādhāyābhiḥ prapūjayet || 4-160 ||

kiṃcidīrghāmbusaṃgṛhya prokṣyaṇyaṃbhasiyojayet |

arghasyottarataḥ kāryaṃ pādya ācamanīyakam || 4-161 ||

ātmānaṃ yāgavastuni maṃḍapaṃ prokṣayed guruḥ |

prokṣaṇī pātrato yena manunānyadapikramāt || 4-162 ||

mūlamantranyase spṛṭvā nyaset tasyāṅgamantrakam |

hṛnmantreṇābhisaṃpūjya hastābhyāṃ chādayen nṛpaḥ || 4-163 ||


japed vidyāṃ ya dhānyā nyāsakramena deheṣu dharmādīn pūjayet tataḥ ||


gandhādyai pīṭhamanubhistasmiśca puradevatām |

paṃcakṛtva punaḥ kuryā puṣpāṃjalimananyadhīḥ || 4-164 ||

uttamāṅge hṛdādhāre pādasarvāṅgake kramāt |

vinā naivedyagaṃdhādyairupacāraiḥ samarcayet || 4-165 ||

gurupadiṣṭavidhinā śeṣamabhyasamāpayet |

sarvametat prayuṃjīte prokṣaṇī sthana (?)vāriṇā || 4-166 ||

visṛjyatoyaṃ prokṣiṇyāḥ pūjayet tāṃ yathā pura |

tatastaṃ maṇḍalaṃ mantrī gaṃdhādyai sādhupūjayet || 4-167 ||

śālibhiḥ karṇikāmadhye sā mpūryoparitaṃḍulaiḥ |

alaṃkṛtastena darbhā nāstiryamantravit || 4-168 ||

kucamakṣatasaṃyuktaṃ nyaset teṣāṃ madhyo pari (?) |


kurcaṃ vistarāṃ ādhāraśaktimārabhya pīṭhamantramayaṃ yajet ||


adhaḥ kūrmaśilārūḍhāṃ śaraccandranibhaprabhān || 4-169 ||

ādhāraśāktaṃ prayajet ṣaṭkajadvayadhāriṇam |

28a) kūmarūpāṇilā taduparisthitā mityarthaḥ || 4-170 ||

muddhni tasyāḥ samāsīnaṃ kurmaṃ nīlābhamurcayet |

ūrdhvaṃ brahmāṇilāśītamanantaṃ kūrmasannibham || 4-171 ||

yajeccakradharaṃ mūddhni dhārayantaṃ vasundharām |

tamālaśyāmalāṃ tatre nīlindīvaladhāriṇīm || 4-172 ||

abhyarccayedvasumataṃ sphurat sāgaramekhalām |

tasyāṃ ratnamayaṃ dvīpaṃ tasmiṃśca maṇimaṇḍapam || 4-173 ||

yaje kalpataruṃ tasmit sādhakābhīṣṭasiddhidam |

adhasthā pūjayet teṣāṃ veḍikāṃ maṇḍalojvalām || 4-174 ||

paścād abhyaccayet tasyāṃ pīṭhamantrādibhiḥ punaḥ |

raktaśyāmaharidriṃdra nīlābhān pāyapūraṇaḥ || 4-175 ||

vṛṣake saribhūte bharūpādharmādhikāṃ nyajet |


bhūto manusyagātreṣu pūjayet tāṃstu na apūrvāna kulakṣaṇān ||


āgneyādiṣu koṇeṣu dikṣucāthāṃvujaṃ yajet || 4-176 ||

ānandakandaṃ prathamaṃ saṃvinnālamanantaram |

sarvatattvātmakaṃ padmamabhyarcya tadanantaram || 4-177 ||

mantrī prakṛtitattvāni vikāramayakesarān |

paṃcāśadvījavarṇāḍhyāṃ karṇikāṃ pūjayet tataḥ || 4-178 ||

kalābhiḥ pūjayet sārddhaṃ tasyā sūryandupāvakān |

praṇavasya tribhirbhāgai rajaḥ satvādikāṅguṇān || 4-179 ||

ātmānamantarātmānaṃ paramātmānamūrccayet (ḥ) |

jñānātmānaṃ ca vidhivat pīṭhamantrāvasānakam || 4-180 ||

pīṭhaśaktikeśareṣu madhya ca savalābhaya |

28b) hemādirocataṃ kuṃbhaṃ astrābhiḥ kṣālitāntaram || 4-181 ||

caṃdanāgurukapūra dhūpitaṃ śobhanākṛtim |

āveṣṭitāṅganīraṃdhraṃ tantunā triguṇātmanā || 4-182 ||


śaktiviṣayeraktisūtreṇa ||

viṣṇuviṣaye śuklasūtreṇeti tāntrikā ||


carcitaṃ gandhapuṣpādyai dūrvākṣatasamanvitam |

navaratnodakaṃ mantrī sthāpayettāramuccaram || 4-183 ||

aikyaṃ saṃkalpyakuṃbhasya pīṭhasya ca vidhānavit |

kṣīrīdrumakakhāyena palāśatvadbhave navā || 4-184 ||

tīrthodakairvvākapūragaṃdhapuṣpasuvāsitaiḥ |

ātmābhedena vidhivan mātṛkāṃ pratilomataḥ || 4-185 ||

japen mūlamanūṃ tadvat pūrayed devatā dhiyā |


pratilomamātṛkāmāha ||

kṣa/ ha/ sa/ ṣa/ śa/ yamityādi akārāntaṃ paṃcāśadvarṇān mūlamantraṃ 
japan pūrayet ||


śaṃkhe kvāthāṃ vu saṃpūrṇe gaṃdhāṣṭakamabhīṣṭadam || 4-186 ||

vilodya pūrayet tasmin nāvāhya sakalāḥ kalā |

daśavahneḥ kalāḥ pūrvaṃ dvādaśaḥ dvādaśātmanaḥ || 4-187 ||

kalāḥ ṣoḍaśasomasya paṃcāpaṃcāśataṃ kalāḥ |

kalātmakaṃ śaṃkhasthaṃ kvāthaṃ kumbheṣu nikṣipet || 4-188 ||

gaṃdhāṣṭakatantrividhaṃ śaktiviṣṇuśivātmakam |

caṃdanāgurukapūra colakuṃkumalocanā || 4-189 ||

jatāmāṃsī kapiyutā śakte gaṃdhāṣṭakaṃ viduḥ |

cora iti kuṇḍaṃ kṛṣṇaśaṭikastūrīti vā kapiraktacaṃḍanam || 4-190 ||

29a) caṇḍanāguruhrīvīra kuṃṣṭhaṃ kuṃkume rocanaḥ |


janmamāsī vyakāḥ || (?)


jatāmāṃsīmuramiti viṣṇu gaṅgāṣṭakaṃ viduḥ || 4-191 ||


hrīvīrabhalhākuṣṭhaṃ kuṭhaṃ sevyakaṃ vīraṇasū (?)lamiti yāvat ||


caṃḍanāgurukapūratamālajalakuṃkumam |

kuśītaṃ kuṣṭhasaṃyuktaṃ śaivagaṃdhāṣṭakaṃ viduḥ || 4-192 ||

tamālo vṛkṣaviśeṣaḥ jalavalhā ityarthaḥ |

kuśītaṃ raktacaṃdanaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ kuḍamiti yāvat || 4-193 ||


phetkāriṇītantre ||


rocanākapikāśmīre nāmītvagaracaṃdanaiḥ |

savorairaṣṭagaṃdhoyaṃ śaive śākte ca sasyate || 4-194 ||


kapiraktacandanaṃ kāśmīraṃ kuṃkumaṃ guḍatvakudārcyanī sacoraiḥ 
kastūrīyuktai ||


pāśādityakṣarātmānte syād amukhyapadaṃ punaḥ |

kramāt prāṇā iha prāṇāstathā jīva iha sthitaḥ || 4-195 ||

amukhyasarvendriyāṇi bhūyo mukhyapadaṃ vadet |

vāṅmano nayanaśrotraghrāṇaprāṇapadātpatha || 4-196 ||

paścād ihāgatya sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭha tuṭhadvayam |

ayaṃ prāṇamataḥ proktaṃ sarvajīvapradāyakaḥ || 4-197 ||


prāṇādityakṣaram ||

ā/ hrī/ kro/ iti vījatrayam ||

ātmā ātmamantraḥ haṃsa iti yāvat |

tena ā/ hrī/ kro/ ha/ saḥ ṭha dvayaṃ svāhā tena |

ā/ hrī/ kro/ ha/ saḥ amukhya prāṇā iha prāṇā ā/ hrī/ kro/ haṃ saḥ amukhya 
jīva iha sthitaḥ |

29b) ā/ hrī/ kro/ ha/ sa amukhya sarvendriyāṇi punaravam ||

amukhyavāṅmanā nayanaśrotraghrāṇaprāṇā ihāgatya sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhaṃ 
tu svāhā ||

amukhyatpatra uhaḥ ||

paścādaśvarthapanasacūtakomalaḥ |

pallavaiḥ indravallīsamāvaddhaiḥ suradrumadhiyā guruḥ ||

indravallī ||

aparājitā ||


kuṃbhavaktrepi dhāyāsmiṃścaṣakeṃ saphalākṣatam |


caśakaṃ śalāvaṃ akṣataṃ yadādi ||



sthāpayecca phaladhiyā vidhivat kalpaśākhinām | 

tataḥ kuṃbhaṃ nirmale nakaumayugmena veṣṭhayet || 4-198 ||

mūlena mūrttiṃ kliptāṃsmin chāyayā kalpaśākhiṇām |

āvāhyapūjayet tasyāṃ mantrī mantrasya daivatam || 4-199 ||

mūlamantraṃ samucārya suṣumnāvat manāsudhīḥ |

ānīyatejaḥ svasthānā nāsikāraṃdhranirgatam || 4-200 ||

karasthamātṛkāṃ bhoje caitanyapūṣpasaṃcayaḥ |

saṃyojya brahmarandhrena murtyāmāvāhayet sudhīḥ || 4-201 ||

devasya mastakaṃ kuryāt sumopahitaṃ sadā |

pūjākāla devatā yāno paribhrāmayat karam || 4-202 ||

saṃsthāpanaṃ sannidhānaṃ sanirodhamanantaram |

sakalīkaraṇaṃ paścād vidadyādavaguṃṭhanam || 4-203 ||

amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā kurvītā paramikṛtim |

kramādetānikurvīta svamudrābhiḥ samāhitaḥ || 4-204 ||


āvāhanādikāmudrā pravakṣyāmi ka mudrā ka yathākramam |


30a) yāti viracitābhistu modante sarvadevatāḥ |

samyak saṃpūjitaiḥ puṣpaiḥ kalābhyāṃ kalpitāṃjaliḥ || 4-205 ||

āvāhanī samākhyātā mudrādaiśikasattamaiḥ |

adhomukhī kṛto saiva proktā sthāpanakarmaṇi || 4-206 ||

āśliṣṭhamuṣṭiyugalā prathitāṅguliyugmakā |

sannidhāne samākhyātā mudreyaṃ taṃ treyedibhiḥ || 4-207 ||

aṃguṣṭhagabhiṇī saiva sannirodhasamīlitā |

devatāṅge ṣaḍaṅgāṇāṃ nyāsaḥ syāt sakalīkṛtaḥ || 4-208 ||

savyahastakṛtā mūrtiṣṭidīrghādhomukhatarjanī |

avaguṃṭhanamudreyamabhitobhrāmitāsatī || 4-209 ||

anyonyābhimukhāśliṣṭa kaviṣṭhānāmikā punaḥ |

tathā tat tarjanīmadhyā dhenumudrā mṛtapradā || 4-210 ||

anyonyagrathitāṅguṣṭhā pramānitakalāṅgulī |

mahāmudreyamuditā paramīkaraṇe vudhaiḥ || 4-211 ||

prayojayadi sā mudrā devatā yogakarmaṇiḥ |

athopacārān kurvīta mantravit svāgatādikān || 4-212 ||


tathā coktaṃ vṛhamatsyasūktam ||


saparyā vividhāḥ proktāstāsāmekāṃ samāśraye |

gaṃdhādikāni vedyāntā upacārā daśakramāt || 4-213 ||


ṣoḍaśopacārāṣṭaduktaṃ śāradāyām ||


pādyārghārcamanaṃ snānaṃ vasanābharaṇāsti ca |

gaṃdhapuṣpadhūpadīpau naivedyāvasanaṃ tathā || 4-214 ||

tāmbulamarcanā stotraṃ tarpaṇaṃ ca namaskṛtā |

30b) prayojayadaśanāyāmupacārāṃstu ṣoḍaśaḥ || 4-215 ||


tathā ca jñānārṇave ||


āsanaṃ svāra?gataṃ cārya pādyamācamanīyakam |

madhuparkā ca malīna (?) vasanā bharaṇāni ca || 4-216 ||

gaṃdhapuṣpai dhūpadīpau naivedyaṃ vadanaṃ tathā |

evaṃ vā yojayet prājñe upacārāṃstu ṣoḍaśaḥ || 4-217 ||


aṣṭādaśopacārāḥ ||

taduktaṃ tripurārṇave ||


āsanā vāhane vīrghe pādyamācamanaṃ tathā |

snānaṃ vāsopavītaṃ ca bhūṣaṇāni ca sarvaśaḥ || 4-218 ||

gandhaṃ puṣ.paṃ tathā dhūpaṃ dīpapannaṃ ca tarpaṇam |

mālyānulepanaṃ caiva namaskāravisarjane || 4-219 ||

aṣṭādaśopacārāṃstu mantrī pūjāṃ samācaret |


atha ṣaṭtriṃśadupacāraḥ ||

taduktaṃ sundarīrahasyavṛttau ||


āsanāt paṃjane tadvad udvarttananirukṣaṇeḥ || 4-220 ||

sammārjane sapi *? deḥ snapanāvāhane tataḥ |

pāpārghācamanīyaṃ ca snānīyamadhuparkakau || 4-221 ||

punarācamanīyaṃ ca vastrayajñopavītake |

antaṃ (?) kāsā? gaṃdhapuṣpe dhūpadīpo tathaiva ca || 4-222 ||

tāmbulāni ca naivedya puṣpamālā tathaiva ca |

anulepanaśaryāṃ ca cāmaraṃ vyaṃjanaṃ tathā || 4-223 ||

ādarśadarśanaṃ caiva namaskārothanantaram |

gītavādya ca dānāni stutirhomapradakṣiṇam || 4-224 ||

dantakāṣṭapradānaṃ ca tato deva visarjjanam |

upacārā rā i ca jñeyāḥ ṣaṭtriṃśat surapūjane || 4-225 ||


31a) atha catuḥṣaṣṭhapacāra ||

āsanāropanaṃ sugaṃdhitailābhyaṃgaṃ majjanaśālā praveśanam 
majjanamaṃḍapapīṭhopaveśanam ||

divyasnānīyam ||

udvarttanaṃ uṣṭodakasnānaṃ kanakakalaśacyutasakalatīrthobhiṣekam ||

dhautavastraparimārjanam ||

aruṇadugulorttarīyam ||

ālepamaṃḍapapraveśam ||

ālīpamaṃḍapamaṇipīṭhopaveśanam ||

caṃdanāgurukuṃkumamṛgamadakapuragorocanā divyaṃ 
gaṃdhasarvāṅgavilepanam |

keśabhārasya kālāgurudhupamallikā mālatī jāticaṇ pakāśo 
kaśatapatrapunnāgakalhārayugma savartukusumamanobhuṣaṇamaṇḍapapraveśana 
bhūṣaṇamaṇipīṭhopaveśana navamaṇimukuṭāṃ candrasakalaṃ sīmantīnī 
siṃduraṃ tilakaratnaṃ kālāṃjanam |

pāṇiyugalanānābharaṇaṃ adharayāvakaṃ grathanabhūṣaṇaṃ 
karacintāmaṇikaṃ mahāpadakaṃ muktāvalī ekāvali devacchaṃdakaṃ 
chadadmavīraṃ keyurayugalacatuṣṭayaṃ valayāvaliṃ ūrmikāvaliṃ 
kāṃciḍāmaṇikatisūtraṃ śobhākhyā bharaṇaṃ pādakaṭakaṃ ratnanūpuraṃ 
pādmaṅgulīyakaṃ ekakare pāśaṃ itarakere ikṣucāpaṃ aparakere 
raktotparābjakumudakaṃ dacūtāṅkurodbhavān puṣpavāṇān śrīmālikya 
pādukāṃ svasamākopaveśanam 

31b) amṛtāsanamekaṃ ācamanaṃ kapūravāṭikā ānaṃdollāsavilāsahāsaṃ 
maṃgalātrikāṃ śvetacchatraṃ cāmalayugalaṃ darpaṇaṃ tālavṛtaṃ gaṃdhaṃ 
puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ dīpaṃ naivedyaṃ ca kalpayet ||

taduktaṃ mānasollāse ||


saṃbuddhyā pravadenmantrī upacārān sadaiva tuḥ |


saṃbuddhyā saṃvodhanapadena ||

sarvopacāramantrāsti tārapūrvākalpayāmi namaḥ ityantaḥ kāryā ||

tathā ca ||

śrī/ hrī/ klī/ śrīmahātripurasundarī pādyaṃ kalpayāmi nama ityādi namaḥ ||

ityādi sarvabodhyam ||

iti pādyam ||

śrīvidyā viṣaye mabhilāṣaḥ ||

anya mratu saṃbuddhyā ||

tathā coktaṃ jñānamālāyām ||


upacāravidhiṃ vakṣya datvā vātha śubhāsanam || 4-226 ||

svāgataṃ kuśalapraśnaṃ nigadedagrato guroḥ |

pādyaṃ pādābuje dadyā devasya hṛdayānunā || 4-227 ||


hṛdayānuma ityarthaḥ ||


etadcchyā (?)kadūrvāṅga viṣṇukrāntābhirīritam |

svadhāmantreṇavadane dadyā vācamanīyakam || 4-228 ||

jātīlavaṃgakakkolaistaduktaṃ tantravedibhi |


svadhā mantraḥ ||

vamiti mantraḥ ||

arghaṃ diśet tato mūrddhni śiromantreṇadaiśikā gaṃdhapuṣpākṣata * * 
kuśādītilaśarṣapaḥ 

madūrdhe sarvadevānāṃ etad arghamudīritam || 4-229 ||


32a) svāhā mantra ityarthaḥ | 


te naiva manunā kuryād abhīrācamanīyakam |


te naiva svadhā nunā tataḥ kuryān madhuparkamukhāṃvuje ||


ājyaṃ dadhimadhunmiśraṃ metad uktaṃ maṇīṣibhiḥ || 4-230 ||


maṅgumantraḥ ||

svadhāmanuḥ ||

vamiti mantra ityarthaḥ ||


tenaiva manunā kuryād abhirācamanīyakam |


te naivamiti mantreṇaiva |

arghadatvā pādyaṃ dadyāt ||

taduktaṃ kramadīpikāyām ||

datvāsanaṃ svāgatamityudīrya tathārghapādyācamanīyakāni deyāni 
ityāṃdiśravaṇāt |

icchā vikalpa iti bhāvāt ||

ācanīyaṃ vālatrayaṃ dadyāt | 

taduktaṃ pūrṇā yāge 


svadhetyācamanīyaṃ hi trivāraṃ mukhapaṃkaje || 4-231 ||

mukuṭoparipuṣpaṃ ca vauṣaḍityaryayet sudhīḥ |


vauṣaditi puṣpadānamiti śruteḥ ||

vauṣaṭ nama iti mantraḥ ||

arghādidāneṣvapi tathā datuktaṃ sārasaṃhitāyām (ḥ) |


namaḥ svadhā ca svāhā ca vauṣaḍiti tathākramam || 4-232 ||

hṛdyuratmānamucārya pādyādīni prakalpayet |

gaṃdhādbhiḥ kārayet snānaṃ vāsasīparidhāpayet || 4-233 ||

dadyād yajñopavītaṃ ca hārādyābharaṇaiḥ sahā |

nyāsakramena munānā paṭhitaimātṛkākṣaraiḥ || 4-234 ||

abhyarcya deva gaṃdhādyairaṅgādīn pūjayet tataḥ |

gaṃdhacandanakapūrakālāgurubhirīritaḥ || 4-235 ||

kamale karavīre dve kumude tulaśī dvayam |

32b) jātī dvayaṃ ketakī dve kalhālacaṃpakot pale || 4-236 ||

kundamaṃdārapunnāga pātalānāgacaṃpakam |

āragvadhaṃ karṇikāraṃ pāvantī navamālikā || 4-237 ||

saugaṃṭhikaṃ sakoraṃṭhaṃ palāśāśokamallikāḥ |

danturaṃ sarjakaṃ vilva marjunaṃ puṣpavilvakam || 4-238 ||

anyāni ca sagaṃdhīni puṣpamātrāṇidaiśikaiḥ |

upadiṣṭāni pūjāyāṃ ādadītavicakṣaṇā || 4-239 ||

malinaṃ bhūmisaṃspṛṣṭaṃ krimikeśādidūṣitam |

aṃgaspṛṣṭaṃ samāghrāṇāṃ tyajet paryuṣitaṃ guruḥ || 4-240 ||


aṃgaspṛṣṭaṃ hastātiriktamiti śeṣaḥ ||


mukulaiḥ patitaścaiva khaṇḍitaiḥ śoṣitairapi |

anarhairapipuṣpaiśca dalaiḥ patraiśca nārcayet || 4-241 ||

yena kenāpipuṣpena patreṇāpidalena vā |

yataḥ kutaśvidānīya yantro tatrodbhavena ca || 4-242 ||

bhāvārthī jīvitārthā ca nāca yagahite sthale |

parāropitavṛkṣabhyaḥ puṣpā nārīya cārcayet || 4-243 ||

avijñāpyai va tairyastu niṣphalaṃ tasya pūjitam |


tathā ca jñānamālāyām ||


puṣpaṃ vā yadi vā patraṃ phalaṃ neṣṭhamadhomukham || 4-244 ||

puṣpāṃjalividhi hitvā yathotpannaṃ tathārpaṇam |


tathā ca kālikāpurāṇe ||


naivedyaṃ dakṣiṇe vāme purato vātapṛṣṭataḥ || 4-245 ||

dīpaṃ dakṣiṇato dadyāt purato vā na vāmataḥ |

vāmatastu tathā dhūpamagre vā navadakṣiṇe || 4-246 ||

33a) nivedayet puro?bhāge gaṃdhaṃ puṣpaṃ ca bhūṣaṇam |


vāme dhūpaṃ dadyād iti viṣṇuviṣayam |

śaktiviṣaye tu dakṣiṇe dadyāt |


pratimādiṣu yad yogyaṃ gotradattaṃ ca tat tanau || 4-247 ||

dadyād ayogyaṃ purato naivedyaṃ bhojanādikam |

ādhāroparidīpaṃ ca dadyād yat tena mānavaḥ || 4-248 ||

sarvasahāvasumatī saha tena tvidaṃ dvayam |

ācāryapādaghātaṃ ca dīpatāpaṃ tathaiva ca || 4-249 ||


iti kālikāpurāṇam ||


aguruśīlakapūraguguruśarkarā madhucaṃdanaiḥ |

dhūpayadājyasaṃmiśrai nīcaidavaspadaiśikaḥ || 4-250 ||


uśīlaṃ vīraṇamūlam |

vārttyā (?)kapūragabhiṇaḥ ||

sarpiṣā tilajena vā ||


āropya darśayen dīpānucaisaurabhaśālinaḥ |

svādupadaṃśavimalaṃ pāyasaṃ sahaśarkaram || 4-251 ||

kadalīphalasaṃyuktaṃ sājyamantreṇavedayet |


upadaṃśacarvaṇīya dravyam ||

modako nālikelādi ||


tatra tatra jalaṃ dadyādrūpacārāṃ tanītareḥ || 4-252 ||

aṃgānilokapālānta yajed vā varaṇāmyapi |

kesareṣvagnikoṇādi hṛdayādīni pūjayet || 4-253 ||

netre magre diśāsvastraṃ dhātavyā aṃgadevatāḥ |

hṛdayaṃ vahnirghrāṇe ca ṇaiṛte śiva īritam || 4-254 ||

vāyu koṇeśi ṣā proktā kavacaṃ śivadiṣvapi |

ṣurato netramabhyarcya prāgādyāśāśca thāstakam || 4-255 ||


33b) śaktiviṣaye pūjāprakāramāha ||

uktaṃ śrīnavadurgākalpe ||


dakṣāṅguṣṭhatarjanībhyāṃ puṣpanikṣepanaṃ caret |

nārcayedekahastena yadicchet siddhimātmanaḥ || 4-256 ||

kevalaṃ dakṣahastena abhicārāyakalpate |

vāmena kevalaṃ bhogaḥ saṃyogā bhogamokṣadam || 4-257 ||

aṃguṣṭhā nāmikā yogā chivaśaktyātmakaṃ param |

aṃguṣṭhaḥ śiva ityukto nāmikāśaktirucyate || 4-258 ||

toya saṃyogamātreṇa dravyasyādramṛtopamam |

śivaśaktyātmakaṃ viṃdu dvayaṃ syād aṅgulīyakam || 4-259 ||

tena taṃ nismṛtaṃ dravyaṃ paramāmṛtamiṣyate |

chinnādidoṣasiddhārthaṃ mantrāṇāṃ sādhakottamaḥ || 4-260 ||

gurūpadeśavidhinā yonimudrāṃ samabhyaset |


yathā ||


ādhāre ca gudasthāne paṃkajaṃ syāccaturdalam || 4-261 ||

tatra svāyaṃbhuvaṃ liṅgaṃ raktagauraṃ vicintayet |


tatra sārddhatrivalayam ||


prasuptabhujagākārā kuṇḍalīnī vicintyatāṃ || 4-262 ||


huṃkāre manodaṃḍā hatāṃ kṛtvotthāpya mūlādhārālliṃgamūlasthaṃ 
svādhisthānanābhisthaṃ maṇipurakacakramānīya maṇipūrakā hṛdayasthaṃ 
anāhataṃ cakramevaṃ pūrva ca vadanāhatāt kaṃṭhasthaṃ viśuddham evam eva 
viśuddhād bhruvorantarālam ājñācakramānīya jñātaḥ sahasradalakamalaṃ 
nītvā saha*dalakamalāvasthita sadāśivajyotiṣā saha tasyāssāmarasyaṃ 
vicintyatām |

34a) mūlamantrasvarūpāṃ kuṇḍalinī sadā 

śivapadājñā cakramānīya tasmād viśuddhānāhatamaṇipūrakatvādhisthāna 
krameṇamūlādhāraṃ nayet |

tatastāṃ matra devatāṃ hṛdi vicintya mānasopacārairārādhya namaskuryāt ||

iti yonimudrā ||


abhyāsakaraṇā saktaḥ saṃskārānācaret sudhīḥ |

mantrasya daśakathyate saṃskārāḥ siddhidāyinaḥ || 4-263 ||

jananaṃ jīvanaṃ paścāt tādanaṃ jīvanaṃ tathā |

athābhiṣeko vimalīkaraṇā pyāyate tataḥ || 4-264 ||

tarpaṇaṃ dīpaṇaṃ gupti deśaitā mantrasaṃskriyā |

mantrāṇāṃ mātṛkāmadhyād uddhāro jananaṃ smṛtam || 4-265 ||


mātṛketi mātṛkāḥ paṃcāśat tanmadhyān mātṛkān madhyā deya 
mantroddhāra iti mantrasya jananam ||


praṇavāntaritāḥ kṛtvā mantravarṇān japet sudhī |

etajjīvaṃ tamityāhurmantratantraviśāradā || 4-266 ||


praṇavāntariteti ||

deya mantrasyaikaikavarṇānantaraṃ praṇavaṃ datvā japed ityarthaḥ ||

etan mantrasya jīvanam ||


mantravarṇān samālikhya tādaye chandanāmbhasā |

pratyekaṃ vāyunā mantraṃ tāḍanaṃ tadudāhṛtam || 4-267 ||


pratyekaṃ deya mantrasyaikaikākṣaraṃ vāyunā vāyuvījena yamiti vījena 
pratyakṣarā paricaṃdanā śrītajatmaṃdadyāt ||

iti mantratāḍanam ||

gopālapakṣe śuklacaṃdanajalena |

34b) devī viṣayaraktacandanajalenatyarthaḥ ||


vilikhemantra tan matrī prasūnaiḥ karavīrajaiḥ |


tanmatrākṣarasaṃkhyātairhanyādyānte na vodhana?||

vilikhye tāmrapaṭṭāṃ dāvityarthaḥ ||

yānte neti yakārasyā te rephaḥ ||

tathā ca ||

ramiti vahnivījamityarthaḥ ||

iti mantrasya vodhanam ||


svatantroktavidhānena mantrī mantrasya saṃkhyayā || 4-268 ||

aśvatthapallavairmantra yadbhiṣiṃced viśuddhaya |


svatantrokteti ||

viṣṇupakṣe śuklacaṃdanena |

devī pakṣe raktacandanenatyarthaḥ ||


saṃcintya manasā mantraṃ jyotimantreṇa nirdahet || 4-269 ||

matrī malatrayaṃ mantrī vimalīkaraṇaṃ tvidam |


jyotimantroryathā |


tāraṃ vyomāgni manuyugdaṇḍījyotirmanurmataḥ || 4-270 ||


tāraṃ oṃkāraḥ ||

vyomahakāraḥ || agniḥ rephaḥ ||

manuḥ oṃkāraḥ daṃḍīḥ ||

tena hrau/ iti jyotimantreṇa ||

oṃ hrau/ iti mantreṇamalatrayaṃ māyīya kārmaṇaśārīrasaṃjñakamiti ||


kuśodakena japtena pratyakaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ manoḥ |

tena mantreṇa vidhivad etadāpyāyanaṃ matam || 4-271 ||


tena mantreṇa deyamantreṇa ||

etanmatrasyāpyāyenam ||


mantreṇa vāriṇāmantrī tarpaṇaṃ tarpaṇaṃ maham |


mantreṇa mūlamantreṇa ||

tathā ca ||

deya mantramuccāryā mukamantraṃ tarpayāmīti prayoga ||

35a) vratanmantrasya tarpaṇam ||


tāraṃ māyāramāyogormanodīpanamucyate || 4-272 ||


tāraḥ oṃ ||

māyā hrī/ ramā śrī yogaḥ saṃvaṃdhaḥ te nadeya mantrasyādau |

oṃ hrī/ śrī/ iti vījatraya datvā japedityarthaḥ ||

etanmantrasya dīpanam |


japyamānasya mantrasya gopanaṃ tvat prakāśanam |

saṃskārā daśasaṃproktāḥ sarvatrantreṣu gopitā || 4-273 ||

yat kṛtvā saṃpradāyena mantrī vāṃcchitamāpnuyāt |

homakṛtvāthaśāstroktaiḥ kuryā cchiṣyasya saṃkriyām || 4-274 ||

tataḥ sudhau tadantācyaṃ snātaśiṣyasamāhitam |

pāyayitvā paṃcagavyaṃ kuṃḍasyāntikamānayet || 4-275 ||

vilokya divyadṛṣṭarātaṃ taccaitanyaṃ hṛdaṃvuje |

gururātmanisaṃyojya kuryāddadhvaviśodhanam || 4-276 ||

uktaṃ kālādhvatattvādhvā bhuvanādhvetitattriyam |

varṇādhvā ca padādhvā ca mantrādhvetya paraṃ trayam || 4-277 ||

nivṛtyādyāḥ kalāḥ paṃcakalādhveti prakīrttitā |

tattvādhvā vahudhābhinnāḥ śaivādyāgamabhedataḥ || 4-278 ||

ṣaṭtriṃśacchivatattvāni dvātriṃśadvarṇa vāni ca |

caturviṃśatitatvāni maittrā niḥ prakṛte punaḥ || 4-279 ||

uktāni daśatattvāni sapta va tripadātmanaḥ |

tattvāniśevān mucyante śivaśaktiḥ sadāśivaḥ || 4-280 ||

iśvaro vidyayā sārddhaṃ paṃcaśuddhānyamūni hi |

māyā kālaśca niyatiḥ kalāvidyā punaḥ smṛtā || 4-281 ||


35b) rāgaḥ puruṣa etā ||

na śuddho suddhāni sapta ca ||


prakṛtivuddhihaṃkāro manojñā vuddhiyānyathaḥ |

karmendriyāṇi tanmātrā paṃcabhūtāni daiśikaḥ || 4-282 ||

etānyāhurasaptāni caturviṃśati vā gamaiḥ |

kramādetā tatadhvanaḥ ṣaṭcchedayaṅgurusattamaḥ || 4-283 ||

pādābjanābhihṛdbhāramūrddhasya piśiśoḥ smaret |

netre śiṣyasya vadhniyā netraṃ mantreṇavāsasā || 4-284 ||


netramantreṇa vauṣaḍimantreṇaḥ ||


kare gṛhītvā taṃ śiṣyaṃ kuṇḍato maṇḍalaṃ nayet |

tasyāñjaliṃ punaḥ puṣpaiḥ pūrayitvā yathāvidhi || 4-285 ||

kalaśe devatā prītyai kṣepayen mūlamucaran |

vyayojyataṃ netravaḍhamāsīnaṃ garbhasaṃstare || 4-286 ||

ātmayāgankramādbhūyaḥ saṃskṛtotyādya daiśikaḥ |

tat tanmantroditā nyāsān kuryād deheśiśostadā || 4-287 ||

paṃcopacāraiḥ kumbhasthaṃ pūjayet veṣṭadevatām |

gaṃdhā va yonivedyāntāṃ pūjā paṃcopacārikā || 4-288 ||

maṃḍale saṃskṛtaṃ śiṣya manyasminnopaveśaya |

vudat supaṃcavādyaṣu sārddhaṃ viprā viṣāguruḥ || 4-289 ||

vidhivat kuṇḍamuddhṛtya tanmukhastān suradrumān |

śiśoṇiyaviśinyasya mā-ṛḥkāṃ manasaḥ japan || 4-290 ||

mūlena sādhitaistoyai mabhiṣiṃcet tu mātmavit |

avaśiṣṭena toyena śiṣyamācamayed guruṃ (ḥ) || 4-291 ||


36a) utthāyaśiṣyo vimale || vāsase paridhāya ca ||

ācamya vāgyato bhūtvā niṣidet saṃnidho guru || 4-292 ||

devatā mātmanaśiṣya saṃkrāntau deśikottamaḥ |

pūjayed gandhapuṣpādyai caikyaṃ saṃbhāvayestayoḥ || 4-293 ||

nivṛtyāḥ dyāḥ kalāḥ paṃcabhūtānāṃ śaktayo yataḥ |

tasmā bhūtamaye dehe dhyātvā tāṃ vedayecchiśoḥ || 4-294 ||

nivṛttijānuparyantaṃ tarādārabhya saṃsthitāḥ |

jānurnonābhiparyantaṃ pratiṣṭhā vyāpyatiṣṭhataḥ || 4-295 ||

nābheḥ kaṇṭhāvadhivyāptā vidyāśāntistataḥ param |

kaṇṭhāllalātaparyantaṃ vyāptā tasyā chirāvadhi || 4-296 ||

śāntyatītā kalājñeyā kalāvyāpti nitīritā |

iti vinyasya śikhyāṅge devatā bhāvayadhviyā || 4-297 ||

amukamantraṃ dadāmīti pratijñāṃ śrāvayan tataḥ |

dadasveti ca te nokte tārocāraṇa pūrvakam || 4-298 ||

dadyād vidyāṃ tatastasmai vinītāyāṃvupūrvakam |

āpo ṛṣyādikaṃ coktā smṛtvā ca devatāṃ dhiyāḥ || 4-299 ||

gurustu prāṅmukho bhūtvā śiṣyaḥ prācīmukhasthitaḥ |

trivāraṃ dakṣiṇe karṇe vāme caiva tathā sakṛt || 4-300 ||

vrāhmaṇānāmeṣavidhistrīśūdrāṇāṃ tu vāmakeḥ |

caturthe pāṭhayitvā tvaṃ śiṣya haste jalaṃ punaḥ || 4-301 ||

ayaṃ ca mantra āvayoḥ samānaphaladostviti |

36b) astviti brahmaṇāpruyuḥ śiṣyaśca pratigṛhya ca || 4-302 ||

gururvidyāṃ samārabhyā aṣṭottaraśataṃ japet |

athavājyaṣṭakṛtvāśca japanmantramananyadhīḥ || 4-303 ||

gurumntra devatā nāmaikyaṃ saṃbhāvayandhiyā |

praṇamedaṃ ḍavadbhūmo guru taṃ devatātmakam || 4-304 ||

tasya pādāṃbujadvandvaṃ nijamūrddhaniyojayet |


atha dakṣiṇā ||

uktaṃ ca kramasaṃhitāyām ||


vṛthā vipravacāyamtu gṛhaṇati na mānavaḥ śubha || 4-305 ||

adatvā dakṣiṇaṃ vāpi sajāti narakaṃ dhruvam |


evaṃ ||


dakṣiṇāṃ vastrayugmaṃ ca ācāryāyani vedayat || 4-306 ||


ityādi yā dakṣiṇyayā āveśyakatvam ||

tat eva viṣadayati dīkṣānteti |

tathā ca vṛhan mahatsūkte dīkṣānte dakṣiṇāṃ prāhu 
svarṇagobhūmibhūṣaṇam ||


vasubhājana vastrān napaśudā sāsanāni ca |

śarīranarthaṃ prāṇāṃśca sarvatasmai nivedayet || 4-307 ||

tataḥ prabhṛti kurvīta guroḥ priyamenanyadhīḥ |

yadya diṣṭatamaṃ vastuguruve tan nivedayet || 4-308 ||

guraye dakṣiṇā dadyāt pratyakṣāya śivātmana |

sarvasvaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā tadājñayā || 4-309 ||

na cet saṃṣāriṇīśaktiḥ kathamasya bhaviṣyati |


tathā ca ||

pārvatī harasaṃvāde ||


sarvasvaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād gāṃ ca dadyāt payasvinīm || 4-310 ||

bhūmiṃ vṛttikarīṃ dadyā putrapautrān yāyinīm |


37a) tathā ca pārijātasvare |


gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt svarṇā vā sasamīritaḥ || 4-311 ||

anyathā naiva siddhiḥ syād adhicārāyakalpate |

ṛtvigtyo dakḍiṇā dadyāt samagrāṃ prītamānasaḥ || 4-312 ||

vittaśāṭhyaṃ vināsarvaṃ gureve sannivedayet |

maṃḍapaṃ maṃḍapadravyaṃ gururevasamarcayet || 4-313 ||

athavā kimihoktena pārvatī prāṇavallabhe |

gurvarthaṃ mate mānnityaṃ sarīramapi santyayet || 4-314 ||


tathā ca ||

smṛtau laghuhārīta |


ekamapyakṣaraṃ yastu guruḥ śiṣyanivedayet |

pṛthivyāṃ nāsti tadravyaṃ daddattvāsau ṛṇibhavet || 4-315 ||

bhojanaṃ cānnapānīya mavyabhyopinivedayet |

gurave brāhmaṇebhyāthamiṣṭaṃ bhakṣyaṃ prakalpayet || 4-346 ||

vṛddhabhyo vālakebhyotha nānābhakṣyaṃ prakalpayet |

bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ tathā coṣyaṃ lojyaṃ payaṃ ca carvaṇam || 4-317 ||

ādau datvā ca gurave viprebhyastadanantaram |

brāhmaṇāṃstarpayet paścā bhakṣaṃ bhojya sadakṣiṇām || 4-318 ||

guruḥ svaśaktirakṣārthaṃ sahasraṃ prajapen manum |

śiṣyaḥ kuryāt prayatnena guroprītimananyadhīḥ || 4-319 ||


tathā ca ||

īśānasaṃhitāyām ||


dīkṣāṃ kṛtvā yadā mantrī upavāsaṃ cared yadi |

tasya devaḥ sadāruṣṭaḥ śāpaṃ patati mūrddhani || 4-320 ||

nā gṛhītā nopyaṣmad pacāsaḥ śriyaṃ haret |

37b) bhaiṣyaṃ haviṣyaṃ śākādi vihitāniphalaṃ payaḥ || 4-321 ||

mūlaṃ sakuryavotpanno bhakṣyāṇyetāmi mantriṇām |

tasmād annaṃ samaśnīyād dhaviṣyānnenaśāṅkari || 4-322 ||


danveṣād aśyādau viṣṇumantrasya dānaśravanā tad vacanasya vā dhāyantaḥ 
kāmena nityasyā vādhāt |

āghrāya pitṛsevitam iti vedasyopapattiḥ vastutastaditaraparamiti bhāvaḥ ||

eṣā kalāvatīkṣā proktā sarvasamṛddhidā ||


ityāgamakalpa * tāyāṃ caturthapaṭala || 4 ||



homastu vidho nityanaimittakakāmyabhedāt |

sarvatra homasyāvaśyakatvamiti ||

taduktaṃ rudrayāmale ||


najaptaḥ siddhāte mantro nāhutaśca kadācana |

japahomo bhayārthatvāt pūjāyāḥ mūrttirakṣaṇam || 5-1 || iti ||

atha homavidhiṃ vakṣya sarvatantrānusārataḥ |

tasyai va kuṇḍasādhyatvād idānīṃ tan virūpyate || 5-2 ||

viprāṇāṃ caturaśraṃ syād rājñāṃ vat tulamiṣyate |

vaiśyānāṃ ardhacaṃdrābhaṃ śūdrāṇāṃ samīritam || 5-3 ||

caturaśraṃ tu sarveṣāṃ kecidīrghatritāntrikā |


taduktaṃ sāgarasaṃhitāyām ||


āgamānenusṛtyaivaṃ vahniḥ saṃkriyate tuyaiḥ || 5-4 ||

taistadaṅgulamānena kuṇḍānyepividhīyatām |

kuṇḍasya rūpaṃ jānīyā paramaṃ prakṛtirvapuḥ || 5-5 ||

prānyāṃ śiraḥ samākhyātaṃ vāhudakṣiṇasaumyayāḥ |

38a) udaraṃ kuṇḍamityuktaṃ yoniḥ pādau tu paścimaḥ || 5-6 ||

sarvasiddhikaraṃ kuṃḍaṃ caturasramudīritam |

sarvādhikālikaṃ kuṇḍaṃ sarvadaṃ caturasrakam || 5-7 ||

catu * sraṃ maheśāni sarvakarmāṇi sādhayat |


hastaniyamoyayā ||

gautamīya ||


rathādi dolikā caiva poṭaṃ śakaṭameva ca || 5-8 ||

mānāṅgulena karttavyaṃ nānyanāpi kadācana |

muṣṭarātripramānāni yat kiṃcit kathitāni ca || 5-9 ||

yajamānasya karttavyaṃ nānyasyāpi kadācana |

mānakriyā yā nuktā yā manuktamānakarttari || 5-10 ||

mānakṛdyayamānaḥ syād viṃduṣāmeva nirṇayaḥ |

caturviṃśatyaṃgulāḍhyaṃ hastaṃ tantrapidoviduḥ || 5-11 ||

karttu dakṣiṇahastasya madhyamāṅguliparvaṇaḥ |

madhyasya dairghyamānena mānāṅgulamudāhṛtam || 5-12 ||


ye je māmāsaṃnidhau ||


yavānāṃ taṃḍulairekamaṃgulaṃ cāṣṭabhirbhavet |


tathā coktaṃ sārasaṃgrahe ||


sasyāṅgulo yastasyā śoyavāṣṭama udāhṛtaḥ || 5-13 ||

tilastat paṃcamāṃśaḥ syāt ṣaṣṭhomāṣastilasya ca |

tāvadaṃśaśca nīvāre tasya ṣaṣṭho lavopi vā || 5-14 ||

sarvatravaṃhitaṃ grāhyamaṅgulaṃ madhyamāṅgule |

madhyamāyā madhyamaparvamānaṃ caturviṃśatikaraḥ || 5-15 ||

adīrghayojitai hastaiścaturviṃśatilaṅgulaiḥ |

38b) aṣṭabhistai yavaijyeṣṭhaṃ madhyamaṃ saptabhiryavaiḥ || 5-16 ||

kaniṣṭhaṃ ṣadbhirudiṣṭaṃ hastaṃ ca munisattama |


tathā ca vaśiṣṭhapaṃcarātram ||

śāradāyāṃ ca ||


muṣṭimātramitaṃ kuṇḍaṃ śatārddhe paricakṣate || 5-17 ||

śatahomena trimātraṃ hastamātraṃ sahasrake |


śrībhaviṣyottare ||


sahasratvethahotavya kuryāt kuṇḍaṃ kalātmakam || 5-18 ||

dvihastamayutaṃ tacca lakṣahome catuṣkaram |


tathā ca jñānalalitāyām ||


vedalakṣaṃ ṣaṭkare ca daśalakṣaṃ karāṣṭake || 5-19 ||


daśahastaṃ tu koṭyāṃ vai hastasaṃkhyā vyavasthitā iti ||

catuśraṃ catuṣkoṇaṃ dvihastādike yāmalaḥ ||


pūrvapūrvasya kuṃḍasya koṇasūtreṇanirmitam |

uttarottarakuṇḍānāṃ mānaṃ tatparikīrttanam || 5-20 ||


pūrvapūrvahastadvihastādimitasya catuhastatvāpatteḥ ||

anyacoktaṃ yāmale ||


dvihastamayute pakṣe caturhastamudīritam |


yāvat kuṇḍasya vistāro ||


daśalakṣe tu ṣaḍhastaṃ koṭyāmastakaraṃ smṛtam || 5-21 ||


athavā ||


ekahastamitaṃ kuṇḍam ekalakṣaṃ vidhīyate |

lakṣāṇāṃ daśakaṃ yāvat āvardhastena vaddhayet || 5-22 ||

daśahastamitaṃ kuṇḍaṃ koṭihome vidhīyate |

iti vaśiṣṭapaṃcarātre kuṇḍasvarūpamāha || 5-23 ||

ekahastapramāṇaṃ tu caturadyamudīritam |

39a) yāvāt kuṃḍasya vistāraḥ khananaṃ tāvadīṣyate || 5-24 ||

kuṇḍānāṃ yā dṛśaṃ rūpaṃ mekhalāyāśca tādṛśam |


catuśraṃ catuṣkoṇamityarthaḥ ||

atra paṃcamekhalā kecid ekamekhalamātraṃ sarvamaṃjasaṃ trimekhalamātraṃ 
vidhīyate |

taduktaṃ siddhāntaśekhare |


pañcamekhalakaṃ kuryāt tantravit sarvakuṇḍake || 5-25 ||

mekhalā tritayaṃ cātha dvitīyaṃ caikameva vā |

vedāgninayanacchedamaṃgulaṃ dvitayaṃ kramāt || 5-26 ||


taduktaṃ kriyāsāre ||


pradhānamekhale chedaṃ uktamatra na cāṅgulam |

tadvāhyamekhalā chedaṃ paṃcāṅgulamiti smṛtam || 5-27 ||


tadvāhya mekhalāchedaṃ marddhāṅgulisu saṃmmitā 
magulaṃ dvitayakramāt |


caturthatryaṅgulachedo mekhalā dvitayasya ca |


iti kecit ||


kuṇḍānāṃ mekhalāstisro muktimātreḥ tutāḥ kramāt || 5-28 ||

ucchadāyāmato jñayā dvyakādvāṅgulisammitāḥ |

aritramātre kuṇḍesyu snāstidvyakāṅgulātmikāḥ || 5-29 ||

ekahastamite kuṇḍa vedāgninayanāṅgulāḥ |

kuṇḍadvihaste tājñayā rasavaṃdaguṇāṅgulāḥ || 5-30 ||

catuhasta tu kuṇḍe tā vasutarkayugāṅgulāḥ |

mekhalānāṃ bhavedantaḥ paritonemiraṃgulā || 5-31 ||


mekhalā brahmacāri mekhalāvat ||

39b) kuṇḍavṛṣṭitā mṛdgraṭitāḥ ||

tāśca khādateśād vājya ekāṅgularūpakaṃṭhaṃ parityajyo cchrāyeśa 
vistāreṇa ityādi krameṇavedādyaṅgulāḥ |

etadviparītāḥ tantrāntaroktāpya vahāravirūddhā vedāścatvāraḥ agnayastrayaḥ 
|

nayane dve pasāḥ? dagulāḥ ||

trayaḥ vasutarkayugāḥ ||

aṣṭaṣaṭcatvāri ||

tathā ca kālottare ||


khātādekāṅgulaṃ tyaktvā mekhalānāṃ vidhirbhave |

ekahastasya kuṇḍasya varddhayet tāṃ kramāt sudhīḥ || 5-32 ||

daśahastāṃ tu madhye ṣā maddhāṅgulavaśā pṛthak |

vistāro chedato jñeyā mekhalā sarvato vudhaiḥ || 5-33 ||


ekakuṇḍasya paścimadirkarttavyatāmāha ||

mahādānanirṇaya ||


bhuktau muktau tathā tuṣṭau jīrṇodvāre tathaiva ca |

sadāhome tathā śāntau ekaṃ varuṇadiggajam || 5-34 ||


sāradātilake ||


hoturagre yonirā sā muparyaśvatthapatravat |


āsāṃ mekhalā nāma śvatthapatravadityena caturaṅgulavistṛtamūlā 
yathoktakramamekāṃgulyaṃ taṃ saṃkucitavistārāḥ |

taduktaṃ vaśiṣṭhasaṃhitāyām ||


prathame mekhale yoni kuṇḍaṣṭohoturagrataḥ || 5-35 ||

kuyādvīpośca vṛttāntaḥ kuṇḍaṃ tat sarvalakṣaṇam |

40a) yoniḥ ṣaḍaṃgulā yā sā catulaṃgulavistṛtā || 5-36 ||

unnatardvyaṃgulā proktā kuḍe hastamite vudhaiḥ |

netravedāṅgulocchadaṃ vistārānnatiratra vai || 5-37 ||

muktyāratrihastamātre yonistadvadudīritā |

dvihastādiṣu kuṇḍeṣu yonisaṃvarddhayet kramāt || 5-38 ||

ekakāṅgulimānena tathā nābhiṃ ca varddhayed iti |

muṣṭāratryaikahastānāṃ kuṇḍānāṃ yonirīritā || 5-39 ||

ṣaṭcatuṣṭhāṃgulāyām avistārānnatiśālinī |

ekāṅgulaṃ tu yonyagraṃ kuryādīṣadadhomukham || 5-40 ||


ṣaḍaṃguladīrghā catulaṅgulavistīrṇā dvāṅguloccatyarthaḥ |

atraikavacano pāṃ tatvād etat parimānakathanaṃ muṣṭāratryaikaviṣaye midam ||


ekakāṅgulato yoniṃ kuṇḍeṣvanyaṣu varddhayet |

yava dvayakremeṇaiva yonyagramapi varddhayet || 5-41 ||


iti hastādiviṣayam ||

atra viśeṣakābhāvād āyāmavistāron natiṣvavivṛddhirititattvam ||

tathā ca yajñapārśve ||


mekhalānāṃ ca bhisthānaṃ sthalamityavidhīyate |

caturaśre tu nālaṃ syā yonyā madhyāsaraṃdhrakam || 5-42 ||

sthūlamūlā tu sūkṣmāgrā unnāsābhyānmanoharā |

vāhyasthāṃ mekhalāṃ vāhyasthātamārabhyakārayet || 5-43 ||


saraṃdhrakamubhayatra saṃvadhyate ||

uktaṃ ca bhāskarācāryaḥ |

yonau nālaṃ saraṃdhraka ||

40b) iti rudrayāmale ||


yonyā sadhyavilaṃ kuryā tadājyagrāhisaṃjñitam |

sthalādārabhya nālaṃ syāt yonyā madhya sarandhrakam || 5-44 ||

nārpayet koṇakuṇḍeṣu yoniṃ tāṃ tantravittamaḥ |


sthalāṃ mekhalānāṃ raṃbhasthalāt ||

athāvāhyasthālyadhikaraṇasthalā dvetyarthaḥ ||

saraṃdhrakaṃ adṛṣṭārthakamiti kecit |

tathā ca yāmale ||


nālayokhalayormadhya paridhasthāpanāya ca || 5-45 ||

randhraṃ kuryān tathā vidvān dvitīye mekharo pari |


iti śruteḥ ||


iti vacanaṃ nālasya nasarandhratā vodhakam || 5-46 ||


kintu nālamekhaloḥ paridhimāha ||

tat saṃyogapariśiṣṭhe ||


vahumātrā paridhiya ṛjavaḥ satṛcobraṇā |

trayobhavantya śīrṇāgrā ekaikāṃ tu caturdiśam || 5-47 ||

tataḥ kuṇḍānurūpaṃ vā mānasasyā nigadyate |

prāgagrā āhataḥ paścād udagagramathāparam || 5-48 ||

nyaset paridhimanyecced udagagraḥ sa pūrvataḥ |

abraṇācchidrarahitā abhitaḥ agre pārśvadvaya || 5-49 ||


dakṣiṇa-uttarataḥ paścāt paścimeḥ ||

udagagramuttarāgram ||

trailokyasāra ||


kuṃbhadvayasamāyuktā aśvatthadalavannataḥ |

aṃguṣṭhamekhalā yuktā madhyacāryasthitistathā || 5-50 ||


kumbhadvayasamāyuktā gajakumbhākāramūladeśayuktā natā nasrā 
aṃguṣṭhamekhalā yuktā aṃguṣṭhamitamūddhati 
tayutā mekhalā veṣṭhanayuktā tathā dvahastagalitājyaṣṭhintyā kuṃḍe utpāto 
bhavatītyarthaḥ ||

41a) ata eva svāyaṃbhuve ||

aṃguṣṭhamānauṣṭakaṇṭhā kāryāśvatthadalākṛtiḥ | 
[+aṃguṣṭhamānauṣṭakaṇṭhā kāryāśvatthadalākṛtiḥ |]

aṃguṣṭhamānau kaṇṭhe yasmāt sā tathā oṣṭho māgram |

tathā ca paṃcarātre ||


kalpayedantareṇābhiṃ kuṇḍasyāmbujasaṃnnibhām |

muṣṭiratryaikahastānāṃ nābhirucchedavistṛtā || 5-51 ||

netravedāṅgulāpetā kuṃḍe ṣvanyeṣu varddhayet |

yavadvayakrameṇaiva nābhiṃ pṛthagudāradhiḥ || 5-52 ||

nābhiṃ kṣatraṃ tridhā kṛtvā madhyakurvīta kanyakām |

vahiraṃśadvaye nāṣṭau patrāṇi parikalpayet || 5-53 ||

strukmdruvau saṃpravakṣyāmi mantrīṇāṃ hitakāmyayā |

struṃvaṃ prakalpayatyāge vakṣyamānena vatmanā || 5-54 ||

śrīparṇiśiṃ śapākṣīriśākhoṣvekatamaṃ guruḥ |


śiṣyapāśamiśīrakha iti kecit gṛhītvā vibhajerddhastamātraṃ ṣaṭtriṃśatā 
punaḥ |


viśatyaṃśai bhaved daṇḍo vedītairaṣṭabhirbhavet || 5-55 ||

ekāṃśena mitaḥ kaṃṭhaḥ saptabhāgamitaṃ mukham |


vedī madhyavidhātavyā tryaṃśemavistāraḥ kaṇṭhasya parikīrttitaḥ |


mukhaṃ kaṇṭhasamānaṃ syāt mukhe mārgaṃ prakalpayet || 5-56 ||

kaniṣṭhāṃgulimānena sarpiṣo nirgamāya ca |

41b) vedīmadhyavidhātavyā bhāgenaikena karṇikā || 5-57 ||

vidadhītavahistasyā ekāṃśe nābhito vaṭam |

tasya khātaṃ tribhirbhāgai vṛttamaddhāśato va hi || 5-58 ||

aṃśe naikena parito dalāni parikalpayet |


avataṃgattam ||


mekhalāmukhavedyāḥ syā paritorddhāṃśamānataḥ || 5-59 ||

daṃdumūlāgrayoḥ kuṃbhau guṇavedāṅgulaiḥ kramāt |

gaṃḍīyugaṃ samāṃsaiḥ syād daṃḍasmānāhṛ īritaḥ || 5-60 ||

ṣadgiraṃśaiḥ pṛṣṭhabhāgo vedyāḥ kumākṛtirbhavet |

haṃsasya vā hastino vā pakṣiṇo vā mukhaṃ likhet || 5-61 ||

mukhasya pṛṣṭhabhāge syā saṃproktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃstu caḥ |


stuvaścaturviṃśatibhibhāge rocayat stuvam ||


dvāviṃśatyādaṃdamānaṃ vaṃśeretasya kīrtitam || 5-62 ||


caturviṃśai cānāhaḥ karṣājyagrāhitāśaraḥ ||

ānāhodīrghaḥ karṣastolakadvayam ||


aṃśadvayena vilikhetyeke mṛge padākṛti |

daṇḍamūlāgrayorgaṇḍī bhavet kaṃkaṇabhūṣitā || 5-63 ||

stuvaṇyavidhirākhyātaḥ sarvatantrānusārataḥ |


gaṇḍīkaṃkaṇakuṃbhayo nityamanaḥ ||

yathoktasruksruvā bhāve tulāghavabhaṭṭavṛtasaṃhitāyām |


pālāśapatranicchitre rucire sruk pruvau smṛtau || 5-64 ||


atha kuṇḍadoṣā nā haviśvakarmā ||


42a) khātādhike bhaved rogī hīne dhenudhanakṣayaḥ |

vakrakuṇḍeṣu santāpo malaṇaṃ chinnamekhale || 5-65 ||

mekhelā lahite śoko jyadhike vittasaṃkṣayaḥ |

bhāryā vināśakaṃ kuṇḍaṃ proktaṃ yonyā vinākṛtam || 5-66 ||

apadyaṃ dhvaṃsanaṃ proktaṃ kuṇḍa yat kaṇṭhavarjitam |


āta eva vaśiṣṭhasaṃhitāyām ||


tasmāt samyak pariṣyaivaṃ karttavyaṃ śubhavedikām || 5-67 ||

hastamātraṃ sthaṃḍilaṃ vāsaṃ kṣipte homakarmaṇi |


kriyāsāre ||


kuṇḍamevaṃ vidhiṃ nasyā sthaṇḍilaṃ vāsamāśrayat || 5-68 ||


śāradātilake ||

nityaṃ naimittīkaṃ kāmyaṃ sthaṃḍile vāsamācaret |

hastamātraṃ tu tat kuryā caturaśraṃ samantataḥ || 5-69 ||


mahākapilapaṃcarātre ||


saṃkhyānukto śataṃ śāstraṃ sahasraṃ vā jayādiṣu |


home svāhāṃ tato māha yajñapārśve ||


ādāya dakṣiṇe pāṇau sravatirmadhuraṃ haviḥ || 5-70 ||

prāṅmukho vahni jāyā te juhuyān yubjapāṇinā |


timadhuraṃ ghṛtamadhuśarkarātmakam |

vahnijāyā svāhā ||


mantreṇāṃkārapūtena svāhānte ca vicakṣaṇaḥ || 5-71 ||

svāhā vasāne juhuyād vyāyen vai mantradevatā |


miti smṛteḥ ||

svāhāntamantrasvāhāntaraṃ nimīṣaduti mantratantraprakāśaḥ ||


namonte na namo dadyāt svāhānte dviṭhameva ca || 5-72 ||

pūjāyāmāhutau vāpi sarvatrāyaṃ vidhiḥ śive |


42b) dviṭha svāhā ||

ṭhakāreṇa lipisāmyād vindurucyate |

tesya dvitvaṃ tena visargaḥ ||

sa ca śakte rūpaḥ tena viśabdenāgniśaktiḥ svāhokteti rāghavabhaṭṭa te vastuḥ ||

atra tu svāhā śabdaḥ svīyadravyatyāgathakaḥ ||

yathā || 5-upaviṣṭahomaḥ svāhākārapradhānā juhota paṭhati hariśarmadhṛtam ||

svāhākāreṇa svāheti padena pradhānaṃ tyāgo yā sutāstathā |

ata eva svāhākārasya pradhānārthatvārddhaviratnyāgasyāmnāya siddhai 
naivakṛtasya dvitīyasyā snānamanarthakaṃ syād iti bhaṭṭaḥ bhāṣyam ||

āsnānaṃ punaḥ pāṭhaṃ prāṇānārthaṃ tu tatra vai yatra mantrasthaṃ nāmni 
caturthāntetā ato jayatītyādau namaḥ svāhā vā punardīyate iti nyubjapānineti 
sākṣyaṃ tarīyam ||

śāṣāntaroktam ||


uttāne naiva hastena aṃgudvāgrena pīḍitam || 5-73 ||

sahitāṅgulipāṇistu vāgyato juhuyād iti |

mahādānanirṇaye dhṛta gobhilīyavacanāt || 5-74 ||


etad vacanaṃ pariśāṣṭiyamiti mādhavācāryaḥ ||

śrābhaviṣya ||


āhutiṃstu ghṛtādīnāṃ stuveṇādho mukhena tu |

huneti lājyāhutistu daivanontānapāṇinā || 5-75 ||


evaṃ ca paṃcāṅgulaṃ tyaktvā śaṃkhamudrayā dhāryāḥ ||

paṃcāṅgulānvahistyaktvā dhārayachaṃkhamudrayeti vācaspati ||

43a) miśrakṛta mahādānanirṇayadhṛtavacanāt ||

agnernāmāni ca gobhila kṛtā gṛhārthasārasaṃgrahaḥ ||


laukike pāvako hyagniḥ prathamaḥ parikīrttitaḥ |

agnistu māruto nāma garbhādhāne vidhīyate || 5-76 ||

pūṃsavane candramāśca śu *?karmaṇiśobhanaḥ |

sīmanto maṃgale nāsaḥ prāgalbhājātakarmaṇi || 5-77 ||

nāgnisyāt pārthivā hyagniḥ prā *?ne ca śucistathā |

satyanāmāthacūḍāyāṃ bratādeva samudbhavaḥ || 5-78 ||

godāne sūryanāmā ca keśāntyajyagnirucyate |

vaiśvānarāvisarge ca vivāhe yojakaḥ smṛtaḥ || 5-79 ||


godāne godānākhyasaṃskāra ||


caturthāṃ tu śikhīnāma dhṛtiragnistathā pare |


apare dhṛti homādau ||


prāyaścittai vidhuścaiva pākayajñe tu sāhasaḥ || 5-80 ||


prāyaścitte prāyaścittātmakamahāvyāhṛti homādau pākayajñapākajahoma ||

vṛṣotsargagṛhapratiṣthā homādau ||


lakṣahome ca vahniḥ syāt koṭihome hutāśanaḥ |

pūrṇāhutyāṃ mṛḍo nāmaṇāntikevaladaḥ sadā || 5-81 ||

pa.uṣṭike valadaścaiva krodhāgniścābhicārikaḥ |

koṣṭhe bhujaṭharonāma kravyādo mṛtabhakṣyaṇeḥ || 5-82 ||


mṛtadāhe ||

guṇaviṣṇudhṛtā smṛtirapi ||


sarvataḥ pāṇipādāntaḥ sarvatokṣiśiromukhaḥ |

43b) viśvarūpo mahānagniḥ praṇītaḥ sarvakarmasu || 5-83 ||


āhūyatyādikamatravodhavyam ||

agniṃ praṇayanānantaraṃ sarvatretnyasyapāṭho yujyate 
praṇīta iti mantraliṅgāt ||

anyathā sthāpanāmantrarametabhidhānaṃ vyarthaṃ smāt |

guṇaviṣṇunāpi śrātiriti ||


āhuya caiva hotavyaṃ yatra yo vihitonalaḥ ||


tathā ||


śubhaṃ pātraṃ tu kāṃsyasyānte nāgni praṇayed budhaḥ || 5-84 ||

tasmā bhāve saṃrāva nana ve nābhimukhaṃ ca tam |


pūrṇāyāṃ viśeṣamāha ||


sāmagānāṃ tu pūrṇohamityādi mūrddhāna dive || 5-85 ||


rātrimiti tu ||

taditarāṇāṃ śāntidīptikāyāṃ vaśiṣṭhaḥ |


aiśānyā māhared bhasmasrucā vātha sruvena vā |

vaṃdānāṃ kārayet tena śiraḥ kaṃṭhāṃsakeṣu ca || 5-86 ||

kalasya paśyati mantreṇa yathānukramayogataḥ |


tathā ||

tathā śāntiṃ prakurvīta avadhāraṇavācanam ||


dakṣiṇāṃ ca pradātavyā grahādīnāṃ visarjanam |

dīkṣā prayogaṃ vakṣyāmi nānātantrānusārataḥ || 5-87 ||



atha kṛtanityakṛtyo guruḥ ||

śubhalagne tu ṣārāṅgārarahitabhūmau dīkṣāṅgayāgamantrapanimāṇaṃ 
kuryāt |

tato dīkṣā pūrvadine maṇḍamadhyayathoktā vedinimāyatadupari 
sarvatobhadramaṇḍalaṃ kuryāt |

maṇḍapamaṃḍalayonimāṇaṃ anyatra pamāṇe nusaṃdheyam ||

44a) tato dīkṣādivase prātaḥ kṛta nitya kṛtyo gurumaṃḍapadvāramāgatya 
vahirūparipya tatra prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vā upaviśet ||

śuciḥ pūrvamupoṣitaḥ śiṣyopi tatrāgatya svastivācana pūrvakaṃ saṃkalpaṃ 
kṛtvā guruṃ vādibhiḥ saṃpūjya vṛṇuyāt |

saṃkalpo yathā ||

viṣṇo oṃ adyatyādi amuka gotrāmukadevaśarmā 
caturvargaphalaprāptikāmomukagotraṃ śrīamukadevaśarmāṇaṃ 
śrīamukadevatāyāḥ yadakṣaramantradaiśikatve naibhiḥ pādyādibhirabhyarcya (-
bhyacya) bhagavatamahaṃ vṛṇe oṃ vṛtosmīti prativacanam tataḥ śiṣyo yathā 
vihitaṃ dīkṣākarmakurvīti vadet ||

yathā jñānataḥ karaṇa vā nīti guruḥ tataḥ kṛtāṃjaliḥ śiṣyo bhagavan 
paramātmarūpiṇ paramakāruṇikagurosakalapuruṣārthasādhanaṃ 
mantradīkṣābhiṣekapūrvakaṃ māṃ prasādayatyuktvā gurorājñayā sahasra 
gāyatrīṃ japan vinayī gurordakṣijapet ||

tato guruḥ sāmānyārghaṃ sthāpayet ||

tedyathā ||

svavāme trikoṇamaṃḍalaṃ kṛtvā ādhāraṃ taduparisaṃsthāpya maṃ 
vahnimaṇḍalāya daśakalātmane nama |

ityādhāraṃ saṃpūjya phaditi arghapātraṃ prakṣālya tadupari saṃsthāpya aṃ 
sūryamaṃḍalāya dvādaśakalātmane namaḥ ||

44b) iti pātraṃ saṃpūjya nama iti śuddhajalenāpūye ||

oṃ somamaṇḍalāya ṣoḍaśakalātmanenama iti pūrvavat ||

tasmin jale ||

oṃ gaṃge ca yamune caiva tyādi aṃkuśamudrayā tīrthamāvāhya tatra 
praṇanagaṃdhākṣatānikṣipya

mūlamantramaṣṭadhā japtvā dhenumudrāṃ pradarśayet |

evaṃ prakāranānyadapi pātraṃ sthāpayet ||

phaditi mantreṇa dvāraṃ saṃprokṣya dvārapūjāṃ saṃkuryāt ||

tadyathā ||

dvārasyordhvaśākhāyāṃ madhyavighnāye namaḥ ||

dakṣiṇe mahālakṣme? tadvāme sarasvatyai dvāradakṣiṇe vighneśāya gaṃgāyai 
dvāravāme kṣetreśāyai yamunāyai namaḥ ||

dehalyāṃ astrāye namaḥ ||

evaṃ pratidvāraṃ pūjāṃ kuryāt |

tatoṅgasaṃkocya dakṣiṇapādaḥ purāsaraṃ gṛhaṃ praviśet ||

tataḥ paṃcagavyasāmānyārghatoyābhyā gṛhā *? taraṃ saṃprokṣya 
saṃskāracatuṣṭayaṃ kuryāt |

yathā mūlamantrena gṛhaṃ vīkṣyat |

phaḍiti gṛhaṃ prokṣayet |

evaṃ dabhestāḍayet ||

tataḥ pūjāsthale raktakaṃ valāsanamāstīryadhṛtvā paṭhet ||

merupṛṣṭa ṛṣiriti pūrvalikhitameva 

pṛthvi tvayā dhṛtā loketi ca paṭhitvā hrī/ śrī/ ādhāraśaktikamalāsanāya 
namaḥ ||

45a) ityāsanāye nama ityāsanaṃ pūsajya tadupari svastikādikrameṇopraviśet ||

tataḥ siddhārthākṣatamādāya apasarpaṃ tu yad bhūtā 

iti paṭhitvā strāya phaṭ ityena nokṣaṃ tānvikīryapārṇighātana bhaumān 
vighnon naṃguṣṭhatarjanībhyāmāsphoṭenāntarikṣa gātvālayā 
samudaṃritavakramūrdhvadṛṣṭā divyāniti trividhavighnānutsārya naiṛtyāṃ 
vāstvīśāye namaḥ ||

brahmaṇe nama iti saṃpūjya bhūtebhya eṣa māṃsabhaktavalirnama ityutsṛjye 
bhūtavaliṃ datvān vikirānvikiret ||

yathā ||

rājā vaṃdanasirddhārtha bhasmadurvākuśākṣatān ekīkṛtya phaḍiti 
saptadhājapan trividhā vikiret ||


astrajaptena dabhāṇāṃ muṣṭināmārjayecchatān |


tānvikirān tathā cāstrāya phaṭ ||

iti mantreṇa kuśamuṣṭināvikirān mārjayet ||

īśānakoṇe varddhanyā āsanāya prakalpayet |

vardhiṇī vikalādhiṣṭhā tṛdevatā tasyā āsanāya īśānakoṇe 
viṣṭharoparivarddhinī saṃjñakaṃ jalādhāraṃ sthāpayet |

tato dakṣiṇapūjādravyāṇi pṛṣṭhakaraprakṣālanārthaṃ pātraṃ sthāpayet |

ghṛtaprajvālitā dīpān paritasthāpayet |

yathā śakti cācāmanādikam ||

45b) tato vāme gurubhyo namaḥ ||

dakṣiṇe gaṇeśāya madhya iṣṭadevatāyai namaḥ ||

iti bhūtaśuddhiṃ kuryāt ||

svāṅge uttānahastau kṛtvā sohamiti mantreṇa pradīpakalikākāraṃ 
hṛdayasthajīvātmānaṃ mūlādhārastha sahasradalakamalakarṇikāntargata 
paramātmani līnaṃ vicintya ||


pādato jānuparyantaṃ caturasraṃ sa cakrakam || 5-88 ||

laṃ yutaṃ pītavarṇaṃ ca bhuvaḥ sthānaṃ vicintayet |

jānorā nābhicandrārdhanibhaṃ padme na lāṃcchitam || 5-89 ||

śuklavarṇaṃ svavījena yutaṃ dhyāyedapāṃsthalam |

nābhitaḥ kaṃṭhaparyantaṃ satrikoṇaṃ saraktam || 5-90 ||

sasvastikaṃ svavījena yuktaṃ vahnestu maṇḍalam |

kaṇṭhādbhrūmadhyaparyantaṃ kṛṣṇavāyostu maṇḍalam || 5-91 ||

ṣaṭkoṇaṃ viṃdubhiḥ ṣadbhiyuktaṃ vījena cintayet |

bhrumadhyād brahmaraṃdhrāntaṃ cattulaṃ dhvajalāṃcchitam || 5-92 ||

dhūmravarṇaṃ svavījena yuktaṃ dhyāyen nabhasthalam |


pṛthivyālaṃ vījajalasya vaṃ agneḥ raṃ vāyoḥ yaṃ ākāsya haṃ evaṃ 
dhyātvā tatastāni bhūtāni pravilāpayet ||


pṛthvīmapsu ca tau vahno vahniṃ vāyosamīraṇam || 5-93 ||

pravilāpya tathā kāśe ākāśaṃ prakṛtau tataḥ |

aparabrahmarūpāṃ tāṃ māyāśaktiṃ parātmani || 5-94 ||


iti samastadehādisrapaṃcaparamātmani 


pravilāpya puruṣākāra bhāganādibhavasaṃcitam |


46a) brahmahatyā śiraskaṃ ca svarṇasteya bhujadvayamiti pūrvaṃ likhitamiti ||

vāmakukṣau vicintayed iti tāvat |

tata iḍāvaktre vāmenasi dhūmravarṇaṃ yamiti vāyūvījaṃ ṣoḍaśavāraṃ 
japan |

vāmanāsāpuṭena vāyuṃ nādehamāpurya punaryamiti catuḥṣaṣṭivāraṃ japan 
kuṃbhakaṃ dukhanadehaṃ saṃsoṣya punaryamiti dvātriṃśadvāraṃ japan 
dakṣinanāsāpuṭena dehamāpuryacatuḥṣaṣṭivārajaptena kuṃbhakaṃ kurcan tad 
bhasmībhūtamiti vicintya ramiti dvātrīṃśadvāraṃ japan |

cāmatā sa yā bhasmanā sahavāyuṃ recayet |

tataḥ ṭhamiti caṃdravījaṃ śudraṃ prakṛtyātmakaṃ vāmanāsāpuṭena 
puruṣātmakaṃ lalāṭacaṃdraṃ ṣoḍaśavāraṃ japannayet ||

tato vamiti catuḥṣaṣṭhivāraṃ japan lalāṭacaṃdrādādikṣāntām 
akṣaramayīvṛṣṭiṃ nipātyatayā devo pāsanayogyaṃ sakalavapuḥ kurvan laṃ iti 
dvātriṃśadvāraṃ japan dakṣināsāpuṭena vāyuṃ recayet ||


punarutpādaya dehaṃ pavitraṃ paramātmanaḥ || 5-95 ||

parabrahmātmikā māyā prakṛtimātṛkā parāḥ |

ajāyata jaganmāturākāśaṃ nabhaśonilaḥ || 5-96 ||

samīranādabhūdvahni vahnerāpastato mahī |

seyamebhyopibhūtebhyastejorūpaṃ karevaram || 5-97 ||

46b) devatārādhane yogyam utpannamiti bhāvayet |

tasmin dehe parātmānaṃ sarvajñaḥ śarvaśaktimān || 5-98 ||

samastadevatārūpaḥ sarvamantramayaḥ śubhaḥ |

ātmarūpena dehasve jīvabhāvena tiṣṭhati || 5-99 ||

itya va bhāvanāmukhyā bhūtaśuddhirihoditā |


nāsāputauddhṛtvā vāyumavaḥṣṭhatyahaṃsaḥ 

sohaṃ avatara 2 śivapadājīvasuṣumanāmathena praviśamūlaśṛṅgāramullasa 2 
jvala 2 prajvala 2 haṃsaḥ sohaṃ svāhā ||

tataḥ pṛthivyādīnitattvāni yathā sthāne sthāpayitvā hṛpadme devatāsamānīya 
hṛdihastaṃ dattvā aṃ ā/ hrī/ kro/ ha/saḥ mamaprāṇāḥ ||

evaṃ mamajīva ihasthitaḥ ||

ciraṃ tiṣṭhantu svāhā ||

etat karaṇāsaktā nāme tat pāṭhamātreṇaiva bhūtaśuddhiḥ syāt |

iti bhūtaśuddhiḥ ||

tataḥ prāṇāyāmaṃ kuryāt |

dakṣiṇā sā puṭena vāyuṃ recayet ||

ityekaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ ||

evaṃ ṣoḍaśavāraṃ dvādaśavāraṃ vāratrayaṃ vā kuryāt ||

tataḥ ṛṣyādi nyāsaṃ kuryāt ||


ātmano mūrdhni vadane hṛdaye ca yathākramam || 5-100 ||


mūdhni amuka ṛṣaye namaḥ mukhe evaṃ ghaṃdase namaḥ ||

47a) hṛdidevatāyai namaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ amukasiddhyarthe viniyogaḥ ||

mātṛkā nyāsaḥ ||

kvacidapi mantreṇa śuyate tathāpya vaśyaṃ karttavyaḥ ||


japādyaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ vinyāsena vinālipeḥ |

kṛtatanniṣphalaṃ vidyān tasmāt pūrvaṃ lipiṃ nyaset || 5-101 ||


tato mātṛkānyāsaṃ kuryāt |


vāgbhavādyā vāk pradāsyā tritārādyā tu kāmadā |

śrīvījādyā mahaiśvaryaṃ dāyitī mātṛkā smṛtā || 5-102 ||


vāgbhavaḥ ai/ tritārā ai/ hrī/ śrī/ iti vījatrayaṃ śrī/ vījaṃ śrī/ iti vījam ||

taduktaṃ sārasaṃgrahe ||


manasā vān nyase nyāsāt kusumaivāthavā nyaset |

aṃguṣṭhānāmikā yogā nyased vā sarvakarmasu || 5-103 ||

dharmaviddakṣiṇaṃ hastamadhonābhernayo japet |


śrībrahmā ṛṣi gāyatrī cchaṃdaḥ mātṛkā sarasvati devatā phalo vījāni svarāḥ 
śaktayaḥ mātṛkā nyāsa viniyogaḥ ||

sirasi brahma ṛṣaye namaḥ ||

mukhe gāyatrī chaṃdase namaḥ ||

hṛdi mātṛkānyā * sarasvatī devatāyai namaḥ ||

guhyephalado?vījebhyo namaḥ |

pādayoḥ svarebhyaḥ śaktibhyo namaḥ ||

kṛtāṃjalimātṛkā nyāse viniyoga ||

pārśvaḥ yoḥ pṛsthatau (?) nābhau jaṭhare hṛdayāṃsake || 5-105 ||

47b) kakudyaṃse ca hṛt pūrvaṃ pāṇipādayugeṣvapi |

48a) śrī/ śa/ astyātmani hṛdayādidakṣiṇakare ||

tato mantraṃ nyāsaṃ kuryāt ||

atha karanyāsaḥ |

aṃguṣṭhābhyāmiti karatarapṛṣṭheyorastrāya phaḍiti tavat ||

karanyāsa ||

evaṃ hṛdayādyārabhyaśikhāyaiṣaṭ tāvaṭ ||

oṃ ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ || kūrmāya ||

anantāya || pṛthivyau ||

punarhṛdaye ||

48b) oṃ saṃvinnārāye namaḥ || oṃ sarvatattvātmakapadāye ||

||

iti nyāsaṃ kṛtvā hṛtpadmadevatāṃ dhyātvā mānasopacārai 
rārādhyamudrāṃ darśayitvā namaskṛtyārghapātraṃ sthāpayet ||

tadyathā tattvasāgarasaṃhitāyām ||

ādhāraṃ tripadaṃ prāhuḥ ṣaṭpadaṃ vā catuṣpadaṃ athavā vartturākāraṃ 
kuryā devī manoharam ||


svarṇarūpyaśilākūrma kapālāmbumṛṇamayam |

nālikelaṃ ca śaṃkhaṃ ca muktāśuktisamudbhavam || 5-108 ||

suvarṇaraupyatāmuṇi? sarvasiddhikarāṇi ca |

nālikelaṃ ca vaśyādā vabhicāra ca kurmajam |

49a) kapālalāvupātrāṇi yogaṃ siddhikarāṇi ca |

puṇyavṛkṣajapātrāṇi sarvapāpaharāṇi ca || 5-112 ||

ukte preteṣu deveśi pātramekaṃ prakalpayet |

ātmadevatayo madhye pātrāṇi sthāpayed vudhaḥ || 5-113 ||

guropātraṃ ghaṭasyānte tadante bhogapātrakam |

tadante śaktipātraṃ ca yoginīnāṃ tadantike || 5-114 ||

tadante vīrapātraṃ ca valeḥ pātraṃ tadantike |

pādyamācamanīyaṃ ca pātrāṇi sthāpayed budhaḥ || 5-115 ||

uttamaṃ navapātrāṇi saptapātrāṇi madhyamam |

paṃcapātrapakṣe ||

paṃcapātreṣu ||
