pādyamācamanīyaṃ ca valeḥ pātrā surārcane || 5-119 ||


tatorghapātrā sthāpanam ||

svavāme trikonamaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā taduparitripadikāmānopya astrāya phaḍiti 
śaṃkhaṃ proṣya tadupari saṃsthāpya mūlamantreṇa japan śuddhajalamāpūrya 
maṃ vahnimaṇḍalāya tyāgni sūryasomānāṃ pūrvaṃ likhitemiti jalaṃ 
saṃpūjya ||

hrā/ hṛdayāya namaḥ || hrī/ śirase namaḥ || svāhā ||

49b) hrū/ śikhāyai vaṣaṭ || hrai/ kavacāya hum ||

hrau/ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ || hu/ astrāya phaṭ ||

ṣaḍaṃgamantrairapi saṃpūjya hastābhyāṃ ācchādya tasmin jale mūla ||

mantrasastradhā japtvāstreṇa saṃrakṣya kavacenāvaguṃṭhā vamiti 
dhenumudrayāmṛtī kṛtya tatra gaṃdhapuṣpākṣataṃ nikṣipet ||

evaṃ prakāreṇa tasya dakṣiṇe prokṣaṇīpātraṃ saṃsthāpya kiṃcid arghajalaṃ 
gṛhītvā prokṣaṇījale nikṣipyate nodakena pūjopakaraṇaṃ 
yāgamantrapaṃcātyukṣet |

evaṃ arghapātrasya vāme pādyapātraṃ ācamanīyapātraṃ sthāpayet ||

ityarghaṃ saṃsthāpya ghaṭasthāpanaṃ kuryāt |

tadyathā ||

prathamato maṃḍalaṃ gaṃdhādibhiḥ saṃpūjya śālibhiḥ 
karṇikāmadhyatemāyūryatedupari kuśānārya tadupari sākṣatāṃ vistāraṃ 
saṃsthāpya tadupari ghaṭaṃ astrāya phaḍiti prakṣālitākṣaraṃ karpūrādi 
sudhupitaṃ nīlaṃdhraṃ sumanoharaṃ yathokta triguṇatantubhiveṣṭitaṃ 
cānādibhilalaṃkṛtaṃ puṣpamālyāṃ lṃkṛta grīvadūrvākṣatasamanvit 
navaratnodalaṃ vilomamātṛkāmūlamantraṃ ca japen 
kṣīradrumaparāsatvogbhavakaṣāya yathokta gaṃdhāṣṭakayuktaiḥ 
sugandhijalairāpuritāntaraṃ aśvatthodumvarapthakṣenyagrodhāsrā 
nāmaparājitā latā veṣṭitamavapallavamukhaṃ mukhadattasaphalayaveśarāvaṃ 
sūkṣmavastrayugma veṣṭitaṃ oṃkāramurcaram bhūrasītyādibhi vaidikamantrai 
viṣṭaropari sthāpayet |

50a) tataḥ kuṃbhaṃ kuśāgreṇa pṛṣṭvā prāṇe pratiṣṭhāṃ mantro yathā |


pāśādityakṣarātmānte syodapuṣpaṃ padaṃ punaḥ |

kramāt prāṇā iha prāṇāstathā jīva iha sthitaḥ || 5-120 ||

amukhya sarvendriyāṇi bhūyo mukhyapadaṃ vadet |

vāṅmanonayanaśrotraghrāṇaprāṇapadānyatha || 5-121 ||

paścād ihāgatya sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhantu ṭha dvayam |

ayaṃ prāṇamanuḥ proktaḥ sarvajīvapradāyakaḥ || 5-122 ||


sarvatra ||

ā/ hrī/ krā/ ha/ saḥ ityante amukhyapadaṃ deyamiti niṣkarṣaḥ || yathā ||

ā/ hrī/ kro/ ha/ saḥ amukadevatāyāḥ prāṇā iha prāṇā ā/ hrī/ miti 
amukadevatā yā jīva iha sthita iti purarevaṃ ā/ hrī/ miti amukadevatā yāḥ 
sarvendriyāṇi ityādi ||

prāṇā ihāgatya sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhantu svāhā ||

iti prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ kṛtvā ||

dinakarakiraṇāruṇakusumāṃjalau svahṛdayā dvahantā sā puṭena devatāṃ 
samānīya mūlena mūrttisaṃkalpya ghaṭe saṃsthāpya saṃsthāpana 
saṃnidhāpana saṃnidhana sakmaṇāvaguṃṭhanāmṛtīkaraṇa paramīkaraṇa 
mudrābhighaṭadevatāmāvāhya tasminnaghaṭe pīṭhān pūjayet ||

oṃ ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ || oṃ kūrmāya ||

oṃ anantāya || oṃ pṛthivyai ||

oṃ sudhāsāgarāya || oṃ ratnadvīpāya ||

oṃ kalpavṛkṣāya || oṃ ratnamaṃḍapāya ||

oṃ ratnavedikāyai || oṃ mahāsiṃhāsanāye namaḥ ||

iti saṃpūjya puṣpaṃ gṛhītvā devatāṃ dhyātvā ghaṭe puṣpaṃ dattvā 
ṣoḍaśopacāraiḥ pūjayet || tadyathā ||

āsanasvāgataṃ cārghyamiti ṣoḍaśopacārān pūrvaṃ likhitam ||

tadukaṃ mānasollāse ||

saṃvuddhyā pradaden mantrī iti saṃvuddhyā saṃvodhanapadena ||

pūjā yathā amukadevate āsanaṃ parikalpyayāminamaḥ ||

svāgataṃ kuśalapraśnam || evam ||

arghaṃ parikalpayāmi svāhā ||

iti mūddhni dadyāt ||

śrī-amukadevate pādyamiti pādayoḥ ||

evaṃ amukadevate ācamanīyamiti mukhe ||

evaṃ madhuparkkaṃ mukhāṃvuje ||

evaṃ punarācamanīyaṃ namaḥ eva snānīyam ||

idaṃ vastraṃ palikalpayāmi namaḥ ||

evaṃ yajñopavītaṃ evaṃ ābharaṇāni evaṃ gaṃdhapuṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ 
parikalpayāmi namaḥ ||

naivedyaṃ pūrataḥ samānīyārghajale nāstrāya phaḍit kāhumityavaguṃṭhā

amukadevate naivedyaṃ parikalpayāmi nama ityutsṛjya amṛto pastaraṇamasi svāhā 
||

jalaṃ gaṃdūṣaṃ dattvā prāṇāyaye svāhā iti praṇavādyaṃ 
paṃcaprāṇāhuti hutvā tasyāḥ ||

51a) svīkāravibhāvyā oṃ amṛtavidhānam asvi svāhā ||

iti punarapośānaṃ dattvā prāṇāya svāhā iti praṇavādyaṃ paṃcaprāṇa

ityarthaḥ ||

etan mantrasya jīvanam ||


51b) mantravarṇān samālikhya tāḍayeccandanāṃbhasā || 5-128 ||

pratyekaṃ vāyunā mantrī tādanaṃ tadudāhṛtam |


pratyekamiti deyamantrasya pratyakṣopariyamiti vāyuvījamurcaran 
caṃdanajalamiśritaṃ dadyād iti mantrasya tāḍanam ||


vilikhya tanmantraṃ mantrī prasūnaiḥ karavīrajaiḥ || 5-129 ||

tanmantrākṣarasaṃ khyātai hanyādyānte navodhanam |


tāmrapaṭṭādāvityarthaḥ ||

yānte negiyakārasmāt eṃ repha tathādharamiti vījena ||

etan mantrasya vodhanam ||


svatantroktavidhānena mantrī mantrasya saṃkhyayā || 5-130 ||

aśvatthapallave mantraṃ abhiṣiṃce viśuddhaya |


svatantrokteti ||

viṅśupakṣe śuklacaṃdane devīpakṣe raktacaṃdane netyarthaḥ ||


saṃcintya manasā mantraṃ jyotimantrena nirdahet || 5-131 ||

mantre malatrayaṃ mantrī vimalīkaraṇaṃ tvidam |


jyotimantreṇa ||

oṃ hrī/ iti mantreṇa ||

oṃ hrī/ malatrayaṃ māyikarmaṇaśālilasaṃjñakaṃ iti malatrayam ||


kuśodakena japtena pratyekaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ mano || 5-132 ||

tena mantreṇa vidhivad etadāpyāyanaṃ mataṃ |


tena etanmantrasyāpyāye namaḥ ||


mantreṇavāriṇā mantrī tarpaṇaṃ tarpaṇaṃ matam || 5-133 ||


mantreṇa deya mūlamantreṇa || tathā ca ||

mūlamuccāryā mukamantraṃ tarpayām iti prayogaḥ ||

etatarpaṇam ||


tārā māyā ramā yogo mano dīpanamucyate |


tārā oṃ māyā hrī/ ramā śrī/ yogaḥ saṃvaṃdhaḥ tena deya mantrasyādau ||

52a) oṃ hrī/ śrī/ iti vījatrayaṃ dattvā japedityarthaḥ ||

etad dīpanam ||


japyamānasya mantrasya gopanaṃ tvan prakāśanam || 5-134 ||

saṃskāradaśasaṃproktāḥ sarvatantreṣu gopitāḥ |

yān kṛtvā saṃpradāyena mantrī vāṃcchitamāpnuyāt || 5-135 ||


tato homaṃ kuryāt ||

tathā ||

prāṅmukha upaviṣṭaḥ svastivācana pūrvake sūryaḥ soma iti paṭhitvā 
kuśajalānyādāya viṣṇoḥ adyatyādi amukagotrāmukaśarmā 
caturvargaphalaprāpti kāmaḥ amukadevatā yā yadakṣaramantrasya 
dīkṣākarmāṅgabhūtāmukadevatā yā ya dakṣarapūrvakaṃ 
tilājyādibhiraṣṭottarasahasraṃ śataṃ vāhāṃ akarmakariṣya iti saṃkalape ||

yajjāgrata iti paṭhet || tataḥ prathamataḥ sarvādhikārikaṃ caturasrakaṃ ḍaṃ 
nirūpayāmi ||

tatra śahasrahomeṇa layādhiśobhate puruṣapramāṇam ||

ādau kuṇḍasthānaṃ nite vā śubhe tat kuryāt ||

taduktaṃ viṣṇusaṃhitāyām ||


ādau bhūmiṃ parikṣe te vāstujñāne viśāradaḥ |

śalpādīn śodhayad yatnād athavā puruṣaṃ khanet || 5-136 ||


puruṣaṃ puruṣapramāṇam ||

ādau kuṇḍasthānaṃ gomayenoparipyasāmānyārghaṃ saṃsthāpya 
sāmānyārghajalenātyukṣya paṃcagavyairatyukṣaṇaṃ kuryāt ||

caturviṃśatyaṃgulapramāṇaṃ caturasraṃ nirmāyāṣṭādaśasaṃskārān 
kuryāt ||

52b) yathā śāradāyām ||


vīkṣaṇaṃ mūlamantreṇa śareṇa prokṣaṇaṃ matam |

atreṇa khananoddharau hṛdayena prapūraṇam || 5-137 ||

samīkaraṇamantreṇa sevane varmaṇāmatam |

kuṭṭanaṃ heti mantreṇa carmamantreṇamārjanam || 5-138 ||

vilepanakalārūpaṃ kalpanaṃ tadanantaram |

trisūtrīkaraṇaṃ paścād dhṛdayenārcanaṃ matam || 5-139 ||

astreṇa vajrīkaraṇaṃ hṛnmantreṇa kuśaiḥ śubhaiḥ |

catuṣpathaṃ tenutreṇa tāḍayādakṣapātanam || 5-140 ||

yoge kuṇḍānisaṃkuryāt saṃskārairabhinīritaiḥ |

mūlamantreṇa deyamantreṇa tat sthānaṃ paśyat || 5-141 ||


astrāyaphaḍiti tat sthānaṃ prokṣyayet puna evaṃ dabhaistāḍanaṃ tādayet |

humiti abhyukṣyayet ||

astrāya phaḍiti kuṇḍakhananam ||


yāvān kuṇḍasya vistāraḥ khananaṃ tāvad iṣyate |


astrāya phaḍiti mṛttikoddharaṇaṃ nama iti mantreṇa puraṇaṃ mṛttikāntara 
samīkaraṇārthaṃ mṛttikoddhārasthānaṃ pūrayet ||

astrāyeti tat sthānaṃ samīkuryāt |

humiti siṃcet ||

phaḍiti kuṇḍanaṃ athavāstrāya phaḍiti kuṃḍanaṃ dṛḍhīkaraṇam ||

athavāhumiti mantreṇa kuṇḍanasthānaṃ mārjayet |

vama iti gomayanopalepayet ||

tato humiti tat kuḍaṃ kalārūpaṃ bhāvayet |

tena mantreṇaraktasūtratrayeṇa śuklasūtratrayeṇa vā madhyamekhalaṃ veṣṭayet 
||

53a) iti trisūtrīkaraṇam ||

nama iti kuṇḍamurcayet astrāya phaḍiti kuṇḍaṃ vajratve na cintayet ||

iti vajrīkaraṇam ||

namaḥ iti mantreṇa kuśaiḥ kuṇḍābhyantare caturaśraṃ kuryāt ||

madhya āvasadhyabhagaḥ pūrvasatyabhāgaḥ ||

paścime āhavanīyabhāgaḥ ||

uttare annāhāryaḥ ||

dakṣiṇe gārhapatyaḥ iti catuṣpathīkaraṇam ||

iti kecit ||

vastutastu kaṃḍamadhyapūrvadakṣiṇottarakrameṇa kuśacatuṣṭayadānam eva tat ||

nama iti mantreṇa kuṇḍasya mekhalāyāṃ paścimottareṣu viṣṭaratraye 
sthāpanārthaṃ dvādaśāṅgulapramāṇān mūlamantreṇābhimantritān 
kuśānāstaret ||

tathā ca rāghavabhaṭe ||



vistarastaraṇārtha tu mūlamantrābhimantritam || 5-142 ||

dvādaśāṅgulamānaṃ tu akṣapāṭo prakīrttitaḥ |


ityakṣapāṭanam ||

ityastādaśasaṃskārān kuryāt ||


athavā tāni saṃkuryāt catubhivīkṣaṇādibhiḥ || 5-143 ||


yathā mūlamantreṇa kuṇḍaṃ paścāt ||

astrāya phaḍiti kuṇḍaṃ prokṣayet ||

evaṃ tāḍayet ||

humiti abhyukṣayet ||

iti saṃskāracatuṣṭayaṃ kuryāt |

kuṇḍakaraṇāśaktau haste pramāṇaṃ sthaṃṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā yathā 
saṃbhavasaṃskāraṃ kṛtvā homaṃ kuryāt ||

sthaṃḍilaṃ yathā ||

hastāyāmaṃ suvistāramaṃguṣṭhonnataṃ caturaśrīyayet ||

53b) taduktaṃ gaṇeśavimarṣiṇyām ||

athavā sthaṃṇḍilaṃ kuryāt |

ddhomārddhaṃrthaṃ mantrasattamaḥ ||


aṃguṣṭhaparvaṇo chedaṃ hastā yāmaṃ samaṃ śubham |

caturaṅgulamūchedaṃ vālukābhiḥ samantataḥ || 5-144 ||


iti ||

tataḥ kuṇḍamadhyapūrvāgraṃ rekhātrayaṃ vilikhyārghapātraṃ saṃsthāpya 
tasya dakṣiṇerghapātravat praṇītapātaṃ prokṣaṇīpātraṃ ca 
saṃsthāpyārghajalaṃ tatra kicinnidadyāt ||

tataḥ praṇavamuccaran |

arghajalena rekhāmatyukṣya prāgagrāyāṃ prathamarekhākrame pūjayet ||

prathamalekhāyāṃ | śrīmukuṇḍāye namaḥ ||

dvitīya | īśānāye ||

tṛtīya | purandarāya |

uttarāgrarekhāyām | brahmaṇe namaḥ |

dvitīya | vaivasvatāya | kurmāya |

anantāya | pṛthivyai | sudhāsāgarāya |

ratnadvīpāya | kalpavṛkṣāya namaḥ |

tṛtīyalekhāmindave namaḥ ||

iti saṃpūjya tan madhya trikoṇaṃ tadupari ṣaṭkoṇaṃ tadupari vṛttaṃ likhitvā 
oṃkāramuccaran arghajalena saṃprokṣya tadupari pīṭhānarcayet |

yathā ||

oṃ ādhāraśaktyai namaḥ ||

oṃ kurmāya | oṃ anantāyaḥ | oṃ pṛthivyai | 

ityādivikāramayakesarabhyo namastāvat ||

oṃ paṃcaśadvījāḍhyakarṇikāyai namaḥ |

oṃ dhamāya | oṃ jñānāya |

oṃ vairāgyāyai |

54a) oṃ aiśvaryāyaḥ | oṃ dharmāya |

oṃ ajñānāya | oṃ avairāgyāya |

oṃ anaiśvaryāya | amukā devatāsthaṃḍilāye namaḥ |

iti pīṭhāni saṃpūjya tan madhyavāgīśvarī mṛtusnātāṃ nīlendīvarasannibhān 
|


vāgīśvareṇa saṃyuktā mupacāraiḥ prapūjyayet ||


yathā ||

vāgīśvarī sahitavāgīśvarahāgacchetyā vāhya idaṃ pādyaṃ oṃ vāgīśvarī 
vāgīśvarābhyāṃ namaḥ ||

eṣorghyati pādyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya vāgīśvarī vāgīśvarābhyāṃ namaḥ ||

iti puṣpāñjalitrayeṇa |

oṃ vāgīśvarī iti pūrvoktavat | namaskuryāt ||

tatognimaraṇi saṃbhavaṃ śrottriyāghārajaṃ vāṇīya svadakṣiṇe 
kāṃśyapātre saṃsthāpya tasmād ekāṃ kāṣṭikāṃ gṛhītvā 

oṃ dhruvādebhyaḥ svāhā 

ityanena kuṇḍasya naiṛtyāṃ diśiprakṣipet ||

tato vahnimūle na vīkṣyāstrāya phaditi arghajale nāgniprokṣyāstrāya phaḍiti 
darbhaiḥ santātryahumityarghajalena vahnimabhyukṣa tasmin ngnau 
rāṃjyātharāgnaye namaḥ |

nīvaindavāgnaye namaḥ || iti jātharavaindavake |

pāgnināmaikyaṃ vibhāvyaramiti pāvakacaitanyaṃ tasmin saṃyojya 
praṇavenāgnimabhimantrya vamiti dhenumudrayāmiti kṛtyāstrāya phaditi 
vahnimabhirakṣyuhumiti avaguṃṭhā vahnimūlamantreṇa saṃpūjya 
vahnikarābhyāṃ gṛhītvā uttolyajānuspṛṣṭhamahītalaiḥ kuṇḍe | 

54b) paritriḥ pari āmyātmano mukhavahni praṇavamuccaran 
paramaśivajīvavuddhyā sohamiti jīvavuddhyā vāgīśvaryā yonau nikṣipet ||

tato vāgīśvarī vāgīśvarābhyāṃ ācamanīyaṃ namaḥ ||

kāmeśvarī kāmeśvarayoḥ saṃgamaṃ vicintya kāmeśvarīgarbhanādyāṃ 
taccaitanyaṃ saṃyojya iti kuśai devyāyoniṃ māchādya gabharakṣārthaṃ 
darbharakṣārthaṃ darbhakaṃkaṇaṃ dattvā jvālinīmudrāṃ pradarśayet |

mudrālakṣaṇamāmāha ||


maṇivaṃdho samaukṛtvā karau pratihatāṅgulau || 5-145 ||

tato vahnimantrenāgni prajvālayet |


citpiṃgala hana 2 daha 2 paca 2 sarvajñāpaya svāhā ||

ityagniṃ prajvālya kṛtāṃjaliḥ |

oṃ agniprajvalitaṃ vande jātavedaṃ hutāsanam |


suvarṇavarṇamamalaṃ samīddhaṃ viśvatomukham |


ityenenāgniyupasthāpayet ||

tato vahneḥ sāntvikīnāṃ saptajihvānāṃ svadehe nyaset ||


liṃgapāyūśirovaktra ghrānanetreṣu sarvata || 5-147 ||

vahnīrarghīśasaṃyuktā sādiyāntāḥ saviṃdhavaḥ |

vahne mantrāḥ samudiṣṭā jihvāṇāṃ saptadaiśikaiḥ || 5-148 ||


pāyuguḍaḥ sarvataḥ sarvā vyāpakaṃ nyaset ||

vahniḥ rephaḥ iroyakāraḥ arghīśaḥ |

55a) ukāraḥ tena vyutkrameṇa | 

sa ṣa śa va la ra yāḥ savindavo vinduyutaḥ ||

tena |

syu/ myu/ śryu/ vyu/ lyu/ tryu/ yryu/ ||

iti vahneḥ saptajihvā mantraḥ ||

prakārāntareṇa jīhvā vījānyāha ||

uktaṃ yāmale ||


vilephāvarttimau ca gauriphaṣaṣṭhasvarasthitauḥ |

antimau vagauya vargaśacalau ya la ra va iti yavarga śa. sa lau saha iti śavargaḥ |

antimocetau vargo virephau rephā rahitau | 

tena | ya la va sa ṣa sahaḥ |

tena vyutkrameṇa ha sa ṣa śa valayāḥ ||

indurardhacaṃdraḥ tadyuktaḥ viṃduranusvārastadyukta ityarthaḥ ||

śāradāmatamālaṃ vyāha ||


jihvāstā trividhāḥ proktā guṇabhedena karmasu |

vahurūpātiraktā ca sāntvikyāyogakarmasuḥ |

lohitānantaraśvetā dhūminī ca kalālikā |

viśvamurtte sphuliṅginyo dhūmravarṇāmanojavāḥ |

etāḥ saptaniyojyante krūrakarmasumantribhiḥ |

svasvanāmasamābhyāḥ syu jihvāḥ kalyāṇaretasaḥ || 5-154 ||


kalyāṇaretaso vahneḥ prathamataḥ sātvikīnāṃ svadehe nyāsaṃ kuryāt ||

syu/ hiraṇyāyai namaḥ ||

liṅge ||

ṣryu/ gagaṇāyai namaḥ || guḍe ||

śryū/ raktāyai namaḥ || śirasiḥ ||

vryū/ kṛṣṇāyai namaḥ || mukhe ||

lryū/ suprabhāyai namaḥ || ghrāṇe ||

rryū/ vahurūpāyai namaḥ || netre ||

pryū/ atiraktāyai namaḥ || sarvagātreṣu nyaset ||

tato hiraṇyagagaṇā raktā kṛṣṇā suprabhā vahurūpā atiraktānāṃ 
saptajihvānāṃ adhidevatā |

amartyapitṛgaṃdharva yakṣanāgapisācakā rākṣasāḥ saptajihvānāṃ īritā 
adhidevatā ||

amarntyo devaḥ ||

adhidevatā nāmapi svadehe nyāsaṃ kuryāt ||

oṃ devebhyo namaḥ || liṅge ||

oṃ piśācebhyo netradvaya ||

oṃ rākṣasebhyo namaḥ || sarvagātre vyāpakaṃ nyaset ||


vahneraṃgamanu nyaset tato vakte? navartmanā |

dhūmavyāpti saptajihvadhanurddhara iti kramāt |

tathā karanyāsaṃ kuryāt ||

56a) oṃ saptajihvāya kaniṣṭhābhyām ||

oṃ dhanurdharāya astrāya phaṭ ||

karatarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ ||

tatogaṃ nyāsaṃ kuryāt ||

evaṃ hṛdayādiṣaḍaṃgaṃ nyāsaṃ kuryāt ||

ityurdhverghaṃ tālatrayachotikābhidaśadiśo vahniyāt ||

athamūrttinyāsaḥ ||

tārāgnaya iti ||

mūrdhni ||

oṃ agnaya vaiśvānarāya pāyā? ||

oṃ agnaya kaumāratejase vāmakaṭyām ||

oṃ agnaya viśvamukhāya namo vāmapārśvaḥ ||

oṃ agnavedamukhāya vāmāṃse ||

tato madhya-āsanakalpayitvā ||

oṃ vahnyāsanāya nama iti saṃpūjya vahni dhyāyet ||

hemākalpaṃ padmasaṃsthaṃ trinetraṃ dhyāyed vahniṃ vadanau? liṃjatābhiḥ ||

56b) iti dhyātvā mūlamantreṇa mūrttiṃ kalpayen ||

kuṃḍasthitāgnau puṣpaṃ dattvā ekībhūtaṃ vicintya |

ca |

saptajihvā pūjayet ||

57a) yathā agnikoṇe sahasrārciṣihṛdayāyaḥ |

teṣā dhyānam ||


pīto raktostathā nīlaḥ śyāmaśvetaśca dhūmrakaḥ |

śuklaḥ śvetastathā śudro raktavarṇaḥstathaiva ca || 5-165 ||


teṣāmastrāṇi ||


vajraṃ śaktiṃ daṇḍamasiṃ pāśamaṃkuśakaṃ gadām |

śūlaṃ cakraṃ tathā padmaṃ kramatostrāṇi pūjayet || 5-166 ||


astrāṇāṃ dhyānam ||

oṃ nakarajatato yadābhracaṃpāruṇa hemanījalapā pravālabhāsaḥ |

57b) nijanijalakṣitamaulayo nijāstrābhayayuta pāṇiyugāstu vajrayuvāḥ |

pūrvelāṃ indraye namaḥ ||

evaṃ āgneyāṃ rā/ agnaye dakṣiṇe yāṃ yamāya naiṛte kṣāṃ niṛtaye paśime 
vāṃ varuṇāya vāyau yāṃ vāyava uttare śāṃ kuvelāya īśāna hā/ 
īśānāye nama ūrddhe oṃ brahmaṇe adhaḥ hrī/ anantāye namaḥ ||

tadvāhye tadastrāṇi pūjayet pūrvavajrāye namaḥ ||

āgneyāṃ oṃ śaktaye dakṣiṇe | 

oṃ daṇḍāye naiṛte | 

oṃ svargāya paścime oṃ pāśāye vāyau oṃ aṃkuśāye uttare oṃ gadāyai iśāne oṃ 
śūlāye namaḥ ||

urdhve oṃ padmāye namaḥ ||

adhaḥ oṃ cakrāye nama iti saṃpūjya madhyamekhalā paripradakṣiṇakrameṇa 
veṣṭhayet ||

madhyamekhalāyāṃ dakṣiṇapaścimottareṣu sthāpanārthaṃ 
dvādaśāṅgulapramāṇā nagabhān kuśānāstāret ||

tadupari savistarān paridhīn dakṣiṇādi pūrvavat sthāpayet ||

cchaṃdogapariśiṣṭhe |

vahumātrāḥ paridhaye ṛjavaḥ ||

satyavovraṇāḥ trayo bhavanti śīrṇāgrā ekeṣāṃ tu caturdiśam |

tat tat kaṇḍānurūpaṃ vā mānaṃ asyā nigadyate ||

58a) tataḥstu ku *? vau saṃskuryāt ||

yathā ||

sukśruvau samādhā yādhaumukhau vahnau tridhā pratāvya vāmahasta kṛtvā 
dakṣiṇahastena kuśena tayoragre mulamadhyānimārganaṃ kṛtvā adyodakena 
trivāśopari sthāpayet ||

ājyamānīyājya sthālyāṃ nikṣipya mūlenājyaṃ vīhmāstrāmaphaḍiti (?) 
prakṣālyāgnauti pratāpya vāmahaste saṃsthāpya dakṣiṇahastenārghodakena 
triḥprokṣyupunaragnau pratāpyātmasammukha sthāpayet ||

ājyamānīya sthālyānti kṣipyaṃ mūle nājyaṃ vīkṣyāstrāya phaḍiti | 

ājyaṃ prokṣyāstrāya phaḍiti darbhairghṛtaṃ santāvyahumiti 
aghajalenātyukṣayet ||

iti saṃskāracatuṣṭayaṃ kṛtvā kuṃḍāraṃgārānuddhṛtye vāyukone 
saṃsthāpya nama iti mantreṇājya sthālīṃ sthāpayet ||

idaṃ tāpanam ||

tato darbhayugalaṃ prajvālyākṣye nikṣipyadagdhaśeṣaṃ nama iti matre vahnau 
kṣipet ||

idaṃ pavītrīkaraṇam ||

punarjvālitena darbhayugena humiti mantramuccaren ghṛtanīrājya agnau prakṣipet ||

ityabhighotanam ||

tataḥ prajvālitakuśamuṣṭiṃ gṛhītvā astrāya phaḍiti mantramuccaran ||

ghṛte prajvālitāndarbhāt pradarśyāgnau prakṣipet |

ityudyotanam ||

tasmād aṃgārā dāhya sthānīmānīyātmasaṃmukhe saṃsthāpya 
tānaṃgārānagnau prakṣipyāṅgārasthāna mārjanaṃ kṛtvā jalajaṃ spṛśet ||

tata-untānahastayo laṃguṣṭhakaniṣṭhābhyāṃ pavitraṃ pṛthak 
pṛthajyṛtvāstrāya phaḍiti mantramuccaran prāṅmukhaṃ ghṛtamutplu ||

58b) pavitraṃ sthānāntare sthāpayet ||

punastenaiva prakāreṇa tathaiva ghṛtena pravitrāntaraṃ gṛhītvā tena pavitreṇa 
nama iti mantramuccalan ātmanobhimukhaṃ ghṛtaṃ saṃplāpya pūrvasthāpite 
pavitramidaṃ ceti pavitradvayamagnau prakṣipet ||

ityutyavanotyavanena vāhyasya saṃkuryāt |

saṃskārāḥ ṣaḍudīritāḥ |

tataḥ prādeśamātraṃ sagranthidarbhayugalamājyasthālyāṃ 
ghṛtamadhyanikṣipyājya dakṣiṇabhāgaṃ śuklapakṣe mājya vāmabhāgaṃ 
kṛṣṇapakṣaṃ vicintya vāme iḍāṃ dakṣiṇe piṅgalāṃ madhyesu ṣumṛ || 
dhyātvā homaṃ kuryāt ||

tataḥ sruveṇa nama iti mantramuccaran dakṣiṇabhāgād ghṛtamodāya ||

oṃ agnaya svāhā ityagne dakṣiṇalocane juhuyāt |

saṃpātamājyaṃ tatraiva dakṣinabhāgena nikṣipet ||

punarnama iti vāmabhāgād ghṛtamādāya somāya svāhā ||

iti vāmalocane juhuyāt |

saṃpātamājyaṃ tatraiva vāmabhāge sthāpayet |

punarevaṃ madhyabhāgād ghṛtamādāya agniṣomābhyāṃ svāhā ||

iti vahne lalāṭe netre hutvā saṃpātamājyaṃ madhyabhāge kṣipet |

gopālapakṣe lalāṭanerāhutiṃ na kuryāt ||

narasiṃhaviṣaya kuryādeva punarnamadakṣiṇabhāgādājya pādāya oṃ agnaya 
sviṣṭakṛte svāhā 

59a) ityegne vaktre hutvā dakṣiṇabhāge pūrvoktavat kṣipet ||

ityagnivaktrodghāṭanam svāhā ityasya hāpadena tasmād bhāgād gṛhītamājyaṃ 
tatra śeṣaṃ sruvasaṃlagnaṃ nyaset |

tathā ca || svāśavdamuccāryāhutiṃ dattvā hāśabdamuccāryāhutiṃ śeṣaṃ 
ghṛtaṃ tatraiva sthāpayed ityarthaḥ ||

tato mahāvyāhṛtihomaṃ kuryāt |

tataḥ svavāme jalapūrṇapātraṃ sthāpayitvā pratyāhutiṃ śeṣaṃ tatraiva 
sthāpayet |

oṃ bhū svāhā ||

oṃ bhuvaḥ svāhā ||

oṃ vaiśvānarajātaveda ihāvahalohitākṣa sarvakarmāṇi sādhaye svāhā ||

ityagnimantreṇāhutitrayaṃ hutvā hutaśeṣajalaṃ pūrṇapātre sthāpayet |

home svāhāṃ tatā māha ||

yajñapārśveḥ ||


ādāya dakṣiṇe pāṇau pravattimadhuraṃ haviḥ |

prāṅmukho vahni jāyā te juhuyānyabjapāṇinā || 5-167 ||


evaṃ ||


mantreṇoṃkārapūtena svāhānte ca vicakṣaṇaḥ |

svāhā vasāne juhuyādhyāyen vai mantradevatām || 5-168 ||


trimadhuraṃ ghṛtamadhuśarkaraḥ |

tathā coktaṃ prayogasāre |


payomadhughṛtaṃ ceti samaḍimadhuraṃ smṛtam |


vahni jāyā svāheti |

tatognergabhādhānā ḍiṣoḍaśasaṃskārān kuryāt ||

yathā || sāradātilake ||


garbhādhānādikā vahneḥ kriyā nivarttayet kramāt || 5-169 ||

aṣṭābhirājyāhutibhiḥ praṇavena pṛthak pṛthak |

59b) garbhādhānaṃ puṃsaṃ vanaṃ sīmaṃnonnayanaṃ punaḥ || 5-170 ||

anantaraṃ jātakarmasyān nāmakaraṇaṃ tathā |

upaniṣkramaṇaṃ paścād annaprāśanamīritam || 5-171 ||

cūḍopakaraṇe bhūyo mahānāmnyamahāvratam |

athopaniṣadaṃ snānaṃ godānodvāhakaumṛtiḥ || 5-172 ||

śubheṣu syu vivāhāntāḥ kriyāstāḥ krurakamaṃsu |

maraṇāntāḥ samudviṣṭā vahnerāgamakarmabhiḥ || 5-173 ||


mahānāmnyaṃ brahmacaryam ||

mṛte abhyeṣṭiḥ ||

tadyathā ||

agnergarbhādhānaṃ kalpanāmi svāhā ||

ityaṣṭāhutīrjuhuyāt |

oṃ agneḥ puṃsavanaṃ kalpayāmi pūrvavat ||

oṃ sīmatonayanameva meva oṃ agnerjātakarma |

oṃ agnernāmakaraṇaṃ kalpayāmi svāhā ||

tasmīn samaye agne tvaṃ amukanāmāsi |

tan tan maṇḍādhiṣṭhā tṛdevatā nāmnā nāmakuryāt ||

athavā ||

agne tvaṃ varadanāmāsiviśvarūpanāmāsi vā nāmakuyāt ||

agnernāmānipūrvalikhita pramāṇe nonusaṃdhe yāni pustakavāhulyāt punaratra 
na likhitāni |

amukanāmāgnaye pādyādibhirvahniṃ saṃpūjyatāṃvulaṃ dantvā 
namaskuryāt |

tata oṃ amukanāmāgnerniṣkramaṇaṃ kalpayāmi svāhā |

ityaṣṭāhutirjuhuyāt ||

amukanāmāgnerannaprāśanaṃ parikalpayāmi svāhā ||

evaṃ kuḍākaraṇa ||

evaṃ upanayana || mahānāmnya ||

mahānāmnīvrataṃ vedādhyayanamiti vā evaṃ mahāvrataṃ upaniṣadvrataṃ 
godānaṃ samāvarttanaṃ vivāhaṃ agnyādhānaṃ kalpayāmi oṃ svāhā || (8) ||

60a) ityagneḥ ṣoḍaśasaṃskārānsajhāpyapitarau tasma saṃpūjyānmaniyojayet |

tasya vahneḥ pitarau vāgīśvarī vāgīśvarau yathā ||

idaṃ pādyaṃ vāgīśvaryai nama iti pādyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya vāgīśvarāya 
namaḥ iti puṣpāṃjali trayaṃ datvā vāgīśvaryai namaskuryāt ||

tato vāgīśvaraṃ pūjayet ||

punaravaṃ iti namaskṛtyaṃ vāgīśvarī sahitavāgīśvarapūjito sikṣamasve yuktā 
saṃhāradrūdrayā sthaṃṇḍilāntaṃ vahannāsāpuṭe ānīya suhṛdisthāpayet |

vaiśvānarasaṃhitāyām ||

vrahmāṇaṃ sthāpayed agnau pharārthe karmasākṣikam ||

tasya kuṇḍasya dakṣiṇe brahmānaṃ saṃsthāpya pādyādibhirarcayet ||

tataḥ samidhaḥ paṃcajuhuyān mūlāgraghṛtasaṃplutāḥ ||

tato ghṛtokta paṃcamedhognau prakṣipet ||

tatognerjihvāṃgamūrtināṃ mantrarekākāhuntī juhuyāt ||

sryū/ hiraṇyāyai nama ityādi dvādaśāhutīn pratyekaikaṃ saptajihvāya 
netratrayāya vauṣaṭ tāvat dadyāt |

gopālapakṣai saptajihvāya netrābhyāṃ vauṣaṭ ||

oṃ dhanuddharāya agnaye jātavedase ||

oṃ havyavāhanāya || aśvodarajāya |

vaiśvānarajāya | kaumāratejasa | 

viśvamukhāya | vedamukhāya svāhā ||

tato vāmahastastu caṃṇaṃ sthāpya dakṣiṇahastena stuvamādāya nama iti mantra 
murccaran | 

sruvena caturājyāhutitīḥ srucidantvādhomukhe masrucaṃpidhāya untiṣṭha 
vaiśvānarajātaveda ihāvahalohitākṣa sarvakarmāṇi sādhaya vauṣaṭ ||

60b) ityagnimantreṇaṃ pūrṇāhutiṃ dattvā śrīmahāgaṇapatimantreṇa vyastena 
ca daśāhutīrjuhuyāt ||

yathā || oṃ svāhā ||

oṃ śrī/ svāhā || oṃ śrī/ hrī/ svāhā ||

oṃ śrī/ hrī/ klī/ svāhā ||

oṃ śrī/ hrī/ klī/ glau/ svāhā ||

oṃ śrī/ hrī/ klī/ glau/ gaṃ svāhā ||

oṃ śrī/ hrī/ klī/ glau/ gaṇapataye varavarada svāhā ||

oṃ śrī/ hrī/ klī/ glaugaṃ varadavarada sarvajanaṃ ma svāhā ||

evaṃ pūrvoktavat ||

sarvajanaṃ meva śamānaya svāhā ||

iti daśāhutī hutvāgnau iṣṭai devatā yantraṃ dhyātvā viṃdupīṭhe devatāṃ 
dhyātvā vājyāgnināsahaikyaṃ vibhāvya vahnirūpāntāṃ vicintya 
pūjākramaparivārāntā saṃpūya naidevyaṃ dattvā punarācamanīyaṃ 
tāmbūraṃ nivedya mūlenāmukadevatāyai nama iti puṣpāṃjalitrayeṇa 
saṃpūjya namaskuryāt ||

tato vahniṣṭadevatayoraikāṃ vibhāvya tasyā mukhe paṃcaviṃśāhutī juhuyāt ||

tathā mantramucaran idaṃ svāhā amukadevatāyai etat krameṇa 
paṃcaviṃśāhutī juhuyāt ||

idaṃ traikīkaraṇam ||

tato vahni daivatayorātmanā ca sahaikyaṃ vibhāvyamūlanaikādaśāhutī juhuyāt 
||

idaṃ tu nāḍīsaṃdhānam ||


juhuyādaṃgamukhyā tāmāvṛttīnāmanukramāt |

ekaikāmāhutiṃ samyak sarpiṣādaiśikottamaḥ || 5-174 ||



aṃgamukhyānāmaṃgapradhānā nāmāvṛtīnām ||

61a) yasya devasya yat saṃkhyā āvareṇa devatāstāsāṃ tata 
āvaraṇadevatāmudiśya mūle naikaikāhutiṃ juhuyāt |

agneraṃgavibhāgamāha śrīsāradāyām ||

yavaḥ kāṣṭhaṃ tataḥ ||

śrotraṃ yato dhūmotra nāsikā ||


yatrālpajvaraṇaṃ netraṃ yato tārastataḥ śiraḥ |

yatra prajvālitā jvālā sā jihvā jātavedase || 5-175 ||

āsyāntajuhuyād agne vipaścit sarvakarmasu |

karṇahome bhaved vyādhi netrendhatva mudīritam || 5-176 ||

nāsikāyāṃ bhavet pīḍā mastake dhanasaṃkṣaya |


prakṛte homaṃ saṃpādya sūryagaṇeśaśivagopālādibhyopi ekaikāhitiṃ 
juhuyāt ||

tato mahāvyāhṛti homaṃ kuryāt |

oṃ bhuḥ svāhetyādi ||

caturāhutīniti mūladevatāṃ gaṃdhādibhisaṃpūjyati lājyādibhiḥ prakṛta 
homaṃ kuryāt ||

homaprakāramāha ||

harvidānaṃ tu nyubjapāṇinā karttavyam ||

taduktaṃ gavākṣaratantre ||


cittahastena karttavyaṃ nācittrena kadācana || 5-177 ||

hetuścakṣuvināśāya yajamānena viśyati |


tathā ca tripurātattvasāre |


cintre haste havirdānaṃ kriyate vahutāśana || 5-178 ||

dātuḥ puṇyaphalaṃ nāsti hotā ca narakaṃ vrajet |


bhairavatantrepi ||


haviruttāttahastena vaiśvānaramukhe hutam || 5-179 ||

hetuścakṣuvināśāya jajamāno vinaśyati |

61b) home mudrātrayaṃ proktaṃ mṛgī haṃsī ca śūkara || 5-180 ||

mṛgī kaniṣṭhā tarjanyo haṃsīmuktakaniṣṭhikā |

sukarīkaraṇaṃko ca mitimudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 5-181 ||

mṛgī ca putrakāmārthā haṃsī sarvaphalapradā |

abhicāreṣu sarveṣu sūkarī kathitā vudhaiḥ || 5-182 ||


prakṛtahomaṃ samāpyaguruḥ śiṣyaṃ paṃcagavyaṃ pāyayitvā vidhivat 
sthāpitāvardhinīruddhṛtya śiśoḥ śirasi mātṛkāmanasā japan mūlena 
sādhitaiḥ kuṃbhajalairabhiṣiṃcet |

avaśiṣṭatoyena siṣyamāvāmet ||

utthāya gātramājana kṛtvādhau teṣā sasīparidhāyārvamat ||

tataḥ sūkṣmavastreṇa vauṣaḍiti mantraṃ muccaran śiṣyasya netre vadhnīyāt |

tataḥ kare gṛhītvā taṃ śiṣyaṃ kuṃḍaśamīpe samānīye netravaṃdhaṃ 
dhṛdūrī kṛtya svavāme kuśāstaraṃ rakṣāpayet ||

tato gururamukamantraṃ dadāmīti pratijñāṃ śrāvayet ||


tataḥ śiṣyodadasveti bhruyāt praṇavakaṃ tate |


guruḥ śiṣyāgne mātṛkānyāsaṃ kṛtvā vastreṇa śiraḥ pravṛtyādau 
ṛṣyādikaṃ saklosmṛtvā ca devatādhiyā śiṣyasya dakṣiṇe karṇe vālatrayaṃ 
vruyāt ||

vāmakarṇe ekavāraṃ vruyāt |

mantraṃ datvā gururjaraṃ gṛhītvā vidyayaṃ mantro vā tava mama 
samānadhātrī dātā vāstu ||

iti vinītaśiṣya hastaṃ jalaṃ dadyāt ||

astvitiśiṣyā vruyuḥ śiṣyo gurumantra devatā nāmaikyaṃ bhāvayet ||

62a) mantramaṣṭottaraśataṃ japet ||

aṣṭavāraṃ vā ||

tathā ca vijñānasaṃhitāyām ||

gurovidyāṃ samālokya tyajyaṣṭottaraśataṃ japet ||


athavāpyaṣṭakṛtvaśca japenmantramananyadhī || 5-183 ||


gururapi svaśaktisiddhyarthaṃ nyāsaṃ kṛtvāṣṭottarasahasraṃ śataṃ vā japet |

tataḥ śiṣyo japaṃ samāpya devatātmakaṃ guruṃ ḍaṃdavat praṇamya tasya 
pādāmbuja dvaṃdaṃ murddhni yojayitvā stavaṃ kuryāt ||

oṃ namaste nāthabhagavan śivāya gururūpiṇe ||


vidyāvatārasaṃsiddhyai svīkṛtānekavigrahaḥ |

nārāyaṇa svarūpāya ramātmakamūrttaye || 5-184 ||

sarvajñāna tato bheda bhānave vitqvarūpiṇe |

svatantrāya padāktiptavigrahāya śivātmane || 5-185 ||

paratantrāya bhaktānāṃ bhavyānāṃ bhavyarūpiṇe |

vivekināṃ vivekāya vimarṣāya vimarṣiṇā || 5-186 ||

prakāśāṇāṃ prakāśāya jñānināṃ jñānarūpiṇe |

tvat prasādādahaṃ deva kṛta kṛtyosmi sarvataḥ || 5-187 ||

ahaṃ mṛtyumahāpāśād vimuktosmi śivosmi ca |


iti paṭhitvā guruṃ bhūyāt ||

tato guruhastābhyāṃ śiṣyamastakaṃ dhṛtvā utthāpayet ||

yathā uttiṣṭhavat samuktosi samyagācāravāgbhavaḥ |


ityuktā śiṣyamutthāpayen mantrācārān kathayet || 5-188 ||


tato gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt ||

62b) taduktaṃ vrahmasaṃhitāyām ||

vṛthā vipravaco yastu gṛhaṇati namānavaḥ 

śubhe adattvā dakṣiṇaṃ vāpi sayāti narakaṃ dhruvam |


dīkṣānte dakṣiṇāṃ prāhuḥ svarṇagobhūmibhūṣaṇam |

bhūmī vṛttikarīndadyāt putrapautrānuyāyinīm || 5-189 ||


tathā ca pārijāta svare ||


gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt pratyakṣāya śivātmane |

gurvarthaṃ matimānnityaṃ śarīramapi santyajet || 5-190 ||

ekaikamakṣaraṃ yastu guruḥ śiṣyaśivodhayet |

pṛthivyāṃ nāsti tadravyaṃ yadattvāsaurjhaṇī bhavet || 5-191 ||


tataḥ pūrṇā pūrvakāle ||

oṃ agne tvaṃ mṛdunāmasi mṛdunāmnegnaye pādyaṃ namaḥ ||

iti pādyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya pūrṇāṃ dadyāt || yathā ||

agnipurāṇam ||

mūrdhānande namantreṇa saṃsravena ca dhārayā ||


mūrdhānaṃ divorattriṃ pṛthivyā vaiśvānaramṛtamājātamagnim |

kapigusraprājamatithiṃ janāmāsannāpātraṃ jāyantadevāḥ || 5-192 ||

dadyād utthāya pūrṇāṃ tu nopaviśya kadācana |


iti mūdhnānandivetti yajuṣām | 

pūrṇahomaṃ yaśasojuhomīti sāmagānām ||

tathā ca kṛṣṇānusaṃhitāyām ||


śuksruvau cottānāsyautāṃ gṛhītvā śaṃkhamudrayā || 5-193 ||

sthitvānābhisamaṃ kṛtvā dhyāyed devaṃ maheśvaram |

paṃcāṅgulaṃ tyaktvā śaṃkhamudrayādharyāḥ || 5-194 ||

paṃcāṅgulaṃ vahistyaktvā dhārayecchaṃkhamudrayati |


63a) vācaspatimiśra kṛta mahādānanirṇaye vacanāt ||

tāntrikamatamāha ||

srukasrūvaucontā nāsyau gṛhītvā ||

oṃ bhuve vāyavo cāntarikṣāya ca mate ca mahate ca svāhā ||

oṃ surādityāya ca caṃdramase ca nakṣatretyaśca mahate ca svāhā ||

sruksruvā cadhomukhau kṛtvā mūlena vauṣaḍantena pūrṇāṃ dadyāt ||

tatogniṃ pādyādibhiḥ saṃpūjya vastranālikelaphalādyagnau dadyā tatogniṃ 
namaskuryāt (ḥ) |

sarvataḥ pāṇipādosi iti ślokaṃ pūrvoktam ||


ayurārogyamaiśvaryaṃ dhṛtiṃ śattvaṃ valaṃ yaśaḥ || 5-195 ||

tejo varcaḥ paśūnvījaṃ sauryaṃ srāmrājye meva ca |

saubhāgaṃ karmasiddhiṃ ca kulajyeṣṭhasukatthanām || 5-196 ||

sarvameta sarvasākṣin iviśodorirīhinaḥ |

agniṃ prajvālitaṃ vande jātavedaṃ hutāsanam || 5-197 ||

suvarṇavarṇamamalaṃ susiddhaṃ viśvatomukham |


iti paṭhitvā namaskuryāt ||

tataḥ kṛtāṃjaliragniṃ prārthayedanena yathā || 5-198 ||


oṃ ābrahman brahmaṇovarcaśijāyatā mārāṣṭro rājanyaḥ śūra iṣavyotīvyādhi 
mahāratho jāyatām ||

bhu dagdhī dhenuvordhā natvā nāśuḥ saptapuraṃdhriryo ṣā viṣṇuratheṣṭaḥ 
sarbheyo yuvasya jajamānasya vīro jāyatām ||

oṃ nikāmenikāmenaḥ parjanyo varṣa tu phalavebhyo na oṣadhayaḥ paśyantāṃ 
yogakṣemonaḥ syāt ||

iti pārthayet ||

tato va devatāgaṇaṃ tadaṅge vilīnaṃ vibhāvya ||

63b) bhagavati de pūjitā sikṣamasveti saṃhāramudrayā svahṛdisamānīya 
kṛtāñjaliragniṃ visarjyate ||

oṃ yajñayajñaṃ gacchajajñapatiṃ gaccha svāṃ yoniṃ gaccha svāhā ||

eṣa te yajño yajñapate sahasuktavāk sarvavīrastvaṃ juṣasva svāhā ||

iti paṭhitvāgne visarjanaṃ kuryāt ||

tato brahmaṇe dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt ||

tathā ca smṛtiḥ ||


brahmaṇe dakṣiṇā deyā yatra yā parikīrtitā |

karmānte mucyamānāyāṃ pūrṇapātrādhikā bhavet || 5-199 ||


hetu vastrayugmaṃ kāṃsyamityapiśrute ||

tathā ca śaktā śaktabhedād ubhayam |

yathā pūrṇapātramānīya tilakuśajalānyādāya viṣṇuḥ ||

oṃ adyāmuke māsi amupakṣemukatithā vamuka deva śarmāmukadevatā yā 
yadakṣaramantrasya dīkṣākarmāṅga homakarmapratiṣṭhārthamidaṃ 
pūrṇapātraṃ brahmāṇe haṃ dade |

iti dakṣiṇā mutsṛjya brahmavimocanaṃ kuryādanena ||

oṃ uttiṣṭhabrahmaṇasya te deva yaṃ tattve maha upaprayaṃ tu marutaḥ svadāna 
cadraṃdrapāśūrbhavāsu ca |

iti brahmavimocanaṃ kuryāt |

tataḥ pariṣṭaraṇakuśān ghṛtāktāstṛṣṇīmagnau ṣakṣipet ||

tatastilakārthaṃ kuṇḍasya śānakoṇāt suvena bhasmānīya pātrāntarasthāpayet |

śāntidīpikāyāṃ vasiṣṭha ||


aiśānyāmāhare bhasmasuveṇāthastu cā pi vā |

vandhanāṃ kārayet tena śiraḥ kaṇṭhāṃsakeṣu ca || 5-200 ||

64a) kaśya yaśyati mantreṇa yathā krameṇa yogataḥ |

tataḥ śāntiprakurvīta avadhāraṇa vācanam || 5-201 ||


atha homadakṣiṇā prapaṃcasāre ||

ekādaśārdhakaśikā va rakāṃcasya dadyāt sadaiva guravetha sahasrahome ||

ardhārdhapaṃcakaṇikādvikaṇā ca sārdhāsyā dakṣiṇe |

ha kakthitā munibhistridhaiva ||

śāradāyām ||


sahasrahome vitared dakṣiṇā nihyamānataḥ |

tadardhaṃ vā suvarṇaṃ vā yathā vibhavasārataḥ || 5-202 ||


niṣkaṃ vā tadarddhaṃ vā iti śaktyapekṣayā vikalpaḥ natu aicchikaḥ tihve 
apravṛtteḥ ||

niṣkaṃpalamiti saṃpradāyaḥ ||

tacca suvarṇacatuṣṭayam | 

paṃcakṛṣṇalakomāṣaste tu varṇastu ṣoḍaśa |

palaṃ suvarṇaścatvāra iti |

śāstrasya niścaya ||

kṛṣṇalaḥ svarṇamāṣakaḥ ||

viśvaprakāśe ||


akṣālaṃkārayonirṣkaṃṣkiḥ svarṇacatuṣṭaya |

niṣkamaṣṭādhikaḥ svarṇaṃ śatedīnārakarṣayo || 5-203 ||


ityuktam ||

oṃ kalmastu nidrā aṣṭakanikenaddhā triśadraktikamitaṃ niṣkamāhuḥ ||


dakṣiṇāṃ tu pradātavyā grahādīnāṃ visarjjanam |


tato hosthaladugdhaṃ dattvā pṛthiviśītalābhavati vadet ||

tataḥ kṛtāṃjali homakarmācchidramastuḥ ||


guruyajñasthalāghaṭasaṃnidhau gatvapaviśet || 5-204 ||


tataḥ śiṣyo gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt ||

yathā tilakuśajalānyādāya viṣṇuḥ adyāmukamāse ityādi pūvoktavidhinā 
yadakṣaramantrasya dīkṣākarmapratiṣṭhārthamidaṃ 
kāṃcanamamukagotrāyāmukaśamarṇe gurave tubhyamahaṃ dade guruḥ svastītī 
vuyāt ||


vittaśāṭhyaṃ vināsarvaṃ gurave saṃnivedayet |

maṇḍapaṃ maḍapadravyaṃ gurave sannivedayet || 5-205 ||


tato gururghaṭe mūladevatāṃ pādyādibhi saṃpūjya tāṃbula dattvā 
punarācamanīyaṃ dattvāṅga devatāgaṇaṃ tadaṃge vilīnaṃ vibhāvya 
nirmālyaṃ gṛhītvā ||

oṃ amukadevatāyai namaḥ iti maṃḍalopari pūjayet ||


gatasāraṃ tu naivedyaṃ tataścaitat samuddharet |

aiśānyāṃ tadbhuje dattvā kiṃcit sthānaṃ viśodhayet || 5-206 ||


tadbhuje nirmālya bhuje te ca viṣvak senaḥ smṛto viṣṇostejaścaṃḍovivasvaḥ |


caṇḍīśvarī tathā śaktiścaṇḍeśaḥ śaṃkarasya ca |

laṃvodalo gaṇapateramījyucchiṣṭabhojinaḥ || 5-207 ||


tathā cāgamakalpadrume ||


dīkṣāyāṃ pūjayet kuṃbhe yantrādau pratyahaṃ yejat |


tato ghaṭaṃ saṃcolya sthānāntare saṃsthāpya kṛtāṃjaliḥ adya kṛtai 
tadamukadevatā yā yan mantrasya dīkṣākarmācchidre mastu ||

ghaṭajale na śiṣyamabhiṣiṃcet ||

yathā ||


surāstvāmabhiṣiṃcantu brahmaviṣṇumaheśvarāḥ || 5-208 ||

vāsudevo jagannāthaḥ tathā saṃkarṣaṇaprabhuḥ |

pradyumnaścāniruddhaśca bhavantu vijayāyate || 5-209 ||

ākhaṇḍalegnirbhagavān yamau vai naiṛtastathā |

varuṇaḥ pavaścaiva dhanādhyakṣastathā śivaḥ || 5-210 ||

65a) brahmaṇaḥ sahitaḥ śeṣo dikpālāḥ yāṃ tu te sadā |

kīrttilakṣmī dhṛtirmedhā puṣṭiśraddhāḥ kṣamāmati || 5-211 ||

buddhirlajjā vapuḥ śānti stutiḥ kāntiśca mātaraḥ |

etattvāmabhiṣiṃcantu dharmapatnyaḥ samāgatāḥ || 5-212 ||

ādityacaṃdramā bhaumo budhajīvesitārkajaḥ |

grahāstvāmabhiṣiṃcantu rāhuḥ ketuśca tarpitāḥ || 5-213 ||

devadānavagandharvā yakṣarākṣasapannagāḥ |

ṛṣayo munayo gāvo devamātara eva ca || 5-214 ||

devapatnyo dhruvā daityāścānsarasāṃ gaṇāḥ |

astrānisarvasāstrāṇi rājāno vāhanāni ca || 5-215 ||

auṣadhāni ca ratnāni kālasyā vayavāśca ya |

saritaḥ sāgarāḥścairāḥ tīrthānijaladāradām || 5-216 ||

ete tvāṃ abhiṣiṃcantu sarvakāmārthasiddhaya |


mahāvāmadevya ṛṣirthirāḍ gāyatrī chaṃdaḥ indro devatā śāntikarmaṇi 
viniyogaḥ ||

oṃ kayānaścitre ābhuvaṭuti sadā vṛdhaḥ sakhākayā śaviṣṭayo vṛtā |

kastvā satyo madānāmaṃ hi*? matsadaṃdhasaḥ ||

dṛḍhāvidāruje vasu abhiṣunaḥ sakhinā savitā jaritrīṇām ||

śataṃ bhavasya taye ||


oṃ svasti na indro vṛddhaśravāḥ svastīnaḥ pūṣā viśvavedāḥ |

svastīnastārkṣyo ariṣṭanemiḥ svastino vṛhaspati dadhātu || 5-217 ||


iti ghaṭajale nābhiṣicya yajñabhasmanā vandenāṃ kuryā |

65b) yajñabhasma gṛhītvā ||

oṃ kaśya pasya trāyuṣaṃ śiṣyasya lalāṭe dadyāt |

jamadagnissthāyuṣamiti kaṇṭhe deyāt ||

oṃ yad devānāṃ trāyuṣamityaṃsayoḥ |

oṃ tat te astrutrāyuṣamiti hṛdi dadyāt ||

tata vāśīrvādaṃ kuryāt ||

tataḥ śiṣyo guruṃ daṃḍavat praṇamya yathā sukhaṃ viharet ||

gururapi yahā sthānaṃ gacchet ||

anyatyopidīnānāthebhyaḥ kiṃcid dadyāt |

kumārī ca bhojayet ||

tathā coktaṃ māgamakalpadrume ||


tataḥ susthiracittena yogavartsasamabhyaset |

gaṃgāyāṃ gopradānena divyavarṣaśatatrayam || 5-218 ||

yat phalaṃ prāpyate nityaṃ ārādhyāpnoti tat phalam |

kurukṣetre tayāyāṃ ca prayāge puruṣottame || 5-219 ||

go sahasra pradānena yat phalaṃ samavāpyate |

tadetadakṣilaṃ puṇyaṃ prāpnotyavanasaṃśayaḥ || 5-220 ||

samagramasamagraṃ vā yo dadyāt pūjitaṃ haret |

kadācidati nityaṃ vā pattrapuṣ.pādikaṃ vahu || 5-221 ||

kiṃ tīrthasevayā dānairanyai vahubhirīritaiḥ |

ārādhanāsamarthastu dadyādarcanasādhanam || 5-222 ||

yo dātuṃ naiva śaknoti kuryādarcanaśarnam |

nistārāyata devālaṃ bhavāvdhai? patataḥ kṣaṇāt || 5-223 ||

iti pūjā vidhānena nayat pratidinaṃ ca yaḥ |

ihā vāpya tulāṃ lakṣmī mantre bhūyāt paraṃpadam || 5-224 ||


dīkṣāṃ kṛtvā yadā mantrī cāpavāsaṃ samācarediti ślokaikaṃ pūrvalikhitam 
||


dātā gṛhītānopoṣyed upavāsaḥ śiyaṃ haret |

66a) tasmādannaṃ samaśrīyād dhaviṣyānnena śāṅkari || 5-225 ||


atha saṃkṣepadīkṣā ||


muhurttasarvato bhadre navaṃ kuṃbhaṃ nidhāya ca |

modakaṃ gaṃdhapuṣpābhyāṃ marcitaṃ vastramaṃ vṛtam || 5-226 ||

sarvauṣadhi navaratnapaṃcavallava saṃyutam |

surāmāṃsī va cākuṣṭaṃ śaileyaṃ rajanīdvayam || 5-227 ||

śaṭī caṇupakaṃ mustaṃ ca sarvausadhigaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |

mṛśniparṇīśyāmalatā tejapattraṃ śatāvarī || 5-228 ||

guḍūcī sahadevā ca mahauṣadhigaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |

surāmāṃsijaṭāmāsī śaileyaṃ śailajam || 5-229 ||


rajanīdvayaṃ haridrā dāruharidrā pṛṣṇaparṇīpippalī sahadevā dhṛtakumārī ||

navaratnaṃ tu śāradāyām ||


kuktāmāṇikyavaiḍūrya gomadānvakravikramān |

padmarāgaṃ marakatanīraṃ ceti yathākramam || 5-230 ||

tato devārccanaṃ kṛtvā hunedattottaraṃ śatam |


hunediti śaktiviṣayam ||


śiṣyaṃ sūlaṃ kṛtaṃ vadyā mupājñamupaveśayet || 5-231 ||

mantritaṃ prokṣiṇītoyaiḥ śāntikuṃbhajalaistathā |

mūlamantreṇāṣṭottaraśatamabhimantrite nābhiṣecayet || 5-232 ||


atha saṃpādayen mantre hastaṃ śirasinidhāya śamastvit pakṣatān dadyāt ||

tataḥ śiṣyorcayed gurum ||

yad vā dīkṣāntarakramaṃ (ḥ) śaṃkhamabhyacya sāṃkṣataṃ tadaṃ 
vunābhiṣicyāṣṭe vālaṃ mūlena kekaram |


vidhāyāṣṭau japet karṇe upadeśatvaṃ yaṃ vidhiḥ |


upadeśāntaramāha ||


candrasūryagrahetīrthe siddhakṣetre śivālaye || 5-233 ||

mantramātraprakathanād upadeśaḥ sa ucyate |


66b) iti śrī yadunāthacakravartti viracitāyā māgaḥ kalpalatāyāṃ prathamaḥ 
kalpaḥ || 1 ||



66b) athātra homadravyānāṃ pramāṇamabhidhīyate |

karṣamātraṃ ghṛtaṃ home śuktimātrāṃ payaḥ smṛtam || 6-1 ||

śāstrīyatolakadvayaṃ karṣapalacaturthāṃśaḥ karṣaḥ |

palaṃ suvarṇāścatvāraḥ śuktirarddhapalaṃ tathāḥ || 6-2 ||

uktānipaṃcagvyāni tat sapāṇimaṇīṣibhiḥ |

tat samaṃ madhudugdhān namakṣamātraṃ udāhṛtam || 6-3 ||

dugdhānnaṃ paramānnaṃ akṣamātraṃ tolakadvayam |

dadhiprakṛtimātrasyā rājāsyumuṣṭisammitāḥ || 6-4 ||


paladvayaṃ pṛthukāṣṭastat pramāṇāḥ syuḥ saktavopi tathoditāḥ |


guḍaṃ parārddhamānaṃ syā ccharkarāpi tathā matā |

grāsārddhaṃ carumānaṃ syā dikṣuḥ parvāvidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 6-5 ||


mayūrāṇḍasamaṃ grāsaḥ |


ekaikapattrapuṣpāṇi tathā pūpāṇikalpayet |

kadalīphalanāraṃga phalānyanekaśo viduḥ || 6-6 ||

māturuṃgaṃ catuḥkhaṇḍaṃ patasaṃdaśadhākṛtam |


māturuṃgaṃ niṃvojamviraviśeṣaḥ panasaṃkaṇṭhakīphalam |


adhiṣṭānārakīlānakhaṇḍitānividurvudhāḥ || 6-7 ||

tridhākṛtaṃ phalaṃ vailvaṃ kapitthaṃ khaṇḍitaṃ dvidhā |

uvārukaphalahome kathitaṃ khaṇḍitaṃ tridhā || 6-8 ||


uvārukaphalaṃ karkaṭī ||


phalānyānyānikhaṃḍāni samidhaḥ syudaśāṅgulāḥ |

durvātrayaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ guḍucī catulaṃgulā || 6-9 ||

vrīhayomuṣṭimātrāḥ syurmudgāmoṣātha vā api |

67a) taṃḍūlāḥ syustadadhāḥ śāko vā muṣṭisammitāḥ || 6-10 ||

godhūmaraktakamalā vihitāmuṣṭimānatāḥ |

tilaśculukamātrāḥ syuḥ sarṣapāstat pramāṇakāḥ || 6-11 ||


culakamātrāḥ kaṣamātrāḥ śuktipramāṇaṃ lavaṇamarīcān yapiviṃśatiḥ |


puravadaramānaṃ syād rāmaṭhaṃ tat samaṃ smṛtam |


puraṃ gugguluḥ rāmaṭhaṃ diṃguḥ ||


candanāgurukarpūrakakastūrīkuṃkumāni ca || 6-12 ||

tittirīvījaṃ mānāni samuddiṣṭānidaiśikaiḥ |

śāradātilake mānamuktamanyat pracakṣate || 6-13 ||

uktaṃ tu dakṣiṇā mūrttau sāvadhānā va dhāraye |

karṣamātraṃ ghṛtaṃ home śuktimātraṃ payaḥ smṛta || 6-14 ||


aśitiguṃjāparimitaṃ śāstrokutolakam ||


tad dvayaṃ karṣaḥ śāstroktatolakadvayaṃ śuktiḥ |

uktāni paṃcagavyāni tat samānimaniṣibhiḥ || 6-15 ||


śuktisamāni ||


tat samaṃ madhudugdhānnamakṣamātramudāhṛtam |


akṣamātraṃ ca vadbhiḍa?phalamātramupayaśculekanānaṃ syāt asyā 
madhvanikecana ||


manusaṃgatametat syād dravadravyestu va śruteḥ || 6-16 ||

tat kathaṃ madhudugdhād e bhavecculakamānataḥ |

ucyate śvatthapatrād e vidhānān tatparaṃ bhavet || 6-17 ||

vastutacculakamadhyatanaṃstu va samaṃ ca tat |

ko virodhasrūveṇaiva dravyadravyahutādbhat || 6-18 ||

dadhiprasṛtimānaṃ syāllājāḥ syurmuṣṭisammitaḥ |

pṛthukāstat samānaḥ syuḥ saktavopitathoditāḥ || 6-19 ||

guḍaṃ palārdhamānaṃ syārcchakarāpitathoditā |

grāsārdhaṃ carumānaṃ syād ikṣuḥ pūrvāvadhirmataḥ || 6-20 ||


67b) mayūrāṃḍasamogrāsastadardhaṃ mayūrāṃḍasamaṃ 
mayūraviṃvasamam ||


ekaikaṃ patrapuṣpāṇi tathā pūpāṇi kalpayet |

kadalīphalanāraṃga phalānyekaikaśoviduḥ || 6-21 ||

mātuluṃgaṃ catuṣavaṃḍaṃ panasaṃdaśadhā kṛtam |


mātuluṃgaṃ niṃvaphalaṃ ekaikaṃ māturuṃgānītyekaṃ kecidihācire ||


aṣṭadhānālikelāni khaṇḍitāni vidurvudhāḥ || 6-22 ||


tridhākṛtvānya nyānyakhaṇḍāni samidhaḥ syurdaśāṅgulāḥ ||


aṃguṣṭhādadhikāgrāhyāḥ samidhaḥ sthūlataḥ kvacit |

navimuktatvavāścaiva nasakīṭāna pāṭitāḥ || 6-23 ||

prādeśānnādhikārānastathā nasyā dviśākhikā |


nādhikānonā iti anyunanatirikta ityarthaḥ naviśākhikā iti śākhārahitā 
ityarthaḥ nasaparṇānanirvīryā homeṣu ca vijānatā ||

nanīrvīryānaśuṣkā ||


prāgagraḥ samīdhodeyaḥ puṣṭārthaṃ vijānatā || 6-24 ||

śārthaṃ satva ca smārdrāviparītā jighāṃsayā |

viśīrṇāvidalāhutvā vakrāḥ sasuṣirāstathā || 6-25 ||

dīrghāḥ sthūlā vvudhai? juṣṭā karmasiddhivināśakā |

sasuṣirāḥ sagarttāḥ śrīsomaśaṃbhuramyāha || 6-26 ||


pramāṇaṃ mūlakādiṣu ||


khaṇḍatrayaṃ ca mūlānāṃ phalānā svasvamānataḥ |

prāsādamānamannānāmakṣāni paṃcahomayet || 6-27 ||

drakṣoroparvikaṃ mānaṃ latānāmagulaṃ dvayam |

kaṃdānāmaṣṭamaṃ bhāgaṃ juhuyādvidhināparam || 6-28 ||

dūrvātrayaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ guḍūcīcaturaṃgulā |

vrīhayomuṣṭimātrāḥ syurmudgamāṣaya vā api || 6-29 ||

68a) tīlāśculukamātrāḥ syuḥ sarṣapāśca tathāditāḥ |

kaiścidāhutiṣu proktāḥ karṣārdhārdhāstu sarṣapāḥ || 6-30 ||

śuktipramāṇalavaṇaṃ maricānya *? viṃśatiḥ |


śuktirardhapalayāṣakacatuṣṭayamiti kecit |


puraṃ vadaramānaṃ syād rāmaṭhaṃ tat samaṃ smṛtam || 6-31 ||

caḍaraṃgulimānaṃ syād vyaṃjanaṃ kṛsaraṃ tathā |


caturaṃgulimāna dvādaśaparvapramāṇaṃ kṛsaraṃ tilataṇḍūlam ||


tilakatkṣaṃ tathā proktaṃ mantratantraviśāradaiḥ || 6-32 ||

candanāgurukapūra kastūrīkuṃkumāni ca |

tintiḍīvījamānāni kathitāni manīṣibhiḥ || 6-33 ||

śrīsomaśaṃbhunā proktapramāṇaṃ candanādiṣu |

kalāyasammitānetā gugguluṃ vadarāsthivat || 6-34 ||

ghanasāraścaṃndanāni kastūrīkuṃkumāni ca |

caṇakapramitānyanyaiḥ proktā *? gamavedibhiḥ || 6-35 ||

anukte tu havirdravye tilājyaṃ harirucyate |

anukte cāṃjanadravye ghṛtamevāṃjanaṃ viduḥ || 6-36 ||

saṃkhyānuktauhasasrasyād dravyānuktau ghṛtaṃ tathā |

samidhānuktaviṣayapālāśyaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 6-37 ||

daksiṇāmūrtti tantre tu prāhaitacchāṅga saṃsthitā |

ājyaṃ dravyamānādeśe homeṣu ca vidhīyate || 6-38 ||

samuddiṣṭamidaṃ sarvaṃ smṛti dṛṣṭena vatmanā |

lājādihomo hastena sāṅgulo na vidhīyate || 6-39 ||

samipuṣpaphalādīnāṃ homaḥ syān mṛgamudrayā |

dravya dravyastu vaḥ proktā mantratatra viśāradaḥ || 6-40 ||

milināmikāṅguṣṭhe mamāsrāṇi yojayet |

68b) śiṣṭhāṅgulyan sṛtāṃ kuryān mṛgamudreyamīritā || 6-41 ||

prasaṃgācchāstroktṃ premāṇamabhidhīyate |

paṃcakṛṣṇalakomāṣastesuvarṇāstu ṣoḍaśa || 6-42 ||

palaṃ suvarṇāścatvāro niṣkamaṣṭottaraṃ śatam |

niṣkamakṣaṃ vadantyeke niṣkaṃpalamathāpale || 6-43 ||

dveguje rūpyamāṣaḥ syāt palānidharaṇo daśa |

dvātriṃśat palakaṃ prasthaṃ svayamuktaṃ svayaṃbhuvā || 6-44 ||

āḍhakaṃ tu catuḥ prasthaṃ catubhiḥ droṇamāḍhakaiḥ |

rāśimānaṃ tadevasyāt vārīṣoḍaśabhiścataiḥ || 6-45 ||


viṃśatyā ca bhavet kuṃbhau vāhaḥ syā daśabhiśca taikhārīṇāṃ tritrayaṃ 
bhāra uditaḥ śāstrataḥ kramāt ||


dve sahasre tu lābhāro bhāraḥ samyagudīritaḥ || 6-46 ||

bhārasya daśamāṃśe na hāramicchanti kecana |

muṣṭipalapramānaṃ syād dvepale prasṛti bhavet || 6-47 ||

aṣṭamuṣṭiṃ bhavet kuṃcaḥ kuṃcasyāṣṭau tu puṣkalam |

puṣkalāni ca catvāri āḍhakaṃ tadanukramāt || 6-48 ||

aṣṭau tu puṣkalānyevaṃ pūrṇapātramathocira |

catuḥ ṣaṣṭyāpalaiḥ kumbhaṃ (ḥ) catuḥ prasthamudīrayet || 6-49 ||

dvepale ca prasaṃgaḥ syāt prasavaṃ tadyugaṃ bhavet |

tadyugaṃ kula vaṃ vighnaṃ prasthaṃ pātramathaḥ kramāt || 6-50 ||

tathāḍhakaṃ śivamathadreṇaṃ khārīti kecanaḥ |

paṃcaguṃjo bhavet māṣastekṣaḥ kaṣastu ṣodaśa || 6-51 ||

māṣāstu dvādaśai vākṣa iti viṣṇurihā vravīt |

69a) vaidike paribhāṣai ṣā rāgameṣu prapūjyate || 6-52 ||

tasmāt prasṛtimukṣyādi (tasmāc)cuśukti vacculukādivat |

laukikaṃ tadupādeyamiti cāgamavedinaḥ || 6-53 ||

vaiśvānaraṃ sthitaṃ dhyāyet sasiddho meṣu daiśikaḥ |

śiyānamājyahomeṣu niṣaṣṇośeṣavastuṣu || 6-54 ||

asyāntarjuhuyād agne vipaścit sarvakarmasu |

karṇahome bhaved vyādhirnetrendhatvamudīritam || 6-55 ||

nāsikāyāṃ manaḥ pīḍā mastake dhanasaṃkṣapaḥ |

yataḥ koṣṭhaṃ tataḥ śrotraṃ yatodhūmotranāsikā || 6-56 ||

yatrālpajvalanaṃ netraṃ yatoṃgārastataḥ śiraḥ |

yatra prajvālitā jvālā sājihvājātavedasaḥ || 6-57 ||


jātavedaso vahneḥ ||


svarṇasiṃdūravārārka kuṃkumakṣaudrasaṃnibhaḥ |

suvarṇaretaso varṇaḥ śobhanaḥ parikīrttitaḥ || 6-58 ||

bhelīvāridahastendra dhvanirvahniḥ śubhāvahaḥ |

nāgacaṇpakapunnāṭ pāṭalā yūthikānibheḥ || 6-59 ||

padmendīvarakat-hāra sarpirgugguluṃ saṃnibhaḥ |

pāvakasya śubhogaṃdha ityustantravedibhiḥ || 6-60 ||

pradakṣiṇāstyaktaṃ pācchantrābhāḥ śikhinaḥ śikhaḥ |

śubhadāyajamānasya rājyasyāpi viśeṣataḥ || 6-61 ||

kundendudhavalodhūmo vahneḥ proktaḥ śubhāvahaḥ |

kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇagatervarṇā yajamānaṃ vināśayet || 6-62 ||


kṛṣṇagate vahneḥ ||


śvetorājyaṃ nihantyāśu vāyasasvarasaṃnibhaḥ |

kharasvarasamo vahne dhvaniḥ sarvavināśakṛt || 6-63 ||

pūtigaṃgohutavaho hoturduḥkhapradā bhavet |

69b) cchinnāvṛtyāśikhā kuryāt mṛtyuṃ dhanaparikṣayam (ḥ) || 6-64 ||

śukapakṣe nibhodhumaḥ pārāvatasamaprabhaḥ |

hāniṃ tu ragajātīnāṃ gavāṃ ca kurute cirāt || 6-65 ||

evaṃ videṣu doṣeṣa prāyaścittāya daiśikaḥ |

mūlenājyanajuhuyāt paṃcaviṃśatirāhutī || 6-66 ||


ityāgamakalpalatāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 6 ||



sāmānya pūjāṃ vakṣyāmi mantrīṇāṃ siddhidāyinīm |

athaprātasamutthāya dhīmānvrāhme muhurttake || 7-1 ||

brahmarandhrasthite padme sahasradalaśobhite |

śrīguruṃ paramātmānaṃ vyākhyā mudrālasatkaram || 7-2 ||

dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ prītaṃ dhyāyed akhilasiddhidam |

evaṃ dhyātvā ca tat pādasmṛtapīyuṣadhārayā || 7-3 ||

prakṣālitaṃ nijaṃ dehaṃ saṃcintyasthiramānasaḥ |

svīya hṛtkamale dhyātvā yathāvat sveṣṭhadevatām || 7-4 ||

tat prabhā paṭalavyāptaṃ dehaṃ svaṃ paricintayet |

praṇamyāgyāṃ gṛhītvāsya ṛṣyādi nyāsa pūrvakam || 7-5 ||

mantrārthaṃ saṃsmaraṃ stīpa gatvā śākhoktavartmanā |

snātvā paścāt svamantreṇa ṛṣyādinyāsa pūrvakam || 7-6 ||

dhyātveṣṭa devatāṃ tīrthaṃ ācāhyāṅkuśamudrayā |

tīrthāvāhanaṃ gaṃge ca yamune caiveti mantreṇa || 7-7 ||

vamityamṛtavījena mudrayā dhenusaṃjñayā |

amṛtī kṛta tat tīrthaṃ śareṇaiva tu rakṣayet || 7-8 ||

tajjale devatā mūrttiṃ dhyātvā taccaraṇasrute |

tīrthe nimajyatrirvidvān manasā mantramuccalam || 7-9 ||

kṛtvāghamarśaṇabhūyaḥ saṃdhyādi kṛta pūrvakam |

70a) japtvā mulamanuṃ tvaṣṭāviśati pramitaṃ tataḥ || 7-10 ||

vakṣyamāṇena mantreṇa tāvat saṃkhyaṃ pratarpayet |

abhiṣiṃcet tato mūrdhni tadvat kalaśamudrayā || 7-11 ||

tantu devasya gāyatrīṃ japitvā paktiśastathā |

oṃ ghṛṇiḥ sūrya ādityebhyeṣṭāṇi na ca bhānave || 7-12 ||

arghaṃ dattvāthamūlena daśadhāmantitraijalaiḥ |

ācamya dikpatīrnatvā gacched deve gṛhaṃ tataḥ || 7-13 ||


vamityamṛtavījenāmṛtīkaraṇam |

snānasya vahuvidhatvāt prakṛte nimajjanaṃ aghamarṣaṇamiti śūdretaraviṣayam ||

tarpaṇaṃ tu ||

santarpapīṭhamantraiḥ sakṛt sakṛt prathamamacyutaṃ tatra ||

avāhya pūjayet tanto yairevākhilai samupahāraiḥ |

vaddhāthadhenumudrāṃ toyaiḥ saṃpādya tarpaṇadravyam ||

tadvuddhyāñjalinābhaṃ suvarṇacaṣakī kṛte natarpaya tu |

bhūyaḥ svakalpavihitān sakṛt sakṛrttarpayet parivārān ||


tarpayāmi padaṃ yojyaṃ mantrānte sveṣunāmasu |

dvitīyāṃ teṣu ca punaḥ pūjā śeṣaṃ samāpayet || 7-14 ||


iti prayogādau ||

gāyatryaścānusaṃdheyāḥ ||

ghṛṇiḥ sūrya iti mantrastu dvijaviṣaya eva vaidikatvāntanmantrasya ||

hrā/ hrī/ saḥ sūryāya namaḥ ityādinā mantrātareṇa śūdrādinārghodeyaḥ ||


tato dvārādi pūjā vai karttavyābhūti micchatā |

ācamya vidhivat kuryāt sāmānyārghaṃ tu tadyathā || 7-15 ||

astreṇapātraṃ saśodhya hṛnmantreṇa prapūjya ca |

tīrthamatreṇa cāvāhya tīrthaṃ saṃvit pramaṃḍalāt || 7-16 ||

70b) tajjale gaṃdhapuṣpāṇi nikṣipet tānamuccaran |

astreṇa śodhayitvai tat smṛtvā? mulaṃ japentrissaḥ || 7-17 ||

darśayed dhenumudrāṃ ca sāmānyārghāyamīritaḥ |


astreṇāstramantreṇa phaḍiti matreṇatyarthaḥ |

hṛnmantreṇa nama iti mantrena ||


gaṃge ca yamune caiva goḍāvari sarasvati || 7-18 ||

iti ||

eṣa tīrthamanuḥ prokto duritaughavināśanaḥ |


tīrthāvahanamaṃkuśamudraye tīrthaḥ ||


tajjalena sāmānyārghajalena tāramurccaran || 7-19 ||


oṃkāramuccaran ||

spṛṣṭvārghajalaṃ spṛṣṭvā mūlaṃ mūlamantram ||

tajjalairastramantreṇa dvāramatyupūjayet |


vighnaṃ dvārobhyaśākhāyāṃ mahālakṣmī sarasvatīm |


etan tritayapūjanaṃ tu prāthakrameṇaiva ||

dvārordhvaśākhāyāmiti |


dvārasya dakṣiṇamadhya vā madhyavāmabhāgeṣu || 7-20 ||


taduktaṃ pūrṇaparddhatau ||

dvārasya dakṣiṇe vāmagāṇaṃ ca sarasvatīm |


urdhvoduṃvarake madhya tato dvāraśriyaṃ yajet |

tato dakṣiṇaśākhāyāṃ vighnaṃ kṣetreśamanyataḥ || 7-21 ||

tayoḥ pārśve gate gaṅgā yamune puṣpavāribhiḥ |

dhātāraṃ ca vidhātāraṃ śaṃkhapadmanidhī tathā || 7-22 ||

dehalyāmarccayedastraṃ pratidvāramiti kramāt |


dakṣiṇavāmaśākhāyoḥ krameṇa gaṅgāyamune pūjanīya |

taduktaṃ pūrṇapaddhatau ||

gaṅgā ca yamunāṃ tadvad dakṣasavyastaśākhayo || riti atra kecit ||

71a) vigneśadakṣapārśvayogaṃ gā ca mune evaṃ kṣetreśapārśvayorapi ||

evaṃ dhātrādayo pītyāhuḥ ||

tanna ||

vacanārthāsaṃgateḥ ||

uktaṃ vacanavirodhācca ||


dvārāntaḥ pārśvayoḥ pūjyā gaṃgā ca yamunānadī || 7-23 ||

koṇeṣu vighnaṃ durgā ca vāṇī kṣetre samarccayet || iti ||


koṇeṣu dvārakoṇeṣviti kalpadrumaḥ ||

tat pramāṇā bhāvāt ||

tato dakṣiṇaśakhāyāmityādi vahuvacanavidhānā cca tatastat tadevatāyāstat tad 
vārādhipānyajet |

tatostra pūjānaṃ tapam ||


viṣṇo dvārādhipānanda surandacandasaṃjñakā || 7-24 ||

pracaṃḍāthavalaścaiva pravalobhadra eva ca |

subhadraścaiva saṃproktāḥ śaivāddvārādhipaḥ punaḥ || 7-25 ||

naṃdisaṃjño mahākāla gaṇeśā vṛṣabhastathā |

bhṛgirīṭastathā skaṃda umā caṇḍeśvarāstvamī || 7-26 ||

vakratuṇḍaścaikadraṃṣṭro mahodaragajānanau |

laṃvodarothavikaṭo vighnarājāhvayo paraḥ || 7-27 ||

dhūmravarṇastvime vaighnāḥ śāktiyomātaraḥ smṛtāḥ |

brāhmīmāheśvarī bhūyaḥ kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā || 7-28 ||

vārāhyanantarendrāṇī cāmuṇḍā saptamī tathā |

aṣṭamī syān mahālakṣmīḥ proktā viśvasya mātaraḥ || 7-29 ||

indroyamothavaruṇaḥ kuverastraipurāmatāḥ |


catudvārinandāda yastu yugmaṇaḥ kuverādayastvekaikaṃ kaithilāḥ 
punaridānīṃ vighnotsāraṇaṃ vahiriti vadanti ||

71b) tannavahiruccyaraṇasya prayojanā bhāvāt |

vahirvighnotsāraṇenāntarvighnotsāraṇā sirddhavastuto yāgamaṃḍapāntaḥ 
śodhanārthameva tat |

tathācāntare veti dik ||


kiṃcit spṛśan vāme śākhā dehalīṃ liṃghayed guruḥ || 7-30 ||

aṃgaṃ saṃkocayannantaḥ praviśen dakṣiṇāṅghiṇā |


vāmaśākhāṃ vāmāvasthitaśākhām |


anantaraṃ daiśikendro divyadṛṣṭyāvarlokanāt || 7-31 ||

divyānutsārayed vighnān astrād bhiścāntarikṣagān |

pārṇighātaistribhirbhaumān nitivighnānnivārayet || 7-32 ||


divyadṛṣṭyā mūlamatreṇa ūrdhvadṛṣṭyā astrād bhirahitāya phaḍiti mantraḥ tat 
sahitād bhirityarthaḥ antarikṣagānatarikṣastad bhūjān tataḥ 
siddhārthākṣatakusumaṃ nārāvāstradhiyā guruḥ |

apasarpaṃ tu ye bhūtā iti ||


ityantestraṃ samuccārya maṇḍapāntaḥ parikṣipet |


pārṇighātānāha ||

vāmapādaṃ tato bhūmau hatvāvāratraya tataḥ || 7-33 ||

tatastālatrayaṃ dadyād ūrdhvodhvaṃ vaṃdhanediśām |

pradakṣiṇākrameṇa tricchoṭikābhiḥ punaḥ punaḥ || 7-34 ||


iti ||

taduktaṃ suṃdarī rahasyavṛttau ||

āgamakalpadrume ca |


naiṛtyāṃ diśivāsvīśaṃ brahmāṇaṃ ca samarcayet |

paṃcagavyārghatoyābhyāṃ prakṣiped yāgamaṇḍapam || 7-35 ||


paṃcagavyaprakāramāha ||

smṛtiśāstre ||


gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣiraṃ dadhisarpikuśodakam |

paṃcagavyamidaṃ prāhurmantratantraviśāradāḥ || 7-36 ||

gomūtrasya ca bhāgārdhaṃ śakṛt kṣīrasya ca trayam |

72a) dvayaṃ dadhnoghṛtasyaikaṃ mekaṃ ca kuśavāriṇaḥ || 7-37 ||

gāyatryādāyagomūtraṃ gaṃdhadvāretigomayam |

āpyāyasveti ca kṣīraṃ dadhijvāvaṇeti vai dadhi || 7-38 ||

tejosīti ghṛtaṃ caiva devasya tvākuśodakam |

mantrādyaparijñānena praṇave naiva śodhayet || 7-39 ||

tathā ||

āhṛtyapraṇave naiva utthāpyapraṇavena ca |

praṇavena samālotrya praṇavena punaḥ pivet || 7-40 ||

paṃcagavyena tad dehaṃ maṇḍapaṃ ca viśodhayet |


tadūktaṃ gotamīye ||


palamātraṃ dugdhabhāgaṃ gomūtratāvadviṣyate || 7-41 ||

ghṛtaṃ ca palamātraṃ syād gomayaṃ tolakaṃ dvayam |

dadhiprasṛtamātraṃ syāt paṃcagvyamida smṛtam || 7-42 ||

athavā paṃcagavyānāṃ samāno bhāga iṣyate |

mūlamantreṇa saṃmntrya kuśāgreṇaiva śodhayet || 7-43 ||

tena sarvaviśuddhiḥ syāt sarvapāpo nikṛntanam |


tathā tīkṣaṇadaṃṣṭramahākāyeti ||


ityanujñāṃ gṛhītvā sya japasthānaṃ viśodhayet || 7-44 ||

catuṣprakāraṃ tacchuddhiṃ vidadhyādvikṣaṇādibhiḥ |

vīkṣaṇaṃ mūlamantre śareṇaprokṣaṇaṃ matam || 7-45 ||

cadanāgurukapūraidhūpayedantaraṃ svadhiḥ |


anantaraṃ maṇḍapasya ||


vikirānvikirantratrajaptajaptān śarānunā || 7-46 ||

rājacandanasiddhārtha bhasmadūrvākuśākṣataḥ |

vikirā iti saṃdiṣṭā mahāvighnaughanāśakāḥ || 7-47 ||

uktesthavāsaneṣvekaṃ vairājinakuśottaram |

vedikāyāṃ pravinyasya prakṣayet saliḥlaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 7-48 ||

ādhārādipṛthivyantastatra saṃpūjya devatāḥ |

72b) meruḥ pṛṣṭhaṛṣiḥ chandaḥ pṛthivyāḥ sutaraṃ matam || 7-49 ||

devatā kūrma ākhyātaḥ saṃsmared ṛṣipūrvakam (ḥ) |


pṛthvi tvayā dhṛtā lokā iti mantraṃ japan pīṭhe prāṅmukho vāpyudaṅmukhaḥ 
||


svastikādikrameṇaiva ṛjukāyo viśedvudhaḥ || 7-50 ||


svastikādikramaśvān naprapaṃcenusaṃdheyaḥ ādhārādi ādhāraśaktyādi 
vaddhapadmāsano maunī iti suvāsitāṃvuṃ sapannamiti bhūtaśuddhi tataḥ 
kuryād iti ca viparītaphalaṃ dadyād abhaktyā pūjanaṃ yathā ||

iti tadakaraṇe nindyārthavādaśruteḥ |

japādyaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ iti ityanena mātṛkā nyāsasya ca tathā tvāt |

tathoktaṃ kramadīpikāyām ||

ityucyatī kṛta tanu nyāsam iti vacanānurodhācca tau pūjād āvaśyakau tayordeva 
bhāvārpakatvāditi |

tathoktaṃ kramadīpikāyām ||


snāto nirmaladhautaśuṣkavasano dhautāṅghipāṇyānanaḥ 

snācāntaḥ supavitramudritakaraḥ śvetodhvapunttrojvalaḥ |

prācīdiśvadano nivaddhasudṛḍhaṃ padmāsanaṃ svastikaṃ

vāsinaḥ svagurun gaṇādhipamato vandeta vaddhāṃjaliḥ || 7-51 ||


kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā vāmadakṣiṇapārśvayoḥ |

natvā guruṃ gaṇeśānaṃ bhūtaśuddhi samācaret || 7-52 ||


pāṭhakrameṇaiva namaskāraḥ ||

vāmaguruṃ dakṣiṇato gaṇeśaṃ durgāṃ puraḥ kṣatrapatiṃ ca paścāt |

ityenena durgā kṣatrapālayorapi kecit ||


tathā puṭāṃjali bhūtvā vāme gurutrayaṃ jagat |

dakṣiṇe ca gaṇeśāna mūrdhi daivenatiṃ caret || 7-53 ||


iti kalpataruḥ ||


73a) oṃ sahasrāra varmāstraṃ sudarśanamanuḥ smṛtaḥ |


oṃ sahasrāra hu/ phaṭ ||

iti mantra ||


suṃgaṃdapuṣpamadena surabhīkṛta hastakaḥ || 7-54 ||

kṣiped uttarataḥ puṣpamantrīnā rā ca mudrayā |


iti pūjāpradīpaḥ ||

atha bhūtaśuddhi pradīpaḥ ||

athāstramanteṇa viśuddhā pāṇītritāladigdhaṃ vahutāśaśālān |

vidhāya bhūtātmakaṃ etad aṃgaṃ viśodhayecchuddhamatikrameṇa |


tritālamūrdhvordhvamastramantrasahitam |


ūrdhvordhvamastramantreṇa kuryān tālatrayaṃ tataḥ |

tena saṃjanitaṃ tejaḥ kuryād rakṣāṃ samaṃ tataḥ || 7-55 ||


iti vacanāt ||

hutāśālaṃ vahniprākāram ||

tataḥ ||


suṣumaṇā vartmanātmānaparamātmaniyojayet |

yogayuktena vidhinā cinmantreṇa samāhitaḥ || 7-56 ||


kāreṇa sarvabhūtānāṃ tat svānyapi vijabhāvena līlāti vyutkramāt 
paramātmani cinmantra sohamiti mantraḥ |

manunā haṃsadevasya ityanena haṃsa iti mantreṇa ānayanamiti pūjāpradīpaḥ |


naitāvatā sohamiti mantrasiddhiḥ vastutaḥ |

cinmantreṇa vyutkumeṇa yojayet paramātmani || 7-57 ||


iti śrūteḥ ||

ubhayo cinmantratvamaviruddhamiti tattvāni paṃcaviṃśatiścaturviṃśati vā |

taduktaṃ prapaṃcasāre ||

bhūtendriyendriyāthairuddiṣṭaṃ tattva paṃcaviṃśatikaṃ 
karaṇopetairataistattvān yuktānyavihitavacanādyairiti |

73b) indriyāṇi jñānendriyāṇi paṃcakarmendriyāṇi paṃceti daśa 
indriyānāmarthośca daśaḥ antakaraṇam ||


maṇovuddhirahaṃkāraścittaṃ ca parakīrttitam |


iti ||

pṛthivyaptejo vāyokāśāḥ gaṃdharasarūpasparśaśabdaghrāṇarasanā-
cakṣustvak 
śrottravākpāṇi pādapāyupastha vacanā dānagamanavisargānaṃdātmaka 
paṃcaviṃśatitattvāni vījabhāvena tatra līnāni vicintya ||

iḍāvaktre dhūmaṃ sa tata *? tivījaṃ saravakaṃ sarenmantrī 
pūrvasakalabhūvanocchoṣaṇakaram ||

svakaṃ dehaṃ tena pratatavapuṣā puryasakalaviśokhyavyā muṃcet 
pavanapathamārgena khamaṇe |

te naiva mārgena vilīnamārutaṃ vījaṃ vicintyāruṇamāśuśukṣaṇaiḥ ||

āpūryadehaṃ paridahya vāmato muṃcat samīraṃ sahabhasmanā va ||

khamaṇe mārgena sūryavatmanā ||

tathā cobhayapakṣamapi ||

pramāṇikam ||

amarendrapurogatena sakālaprathamena repheṇetyarthaḥ ||

pūrakaṃ ṣoḍaśamātrayā ||

kuṃbhakaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭhimātrayā recakaṃ dvātriṃśa mātrayeti bhāvaḥ ||

ayamapi prāṇāyāmaviśeṣaḥ ||

prāṇāyāme ca tathā vasya vakṣyamānatvāt ||

atra kecit yā pa puruṣasya śodhanādikaṃ vadanti tanmate kukṣau pāpapuruṣaṃ 
cintayet |

74a) yatastataḥ puruṣanibhaṃ pāpamanādibhavasaṃcitam ||

brahmahatyā śiraskaṃ ityādi vāmakukṣau vicintayeti yāvat ||

tataḥ saṃdhayed dehamityādi gurutalpagamane netyarthaḥ |

vidadhyān mātṛkā nyāsaṃ iti vacanān mātṛkā nyāsasyāvaśakatvam ||

tathā mātṛkāmantrasya ṛṣibrahmā samuddiṣṭo gāyatrīcchaṃda īrītṃ ityādi 
paṃcāṅgāni manoryasya tatra |

manuṃ tyajed iti tāvat ||


aṃgahīnasya mantrasya svenaivāṅgāni kalpayet || 7-58 ||


jātayaśca namaḥ svāhā vaṣaṭ huṃ vai vauṣaṭ phaṭkāra eva ca ||


jātiṣaṭkaṃ idaṃ proktaṃ dvadādyaṣṭāṃgasaṃyutam |


astraṃ tu tarayoriti yadyapi astraṃ talayo nyased iti vacanāt paṃcāṅgapakṣa 
astraṃ nyāsa ||

karatarayoreva tathāpi racaya tu karadvaṃdvapaṃcāṅgaṃ aṃgulipaṃcaka iti 
kramadīpikā vacanā ||

kaniṣṭhayorevāstra nyāsa iti kavacamantrastu paṃcamasvarasaṃyukto hakāraḥ 
paṃcamaḥ svaraḥ |

hrasva-ukārastadyukto hakāraḥ saviṃdurityarthaḥ tena huṃ iti vījaṃ bhavati tathā 
kavacāyeti pūrvavat ||

kavacamantra ||

sadaurṣaṭ śikhāhumiti varmaṇi sthitamiti kramadīpikā vacanāt |

anyathā tatra cchaṃdo bhaṃgāpatyaḥ ataḥ tvaritā mantre dīrghaṃ tanu 
cchadamityabhihitam ||

74b) tathā cobhayoreva kavacamantratva māyāti netratrayāyeti saṃbhavaduktikaṃ 
idaṃ yatra tu netradvayaṃ tatra netrābhyāmiti ||

aṃgahīnasyānuktāṅgamantrasya sve naiva mūlamantreṇaiva atra karanyāse 
aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ nama ityādi prayogasya pramāṇābhāvāt ||

tadaṃgapaṃcakaṃ tathā yathā samīritaṃ kare ||

iti kramadīpikā vacanācca hṛdayāye nama ityeva prayogaḥ ||

iti pūjāpradīpaḥ ||

tanna ||

aṃguṣṭhādiṣvaṃguliṣu nyased aṃgaiḥ sajātibhiriti sāradātiraka 
vacanānurodhāt ||

tasyārthaḥ ||

aṃguṣṭhasajātibhiraṃguṣṭhādiṣvaṃguleṣu nyased ityarthaḥ ||

navāṅguli sā jābhyaṃ hṛdayādiṣvapīti vācyam vyavahāravirodhād iti kiṃ 
cakramadīpikā vacanasyāpyayamevārthaḥ ||

yathā ||

yan prakāreṇāṅgapaṃcakaṃ kare samīritaṃ tathā tan prakāreṇaiva hṛdaye pīti 
||

api ca ||

ādau cāṅgasamuccārya mantraṃ paścān nyaset sudhīriti vacanavirodhāt ||

karanyāse ityabhidhānasya vyarthatvāt ||

tasmād aṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ nama ityeva prayogaḥ ||

vahnijāyānte vacanāt svāhādipadaprayogo nusaraṇīyastarjanyādau eva 
karāṃganyāsau vidhāyamātṛkā nyāsaṃ kuryāt ||

yathā ||

paṃcāśallipibhirvibhakta mukhadopan madhyavakṣasthalāṃ bhāsvan 
maulinivaddhacaṃdraśakalāmopīnatuṃgastanīm ||

mudrāmakṣaguṇaṃ sadādhyakalaśaṃ vidyā ca hastāṃvujaiviprāṇāṃ 
viṣadaprabhā trinayanāṃ vāgdevatāmāśraya ||

75a) paṃcāśalipibhiriti akārādikṣakārāntalipibhiryathākrameṇāṅgasaptakaṃ 
cintanīyam |

taduktaṃ prapaṃcaśāre ||

a ka ca ṭa ta payādyaiḥ sastabhiḥ varṇavarge viracita mukhavāhupādamadhyākhya 
hṛtketi mudrātikaṃ dakṣiṇādhaḥ krameṇa prādakṣiṇyena mudrā jñānamudram 
||

tathā aṃguṣṭhatarjanīyogā jñānamudrā pakīrttiteti |

sthānānyāha ||



lalāṭamukhavṛrttākṣi śrutighrāṇeṣugaṇḍayoḥ || 7-59 ||

oṣṭhadantottamāṅgāsya doḥ pasaṃdhyagrakeṣu ca |

pāśvayoḥ pṛṣṭhato nābhau jaṭhare hṛdayāṃsake || 7-60 ||

kakudyaṃse ca hṛtpūrvapāṇipādayuge api |

jaṭharānanayornyaset mātṛkārṇānyathākramam || 7-61 ||


keṣāṃ cinmata hṛdayādiṣu aṃsādiṣurapha nyāsaḥ ||

pādayoḥ śaṣoḥ bhujayośca honnyāsaḥ |


omādyanto namāntavā savinduvinduvarjitā |

paṃcāsadakṣaranyāsaḥ krameṇaiva vidhīyate || 7-62 ||


iti viśvasāraśraveṇāt ||

atra kevalānāṃ savisargāṇā ca sṛṣṭikrameṇa visargaviṃdusahitānāṃ 
sthitikrameṇa viṃdunāṃ sṃhārakrameṇa nyāsaḥ ||

sthityādau nyāse viśeṣaḥ ||

taduktaṃ śāradātilake ||


nyasessargānvitāṃ sṛjyā dhyātvā devī yathā vidhi |

sargacindvanti kānyasaṃḍḍārṇāyāṃ sthiti vartmanā || 7-63 ||


tathā ca vaiśaṃ pāyanasaṃhitāyā ||

75b) ṭaparamatī va śubhramamṛtāṃ śupathena vidhum ||

naya tu lalāṭaṃ cadramamutaḥ sakalārṇamayīm |

laparajapānnipātpara ca yeccata yā sakalaṃ 
vapulamṛtaughavattimathavakrakarādyamidam ||

ṭapanaṃ vidhuṃ ṭhamiti cadravījamītyarthaḥ amutolalāṭacaṃdrāt ||

laparajapāllapālakārasya parovakārastasya japāt |

vamitya mṛtavījajapāt |

amṛtaughnavṛtti nipātyatayā vaśiṣṭhabhasmanāsakaraṃ vapuḥ racayediti 
pradīpaḥ ||

evaṃ ||


tataḥ śoṣayed dehaṃ vāyuvījena vāyunā |

vahnivījena te naiva saṃdahet sakalāṃ tanum || 7-64 ||

viśleṣayestadoṣāna mṛtanāmṛtāṃbhasā |

āpādyaplāvayed dehe māpādatalamastakam || 7-65 ||

saṃjāte nirmaladehe devatārādhanakṣame |

ātmalinānitattvāni svasthānaṃ prāpayet tataḥ || 7-66 ||

ātmānaṃ hṛdayāṃbhojamānayet paramātmanaḥ |

manunā haṃsadevasya kuryān nyāsādikaṃ tataḥ || 7-67 ||


nyāsādikaṃ svatantroktaṃ haṃsadeva mantraḥ haṃsa iti ||

uktaṃ ca sārasamuccaye ||

saṃyojya jīvamathadurgama madhyanāḍīmārgeṇa puṣkaraniviṣṭaśiveṣu 
sūkṣmye ||

haṃsena devamamarendrapurogate nasārdhandunābhasitamādvidadhītavuddhyā ||

tathā cobhayapakṣamapi prāmāṇikam ||


vindusargānvitāṃ nyased gulphādi sthiti vartmanā |

jānvantaṃ tu tato devī gṛhasthānāṃ bhavedayam || 7-68 ||

ḍakārādiṭhākārāntāṃ yojayet paradevatām |

iti ||

vidyāt pūrvoditān vidvān ṛṣyādīnagasaṃyutān || 7-69 ||

76a) dhyāyed varṇeśvarīmatra vallabhena samanvitām |


vallabho maheśastena yutam ||

tasya dhyānamāha |


siṃdūrakāntim asitābharaṇaṃ trinetraṃ vidyākṣasutra mṛgayo 
tavadāndadhāram |

pārśva sthitāṃ bhagavatimapi kāṃcanāṅgī dhyāyet karābjadhṛta 
pustakavarṇamālām || 7-70 ||


viśeṣāna vidhānād vindusargarahitopi sṛṣṭikrameṇaiva nyāsaḥ 
prathamopasthitatvāt |


akārādikṣakārānta nyāsa eva sṛṣṭiprakāraḥ sthitītivyā caṣṭe ṭārṇād yāmiti 
||

takārādikṣakāraparyantaṃ varṇānvinyaset takārādi aikārāntā vinyasediti || 7-
71 ||


kevalavinduyukta nyāsamāha ||


vinduyuktāṃ imāṃ nyasyat saṃhṛtyā pratilomataḥ |

vidyā pūrvoditā nyāsān ṛṣyādīnaṃgasaṃyutān || 7-72 ||

dhyāyed varṇamaye padme devīṃ vāgvallabhāśivām |


akṣasrajaṃ hariṇapotamudragraṭeṃkāṃ vidyāṃ karairavirataṃ dadhatīṃ 
trinetrām ||

arddhendumaulimaruṇāmaraviṃdabhāsāṃ varṇeśvarī praṇamataṃ 
stanabhāranamrām || 7-73 ||


saṃhṛtya (tya)sya vivaraṇaṃ pratilomata iti || tathā ca ||

kṣakāramārabhyākāraparyantaṃ nyased ityarthaḥ ||

anyaprakāraśca gauravahayānnalikhitaḥ ||

prāṇāyāmaṃ pravakṣyāmi iti ||

76b) pavanaseyamanaṃ tvamunā caredyamuhajaptumasaumanumicchati |

athavā akhileṣu harimantrajapādiṣu mūlamantra ta ityanena 
mūlamantrasyābhidhātāt || athavā ||

aṃgajanmamanunā susaṃyamaṃ sakaleṣu kṛṣṇajapakarmasusaditaika-

vījasyāpyebhidhānāt ||

tasmād ubhayapakṣakīttanād ekadeśenvayaḥ ||

tathā cecchāvikalpa ||

mūlamantroṣṭākṣaragopālamantraḥ ||

kramadīpikāyāṃ tathaivamūlamantreṇābhidhānād daśākṣaro vā ||

pakṣāntaramāha ||


athavā sādhakaḥ kuryāt praṇaveṇā susaṃyamam |

strībhiḥ śudraiśca karttavyo laukikena susaṃyama || 7-74 ||

triṣad vā daśavāraṃ vā parataḥ paratau japet |


parataḥ parata iti paraḥ para ityarthaḥ ||


śuciḥ prāṇāyāmān praṇavasahitān ṣoḍaśavaśī prabhāte sāyaṃ ca 
pratidivasamevaṃ vitanute ||

dvijojastaṃbrunapraharaṇaṃ kṛtāṃhovikalitaṃ ṣunantye te māsādihadulita 
talaughadahanam (ḥ) ||

sraprabhāte sāyaṃ ca ityatraphalabhūmā ||

aṃśatrayātmakaḥ soyaṃ pūrakakuṃbhakarecakaiḥ prāṇāyāmastāvad 
dvividho bhavati sagarbho'garbhaśca ||


japadhyānādibhiyuktaṃ sagarbhaṃ taṃ vidurvudhaḥ || 7-75 ||

tada petamagarbhaṃ tu prāṇāyāmaṃ parejaguḥ |


tatprakāramāha ||

 77a) iḍayā karṣayed vāyu vāhyaṣoḍaśamātrayā || 7-76 ||

tārayet pūritaṃ mantrī catuḥṣaṣṭyā tu mātrayā |

suṣumaṇāmadhyakaṃ samyag dvātriṃśanmātra-āsanaiḥ || 7-77 ||

nāḍyā piṃgalā caina recayed yāgavittamaḥ |

prāṇāyāmamidaṃ prāhuryogaśāstraviśāradāḥ || 7-78 ||

bhūyobhūyaḥ kramāt tasya vṛtyāsena samācaret |

mātrāvṛddhikrameṇaiva samyag dvādaśaṣoḍaśa || 7-79 ||


itrayeti pūraṇaṃ prathamaprāṇāyāmābhiprāyena dvitīye piṃgalai va puraṇāt ||

ye naiva santyajet tena purayed iti śravaṇāt |

yathā puraṇaṃ tadanya yāne ca namiti sakalaprāṇāyāmānugataṃ 
ṣoḍaśamantrayet pupalakṣaṇam ||

mātrāvṛddhikrameṇaivatyādinādhike nāpi pūraṇa śravaṇāt ||

sanatkumārasaṃhitāyām ||

dakṣayā recayed vāyuṃ dvātriṃśan mātrayā tataḥ 

mātrā ca uktaṃ mārkaṃḍeyapurāṇe ||

nimeṣānmeṣaṇe mātrā kāla iti |


iḍayā karṣayed vāyuṃ manoḥ ṣoḍaśamātrayāḥ |

suṣumaṇāmadhyagaṃ kṛtvā vidyāvatkṣaṇadhārayet || 7-80 ||

ye naiva recayed vāyuṃ te naiva recayet punaḥ |

ubhayormadhyagaṃ kṛtvā tanmātrābhidhāraṇam || 7-81 ||


pīṭhanyāsamatho vakṣye yathāvedavadhārayeti pūrvoktam |

śāradāyām ||

ṛṣyādi nyāsānantaraṃ pīṭhanyāsa uktaḥ ||


tantaścopāsya mantrasya nyāsān ṛṣyādikāṃ caret |


tathā ca viśudveśvaramantre ||


ṛṣinyāsāmūrdhnideśe chaṃdastu mukhapaṃkaje || 7-82 ||

devatā hṛdaye caiva vījaṃ tu guhyadeśike |

77b) śaktiṃ tvayā dayoścaiva sarvādikīlakaṃ nyaset || 7-83 ||


ṛṣistu ||


maheśvaramukhā jñātvā yaḥ sākṣāt tapasā manum |

saṃsādhayati śuddhātmā sa tasya ṛṣirīritaḥ || 7-84 ||

gurutvāt mastake cāsya nyāsastat parikirttitaḥ |

sarveṣāṃ mantratattvānāṃ sādhanā chanda ucyate || 7-85 ||

akṣaratvāt padatvācca mukhe cchaṃdaḥ samiritam |

ṛṣicchaṃdoparijñātvān namantraphalabhāgbhavet || 7-86 ||

daurvalyaṃ yāti mantrāṇāṃ viniyogamajānatām |


iti ṛṣinyāsā vaśyakatvamiti bhāvaḥ ||


mantraśāstre tathā coktaṃ prāptamantreṇadhīmatā || 7-87 ||

yasya mantrasya ye nyāsāḥ karttavyā siddhimicchatā |

japatarpaṇahomārcāḥ siddhamantra kṛtā api || 7-88 ||

aṃgaṃ nyāsādibhirhīnā nadāsyanti phalānyamī |


nyāsān samastān nyasataḥ svadehe trailokyametad vaśameti puṃsaḥ |

pāpāni sadyaḥ praṇamaṃ prayānti tresyānti bhūtāni sapannagāni || 7-89 ||


ādau ṛṣyādi vinyāsaḥ karanyāsamanantataḥ |

tato yadyakṣara nyāsaḥ padanyāsastataḥ param || 7-90 ||


yadīti padaṃ nyāsepyanuṣahyate ||

ausargikakramo yaṃ kvacit kalpoktenāpi vādhyate ||

puraścaraṇacandrikāyāṃ ca ||

tattkalpaviśeṣoktā nyāsānanyān samācaram |

kramadīpikāyāṃ tu ||

aṃgādinyāsaṃ vidhāya ṛṣyādi nyāsa uktaḥ ||

78a) dhyānasvarūpamāha ||


dhyānaṃ cet svaṣṭadevasya manasāvedanaṃ matam |

yajjīvabrahmaṇoraikyaṃ soheṃsyāditi vedanam || 7-91 ||

tanniguṇaṃ hṛdaṃ bhojaṃ karṇikākesarānvitam |

svīyaṣṭadevatāṃ tatra dhyāyed āgamadaiśitām || 7-92 ||

evaṃ yad vedanaṃ tastu saguṇadhyānamucyate |

evaṃ dhyātvā mahātmānaṃ pūjayed vāhyayogata || 7-93 ||

ātmāgrataścatuṣkoṇaṃ ṣaḍaśraṃ ca trikoṇakam |

sāmānyārghajale naiva kṛtvāṅgāni prapūjayet || 7-94 ||


āgneyādiṣu koṇeṣu hṛdādyaṃgacatuṣṭayamiti vāmato vahnimaṃḍale yāvat ||

vahnimaṃḍale trikoṇe ||


tatra saṃdhyādike devi nikṣipyaddhṛdayānunā |


ādipadāt puṣpākṣataparigrahaḥ |

hṛdayāṇunā namaḥ iti mantreṇa |

arghasvarūpamāha |

gaṃdhapuṣpākṣatayavā iti arghoyaṃ parikīrttitā |

puraṇamāha ||

pūrayed vimalapātha sāsudhīrakṣaraiḥ pratigatai śirāntagaiḥ |

akṣare mātṛkākṣaraiḥ ||

pratigatai vimalomasthaiḥ śirotagaiḥ svāhāntaiḥ akārāntairapi kecit |

vilomamātṛkāmantra pāthāntaraṃ mūlamatropi paṭhanīyaḥ |

ityapi kecit ||


tatra tīrthamanunābhivāhayantirthamuṣṇarucimaḍalāntataḥ |

svīya hṛt kamalato hariṃ tathā gāliṃnī tadupari pradarśayet || 7-95 ||

tajjalaṃ nayanaṃ mantravīkṣitaṃ varmaṇā samavaguṇṭhādoyujā |

78b) mūlamantrasakalī kṛtaṃ nyased aṃgakaiśca kalayed diśostranaḥ || 7-96 ||


nayanamantravīkṣitaṃ vauṣaḍiti mantravīkṣitaṃ varmaṇā humiti mantreṇa |

tīrthāvāhanamaṃkuśamudrayā ||

taduktaṃ gautamīye ||


tīrthamāvāhayed vidvān mudrayāṅkuśasaṃjñayā |


prāk pradarśitoyaṃ tīrthamantrī sakalīkṛtaṃ abhimantritam ||

tathā ca śāradāyām ||


mūlamantraṃ japet spṛṣṭvā jayet tasyāṅgamantravit || 7-97 ||


dhanumudrayāmṛtīkṛtya sakalīkaraṇam ||

taduktam ||


amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā cintayediṣṭadevatāt |

aṃganyāsena sakalīkṛtyāstreṇadiśodaśa || 7-98 ||


akṣatādiyutamacyutī kṛtaṃ saṃspṛśan japati mantraṃ maṣṭaśaḥ ||

matsyamudrayā cchādyapet |

tadukta ||

aṣṭa dṛtvo japet mūlamācchādya matsyamudrayeti dakṣiṇe 
prokṣaṇipātramādāyādbhiḥ prapūrayet ||


kiṃcidarghāṃvusaṃgṛhya prākṣiṇyaṃbhasi yojayet |

prokṣaṇīpātratoyena tenātmānaṃ trivārataḥ || 7-99 ||

prokṣya puṣpādikaṃ cāpi mūlena prokṣayet sudhīḥ |


dakṣiṇe pūrvasthāpitārghasya dakṣiṇe ||

prokṣaṇaṃ ca kareṇaiva prokṣayen nijatanuṃ tato munātriḥ kareṇamanuṇā 
tathākhilam |

sādhanaṃ kusumacandanādikamiti ||


pādyasyācamanīyasya madhuparkasya cāpyatha || 7-100 ||

protrāṇyādhārayuktāni svarṇādiracitāni ca |

sthāpayet puratasnāni pūrayecchāstravartmanā || 7-101 ||


pādyādipātrasthānamāha ||


79a) arghasyottarataḥ kāryaṃ pādyamācamanīyakam |


iti vacanāt ||

svapāṇyāha ||


pādyaṃ śyāmārkadūrvāvja viṣṇukāntājalauḥ smṛtam || 7-102 ||


jātīlavaṅgakarkolajalairācamanīyakam ||


ājyaṃ dadhimadhumiśraṃ madhuparkaḥ samīriteḥ |


punarācamanīyakam kevalaṃ jalora ||

taduktaṃ gautamīye ||


te naiva manunākuryād abhicāramaṇīyakam || 7-103 ||

ekasminnapi pātre vā pādyāni prakalpayet |

sarvopahāravastūnāmabhāve bhāvayed dhiyā || 7-104 ||


sarvopahāra iti ||

upahāraviśeṣābhāve manasā kalpanemiti pūjāpradīpaḥ taccintyam ||

somānyābhāvaparamiti niṣkarṣaḥ ||

atha mānasapūjā ||

atha mūrdhanimūlacakramadhyanijanāthaṃ gaṇanāyakaṃ samabhyarcya 
āsanakramataśca pīṭhamantraijalagaṃdhākṣapuṣpadhūpadīpaiḥ prayajed atha 
mūlamantrato nijamūle hṛdaye dhruvośca madhya |

tritayaṃ smarataḥ smaret tad ekīkṛtamānandagaṇaṃ taḍillatābham |

tat tejāṅgaiḥ sāvayavī kṛtya nijanāthaṃ guruṃ pīṭhamantraiḥ ||

oṃ ādhāraśaktaye namaḥ ||

ityādi mantraiḥ smarataḥ kāmavījenetyarthaḥ ||



tataḥ saṃpūjayed devaṃ gandhādyai mūlamūccaran |

paṃcakṛtya punaḥ kuryāt puṣpāṃjalimananyadhī || 7-105 ||

uttamāṅge hṛdādhāre pādasarvāṅgake kramāt |


athavā pādyam ||

datvā samarccagandhapūṣpādyaistāmbulagītanavādyaiḥ santoṣya cūlakasalilena 
|

79b) brahmārpanākhya manunā kuryāt svātmārpaṇaṃ mantrī |

arpaṇamantrastu agre likhyate mānasapūjāyāṃ naivedyadānaṃ nāsti ||

vinānaivedyagandhādyairūpacāraiḥ prapūjayediti vacanāt |


gurupadiṣṭavidhinā śeṣamanyāt samāpayet || 7-106 ||


śeṣaṃ tat kalpoktamiti mānasī pūjā |


vāhyapūjāṃ pravakṣyāmi mantriṇāṃhitakāmyayā |

evaṃ devamaye pīṭhe cintayed iṣṭadevatām || 7-107 ||

mudrāḥ pradarśya vidhivad arghasthāpanamācaret |


iti vacanāt ||

arghasthāpanamidānīmeva tadanuṣṭhānaṃ dīkṣā pallavenusaṃghepam ||

sastaṃ nijaikena tathaiva cakre cittre paṭe vā yajanapānabhūmau ||

mohādasausthaṃ ḍilagaṃ yajeccadbhṛśettrivargādapi siddhamantraḥ |


sāligrāmamanau yantre maṇḍale pratimāsu ca || 7-108 ||

nityaṃ pūjā hareḥ kāryā na tu kevalaṃ bhūtaleḥ |


bhuniṣedastu maṇḍalarahitabhūmiparaḥ |

yantrasyāvaśyakatvamāha ||


ādau likhedyantrarājaṃ tat tat kalpoktalakṣaṇam || 7-109 ||

sauvarṇe rājatatāstrapīṭhe bhūrje paṭe bhuvi |

vināyantreṇa cet pūjā devatā na prasīdatī || 7-110 ||

duḥkhaniryantreṇādyantraṃ ityāhustatra vedinaḥ |


iti kuryādyantramiti vacanāt |

yantrasyāvaśakasvam ||

pīṭhe śrīkhaṇḍādinimite anuktakalpe yantrakaranamāha ||

vṛhanmahatsyasūkte ||


yantraṃ tu vilikhet padmadalāṣṭakasamanvitam || 7-111 ||

80a) ṣaṭkoṇakarṇikaṃ tatra vedadvāropaśobhitamiti |


paṃcāyatanadīkṣā pakṣe |

paṃcadevatā sthānaniyamamāha ||


śabhaumadhyagate harīnaharabhūdevyo haro śaṃkare 
bhāsye nāgasatāravau gaṇapatī śājāṃvikā sthāpitaḥ |

devyo viṣṇu haraikadantaravayo laṃvodare jaśvare 
nāryāḥ śaṃkarabhāgatoti śubhadā vyastāstu hāriṣpradāḥ || 7-112 ||


śaṃkarabhāgataḥ īśānāditaḥ ||

tathā coktaṃ samayojya mātṛkāyām ||


pūrve sūryaṃ prapūjyātha dakṣiṇe gaṇanāyakam |

īśvaraṃ paścime deśe urttare viṣṇumarcayet || 7-113 ||

tasmāccakracaturddaśī kramavaśān nīrmāyacakraṃ śubhaṃ śuryahastimukhaṃ 
paraṃ smaraharaṃ gopālamevaṃ tathā ||

dhyātvārādhanato sadā vahuvidhaiḥ puṣpaiśca pādyādibhi 
nānādivyasugandhamodakavalairudvāse yet svahṛdi || 7-114 ||


tathā ca jñānamālāyāṃ ||


bhavānīṃ yadā madhya aiśānyāṃ mādhavaṃ yajet |

āgneyāṃ pārvatīnāthaṃ naiṛtyāṃ gaṇanāyakam || 7-115 ||

pradyotanaṃ tu vāyavyā mācaryastu prapūjayet |

ātmanaḥ purato bhadre devataḥ purataḥstathā || 7-116 ||

pūrvā śā kalpitā śaiṣā sarve daivārcane matā |


iti dakṣiṇāmūrtti saṃhitāyām ||

devāgre svasya cāpyagre prācī proktā gurukramairiti ||


uttarā śā mukhe devi pūrvā śaivena saṃśayaḥ || 7-117 ||

vāyukoṇaṃ tadāgneyaṃ īśānaṃ rākṣasaṃ bhavet |

 (da)dakṣiṇābhimukho mantrī yadā cakrasamuddharet || 7-118 ||

80b) pūrvāṃ śaiva tadā sādi śrakṣaḥ koṇaṃ tu vahnivat ||


asyārthaḥ ||

viṣṇuśivadurgā gaṇeśasūryānāṃ madhya yā iṣṭadevatā |

tāsāṃ madhya pūjanīyā tadbhinnacatuṣṭayemisādikoṇacatuṣṭayaṣu 
pūjanīyam ||

yathā śaṃbho madhyagate hariḥ sūryaḥ gaṇeśadurgā iti pūjanīyāḥ evaṃ 
viṣṇuvādiṣvapi vodhyam ||

pīṭhasyottarasyāṃ gurupaṅktistu ||

guruparamaguru parameṣṭiguru parāparaguru pīṭhaśaktayaḥ ||

ādhāraśaktyādijñānātmanontaḥ pūjyāḥ pūrvādikeśareṣu madhya ca tat tat 
kalpoktapīṭhaśaktīḥ saṃpūjyāḥ ||

madhyapīṭhamanuṃ yajed iti ||

pūrvādidiṅniyamastu pūjyapūjakayo madhyaprācīti kathyate vudhaiḥ ||


tad dakṣiṇaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ syād uttaraṃ cottaraṃ matam || 7-119 ||

pṛṣṭhaṃ tu paścimaṃ jñeyaṃ sarvatraivaṃ prapūjayet |


pūrṇā yāge pūjayed ityanuvṛttau ||

mūlena mūrttiṃ saṃkalpe iti || pūrvoktam ||

mūrttisaṃkalpe pīṭhoparisthāpayitvā kalpadrumachāyāyāḥ saṃsthitāṃ 
mūrttidhyātvā mūlamuccāryā mukamūrttaye nama iti pūjayet |

mūlena mūrttimiṣṭvāsyāṃ chāyāyāṃ kalpaśākhināmiti vacanāt ||

āvāhanaprakārastu pūrṇāyoge ||


mūrādhāre sphurad dīptaśikhākāraṃ śivāṃsmaret || 7-120 ||


śāradāyāṃ ca ||


mūlaṃ mantraṃ samuccārya suṣumnāṃ vatmanā sudhīḥ |

81a) ānīyatejaḥ svasthānān nāsikārandhranirgatam || 7-121 ||


karasthamātṛkāṃbhoje caitanyapuṣpacintayā saṃyojya brahmarandhrena murttyā 
vā māhayet suye |

mātṛkāṃbhoja svarūpaṃ tu śāradāyām ||

vyomendaurasanārṇakarṇikamityādirūpam ||

karasthapuṣpanicayaṃ vibhāvyamūrttyāmāropayet ||

āvāhya saṃsthāpanasaṃnidhā mātṛkāṃbhojasvarūpaṃ tu śāradāyām ||

vyomandaurasanārṇakarṇikamityādirūpam ||

karasthapuṣpanicayaṃ vibhāvyamūrttyāmāropayet ||

āvāhya saṃsthāpanaṃ sannidhānaṃ sannirodhanamanantaram |




sakalīkaraṇaṃ paścād vidadyādavaguṇṭhanam |

amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā kūrvītaparamīkṛtim || 7-122 ||

kramādetāni kurvīt samudrābhiḥ samāhitaḥ |


āvāhanādi rūpaṃ siddhāntasāre ||


ata evābhipūrṇasya sattvasyaivārcanādiṣuḥ || 7-123 ||

sadevaṃ samukhībhāvaṃ āvāhanamitīritam |

śivasyāvāhitasyāsya vidyādehe tre santatam || 7-124 ||

sthirīkaraṇamuddiṣṭaṃ sthāpanaṃ bhaktitorcane |

pūjāṃ prayojyamānāṃ tu gṛhītvānugrahādikam || 7-125 ||

karttuṃ sāmarthyamasyeha tat sānnidhyaṃ pracakṣyate |

āsamāptestu pūjāyāṃ sānidhyaṃ hi śivasya yet || 7-126 ||

saṃ sanirodha udiṣṭo vibhorapyasya bhaktitaḥ |

ānandāyatanaṃ tattvaṃ saccinandalakṣaṇam || 7-127 ||

81b) tadatra sakale vyāptaṃ dheyaṃ syādavaguṇṭhanam |

avibhinnasvabhāvānāṃ yadabhinnaṃ prayojanam || 7-128 ||

aṃgānāṃ aṃgināsārdhaṃ amṛtikaraṇaṃ tvidam |

kṣamā tasyāparādhāraṃ vijñayāparamīkṛtiḥ || 7-129 ||


somaśaṃbhunāpyetadevoktam ||

evaṃ svarūpamāvāhanādividheyam ||

athopacārān kurvīt mantravittantradeśitān |


prayojayed arcanāyāmuṣacārāṃstu ṣoḍaśa |


āsanaṃ svāgataṃ cārghyamiti pūrvaṃ likhitam |


arghādyāḥ paṃcapaṃcaiva gaṃdhādyā iti bhedataḥ || 7-130 ||


iti ca pūrvoktam ||

paṃcopacārādau tāṃvurāvaśyakatvavyavahārāstu bhramamūlaka evād 
ullabheṣṭapacāreṣu yat tat kavyatadāha ||


upacāreṣu sarveṣu yat kiṃcidullabhaṃ bhavet |

tat sarvamanasā dhyātvā puṣpakṣepeṇa pūjayet || 7-131 ||

tatra tatra jalaṃ dadyād upacārāntarāntare |

devatāyāḥ śiraḥ kuryāt kusumopahitaṃ sadā || 7-132 ||

pūjākāle devatāyāno paribhrāmayat karam |


iti pūjāpradīpa ||

puṣpadāne viśeṣamāha ||


puṣpaṃ vā yadi vā patraṃ neṣṭatāvad adhomukham || 7-133 ||

duḥkhadaṃ tat samākhyātaṃ yathotpannaṃ tathārpaṇam |

puṣpaṃ visarjayed devi mudrayā jñānasaṃjñakā || 7-134 ||

aṃguṣṭhatarjanīyogā jñānamudreyamīrtiā |

tarpaṇāni punaḥ kuryā trivāraṃ tattvamudrayā || 7-135 ||

aṃguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tu tattvamudreyamīrito |


82a) iti sundarirahasya vṛttau ||


adhomukhaṃ phalaṃ neṣṭhaṃ puṣpāṃjalividhau na tu || 7-136 ||


iti vacanādau jalidananiṣamo nāstiḥ |


upacāravidhiṃ vakṣya datvā cāthaśubhāsanam (ḥ) |

svāgataṃ kuśarapraśnaṃ nigatedagrato guro || 7-137 ||

pādyaṃ pādāṃvuje dadyād avasya hṛdayānunā |


hṛdaye manunānema iti mantreṇa ||


svadhāmantreṇa vadame dadyād ācamanīyakam || 7-138 ||


svadhāmantraḥ svadhetirūpaṃ ||


arghaṃ diśet tato mūrdhni śiromantranadaiśika |


śiromantraḥ svāheti mantra ||


svadhānunā tataḥ kuryāt madhuparkaṃ mukhāṃvuje || 7-139 ||


anurmantraḥ svadhānuḥ vamiti mantra ityarthaḥ |


tenaiva manunā kuryād abhicāramanīyakam |


te naiva svadhā manunai vā arghaṃ datvā vā pādyaṃ dadyāt |

tadā hakkamadīpikāyām ||

dattvāsanaṃ svāgataṃ ityudīrya tathārghapādyācamanīyakāni deyāmityādi 
śravanādicchādikalpa iti bhāvaḥ ||

ācamaneyaṃ vāratrayaṃ deyam |

taduktaṃ pūrṇāyāge ||


svadhetyācamanīyaṃ hi trivāraṃ mukhapaṃkaje || 7-140 ||

mukuṭopari puṣpaṃ ca vauṣadityaryayet sudhīḥ |


iti pūrṇāyāgavacanāt |

vauṣaḍiti pūṣpadānamiti śruteḥ ||

vastuto vauṣaṭ nama ityeva mantraḥ ||

kecit tu svadhārghādidānaśvapi tathā ||

taduktaṃ pāṣāṃḍāṅkuśe ||


namaḥ svadhā ca svāhā ca vauṣaḍiti yathākramam || 7-141 ||


82b) hṛtpuraḥ saramuccārya pādyādīni prakalpayed iti |

tathā corgho nama iti dānamatram (ḥ) ||

kecit tu svadhāmantreṇa vacana iti vacane svadhati pāṭhaṃ kalpayanti taccintyam ||


gandhādibhiḥ kārayet snānaṃ vāsasīparīdhāpayet |

dadyād divyopavītaṃ ca hārādyābharaṇaiḥ saha || 7-142 ||

abhyercyadevagaṃdhādyairaṃgādīn pūjayet tataḥ |


aṃgapūjāreyam ||?

tathā ca śāradāyām ||

dhūpādidānānantarumuktam ||

aṃgādilokapālāntamiti parivārārcanaṃ cared yāvad iti rāghavabhaṭṭa 
ityetraikavacana śruteḥ saṃkhyāyāṃ sādhakābhāvād iti ||

athāvaraṇapūjā ||

tatra keśareṣvagnikoṇādi hṛdayānī pūjayet |


netramagre diśāsvastraṃ dhyātavyā aṃgadevatāḥ || 7-143 ||

tuṣārasphaṭikaśyāmanīlasnāruṇārciṣaḥ |

varadābhayadhāriṇyaḥ pradhānatanavastriyaḥ || 7-144 ||


evaṃ ca ||


agnīśāsuravāyūnā dīkṣucatvāryupakramāt |


iti śaṃbhuvacanaṃ śivapūjāviṣayamiti pūjāpradīpaḥ ||

keśareṣu sthānaviśeṣānupadeśakalpeṣu |

upadeśe tu tantraivāṅgapūjā ||

praṇavādi caturthyārthaṃ aṃgānāṃ nama uktā namonte na pūjayet |

oṃ hṛdayāye namaḥ ||

oṃ śirase svāheti kecit ||

kaścit tu ||

oṃ hā/ hṛdayāye namaḥ ||

oṃ hī/ śirase svāhā ||

ṣaḍdīrghabhājāvījena pūjāmāha ||

pradhānatara ca iti ||

83a) mukhyadevatāyāḥ śarīrabhūtā ityarthaḥ |


paścād abhyarccanīyāḥ syuḥ kalpoktavṛtayaḥ kramāt || 7-145 ||

ante yaje lokapālānamutra?pāriṣadānvitān |

indraṃ agniṃ yamaṃ rakṣo varuṇaṃ pavanaṃ vidrum || 7-146 ||

īśānaṃ pannagādhīśaṃ adha-urdhvaṃ pitāmaham |


rakṣo nirṛtiṃ adho nirṛti varuṇayormadhyajātyadhipa iti bhāvapradhāna nideśāt 
sādhipatyo petāni nityartho labhyate|

tathā surādhipataye indrāya nama iti kecit ||

candrikāyāṃ ca ||


pattrādhīśvaravarṇāstra parivārāṅgasaṃgatān || 7-147 ||


indrādīnacaryaṃ dityuktaṃ pattraṃ vāhanam ||


kanakakapilanilaśyāmalaśvetadhūmrāmalasitaśuciraktā varṇato vā savādyāḥ |

kalakamalavirājat svāyudhā divyaveśā vividhamaṇigaṇasrak prasphurat 
toraṇādyām (ḥ) || 7-148 ||


eṣāṃ vāhanānyāha ||


airāvatestathā meṣo mahiṣaḥ aitasaṃjñakaḥ |

makārākhyā mṛgaḥ paścād uragovṛṣabhastathā || 7-149 ||

kurmo haṃsaḥ kramād eṣāṃ vāhanāni vidurvudhāḥ |


kvacidanantarasya ratho vāhanamuktaḥ svarūpamāha |


atha vikasita kiṃśukābhamagniṃ chagalagataṃ sitaśaktiyuktahastam |

maṇimayamukuṭaṃ yajet sugandhaiḥ kusumavarai dhṛtavānsukuṇḍalaṃ ca || 7-150 
||


iti sārasamuccaya vacanastha cchagalapadasya cchagalomeṣa iti ṭīkākāraḥ ||

evaṃ smṛtau ca ||

83b) bhagandarajanakapāpaprāyaścittameṣīdāne |

devānāṃ yo mukhaṃ ha vāhanaḥ sarvapūjitaḥ |

tasya tvaṃ vāhanaṃ pūjyaṃ devaśendrairmaharṣibhirityādi mantramuktavān |

prasiddhistu vahneḥ ||

cchagalavāhatve naiva pūjāmantramāha |

tathā ca pūrṇā yāge ||

indrāya vajrahastāya surādhipataye padam ||


savāhanāya padaṃ paścāt saparivārāyetyapi |

namonaṃ caivamanyeṣāṃ vāhanādikamuddharet || 7-151 ||


anyeṣāṃ agnyādīnāṃ vāhanādīnāmityādinā 
svanāmāstrādhipatyaparigrahasūcitaḥ ||

mantro yathā ||

iṃdrāya vajrahastāya surādhipataye savāhanāya saprivārāya nama iti mantraḥ 
pūjāyām ||

evaṃ agnyādīnāmapi mantro vodhavyaḥ ||

kecid granthāntara svarasāt sā yudhā yetyādi prakāreṇāpi yojayanti 
pitādivarṇāyetyapi kaścit ||

eṣāṃ mantrāṇyāha ||


dabholiśaktyabhidhadaṇḍakṛpāṇapāśaṃ caṇḍāṅkuśāṭkayagadā 
triśikhārapadmāḥ |

arcyā vahirnijasvarakṣitamauliyuktāḥ svā svā yudhā 
bhayasamudyatapālipadmāḥ || 7-152 ||


daṃbholirvajraḥ kṛpālaḥ kharḍgaḥ triśikhā triśūlaṃ eṣāṃ yathā 
saṃkhyenānvayaḥ ||

nijasvalakṣitamauliyuktaḥ iti svasvakiriṭanirmita svasvanāmasvāyudhacihna 
ityarthaḥ ||

susvāyudheti svasvamāyudhamityarthaḥ tena vajrahastena vajramityarthaḥ ||

athavā ||

indrasya haste vajramevāyudhamucayaṃ ceti bhāvaḥ keṣāṃ 
cinmadhyavāyurdhvajāyudhaḥ ||

84a) taduktaṃ pūrṇāyoge ||


vāyavadhvajahastāya prāṇādhipataye tataḥ |


iti pūrṇāyāge svarajā devaṃ vadanti |

śaktigadastrīrūpe ||

atrottare vidhuḥ pūjanīyaḥ kvacid uttaradik pūjyaḥ kuver iti śravaṇā |

tasya vāhanaṃ manuṣyaḥ ||

astraṃ gadeti bhāvaḥ ||

evaṃ ca dhūpadīpadānāntaranevāvaraṇa pūjā |

abhyarcya devaṃ gaṃdhādyairaṃgādīn pūjayet tata iti śāradā vacanāt ||

ādi padasvara sā dhūpadīpaparigrahaḥ ||

tatra pa *? ramāha |

aguruśrīraguggula iti ca śāradāyām ||

granthāntarepi ||



śārāṅgārairghṛtavilepitairjarjaraiḥ 
saṃvikīrṇairguggulvādyairghanaparimalairdhūpamāpādya mantrī ||

dadyān nīcairdanujamathanā yā pareṇa | thadoṣmā ghaṇṭāṃ 
gaṃdhākṣatasukusumairarcitāṃ vādayānaḥ || 7-153 ||

tat tad dīpaṃ surabhighṛtasaṃsīktakarpūravarttyādiptaṃ dṛṣṭyā 
prativiṣadadhīḥ padmaparyantamuccaiḥ |

kṛtvā dattvā stutiṃ atha vidhāyārpayitvātha podyaṃ sā cāsaṃkalpaya tu 
vipūrasvarṇapātre nivedyam || 7-154 ||
