nīcairdevasyādhaḥ kṛtvā dānamityarthaḥ apareṇa vāmakareṇa ghaṇṭāṃ 
vādayāna iti śravaṇāt ||


dhūpadīpadānepi udvāsanamāvaśyakamityarthaḥ ||

padmaparyantaṃ mastapadmaparyantaṃ uccairuccairdarśanaṃ dvīpasyatyarthaḥ 
ghṛte netyupalakṣaṇaṃ tathā ca vṛrttyā kapūrargarbhiṇyā sarpikhā tilajena vā ||

āropya darśayed vīpān uccairsaṃsthāpya purataḥ prokṣatāreṇa sumano nyased 
isaurabhaśālina iti śravaṇāt tairasyāpyabhidhānāt ||

84b) naivedya dānamāha ||

naivedyapurataḥ kṛtvāstreṇa prokṣya oṃkāreṇa tadupari puṣpaṃ nikṣipet ||

tathā coktam ||

saṃsthāpya purataḥ prokṣatāreṇa sumanonyasaditi ||

pratipattiprakāramāha ||

vītihotre madayitāṃ tamuccaran mūramantramathanikṣipejjalam ||

arpayet tadamṛtātmakaṃ havirdoryujāsukusumaṃ samudvaran ||

nivedayāmi bhavate juṣoṇe dehavirhare ||

naivedyārpaṇamantroyaṃ sarvāsunijākhyayā ||

vītahotradayiteti vītahotre agniḥ tasya dayitā svāhetyarthaḥ sarvārcāsu 
sarvadevārcāsu nijākhyayā tat tad devatā nāmnā tathā ca ūha iti bhāvaḥ 
amṛtopastaraṇamasi svāhetyā ca mayet tataḥ ||


grāsamudrāṃ vāmadosṇā vikarotpalasaṃnibhām |

pradarśaddakṣiṇena prāṇādīnāṃ ca darśayet || 7-155 ||


prāṇāpānavyānodānasamānāḥ kramācaturthyā yuktā ||


uccāraṇīyāḥ kramaśo vahnijāyāyutāstathā |

pānīyamamṛtī kṛta mudrayā dhenumudrayā || 7-156 ||

culukodakamamṛtāpidhānamasiṭhadvayam |

anena devatā haste datvā vātha vicintayet || 7-157 ||


ācārāt punarācamanīyamapi ||


tato ghṛtādisaṃdīptān piṣṭikādivinirmitān |

pārāvatabhramākārānucairdīpān pradarśayet || 7-158 ||

ārātrikamidaṃ proktaṃ ghaṇṭāvādanapūrvaka |

gatasāraṃ tu naivedyaṃ tataścaitat samuddharet || 7-159 ||

aiśānyāṃ tad bhuje dattvā kiṃci sthānaṃ viśodhayet |


85a) tadbhuje devatocchiṣṭaṃ bhuje sthānaviśodhanamucchiṣṭabhojine dātum ||

te ca ||


viṣvaksena smṛto viṣṇostejaścaṇḍo vivasvataḥ || 7-160 ||

caṇḍeśvarī tathā śakteścaṇḍeśaḥ śaṃkarasya ca |

tvaṃ vodarogaṇapateramī ucchiṣṭabhojinaḥ || 7-161 ||


tāṃvulakamabhisamarpya suvādyagītanṛttaiḥ sutṛptamabhipūjayatāt pure va ||

gandhādibhiḥ svaparimathārghamasmaidatvā vidhāya kusumāṃjali mādareṇa |

stutvā praṇamya śirasā culukodakena ātmānamarpaya tu taccaraṇābjamūle ||

tataḥ pūrvaṃ prāṇabudhidehadharmādhikāranaḥ ||

jāgratsvapnasuṣuptyavasthā sumana sā vācākarmaṇā hastābhyāṃ 
udareṇaśiśnā yat kṛtaṃ yaduktaṃ yat smṛtaṃ tat sarvaṃ brahmārpaṇaṃ 
bhavatu svāhā ||

māṃ madīyaṃ ca sakalaṃ haraye samarpaye ||

punaḥ pūjā gandhādibhirdīpānte naiva ||

tathā coktamāgamakalpadrume |

candanādipradīpāntairupacāraiḥ punaryajed iti śāradāyāṃ tu 
paṃcopacārairityuktam ||

gandhādayo nivedyānta pūjāṃ paṃcopacāriketi prasannārveyamākhyātā 
sarvadeva prasādinī ||


yathāśakti japaṃ kṛtvā taṃ japaṃ sarvasaṃpade |

dakṣiṇe devahaste tu arpeyet pūrvavat sudhīḥ || 7-162 ||


arpaṇamantrastu ||

oṃ guhyāti guhyatyādirūpaḥ ||

yathāśaktīti ||

attoṣṭarasahasraṃ vā attoṣṭaraśataṃ vā daśa vā ||

taduktaṃ pūrṇāpaddhatau ||

saṃkhyānuṣṭau śataṃ sāṣṭasahasraṃ vā japādiṣviti puṣpāṃjali punardattvā 
sāṣṭāṅga praṇamen muhuḥ ||

85b) tathā coktaṃ śrīviṣṇukalpalatāyām ||

dorbhyāṃ ca padbhyāṃ maṃsābhyāmiti ||


vāhubhyāmatha jānubhyāṃ ca ca sā śirasā vṛśā |

pañcāṅgakaḥ praṇāmoyaṃ pūjāsu pravarāvitau || 7-163 ||

aṣṭāṅga uttamaḥ proktaṃ (ḥ) paṃcāṅgo madhyamaḥ smṛtaḥ |


nadevaṃ pṛṣṭataḥ kṛtvedi |


bhrāntvā bhrāntvā caturppārśvaṃ yaḥ kṛṣṇaṃ praṇamennaraḥ || 7-164 ||

juṣṭāṅgapraṇipātena muktistasya kare sthitāḥ |

parivāraṃ gaṇaṃ sarvamupasaṃhāramudrayā || 7-165 ||

tenmuhurtāvupasaṃhṛtya devatāṃ prītamānasaḥ |

kṣamasvati visṛjyathamantreṇa vyāpakaṃ triśaḥ || 7-166 ||

vidhāya devatāṃ paścāt svīyahṛnsarasīruhe |

suṣumaṇā vartmanā puṣpamātryā?yodvāsayet sudhīḥ || 7-167 ||


evaṃ ca nirmālyaṃ śirasādhāryam |

savāṅgule ṣvanulepanam |

naivedyaṃ copabhuṃjīt dattvā tad bhaktiśāline ||

tato gurornamaskāraḥ || evaṃ ca ||


devārcanā vaśiṣṭhaṃ yat salilaṃ śaṃkhamadhyagam |

aṃge lagnaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ brahmahatyāṃ vyapohati || 7-168 ||

etāṃ nyanyāni pūjoyāṃ sādhanā vahunānyapi |

uttamāni ca madhyāni nyunāni vividhāni vai || 7-169 ||

svasthaḥ samarthaḥ kurvīte nyūttamairadhasādhanai |

madhyamāmadhyamairavanyūnā nyūnaiśca sādhanaiḥ || 7-170 ||

āpagrapi samarthopi nyūnaireva samācaret |

yathā śakti yathā nyāyaṃ yathālokavigarhitam || 7-171 ||

pūjāṃ vitelataḥ jatudhyātvā samyak prapūjayet |

āthavā mānasī kuryād ātmapūjāmathāpi vā || 7-172 ||

athavā ke*? lāṃ pūjāṃ rādyāṃ? kuryād vicakṣaṇam (ḥ) |

86a) ārcāmātre samarthaśced dadyādarcanasādhane || 7-173 ||


sapūjyaṃ va japaṃ kuryān namantraṃ kevalaṃ japed iti vacanānyūjā yo 
āvaśyakatvamiti pūjāpradīpaḥ ||

taccintyam ||


pūjā tu nirjane kāryā nakuryājjanasaṃnidhau |

kadācid dehahānistu na ca vyaktiḥ kadācana || 7-174 ||

varaṃ pūjā parityājyā na prakāśyā kadācana |


iti kulacuḍāmaṇi viro ca virodhāt ||

vastutodhikāliniyantritamiti dhyeyam |


atha dānā ca pūjāyāṃ muktaṃ yad vihitaṃ tathā || 7-175 ||

niṣiddhaṃ cāpi tat sarvaṃ mucyate granthavaryake |


taduktaṃ yāmale ||


nākṣatairacayed viṣṇuṃ nalasyā vināyakam || 7-176 ||

nadūrvapārcayed durgāṃ vilvapattraidivākaram |

uttanmarkapuṣpaṃ ca viṣṇorvajyaṃ sadābudhaiḥ || 7-177 ||


evaṃ ca ||


gaṃdhapuṣpākṣatayavakuśāgratilasarṣapaiḥ || 7-178 ||

sāmānyaḥ sarvadevānāṃ marghoyaṃ parikīrtitaḥ |


ityādi prāptasyākṣatadānasya naniṣedhakamiṭam ||

kitu ||


puṣpā bhāve jalenāpi durvayā tandūlena ca || 7-179 ||

nityaṃ pūjā prakarttavyā bhaktibhāvena daisikaiḥ |


ityādinā puṣpādya bhāvātideśa prāptasya kevalaṃ taṃḍuladānasya 
niṣedhakamiti bhāvaḥ ||

gautamīyaḥ ||


naraktaṃ jātu gṛhṇīyad candanaṃ raktapuṣpakam || 7-180 ||

vilvapattraiḥ prasūnairvā nārcayed devakīsutam |


granthāntare ||


devīnāṃ cākarmadālamādityetaganaṃ tathā || 7-181 ||

gaṇeśāye ca sūryāye raktapuṣpamati priyam |


niṣidvānyāha ||


86b) śive kundaṃ madantīṃ ca yūthīṃ vandhukaketakī || 7-182 ||

japāṃ raktāṃ trisaṃdhye ca māratīṃ raktacandanam |

raktavarṇaṃ hayāriṃ ca varvarāṃ parivarjayet || 7-183 ||

tathā ||

śuṣkaistu nārcayedviṣṇu patraiḥ puṣpaiḥ phalairapi |

snātvā nītaiḥ paryuṣitai yācitaiḥ kṛṣṇavarṇakaiḥ || 7-184 ||


snātvā nītairmadhyāhna sthānāṃ tasmānītai viti saṃpradāyaḥ |


svayaṃ vikasitaṃ puṣpaṃ svayaṃ ca patitaṃ bhuvi |

varjayedvṛhatī puṣpasagaṃdhaṃ ca viseṣataḥ || 7-185 ||


svayaṃ vikasitaṃ svayaṃ hastena vikāsitamityarthaḥ ||

tathā ||


kālikādau tathā tyājyaṃ vinā caṃpakapadmakaiḥ |

tathā ||

tripatrān pūnakusumai * *? yetra kadācana || 7-186 ||

bhūgataṃ cāṅgasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ keśakīṭādidūṣitam |

kumudaṃ pāṭalaṃ caiva śireṣaṃ parivarjayet || 7-187 ||

bhūgataṃ vajayet puṣpaṃ śophāliṃ vakulaṃ vinā |


chinnabhinnadoṣābhāvamāha ||


vilvasya khadirasyaiva tathā dhātrīdalasya ca || 7-188 ||

tamālasya ca patrasya chinnabhinnenaduṣpati |

tathā nakṣatravidyādau vṛhanmatsyasukte || 7-189 ||

puṣpaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ raktakokaṃ vaṃdhukaṃ satapatrakam |

varvarā dvitayaṃ caiva karṇikāradvayaṃ tathā || 7-190 ||

vakamaṃdāracutāni karavirāniśasyate |

mallikātritayaṃ jātī kṣaumapuṣpaṃ jayatikā || 7-191 ||

vilvapuṣpaṃ kuravakaṃ munipuṣpaṃ ca kesaram |

vasantī dvitayaṃ caiva kāśapuṣpaṃ maruvakam || 7-192 ||

damanaṃ ca lavaṃgaṃ ca puthīṃ śephālikāṃ tathā |

svagaṃdhiśvetalauhityaiḥ kusumairarcayed dalaiḥ || 7-193 ||

tathā ||

devī pūjā sadā kāryo jalajaiḥ sthalajairapi |

87a) vihitairahitairvāpi bhaktiyuktena cetasā || 7-194 ||

rāghavabhaṭṭopi ||

sarvaiḥ puṣpaiḥ sadā pūjāṃ vihitāvihitairapi |

karttavyā sarvadevānā bhaktiyogotrakāraṇamiti || 7-195 ||


tathā coktaṃ bhaviṣye ||

alābhena tu puṣpāṇāṃ ityādi bhaktyā saṃpūjito bhevetiti yāvat |

etad vacanadvayaṃ abhāvetyantabhaktiviṣayam ||

kālītantre ||

apāmārgaistu bhṛṃgaistu sugadhaiḥ śvetalohitairiti anyacca granthagauravabhayān 
nalikhitamiti saṃkṣepaḥ |

ityāgamakalpalatāyāṃ saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 7 ||


atha saṃkṣepataḥ sarvadevānāṃ nityapūjā ||

taduktaṃ yāmale ||


ādāvṛṣyodibhirnyāsaḥ karaśuddhistataḥ param |

aṃgādi vyāpakanyāsau hṛdādinyāsa eva ca || 8-1 ||

tālatrayaṃ ca digvandhaḥ prāṇāyāmastataḥ param |

dhyānaṃ pūjājapaśceti sarvatantre ṣvayaṃ kramaḥ || 8-2 ||


pūjā tu mūladevatā eva ||

atha pūjopayuktā sa namudvadrālakṣaṇāni ca |


jānurvorantare kṛtvā samyak pādatale ubhe |

ṛjukāyo viśedyogī svastakaṃ tadudīritam || 8-3 ||

vāmārūparidakṣiṇaṃ ca caraṇaṃ saṃsthāpya vāmaṃ punar

dakṣorūpariprapaścimena vidhinā dhṛtvā karābhyāṃ dṛṭam |

aṅguṣṭhe hṛdaye nidhāya civukaṃ ṇāsāgramālokayed 

etad vyādhivighātakāriyamināṃ padmāsanaṃ procyate || 8-4 ||

atha mudrāḥ pravakṣyāmi sarvatantreṣu gopitāḥ |

yābhirviracitābhistu modaṃ te sarvadevatāḥ || 8-5 ||

87b) dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhatarjanyā vagralagne parāṅgulī |

prasārya saṃhatottānā eṣa vyākhyāna mudrikā || 8-6 ||

dakṣāṅguṣṭhakṣiptā * * * * hastadvayena tu |

sāvakā śāmekamuṣṭiṃ kuryāt sā kuṃbhamudrikā || 8-7 ||

adhoṅguṣṭhā khalutuṣṭiḥ śikhāyāṃ karadvandvāṃgurayovarmaṇisyuḥ |

nānā ca mukṣyādgatavāhu yugmakaṅguṣṭhatarjanyuditodhvaniśca || 8-8 ||

viṣvagvibhaktaḥ kathitāstu mudrā yatrākṣiṇī tarjanimadhyamestaḥ |

netradvayatrabhavedanāmā ṣaḍaṃgamudroḥ kathitā yathāvat || 8-9 ||

aṃgulyā dhyānamudrā syā vāsudevābhidhā ca sā |

kaniṣṭhāṃguṣṭhakausaktau karayoriti cetaram || 8-10 ||

tajjanīmadhyamānāmā saṃhitā bhugnavarjitā |

mudraiṣāgālinī proktā śaṃkhasyoparcāriteti || 8-11 ||


śaṃkhasyoparivāritā satīgāriṇyākhyāṃ labhata ityarthaḥ ||


ṛjvī ca madhyamāṃ kṛtvā tarjanī madhyaparvaṇi |

saṃyojyā kuṃcayet kiṃcin mudraiṣāṅkuśasaṃjñitā || 8-12 ||

vāmāṅguṣṭhaṃ tu vadhnīyād dakṣiṇena tu muṣṭhinā |

kṛttottānāṃ tato muṣṭimaṃguṣṭhaṃ tu prasādayet || 8-13 ||

vāmāṅgulya tathā śliṣṭhoḥ (saṃyuktāḥ suprasāritā) mudraiṣā 
śaṃkhasaṃjñakā |

mithaḥ kaniṣṭhake vaddhā tarjjabhyāmanāmike || 8-14 ||

anāmikordhvasaṃśliṣṭa dīrghamadhyamayoradhaḥ |

aṃguṣṭhāgradvayaṃ nyasya yonimudrayamīritā || 8-15 ||

anyonyābhimukhāśliṣṭa kaniṣṭhānāmike punaḥ |

tathaiva tarjanīmadhyā dhenumudrāsamīritā || 8-16 ||

88a) adhomukhe dakṣahaste tādṛśaṃ vāmahastakam |

dhārayen matsyamudreya marcaneṣṭhā parematā || 8-17 ||


āvāhane samākhyātā mudrādaiśikasattamairiti vacanāt ||


adhomukhī tvayaṃ cet syāt thāpiṇīmudrikā bhavet |

utsṛtāṃguṣṭhamujyostu saṃyogāt saṃnidhāyanī || 8-18 ||

ataḥ praveśitāṅguṣṭhā saivasaṃrodhinī matā |

uttānamuṣṭiyugalā sanmukhīkaraṇī matā || 8-19 ||

devatāṅge ṣaḍaṃgānāṃ nyāsaḥ syāt sakalīkṛtiḥ |


tathā ca ||

ṣaḍaṃgamudraiva sakarīkaraṇamudreti tābhyajet |

anyathā mudrāprakaraṇe tat svarūpābhidhānamarthātaraṃ syāt ||


savyahastakṛtā muṣṭi dīrghādhomukhatarjanī || 8-20 ||

avaguṃṭhanamudreyaṃ abhitobhrāmitāsatī |

anyonyābhimukhetyādinā dhenumudrā uktā || 8-21 ||

amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt tathā sādhakasattamaḥ |

anyonyagrathitāṅguṣṭhā prasāritaparāṅguliḥ || 8-22 ||

mahāmudreyamuditā paramīkaraṇe vudhaiḥ |

prayojaye hi māṃ mudrāṃ devatāhlānakarmaṇi || 8-23 ||

adhaḥ kṛtādakṣahastaḥ prasṛtovaramudrikā |

urdhvī kṛto vāmahastaḥ prasṛtobhayamudrikā || 8-24 ||

hastau tu sanmukho kṛtvā saṃnagnau suprasāritau |

kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhakau saktau mudraikhācakrasaṃjñakā || 8-25 ||

spṛśe kaniṣṭhopakaniṣṭhake dve svāṅguṣṭhamurdhnā prathamehamudrā |

88b) tathā parātarjanimadhyamesyādanāmikā madhyamike ca madhyā || 8-26 ||

ānāmikā tarjanimadhyamāsyāstadvaccaturthī sakaniṣṭhakāstāḥ |

syāt paṃcamīmadhyamataḥ pradiṣṭhā prāṇādi mudrānijamantrayuktā || 8-27 ||

adhomukhe vāmahastamūrdhvāṃsya dakṣahastakam |

kṣiptāṅgulīraṃgulībhiḥ saṃgrathyaparivarjayet || 8-28 ||

eṣā saṃhāramudrāsyād visarjanavidhaumatā |

vakṣye hamupacārāṇā mudrāmantreṣu deśitāḥ || 8-29 ||

prasārayed ubhauhastau vidveṣa viniyojitau |

agulyovistṛtaḥ kāryā madhyeṅguṣṭhau tu vinyaset || 8-30 ||

devasyāsanavinyāse padmamudrāṃ pradarśayet |

eṣannamrāṅgulidakṣāṃ saṃyojyāṅguṣṭhakaṃ varam || 8-31 ||

svāgatasvastikāmudrā madhyamūlagatāṅguliḥ |

upacāre svāgate tu prayoktavyāvipaścitā || 8-32 ||

svastimudrādvihastau tu mudrā dīrghasya kīrtitā |

tau ca prasāritau pādya mudrā prakīrttitā || 8-33 ||

deśinī mūlaśāṅguṣṭhā dakṣiṇāyakanīyasi |

prasāryāmadhyamāsti sromudrā vāme prakīrtitā || 8-34 ||

saṃyuktā nāmikāṅguṣṭhau tisronyāḥ saprasāritāḥ |

madhuparka tu sā mudrā saṃkalpye kalasaṃ kare || 8-35 ||

kṛtvā muṣṭiṃ tathā snāne madhyamāṅguṣṭhakaumatau |

anyāḥ prasāritāstisro mudrāḥ vaśyakīrttitā || 8-36 ||

kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhakaulagnau tisropyanyāḥ prasāritā |

yajñopavītamudreyaṃ kathitā mantrapāragaiḥ || 8-37 ||

madhuparkkā samuttānā mudrālaṃkaraṇī matā |

muktānirnāmikāmuṣṭi gaṃdhamudreti kīrttitā || 8-38 ||

89a) utthitādhomukhī madhyā varddhāṅguṣṭhāyadīritā |

puṣpamudrā tathākhyā puṣpadāne vivarddhinī || 8-39 ||

aṃguṣṭhatarjjanīlagnā tisrāḥ saṃkucitāḥ paraḥ |

mudrādhūpapradānasyād devānāṃ tuṣṭikāriṇī || 8-40 ||

uttānā pauṣpikīmudrā devamudreti gīyate |

paṃcāṅgulyagrasaṃyuktā protthitordhvamukhī yadi || 8-41 ||

tridhā nivaddhā mudreyaṃ naivedyasya prakīrttitā |

madhyamānāmikāṅguṣṭhaḥ saṃragnāgrā adhomukhāḥ || 8-42 ||

anyāḥ prasāritāṅgulyo gomukhī sā prakīrttitā |

tyaktapradeśinī muktiṃṣṭiḥ sūcīmudreti kīrttitā || 8-43 ||

rakṣāmudreti śaivoktā samantāgramanaṃ yadi |

dvaukarau pṛṣṭasaṃragnau bhrāmayedgrathitāṅgulī || 8-44 ||

chotiketi samākhyātā praṇāmetāṃ pradarśayet |

deśinyaṃguṣṭhayormadhya dattvāṅgulicatuṣṭayam || 8-45 ||

vidhāyasaṃpuṭīkṛtya lagnāṅguṣṭhau samutsṛtau |

kuṃbhamudre dṛśīsasya hṛllekhākacchapīmatā || 8-46 ||

prasāryadakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ kaniṣṭhādyagrimāṅgulīḥ |

kramādokuṃcayedīrghamudrāṃ * * riti mate || 8-47 ||

mithaḥ pṛṣṭhārpitau hastā vanyonyagrathitāṅgulī |

kṛtvā vaddhā parāvṛtya natvā sāsyāt kṣamāpiṇī || 8-48 ||


ityāgamakalpatoyāṃ aṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 8 ||



puraścaryāṃ pravakṣyāmi mantrīṇāṃ siddhidāyinīm |

yasya vijñānamātreṇa sarvasiddhiśvarobhavet || 9-1 ||

89b) puraścaraṇasampanno mantro hi phaladāyakaḥ |

kiṃ homaiḥ kiṃjapaścaiva kiṃ mantraṃ nyāsavistaraiḥ || 9-2 ||

rahasyānāṃ ca mantrāṇāṃ yadinasyāt puraskriyā |

puraskriyā hi mantrāṇāṃ pradhānaṃ jīvamucyate || 9-3 ||

jīvahīno yathā dehi sarvakarmavivarjitaḥ |

puraścaraṇahīno hi tathā mantraḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 9-4 ||

tasmādādau puraścaryāṃ kṛtvā sādhakasattamaḥ |

prayogaṃ tu tataḥ kuryāt sarvasādhakadurlabham || 9-5 ||

labdhvā tu sadgurorvidyāṃ puraścaryāṃ samācaret |


tathā ca tārārahasya vṛttau ||


mantraṃ nītvā guroḥ pārśve gurubhaktiparāyaṇaḥ (m) || 9-6 ||

mantraṃ śrotrasya hṛnnetra prāṇā vijñāya tattvataḥ |

yatnācca kīlakaṃ jñātvā kuryānmantrapuraskriyām || 9-7 ||

mantrāṇāṃ praṇavaḥ śrotraṃ mukhaṃ rephaḥ prakīrttitam |

svarāśca cakṣuṣī jñeyā mantravidbhiranantaram || 9-8 ||

atiriktāśca ye varṇā hṛdayaṃ parikirttītam |


tathā ca ||

japahomatarpaṇābhiṣeka brahmaṇabhojanam || 9-9 ||

paṃcāṅgopāsanaṃ loke puraścaraṇamiṣyate |


yatra japahomādau saṃkhyānoktā tatrāṣṭasahasrasaṃkhyā syāt ||

taduktaṃ vārāhetantre ||


yeṣāṃ jape ca home ca saṃkhyā noktā manīṣibhiḥ || 9-10 ||

teṣāṃ aṣṭasahasrāṇi saṃkhyā syājjapahomayoḥ |


sahasramaṣṭau juguyāllakṣajāpe prakīrttayed ityuttaradale pāṭhaḥ |


yasmiṃśca nigame caiva mantrasaṃkhyā vidhiyate || 9-11 ||

tatra sarvatramantrāṇāṃ saṃkhyā vṛddhi yugakramāt |

90a) kalpoktai va kṛte saṃkhyā tretāyāṃ dviguṇaṃ bhavet || 9-12 ||

dvāpare triguṇā saṃkhyā kalausaṃkhyā catuguṇā |


taduktaṃ pūrṇāparddhatau ||


saṃkhyānuktau śataṃ sāṣṭaṃ sahasraṃ vā daśāpivet || 9-13 ||


japasthānānyāha ||

taduktaṃ yoginīhṛdaya ||


puṇyakṣatraṃ nadītīraṃ guhāparvatamastakam |

tīrthaḥ pradeśaḥ sindhunā saṃgamaḥ pāvanaṃ vanam || 9-14 ||

udyānāni viviktāni vilvamūlaṃ taṭaṃ gireḥ |

tulaśīkānanaṃ goṣṭhaṃ vṛṣaṃ śūnyāśivālayaḥ || 9-15 ||

aśvatthāmalakīmūlaṃ gośālājalamadhyataḥ |

devatāyatanaṃ kulaṃ samudrasya nijaṃ gṛham || 9-16 ||

guruṇāṃ sannidhānaṃ ca vittaikāgrasthalaṃ tathā |

pretabhumyādikaṃ caiva tat tat kalpapradarśitam || 9-17 ||

dīpasthānaṃ samāśritya kṛtaṃ karmaśubhāvaham |

caturasraṃ bhuvaṃ kṛtvā koṣṭhānāṃ narakaṃ likhe || 9-18 ||

pūrvādikoṣṭhe vilikhet saptavarṇānanukramāt |

lakṣamīśe svarānyugma kramāccaiva likhet tata || 9-19 ||

dikṣuparvādito yatra kṣatrākṣādyantaraṃ sthitam |

mūravaṃ tasya tu jānīyād dhastāvubhayataḥ sthitau || 9-20 ||

koṣṭhe kukṣe ubheyādo dveśiṣṭhe pucchamīritam |

krameṇānena vibhaje madhyasthamapi sādhake || 9-21 ||

muravastholabhate siddhiṃ karasthaḥ svalpajīvanaḥ |

tadāsīnaḥ kukṣisaṃsthaḥ pādastho duḥkhamāpnuyāt || 9-22 ||

pucchasthaḥ pīḍyate mantrī vaṃdhanoccāṭanādibhiḥ |

kūrmacakramidaṃ proktaṃ mantrāṇāṃ siddhisādhakam || 9-23 ||

90b) aṭṭāṭakeśaktipīṭhe tīthakṣetreṣu vā punaḥ |

araṇyaparvate puṇya kūrmacakraṃ na cintayet || 9-24 ||

ajñātānāmavāstau ca tatra nāma prakalpayet |


saptavarge ||

ka ca ṭa tapāḥ paṃcavargāḥ ya la ra va śa ṣa saheti dvauvargo evaṃ saptavargā 
bhavanti lakṣamiti svarūpaṃ īśe aiśānyāṃ diśi likhed ityarthaḥ ||

madhyamadhyakoṣṭhamapi navadhā kṛtvāṣṭasukoṣṭheṣu akārādi dvau dvau 
svarau likhet |

madhyamūlamantraṃ likhet ||


athavānyaprakāreṇa puraścaraṇamiṣyate || 9-25 ||


taduktaṃ vīratantre ||


aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśapakṣayorubhayorapi |

kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣeṇa sādhayed vīrasādhanam || 9-26 ||

tat sārdhaprahare yāme gate ca surasundari |


śavaṃ vātha citāṃ vāpi ||

nītvā yathā sukham ||


sādhayecca hitaṃ mantrī mantradhyānaparāyaṇa || 9-27 ||

bhayaṃ caiva na karttavyaṃ hāsyaṃ tatra viparyayet |

caturddiśaṃ nanīrīkṣe mantrametat samabhyaset || 9-28 ||

pūjādravyaṃ pravakṣyāmi nānātantrānusārataḥ |

samiṣānnaṃguḍaṃ sthāgaṃ madhūpāyasapiṣṭakam || 9-29 ||

nānāpuṣpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ svasvakalpoktasādhitam |

citāsthānaṃ samānīya vaṃdhubhiḥ śastrapāṇibhiḥ || 9-30 ||

samānaśīlasapannaiḥ sādhakonirbhayaḥ śuciḥ |


tathā ca yoginī hṛdaye ||


valyarthaṃ sāmiṣānnaṃ ca guḍachāgaṃ tathā madhu || 9-31 ||

piṣṭakaṃ palamānnaṃ ca payomūlaphalaṃ tathā |

91a) saptarātraṃ valiṃ dattvā catuḥpātraṃ caturdiśi || 9-32 ||

pātratrayaṃ sadāsavye sthāpayen manunā sadā |

svaguruṃ bhrātṛvargaṃ vā brāhmaṇān vāpi suvratān || 9-33 ||

suhṛdānapi cakṣārthaṃ kiyaddureniveśayet |

citā yā lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye sarvasiddhipradāyakam || 9-34 ||


taduktaṃ yāmale ||


asaṃskṛtā citāgrāhyā na tu saṃskārasaskṛtati |

sādhakā tu pravakṣyāmi pūrvoktaṃ bhairavena tu || 9-35 ||

mahāsāhasikaścaiva mahāvalaparākramaḥ |

dayāluḥ svacchahṛdayaḥ sarvaprāṇihiterata || 9-36 ||

evaṃ vidhiguṇopetaḥ kuryācca citisādhanam |

saṃkalpaṃ prathamaṃ kuryāt svastivācanapūrvakam || 9-37 ||

sāmānyārghaṃ vidhāyaiva tataḥ pūjāṃ samārabhet |

vastrālaṃkārabhūṣādyai bhūṣitaḥ pūrvadiṅmukhaḥ || 9-38 ||


saṃkalpo yathā ||

oṃ adyatyādi | amukagotraḥ śrī-amukaśarmāṇmasānaśādhanamahaṃ kariṣya iti 
saṃkalpe asrāṃntamūlaṃ mantreṇa yāgabhūmiṃ ca śodhayet ||

mūlamantreṇa phaṭkārāntena ||


dhyātvā guruṃ pādapadmaṃ gaṇeśaṃ vaṭukaṃ tathā |

yoginī mātṛkāṃ caiva kṣatrapālaṃ tathaiva ca || 9-39 ||

saṃpūjya vaṃ gṛhaṃ gacched vāmapāda puraḥ saram |

astramantreṇa mantrajño nakṣāmātmani kārayet || 9-40 ||

vakṣyamāṇena mantreṇa puṣpāñjalitrayaṃ kṣipet |


mantroyathā ||

uktaṃ vīratantre ||


ye cātra saṃsthitā devā rākṣasāśca bhayānakāḥ || 9-41 ||

91b) piśācā siddhayoyakṣā gaṃdharvāssarasāṃgaṇā |

yoginyo mātaro bhūtāḥ sarvāśca khecarastriyā || 9-42 ||

siddhidā snābhavantvaḍa tathā ca malarakṣakāḥ |

praṇamyamanunānena puṣpāñjalitrayaṃ kṣipet || 9-43 ||

masānādhipatiṃ paścā bhairavaṃ kālabhairavam |

mahākālaṃ yajed yatnāt pūrvādik catuṣṭaye || 9-44 ||

pādyādibhiśca saṃpūjya valiṃ paścān na vedayet |


validānamāha ||

yathā ||


pūrve śmasānādhipatiṃ paṃcopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya || 9-45 ||


aṇena valiṃ dadyāt || yathā ||

oṃ hu/ śmasānādhipa imaṃ sāmikhānnaṃ huṃ mahāvaliṃ gṛhna 2 gṛhnā paya 
2 vighnanivāraṇaṃ kuru siddhiṃ mama prayaccha svāhā ||

tato dakṣiṇasyāṃ pūrvabhairava saṃpūjya 

oṃ huṃ bhairava bhayānaka śmasānādhipaṃ imaṃ pūrvavat ||

tataḥ paścimāyāṃ evamuttare pādyādibhimahākālaṃ imaṃ valiṃ dadyāt |


citāmadhyavaliṃ dadyād valitrayamanuttamam |

kālarātri mahārātri kālike ghoraniḥ svane || 9-46 ||

gṛhāṇe maṃ valiṃ māta dehī sādhyamanuttamam |


oṃ huṃ bhūtanātha śmasānādhipa ||

imaṃ mantraḥ ||

oṃ huṃ śabdānte sarvagaṇanāthā te cādhipāye |


śmasānamastake dattvā pūrvavacca samurddharet || 9-47 ||

tārādyana valiṃ dattvā paṃcagavyena sundari |

abhiḥ saṃprokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā pītavastralikhet tataḥ || 9-48 ||

92a) bhūrje vā vaṭapattre vā tatra pīṭhamanuṃ tataḥ |

pīṭhamāstīrya tasmin vai vaddhavīrāsanaṃ tata || 9-49 ||

virānena deveśi rakṣāṃ dikṣva prakalpayet |


asyārthaḥ ||

pītavastrādau tat tat kalpokta pīṭhamantraṃ likhitvā tadupari 
vyāghracarmādyāsanamāstīrye tadupari vīrāsanakrameṇaiva vidyā 
virāndanamantreṇa caturdikṣurakṣāṃ kuryāt ||

vīrāndanamantrastu ||

hu/ hu/ hrī/ hrī/ kālīke ghoradaṃṣṭre pracaṃḍanāyike dāraya 2 hana 2 asmin 
mahāvighnaṃ cchedaya 2 svāhā phaṭ ||


anena mantritaṃ loṣṭhaṃ daśadikṣu vinikṣipet || 9-50 ||


yadi vā sādhako bhayavihvalo bhavati tadā sarvaiḥ suhṛdvārgaiḥ parirakṣaṇīyaḥ 
tatastu tat tat kalpokta bhūtaśuddhyādi nyāṃsajālaṃ samāpyeṣṭa devatāṃ 
saṃpūjya saṃkalpaṃ kṛtvā iṣṭadevatāṃ dhyāyen mantraṃ japet ||

saṃkalpastu ||

adyatyādyamukagotrāmukaśarmāmukamantrasya siddhikāmaḥ amukamantrasya tat 
saṃkhyaṃ japamahaṃ kariṣye iti saṃkalpya japet ||


ekārakṣasya mantrasya dik sahasraṃ japaṃ caret |

dvyakṣare ayutārdhaṃ ca tryakṣareṣṭasahasrakam || 9-51 ||

ataḥ paraṃ tu mantrāṇāṃ gajāṣṭakasahasrakam |

gajoṣṭasaṃkhyākeḥ aṣṭottarasahasramityarthaḥ || 9-52 ||

yadya sajyabhyaṃ karṇe netre vastreṇa vadvayata |

tatorgharātraparyantraṃ yadikiṃcin nadṛśyate || 9-53 ||

jayadurgākhyamanunā sarṣapān dikṣu nikṣipet |


oṃ durge 2 rakṣiṇi svāhā ||

oṃ tilosi somadaivatyo iti ||


92b) piḍuṇāṃ sukhadātātvaṃ martyāṇāṃ mamarakṣakaḥ || 9-54 ||

bhūtapretapiśācānāṃ vighneśāntikārakaḥ |

iti mantraṃ samuccārya tilāndikṣuvinikṣipet || 9-55 ||


iśānādicatudikṣviti śeṣaḥ ||


tataḥ saptapadaṃ gatvā punastatre vasaṃ viśet |

punaḥ sveṣṭadevatāṃ dhyātvā saṃpūjya punarjapet || 9-56 ||

yadyāgatya varaṃ gṛhna iti vākyamudīrayet |

dadatāṃ kāmayat satyaṃ mantrī japaparāyaṇam (ḥ) || 9-57 ||

tataḥ satyaṃ kārayitvā varayedvaramuttamam |

japādau valimāhṛtyaṃ japānte ca valiṃ haret || 9-58 ||


tathā coktaṃ yoginihṛdaye ||


japānte ca vali dadyād devatāyai yathāvidhi |

māhiṣaṃ cchāgalaṃ vāpi gṛhītvā varameva ca || 9-59 ||

japānte japamadhye vāṃ valiṃ dehīti bhāṣyate |

piṣṭake na valiṃ dadyān māhiṣaṃ cchāgameva vā || 9-60 ||


valiṃ ca yavapiṣṭake nirmitaṃ dhyānvodbhavaṃ vā valiṃ dadyāt ||


punaryadvinaraṃ dehi kuṃjaraṃ dehi yā ca te |

dināntare pradāsyāmi pratiṣṭrāṃ śrāvayet tataḥ || 9-61 ||

pratijñāṃ * * saṃgṛhya gṛhaṃ gacched yathā sukham |

aparehni tato dadyā piṣṭena narakuṃjaram || 9-62 ||


tathā coktaṃ kālikāpurāṇe ||


yavakṣodamayaṃ vāpi śālikṣodamayaṃ ca vā |

candrahāse na vidhivat tat tan mantreṇa ghātayet || 9-63 ||


candrahāsena khaḍgena ||

samāpya sādhanaṃ devi dakṣiṇāṃ vibhavāvadhi guruve guruputrāya tat patnyai 
vā nivedayet ||

parehni nityakriyāṃ kṛtvā paṃcagavyaṃ pītvā piṣṭhakanirmita valiṃ dadyād 
anena ||

yeṣāṃ yajamānohante gṛhnantu imaṃ valim |

93a) iti valiṃ dattvā paṃcaviṃśabrāhmaṇān bhojayet ||

tathā ca yoginītantre ||


parehni nityamācarya paṃcagavyaṃ pivet tataḥ |

brāhmaṇān bhojayet tatra paṃcaviṃśatisaṃkhyakān || 9-64 ||

paṃcasaptavihīnāmvā krameṇaivadaśāvadhi |

bhuktvā ca vāndhavaiḥ sārdhaṃ nivased uttamasthale || 9-65 ||

yadinasyād viprabhojyaṃ gadānirdhanatāṃ vrajet |

tena ce nirdhanatvaṃ syāt tadā devi prakupyati || 9-66 ||

trivāraṃ vā tu ṣaḍrātraṃ navarātraṃ tu gopayet |

strīśarmyā yadi gacche tu tadā vyādhiṃ vinirddiśet || 9-67 ||

gītaṃ śrutvā tu vadhiro niścakṣu nṛtyadarśanāt |

yadi vakti dine vājyaṃ tadāsya mukatā bhavet || 9-68 ||

tripaṃcadivasaṃ yāvad dehe devasya saṃsthiti |

nasvīkuryāṅgandhapuṣpaṃ vahiyāti yadā tadā || 9-69 ||

tadā vastraṃ parityajya dhautavastraṃ ca dhārayet |


tathā kalpāgamadrume ||


atha vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepād vīrasādhanamuttamam || 9-70 ||

kṛtena divase kena siddhiṃ vindatimānavaḥ |

yaṣṭi prabhṛtyastrabhinnaṃ śūlavidvaṃ yayomṛtam || 9-71 ||

śavamānīyakarttavyaṃ nagrāhyaṃ svecchayāmṛtam |

rātrau prathamayāmārghe tatra gacchet sahitāḥ || 9-72 ||

paṃcopacāra sāsaṃgrī sasurāṃ tatra saṃharet |

guḍārdrakarasainaiva surātrabrāhmaṇasya ca || 9-73 ||

śauḍīne yākṣantriyeṇa mādhvī vaiśyena tatra tu |

kadalīmadhusaṃmiśra śānatvasvarasaiḥ surā || 9-74 ||

śūdreṇātra pradātavyā dātavyaṃ naiva saṃspṛśet |


māṣabhaktaṃ ca valyarthamiti pūrvoktam |

93b) oṃ ādhāraśakyai nama ityuccāryāsane samupaveśayet |


bhūtaśuddhiṃ tataḥ prāṇaṃ saṃyamya mātṛkāṃ nyaset || 9-75 ||

āghorasya muniḥ cchando devatāṅgāni vinyaset |

śikhāṃ te naiva vaddhāgni prākāraṃ vandhanaṃ diśaḥ || 9-76 ||

sudarśanena vā rakṣā mulābhyāṃ vā prakalpayet |

jayadurgākṣamantreṇa sarṣapān dikṣuvikṣipet || 9-77 ||


oṃ tilosi somadaivatyogasavatṛptikārakaḥ |


pitrūṇāṃ svarga dātā tvaṃ matyānāṃ mamarakṣakaḥ |

iti kṣiptvā tilānātmacaturddikṣu śivāditaḥ || 9-78 ||


kārabhairavanāmānamiti māṣabhaktai valiṃ diśet ||

yāvat |


tataḥ śavaṃ kṣālayitvā snāpayecca prayatnataḥ |

yadi raktāktakastiṣṭhe bhairavāśca bhayaṃ bhavet || 9-79 ||

elālavaṃgakarpūra jātīkhadirasāṃrasādrakaiḥ | (?)

tāmbulaṃ tanmukhe kṣiptvā śavaṃ kuryād adhomukham || 9-79 ||

sthāpayitvā ca taspṛṣṭhaṃ caṃdanena vilepayet |

vāhumūlāntu kaṭyantaṃ caturasraṃ vibhāvya ca || 9-80 ||

madhyapadmaṃ samālikhya dalāṣṭakasamanvitam |

tataḥścaireyamajinaṃ kavalāntaritaṃ nyaset || 9-81 ||

pūjādravyaṃ sannidhau ca dure cottarasādhakam |

santāpyāsanamabhyarcya tatrāsvārohavadviśet || 9-82 ||

kuśān pādatale datvā śavakeśān pramārjya ca |

dṛḍhaṃ nibadhyajutikāṃ tat tad devasvarūpiṇām || 9-83 ||

tasyāṃ devaṃ susaṃpūjya paṭhed utthāya sammukheḥ |


oṃ vaśe bhava deveśa svakāṃmameṣṭhasiddhi dehi vīrasiddhiṃ dehi vīrasiddhi 
kṛtāṃvaravaddhā bhave deveśi amukamameṣṭhasiddhiṃ dehi vīrasiddhi 
kṛtāṃvaram ||


94a) paṭṭasūtrasya rajvāthaṃ caraṇau vaṃdhaye dṛḍham || 9-84 ||

oṃ bhīmabhīmabhayābhāva bhāvyalocanabhāvuka |

trāhi māṃ deva deveśa śavānāmadhiyādhipa || 9-85 ||

iti pādatale tasya trikoṇaṃ cakramullikhet |

tenotthāṃ tu na śaknoti śavaśca niścalo bhavet || 9-86 ||

upaviśya punastasya vāhunissārya pārśvayo |

hastayoḥ kuśamāstīrya pādau tatra nidhāpayet || 9-87 ||

oṣṭhau saṃpuṭakau kṛtvā sthiracittā sthirandriyaḥ |

sadā devaṃ hṛdidhyātvā mānījapamathārabhet || 9-88 ||

valāsanā bhayaṃ nāsti bhayajāte vadet tat |

yat prārthayasivalitvena dātavyaṃ kuṃjarādikam || 9-89 ||

dināntare tu dāśyāmi svanāmakathayasvame |

ityuktā saṃskṛto bhaktyā nirbhayastu punajapet || 9-90 ||

punaścenmadhuraṃ vakti vaktravyaṃ vinaye na vai |

tasmāt satyaṃ kārayitvā varaṃ ca pārthayet tataḥ || 9-91 ||

yadi satyaṃ na karoti varaṃ vāna prayacchati |

tadā punarjapen mantre mekāgrayatamānasaḥ || 9-92 ||

satyaṃ kṛte valaṃ rabdhā satyajet tu japādikam |

phalaṃ jātamiti jñātvā jutikāṃ mocayet tataḥ || 9-93 ||

śavaṃ prakṣālya saṃsnāpya vandhanaṃ mocayet pade |

pādacakraṃ mārjayitvā pūjādravyaṃ jalekṣipet || 9-94 ||

śavaṃ jalethagante vā nikṣipya snānamācaret |

tatastu svagṛhaṃ gatvā valiṃ dadyādinātare || 9-95 ||


94b) agrimarātrau yeṣāṃ devānāṃ jayamānohaṃ te gahaṃ tu mayā 
dattanimaṃ valim |


iti yairyācitānaśca narakuṃjalaśūkarām |

dattvā piṣṭhamayā neva karttavyaṃ samupoṣaṇam || 9-96 ||

parehni nityamācarya paṃcagavyaṃ pivet tataḥ |

brāhmaṇān bhojayet tatra paṃcaviśatisaṃkṣakān || 9-97 ||

paṃcapaṃcavihinān vā krameṇaivaṃ daśāvadhi |

tata snātvā catuskvātha ca nivased uttame sthale || 9-98 ||


yadinasyād viprabhojyamiti pūrvavat |

yadīvaktidīnavākyamiti ca ||


deva go brāhmaṇāṃdīśca pratyahaṃ spṛśecchuciḥ |

prātarnityakriyā te tu vilvapattraudakaṃ pivet || 9-99 ||

tataḥ snāyāt tu gaṃgāyāṃ prāpte ṣoḍaśavāsare |

svāhāntaṃ mulamuccārya tarpaṇānte namaḥ padam || 9-100 ||


anenavidhi devaṃ tarpayed daiśikottamaḥ ||

tathā ca mūlamālāyām ||


śrīdevyuvāca ||

rahasyātirahasyaṃ me puraścaryavidhi prabho |

vadasva yadanuṣṭānāt sarvakāmābhavanti hi || 9-101 ||

śrī-iśvara uvāca ||

gṛhītvā sadgurordīkṣāṃ śubhekāledi vāṇiśi |

ārāmo vaiṣṇavaḥ prokto madhyamaḥ śaivadīkṣitāḥ || 9-102 ||

anayā dīkṣitā yo vai sa eva paramoguruḥ |

śaktikṣetreṣu gaṃgāyāṃ nadyāṃ parvatamastake || 9-103 ||

pavitre ca sthale devi yaje vidyā prasādhayan |


śaktikṣetre kāmarūpādau ||


tatra sthitvā japellakṣaṃ sākṣād devīsvarūpakaḥ || 9-104 ||

tato bhavati vidyeyaṃ trailokyavaśakārini |

caturdaśasahasrāṇi sevite hari padāṃvuje || 9-105 ||

95a) tato bhava deveśi ta pujārataḥ pumān |

kāyane maṇasā vācā suvarṇakaśatādibhiḥ || 9-106 ||

santoṣyaparayā bhaktyā guruṃ dīkṣāṃ samāśrayet |

pūrvoktasthānamāśritya japapūjāṃ samāśrayet || 9-107 ||

prātaḥ pālaṃ samārabhya japen madhyandināvadhi |

prathame haniyajjapte tajjaptaṃ vyadine dine || 9-108 ||?

nyunādhikaṃ na japtavyaṃ nāsamāpte sadājapet |

gate prathamayāme tu tṛtīyapraharāvadhi || 9-109 ||

niśāyāṃ ca prajaptavyaṃ rātrīśeṣajapena ca |

haviṣye bhakṣya yad rātau nakadācidinena ca || 9-110 ||

ladvāhāraṃ prakurvīta yuvatī pūjane rataḥ |


bhaikṣaṃ haviṣyaṃ śākādi iti pūrvoktam |

tathā ca vṛhatmatsyasūkte ||

śākaṃ mūlaṃ bhaikṣyaṃ iti ca pūrvoktam |

niṣiddhamāha ||


madhumāṃsaṃ tathā kṣāralavaṇaṃ telameva ca || 9-111 ||

snigdhaṃ paryuṣitaṃ caiva nisnehaṃ kīṭaduḥkhitam |

kācikaṃ gṛjanaṃ vilva karajaṃ laśunaṃ tathā || 9-112 ||

masuraṃ ko ivaṃ māṣaṃ maṃḍukaṃ ca na kādikam |

tāmbulaṃ kāṃsyapātraṃ ca divābhojanameva ca || 9-113 ||

evamādīni cānyāni japakāle vivarjayet |

anyathā bhojanān teṣāṃ siddhiṃ nāpnoti sādhaka || 9-114 ||


gṛjanaṃ mūlikākṛtiraktavarṇaṃ gājala iti yasya prasiddhi |

kodravaṃ koḍa iti maṇḍūkaṃ mandū vā iti |


svanārīmanyanārī vā pūjayet sarvaparvasu |

nādhame saṃgatiḥ kāryā sarvaprāṇahiterataḥ || 9-115 ||

95b) yasyā pāvā japaḥ proktastaddaśāṃśamanukramāt |

tantradravyairjapasyānte homaṃ kuryād dine dine || 9-116 ||

homasya tu daśāṃśena tarpaṇaṃ proktameva ca |

parvaṇasya daśāṃśena śiromārjanameva ca || 9-117 ||

taddaśāṃśena viprendrā nyuvatīkulakanyakāḥ |

saṃbhājayet prītiyutaidravyai nānāvidhaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 9-118 ||


tarpaṇaprakāramāha ||

haṃsapārāyaṇe |


tarpaṇaṃ tu prakurvīta ya ya sāpāyasana vā |

ājyanamadhunā vāpi surayāmantrasādhakaḥ || 9-119 ||

brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyaiḥ śūdrairasyā yathākramāt |

vināmāṃsena matsena puṃścarīsevanaṃ vinā || 9-120 ||

namantrasiddhi bhavati tasmādyatna paro bhavet |


tathā ||


mādanaṃ sakalaṃ vastu varjayet kaṇakādikam || 9-121 ||

kanakaṃ pātakī tasmā dharmovedavidāmvaraḥ |


kālikāpurāṇe ||


brāhmanasturāṃ datvā brahmaṇyā deva hī yate || 9-122 ||

svagātrarudhiraṃ datvā ātmavadhyamavāpnuyāt |


evaṃ cāśavaṃ sarvatra camarajātī paraṃ caramajjāti paraśūdrajāti 
paramityarthaḥ ||

anyeṣāṃ pātaka hetuttvāt ||


kṣīreṇa brahmaṇaistaryā ghṛtena nṛpavaṃśajaḥ || 9-123 ||

mākṣikaivaśyavaṃśaiśca āsavaiḥ śūdrajātibhiḥ |

homādyaśaktau deveśi kuryāt tad dviguṇaṃ japan || 9-124 ||

yadi pūjādyaśaktaḥ syād dravyābhāve na sundari |

kevalaṃ japamātreṇa puraścaryāvidhīyate || 9-125 ||

divyothayadibhāvestu bhāvacuḍāmaṇāvaṇu |


96a) saṃdheyaḥ ||

matsyamāṃsādyaraneśakuryāt puraskriyām |


ekarātrauśmasāne vā śave vā proḍhavālayo || 9-126 ||

mayoktaṃ bhairavakalpe vidhāne varavarṇinī |

hastamātranikhāte vā muḍe vā vijane vane || 9-127 ||

virāṇā kathite devi sādhanaṃ tu vidullabham |

kumārī pūjanā deva puraścaryā vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 9-128 ||


uktaṃ gandharvatantre ||


kumārīkā dvivarṣā ca trivarṣā ca trimurttinī |

catuvarṣā tu kalpāṇi paṃcavarṣā tu rohiṇī || 9-129 ||

ṣaḍvarṣā tu bhavet kālī saptavarṣā tu cadrikā |

aṣṭavarṣā śāṃbhavī tu durgā tu navabhiḥ smṛtā || 9-130 ||

daśavarṣā tu bhadreti nāmabhiḥ parikīrttitā |

oṃ mantrākṣaramayī lakṣmī mātṛṇāṃ rūpadhāriṇīm || 9-131 ||

navadurgātmikā sā ata kanyāmāvāhayāmyaham |

nānājāti bhavāḥ kanyā rūpayauvana saṃyutā || 9-132 ||

abhyante prauḍhā vālā vā pūjanīyā yathā vidhiḥ |

dravyaiḥ priyakaraiḥ sarvai bhakṣyabhojyādibhiḥ śubhaiḥ || 9-133 ||

pūjayet parabhāvena nānārūpamanoharāḥ |

bhaginīkanyakā vāpi dauhitrī vā kuṭuṃvini || 9-134 ||

mātṛvarjyaṃ sadā pūrjyā nānājāti samudbhavāḥ |

athavānyaprakāreṇa puraścaraṇamiṣyate || 9-135 ||

sūryodayaṃ samārabhya yāvat sūryodayaṃ bhavet |

tāvajjapte maheśāni puraścaryāvidhiyate || 9-136 ||

96b) kṛṣṇāṣṭamīṃ samārabhya yāvat kṛṣṇauṣṭamī bhavet |

sahasrasaṃkhyajaptena puraścaraṇamiṣyate || 9-137 ||

śuklāṃ catudaśīṃ prāpya navamyaṃ taṃ mahotsave |

aṣṭamī navamī rātrau pūjāṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ || 9-138 ||

daśamyāṃ pāraṇaṃ kuryān matsyamāsāṃdibhiḥ priye |

ṣaṭsahasraṃ japennityaṃ bhaktibhāvaparāyaṇaḥ || 9-139 ||

caturdaśīṃ samārabhya yāvadanyā caturdaśī |

tāvajjapte maheśāni puraścaraṇamiṣyate || 9-140 ||

kevalaṃ japamātreṇa mantrāḥ siddhā bhavanti hi |

vinā homādidānena viśeṣāt pīṭhapūjane || 9-141 ||

yonipīṭhaṃ mahāpīṭhaṃ kāmarūpaṃ tathāparam |

tayorekatalaṃ pūjyaṃ rudradeha ivāparaḥ || 9-142 ||

athavānyaprakāreṇa puraścaraṇamiṣyate |

śaratkāre caturthyādi navaṃmyataṃ viśeṣataḥ || 9-143 ||

bhaktitaḥ pūjayitvā ca tāvadātrau sahasrakam |

japedekākivijane kevalaṃ timirālaya || 9-144 ||

aṣṭamyādi navamyāntamupavāsaparo bhavet |

aṣṭamyāṃ siddhivelā yā yāmaṣṭottaratāyutam || 9-145 ||

praviśyamantravidhivantāḥ samabhyarcya yatnataḥ |

pūrvoktaṃ nyāsamāsādya japapūjādikaṃ caret || 9-146 ||

kevalaṃ kāmadevosau japādaṣṭottarācchatān |

aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturddaśyāṃ navamyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 9-147 ||

rātriyogena deveśi gatvā ca pitṛkānane |

digaṃvaro muktakeśo bhutvā ca surasundari || 9-148 ||

prajapedayutaṃ mantraṃ devatābhāva tatparaḥ |

97a) mantrasiddhi bhavet tasya devī pūtra tu bhūtale || 9-149 ||

athavā prajepedvidyāṃ śūnyāgāracatuṣpathe |

rājadvāre śmasāne vā sabhavet sādhakottamaḥ || 9-150 ||

bhaumāvāsyā likhitvā ca yojayet sādhakottamaḥ |

mantrasiddhi bhavet tasya caṇḍikāṃ tatra pūjane || 9-151 ||

kuje vā śanivāre vā naramuṇḍaṃ samāhṛtam |

paṃcagavyaṃ militaṃ caṃdanādyairviśeṣataḥ || 9-152 ||

nikṣibhūmau hastārdhamānato nijane vane |

ekākī pratape (?)mantraṃ sabhāvet kalpapādapaḥ || 9-153 ||

ekarātrau japenmantramekākī nirjane vane |

sabhavet sādhakaśreṣṭho mahasiddhīśvaro bhave || 9-154 ||

vitāyāṃ ca japen mantraṃ mantrasiddhirnasaṃśayaḥ |

sabhavet sādhakaśreṣṭho devī putrona saṃśayaḥ || 9-155 ||

iti bhairavānandasaṃhi (sā)tām ||

sanatkumārasaṃhitāyām ||


athavānyaprakāreṇa puraścaraṇamiṣyate |

caṃdrasūryagrahe caiva grāsāvadhivimuktita || 9-156 ||

yāvat saṃkhyāmanujaptvā tāvad vomādhikaṃ caret |

śuddhadānānurodhena yadi jāpyaṃ tyajennaraḥ || 9-157 ||

sabhaved devatā drohi pintrun saptarajapyadhaḥ (?) |


iti sanatkumārasaṃhitā vacanena prametapitṛkasyāpi grahaṇe japaśyāvaśyakatve 
smṛtyukte vahuvacana vyākopane ||

tathā hi || 


sarvasvenāpi karttavyaṃ śrārddhaṃ vā rāhudarśana || 9-158 ||

akurccānastutacchrārddhaye ke gauri va sīdati |


tathā viruddhadharmasamavāya bhūyasāṃ syāt sadharmakatvamiti jaimina 
sūtrān ||

evaṃ paṭyapyadantakaḥ pūṣāpaimanti sadāvaram |


agnindreśvarasāmānyān taṇḍulātra vidhiyate || 9-159 ||


iti cchaṃdogapariśiṣṭīyāt ||

smṛtervalavattvena sanatkumārīyasyanīrviṣayāpantimi?riti cet sā vai vam |

āravdhapaṃcāṅgamahāpuraścaraṇamadhye pūrvānnapatita 
sūryagrahaṇaviṣayatve naivopapanteḥ ||

evaṃ ca niṃdyārthavādena grahaṇaśrāddhasyāvaśakatve pratinidhidvārātaditi 
||

atha bhūtabhairavam ||


yaduktaṃ sārasarvasve sarvatantreṣu gopitam |

vīrasiddhervidhānaṃ tu deva devena śaṃbhunā || 9-160 ||

yasya vijñānamātreṇa saṃdabhāgyopi siddhyati |

ahorātreṇakālena nahelayā sādhakottamaḥ || 9-161 ||

mantraṃ tvītvāguroḥ pārśve gurubhaktipurassaraḥ |

mantraśrotrāsya hṛnnetra ghrāṇānvijñāyayatnataḥ || 9-162 ||

yatvā ca kīlakaṃ jñātvā kuryān mantra puraskriyām |

puraścaraṇasaṃpanno virasiddhiṃ samācaret || 9-163 ||

putradhāradhanasneha lobhamohavivarjitaḥ |

mantraṃ vā sādhayiṣyāmi dehaṃ vā yātayāmyaham || 9-164 ||

pratijñāmīdṛśīṃ kṛtvā validravyaṃ samāharet |

yasya mantro yadguktaṃ tad gṛhyā śeṣavidhānataḥ || 9-165 ||

strīvarjyaṃ patitaṃ varjyaṃ kuṣṭarogādiniditam |

nadurbhikṣamṛtaṃ vṛddhaṃ naparyuṣitameva vā || 9-166 ||

strījanaṃ cedṛśaṃ rūpavarjitaṃ parivarjayet |

98a) saṃsthāpya kuśaśaryā yā muttānaṃ divyarūpiṇam || 9-167 ||

kṛtanityakriyodikṣu vidikṣupi ca kīlayet |

yantraṃ likhitvā vīrasya lalāṭabhyarcyadevatām || 9-168 ||

validānena santoṣya śavasya hṛdi saṃsthitaḥ |


valimāhamuṇḍamālāyām ||


kevalaṃ validānaṃ na tuṣṭā bhavati sundari || 9-169 ||

kathitaṃ pūrvamasmabhyaṃ prakāśaṃ kuru śaṃkaraḥ |


śrīśaṅkara uvāca 


atyantaguhyaṃ deveśi vidhānaṃ valipūjitam || 9-170 ||

kathayāmi varārohe susthirābhava sāṃpratam |

dadhikṣīraṃ gudaṃ nnānnapāyasaṃ sarkarānvitam || 9-171 ||

pāyasaṃ kṣaudramātraṃ vā nārikelaṃ samodakam |

śaśakaṃ mekhaṣaṃḍaṃ ca sādrakaṃ sahasarkaram || 9-172 ||

rambhāphalaṃ laḍukaṃ ca bharjitānnaṃ tu pīṭhakam |

śālamatsyaṃ ca pāṭhinaṃ śakulaṃ ciṅkaṭa tathā || 9-173 ||

maṅguraṃ cilliṣaṃ dadyān māṃsameṣasya santatam |

pakṣimāṃsaṃ varārohe ḍiṃbhaṃ nānāsamudbhavam || 9-174 ||

kṛṣṇabhāgaṃ meṣamāṃsaṃ godhikāhariṇīṃ tathā |

jalaje matsyamāṃse ca gaṃdhakīmāsameva ca || 9-175 ||

nānāvyaṃjanadugdhāni vyaṃjanāni madhuni ca |

īṣaddagdhaṃ ghṛte noktaṃ niśāyāṃ divasepi vā || 9-176 ||

valiṃ dadyād viśeṣeṇa kṛṣṇapakṣe śubhe dine |

cchāge datte bhaved vāgmī matsau dantaiḥ kavidhruvam || 9-177 ||

mahiṣedhanavṛddhiḥ syān mṛge bhogaphalaṃ bhavet |

datte pakṣiṇe ṛddhiḥ syāṅgodhikāyāṃ mahāphalam || 9-178 ||

98b) meṣadatte maharddhiḥ syāt sarvasiddhiranuttamā |

lalāṭahastahṛdaya śirovakṣastu madhyataḥ || 9-179 ||

svadehādrudhiredatte varadeha ivāparaḥ |

surādānena deveśi mahāyogīśvaro bhavet || 9-180 ||

rātrau prathamayāmārdhe pūjārthaṃ valimāharet |

paṃcopacāradravyāṇi sasurāṇi prayatnataḥ || 9-181 ||

guḍārdrakare senaiva surātre brāhmaṇasya ca |

gauḍīne yā kṣatriyeṇa mādhvī vaiśyena tatra tu || 9-182 ||

kadalīphalasaṃmiśra śānatvak svarasaiḥ surāḥ |

śūdreṇātra pradātavyā deyā vai naiva saṃspṛset || 9-183 ||

surā tu tridhidhā devi sphāṭikī ḍākinī tathā |

kārṇikī surayā devi yorcayet palameśvarīm || 9-184 ||

guṭikāṃjanastaṃbhādi māraṇoccāṭanādibhiḥ |

mahāsiddhiśvaro bhūtvā vaset kalyāyutaṃ divi || 9-185 ||

arghe dante maheśāni sarvakāmārthasiddhaye |


jayā kumārī 


suśītalajalairvāpi kastūrī kuṃkumānvitaiḥ || 9-186 ||

mūlamantreṇa deveśi ākṛṣyanibharyaḥ śuciḥ |

arghaṃ dattvā viśeṣeṇa cakravātena pujitam || 9-187 ||

kumārī pūjane śakto nārīsvapnepi nasmasmaret |

tāmrapātre kapāle vā śmaśānakāṣṭanirmite || 9-188 ||

śanibhaumadine vāpi śarāve mṛtsumudbhave |

svarṇairaupyathalohe vā cakramarcyadhidhānataḥ || 9-189 ||

puṣpāṇyapi tathā dadyād raktakṛṣṇasitāni ca |

śvetaṃ raktaṃ jayāpuṣpaṃ karavīre tathā priye || 9-190 ||

tagalaṃ mallikājāti mālatī yuthikā tathā |

99a) dusturāśokavahulaśvetakṛṣṇāparājitā || 9-191 ||

vakapuṣpaṃ vilvapatraṃ campakaṃ nāgakeśaram |

raṃgikācintrikākāṃci raktaṃ yat parikīrttitam || 9-192 ||

arkapuṣpaṃ javāpuṣpaṃ varvaraṃ ca priyaṃvade |

aṣṭamyāṃ ca pradānena santuṣṭā tasya pārvatī || 9-193 ||

padmapuṣpe saṃraktenaḥ santuṣṭā sarvadevatāḥ |

kṛṣṇavāyadivāśuklaṃ kālikāvaradā śubhā || 9-194 ||

śmaśāne dustareṇaiva tuṣṭā bhūmāvatī śivā |

āmalakṣyāstu patreṇa tuṣṭā puṣpaṇa parvaṇi || 9-195 ||

aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ nānāpuṣpaiḥ samarcayet |

śmaśāne rātriśeṣe vā śanibhaumadine niśi || 9-196 ||

pūjayitvā viśeṣeṇa sarvasiddhisvaro bhavet |

valiṃ datvā prayatnena bhūtaśuddhiṃ samācaret || 9-197 ||

bhūtaḥ śuddhiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā karāṅganyāsamācaret |

dhyātvā tu dehi devaṃ ca nānya cetā japaṃ caret || 9-198 ||

svatantroktavidhānena nadiśaścānavalokayana |

na paśyadadbhutaṃ kiṃcin nabhāṣe tatavaspṛśet || 9-199 ||

ahorātraṃ japaṃ kuryāt yāvat pratyakṣatāṃ vrajet |

vadabhyāgatya ceṣṭāṃ vā dehasphutiṃ karoti ca || 9-200 ||

ete na jāyate vīrasiddhividyā tato valim |

devatāṃ svaguruṃ natvā vismṛtya hṛdaye puna || 9-201 ||

sthāpaye bhojayedviprān śavaṃ to ye vinikṣipet |

tārākālī manānārī dvālasiddhiṃ samāharet || 9-202 ||

99b) athāsanavidhiṃ vakṣya sarvatantrānusārata |

āsanena vināsiddhiṃ navindeyuḥ sarā api || 9-203 ||

stambhanaṃ mohanaṃ caiva pūrvoktameva |

āsanaṃ kurute mantrī sarvakāmārthasiddhaye || 9-204 ||


taduktaṃ bhairavītantre ||


meṣavyāghragajoṣṭravṛkṣaturagalagbhistu ṣaṭkarmasu pratyakaṃ vividhāsanāni 
krimijaṃ tārākṣasāraṃ gajam |

mokṣaṃ bhogadamāsanaṃ ca kurute aurvaṃ tathā cailajaṃ vainaṃ caiva 
mṛgājinaṃ śubhakaraṃ sarvapraṇītaṃ vudhaiḥ || 9-205 ||


niṣiddhamāha ||


bhūmau bhūpabhayaṃ kulakṣayakaraṃ vaṃśāsanaṃ rogadaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ caiva 
tathāsanaṃ prakurute śailaṃ mahaduḥkhadam |

vaitrapallavamṛttikāsanayutaṃ traiṇaṃ ca vidveṣakṛccheṣaṃ kraimijamāsanāni 
sakalaṃ mokṣādi siddhipradam || 9-206 ||

kṛṣṇāṃjine jñānasiddhiḥ mokṣa śrīvyāghracarmaṇi |

carmāśane vyādhināśa urṇāyāṃ duḥkhamocani || 9-207 ||

kuśāsane mantrasiddhi nātrakāryā vicāraṇā |

kāmyārthaṃ kaṃvaraṃ caiva sarvārtheraktakevalam || 9-208 ||

svastikādikrameṇoktaṃ viśen tatra nicāmaṣa |

svastikādīni vakṣyāmi āvanāni viśeṣataḥ || 9-209 ||

svastikaṃ padmakaṃ vajraṃ vīraṃ ceti catuṣṭayam |

jape praśastameteṣu pūjite punarādime || 9-210 ||

teṣāṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣe svastyādīnāṃ yathākramam |

jānūpūrvorataraṃ kṛtvā samyak pādatala ubhe || 9-211 ||

100a) ṛjukāyaḥ samāsīnaṃ (ḥ) svastikaṃ tadudāhṛtam |

vāmorūparivinyasya dakṣiṇaṃ caraṇaṃ vudha || 9-212 ||

vāmaṃ puna dakṣiṇārauvyukramasya vidhānataḥ |

dhṛtvā karābhyāmaṅguṣṭhe nidhāya civukaṃ hṛdi || 9-213 ||

nāsāgraṃ vekṣeyed vidvān padmāsanamidaṃ matam |

japādāvacamākhyātaṃ padmāsanamitaṃ vudhaiḥ || 9-214 ||

vāmorau dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ nyasedvāmaṃ tathānyataḥ |

etā vadeva nivarttyajapahomādikaṃ caret || 9-215 ||

nāradīya tathā prāha nārāyaṇamaṇau muniḥ |

ubhātyāmurumūlābhyām agrapādāvubhāvapi || 9-216 ||

uparyāropya āsīte padmanāma tadāsinam |

ekapādaṃ tathaikasmin vinyasyoruṇi saṃsthitam || 9-217 ||

itarasmin tathā cārau vīrāsanamudīritam |

meḍhrāduparivinyasya savyaṃ gulphaṃ tatopari || 9-218 ||

gulphāntaraṃ ca vinyasya vakrāsanamudīritam |

atha mālāvidhiṃ vakṣye pravakṣyāmi viśeṣataḥ || 9-219 ||


prathamataḥ kalamālā prakāramāha ||


tatrāṅguli japaṃ kuryān naṅguṣṭhāṅgulibhirjapet |

aṃguṣṭhenavinākarma kṛtaṃ tadaphalaṃ bhavet || 9-220 ||


sāmānyaṃ tu ||

taduktaṃ gautamīye ||


ārabhyo nāmikaṃ madhyā pradakṣiṇakrameṇa ca |

tarjanīmūlaparyantaṃ japedaśasu parvasu || 9-221 ||


tathāhaṃ samārāyaṇi ||


amāmikātrayaṃ parva yathā devo tathā hariḥ |

100b) tasmāt tu sthiracittena karmakuryādahariśam || 9-222 ||

etadrahasyaṃ paramaṃ mayoktaṃ te svareśvari |

tvayā guptataraṃ kāryaṃ nākhyeyaṃ yasya kasyacit || 9-223 ||


prakārāntareṇaṃ bhairavakalpe [bhaivakalpe] ||


anulomavilomasthai viṃduvasmātṛkākṣaraiḥ |

kṣamerūkaiḥ sāṣṭavargaiḥ kliptayāvarṇamālayā || 9-224 ||

pratyekaṃ varṇayudvmatrā japtāḥ syuḥ kṣiprasiddhidāḥ |

vairimantrā api nṛṇāmanye maṃśca kiṃ punaḥ || 9-225 ||

akacaṭatapa yasāvargādyārṇāḥ samīritāḥ |

mantrāṇāṃ varṇayuktatvaṃ prathamaṃ varṇamurcaret || 9-226 ||

anayā yo japaṃ kuryā tasya siddhiradūrataḥ |


anulometi ādāvanulomena japaḥ |

akārādikṣakārānte japaḥ paścādvilomena japaḥ ||

kṣakārākṣi akārā tena japa ityarthaḥ ||

atra akṣaromerutvena alpanīyaḥ ||

tathā ca ||

hakārānantaraṃ rakārodeyaḥ ||

anyathā paṃcāśadvarṇānupapatteḥ śatārddhakeneti |

kramadīpikoktapāṭhavirodhācca śatāddhe neti pāṭha naiva nirvahati kathaṃ 
śatārddhakena tasyā cchatārddhake kiṃ viśiṣṭena kena pṛthivī jena 
saharakāreṇa sahetyarthaḥ ||

ete na napaṃcāśadvarṇatvahānirityarthaḥ ||

ata japamālā vidhānam ||


ādikṣāntaṃ tu yogatvā dakṣamāleti kīrttitaḥ || 9-227 ||

tad varṇasaṃkhyairmaṇibhirjapamālāṃ prakalpayet |

101a) rudrākṣamālikā devi japena samanorathān || 9-228 ||

padmākṣairvihitā mālā śatrunāśakarī matā |

kuśagranthiyutā mālā sarvapāpapraṇāsinī || 9-229 ||

putraḥ vījaphalaiḥ kliptā kurcate putrasaṃpadā |

nirmitāraupyamaṇibhi japamālepsita pradā || 9-230 ||

svarṇai viracitā mālā iṣṭān kāmān prayacchati |

pravālairvihitā mālā prayacchet puṣkalaṃ dhanam || 9-231 ||

saubhāgyaṃ sphāṭikī mālā mauktikairvihitā tathā |

vihitāḥ śaṃkhamaṇibhiḥ kurute kīrttimavyayam || 9-232 ||

sarvairetairviracitā mālāsyānmuktaye nṛṇām |

nityajapaṃ kare kuryān na tu kāmyamevādhanāt || 9-233 ||

nāsābhistu prakurvīt nityaṃ kāmyaṃ ca sādhakaḥ |

uccāṭana svadantānāṃ mālā proktā tu sūribhiḥ || 9-234 ||

kharaṃdantairadhobhūtai mānuṣyasnāyutantunā |

grathitainimito mālā śatrunāṃ nāśinī matā || 9-235 ||

pretadantairadhobhūtaiḥ karttavyā japamālikā |

sādhyadehabhavaiḥ keśaiḥ srotavyā dveṣakarmaṇi || 9-236 ||

vaiṣṇave tulasīmālā gajadantairgaṇeśvare |

tripurā yā japeśastā idrākṣai raktacandanaiḥ || 9-237 ||

mahāśaṃkhamayī mālā nīlasārabhyatīmanau |

aṃgulī japasaṃkhyānamekamekamudāhṛtam || 9-238 ||

putravījaidaśaguṇaṃ śataṃ śaṃkhaisahasrakam |

pravālairmaṇiratnaiśca dasasāhasrakaṃ matam || 9-239 ||

tadeva sphātikī proktaṃ mauktikailakṣamucyate |

101b) svarṇaidaśasahasraṃ tu śaṃkhaiḥ śataguṇaṃ tathā || 9-240 ||

kuśagranthyā ca rudrākṣairanantaguṇitaṃ bhavet |

śuklapadmākṣamaṇibhirapi syādamitaṃ phalam || 9-241 ||

mātṛkāt tu samūbhūta mālayāpi ca tatphalam |

athavā tāṃ śataiḥ kuryāt sarvasādhāraṇe jape || 9-242 ||

paṃcāśadbhiḥ kāmya karmasiddhiḥ syāccaturoharaiḥ |

aṣṭottaraśataiḥ sarvasiddhirukta kṛtasrajā || 9-243 ||

mātṛkā tu samudbhūta mālayāpi ca tat phalam |

athavā tāṃ śataiḥ kuyāt sarvasādhāraṇe japet || 9-244 ||

paṃcāśadbhiḥ kāmyakramasiddhiḥ syācaturottaraiḥ |

aṣṭottaraśataiḥ sarvasiddhiruktakṛtasrajā || 9-245 ||

mokṣārthī paṃcaviśatyā dhanārthī triśantā japet |

puṣṭyārthe saptaviṃśatyā paṃcadaśābhicārake || 9-246 ||

nāradīye tu saṃproktaṃ napaṃcadaśakasyacit |

anayoḥ pakṣayo tasmād vikalpaḥ syād devi sthitaḥ || 9-247 ||

athavānyaprakāreṇa | mālāvakṣyāmi tatvata |


taduktaṃ sārasaṃhitāyām ||


aṃgulyā japasaṃdhānamekamekaṃ varānane || 9-248 ||

parvacāṣṭaguṇaṃ vidyāt putrajīvai phalairdaśa |

śataṃ vai śaṃkhamaṇibhi pravālaiśca sahasrakam || 9-249 ||

mauktikaidarśalakṣaṃ tu suvarṇaiḥ koṭirucyate |

kuśagranthyā daśakoṭi rudrākṣaiścāpyanantakam || 9-250 ||

mauktikaiḥ śvetapadmākṣai rudrākṣaimukticetare |

staṃbhe suvarṇaṃ jānīyādābhicāna tu niṃdakam || 9-251 ||

102a) raktacaṃdanapadmaṃ ca putrajīva pravālakam |

vaśyākarṣaṇayordevi puraḥ kṣobhe viśeṣataḥ || 9-252 ||

sarvā sā madhyamālānāṃ jape yat phalamucyate |

tatphalaṃ śatasāhaṃ prajapedaṃguliparvaṇi || 9-253 ||

tasmādaṃguliparvāṇi japārthaṃ pravalāni ca |

aṃguṣṭhela japen mokṣa tarjanyātvabhircarike || 9-254 ||

madhyamā vanikāmārthā śāntau puṣṭāvanāmikā |

kaniṣṭhākarṣaṇe cāsāṃ japakarmaṇiśobhane || 9-255 ||

aguṣṭhena japejjapyamanyairaṃgulibhiḥ saha |

aṃguṣṭhena vinākarma kṛtaṃ tanniṣphalaṃ bhavet || 9-256 ||

paṃcaviṃśatimokṣārthī saptaviṃśati pauṣṭike |

dhanārthī triṃśatāt kuryāt paṃcadaśyābhicālike || 9-257 ||

kullukāṃ ca tato natvā mālā pūjāṃ vidhāya ca |

gṛhniyād dakṣiṇe nātha na ca vāmena saṃspṛśet ||

nābhi hṛcchiradeśe tu kullukāṃ paricintayet || 9-258 ||


sphāṭikyarudrākṣakaputrajīvairindrākṣapadmākṣakamauktikaiśca |

pravālasauvarṇikakośaśaṃkhai mālā bhavet saukhyakarīrarāṇām || 9-259 ||

devyā bhavet prītikarī pravālaiḥ sovarṇikaiḥ sphāṭikamoktikairvā |

śivasya rudrākṣavinirmitaiśca prītirbhavesphāṭikanirmitairvā || 9-260 ||

viṣṇoḥ sadā prītikarī praśaste rudrākṣamālātha ca kauśavī vā |

padmākṣakai vā pyatha mauktikairvā rudrākṣamālā satataṃ praṇastā || 9-261 ||


pravālaireva karttāvyā tripurā yā viśeṣataḥ |

102b) athavā śaṃkhamaṇibhi mālākāryā viśeṣataḥ || 9-262 ||

aṣṭottaraśatairvījai praśastā sarvakarmaṇi |

śatārdhenāpi karttavyā tathaiva parikīrttitā || 9-263 ||

paṃcapaṃcāśatairvāpi māraṇoccāṭanādiṣu |

sāttvikī rājasī caiva tāmasīti tridhoditā || 9-264 ||

sāttvikī prathamāmālā dvitīyā rājasī matā |

tṛtīyā tāmasī proktā māraṇādau prayojayet || 9-265 ||

śuklairvinirmitā mālā mokṣakarmaṇi śasyate |

kṛṣṇairvinirmitā mālā māraṇādau prayujyate || 9-266 ||

paṭṭasūtraiḥ kṛtā mālā devyāḥ prītikarī sadā |

karpāsaivaiṣṇavī mālā paṭṭasūtrairathāpi vā || 9-267 ||

ūrṇābhirvālkalairvāpi saurīmālā prakīrttitā |

anyeṣāmapi devānāṃ karttarvyā kāmacārataḥ || 9-268 ||

dvādaśyāṃ vaiṣṇavīmālā karttavyā sopavāsakaiḥ |

mantrajñairviśvamantreṇa divābhāgena śalyate || 9-269 ||

śaktīnāmapi karttavyā rātrau tulkā yathāvidhi |

aṣṭamyāṃ ca navamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ tathaiva ca || 9-270 ||

trayodaśyāṃ tithau kuryā cchivasyāpi sureśvari |

ardhavatulatāmālā sthūlāsūkṣmāniyo japet || 9-271 ||

sarpākārā bhavet sā tu śubhadā sarvakarmaṇi |

sarvathāpi hi mālānāṃ meru pārśvadvayaṃ tathā || 9-272 ||

na spṛśet tu kadācit tu spṛṣṭhe tvā varttanaṃ punaḥ |

hastaparvaṇi vā kuryāt sarvatraiṣavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 9-273 ||

anāmikā triparvāṇi kaniṣṭhā mūlato japet |

103a) madhyamāyā dvayaṃ meruraṃguṣṭhāyāstrayaṃ tathā || 9-274 ||

nakadācit spṛśen mantrī satataṃ japakarmaṇi |

yathā śaktijapenmantrī sahasramayutaṃ tathā || 9-275 ||

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ vāpi japānte śirasikṣipet |


oṃ tvaṃ māle sarvadevānāṃ prītidā śubhadā mama |


śivaṃ kuruṣva me bhadre yaśovīrya ca sarvadā || 9-276 ||

grahaṇe sthāpane caiva pujane vinijojayet |


atha japavidhānam ||

tathoktaṃ kapilapaṃcarātre ||


suptaḥ pravodhakālotha mantrāṇāmabhidhiyate || 9-277 ||

agnisomātmakāmantrā vijñayā krurasaumyayāḥ |

kamaṇo vahnitārābhyaṃ viṣatprāyasamīritāḥ || 9-278 ||

agneyāmanavaḥ saumyā bhuyiṣṭhendvamṛtākṣarāḥ |

āgneyāṃ saṃprabuddhyate prāṇe bhavati dakṣiṇe || 9-279 ||

bhāgenyasmit sthite prāṇe saumyavodhaṃ prayānti ca |

nāḍīdvayagate prāṇe sarvavodhaṃ prayāntite || 9-280 ||

prayacchanti phalaṃ sarve pravudvā mantriṇaḥ sadā |


yadvā ||


prāṇāpāna samāyuktā śivaśaktyostu melanam || 9-281 ||

pravodhakālo vijñeyaḥ svāpakālastataḥ palaḥ |

vāmavāhau yadā vāyurdīrghāṇāṃ jojanaṃ tadā || 9-282 ||

dakṣiṇasyāṃ yadā vāyustadā hrasvāni yojitaḥ |

ubhayastho yadā vāyustadānya dvitayātmakaḥ || 9-283 ||

saṃpuṭīkṛtya mantreṇa nātā *? dyāt saviṃdukān |

punaśca savisargāṃstāt kṣakāre kevalaṃ japet || 9-284 ||

evaṃ japtā yadiṣṭhaścat pravuddhaḥ śīghrasiddhidaḥ |


iti suptapravodhakālaḥ ||


103b) athātaḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi japaṃ mantrasya tattvataḥ || 9-285 ||

ādau tu kullukāṃ dhyāyen mantrasiddhi pradāyinīm |

nābhihṛcchiradeśe tu mantrī niyatamānasaḥ || 9-286 ||


dhyānaṃ yathā ||


raktāraktasarojasthāṃ raktāraṅkārabhūṣitām |

raktāmvaradharā devīṃ visatantu tanīyasīm || 9-287 ||

kāmarājagatāṃ sārddha veṣṭane trayaśobhitām |

saptasaryanibhāṃ bhāsvat bhāskarākāramaṃḍalām || 9-288 ||

evaṃ vicitya prajapedāsanastho jītendriyaḥ |


evamādau kullukāṃ dhyātvā tasmāmattottaraṃ śataṃ japtvā 
punamūlamantraṃ japet ||

kullukāyāmantro yathā ||


eṇa vāhuṃ namaḥ paścān mantroyaṃ caturakṣaraḥ || 9-289 ||


oṃ huṃ namaḥ ||


kullukāṃ ca tato natvā mūlā pūjāṃ vidhāya ca |

gṛhnīyād dakṣiṇe nātha navavāmena saṃspṛśet || 9-290 ||

japastu trividho jñeyo vācikopāṃśumānasaḥ |

nātissṣṭhāṃ na cāspaṣṭanāti drutanilaṃvitam || 9-291 ||

japen mantramabhinnaṃ ca hyekacittaḥ samāhitaḥ |

yadyaduccarate mantrī spaṣṭaśabdayutaiḥ svaraiḥ || 9-292 ||

śanairuccārayen mantraṃ japāyajñaśca vācikaḥ |

śanairuccārayen mantramīṣadoṣṭhaṃ pracālayet || 9-293 ||

kiṃci cchravaṇayogyaḥ syād upāṃśuḥ sajapaḥ smṛtaḥ |

dhipā yadakṣaraśreṇīṃ varasvamaparātmikām || 9-294 ||

śabdārthacitanābhyāsaḥ sa-ukto mānaso japaḥ |

uccaije pādupāṃśuryaḥ sasahasraguṇo bhavet || 9-295 ||

104a) sahasraguṇamuddiṣṭastasmādapi ca mānasaḥ |

dhyāyet tu manasā mantraṃ jihvoṣṭau na ca cālayet || 9-296 ||

nakaṃpayecchirogrivaṃ dantānnaiva prakāśayet |

arākṣarabhedena siddhavidyādharāgaṇāḥ || 9-297 ||

haranti prakaṭaṃ yasmāt tasmād guptaṃ sadākuru |

āsanastho japet samyaṅmattrānvai yata mānasaḥ || 9-298 ||

viṃdulīnaṃ namaḥ kṛtvā nādamārge niyojayet |

nādamārge vilīnaṃ tu manonānyatra gacchati || 9-299 ||

prāṇāyāmaṃ trayakṛtvā gāyantyādin japed iti |

anenaiva japed yastu mahāpātakasaṃcayaḥ || 9-300 ||

sakṛduccarite naiva vakṣayaṃ yāt pupapātakam |


iti ||


mālayāgrathanaṃ vakṣye sarvatantreṣu gopitam || 9-301 ||

kṛtanitya kriye nityaṃ eteṣukteṣu mantravit |

yathā kāmaṃ yathā lobhamakṣamānīya yatnataḥ || 9-302 ||

anyonya samarūpāṇi nātisthūlakṛṣāni vai |

kīṭādibhiraduṣṭāni najirṇāni navāni vai || 9-303 ||

gavyaistu paṃcabhistāni prakṣālya ca pṛthak pṛthak |

gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhisarpiḥ kuśodakam || 9-304 ||

paṃcagavyaṃ vijānīyāt sarvakarmasumantravit |

payodadhighṛtaṃ kṣaudraṃ śarkarādyairanukramāt || 9-305 ||

paṃcāmṛtaṃ vijāniyāt sarvakarmasusādhakaḥ |

tato dvijendre puṇyastrī nirmitaṃ graṃrthivarjitam || 9-306 ||

triguṇaṃ triguṇī kṛtya sūtraṃ prakṣālya pūrvavat |

śuklaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ tathā raktaṃ śātivaśyābhicārake || 9-307 ||

104b) sutraṃ saṃpādayed viprān paṭṭasūtramathāpi vā |

aśvatthapattre navake padmākāreṇa sthāpite || 9-308 ||

sūtraṃ maṇīṃścagadhābhaḥ kṣālitāṃ smatranikṣipet |

tāraṃ śaktimātṛkāṃ ca sūtre caiva maṇiṣvatha || 9-309 ||

vinyasya pūjayedājyairjuhuyāccaiva śaktitaḥ |

homakarmaṇye śaktaśced dviguṇaṃ japamācaret || 9-310 ||

maṇimekaikamādāya sūtre vai yojayet tataḥ |

mukhaṃ mukhe ca saṃyojya pucchaṃ pucche ca yojayet || 9-311 ||

gopucchasadṛśī kāryā yadvā sarpākṛtiḥ śubhā |

tat sajātīyamekaṃ tu marutvenāgrato nyaset || 9-312 ||

ekaikaṃ maṇimadhye tu brahmagranthiṃ ca dāpayet |

japamālāṃ vidhāyetthaṃ tataḥ saṃskāramācaret || 9-313 ||

kṣālayet paṃcagavyena sadyojātena sajjanaiḥ |


mantro yathā ||

oṃ sadyojātaṃ praṇyāmi sadyojātāya vai namaḥ 
bhave bhevenāti bhave bhajasvaṃ mābhavodbhavāya namaḥ 
caṃdanāgurugaṃdhādyairvāmadevena ghaṣayet ||

oṃ vāmadevāya namo juṣṭāya namo rudrāya namaḥ kālāya 
namaḥ kalavikaraṇāya namo vikaraṇāya namo valapramathanāya 
namaḥ sarvabhūtadamanāya namo manonmanāya namaḥ ||


dhūpayet tāṃ aghoreṇa lepayet tat puruṣeṇa tu || 9-314 ||

oṃ aghorebhyotha ghorebhyo ghoraghoratarebhyaḥ |

sarvataḥ sarvasarvebhyo namaste rudrarūpebhyaḥ || 9-315 ||


oṃ tat puruṣāya vidmahe mahādevāya dhīmahe tanno rudrapracodayāt || ||


105a) mantrayet paṃcame ṇaiva pratyekaṃ ca śataṃ śatam |


oṃ īśāna sarvavidyānāmīśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ brahmādhipati 
brahmaṇodhipati brahmāṇi vāmestra sadāśivām ||


sumerupaṃcame ṇaiva tato mantreṇa mantrayet || 9-316 ||

saṃskṛtyaivaṃ vudhomālāṃ tat prāṇāt sthāpayet tataḥ |

evaṃ kṛtvākṣamālāyāṃ japen mātṛkayā sudhīḥ || 9-317 ||

mātṛkāmantrabhedebhyaḥ sarvemantrāḥ prajajñire |

ataḥ? sakalamantrāṇāṃ japaḥ kāryāna yā srajā || 9-318 ||

guruṃ saṃpūjya taddhastād gṛhnīyāt sarvasiddhaye |

gāyatrī japena kecin mālāṃ granthaṃ lya mantrakam || 9-319 ||

nahītthaṃ grathanaṃ kvāpi mantraśāstre nirūpitam |

aśucirnaspṛśedenāṃ karabhraṣṭāṃ nakārayet || 9-320 ||

aṃguṣṭhasthāmakṣamālāṃ cālayen madhyamāgrataḥ |

tarjanyā naspṛśet seyaṃ muktido gaṇanakramaḥ || 9-321 ||

bhukto muktau tathā kṛṣṇau madhyamāyāṃ japet sudhīḥ |

aṃguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ ca japed uttamakarmaṇi || 9-322 ||

tarjanyaṃguṣṭhasaṃyogād vidveṣoccāṭanā japeḥ |

kaniṣṭhāṅgu saṃyogājjapen māraṇakarmaṇi || 9-323 ||

japānyaṃ kāle tāṃ mālāṃ pūjayitvā tu gopayet |

guruṃ prakāśayed vidvān mantraṃ naiva prakāśayet || 9-324 ||

akṣamālāṃ ca mudrāṃ ca gurorepi na darśayet |

bhūtarākṣasavetārāṃ (h) siddhe gaṃdharvacāraṇāḥ || 9-325 ||

haṃranti prakaṭaṃ tasmāt tasmāguptaṃ japet sudhīḥ |

jīrṇe sūtre punaḥ sutyuṃ grathayitvā śataṃ japet || 9-326 ||

105b) pratiṣṭhāyāṃ vidhiṃ deva śrotumicchāmi tattvataḥ |


śrī-īśvara uvāca 


śilpinaṃ pūjayedādau vastragaṃdhānulepanaiḥ || 9-327 ||

shṛṣṭakālayet mālāṃ viśvasthāṃ svarṇarūpiṇīm |

yathākramaṃ yathātattvaṃ yathāpūrvayathoditam || 9-328 ||

nirviṭaṃkā ca sā mālā nirmalākṣatavarjitā |

yathāyogya vedhavatī mālā kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ || 9-329 ||

varṇināyai na yā kāryā paṃcagavyatryahaṃ kṣipet |

sūtraṃ cāpi caturthehni kṣālayedastramantrakaiḥ || 9-330 ||

samānavarṇasūtrāṇāṃ grathayeddhṛdayānunā |


nama iti mantreṇa ||


suvarṇādiguṇevāpi grathayet sādhakottamaḥ || 9-331 ||

brahmagranthi tato dadyān nāgapāśamathāpi vā |

kavacenāvadhnīyā māna saṃdhyāmayogataḥ || 9-332 ||


kavace na huṃ miti mantreṇa ||


sarvaśeṣe tato meruṃ sūtradvayasamanvitam |

grathayet praṇavayogena vadhnīyāt sādhakottamaḥ || 9-333 ||

evaṃ niṣpādya deveśi pratiṣṭhāṃ ca samācaret |

sthaṇḍile maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā yathā bhāgavidhikramāt || 9-334 ||

pūjayitvā yathā nyāyamiṣṭadevamanukramāt |

nyāsapūrvaṃ japen mantraṃ maṣṭottarasahasrakam || 9-335 ||

juhuyāt tad daśāṃśena yasya devasya yat priyam |

tato maṇḍalamadhya tu tanmālāṃ sthāpayed budhaḥ || 9-336 ||

astramantraṃ yathā nyasya mūlamantraṃ tato nyaset |


astramantraṃ phaḍitimantram ||


aṅgāni tāni vinyasya devarūpaṃ vicintayet || 9-337 ||

106a) abhedarūpamāsādya mālāṃ kuryāt tadātmikām |

tato valiṃ yathā nyāyaṃ dadyād vai sādhakottamaḥ || 9-338 ||

evaṃ pratiṣṭhāmāsādya mālāyāmiṣṭadevatām (ḥ) |

ācāryaṃ pujayen mantrī śilpinaṃ ca yathāvidhi || 9-339 ||

nānya mantraṃ japen tatra yadīcchet paramātmanaḥ |


iti mālā pratiṣṭhāmantravidhiḥ ||

atha puraścaraṇasāmānya dharmāḥ ||


mantraṃ sādhayamānastu trisaṃdhya devamarcayet || 9-340 ||

dvisaṃdhyaṃ ekasaṃdhyaṃ vā namantraṃ kevalaṃ japet |

japānte vā bhavet pūjā pūjānte vā japen manum || 9-341 ||


iti vacanāt ||

pūjā yā āvaśyakatvamiti ||


devī cittagatā kāryā kārya ca hṛdayasthiram |

tat tarjaptāmnapānīyaiḥ kuryāt pānādibhojanam || 9-342 ||

nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kuryāt saṃgamaṃ sādhubhiḥ saha |

snāyācca paṃcagavyeta kevalāmalakena vā || 9-343 ||

ājyena dadhnā kṣīreṇatilakalkena vā kṛtam |

puraścaryā vidhau tadvat snānaṃ kuryād viśuddhaye || 9-344 ||

śaktau triṣavaṇasnānamaśaktau dvisakṛt tathā |

asnātasya phalaṃ nāsti na ca tarpayataḥ pitrun || 9-345 ||

anyāni kāmyakarmāṇi tathā sādhusamāgamam |

strī śūdrapatita vātya nāstiketitabhāṣaṇam || 9-346 ||

asatyabhāṣaṇaṃ coktiṃ kauṭilyaṃ parivarjayet |

tyajed uṣṇodakasnānaṃ aniveditabhojanam || 9-347 ||

śayita kuśaśayyāyāṃ śucivastradharaḥ sadā |

pratyahaṃ kṣālaye cchayyāmekākīnibhayaḥ svayet || 9-348 ||

106b) śrutismṛtivirodhena japaṃ rātro vivarjayet |

gurave dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād bhojanācchādanādibhiḥ || 9-349 ||

gurusantoṣamātreṇa sarvasiddhirbhaveddhruvam |

gurumūlamidaṃ sarvamityāhustatra vedinaḥ || 9-350 ||

ekagrāma sthito nityaṃ gatvā vande tu vai gurum |

guruleva paraṃbrahmaṃ tasmādādau tamarccayet || 9-351 ||

miṣṭaṃ nānāvidhaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhujītavaṃdhubhiḥ saha |


tataḥ ||


prātaḥ kāre samārabhya japen madhyandināvadhi || 9-352 ||


ityādau madhyaṃ dināvadhītinaniyamaḥ ||

punaravadhimātravodhakamidam ||

anyathā niyame sati samāpti divase kadācijjapasaṃkhyayāt 
punatvānniyamahānyāpatteriti bhāvaḥ ||


manaḥ saṃhṛtya viṣayān mantrārthagatamānasaḥ |

nadrutaṃ navilaṃvitaṃ japen mauktikapaṃktivat || 9-353 ||

japaḥ syād akṣarāvṛtti mānasopāṃśuvācikaḥ |

dhiyāyadakṣaraśreṇāṃ varṇasvarapadātmikām || 9-354 ||

uccredarthamuddiśya mānasaḥ * japaḥ smṛtaḥ |

jihvoṣṭau cālayet kiṃcid devatāgatamānasaḥ || 9-355 ||

kiṃcicchravaṇayogyaṃ syād upāṃśuḥ sajapaḥ smṛtaḥ |

mantramuccārayed vācā vācakaḥ sajapaḥ smṛtaḥ || 9-356 ||

vācikāllakṣaguṇita upāṃśuḥ parikīrttitaḥ |

upāṃśoḥ koṭiguṇitaṃ mānaseṣu praśrasyate || 9-357 ||

kṛtvā japaṃ purā caivaṃ tejorūpaṃ samarcayet |

devasya dakṣiṇe haste kuśapuṣpārghavāribhiḥ || 9-358 ||

107a) śaktiviṣaya vāmahaste japaṃ samarpayet |


tathā ca ||


evaṃ japaṃ puro kṛtyā gaṃdhākṣatakuśodakaiḥ || 9-359 ||

japaṃ samarpayed devyā vāmahaste vicakṣaṇa iti vacanāt |

nanyūnaṃ cādhikaṃ vāpi japaṃ kuryād dine dine || 9-360 ||

yadi kuryāt pramādā tu neṣṭaṃ phalamavāpnuyāt |

japā te pratyahaṃ mantrī homayet tu daśāṃsataḥ || 9-361 ||

tarpaṇaṃ cābhiṣekaṃ ca tad daśāṃśasatomune |

pratyahaṃ bhojayed viprān nyūnādhikapraśāntaye || 9-362 ||

athavā sarvamūrttau ca homādhikamathācaret |


haumādyaśaktau tu ||


yadyadaṅgaṃ bhaved bhaṃgaṃ tat saṃkhyā dviguṇo japaḥ || 9-363 ||

homābhāve japaḥ kāryo homasaṃkhyā caturguṇaḥ |


tathā ca gautamīye ||


dviprāṇāṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ ca samasaṃkhyā ca guṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 9-364 ||

vaiśyānāṃ vasusaṃkhyā kameṣāṃ strīṇāmayaṃ vidhiḥ |

yaṃ varṇamāśritaḥ śūdraḥ sa ca tasya vidhiṃ smaret || 9-365 ||


iti yadvacanaṃ tad vaiṣṇave viṣayam ||

tathā ca yoginīhṛdaye |


homarkamanyasaktānāṃ viprāṇā dviguṇo japaḥ |

itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāṃ triguṇādi samanvita || 9-366 ||


iti sarvatraviṣayam ||

etena śūdrasyāpi homādhikāra iti tanna ||


praṇavasya japāddhomāt sāligrāmaśilārcanāt |

brahmaṇī gamanāścaiva śūdraś*?ḍāṃ latāṃ vrajet || 9-367 ||


tasyā brāhmaṇakṣattriya vaiśyānāmeva homādhikāraḥ |

śūdrastu homānukalpadviguṇajapaṃ kuyāt ||

107b) tathā ca nāradapaṃcarātre ||


trayāṇāmevavarṇānāṃ home vidhirudīritaḥ |

śūdrastu tat phalākāṃkṣī homāt tu dviguṇaṃ japet || 9-368 ||


tathā ca smṛtiḥ ||


amantrasya tu śudrasya vipro mantreṇa gṛhyata |


iti vacanāt ||

homastu brahmaṇa dvārai va karttavyaḥ ||

anyathā vinigamakā bhāvāt ||

śūdrasyāpi somayāgā vadhikārāpatteḥ ||

tathā ca vacanamidam ||

brahmaṇādivarṇatrayaviṣayam ||

tathā ca strīṇāmapi homanādhikāraḥ ||

evaṃ ca ||


lājā trimadhuropetai homakanyā prayacchati || 9-369 ||


aṇena vidhinākanyā varamāpnoti vāṃcchitamiti vacanasya vivāha 
prakaraṇīyatyādya jamānapatnīvat prekṣaṇamātravidhānayakatvācca strīṇāṃ 
homepi brahmaṇadvārai va itmeṣāṃ tu varṇānāmiti vacanaṃ 
sarvavarṇaviṣayamiti etena sudrasyāpi homādhikāra iti kecit ||

tat tucchaṃ etad vacanārthā vodhāt |

etad vacanasya sūdrahomavidhāya katvā? bhāvāt |

sūdre taraviṣaye tve ita rekhāmiti vahuvacanāmupattestasmāt 
sarvavarṇānukalpayenametat ||

nanujapānte pratyaṃhamantrī iti vacana mantri svenopadānā cchudrasyāpi 
māntrike tvā ddhaumādhikāropatteriti vet |

strī śūdrānādhiyatāmiti viśeṣaśravaṇād vidyāprayukte kalpanāt |

108a) na ca sthapatyadhikaraṇe virodha iti vācyam |

vidherādhārasya prāpakatvāt ||

nucādhānānupattiḥ ||

asya śudrasya vipro mantreṇa gṛhyata iti vacanasya valavattvāt ||

na ca prakaraṇaniyantriteti vācyam ||

sāmānyavādhakā bhāvāt |

śūdrādyarūpatteśca tasmalaukikādikaraṇa nyāyena sarvaviṣayamidam ||

tacca śūdrāṇāṃ dvyaśrakuṃḍamiti vacanaṃ tanna ||

śūdrasya sākṣātkatvādhidāyakaṃ brāhmaṇakartṛtve naiva patteḥ || na ca ||


mokṣaśaktau dvijātīnāṃ manukalpo vidhīyate |


iti || smṛti vacanān mokṣaśaktasyaivānukalpedhikārā nāsti kathaṃ tasya 
homānukalpaṃ vidhānamiti vācyam ||

śudrasyānukalpā bhāvāt ||

brāhmaṇa dvārā homādividhānāt ||

tasmāt strīśudrāṇāṃ brāhmaṇadvārā homa vidhiriti kecit ||

nanu puraścaraṇādau phalaśravaṇāt kāmyameva tathā ca yatīnāṃ kathamatra 
samāveśaḥ tathā śrībhagavadgītāsu ||

kāmyānāṃ karmaṇā nyāsaṃ nyāsaṃ kavayoviduḥ ||

iti vacanāt ||

teṣāṃ kāmyakarmādhikārā bhāvāt |

na teṣāṃ puraścaraṇādhikāra iti puraścaraṇastāvad vidhī bhavati paṃcāṅgaḥ 
kevalajapaśca yatīnāṃ kevalajapapuraścaraṇedhikāraḥ naviśiṣṭha ityarthaḥ 
vastutasteṣāṃ pāpakṣayasādhanatayo japedhikārāt ||

108b) kṣa mokṣārthi tayā japādau pravṛttirvodhakā bhāvāt |

pravṛttiśca vedāntaśravaṇa prāṇāyāmādau tadvodhakaśrutiriti dvik ||

homāśaktau tu sarveṣāṃ dvi śojapaḥ ||

tathā ca yoginīhṛdaye ||



homārthaṃ dviguṇaṃ vāpi japanmantramananyadhīḥ || 9-370 ||

naikavāso japen mantraṃ vahuvāsastu saṃyutaḥ |

patitānāmantyajānāṃ daśarne bhāṣaśe śrute || 9-371 ||

kṣutedho vāyugamane jṛbhanejapamutsṛjet |

tathācamya ca tat prāptau prāṇāyāmaṃ ṣaḍaṃgakam || 9-372 ||

kṛvā samyag jape ccheṣaṃ yad vā sūryādidarśanam |


ādi padādvahniṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ca ||


evaṃ siddhamanurmantrī sādhayet sakalepsitān || 9-373 ||

athavānyaprakāreṇa puraścaraṇamiṣyate |

grahaṇe kasya cendorvā śuciḥ pūrvamupāṣitaḥ || 9-374 ||

nadyāṃ samudragāminyāṃ nābhimātrodakasthitaḥ |

sparśād vimuktiparyantaṃ japen mantramananyadhīḥ || 9-375 ||


nadyābhāve ||


yad vā puṇyodake snātvā śuciḥ pūrvamupoṣitaḥ |

grahaṇādivimokṣāntaṃ japen mantramananyadhīḥ || 9-376 ||

dṛṣṭvā snātvā sasaṃkalpo vimokṣāntaṃ japaṃ caret |


iti japanmantrasiddhirbhavatyeva nasaṃśayaḥ |


śraddhadānāvarodhena yadi jāpyaṃ tyajennaraḥ || 9-377 ||

sabhaved devatā drohī pitrūtsaptanayatyadhaḥ |


109a) iti sanatkumālasaṃhitāyā vacanaṃ ārabdha mahāpuraścaṇamadhyapatita 
śrāddhaniṣedhaparam |

yoginihṛdaye ||


kalpoktavidhināmatrī kuryāddhaumaṃ samāhitaḥ || 9-378 ||

athavā tad daśāṃśena homāṅgāśca samācaret |

nanupaṃcāṅgopāsanaṃ loke puraścaraṇamiṣyate || 9-379 ||


iti vacanāt puraścalaṇapadajaparhomatarpaṇābhiṣeka brahmaṇabhojanānīti 
paṃcāṅgaparam ||

grasanādau tu ||


sūryodayaṃ samārabhya yāvat sūryodayāvadhi |


ityādyekavākyatāvalāt puraścaraṇapadaṃ japamātravodhakamiti ||

kathaṃ homādi prasaṃga iti cet ||

tāvad yajñādhikaṃ kāryaṃ grahaṇānte śuciḥ pumān ||

iti vacanāt ||

na ca puraścaraṇasya paṃcāṅgavodhakatvāt ||

sarvasya va tad vodhakatvaṃ syāti vācyam |

atra viśeṣo pādāntapada vai yakyāpattaiḥ ||

grahaṇa puraścaraṇe vidhānaṃ tu pradhānatulyatākhyāpanāya ete na 
paṃcāṅgasvarūpe kṛte mokṣepyadhikāra iti prakāśitaṃ 
tasmāt tayorakaraṇe kevalajapamātrāṅgapuraścaraṇenādhikāra iti 
sarvajanasiddhāntaḥ ||


athavajye parāṃ siddhiṃ śmaśāne vīrasādhanam || 9-380 ||


pūrvoktadravyamāhṛtya śmaśāne copaviśya ca nyāsādikaṃ tu sakalaṃ 
rakṣārthaṃ purvavaccaret ||

āmmanaḥ paritaḥ paścād yaje detā ḍiśavāditaḥ śivāditaḥ īśānoditaḥ ||


109b) kārabhairavanāmānaṃ mahābhairavasaṃjñakam |

vaṭubhairava bhairavau māṣabhaktaivaliharet || 9-381 ||

tataḥ paṃcopacāreṇa pūrato devatāṃ yajet |

nimīlaṃ cakṣuṣī paścād devaṃ dhyāyen manuṃ japet || 9-382 ||


yadya sajya bhayaṃ bhayaṃ karṇanetrā vastreṇavaṃdhayet |


astraprahāranetre vā karṇe vā dhairyamāvahet |

tatorddharātriparyantaṃ yadi kiṃcin nalabhyate || 9-383 ||

jayadurgānunācārghya te naiva sarṣapālkṣipet |


mantrastu ||

oṃ durge 2 rakṣaṇī svāhā ||


purvavarttiramutkṣipya gatvā saptapadānyathā || 9-384 ||

devaṃ tatrāpi saṃpūjya prajapen manuvaryakam |

nirbhayet sajaped yāvat siddhiṃ vā guvyate naraḥ || 9-385 ||

tataḥ satyaṃ kārayitvā prārthayedvaramuttamam |

yadi devaḥ prārthayate narakuṃjalameva vā || 9-386 ||

dināntare tu dāsyāmi svīkṛtya svagṛhaṃ vrajet |

parehni pūrvavad dadyāt piṣṭhena narakuṃjarān || 9-387 ||

anyat sarvaṃ yathā pūrvaṃ kalpayet sādhakottamaḥ |

athavānya prakāreṇa puraścaraṇamiṣyate || 9-388 ||


grahaṇe kasya candorveti nadyāṃ samudragāminyāmityādi pūrvoktam ||


dviguṇaṃ vā japen mantraṃ śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret |

tadante mantrasiddhārthaṃ guruṃ saṃpūjya pūjayet || 9-389 ||

tataḥśca mantrasiddhiḥ syād devatā ca prasīdati |

athavā devatā rūpaṃ guruṃ bhaktyā pradoṣayet || 9-390 ||

110a) puraścaraṇahītopi mantrasiddho na saṃśayaḥ |

atha prayogaṃ vakṣyāmi puraścaraṇakarmaṇaḥ || 9-391 ||


purvadine nirāmiṣamekavāmaṃ bhitkā (?)paradine nadyādau gatvā snānādikaṃ 
kṛtvā jalādutthāya dhaute vāsasīparidhāyācamya śrī-amukadevatāyā 
yadakṣarasya mantrasya mantrasya puraścaraṇasiddhaye ||


maye yaṃ gṛhyate bhūmirmantroyaṃ siddhyatāmiti |


nadīparvatādau yathecchayā grāmānnagarācca krośamitaṃ krośayugamitaṃ vā 
dūraṃ gatvā puraścaraṇayogyasthalaṃ gṛhītvā 
kṣīravṛkṣodbhavānvitastiparimitā naṣṭā kīlakān gṛhitvāstramantreṇa 
pratyekamekādaśavāraṃ ṣaḍvāraṃ vābhimantrya teṣu cāstraṃ saṃpūjya 
gṛhītakṣettramadhye kṣetrapālaṃ saṃpūjyatasmaimāṣabhaktavaliṃ dadyāt ||

yathā kṣetrapālasya mantrasya brāhma-ṛṣiḥ gāyatrīcchaṃdaḥ kṣetrapālo 
devatā puraścaraṇarakṣārthe viniyogaḥ ||

iti karāṅganyāsau kṛtvā dhāraṇaktyādipiṭhāt saṃpujya vāmā jyeṣṭhā raudrī 
kālī kalavīkaraṇī valapramathanī sarvabhūtadamanī manonmanī pīṭhaśaktiḥ 
pūrvādike sare ca madhye ca saṃpūjya ||

oṃ namo bhagavate sakalaguṇātmaśaktiyuktā yānaṃ tāya yogapīṭhātmane nama 
iti pīṭhasaṃpūjyadhyāyet ||

yathā ||


nīlāṃjanādrinibhamūrdhvapi śaṃgakeśaṃ 

vṛttogralocanamupā tu kapālaśūlam ||

āśāṃvaraṃ tuṃgabhūṣaṇamudragradaṃṣṭraṃ 

kṣetreṇa madbhutatanuṃ praṇamāmi devam || 9-392 ||


110b) iti dhyātvā ||

oṃ kṣī/ kṣa/ttrapālāya nama iti gadhādibhiḥ pūjayet ||

kesareṣvaṃgāni pūjayet ||

patreṣu anilāgnikeśe kalālaghaṃṭā ravamahākopapiśitā 
śanapiṃgalākṣordhvakeśā narcayet ||

indrādīn tadvahisteṣāmastrāṇi pūjayet ||

tato gaṃdhādibhiḥ saṃpūjya māṣabhaktavaliḥ saparivārāya trapālāyanama iti 
sopadaṃśaṃ vṛhatpintraṃ māṣabhaktavaliṃ tat karakapāle dadyāt ||

tatoṣṭaddikṣuvaliṃ dadyād anena ||


ye raudrā raudrakarmāṇo raudrasthāna nivāsinaḥ |

mātaropyagrarūpāśca gaṇānāmadhipāśca ye || 9-393 ||

vighnabhūtāstathā cānye digvidikṣusamāśritāḥ |


sarve te prītamānasaḥ prītyā gṛhnantvimaṃ valimityaṣṭadikṣuvaliṃ dadyāt ||

tataḥ parigṛhītakṣetrasya pūrvasīmāṃ gatvā pūvādikrameṇāṣṭadikṣu kīlakā 
nāropayedanaina ||


ye cātra vighnakarttāro divibhuvyantarikṣagāḥ || 9-394 ||

vighnabhūtaśca ye cānye mama mantrasya siddhiṣu |

mayai tat kīlitaṃ kṣatraṃ parityajya vidūrataḥ || 9-395 ||

arpasarpaṃ tu te sarve nivighnaṃ siddhirastu me |


iti paṭhan aṣṭadikṣukīlakānāropayet |

tatasteṣu kīlakeṣu pūrvādikrameṇeṃ drādīnāvājyatannāmamantraireva 
saṃpūjyaiṣamāṣabhaktavaliṃ riṃdrāya nama iti pūrvādikrameṇa 
māṣabhaktavaliṃ dadyāt ||

puvalaścaraṇa pūrvatṛtīyadivase haviṣyānnamekavāraṃ bhuktyā grimadivase 
prātaḥ snātvā mantrasiddhi prativaṃdhakajñatājñata 

111a) kapāpakṣayārthamayutādigāyatrī japaprāyaścittaṃ vidhāya bhojanā 
cchādanādibhi brāhmaṇān santoṣya vahuvastrālaṃkārādibhi guruṃ saṃpūjya 
suprītaṃ kṛtvā tadanujñāṃ gṛhītvā tad dine haviṣyāśīrotrau 
kuśaśayyāyāṃ śayanaṃ kuryāt ||

tato gurorasaṃnidhāne manasaivagurorājñāṃ gṛhnīyāt ||

tato brāhme mūhūrtte utthāya prātaḥ kṛtyaṃ kṛtvā snātvā nitya kṛtyaṃ 
samāpyakuṭīdvāramāgatyācamya dikpatīnnatvā 
prāṅmukhastilakuśajalānyādāya oṃ adyatyādi amukagotraḥ śrī-
amukaśarmāmukadevatāya yadakṣaramantrasya 
siddhikāmodyārabhyāmukamantrasya yat saṃkhyā japa tad daśāṃśa tan 
mantraṃ karaṇakahomaddaśāṃśa tan mantrakaraṇatarpaṇa tarpaṇadaśāṃśaka 
tan mantrakaraṇakābhiṣeka daśāṃśa brāhmanabhojana rūpapuraścaraṇamahaṃ 
kariṣye ||

keṣāṃcin mate japadaśāśe naiva homatarpaṇamārjanāni devatā 
citanamadhikaṃ brāhmanabhojana sthāne iti viśeṣaḥ ||

yatra homodi saṃkhyā viseṣyoktā tathaiva homādikamiti yatra 
viśiṣyasaṃkhyānoktā tatrāṣṭasahasrāṇi saṃkhyā syāt ||

tathacoktaṃ vārāhītantre ||



yeṣāṃ jape ca home ca saṃkhyā noktā manīṣibhiḥ || 9-396 ||

teṣāṃ maṣṭasahasrāṇi saṃkhyāsyājjapahomayoḥ |


sahasramaṣṭau juhuyāllakṣayāpye prakīrtayed ityuttaradale pāṭhaḥ ||

111b) taduktaṃ pūrṇāpaddhatau ||


saṃkhyānuktau śataṃ sāṣṭaṃ sahasraṃ vā japādiṣu || 9-397 ||


kṛtāṃjaliḥ ||

amukamatrasyāmuka-ṛṣiḥ amukaḥ cchaṃdaḥ amukadevatā amukavījaṃ 
amukāśaktiḥ puraścaraṇa jape viniyogaḥ ||

iti kuśākṣatajale saṃkalpānantaraṃ kuryāt ||

tataḥ kuṭīnikaṭe sāmānyārghaṃ kṛtvā dvāradevatāṃ saṃpūjya gṛhaṃ 
praviśyārghya jalena gṛhaṃ prokṣya raktakaṃvalāsanamāstīrya 
taduparyupaviśya śivasya saṃnidhānaṃ agnerviṣṇurvā sūryasya vā gurorvā 
pradīpasya saṃnidhānaṃ prakalpya pīṭhasyāgnikoṇeṣu 
pūrṇakuṃbhacatuṣṭayaṃ sthāpayitvā teṣu krameṇa gaṇeśasarasvatī durgā 
kṣetrapālān saṃpūjya yathoktaprakāreṇeṣṭadevatāṃ saṃpūjya homāntaṃ 
karmasamāpya valiṃ dadyāt ||

tadyathā ye raudrā raudrakarmāṇa iti paṭhitvāṣṭadikṣuvaliṃ dattvā bhūtavaliṃ 
dadyāt ||

yathā ||

bhūtāni yānī havasanti bhūtale valiṃ gṛhītvā vidhivat prayuktam ||
santoṣamāsādya vrajantu sarve kṣamantu tānyatra namostutebhyaḥ ||

tataḥ pūjāṃ samāpya puṣpamāghrāya hṛtyaṃ kaje devatāṃ 
mānasairupacārairārādhya karāṅganyāsau kṛtvā prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā 
yathā śakti japet ||

mantrasya śrotramukhahṛdayanetrāṇi jñātvā japet ||

taduktaṃ viśuddhe śvaratantre ||


mantrasya praṇavaḥ śrotraṃ mukhaṃ rephaḥ prakīrttitam |

svarāśca cakṣuṣī jñeyā mantravidbhiranantaram || 9-398 ||

atiriktāśca ye varṇā hṛdayaṃ parikīrttitam |


japasthānamāha ||

puṇyakṣetraṃ nadītīraṃ guhā parvatamastakam || 9-399 ||

tīrthapradeśāḥ sindhunāṃ saṃgamaḥ pavanaṃ vanam |

udyānāniviviktāni vilvamūlaṃ taṭaṃ gireḥ || 9-400 ||

tulasī kānanaṃ goṣṭhaṃ vṛṣaśūnyaḥ śivālayeḥ |

aśvatthāmalakīmūlaṃ gośālājalamadhyataḥ || 9-401 ||

devatāyatanaṃ kulaṃ samudrasya nijaṃ gṛham |

113a) śrīvījaṃ cāpi nikṣipya japen mantraṃ tu siddhaye |


hṛllekhā hrī/ kāmavījaṃ klī/ ||

śrīvījaṃ śrī ||

hrī/ klī/ śrī/ iti vījatrayaṃ mantrādau dattvā japet ||


tāraṃ saṃpuṭitaṃ vātha duṣṭamantropi siddhati || 9-403 ||


tāraḥ praṇavaḥ iti saṃhitāyām ||

athārimantratyāgaḥ ||


śuciḥ samāhito bhūtvā jyārabhet tu śubhe dine |

aśeṣaduḥkhanāśāya daiśikaḥ pravarovidhim || 9-404 ||

tavrādau bhavane kuṃbhaṃ sthāpya dīkṣāvidhikramāt |

maṃḍale sthāpayed vidvān purayet taṃ śubhairjalaiḥ || 9-405 ||

vilome mantrapāṭhena tatrāvāhya tu devatām |

sakalīkṛtya saṃhṛtya cāṣṭāṅgāvaraṇaṃ yajet || 9-406 ||

evaṃ sāvaraṇānniṣṭhā mantrīmantrasya devatām |

hutvā vilomamantreṇa sarpiṣā gorapi dvijaḥ || 9-407 ||

tato yathāvad undhā?nnairdevatābhyo valiṃ haret |

vidikṣudikṣu ca tathā vakṣyamāṇairmanukramaiḥ || 9-408 ||

āyāhīndrasurādhīśa śatamanyośacīpate |

namastubhyaṃ gṛhāṇe maṃ puṣpadhupādikaṃ valim || 9-409 ||

āyā hi tejasāṃ nātha havyavāhavara prade |

gṛhāṇa puṣpadhūpādi valimenaṃ supūjitam || 9-410 ||

aitarājasamā yā hi bhinnāṃjanasamadyute |

valiṃ dattaṃ gṛhāṇe maṃsuprīto varadā bhava || 9-411 ||

namaste rakṣasāṃ nātha nirṛte tvamiha gataḥ |

gṛhāṇavalipūjādi mayā bhaktyā niveditam || 9-412 ||

ehi paścimadikpāla jalanāthanamostu te |

bhaktyā niveditā pūjāṃ gṛhītvā prītimāpnu hi || 9-413 ||

prabhaṃjana prāṇapate tvamehi saparicchadaḥ |

mayā prajuṣṭaṃ vidhivadguhāṇavalimādarāt || 9-414 ||

kuveratārakādhīśau āgacchattāṃ surottamau |

puṣpadhūpādibhiḥ prītau bhavetāṃ varadaumamaḥ || 9-415 ||

īśatvameva bhagavan sarvavidyāśraya prabho |

pūjitaḥ puṣadhūpādyaiḥ prīto bhavati bhūtaye || 9-416 ||

āyā hi sarvabhūtānāṃ nāthabrahman samārcanam |

gṛhāṇa sarvavighnān me nivarttayanamostu te || 9-417 ||

āgacchavaradā bhyakta viṣṇo viśvasya nāyakaḥ |

pujitaḥ parayā bhaktyā bhavatvaṃ sukhado mama || 9-418 ||

tataḥ saparivārāṃśca pūjayen mantradevatām |

mantreṇa viparītena puṣpadīpopacārakaiḥ || 9-419 ||

tatastu prārthayed vidvān pūjitāṃ mantradevatām |

113b) anukulaṃ samālocya mayā ca valavuddhinā || 9-420 ||

yadupātraṃ pujitaṃ ca prābho mantrasvarūpakaḥ |

tena me manasaḥ kṣobhamaśeṣaṃ vinivarttaya || 9-421 ||

pāpaṃ pratihataṃ bhūyād bhūyaḥ śreyaḥ sanātana |

tato nu mama kalyāṇaṃ pārakī bhaktireva te || 9-422 ||

iti saṃprārthya mantreśaṃ pattre mantraṃ vilomataḥ |

likhitvāmalakarppura caṃdanena samarcayet || 9-423 ||

tat patraṃ matimān paścād vaddhā nijaśirasyatha || 9-424 ||

śrīrāmāya namaḥ 

mantroddhāraṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvatantrānusārataḥ |

śrīraghunāthamantraṃ nirupayāmi ||

tathā coktaṃ vasiṣṭaṃ saṃhitāyām |


vināyakaṃ raghuśreṣṭhaṃ vande vyākhyālasatkaram |

vasiṣṭavāmadevādi ṛṣibhiḥ parivāritam || 10-2 ||

kṛṣṇadvaipāyanaṃ vyāsaṃ guruṃ satyavatīsutam |

114a) sacidānandamātmānaṃ jñātavān brahmaśāśvatam || 10-6 ||

indrānīṃ śrotumicchāmi mantraṃ sāṅgaṃ sahārcanam |

rāmātmanāvatīrṇasya viṣṇoramitatejasaḥ || 10-7 ||

vruhi me bhugavan sarve śrotuṃ yogotmyahaṃ yadi |

asaubhāgyena loko yaṃ dāridryeṇa ca pīḍitaḥ || 10-8 ||

yena saubhāgyamatulaṃ mahadaiśvaryasaṃbhavam |

śāstrajñānaṃ kavitvaṃ ca sarvajñatvaṃ labhennaraḥ || 10-9 ||

śrīvyāsa uvāca 

sādhuvṛṣṭaṃ tvayā brahman kathaye sakalaṃ tava |

brahmaṇā kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ vasiṣṭāya mahātmane || 10-10 ||

prasaṃnahṛdayaṃ śuddhaḥ pitā me karuṇānidhiḥ || 10-11 ||


kurukṣtre mahāpuṇye parvaṇi dattavān ||


mayā ca sādhito mantro japahomārcanādibhiḥ |

dutaḥ pūrvomayā nokto mantrarājastu kasyacit || 10-12 ||

evaṃ paraṃ parāproktaṃ bhaktyā mantraṃ śṛṇuṣva me |

ayogyāyanadātavyo mantrarājastvayā mune || 10-13 ||

yatnena gopayatvaṃ ca rahasyaṃ śrīprasiddhidam |

alasaṃ malinaṃ kliṣṭaṃ daṃbhalobhasamanvitam || 10-14 ||

daridraṃ rogiṇaṃ kruddhaṃ rāginaṃ bhogalālasam |

asuyāmatsaragrastaṃ śaṭhaṃ paruṣavādinam || 10-15 ||

anyāyopārjitadhanaṃ paradārarataṃ sadā |

viduṣāṃ vairiṇaṃ nityamajñaṃ paṃḍitamāninam || 10-16 ||

bhraṣṭavrataṃ kavṛttiṃ piśunaṃ duṣṭamānasam |

vahvāśanaṃ kruraceṣṭamagragaṇyaṃ durātmanam || 10-17 ||

etairdoṣairvihīnataṃ śiṣyatvena prakalpayet |

gṛhaṇīyādyadi tad doṣaṃ prāyogurumapi spṛśet || 10-18 ||

amātyadoṣo rājānaṃ jāyā doṣāpatiṃ yathā |

tathā śiṣya kṛto doṣo guruṃ prāpnoti niścitam || 10-19 ||

tasmācchiṣyaṃ gururnityaṃ parīkṣya tu parigrahet |

kāyena manasā vācā guruśuśruṣaṇeratam || 10-20 ||

asteyavṛttimāstikyaṃ sadā mokṣakṛtodyamam |

brahmacaryarataṃ nityaṃ satyavratamakalmaṣa || 10-21 ||

prasaṃnahṛdayaṃ śuddhamaśaṭhaṃ vilāśayam |

paropakāranirabhaṃ parārthe vigataspṛham || 10-22 ||

sucittavittadehaiśca paritoṣakaraṃ guroḥ |

idṛgvidhāya śiṣyāya mantraṃ dadyāt tu nānyathā || 10-23 ||

yadanyathā vadet tasmin devatā śāpadā bhavet |

mantroddhāramāha ||

115a) brahmādhvanā nayedūrdhve dvādaśāntendumaṇḍalam || 10-38 ||

115b) tatrokte vaiṣṇave pīṭhe kuryādārādhanaṃ vahiḥ |


taduktaṃ gautamīye ||

upādānaṃ nāmagaṃdhapuṣpādi cayanaṃ tathā |

svādhyāyo nāmamantrātmā saṃdhyāna pūjakojapaḥ |

suktaḥ stotrādipāṭhaśca hareḥ saṃkīnaṃ tathā || 10-61 ||

iti paṃcaprakārāśca kathitāstavasuvrate |

athocyate mahāyantro raghunandanavigraham |

saubhāgyadaṃ vaśyakaraṃ ripunāśakaraṃ param || 10-64 ||

āyurārogyadaṃ puṃsāṃ grahajvaranivāraṇam |

yantraṃ mantramayaṃ proktaṃ mantrātmā devateti ca || 10-65 ||

purābhagavatā proktaṃ tantre sammohanāhvaye |

dehātmano yathā bhedo mantradaivatayostathā || 10-66 ||

tasmāt pīṭhe mahāmantraṃ likhitvā tatra pūjayet |
