tato vakṣyāmi yantrasya rūpaṃ svagurudarśitam |

ṣaṭkoṇaṃ pūrvamālikhya tadagre vṛttamālikhet || 10-68 ||

vahiraṣṭadalaṃ padmamadhaḥ pārthivamaṇḍalam |

evaṃ maṇḍalamālikhya śobhanaṃ vyaktamujjvalam || 10-69 ||

asminyantra mahāmantro likhitvā tatra pūjayet |

ṣaṭkoṇāntarlikhed vījaṃ sādhyākhyā karmagarbhitam || 10-70 ||

manmathe naiva vījena tadvījaṃ veṣṭayed vahi |

aviśiṣṭairmanovarṇai manmathaṃ tacca veṣṭayet || 10-71 ||

daśākṣareṇa mantreṇa tat sarvaṃ veṣṭayet punaḥ |

agnyādikoṇaṣaṭkeṣu ṣaḍaṃgāni ca vinyaset || 10-72 ||

 oṃ hrī/ śrī/ iti likhen paścāt ṣaṭkoṇasya kapolayoḥ |

koṇāgreṣu ca ī-vījaṃ kesareṣu svarāt kramāt || 10-73 ||

tato mālā manovarṇaiḥ pūrvādyaṣṭadaleṣu ca |

teṣu saptasumantrajño vinyaset tarkasaṃkhyayā || 10-74 ||

avaśiṣṭān paṃcavarṇān vilikhedaṣṭame dale |

116a) vṛttākāreṇa tat sarvaṃ veṣṭayet kādikṣāntagaiḥ || 10-75 ||

vījadvayaṃ likhet paścād bhūgṛhe digvidikṣvapi |

nārasiṃhaṃ mahāvījaṃ vārāhaṃ ca mune kramāt || 10-76 ||

asmin yantre samāvāpya pūjaye ighunaṃdanam |

prasaṃnavadanaṃ śāntamaihikāmuṣmikapradam || 10-77 ||

prapaṃcamaṃ vilomena samuddhṛtya carāntakam |

śāntiviṃdusamāyuktaṃ kāmavījamudāhṛtam || 10-78 ||

praṇavaṃ pūrvamuccārya jānakīti padaṃ tataḥ |

punaḥ savallabhāyeti svāhāntaṃ mantramuddharet || 10-79 ||

daśākṣaroyaṃ mantraḥ syāt sarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ |

oṃ namo padamābhāṣya tato bhagavate padam || 10-80 ||

raghunaṃdanāyeti padamābhāṣya mantravittamaḥ |

rakṣoghnaviṣadāyeti caturthapadamuddharet || 10-81 ||

madhuraprasaṃnavadanā * yāmitatejase |

valāyeti padaṃ paścād rāmāyeti vadet tathā || 10-82 ||

viṣṇave padamābhāṣya namo taṃ mantramuddharet |

mālāmantramano proktaḥ saptacatvāriśāṃkṣaraiḥ || 10-83 ||

kṣakārarephajaikāro viṃdubhibhūṣito mune |

nārasiṃho mahāmantro japatāṃ kalpabhuruha || 10-84 ||

paṃcamasvarasaṃyukto hakāro viṃdubhūṣitaḥ |

ṣaṣṭasvarasamāyukto hakāroviṃdubhūṣitaḥ || 10-85 ||

vārāhaṃ vījamityuktaṃ lakṣmīdhanadharāpradam |

bhugṛhaṃ caturaśraṃ syād aṣṭavajravibhūṣitam || 10-86 ||

etadyantraṃ samālikhya sauvarṇe rājate paṭe |

bhurjje vā samyagālikhya gulakī kṛtya dhārayet || 10-87 ||

aputro labhate putraṃ nirdhanodhanamāpnuyāt |

kimatra vahunoktena sarvasiddhipradaṃ nṛṇām || 10-88 ||

yantrametan mahāpuṇyaṃ dhāraṇāt pātakāpaham |

asmin yantra samāvāhya rāmamārādhayet kramāt || 10-89 ||

maṃḍukādi pṛthivyātaṃ karṇikāyāṃ yajet kramāt |

agnyādi pīṭhapādeṣu dharmāṃdīścaturo yajet || 10-90 ||

pūrvādi dikṣutāt sarvānañca pūrvān kramād yajet |

karṇikāyāṃ tathānantaṃ padmaṃ saṃpūjayet tataḥ || 10-91 ||

tāravarṇaprabhinnāḍhya arkāgnīndutrimaṇḍalam |

guṇatrayaṃ ca satvādi tataścātmacatuṣṭayam || 10-92 ||

ātmātaṃrātmāparamāt saṃjñānāt mātmanā ca te |

116b) vimalotkarṣiṇī jñānā kriyāyogā ca paṃcamī || 10-93 ||

prahvī satyā tatheśānā nugrahānavamī parā |

pūrvādyaṣṭasupatreṣu karṇikāyā nyaset kramāt || 10-94 ||

caturthyānamasāyuktaiḥ svaiḥ svaireva ca nāmabhiḥ |

karṇikāyāṃ yajet paścāt pīṭhamantreṇamantravit || 10-95 ||

praṇavaṃ pūrvamurcārya namaḥ śabdaṃ tato vadet |

bhagavate padamābhāṣya viṣṇave iti padaṃ vadet || 10-96 ||

sarvabhūtātmane ceti vāsudevāya ityapi |

tataḥ sarvātmasaṃyoga yogapīṭhātmane namaḥ || 10-97 ||

iti mantreṇa cenmadhye kuryāt puṣpāṃjaliṃ punaḥ |

evaṃ saṃpūjyate pīṭhe devamāvāhya pūjayet || 10-98 ||

arghādi dīpadhūpāntaṃ upacāraṃ vidhāya ca |

tatonujñāpya deveśaṃ parivārāṃśca pūjayet || 10-99 ||

pūrvaṣaṭkoṇamadhyeṣu hṛdayādīni ṣaṭkramāt |

vāmabhāge samāsīnāṃ sītāṃ tan manunāyajet || 10-100 ||

śrīvījaṃ pūrvamuccārya sītā yai padamuddharet |

vahni jāyānvito mantro bhajatāṃ kalpabhūruhaḥ || 10-101 ||

janakosya ṛṣiḥ prokta gāyatrī cchaṃda ucyate |

sītākhyā yā jagaddhātrī devatā śrījaganmayī || 10-102 ||

āṅge devasya purato vāmadakṣiṇataḥ śarān |

tataścāṣṭadale padme hanumantaṃ ca vācakam || 10-103 ||

devasyāgre samāsīnaṃ paṃḍitaṃ dhṛtapustakam |

sugrīvamagniṃ dik patre marutaṃ dakṣiṇe dale || 10-104 ||

vibhīṣaṇaṃ ca naiṛtye lakṣmaṇaṃ vāruṇe dale |

agadaṃ vāyudikpatre śatrughnaṃ cottare dale || 10-105 ||

īśāne jāṃvavantaṃ ca dvitīyā varaṇe yajet |

praṇavādinamontai snānarcayet sva svanāmabhiḥ || 10-106 ||

sṛṣṭirjayantavijayaḥ surāṣṭrorāṣṭravardhanaḥ |

akopodharmapālaśca sumantraścāṣṭamo mataḥ || 10-107 ||

ityaṣṭau mantriṇaḥ pūjyastṛtīyāvaraṇe yajet |

daśadikṣudiśāṃ pālānanantāntānyajet kramāt || 10-108 ||


indrādi anantāndaśa ||


vasiṣṭavāmadevādi munibhiśca mahātmabhiḥ |

nalanīlādibhiścānyai divyairvā naravigrahaiḥ || 10-109 ||

parīvāraiḥ samāsīnaṃ sva svanāmaprakāśakam |

saṃsbhūyamānaṃ deveśaṃ ṛṣibhirnāradādibhiḥ || 10-110 ||

evaṃ saparivārāntamārādhya raghunaṃdanam |

117a) gaṃdhapuṣpādibhirdivyairdhūpairdīpaiśca sākṣataiḥ || 10-111 ||

saṃpūjya bhaktyā vidhivan naivedyaṃ parikalpayet |

rājopacārānkhilān darśayitvā mahāmuniḥ || 10-112 ||

stutvā yathāvat praṇamedbhaktiyuktaśca sādhakaḥ |

tataḥ samuddhared devaṃ parivāraiḥ samaṃ hṛdi || 10-113 ||

ātmadaivatayoraikyaṃ saṃbhāvyamunisattama |

abhyarcya gaṃdhapuṣpādyaistataḥ pūjāṃ samāpayet || 10-114 ||

evaṃ pratidinaṃ kuvan kṛta kṛtyo bhaveddhruvam |

ātmānaṃ satataṃ rāmaṃ saṃbhāvya vicaret sudhīḥ || 10-115 ||

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi pauraścaraṇikaṃ vidhim |

bhikṣāśivāhaviṣyo śrī śākayāvakabhojanaḥ || 10-116 ||

paścimābhimukhe deve bhṛśaśūnye śivālaye |

nadyāḥ samudragāminyāstīre goṣṭhegavāṃmune || 10-117 ||

aśvatthavilvayormule sindhutīre jalāśaye |

parvatāgrethavā mantrī sthāne cāṣṭatame japet || 10-118 ||

tarkalakṣaṃ japen mantraṃ tarpayecca daśāṃśataḥ |


tarkalakṣaṃ ṣaṭlakṣam ||


vittveḥ phalaiḥ prasunaiśca patraistrimadhusaṃyutaiḥ || 10-119 ||

kuryād daśāṃśato homaṃ tataḥ siddho bhaviṣyati |

evaṃ mantrādibhiḥ siddhe manaukarmāṇi sādhayet || 10-120 ||

jātī prasunairjuhuyāccaṃdanāṃbhaḥ samanvitaiḥ |


rājavaśyāyakamalairdhānyairdhānyādindirāvāptaye naraḥ ||

indirālakṣmīḥ ||


durvā homena dīrghāyurbhaven mantrīnirāmayaḥ || 10-121 ||

raktotpalahutānmantrī dhanamāpnoti vāṃcchitam |

medhā kāmena hotavyaṃ palāśakusumainaraiḥ || 10-122 ||

tajjaptamaṃbhaḥ prapivet kavirbhavati vatsarāt |

tanmantryamannaṃ bhuṃjītamahadārogyamāpnuyāt || 10-123 ||

madhutritayake naiva juhuyāt pāyasena vā |

kurvīrasadṛśo dṛkṣyā vidyayādhijanāyate || 10-124 ||

yastvevaṃ bhajate bhaktyā japahomārcanādibhiḥ |

sāntayā sahitaṃ devaṃ rāghavaṃ lakṣmaṇānvitam |

sabhuktyā sakalān bhogānante tat padamāpnuyāt || 10-125 ||


alaṃvivādanamamānuṣoyaṃ rāmātmanārākṣasarājahantā |

bhujaṃgaśayyāṃ parimucyasākṣān nārāyaṇo bhūtalamājagām || 10-126 ||



iti śrīrāmaṣaḍakṣarakalpaḥ ||

atha yantroddhāraḥ ||

117b) caturaśraveṣṭitasyāṣṭadalakamalakarṇikāmadhye ṣaṭkoṇam |

tanmadhye rā iti vījam ||

madhye mamāmukaṃ kuru śrīyamiti ||

sādhyaṃ likhitvā sasādhyaṃ vījaṃ klīmiti |

manmathavījena veṣṭayitvā ṣaṭkoṇeṣu ||

rā/ rī/ ru/ rai/ rau/ raḥ ||

iti ṣaḍaṅgāni likhitvā ||

hrī/ iti koṇakapolayāḥ ||

hu/ iti vārāhavījaṃ koṇāgreṣu kesareṣu akārodiṣoḍaśasvarān dvaṃdvaśo 
likhet ||

tataḥ ||

oṃ namo bhagavate raghunaṃdanāya rakṣoghna viṣadāya madhuraprasaṃ navadanā 
yā amitatejase valāya rāmāya viṣṇave namaḥ ||

iti saptacatvāriṃśadvarṇānāṃ varṇaṣaṭkamekaikasmin daleṣṭamapattre 
paṃcavarṇakaṃ likhitvā padmādvahiḥ ||

oṃ jānakīvallabhāya svāheti daśākṣarairveṣṭayitvakādikṣāntairveṣṭayet ||

caturaśrasyātaḥ prāgādidikṣukṣaumiti narasiṃhavījamāgnīyādikoṇeṣu huṃ iti 
||

varāhavījaṃ bhūgṛhasyāṣṭadikṣu īṃ vajrāṣṭakaṃ ca likhet ||

iti śrīraghunāthasya dhāraṇayantrametat |

tathā ca śāradātilake ||


anantodhyāsanaḥ sendurvījaṃ rāmāya hṛnmanuḥ |

ṣaḍakṣaro yamādiṣṭo bhajatāṃ kāmadomaśiḥ || 10-127 ||


asyārthaḥ ||

anantaḥ akāraḥ agniḥ rephaḥ tathā cākārayukto rephaḥ induyuktaḥ 
induranusvāraḥ tadyuktaḥ rā iti bhavati tathā carāvījamuddhṛtya rāṃ rāmāya 
namaḥ ||

ityuddharet ||

tathā ca ||

rāṃ rāmāya nāma iti ṣaḍakṣaraṃ matro bhavati ||

asya ṣaḍakṣaramantrasya brahmā ṛṣirgāyatrīcchaṃdaḥ śrīrāmacandro devatā 
dhanādyabhīṣṭasiddhau viniyogaḥ ||

rāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ||

rī/ śirase svāhā ||

rū/ śikhāyai vaukhaṭ |

rai/ kavacāya hū/ |

rau/ netrābhyāṃ vauṣaṭ |

raḥ astrāya phaṭ |

iti ṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ ||

karāṅganyāsānantaraṃ mantravarṇanyāsaḥ ||

brahmaraṃdhre rām |

bhrumadhye rā || hadi mā || nabhau ya || liṃge na || pādayoḥ maḥ ||

vimalotkarṣiṇī jñānā kriyāyogā prasvī satyā ||

īśānā anugrahā etāḥ pītaśaktayaḥ ||

118a) oṃ namo bhagavate viṣṇave sarvabhūtātmane vāsudevāya 
sarvātmasaṃyogapadmapīṭhātmane namaḥ ||

iti pīṭhamantraḥ ||


oṃ kālāṃ bhodharakāntikāntamaniśaṃ vīrāsanādhyāsinaṃ 

mudrāṃ jñānamayīṃ dadhānamaparaṃ hastāvujaṃ jānuni |

sītāṃ pārśvagatāṃ saroruhakarāṃ vidyunnibhāṃ rāghavaṃ 

paśyantī mukuṭāṅgadādivividhā kalpojjvalāṅgaṃ bhaje || 10-128 ||


iti dhyānam ||

āvaraṇapūjāyāṃ pārśve ||

śrīṃ sītāyai svāhā |

iti matreṇa sītām ||

agre vāmadakṣiṇapārśve yoḥ śārdgaṃ rāṃśca tad vahiḥ 
kesareṣṭāgneyādikoṇeṣu hṛdayaśiraḥ śikhākavacāni |

agrenetraṃ dikṣu astraṃ devasyāgradalamārabhya prādakṣiṇyena daleṣu |

hanumat sugrīvabharatavibhīṣaṇalakṣmaṇāṅgadaśatrughnaṃ jāṃvavataḥ ||

agrādidalāgreṣu sṛṣṭijayantavijayasurāṣṭrarāṣṭravardhana 
akopadharmapālasumantrān | tadvahirlokapālān ||

tadvahistad astrāṇi ca pūjayet ||

atra hanumadādi dhyānam ||


oṃ vācayantaṃ hanumantaṃ agrato dhṛta pustakam |

yajed bharataśatrughnau pārśvaṃ yorddhṛta cāmarau || 10-129 ||

dhṛtāta patraṃ hastābhyāṃ lakṣmaṇaṃ paścime yajet |

varṇalakṣaṃ japen mantraṃ daśāṃśaṃ kamalaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 10-130 ||

juhuyād arcite vahnau brahmaṇānapi bhojayet |


iti śrīrāmacandra pūjāpaddhatiḥ samāptaḥ ||

śrīrāmāya namaḥ ||


śrīpārvatyuvāca 

oṃ bhagavan sarvadeveśa deva deva namostu te || 10-131 ||

sarvaṃ me kathitaṃ deva rāmamantraviśeṣataḥ |

trailokyamohanaṃ nāma kavacaṃ pūrvasūcitam || 10-132 ||

kathayasva mahādeva yadguhyaṃ tava vallabhaḥ |

īśvara uvāca 

śṛṇu vakṣyāmi deveśi kavacaṃ paramādbhutam || 10-133 ||

asyaṃ tagopanaṃ guhyaṃ brahmamantrodyavigraham |


oṃ asya śrīrāmakavacasya brahmā ṛṣiḥ gāyatrī cchaṃdaḥ śrīrāmacandro 
devatā mama caturvargasādhane jape viniyogaḥ ||


oṃ praṇavo me śiraḥ pātu tārakabrahmarūpakaḥ || 10-134 ||

ananto gyā?sanaḥ sendurnāmāmekākṣaro va tu |


oṃ rāṃ rāmāya nama iti ṣaḍarṇo bhuktimuktidaḥ |


118b) bhālaṃ pāyānnetrayugmaṃ rāmodvyakṣarasaṃjñakaḥ || 10-135 ||

klī/ pāyācchrotrayugmaṃ ca ekākṣaraśca mohanaḥ |

klī/ rāmāya nama iti mukhaṃ me parirakṣa tu || 10-136 ||

oṃ rāṃ rāmacandrāya svāhā aṣṭārṇo me bhujadvayam |

śrī/ rāmāya nama iti skaṃdhau yā tu kharāntakaḥ || 10-137 ||


śrī/ rāmacandrāya rakṣoghnāya nama iti dvādaśākṣaraḥ |


hṛdayaṃ me sadā pātu raghuvaṃśasamudbhavaḥ |


hrī/ rāmacandrāya nama iti pātu ūruyugaṃ mamaḥ |


śrīrāmabhadrāya svāhā nābhimaṣṭākṣarau va tu || 10-138 ||

hlī/ rāmabhadrāya sītāpataye ca dviṭhāntakaḥ |

saktinī satataṃ pātu rākṣasendravidārakaḥ || 10-139 ||

praṇavo me rāmabhadrāya hṛdantoṣṭākṣaro manuḥ |

jānuno me sadā pātu rakṣaḥ kulavināśakaḥ || 10-140 ||

praṇavo rāmabhadrāya dviṭhāntoṣṭhākṣaro manuḥ |

jānuno me sadā pātu rakṣaḥ kulavināśakaḥ || 10-141 ||

praṇavo rāmabhadrāya dviṭhātoṣṭākṣaro manuḥ |

pādayugmaṃ sadā pātu va narendraśriyāḥ pradaḥ || 10-142 ||

hrī/ sītāpataye nama iti prācyā māṃ sarvadā va tu |

hu/ jānakīvallabhāyeti pātu yāmyāṃ sadā prabhuḥ || 10-143 ||

klī/ jānakīnāthāyeti pratīcyāṃ māṃ sadā va tu |

śrī/ raghunāthāya nama iti kauveryādiśirakṣa tu || 10-144 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate vruyāccaturthyo raghunandanam |

rakṣoghnaṃ viṣadāyāntaṃ madhurādisamīrayet || 10-145 ||

prasaṃnavadanāyeti paścād amitatejase |

valāyapaścād rāmāya viṣṇave tadanantaram || 10-146 ||

praṇavādinamontoyaṃ mālāmantra udīritaḥ |

rājasthāne sadā pātu raghuvaṃśasamudbhavaḥ || 10-147 ||

iti te kathitaṃ devi sarvamantroghavigraham |

trailokyamaṃgalaṃ nāma kavacaṃ brahmarūpakam || 10-148 ||

prātaḥkāle paṭhedyastu sobhīṣṭaṃ phalamāpnuyāt |

pūjākāle paṭhedyastu kavacaṃ sādhakottamaḥ || 10-149 ||

kīrttiśrīkāntimedhāyurvṛṃhito bhavati dhruvam |

rāmamantramayaṃ brahma kavacaṃ sanmukhoditam || 10-150 ||

gurumabhyarcya vidhivat kavacaṃ hi paṭhet tataḥ |

dviḥ sakṛd vā yathā nyāyaṃ sopi puṇyavatāṃ varaḥ || 10-151 ||

devamabhyarcya vidhivat puraścaryā samācaret |

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā daśāṃśahavanādikam || 10-152 ||

tatastu siddhakavacaḥ sarvakāryātisādhayet |

119a) mantrasiddhirbhavet tasya puraścaryāṃ vidhānataḥ || 10-153 ||

gadyapadyamayī vāṇī vaktre yasya pravarttate |

vaktre tasya vased vāṇī kamalā niścalā gṛhe || 10-154 ||

puṣpāṃjalyāṣṭake dattvā mūlamaiva paṭhet sakṛt |

api varṣasahasrāṇāṃ pūjāyāḥ phalamāpnuyāt || 10-155 ||

vilikhya bhūrjapattre tu svarṇasthaṃ dhārayedyadi |

kaṃṭhe vā dakṣiṇe vāhau sakuryād dāsavajjagat || 10-156 ||

trailokyaṃ kṣobhayatyeva trailokyavijayī bhavet |

tadgātraṃ prāpyaśastrāṇi brahmāstrādīni yāni ca || 10-157 ||

mālyāni kusumānye va sukhadāni bhavanti hi |

syarddhā muddhuyabhavane lakṣmīrvāṇī mukhe vaset || 10-158 ||

idaṃ kavacamajñātvā yo japedrāmamantrakam |

saptalakṣajaptopi namantraḥ siddhidāyakaḥ || 10-159 ||

saśastraghātamāpnoti so cirān mṛtyumāpnuyāt |


iti śrībrahmayāmalatantre umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde śrīrāmasya [śrīmasya] 
trailokyamohanaṃ nāmakavacaṃ samāptam ||


śrīsuta uvāca

oṃ ekadā sukhadāsīno pārvatī parameśvarau || 10-160 ||

anyonyā śliṣṭahṛdvāhu lokarakṣaṇatatparau |

indrādilokapālaiśca sevitaṃ taṃ parātparam || 10-161 ||

pārvatīparipapraccha tadā dharmānnuttamān |

śrīpārvatyuvāca 

bho nātha jagatāṃ nātha sarvalokamaheśvara || 10-162 ||

tvat prasādān mayājñāta dharmaśāstramanuttamam |

prāyaścittaṃ tu pāpānāṃ śrutaṃ sarvamaśeṣataḥ || 10-163 ||

brahmahatyādipāpānāṃ niṣkṛtiṃ vaktumarhasi |

śrīmahādeva uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi guhyādgutamaṃ mahat || 10-164 ||

sanatkumāravighneśaṃ saṃvādaṃ pāpanāśanam |

upaviṣṭaṃ gaṇādhyakṣamekānte praṇipatyasaḥ || 10-165 ||

sanatkumāraḥ papraccha sarvadharmavinirṇayam |

sanatkumāra uvāca

bhagavan sarvadharmajña sarvavighnavināśana || 10-166 ||

niṣkṛtiṃ brahmahatyānāṃ baktumarhasi me prabho |

tvāṃ vināmuṣyadharmasya vaktā nāsti jagattraye || 10-167 ||

tasmād gaṇapate mahyaṃ prasādaṃ kuru nirbharam |

śrīvināyaka uvāca 

sādhupṛṣṭhaṃ tvayā brahman sarvalokopakārakam || 10-168 ||

119b) mayāciraṃ kṛtaṃ karma smāritaṃ bhavatā nagha |

purāhaṃ gajarūpeṇa jātaḥ parvatasaṃnibhaḥ || 10-169 ||

manto vṛkṣāt samutpādya munihiṃsāṃ samārabham |

tadā mayā munigaṇā hiṃsitā vahavo valāt || 10-170 ||

hāhākāro mahānāsīd brāhmaṇānāṃ samantataḥ |

tadā hatyā sahasreṇa veṣṭitaḥ paritosmyaham || 10-171 ||

nissaṃjñaṃ mṛtakalpaṃ māṃ vīkṣyadevaḥ pitāmama |

ārādhya jagatāmīśaṃ rāmaṃ sarvahṛdi sthitam || 10-172 ||

pratyakṣamakarod devaṃ maddhitaṃ raghunaṃdanam |

tadā provāca bhagavā śrīrāmaḥ pitaraṃ mama || 10-173 ||

brahmaviṣṇumaheśāna pūjitāṃghrisaroruhaḥ |

śrībhagavānuvāca 

prasaṃnosmi mahādeva kiṃ prārthayasi me prabho || 10-174 ||

dāsyāmi yadabhīṣṭaṃ tat triṣulokeṣu durlabham |

śrīmahādeva uvāca

brahmahatyāsamāviṣṭaṃ mama putramimaṃ prabho || 10-175 ||

niṣpāpaṃ kuru deveśa yadyastimayite dayā |

tathet puhtvā? tadā tena dayayā vīkṣitosmyaham || 10-176 ||

tatkṣaṇāllabdhacaitanyo nirmalajñānavṛṃhitaḥ |

vahubhirgadyapadyaiśca stutvā taṃ praṇatosmyaham || 10-177 ||

tataḥ provāca māṃ rāmaḥ satyasaṃkalpa īśvaraḥ |

śrīrama uvāca 

brahmahatyā sahasrasya prāyaścittaṃ vadāmi te || 10-178 ||

mucyate sarvapāpebhyo japannāmasahasrakam |

iti guhyaṃ dadau rāmo nijaṃ nāmasahasrakam || 10-179 ||

dharmārthakāmamokṣādi sarvavāṃcchitasādhanam |

tatastadgrahaṇādeva niṣpāposmi tadaiva hi || 10-180 ||

tadā di sarvadevānāṃ pūjyosmi munisattam |

tvamapyetadadhīyāno rāghavasya mahātmanaḥ || 10-181 ||

nāmnāṃ sahasraṃ lokeṣu prākhyāpayamahāmate |

sanatkumāra uvāca

dhanyosmyanugṛhītosmi kṛtārthosmi gaṇādhipa || 10-182 ||

tvat prasādājjapiṣyāmi rāmanāmasahasrakam |

śrīmahādeva uvāca 

iti vijñāpya deveśaṃ parikramyapraṇamya ca || 10-183 ||

tadādi satataṃ japtvā stotrametad varānane |

adhyagāt paramāṃ siddhiṃ mama putro vināyakaḥ || 10-184 ||

gaṇānāmādhipatyaṃ ca puṇyapāpavivarjitaḥ |

120a) śrīpārvatyu vāca 

śrotumicchāmi deveśa tadahaṃ sarvakāmadam || 10-185 ||

nāmnāṃ sahasraṃ rāmasya brūhi padyasti medayā |

śrīmahādeva uvāca

atha vakṣyāmi bho devi rāmanāmasahasrakam |

śṛṇu caikamanāḥ stotraṃ guhyādguhyataraṃ mahat || 10-186 ||


asya śrīrāmasahasranāmamālāmantrasya śrīmahādeva-ṛṣiḥ 
anuṣṭupachaṃdaḥ śrīrāmaḥ paramātmā devatā śrīrāmaprītyarthe 
caturvidhapuruṣārtha prāptyarthaṃ sakalakāmanā siddhyarthe 
śrīrāmasahasranāmastavarājapāṭhe viniyogaḥ ||


atha dhyānaṃ 

dhyāyed brahmasvarūpaṃ paramamṛtamayaṃ sevyamānaṃ surendraiḥ 

sītā saumitrijuṣṭaṃ vanavasanadharaṃ phullanīlotpalābham |

viśveśaṃ viśvamurttiṃ bhavabhayaharaṇaṃ sūryakoṭiprakāśaṃ 

kākusthaṃ kosaleśaṃ malatṛṇadahanaṃ kāḍakodaṃḍapāṇim || 10-187 ||


oṃ rājīvalocanaḥ śrīmān śrīrāmoraghupuṃgavaḥ | 

rāmabhadraḥ sadācāro rājendro jānakīpatiḥ || 10-188 ||

agragaṇyo vareṇyaśca varadaḥ parameśvaraḥ |

janārdanojitāmitraḥ parārthaikaprayojanaḥ || 10-189 ||

viśvāmitrapriyodāntaḥ śatrujicchatutāpanaḥ |

sarvajñaḥ sarvadevādiḥ śaraṇyo vālimardanaḥ || 10-190 ||

jñānabhovyoparicchedyo vāgmīsatyavrataḥ śuciḥ |

jñānagamyo dṛḍhaprajñaḥ kharadhvaṃsī pratāpavān || 10-191 ||

dyutimānātmavānvīro jitakrodhorimardanaḥ |

viśvarūpo viśālākṣaḥ prabhuḥ parivṛḍhodṛḍhaḥ || 10-192 ||

īśaḥ khaḍgadharaḥ śrīmān kausalye yonasuyakaḥ |

vipulāṃsomahoraskaḥ parameṣṭhī parāyaṇaḥ || 10-193 ||

satyavrataḥ satyasaṃdho guruḥ paramadhārmikaḥ |

lokajño lokavaṃdyaśca lokātmā lokakṛt prabhuḥ || 10-194 ||

anādirbhagavān sevyo jitamāyoraghūdvahaḥ |

rāmo dayākaro dakṣaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvapāvanaḥ || 10-195 ||

brahmaṇyonītimān goptā sarvadeva priyo hariḥ |

suṃdaraḥ pītavāsāśca sutrakāraḥ purātanaḥ || 10-196 ||

saumyo maharṣiḥ kodaṃḍī sarvagaḥ sarvakoviduḥ |

kaviḥ sugrīvavaradaḥ sarvapuṇyādhikapradaḥ || 10-197 ||

bhavyojitāriṣaḍvargo mahodāroghanāśanaḥ |

120b) sukīrttirādipuruṣaḥ kāntaḥ puṇyakṛtāgamaḥ || 10-198 ||

akalmaṣaścaturvāhuḥ sarvāvasodurāsadaḥ |

smitabhāṣīnivṛttātmā smṛtimānvīryavān prabhuḥ || 10-199 ||

dhīrodānto ghanaśyāmaḥ sarvāyudhaviśāradaḥ |

adhyātmayoganilayaḥ svamānīlakṣmaṇāgrajaḥ || 10-200 ||

sarvatīrthamayaḥ śuraḥ sarvayajñaphalapradaḥ |

yajñasvarūpī yajñeśo jarāmaraṇavarjitaḥ || 10-201 ||

varṇāśramagururvarṇī śattrujitpuruṣottamaḥ |

śivaliṃgapratiṣṭhātā praramātmā parātparaḥ || 10-202 ||

pramāṇabhūto durjñeyaḥ purṇaḥ parapuraṃjayaḥ |

anantadṛṣṭirānaṃdo dhanurvedodhanurddharaḥ || 10-203 ||

guṇākaro guṇaḥśreṣṭaḥ saccidānaṃdavigrahaḥ |

abhivādyo mahākāyo viśvakarmā viśāradaḥ || 10-204 ||

vinītātmā vītarāgastapasvīśojaneśvaraḥ |

kalyāṇaprakṛtiḥ sarvaḥ sarveśaḥ sarvakāmadaḥ || 10-205 ||

akṣaraḥ puruṣaḥ sākṣī keśavaḥ puruṣottamaḥ |

lokādhyakṣo mahākāyo vibhīṣaṇavarapradaḥ || 10-206 ||

ānaṃdavigraho jyotirhanumat prabhurakṣayaḥ |

bhrājriṣṇuḥ sahanobhoktā satyavādī vahuśrutaḥ || 10-207 ||

sukhadaḥ kāraṇaṃ karttā bhavavaṃdhanamocanaḥ |

devacūḍāmaṇirnetā brahmaṇyo brahmavarddhanaḥ || 10-208 ||

saṃsāratārako rāmaḥ sarvaduḥkhavimokṣakṛt |

vidvattamā viśvaharttā viśvabharttā ca viśvasṛk || 10-209 ||

nityo niyatakalyāṇaḥ sītā śokavināśanaḥ |

kākusthaḥ puṃḍarīkākṣo viśvāmitrabhayāpaha || 10-210 ||

mārīca mathano rāmo virādhavadhapaṇḍitaḥ |

duḥsvapnanāśano ramyaḥ kirīṭī tridaśādhipaḥ || 10-211 ||

mahādhanurmahākāyo bhīmo bhīmaparākramaḥ |

tattvasvarūpī tattvajñastattvavādīsuvikramaḥ || 10-212 ||

bhūtātmā bhūtakṛtsvāmī kālajñānī mahāpaṭuḥ |

atirviṣṇo guṇagrāmo niṣkalaṃkaḥ kalakahā || 10-213 ||

svabhāvabhadraḥ śattrughnaḥ keśavaḥ sthāṇurīśvaraḥ |

bhūtādiḥ śaṃbhurādityaḥ sthaviṣṭaḥ śvāśatodhruvaḥ || 10-214 ||

kavacī kuṇḍalīcakrī khardgībhaktajanapriyaḥ |

amṛtyurjanmarahitaḥ sarvajit sarvagocaraḥ || 10-215 ||

121a) anuttamoprameyātmā sarvādirguṇasāgaraḥ |

samaḥ samātmāsamago jaṭāmukuṭamaṇḍitaḥ || 10-216 ||

ajeyaḥ sarvabhūtātmā viṣvakseno mahātapāḥ |

lokādhyakṣo mahāvāhuramṛtovedavittamaḥ || 10-217 ||

sahiṣṇuḥ sadgatiḥ śāstā viśvayonirmahādyutiḥ |

anidra-ūrjitaḥ prāṃśurupendro vāmano valiḥ || 10-218 ||

madhubhedī vidhātā ca brahmāviṣṇuśca śaṃkaraḥ |

haṃsomarīcigovindo ratnagarbho mahādyutiḥ || 10-219 ||

vyāsovācaspatiḥ sarvadṛk pitāsuramardanaḥ |

jānakīvallabhaḥ pūjyaḥ prakaṭaḥ prītivarddhanaḥ || 10-220 ||

saṃbhavotīndriyo vedyo nirdeśyo jāṃvavat prabhuḥ |

madanomanmathojyotirviśvarūpo niraṃjanaḥ || 10-221 ||

nārāyaṇograṇīḥ sādhurjaṭāyuprītivarddhanaḥ |

naikarūpo jagannāthaḥ surakāryahitaḥ prabhuḥ || 10-222 ||

jitakrodhojitārājiḥ plavaṃgādhiparājyadaḥ |

vasudaḥ subhujoneka māyobhāvyapramodanaḥ || 10-223 ||

caṇḍāṃśuḥ siddhasaṃkalpaḥ śaraṇāgatavatsalaḥ |

agadorogaharttā ca mantravitmantrabhāvanaḥ || 10-224 ||

saumitrirvatsalodhuryo vyāktāvyaktasvarūpadhṛk |

vasiṣṭhogrāmaṇīḥ śrīmānanukulaḥ priyaṃvadaḥ || 10-225 ||

atulaḥ sāttvikodhīraḥ śarāsanaviśāradaḥ |

jyeṣṭhaḥ sarvaguṇopetaḥ śaktimāṃstāḍakāntakaḥ || 10-226 ||

vaikuṇṭhaḥ prāṇināṃ prāṇaḥ kamaṭhaḥ kamalādhipaḥ |

govardhanadharomatsya rūpaḥ kāruṇyasāgaraḥ || 10-227 ||

kuṃbhakarṇaprabhettā ca gopīgopālasaṃvṛtaḥ |

māyāvīsvāpano vyāpī raiṇukaiyavalāpahaḥ || 10-228 ||

pinākamathānovaṃdyaḥ sanmattogaruḍadhvajaḥ |

lokatrayapriyoloka bharitobharatāgrajaḥ || 10-229 ||

śrīvaraḥ sadgatirloka sākṣī nārāyaṇo vibhuḥ |

manovegī manorūpī purṇaḥ puruṣapuṃgavaḥ || 10-230 ||

yaduśreṣṭho yadupatirbhūtāvāsaḥ suvikramaḥ |

tejo dharādharovyagraścaturmurttirmahānidhiḥ || 10-231 ||

vāṇuramarddanovaṃdyaḥ śānto bharatavaṃditaḥ |

śabdāti gogabhīrātmā komalāṅgaḥ prajāgaraḥ || 10-232 ||

lokādhvagaḥ śeṣaṇāyī kṣīrāvdhinilayonabhaḥ |

ātmajyotirahīnātmā muha?sārviḥ sahasrapāt || 10-233 ||

parjanyaḥ kumudo bhūto vāsaḥ kamalalocanaḥ |

121b) śrīvatsavakṣāḥ śrīvāso vīrahālakṣmaṇāgrajaḥ || 10-234 ||

lokābhirāmo lokārimardanaḥ sevakapriyaḥ |

sanātanatemomegha śyāmalokṣaṃ sāntakaḥ || 10-235 ||

divyāyudhadharaḥ śrīmānaprameyo jitendriyaḥ |

bhūdeva vaṃdyo janakapriya kṛt prapitāmahaḥ || 10-236 ||

uttamaḥ sāttvikaḥ satyaḥ satyasaṃdhastrivikramaḥ |

suvrataḥ sulabhaḥ sukṣmaḥ aghoṣaḥ sukhadaḥ sudhīḥ || 10-237 ||

dāmodarocyutaḥ śārṅgī vāmanomathurādhipaḥ |

devakīnaṃdanaḥ śaurīḥ śāṅgīkaiṭabhamardanaḥ || 10-238 ||

saptatālaprabhettā ca mittravaṃśapravarddhanaḥ |

kālasvarūpī kālātmā kālaḥ kalyāṇadaḥ kaliḥ || 10-239 ||

saṃvatsara-ṛtuḥ pakṣo hāyanodivasosugaḥ |

stavyo vivikto nirlepaḥ stavovyāpī nirākulaḥ || 10-240 ||

anādinidhanaḥ sarvalokapūjyo nirāmayaḥ |

rasorasajñaḥ saraso lokasāgarasātmakaḥ || 10-241 ||

sarvaduḥkhāti govaṃdyo rāśiḥ paramagocaraḥ |

śeṣo-viśeṣo vigatakalmaṣo raghupuṃgavaḥ || 10-242 ||

varṇaśreṣṭho varṇabhāvo varṇovarṇaguṇeśvaraḥ |

sarvasākṣī guṇaśreṣṭho devāsuravarapradaḥ || 10-243 ||

devādidevo devarṣirdevāsuranamaskṛtaḥ |

sarvadevamayaśca krīśārṅgapāṇiranuttamaḥ || 10-244 ||

manovuddhirahaṃkāraḥ prakṛtiḥ puruṣottamaḥ |

nyāyyonyāyīnayī śrīmān nayonaṃgadharodhruvaḥ || 10-245 ||

lakṣmīrviśaṃbharobharttā devendro vālimardanaḥ |

vāṇārirmardano yajvānuttamomunisattamaḥ || 10-246 ||

devāgraṇīḥ śivadhyānatatparaḥ paramaḥ paraḥ |

sāmageyaḥ priyaḥ śuraḥ puṇyakīrttiḥ sulocanaḥ || 10-247 ||

puṇyaḥ puṇyādhikaḥ pūrvaḥ pūrṇaḥ purayitāraviḥ |

jaṭilaḥ kalmaṣadhvāntaprabhaṃjanavibhāvasuḥ || 10-248 ||

avyaktalakṣaṇo vyakto daśāsyadvipakesarī |

kalānidhiḥ kalārūpaḥ kamalānaṃdavarddhanaḥ || 10-249 ||

japojayāriḥ saṃpūrṇaḥ śyāmalobhavabhaṃjanaḥ |

alaṃkariṣṇuracalorociṣṇurvikramonnataḥ || 10-250 ||

āśuḥ śabdapatiḥ śabdagocaro raṃjanolaghuḥ |

niḥśabdaḥ puruṣomāyī sthūlaḥ sūkṣmovilakṣaṇaḥ || 10-251 ||

ātmayonirayoniśca saptajihvaḥ sahasrapāt |

amṛtāṃśo mahāgarbho nivṛttaviṣayaspṛhaḥ || 10-252 ||

122a) trikālajño muniḥ sākṣī vihāya sagatiḥ kṛtiḥ |

sanātanatamaḥ sugvīpeśalojayātāṃvaraḥ || 10-253 ||

śaktimān śaktitṛnnātho gadādharorathāṅgabhṛt |

nirīhonirvikalpaśca vidrūpotītasādhvasaḥ || 10-254 ||

śatānanaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ śatamūrtirghanaprabhaḥ |

hṛtpuṇḍarīkaśayanaḥ kathinodrava eva ca || 10-255 ||

sūryo grahapatiḥ śrīmān samarthonarthanāśanaḥ |

adharmaśatrurakṣapyaḥ puruhutaḥ puruṣṭutaḥ || 10-256 ||

brahmagarbho vṛhadgarbho dharmadhenurddharāgamaḥ |

hiraṇyagarbho jyotiṣmān sulalāṭaḥ suvikramaḥ || 10-257 ||

śivapūjāratiḥ śrīmān bhavānī priya kṛdvaśī |

naro nārāyaṇo rāmaḥ kapardīnīlalohitaḥ || 10-258 ||

rudraḥ paśupatiḥ sthāṇurviśāmittro dvijeśvaraḥ |

mātāmahomātariśvāviriṃco viṣṭaraśravāḥ || 10-259 ||

akṣobhyaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ caṇḍaḥ satyaparākramaḥ |

vālakhilyo mahākalpaḥ kalpavṛkṣaḥ kalādhipaḥ || 10-260 ||

nidāghastapanomeghaḥ śakraḥ paravalāpahaḥ |

kavaṃdhamathanodivyaḥ kaṃvugrīvaḥ śivapriyaḥ || 10-261 ||

sukhānilaḥ suniṣpannaḥ surabhiḥ śiśirātmakaḥ |

asaṃspṛṣṭhotithiḥ śūraḥ pramāthīpāpanāśakaḥ || 10-262 ||

vasuśravāḥ kavyavāhaḥ pratapnā viśvabhojanaḥ |

romo nīlotpalaśyāmo jñānaskaṃdho mahādyutiḥ || 10-263 ||

pavitrapādaḥ pāpārirmaṇipuro nabhogati |

uttāraṇo duṣkṛtihādurdharṣo dussaho bhayaḥ || 10-264 ||

amṛtāṃśomṛtavapurdharmī dharmakṛpākaraḥ |

bhago vivasvānādibhyo yogācāryo vṛhaspatiḥ || 10-265 ||

udārakīrttirudvyogī vāṅmayaḥ sadasanmayaḥ |

nakṣatramālīnākeśaḥ svādhiṣṭhānaḥ ṣaḍāśrayaḥ || 10-266 ||

catuvargī phalaṃ vargī śaktitrayaphalaṃ nidhiḥ |

nidhānagarbhonirvyājo nirīśo vyājamardanaḥ || 10-267 ||

śrīvallabhaḥ śivāraṃbhaḥ śānto bhadrasamaṃjasaḥ |

bhūśayo bhūtakṛd bhūti bhūṣaṇo bhūtavāhanaḥ || 10-268 ||

akāyo bhūtakāyasthaḥ kālajñānī mahāpaṭuḥ |

parān vṛtti taralo * viviktaḥ śrutisāgaraḥ || 10-269 ||

svabhāvabhadro madhyasthaḥ saṃsārabhayanāśanaḥ |

vedavedyo viyadgoptā saptāvaramunīśvaraḥ || 10-270 ||

122b) surendraḥ karaṇaṃ karmakaraḥ kasrodyudhokṣajaḥ |

dhyeyogradhuryo dhātrīśaḥ saṃkalpaḥ śarvarīpatiḥ || 10-271 ||

paramārthagururvṛṣṭiḥ śucirāśritavatsalaḥ |

viṣṇurjiṣṇuvirbhuryajño yajñeśo yajñapālakaḥ || 10-272 ||

prarbhuviṣṇurgrasiṣṇuśca lolokātmālokabhāvanaḥ |

keśavaḥ kleśahākāyaḥ katiḥ kāraṇakāraṇam || 10-273 ||

kāvyaḥ karttā kalāśeṣo vāsudevaḥ puruṣṭutaḥ |

ādikarttā varāhaśca mādhavo madhusudanaḥ || 10-274 ||

nārāyaṇo narohaṃso viṣvakseno janārdanaḥ |

viśvakarttāmahāyajño jyotiṣmānpuruṣottamaḥ || 10-275 ||

vaikuṇṭhaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ sūryasurārcitaḥ |

narasiṃho mahābhīmaścakradaṃṣṭro nakhāyudhaḥ || 10-276 ||

ādidevo jagatkarttā yogīśo garuḍadhvajaḥ |

goviṃdo gopatirgoptā rūpatirbhuvaneśvaraḥ || 10-277 ||

padmanābho hṛṣīkeśo dhātādāmodaḥ prabhuḥ |

trivikramastrilokeśo brahmeśaḥ prītivardhanaḥ || 10-278 ||

vāmanoduṣṭadamano gopendrogopavallabhaḥ |

bhaktipriyocyutaḥ satyaḥ satyakīrttirdhṛtiḥ smṛtiḥ || 10-279 ||

kāruṇyaṃ karuṇā vyāsaḥ pāpahā śāntivardhanaḥ |

sanyāsīśāstratattvajño maṃdarogiriketanaḥ || 10-280 ||

vadarī nilayaḥ śāntastapasvī vicyutaḥ prabhuḥ |

bhūtāvāso guhāvāsaḥ śrīnivāsaḥ śriyāḥ patiḥ || 10-281 ||

tapovāso mudāvāsaḥ satyavāsaḥ sanātanaḥ |

puruṣaḥ puṣkaraḥ puṇyaḥ puṣkarākṣo maheśvaraḥ || 10-282 ||

pūrṇamūrtiḥ purāṇajñaḥ puṇyadaḥ puṇyavarddhanaḥ |

śaṃkhīcakrāgadīśāṅgī lāṅgalīmusalīhalī || 10-283 ||

kirīṭikuṇḍalīhārī mekharīkavacīdhvajī |

yoddhājetā mahāvīryaḥ śatrughnaḥ śatrutāpanaḥ || 10-284 ||

śāstā svastikaraḥ śāstraṃ śantanaḥ śaṃkaraḥ sutaḥ |

sārathisāttvikaḥ svāmī sāmavedapriyaḥ samaḥ || 10-285 ||

savalaḥ sāhasaḥ śakraḥ saṃpūrṇāṅgaḥ samṛddhimān |

svargadaḥ kāmadaḥ śrīdaḥ kāntidaḥ kāntidāyakaḥ || 10-286 ||

mokṣadaḥ puṃḍarīkākṣaḥ kṣīrāvdhikṛtaketanaḥ |

sarvān sā sarvalokeśaḥ priyakṛtyāpanāśanaḥ || 10-287 ||

vaikuṃṭhaḥ puṃḍarīkākṣaḥ sarvadevanamaskṛtaḥ |

sarvavyāpī jagannāthaḥ sarvalokamaheśvaraḥ || 10-288 ||

123a) sargasthityantakṛd devaḥ sarvadevāṃ sukhāvahaḥ |

akṣayaḥ śāśvatonantaḥ kṣayavṛddhivivarjitaḥ || 10-289 ||

nirlepo nirguṇaḥ sukṣmo nirvikāro niraṃjanaḥ |

sarvopādhivirmuktaḥ sattāmātravyavasthitaḥ || 10-290 ||

avikārīvibhurnityaḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ |

avalo nirmalo vyāpī nityatṛpto nirāśrayaḥ || 10-291 ||

śyāmo yuvā lohitākṣo dīptāsyo mitabhāṣaṇa |

ājānuvāhuḥ sumukhaḥ siṃhaskaṃdho mahābhujaḥ || 10-292 ||

sattvavān guṇasaṃpanno dīpyamānaḥ svatejasā |

kālātmā bhagavān kālaḥ kālacakrapravarttakaḥ || 10-293 ||

nārāyaṇaparaṃ jyotiḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ |

viśvabhugviśvabhoktā ca viśvagoptā ca śāśvataḥ || 10-294 ||

viśveśvaro viśvamūrttirviśvātmā viśvabhāvanaḥ |

sarvabhūtasuhṛcchāntaḥ sarvabhūtānikaṃpanaḥ || 10-295 ||

sarveśvareśvaraḥ sarvaḥ sarvasthāśritavatsalaḥ |

sarvagaḥ sarvabhūteśaḥ sarvabhūtāśaya sthitaḥ || 10-296 ||

ābhyantarasya tamase cchettānnārāyaṇaḥ paraḥ |

anādinidhanaḥ sraṣṭā prajāpatipatirhariḥ || 10-297 ||

narasiṃho hṛṣikeśaḥ sarvātmā sarvasṛgvaṇī |

jagatstasthupabhurnetā sanotanaḥ * * * || 10-298 ||?

kāntā dhātā vidhātā ca sarveṣāṃ patirīśvaraḥ |

sahasramūrdhā viśvātmā viṣṇurviśvadṛgavyayaḥ || 10-299 ||

purāṇaḥ puruṣaḥ sraṣṭāḥ sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrapāt |

tattvaṃ nārāyaṇo viṣṇurvāsudevaḥ sanātanaḥ || 10-300 ||

paramātmā paraṃ jyotiḥ paraṃbrahmaparātparaḥ |

acyutaḥ puruṣaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ śāśvataḥ śiva-īśvaraḥ || 10-301 ||

nityaḥ sarvagataḥ sthāṇu rudraḥ sākṣī prajāpatiḥ |

hiraṇyagarbhaḥ savitā lokakṛllokabhugvibhuḥ || 10-302 ||

akāravācyo bhagavān śrībhūrlīlāpatiḥ prabhuḥ |

sarvalokeśvaraḥ śrīśānsarvajñaḥ sarvatomukhaḥ || 10-303 ||

svāmīsuśīlaḥ sulabhaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvaśaktimān |

nityasaṃpūrṇakāmaśca naisargikasuhṛtsukhī || 10-304 ||

kapā pī yuṣajaladhiḥ śaraṇaṃ sarvadehinām |

śrīmānnārāyaṇaḥ svāmī jagatāṃ prabhurīśvaraḥ || 10-305 ||

śrīśaḥ śaraṇyo bhūtānāṃ saṃśritābhīṣṭadāyakaḥ |

anantaḥ śrīpatīrāmo guṇabhṛnniguṇo mahān || 10-306 ||

123b) harerāma harerāma rāmarāma hare hare |

etadeva sadā bruyād vāṇī me madhusudana (1000) || 10-307 ||

evamādīni nāmāni saṃkhyātāni parāṇi ca |

aikaikaṃ nāmarāmasya sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 10-308 ||

sahasranāmaphaladaṃ sarvaiśvaryapradāyakam |

sarvasiddhipradaṃ nṛṇāṃ bhuktimuktiphalapradam || 10-309 ||

mantrātkakamidaṃ sarvamākhyātaṃ sarvamaṅgalam |

yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādvāpi sa tu brahmapadaṃ labhet || 10-310 ||

tāvadevadhasteṣāṃ mahāpātakadatinām |

yāvan naśruyate rāmanāmapaṃcānanadhvani || 10-311 ||

brahmaghnaśca surāṃyaśca steyaśca śurutalpakaḥ |

śaranāgataghātīva mintraviśvastaghātakaḥ || 10-312 ||

mātṛhā pitṛhā caiva bhruṇahā vīrahā tathā |

japtvā śuddhyanti te sarve rāmanāmasahasrakam || 10-313 ||

koṭi koṭi sahasrāṇi jyupapāpāni yānyapi |

tatkṣaṇaṃ tāni naśyanti japtvā nāmasahasrakam || 10-314 ||

saṃvatsarakramājjaptvā pratyahaṃ rāmasaṃnidhau |

niṣkalaṃkaḥ sukhī bhūtvā tato mokṣamavāpnuyāt || 10-315 ||


iti śrīliṅgapurāṇe umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde śrīrāmanāmasahasraṃ 
saṃpūrṇasamāptam ||

atha rāmastavarājaḥ ||

śrījānakīvallabhāya namaḥ ||

oṃ asya śrīrāmacandrastavarājastotrasya sanatkumāra ṛṣiḥ anuṣṭupacchaṃdaḥ 
śrīrāmo devatā sītāvījaṃ hanumān śaktiḥ śrīrāmaprītyarthe jape viniyogaḥ ||


suta uvāca 

sarvaśāstrārthatattvajña vyāsaṃ satyavatī sutam |

dharmaputraprahṛṣṭātmā pratyuvāca munīśvaram (1) || 10-316 ||

yudhiṣṭhira uvāca 

bhagavadyogināṃ śreṣṭha sarvaśāstraviśārada |

kiṃ tattvaṃ kiṃ paraṃ jāpyaṃ kiṃ dhyānaṃ muktisādhanam (2) || 10-317 ||

śrotumicchāmi tat sarve bruhi me munisattama |

vyāsa uvāca

dharmarājamahābhāga śṛṇu vakṣyāmi tattvataḥ (3) || 10-318 ||

yatparaṃ yadgaṇātītaṃ yajñapotiramalaṃ śivam |

tadekaparamaṃ tattvaṃ kaivalyapadakāraṇam (4) || 10-319 ||

śrīrāmeti paraṃ jāpyaṃ tārakaṃ brahmasaṃjñakam |

brahmahatyādipāpaghnaṃ iti vedavido viduḥ (5) || 10-320 ||

124a) śrīrāmanāmeti janāye japati ca sarvadā |

te vā bhuktiśca muktiśca bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ (6) || 10-321 ||

stavarājaṃ purāproktaṃ nāradena ca dhīmatā |

tatsarvaṃ sapravakṣyāmi haridhyāna puraḥ saram (7) || 10-322 ||

tāpatrayāgniśamanaṃ sarvāghaughanikṛntanam |

dāridraduḥkhaśamanaṃ sarvasaṃpatkaraṃ śivam (8) || 10-323 ||

vijñānaphaladaṃ divyaṃ mohaukaphalasādhanam |

namaskṛtvā pravakṣyāmi rāmaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ jaganmayam (9) || 10-324 ||

ayodhyānagare ramye ratnamaṇḍapamadhyage |

smaret kaplatarormule ratnasiṃhāsanaṃ śubham (10) || 10-325 ||

tanmadhye mṛdalaṃ padmaṃ nānāratnaiśca veṣṭitam |

smaren madhye dāśarathiṃ sahasrāditya tejasam (11) || 10-326 ||

pituraṃkagataṃ rāmaṃ draṃdranīlamaṇiprabham |

komalāṅgaṃ viśālākṣaṃ vidyudvarṇāṃ varāvṛtam (12) || 10-327 ||

bhānukoṭipratīkāśaṃ kirīṭena virājitam |

ratnagraiveyakeyura ratnakuṇḍalamaṇḍitam (13) || 10-328 ||

ratnakaṃkaṇamaṃjīra kaṭisūtrairalaṃkṛtam |

śrīvatsakostubhoraskaṃ muktāhāropaśobhitam (14) || 10-329 ||

divyaratnasamāyuktaṃ mudrikābhiralaṃkṛtam |

rāghavaṃ dvibhujaṃ vālaṃ rāmamīṣatsmitānane (15) || 10-330 ||

tulasīkuṃdamaṃdāra puṣpamālairalaṃkṛtam |

karpūrāgurukastūrī divyagaṃdhānulepanam (16) || 10-331 ||

yogaśāstreṣvabhirataṃ yogīśaṃ yogadāyakam |

sadābharatasaumitri śatrughnairūpaśobhitam (17) || 10-332 ||

vidyādharasurādhīśaiḥ siddhagaṃ[dha]rvakinnaraiḥ |

yogīndrairnāradādyaiśca stūyamānamaharniśam (18) || 10-333 ||

viśvāmitravasiṣṭhādi munibhiḥ parisevitam |

sanakādimuniśreṣṭhairyogivṛṃdaiśca sevitam (19) || 10-334 ||

rāmaṃ raluvaraṃ vīraṃ dhanurvedaviśāradam |

maṃgalāyatanaṃ devaṃ rāmaṃ rājīvalocanam (20) || 10-335 ||

sarvaśāstrārthatattvajñamānaṃdakarasundaram |

kausalyānaṃdanaṃ rāmaṃ dhanurvāṇadharaṃ harim (21) || 10-336 ||

evaṃ saṃcitayedviṣṇuṃ yajyotiramalaṃ vibhum |

prahṛṣṭamānaso bhutvā munivaryaḥ sanāradaḥ (22) || 10-337 ||

sarvalokahitārthāya tuṣṭā va raghunaṃdanam |

kṛtāṃjalipuṭo bhūtvā cintayannadbhutaṃ harim (23) || 10-338 ||

yadekaṃ yatparaṃ nityaṃ yadanantaṃ cidātmakam |

yadekaṃ vyāpakaṃ loke tadrūpaṃ cintayāmyaham (24) || 10-339 ||

124b) vijñānadetuṃ vimalāyatākṣaṃ prakṣānarūpaṃ sumukhaikahetum |

śrīrāmacaṃdraṃ harimādidevaṃ parātparaṃ rāmamahaṃ bhajāmi (25) || 10-
340 ||

kaviṃ purāṇaṃ puruṣaṃ purastāt sanātanaṃ yoginamośitāram |

aṇoraṇīyāṃ samanantavīryaṃ prāṇeśvaraṃ rāmamasaudedarśa (26) || 10-341 ||

nārada uvāca

nārāyaṇaṃ jagannathamabhirāmaṃ jagatpatim |

kaviṃ purāṇaṃ vāgīśaṃ rāmaṃ daśarathātmajam (27) || 10-342 ||

rājarājaṃ raghuvaraṃ kausalyānandavardhanam |

bhargaṃ vareṇyaṃ viśveśaṃ raghunāthaṃ jagadgurum (28) || 10-343 ||

satyaṃ satyapriyaśreṣṭhaṃ jānakīvallabhaṃ vibhum |

saumitripūrvajaṃ śāntaṃ kāmadaṃ kamalekṣaṇam (29) || 10-344 ||

ādityaṃ ravimīśānaṃ ghṛṇisūryamanāmayam |

ānaṃdabhūṣiṇaṃ saumya rāghavaṃ karuṇāmayam (30) || 10-345 ||

jāmagnitapomūrtti rāmaṃ paraśvadhāriṇam |

vākpatiṃ varadaṃ vācyaṃ śrīpatiṃ pakṣavāhanam (31) || 10-346 ||

śrīśāṅgadhāriṇaṃ rāmaṃ cinmayānaṃdavigraham |

haladṛgviṣṇumīśānaṃ valarāmaṃ kṛpānidhim (32) || 10-347 ||

śrīvallabhaṃ kṛpānāthaṃ jaganmohanamacyutam |

matsyakurmavarāhādi rūpadhāriṇamavyayam (33) || 10-348 ||

vāsudevaṃ jagadyoni anādinidhanaṃ harim |

govindaṃ gopatiṃ viṣṇuṃ gopījanamanoharam (34) || 10-349 ||

gopālaṃ goparivāraṃ gopakanyā samāvṛtam |

vidyutpuṃjapratīkāśaṃ rāmaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ jaganmayam (35) || 10-350 ||

gogopikāsamākīrṇaṃ veṇuvādana tatparam |

kāmarūpaṃ kalāvantaṃ kāmanīkāmayaṃ vibhum (36) || 10-351 ||

manmathaṃ mathurānāthaṃ mādhavaṃ makaradhvajam |

śrīdharaṃ śrīkaraṃ śrīśaṃ śrīnivāsaṃ parātparam (37) || 10-352 ||

bhūteśaṃ bhūpatiṃ bhadraṃ vibhūtiṃ bhūtibhūṣaṇam |

sarvaduḥkhaharaṃ vīraṃ duṣṭadānavavairiṇam (38) || 10-353 ||

śrīnṛsiṃhaṃ mahāvāhuṃ mahāntaṃ dīptatejasam |

cidānandamayaṃ nityaṃ praṇavaṃ jyotirūpiṇam (39) || 10-354 ||

ādityaṃ maṇḍalagataṃ niścitārthakarūpiṇam |

bhaktapriyaṃ padmanetraṃ bhaktānāmīpsitapradam (40) || 10-355 ||

kausaleyaṃ kalāmūrttiṃ kākusthaṃ kamalāpriyam |

saṃhāsane samāsīnaṃ nityavratamakalmaṣam (41) || 10-356 ||

125a) viśvāmitrapriyaṃ dāntaṃ svadārāniyatavratam |

yajñeśaṃ yajñapuruṣaṃ yajñapālana tatparam (42) || 10-357 ||

satyasaṃdhaṃ jitakrodhaṃ śaraṇāgatavatsalam |

sarvakleśapraharaṇaṃ vibhīṣaṇavarapradam (43) || 10-358 ||

daśagrīvaṃ haraṃ rudraṃ keśavaṃ keśimardanam |

vālipramathanaṃ vīraṃ sugrīvepsitarājyadam (44) || 10-359 ||

naravānaradevaiśca sevitaṃ hanumat priyam |

śuddhaṃ sūkṣmaṃ paraṃ śāntaṃ tārakaṃ brahmarūpiṇam (45) || 10-360 ||

sarvabhūtātmabhūtasthaṃ sarvādhāraṃ sanātanam |

sarvakāraṇakarttāraṃ nidānaṃ prakṛteḥ param (46) || 10-361 ||

nirāmayanirābhāsaṃ niravādaṃ niraṃjanam |

nityānandaṃ nirākāraṃ advaitantamataḥ param (47) || 10-362 ||

parātparātparaṃ tattvaṃ satyāṃnaṃdacidātmakam |

manasā śirasā nityaṃ praṇamāmiraghuttamam (48) || 10-363 ||

bhavodbhavaṃ vedavido variṣṭamādityacaṃdrānilasuprabhāvam |

sarvātmakaṃ sarvagataṃ svarūpaṃ namāmi rāmaṃ tamasaḥ parastāta (49) || 10-
364 ||

niraṃjanaṃ niḥpratimaṃ nirīhaṃ rāśrayaṃ niṣkalamaprapaṃcam |

nityaṃ dhruvaṃ nirviṣayasvarūpaṃ nirantaraṃ rāmamahaṃ bhajāmi (50) || 10-
365 ||

bhavādhipotaṃ bharatāgrajātaṃ bhaktapriyaṃ bhānukalapradīpam |

bhūtatrināthaṃ bhuvanādhipatyaṃ bhajāmirāmaṃ bhavarogavaidyam (51) || 10-
366 ||

sarvādhipatyaṃ samaraṃgadhīraṃ satyaṃ cidānaṃdamayaṃ svarūpam |

satyaṃ śivaṃ śāntimayaṃ śaraṇyaṃ sanātanaṃ rāmamahaṃ bhajāmi (52) || 
10-367 ||

kāryakriyākāraṇamaprameyaṃ kaviṃ purāṇaṃ kamalāyatākṣam |

kumāravedyaṃ karuṇāmayantaṃ kalpadrumaṃ rāmamahaṃ bhajāmi (53) || 10-
368 ||

trilokanāthaṃ sarasīruhāktaṃ dayānidhiṃ dvaṃdvavināśahantum |

mahāvalaṃ vedanidhiṃ sureśaṃ sanātanaṃ rāmamahaṃ bhajāmi (54 ) || 10-369 
||

vedāntavedyaṃ vidhimīśitāraṃ anādimadaṃ pātamacintyamādyam |

agocaraṃ nirmalarmakarupaṃ namāmi rāmaṃ tamasaḥ purastāt (55) || 10-370 ||

aśeṣa vedātmakamādisaṃjñaṃ ajaṃ hariṃ viṣṇumanantamūrtti |

apārasaṃvit sukhamekarūpaṃ namāmi rāmaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt (56) || 10-371 ||

125b) aśeṣavedātmakamādisaṃjñaṃ ajaṃ hariṃ viṣṇumanantamūrtti |

apārasaṃvit suhamekarūpaṃ parātparaṃ rāmamahaṃ bhajāmi (57) || 10-372 ||

tattvasvarūpaṃ puruṣaṃ purāṇaṃ svatejasā pūritaviśvamekam |

rājādhirājaṃ ratimaṇḍalasthaṃ viśveśvaraṃ rāmamahaṃ bhajāmi (58) || 10-
373 ||

lokābhirāmaṃ raghuvaṃśanāthaṃ hariṃ cidānandamayaṃ mukundam |

aśeṣavidyādhipatiṃ kavīndraṃ namāmi rāmaṃ tamasaḥ purastāt (59) || 10-374 ||

yogīndrasaṃjñaiḥ śatasevyamānaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ nirmalamādi devam |

natosmi nityaṃ jagadekanāthaṃ ādityavarṇaṃ namasaḥ parāt (60) || 10-375 ||

vibhūtidaṃ viśvasṛjaṃ virājaṃ gajendramīśaṃ raghuvaṃśanātham |

acintyamavyaktamanantamūrttiṃ jyotirmayaṃ rāmamahaṃ bhajāmi (61) || 10-376 ||

aśeṣasaṃsāravikārahīnaṃ ādistu saṃpūrṇamukhābhirāmam |

samastasākṣī tamasaḥ parastān nārāyaṇaṃ viṣṇumahaṃ bhajāmi (62) || 10-377 
||

munindraguhyaṃ paripūrṇamekaṃ kalānidhiṃ kalmaṣanāśahetum |

parātparaṃ yat paramaṃ pavitraṃ namāmirāmaṃ mahato mahāntam (63) || 10-
378 ||


brahmāviṣṇuśca rudraśca devendro devatāstathā |

ādityavigrahāścaiva tvameva raghunandana (64) || 10-379 ||

tapasā ṛṣayaḥ siddhā sādhyaśca marutastathā |

viprā vedāstathā yajñaḥ purāṇaṃ dharmasaṃhitā (65) || 10-380 ||

varṇāśramāstathā dharmā varṇadharmā tathaiva ca |

yajñarākṣasagaṃdharva dikpālādigajādibhiḥ (66) || 10-381 ||

sanakādi muniśreṣṭā tvamevaraghupuṃgavaḥ |

vasavoṣṭhau trayaḥ kālā rudrā ekādaśā smṛtā (67) || 10-382 ||

tārakā daśadik caiva tvameva raghunandana |

saptadvīpāḥ samudrāśca nāgānadyastathā drumāḥ (68) || 10-383 ||

sthāvaraṃ jaṃgamaścaiva tvameva raghunāyaka |

devatīryaṅmanuṣyāṇāṃ dānavānāṃ tathaiva ca (69) || 10-384 ||

mātāpitā tathā bhrātā tvameva raghuvallabha |

sarveṣāṃ tvaṃ paraṃ brahma tvan mayaṃ sarva eva hi (70) || 10-385 ||

tvamakṣaraṃ paraṃ jyotiḥ tvameva raghunandana |

śāntasarvagataṃ sūkṣmaṃ paraṃ brahmasanātanam (71) || 10-386 ||

rājīvalocanaṃ rāmaṃ praṇamāmi jagatpatim |

126a) tataḥ prasaṃnaḥ śrīrāmo provāca munipuṃgava (72) || 10-387 ||

tuṣṭāsmi muniśārdūlavṛṇāṃ ṣvevaramuttamam |

nārada uvāca

yadi tuṣṭosi sarvajña śrīrāmakaruṇānidhe (73) || 10-388 ||

tvak mūrttidarśane naiva kṛtārthohaṃ mamepsitam |

dhanyāhaṃ kṛtyakṛtyohaṃ punyohaṃ puruṣottama (74) || 10-389 ||

adya me saphalaṃ janma jīvitaṃ saphalaṃ ca me |

adyameva phalaṃ jñānaṃ adyameva phalaṃ tapaḥ (75) || 10-390 ||

adyameva phalaṃ sarvaṃ tvnnāma smaraṇaṃ tathā |

tvat pādāṃbhoruhadvaṃdva sadbhaktiṃ dehi rāghava (76) || 10-391 ||

tataḥ paramasaṃprīto rāmaḥ prāhasanāradam |

munivaryamahābhāga mune tviṣṭaṃ dadāmi te |

yat tvayā cepsitaṃ sarvaṃ manasā tad bhaviṣyati (77) || 10-392 ||

nārada uvāca 

varaṃ nayā ceraghunātha yuṣmat pādābjabhaktiḥ satataṃ mamāstu |

idaṃ priyaṃ nātha varaṃ prayaccha punaḥ punastvāmayam eva yā ca (78) || 10-393 
||

itye ca mīḍito rāmo prādāt tasmai varāntaram |

advaita mama tvaṃ jñānaṃ tvannāmasmaraṇaṃ tathā (79) || 10-394 ||

antardhyāna jagannātha puratastasyarāghava |

iti śrīraghunāthasya stavarājamanuttamam || 10-395 ||

sarvasaubhāgyasaṃpattidāyakaṃ muktidaṃ śubham (80) ||

kathitaṃ brahmaputreṇa vedānāṃ sāramuttamam || 10-396 ||

guhyādguhyatamaṃ divyaṃ tava snehāt prakīrttitam (81) ||

yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādvāpi trisaṃdhyaṃ śraddhayānvitam || 10-397 ||

brahmahatyādipāpāni tat samāni vahuni ca (82) ||

svarṇaste yī surāpānī gurutalpayutāni ca || 10-398 ||

govadhādyupapāpāni anṛtāt saṃbhavāni ca (83) ||

sarvaiḥ pramucyate pāpaiḥ kalpāyutaśatodbhavaiḥ || 10-399 ||

mānasaṃ vācikaṃ pāpaṃ karmaṇā samupārjitam (84) ||

śrīrāmasmaraṇe naiva tatkṣaṇān naśyati dhruvam || 10-400 ||

idaṃ satyamidaṃ satyaṃ satyametad ihocyate (85) |

rāmaṃ satyaṃ paraṃ brahma rāmātkiṃcinnavidyate |

tasmād rāmasya bhūpoyaṃ satyaṃ satyamidaṃ jagat (86) || 10-401 ||


śrīrāmacandraraghupuṃgava rājavaryaṃ rājendrarāmaraghunāyaka rāghaveśa |

rājādhirājaraghunaṃdana rāmabhadradāsohamadya bhavataḥ śaraṇāgatosmi 
(87) || 10-402 ||


rāmaṃ ratnakirīṭakuṃḍalayutaṃ keyurahārānvitaṃ 
sītālaṃkṛtavāmabhāgaṃ amalaṃ siṃhāsanasthaṃ vibhum |

126b) sugrīvādiharīśvaraiḥ suragaṇaiḥ saṃsevyamānaṃ sadā 
viśvāmitraparāśarādi munibhiḥ saṃstūyamānaṃ vibhum (88) || 10-403 ||

sakalaguṇanidhānaṃ yogibhiḥ stūyamānaṃ bhujavijitavimānaṃ 
rakṣasendrādimānam |

sahitavṛṣabhyānaṃ sītayo sāṃbhamānaṃ smṛta hṛdayabhānuṃ 
brahmarāmābhidhāmam (89) || 10-404 ||

raghuvaratavamūrttimāmakamānasājaṃ narakagatiṃ harante nāmadheyaṃ mukhe 
me |

aniśamatulabhaktyā mastake tvat padābjaṃ bhavajalanidhimagnaṃ 
rakṣamamārttavandhe (90) || 10-405 ||

rāmaratnamahaṃ vande citrakūṭapatiṃ harim |

kausalyāśuktisaṃbhājaṃ jānakīkaṃṭhabhūṣaṇam (91) || 10-406 ||


iti śrīsanatkumārasaṃhitāyāṃ nāradoktaṃ śrīrāmastavarājastotraṃ 
samāptam ||

samāptamidaṃ śrīrāmapaṃcāṅgam ||


śrīgopālāya namaḥ 

atha śrīgopālamantraḥ ||


gopījanānte pravaded vallabhāyāgnisundarīm |

ayaṃ daśākṣaro mantro dṛṣṭā dṛṣṭa phalapradaḥ || 10-407 ||


gopījanānte pravaded iti gopījanapadamuddhṛtya vallabhāya svāheti vadet |

tathā ca ||

gopījanavallabhāya svāhā ||

iti daśākṣaro mantraḥ |

agnisuṃdarī svāhā ||

atha pūjāpaddhatiḥ ||

prātaḥ śirasi śuklebje dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ guruṃ varābhayakaraṃ śāntaṃ 
smaret tan nāmapūrvakam ||

ajñānatimirāndhasya ti | śirasi sahasradalakamale paramātmā sūryarūpo hṛdaye 
jīvātmā vahni rūpā mūlādhāre kuṇḍalinī caṃdrarūpā tat tejastrayaṃ 
ekībhūtaṃ vicintyate na tejasā śrīkṛṣṇasya saccidānandarūpaṃ śarīraṃ 
ārvibhūtaṃ iti hṛdi vicintya tatra dehe yaḥ koṭi parārddhānāṃ 
brahmāṇḍānāṃ vyāpakaḥ parameśvaraśca śrīkṛṣṇaḥ sodhi tiṣṭhata iti 
bhāvayitvā ||


koṭi koṭi parārdhānāṃ brahmāṇḍo namadhīśvaraḥ |

prasīda paramānanda tvāṃ namasyāmi mādhava || 10-408 ||


127a) iti vācā manasā kāye na natvā śrīkṛṣṇaṃ praṇamya mulādhāracakraṃ 
pṛthivīsthānaṃ raktavarṇaṃ raśavasavarṇacatuṣkayuktacaturdalaṃ kuṇḍalinī 
suryatulyapracaṇḍā candratulyaśītalā vidyuttulyanibhā tiṣṭhati mulādhārebhyo 
parimūlādhārādvyaṃgulādūrdhvaṃ medrādvyaṃgulād adha-
ekāṅgulaparititaṃ svādhiṣṭhānacakraṃ vahnisthānaṃ 
vabhamayaralavarṇaṣaṣṭkayuktaṃ ṣaḍdalaṃ svādhiṣṭhānasyoparinābhau 
manipurakacakre ratnādyaṃ jalasthānam ||

ḍa ḍha ṇa ta tha da dha na pa pha daśavarṇayuktadaśadalaṃ 
maṇipūrakasyoparihṛdaye |

anāhatacakraṃ vāyusthānaṃ suṣuptisthānaṃ ca ka kha ga gha ṅa ca cha ja jha 
ña ṭa ṭha dvāda * varṇayukta dvādaśadalaṃ anāhatapadmasyoparigale 
viśuddhacakraṃ svapnasthānamākāśasthānaṃ ca |

a ā i ī u ū ṛ ṝ ḷ ḹ e ai o au aṃ aḥ ṣoḍaśasvarayukta ṣoḍaśadalam |

viśuddhacakrasyoparibhrumadhye ājñācakraṃ jāgratsthānaṃ ākāśasthānaṃ 
ca |

hakṣaḥ yuktadaladvayamiti cintayet ||

tato mūlādhārasvādhiṣṭhānamaṇipurakānāhataviśuddhā jñānāmakāni 
ṣaṭcakrāṇibhitvā kuṇḍalinī sahasradalakamalastha caṃdramaṇḍalaṃ prāpapya 
caṃdramaṇḍalāmṛtam pītavatī punarājñā 
viśuddhānāhatamaṇipurakasvādhiṣṭhānāni bhitvā kuṇḍalinī mulādhāre 
gateti bhāvayet |

tata utthāye malamutrādi tyāgaṃ kṛtvā hastapādādikaṃ śodhayitvā 
dantadhāvanaṃ kṛtvācamyavidhivajjalāśaye uddhṛtodakena vā snānaṃ kuryāt ||

aṃkuśamudrayādityamaṇḍalāt ||


oṃ gaṅge ca yamune caiva godāvari sarasvati |

kāvire narmade siṃdhorjalesmin sannidhiṃ kuru || 10-409 ||


iti mantreṇatīrthamāvāhya jale mūlamantramaṣṭadhā japet |

tasmiñjale hariṃ ca cintayet |

tataḥ kuṃ.bhamudrayā svaśirasi vāratrayaṃ jalaṃ dadyāt |

tato gātraṃ mārjayitvācamanaṃ kṛtvā devān ṛṣīn pitruṃstarpayet ||

tato dhenumudrayāmṛtīkṛtya ravimaṇḍalā cchrīkṛṣṇe hā gaccha 
gacchetyāvāhya mūlamuccārya śrīkṛṣṇaṃ tarpayāmiti santarppa gāyatrīṃ 
japtvā sūryāyārghaṃ dadyāt ||

127b) tato jalādutthāya dhaute vāsasī paridhāyācamya prakṣālitapāṇipādaḥ 
pavitramudrāyukta dakṣiṇahastānāmikāmadhyaparvataśvetordhvatilaka ācamya 
dakṣiṇahaste jalaṃ gṛhītvā nama iti mantreṇa vāmahaste jalaṃ nidhāya 
vāmahastāṅgulisaṃdhigatairjalavindubhiḥ svāheti mantreṇa svaśiraḥ prokṣayet ||

tato strāya phaṭ iti ||

vāmahastād dakṣiṇahastena jalaṃ gṛhītvādhaḥ prakṣipet |

evaṃ vāracatuṣṭayaṃ kuryāt |

punardakṣiṇahaste nama iti mantreṇa jalaṃ gṛhītvā vāmanāsikayāghrāya 
kiṃcijjalaṃ nāsikāntargataṃ dakṣiṇaprāṇenani smṛtaṃ iti vicintyādhaḥ 
prakṣipeta ||

oṃ gopījanavallabhāya vidmahe vāsudevāya dhīmahi tannaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ pracodayāt 
(3) ||

iti śrīkṛṣṇagāyatrīṃ japtvānenamantreṇādityamaṇḍalasthāya 
śrīkṛṣṇāyārgho namaḥ ||

iti śrīkṛṣṇāya jalairarghaṃ dadyāt ||

iti mantrasaṃdhyā ||

tathā pūjāmaṇḍapadvāre upaviśya dvāradevatābhyo namaḥ |

iti gaṃdhādinā dvāradevatāḥ saṃpūjya gṛhābhyantaraṃ praviśya 

oṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ ||

oṃ vāstu puruṣāya namaḥ iti pūjayet ||

tadagre kuśāsanaṃ tadupari vyāghracarmamṛgacarmā vā tadupari vastraṃ 
pātayitvā prokṣya kṛtāṃjaliḥ | 

merupṣṭha-ṛṣiḥ sutalaṃ cchandaḥ kūrmo devatā āsanopaveśane viniyogaḥ ||

iti paṭhitvā ||


pṛthvi tvayā dhṛtā lokā devi tvaṃ viṣṇunā dhṛtā |

tvaṃ ca dhāraya māṃ devi pavitraṃ kuru cāsanam || 10-410 ||


iti pṛthivīmantreṇa pṛthivīṃ ūjayet ||

tata ādhāraśaktikamalāsanāya nama ityāsanaṃ sapūjya |

tadupari svastikāsanādi āsanaṃ vaddhvā upaviśet |



sthāpayed dakṣiṇe bhāge pūjā dravyāni cātmanaḥ |

suvāsināṃ vusaṃpūrṇaṃ savye kuṃbhaṃ suśobhanam || 10-411 ||

prakṣālatālakarayoḥ pṛṣṭhe pātraṃ nidhāpayet |

dīpasya jvalitasyāpi sannidhānaṃ prakalpayet || 10-412 ||

devasya mastakaṃ kuryāt kusumopahitaṃ sadā |

pūjākāle devatāyāto paribhrāmayet karam || 10-413 ||


vāmakapole gurubhyo namaḥ |

dakṣiṇakapole gaṇeśāya namaḥ |

madhye śrīkṛṣṇadevatāyai namaḥ ||

iti praṇamya ||

asurāntakacakrāya svāhā |

astrāya phaṭ |

iti uccārya hastau karatalapṛṣṭayoḥ saṃśodhya ūrdhvordhvakrameṇa tālatrayaṃ 
dattvā nārācamudrayā sphoṭikābhirdaśadigvandhanaṃ ca kṛtvā trailokyaṃ 
rakṣa rakṣa hūṃ phaṭ svāhā ||

iti mantreṇajalenātmānaṃ veṣṭayitvā caturddikṣvagniprākāraṃ cintayet |

tato bhūtaśuddhiṃ kuryāt || yathā ||

svāṅke uttānahastau kṛtvā sohamiti mantreṇa pradīpakalikākāraṃ hṛdisthaṃ 
jīvātmānaṃ mūlādhārasthaṃ kuṇḍalinyā sahaśirastha sahasradalakamalastha 
paramātmanilīnaṃ vicintya ||


pādatojānuparyantaṃ caturaśraṃ sa cakrakam |

laṃyutaṃ pītavarṇaṃ ca bhuvaḥ sthānaṃ vicintayet || 10-414 ||

jānvorānābhicandrārdhanibhaṃ padmena lācchitam |

śuklavarṇaṃ savījena yutaṃ dhyāyedapāṃsthalam || 10-415 ||

nābhitaḥ kaṇṭhaparyantaṃ satrikoṇaṃ saraktakam |

sasvastikaṃ svavījenayuktaṃ vahnestu maṇḍalam || 10-416 ||

kaṇṭhādbhrūmadhyaparyantaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ vāyostu maṇḍalam |

ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vindubhiḥ ṣaḍbhiyuktaṃ vījena cintayet || 10-417 ||

bhrumadhyādbrahmarandhrāntaṃ vartulaṃ dhvajalāṃcchitam |

dhūmravarṇaṃ svavījena yuktaṃ dhyāyen nabhasthalam || 10-418 ||

pṛthivyā laṃ vījaṃ jalasya vaṃ vījaṃ agneḥ raṃ vījaṃ vāyoryaṃ vījaṃ 
ākāśasya haṃ vījam ||


evaṃ dhyātvā tatastāni bhūtāni pravilāpayet |

pṛthvīmapsu ca tau vahnau vahni vāyau samīraṇam || 10-419 ||

pravilāpya tathākāśe ākāśaṃ prakṛtau tataḥ |

aparabrahmarūpāṃ tāṃ māyāśaktiparātmani || 10-420 ||

iti samastadehādi prapaṃcaṃ paramātmani |

pravilāpya puruṣākāra bhāganādi bhavasaṃcitam || 10-421 ||

brahmahatyāśiraskaṃ ca svarṇasteyabhujadvayam |

surāpāna hṛdāyuktaṃ gurutalpakaṭidvayam || 10-422 ||

tat saṃsargipadadvaṃdvaṃ aṃgapratyaṃgapātakam |

upapātakaromāṇaṃ raktaśmaśruvilocanam || 10-423 ||

kharḍgacarmadharaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ vāmakukṣau vicintayet |


tat iḍāvaktraṃ vāmenasidhumravarṇaṃ yamiti vāyuvījaṃ ṣoḍaśavāraṃ japan 
vāmanāsāpuṭena vāyunā dehamāpūryamiti catuḥṣaṣṭivāraṃ japan kurvan 
dehaṃ saṃśoṣya punaryamiti dvātriṃśadvāraṃ japan dakṣināsāpuṭe na 
vāyuṃ recayet |

128b) tatoruṇena vāyusaṃvaddhe naramiti vahnivījaṃ ṣoḍaśavāra japtena 
dakṣanāsāpuṭena vāyunādehamāpūrya catuṣṣaṣṭhivāra japtena kuṃbhakaṃ 
kurvan tadbhasmībhūtamiti vicintya ramiti dvātriṃśadvāraṃ japan vāmanāsayā 
bhasmanā sahavāyuṃ recayet |

tataḥ ṭhamiti caṃdravījaṃ śubhraṃ prakṛtyātmakaṃ vāmanāsāpuṭena 
puruṣātmakaṃ lalāṭacandra ṣoḍaśavāraṃ japannayet ||

tato vamiti catuḥṣaṣṭhivāraṃ japan lalāṭacaṃdrādādikṣāṃ tāṃ 
akṣaramayīṃ amṛtavṛṣṭiṃ nipātya tayā devopāsanayogyaṃ sakalaṃ vapuḥ 
kurvan lamiti dvātriṃśadvāraṃ japan dakṣiṇanāsayā vāyuṃ recayet | 



punarutyādayed dehaṃ pavitraṃ paramātmanaḥ || 10-424 ||

śabdabrahmātmikā māyā mātṛkā prakṛtiḥ parā |

ajāyanajaganmātu rākā tu rākāśaṃ nabhesonilaḥ || 10-425 ||

samīraṇād abhūdvahnirvahnerāpastato mahī |

svīyametyopibhūtebhyastejorūpaṃ kalevaram || 10-426 ||

devatārādhane yogyamutpannamiti bhāvayet |

asmin dehe parātmāyaṃ sarvajñaḥ sarvaśaktimān || 10-427 ||

samastadevatā rūpaḥ sarvamantramayaḥ śubhaḥ |

ātmarūpeṇa dehe svajīvabhāvena tiṣṭhati || 10-428 ||

ityavabhāvanā mukhyā bhūtaśuddhirihoditā |


tato hṛdihastaṃ datvā ||

ā/ hrī/ kro/ ya/ ra/ la/ va/ śa/ ṣa/ sa/ ha/ sa iti mama prāṇā iha prāṇā evam (12) ||

mama jīva iha sthitaḥ ||

evaṃ 12 mama sarvendriyāṇi vāṅmanaścakṣuḥ śrotraghrāṇaprāṇāḥ 
ihāgatya sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhantu svāhā ||

iti vījaṃ bhyaset ||

tataḥ śirovadanadṛk śravaṇaghoṇagaṇḍauṣṭhadantadvayeṣu saśiromukheṣu ca iti 
kramād vinyaset ||

halaśca karapādasaṃdhiṣu udagagrakeṣu vāmapārśve 
dvayapṛṣṭhanābhyudarakeṣuyādyā navahṛdayakakṣa kakutkaramūlado 
padayugodaravaktragatān vudhaḥ ||

hṛdaya pūrvamanena yathānvahaṃ nyasatu kalevara siddhaye ||

oṃ brahmarṣiḥ gāyatrī cchandaḥ mātṛkāsarasvatī devatāhalo vījāni svaraḥ 
śaktayaḥ mātṛkā nyāse viniyogaḥ |

129a) tataḥ paṃcāśallipibhirityādināvāgdevatāṃ dhyātvā namaskuryāt |

tataḥ || aṃ namo brahmaraṃdhre | 

i/tyādi mātṛkā nyāsaṃ samāpya keśavakīrtyādi nyāsaṃ kuryāt | 

yathā |

udyat pradyotanaśataruciṃ taptahemāvadātaṃ pārśvadvaṃdve jaladhisutayā 
viśvadhātryā ca juṣṭam |

nānāratnollasitavividhā kalpamā prītavastraṃ viṣṇuṃ vande 
darakamalakaumodakrīcakrapāṇim |

aṃkeśa vāyakīrtyai namaḥ |

āṃ nārāyaṇāya kāntyai namaḥ |

iṃ mādhavāya tuṣyai namaḥ |

ī/ govindāya puṣyai namaḥ |

oṃ viṣṇave vṛttyai namaḥ |

oṃ madhusūdanāya śāntyai namaḥ |

ṛ/ trivikramāya kriyāyai namaḥ |

ṛ/ vāmanāya dayāyai namaḥ |

ḷ/ śrīdharāya medhāyai namaḥ |

ḹ/ hṛṣīkeśāya harṣāya namaḥ |

e/ padmanābhyāya śraddhāyai namaḥ |

ai/ dāmodarāyajjāyai namaḥ |

o/ vāsudevāya lakṣmyai namaḥ |

au/ saṃkarṣaṇāya sarasvatyai namaḥ |

a/ pradyumnāya prītyai namaḥ |

aḥ aniruddhāya ratyai namaḥ |

kaṃ cakriṇe jayāyai namaḥ |

khaṃ gadine durgāyai namaḥ |

gaṃ śārṅgiṇe prabhāyai namaḥ |

ghaṃ khaḍgine satyāyai namaḥ |

ṅaṃ śaṃkhine caṇḍāyai namaḥ |

caṃ haline vāṇyai namaḥ |

chaṃ musuline vilāsinyai namaḥ |

jaṃ śūline vijayāyai namaḥ |

jhaṃ pāśine virajāyai namaḥ |

ñaṃ aṃkuśine viśvāyai namaḥ |

ṭaṃ mukundāya vinodāyai namaḥ |

ṭhaṃ nandajāya sunandāyai namaḥ |

ḍaṃ nandine smṛtyai namaḥ |

ḍhaṃ nārāyaṇāya ṛddhyai namaḥ |

ṇaṃ narakajite samṛddhyai namaḥ |

taṃ harāya śuddhyai namaḥ |

thaṃ kṛṣṇāya vuddhyai namaḥ |

daṃ satyāya bhuktyai namaḥ |

dhaṃ sāttvatāya satyai namaḥ |

naṃ sārāya kṣamāyai namaḥ |

paṃ śūrāya ramāyai namaḥ |

phaṃ janārdanāya umāyai namaḥ |

baṃ bhūdharāya kledinyai namaḥ |

bhaṃ viśvamurttaye klinnāyai namaḥ |

maṃ vaikuṇṭhāya vasudāyai namaḥ |

yaṃ tvagātmane puruṣottamāya vasudhāyai namaḥ |

129b) raṃ sṛgātmane valine parāyai namaḥ |

laṃ māṃsātmane valānujāya parāyaṇāyai namaḥ |

vaṃ vedātmane valāyasūkṣmāyai namaḥ |

śaṃ asthyātmane vṛṣaghnāya saṃdhyāyai namaḥ |

ṣaṃ majjātmane vṛṣāya prajñāyai namaḥ |

saṃ śukrātmane haṃsāya prabhāyai namaḥ |

haṃ prāṇātmane varāhāya niśāyai namaḥ |

laṃ śaktyātmane vimalā yā moghāyai namaḥ |

kṣaṃ krodhātmane narasiṃhāya vidyutāyai namaḥ |

iti mātṛkā sthāneṣu nyaset | 

śrīkāmaścet ||

śrī-aṃkeśavāyakīrtyai nama ityādi nyaset |

atha tattva nyāsaḥ |

maṃ namaḥ parāya jīvatattvātmane |

paṃ namaḥ parāya namastattvātmane |

iti trayaṃ hṛdaye |

naṃ namaḥ parāya śabdatattvātmane namaḥ |

iti śirasi |

dhaṃ namaḥ parāya sparśatattvātmane iti mukhe ||

daṃ namaḥ parāyarūpatattvātmane namaḥ |

iti hṛdaye ||

thaṃ namaḥ parāya rasatattvātmane namaḥ liṃge |

taṃ namaḥ parāya gaṃdhatattvātmane pādayoḥ |

ṇaṃ namaḥ parāya śrotratattvātmane śrotrayoḥ |

ḍhaṃ namaḥ parāya tvak tattvātmane namaḥ |

iti tvaci sarvāṅge ||

ḍaṃ namaḥ parāya dṛk tattvātmane namaḥ cakṣuṣoḥ |

ṭhaṃ namaḥ parāya jihvātattvātmano jihvāyāṃ | 

ṭaṃ namaḥ parāya nāsātattvātmane nāsāyām |

ñaṃ namaḥ parāya vāktattvātmane namaḥ vāci mukhe |

ṛṃ namaḥ parāya pāṇitattvātmane pāṇyo |

jaṃ namaḥ parāya pādatattvātmane namaḥ pādayoḥ |

chaṃ namaḥ parāya pāyutattvātmane namaḥ gude |

caṃ namaḥ parāya upasthatattvātmane upasthe ||

ṅaṃ namaḥ parāya ākāśatattvātmane śirasi |

ghaṃ namaḥ parāya vāyutattvātmane mukhe |

gaṃ namaḥ parāyastejastattvātmane hṛdi |

khaṃ namaḥ parāya jalatattvātmane namaḥ varaṇayoḥ |

gaṃ namaḥ parāya hṛtpuṇḍarīkatattvātmane namaḥ hṛdaye |

haṃ namaḥ parāya dvādaśakalāvyāptasūryamaṇḍalatattvātmane namaḥ |

saṃ namaḥ parāya ṣoḍaśakalāvyāpta candramaṇḍalatattvātmane namaḥ |

vaṃ namaḥ parāya daśakalāvyāptavahnimaṇḍalatattvātmane namaḥ |

130a) trayamuparyuparikrameṇa nyaset |

ṣaṃ namaḥ parāya parameṣṭitattvātmane vāsudevāya namaḥ śirasi |

yaṃ namaḥ parāya pustvatattvātmane nārāyaṇāya namaḥ |

asyo laṃ namaḥ parāya viśvatattvātmane pradyumnāya namaḥ hṛdi |

raṃ namaḥ parāya nivṛttitattvātmane aniruddhāya namaḥ liṃge |

laṃ namaḥ parāya sarvatatvātmane nārāyaṇyāya namaḥ caraṇayoḥ |

kṣauṃ namaḥ parāya kāṃpatattvātmane nṛsiṃhāya namaḥ sarvagātreṣu |

iti tattva nyāsaḥ ||

iti ketedhikṛto bhavati dhruvaṃ sakalavaiṣṇavamantra japādiṣu ||

tataḥ ṣoḍaśavāraṃ kāmavījaṃ japtvā dakṣiṇanāsāpuṭena vāyuṃ recayet |

134a) vauṣaḍiti tajjalaṃ vīkṣet |

137a) sugaṃdhijalena snānīyam ||

ramiti vahnivījena hastena spṛṣṭvā doṣān saṃdahya vāstena vidhāya vamiti 
amṛtavījena japtenāmṛtarasābhipūrṇaṃ vicintya mūlamantreṇāmṛtasvarūpaṃ 
cintayitvā spṛṣṭvā mūlamantramaṣṭadhā japtvā dhenumudrayāmṛtapūrṇaṃ 
vibhāvyagaṃdhapuṣpābhyāṃ naivedyāya nama ityabhyarcya ||

śrīkṛṣṇāya namaḥ |

gaṃdhapuṣpābhyāmbhyarcya śrīkṛṣṇamukhanirgataṃ tejo naivedye lagnamiti 
bhāvyamūlamūcārya śrīkṛṣṇāye daṃ naivedyaṃ namaḥ ||

iti naivedye jalaṃ dadyāt ||

tannaivedyaṃ hastābhyāṃ gṛhītvā nivedayāmi bhagavate juṣāṇedaṃ 
haverhareḥ |

ityuktvārghayet ||

vāmahastena vikacotpalasaṃnibhāṃ grāsamudrāṃ pradarśayet ||

amṛtopastaraṇamasi svāheti devamukhe jalaṃ datvā dakṣiṇahastasyāṅguṣṭhe na 
kaniṣṭhānāmikāṃ ca spṛśan prāṇāya svāhā ||

eva aṃguṣṭhe na tarjanīmadhyamāṃ ca spṛśan |

137b) apānāya |

evaṃ aṃguṣṭhena madhyamānāmikāṃ ca spṛśan samānāya ||

evaṃ aṃguṣṭhena tarjanīṃ madhyamānāmikāṃ ca spṛśan ||

udānāya svāhā ||

evaṃ sarvāṅgulībhirvyānāya svāhā ||

iti vadet ||

iti prāṇādīnāṃ paṃcamudrāḥ proktaḥ ||

ṭhaṃ voṃ namaḥ parāya paramātmane niruddhāya naivedyaṃ kalpayāmi nama iti 
mantraṃ paṭhan 

aṃguṣṭhābhyāmanāmikādvaya sparśanarūpā naivedya mudrāṃ pradarśayet ||

amṛtāpidhānamasi svāhā iti devamukhe jalaṃ datvā punarācamanīyaṃ datvā 
sakarpuraṃ tāṃvulaṃ dadyāt |

sādhyasādhanayormadhye devasya pūrvamiṣyate |

devasya pūrve ||

pūrvādicaturdikṣu asurāntakacakrāya astyrāya phaṭ |

iti paṃcāṅgāni pūjayet |

oṃ vasudevāya namaḥ |

tataḥ caturaśre devasya |

138a) oṃ vāyave namaḥ ||

indreśānayormadhye ||

naiṛtyavaruṇayormadhye |

punaḥ pūrvādikrameṇa tat samīpe pūjayet ||

oṃ śaktyai |

oṃ daṇḍāya ||

madhye śrīkṛṣṇāya namaḥ [naḥ] |

oṃ nārāyaṇāya ||

oṃ devakīnandanāya ||

oṃ yaduśreṣṭhāya namaḥ ||

oṃ vārṇeyāya namaḥ ||

oṃ asurabhārahāriṇe namaḥ ||

oṃ dharmasaṃsthāpakāya namaḥ |

iti saṃpūjya punarapi śrīkṛṣṇamarghādibhiḥ pūjayet ||

tatra dhūpe viśeṣaḥ |

ghaṇṭāya namaḥ |

iti puṣpeṇaghaṇṭāṃ saṃpūjya vāmahastena ghaṇṭāṃ vādayan |

dakṣiṇahastena dhūpaṃ gṛhītvā cintayet ||

evaṃ dakṣiṇahastena dīpaṃ gṛhītvā devasya cakṣurādipādaparyantaṃ darśayet 
||

tato mūlamūrccārya śrīkṛṣṇaṃ tarpyayāmīti śrīkṛṣṇasya mukhe jalena 
vāratrayaṃ tarpayet ||

tataḥ pānārthaṃ sugaṃdhijalaṃ dadyāt |

tato mṛtāpidhānamasi svāhā |

iti devasya mukhe jalagaṇḍūṣaṃ datvā dantaśodhanārthaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ 
hastaśodhanārthaṃ karpuracūrṇaṃ ācamanāthaṃ jalaṃ ca nivedayet |

tatonulepanaṃ sakarppuraṃ tāṃvulaṃ puṣpāṇi dadyāt ||

śrīkṛṣṇamukhānnirgataṃ tejaḥ punarapi mukhe praviṣṭamiti vibhāvayet ||

tataścāmareṇa saṃvīkṣya darpaṇaṃ darśayitvā nṛttagītavādyairdevaṃ 
santoṣya prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā mūlamaṣṭottarasahasraṃ śataṃ vājaptvā 
punaḥ prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā guhyāti guhyagoptātvaṃ gṛhāṇāsmatkṛtaṃ 
japam |

siddhirbhavatu me deva tvat prasādājjanārdana |

ityarghodakena devasya dakṣiṇahaste japaṃ samarpayet |

tato navīnanīradaśyāmamityādināstutvā pradakṣiṇī kṛtya 
daṇḍavadaṣṭāṅgapraṇāmaṃ kuryāt |

urasāśirasā dṛṣṭyā manasā vacasastathā ||

138b) padbhyāṃ karābhyāṃ jānubhyāṃ praṇāmoṣṭāṅga īritaḥ ||

atha ||


padbhyāṃ karābhyāṃ śirasā paṃcāṅganatayaḥ smṛtāḥ |

aṣṭāṅgā uttamaḥ proktāḥ paṃcāṅgā madhyamāḥ smṛtāḥ || 10-446 ||



na devaṃ pṛṣṭataḥ kṛtvā praṇāmaṃ kvacidācaret |

tata upaviśya jalagaṇḍūṣaṃ gṛhītvā ||

tataḥ pūrvaṃ prāṇavuddhirdehadharmādhikārataḥ |

jāgratsvapnasuṣuptyavasthā sumana sā vācākarmaṇā hastābhyāṃ padbhyāṃ 
mudareṇa śiśnā yat smṛtaṃ yaduktaṃ tat sarvaṃ brahmārpaṇaṃ bhavatu 
svāhā ||

māṃ madīyaṃ ca sakalaṃ haraye samarpaye ||

oṃ tat sadini brahmārpaṇamantreṇātmānaṃ devasya caraṇāṃvuje samarpayet ||

tataḥ saṃhāramudrayā nirmālyaṃ gṛhītvā ||

bhavan pūjito sikṣamasvetyuktvāghrāyajyotirmayaṃ śrīkṛṣṇaṃ vahan 
nāsāpuṭe na svahṛdaye samānīya nirmālyaṃ bhumau tyajet |

tato nirmālyāntaraṃ gṛhītvā viṣvaksenāya namaḥ ||

ityaiśānyāṃ nikṣipet |

naivedya śeṣaṃ gṛhītvā viṣvaksenāyedaṃ ucchiṣṭanaivedyaṃ namaḥ |

iti īśānyāṃ kṣipet ||

nirmālyaṃ śirasi dhṛtvā caraṇodakaṃ naivedyaṃ ca bhuktvā pītvā 
śrīkṛṣṇasvarūpaṃ svaṃvibhāvya yathāsukhaṃ tiṣṭhet |

puraścaraṇavidhau prajapedayutacatuṣkaṃ daśākṣaraṃ manuvaraṃ pṛthak 
kramaśaḥ |

aṣṭādaśākṣaraṃ ced ayutadvayamīritaṃ saṃkhyā ||

athānukramikā ||

tataḥ prathamataḥ prāṇāyāmaṃ murttipaṃjaranyāsaḥ |

karanyāsaḥ |

aṃganyāsaḥ |

ṛṣyādinyāsaḥ |

pīṭhanyāsaḥ |

dhyānaṃ |

mudrādarśanam |

arghapātrasthāpanam ||

tataḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ ||

tataḥ kāmagāyatrī japaḥ ||

mūlamantrāṣṭottaraśataṃ japaḥ |

praṇavapuṭitaṃ kāmavījasyāṣṭottaraśatajapaḥ |

tataḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ ||

devasya dakṣahaste japasamarpaṇaṃ |

mūrdhnigurum ||

139a) mūlādhāre gaṇeśaṃ namaskuryāt |

tato yogaḥ ityantaryāgaḥ samāptaḥ ||


tato vahiḥ pūjā yathā ||

devasyopari ekapuṣpāṃjali dānam |

aiśānyāṃ śivaṃ āgneyāṃ gaṇeśaṃ naiṛtyāṃ sūryaṃ vāyavyāṃ 
durgāṃ pūjayet |

tataḥ puna dhyātvā pratimāyāṃ devamāvāhayet |

ṣoḍaśopacāra pūjā ||

deve puṣpāṃjalitrayadānam ||

prāṇāyāmatrayam ||

mūlamantrajapaḥ ||

punaḥ prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā devasya dakṣahaste japasamarpaṇam ||

tataḥ pradakṣiṇam ||

daṃdapraṇāmaḥ ||

ityanukramikā ||

iti śrīgopārapūjāpaddhatiḥ samāptā ||

athā gopālakavacam ||


purastā uvāca 

bhagavan sarvadharmajña kavacaṃ yat prakāśitam |

trailokyamaṃgalaṃ nāma kṛpayā kathaya prabho || 10-447 ||

śrīsanatkumāra uvāca 

śṛṇuṃ vakṣyāmi vipendra kavacaṃ paramādbhutam |

nārāyaṇe na kathitaṃ kṛpayā brahmaṇe purā || 10-448 ||

brahmaṇā kathitaṃ mahyaṃ paraṃ snehād vadāmite |

ati guhyataraṃ tattvaṃ brahmamatraughavigraham || 10-449 ||

yaddhṛtvā paṭhanādbrahmā sṛṣṭivitanu te dhruvam |

yaddhṛtvā paṭhanāt yāti mahālakṣmīrjagattrayam || 10-450 ||

paṭhānādvāraṇā cchaṃbhuḥ saṃharttā savatatvavit |

trailokyajananī durgā mahiṣādi mahāsurān || 10-451 ||

varadṛptājjaghāṃ naiva paṭhanāddhāraṇāt tataḥ |

evaṃ indrādayaḥ sarve sarvaiśvaryamavāpnuyuḥ || 10-452 ||

śiṣyāya viṣṇabhaktāya sādhakāya prakāśayet |

śaṭhāya paraśiṣyāya datvā mṛtyumavāpnuyāt || 10-453 ||

trailokyamaṅgalasyāpi kavaca sā prajāpatiḥ |

ṛṣiḥ cchaṃdaśca gāyatrī devo nārāyaṇaḥ svayam || 10-454 ||

dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu viniyogaḥ prakīrttitaḥ |

oṃ praṇavo me śiraḥ pātu namo nārāyaṇāya ca || 10-455 ||

139b) bālaṃ pāyānnetrayugāṃ aṣṭārsmo bhuktimuktidaḥ |

klī/ pāyānnetrayugalaṃ caikārṇaḥ sarvamohanaḥ || 10-456 ||

klī/ kṛṣṇāya sadā ghrāṇaṃ (?) goviṃdāya ca jihvikām |

gopījanavallabhāya padaṃ svāhā nanaṃ mama ||| 10-457 ||

aṣṭādaśākṣaromantraḥ kaṇṭhaṃ pātu daśākṣaraḥ |

gopījanavallabhāya padaṃ svāhā bhujadvayam || 10-458 ||

klī/ glau/ klī/ śyāmalāṅgāya namaskaṃdhau daśākṣaraḥ |

klī/ kṛṣṇaḥ klī/ karaupāyān klī/ kṛṣṇāyāṅgajova tu || 10-459 ||

hṛdayaṃ śrībhuvaneśī klī/ kṛṣṇāya stanau mama |

gopālāyāgnijāyāyāṃ kukṣiyugmaṃ sadāva tu || 10-460 ||

klī/ kṛṣṇāya sadā ptātu pārśvayugmaṃ manuttamaḥ |

kṛṣṇagovindakau pātu smarādyau ḍe yuto manuḥ || 10-461 ||

aṣṭākṣaraḥ pātu nābhiṃ kṛṣṇe vidvākṣarova tu |

pṛṣṭhaṃ klī/ kṛṣṇakaṃ kālaṃ klī/ kṛṣṇāya dviṭhāntakaḥ || 10-462 ||

saktinī satataṃ pātu śrī/ hrī/ klī/ kṛṣṇaṭhadvayam |

ūrū saptākṣaraḥ pāyāt trayodaśakṣarova tu || 10-463 ||

śrī/ hrī/ klī/ padato gopījanavallabhapadaṃ mamaḥ |

bhāya svāheti pāyuṃ vai klī/ hrī/ śrī/ sadaśārsmakaḥ || 10-464 ||

jānunī ca sadā pātu hrī/ śrī/ klī/ ca daśākṣaraḥ |

trayodaśākṣaraḥ pātu jaṃghe cakrādyudāyudhaḥ || 10-465 ||

aṣṭādaśākṣaro hrī/ śrī/ pūrvako viṃśavarṇakaḥ |

sarvāṅga me sadā pātu dvārakānāyakovalī || 10-466 ||

namo bhagavate paścād vāsudevāya tat param |

tārādyo dvādaśārṇoyaṃ prācyāṃ māṃ sarvadāva tu || 10-467 ||

śrī klī/ hrī/ daśavarṇastu klī/ hrī/ śrī/ ṣoḍaśākṣaraḥ |

gadādyudāyudho viṣṇurmāmagnerdiśirakṣa tu || 10-468 ||

hrī/ śrī/ daśākṣaro mantro dakṣiṇe māṃ sadā va tu |

tārā namo bhagavate ruvibhaṇīvallabhāya ca || 10-469 ||

svāheti ṣodaśārṇoyaṃ naiṛtyāṃ diśi rakṣatu |

klī/ hṛṣike padaṃ śāya namo māṃ varuṇeva tu || 10-470 ||

aṣṭādaśārṇaḥ kāmānto vāyavye māṃ sadāva tu |

śrīkāmāya sakṛṣṇāya govindāya dviṭho manuḥ || 10-471 ||

dvādaśārṇātmako viṣṇuruttare māṃ sadāva tu |

140a) vāgbhavaṃ kāmakṛṣṇāya hrī/ govindāya tataḥ param || 10-472 ||

śrīgopījanavallabhāya ca svāhā caiva sova tu |

dvāviṃśatyakṣaro mantro māmaiśānyāṃ sadāva tu || 10-473 ||

kīlīyasya phaṇāmadhye divyanṛtyaṃ karoti tam |

namāmi devakīputraṃ nṛtyarājānamucyutam || 10-474 ||

dvāviṃśatyakṣaro mantropyato māṃ sarvato va tu |


kāmadevāya vidmahe puṣpavāṇāya dhīmahi tanno naṃgaḥ pracodayāt ṣā 
māṃ pātu cordhvataḥ ||


iti te kathitaṃ vipra brahmamantraughavigraham || 10-475 ||

trailokyamaṅgalaṃ nāma kavacaṃ brahmarūpiṇam |

brahmeśa pramukhādhiśa nārāyaṇamukhācchutaḥ || 10-476 ||

tava snehān mayākhyātaṃ pravaktavyaṃ na kasyacit |

guruṃ praṇamya vidhivat kavacaṃ prapaṭhen tataḥ || 10-477 ||

sakṛd vā triryathājñānaṃ sopi sarvatayo mayaḥ |

mantreṣu sakaleṣvevaṃ daiśiko nātra saṃśayaḥ || 10-478 ||

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ vāpi puraścaryavidhiḥ smṛtiḥ |

havanādīndaśāṃśena hutvā tat sādhayaṃddhruvam || 10-479 ||

yādicet siddhakavaco vibhureva bhavet svayam |

mantrasiddhirbhavet tasya puraścaryāṃ vidhānataḥ || 10-480 ||

spardhāmuddhūyasatataṃ lakṣmarvāṇī vaset tataḥ |

puṣpāṃjalyāṣṭakaṃ deva mūle naiva paṭhet sakṛt || 10-481 ||

daśavarṣasahasrāṇāṃ pūjāyāḥ phalamāpnuyāt |

bhūrjavilikhya guṭikāṃ svarṇasthāṃ dhārayed yadi || 10-482 ||

kaṇṭhe vo dakṣiṇe vāhau sopi viṣṇurna saṃśayaḥ |

aśvamedhasahasrāṇi vājapeya śatāni ca || 10-483 ||

mahādānāni yānye va prādakṣiṇyaṃ bhuvastathā |

kalāṃnārghanti tānyeva sakṛduccāraṇāt tataḥ || 10-484 ||

kavacasya prasādena jīvanmukto bhaven naraḥ |

trailokyaṃ kṣobhayatyeva trailokyavijayī bhavet || 10-485 ||

idaṃ kavacamajñātvā bhajed yaḥ puruṣottamam |

śatalakṣaprajaptopi na mantrāḥ siddhidāyakaḥ || 10-486 ||



iti śrīsanatkumārasaṃhitāyāṃ trailokyamaṅgalaṃ nāmakavacaṃ samāptam ||

atha gopālasahasranāmam ||


kailāsaśikhare ramye gaurī pṛcchati śaṅkaram |

brahmāṇḍākhilanāthastvaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakaḥ (1) || 10-487 ||

tvameva pūjyase lokairbrahmaviṣṇusurādibhiḥ |

140b) nityaṃ paṭhasi deveśa kasya stotraṃ maheśvaram (2) || 10-488 ||

āścaryamidamākhyātaṃ jāyate mayi śaṃkara |

tat prāṇeśa mahāprājña saṃśayaṃ cchindhiśaṃkaraḥ) || 10-489 ||

śrīmahādeva uvāca [uvā]

dhanyāsi kṛta puṇyāsi pārvatīprāṇavallabho |

rahasyeti rahasyaṃ ca yat pṛcchasi varānane (4) || 10-490 ||

strī svabhāvān mahādevi punastvaṃ paripṛcchasi |

gopanīyaṃ gopanīyaṃ gopanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ (5) || 10-491 ||

datte ca siddhirhāniḥ syāt tasmād yatnena gopayet |

idaṃ rahasyaṃ paramaṃ puruṣārthapradāyakam (6) || 10-492 ||

dhanaratnaughamāṇikya turaṃgasragajādikam |

dadāti smaraṇādeva mahāmokṣapradāyakam (7) || 10-493 ||

tat te haṃ sapravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāvahitā priye |

yosau niraṃjano devaḥ citsvarūpījanārdanaḥ (8) || 10-494 ||

saṃsārasāgarottīrṇa karaṇāya nṛṇāṃ sadā |

śrīgaṃgādravarūpeṇa trailokyaṃ vyāpyatiṣṭhati (9) || 10-495 ||

tato lokamahāmūḍhā viṣṇubhaktivivarjitāḥ |

niścayaṃ nadhigacchanti punarnārāyaṇo hariḥ (10) || 10-496 ||

niraṃjano nirākāro bhaktānāṃ prītikāmataḥ |

vṛndāvana vihārāya gopālaṃ rūpamudgina (11) || 10-497 ||

muralīvādanādhārī rādhāyai prītimāvahan |

aṃśaṃ śobhyaḥ samunmīlya pūrṇarūpakalāyutaḥ (12) || 10-498 ||

śrīkṛṣṇacandro bhagavānnandagopavarodyataḥ |

dharaṇī rūpiṇī mātṛ yaśodānandadāyinī (13) || 10-499 ||

dvābhyāṃ priyācito nātho devakyāṃ vasudevataḥ |

brahmaṇābhyarthito devo devairapi sureśvari (14) || 10-500 ||

jāto avanyāpinuto muralīvada recikā |

tayā sārdhaṃ vacaḥ kṛtvā tato jāto mahītale (15) || 10-501 ||

saṃsārasārasarvasvaṃ śyāmalaṃ maha ujjvalam |

eta jyotirahaṃ vandyaṃ cintayāmi sanātanam (16) || 10-502 ||

gauratejo vināyastu śyāmastejaḥ samarcayet |

japed vā dhyāyate vāpi sabhavet pātake śive (17) || 10-503 ||

sabrahmahā surāpī ca svarṇaste ye ca paṃcamaḥ |

etairdoṣairvilipyeta tejo bhedān maheśvari (18) || 10-504 ||

tasmā jyotirabhudvayā rādhāmādhavarūpakam |

141a) tasmād ahaṃ mahādevi gopāle naiva bhāṣitam (19) || 10-505 ||

durvāsaso munermohe kārttikyāṃ rāsamaṇḍale |

tataḥ pṛcchāvatīrādhā sandehe bhedamātmanaḥ (20) || 10-506 ||

niraṃjanāt samutpannaṃ mayādhītaṃ jaganmayi |

śrīkṛṣṇaṃ na tataḥ proktaṃ rādhāyai nāradāya ca (21) || 10-507 ||

tato nāradataḥ sarvai viralā vaiṣṇavāstathā |

kalaujānanti deveśi gopanīyaṃ prayatnataḥ || 10-508 ||

śaṭhāya kṛpaṇāyātha dāṃbhikāya sureśvari |

brahmahatyāmavāpnoti tasmād yatnena gopayet (23) || 10-509 ||


asya śrīgopālasahasranāmastotrasya nārada ṛṣiranuṣṭupacchandaḥ 
śrīgopāladevatā kāmo vījaṃ māyāśaktiḥ candraḥ kīlaka 
śrīkṛṣṇacandrabhaktijanahalaprāptaye śrīgopālasahasranāma pāṭhe viniyogaḥ ||

atha dhyānam ||


phullendīvarakāntiminduvadanaṃ varhāvataṃ sapriyaṃ 
śrīvatsāṅkamudārakaustubhadharaṃ pītāmvaraṃ sundaram |

gopīnāṃ nayanotpallārccitatanuṃ gogopasaṃghāvṛtaṃ

śrīgopālo mahīpālo vedavedāṅgapāragaḥ |

kṛṣṇakamalapatrākṣaḥ puṇḍarīkaḥ sanātanaḥ (25) || 10-511 ||

gopatirbhūpatiḥ śāstā praharttā viśvato mukhaḥ) |

ādikarttā mahākarttā mahākālaḥ pratāpavān (26) || 10-512 ||

jagajjivo jagaddhātā? jagadbharttā jagadvasuḥ |

sarvamaṅgaladātā ca sarvakāmapradāyakaḥ (31) || 10-517 ||

ādikarttāmahībharttā sarvanāgarasiṃdhujaḥ |

141b) kalaṃkarahitaścandro viṃvāsyo viṃvasattamaḥ |

candrāpatiścaṇḍapatiścaṇḍa kodaṇḍabhaṃjanaḥ |

ātmārāmojitakrodho moho mohāndhabhaṃjanaḥ |

vṛṣabhānabhavo bhāvīkāśyapiḥ karuṇānidhiḥ (41) || 10-527 ||

142a) aghāsuravināśī ca pūtanā mokṣadāyakaḥ (51) || 10-537 ||

kubjā vinodī bhagavān kaṃsamṛtyurmahāmakhī |

candrāvalīṃ patiḥ kūlaṃ (h) kaṃsī kaṃsavadhomaraḥ |

tathā tālavanoddeśī bhāṇḍīrāvanasaṃkahā |

tṛṇāvarttā kṛpākāri vṛṣabhāna sutāpatiḥ (60) || 10-546 ||

kāmaḥ kāmāribhaktoyaṃ purāṇaḥ puruṣaḥ kaviḥ |

142b) pāṃcajanyakaro rāmī trināmī varajojayaḥ (68) || 10-554 ||

phalgunaḥ phālgunasakhā virādhavadhakārakaḥ |

143a) rālaṃvī ca hayagrīvo vānarārirvanāśrayaḥ |

brahmaṇya devodī nānāmuddhārakaraṇakṣamaḥ (92) |

dyodivaudivaso dīṣyo bhavyo bhāvībhayāvahaḥ (100) |

murārirlokadharmajño jīvano jīvanāntaka || 10-588 ||

143b) yamo yamāriyamāno yāmī yāmavidhāyakaḥ (2) |

vaṃśulī pāṃśulī pāḍuḥ pāḍurarjunavallabhaḥ || 10-589 ||

sahasranāmam (1000) ||


iti śrīrādhikānātha sahasraṃ nāmakīrttitam || 10-593 ||

brahmahatyāṃ surāpānaṃ parastrīgamaṃ tathā (8) |

paradravyāpaharaṇaṃ paradveṣasamanvitam || 10-595 ||

sahasranāmapaṭhanāt sarvaṃ naśyati tatkṣaṇāt) || 10-596 ||

mahādāridryayukto yo vaiṣṇavo viṣṇubhaktimān (10) |

kārttikyāṃ yaḥ paṭhed rātrau śatamaṣṭottaraṃ paṭhet || 10-597 ||

pustakaṃ pūjayitvā ca naivedyādibhireva ca || 10-598 ||

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ devi paṭhennāmasahasrakam || 10-599 ||

caitraśukle ca kṛṣṇe ca kuhūsaṃkrānti vāsare (13) |

patitavyaṃ prayatnena trailokyaṃ mohayet kṣaṇāt || 10-600 ||

ravivāre ca śukre ca dvādaśyāṃ śrāddhavāsare || 10-601 ||

brāhmaṇaṃ pūjayitvā ca bhojayitvā vidhānataḥ |

paṭhennāmasahasraṃ ca tataḥ siddhiḥ prajāyate || 10-602 ||

mahāniśāyāṃ satataṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ yaḥ paṭhet sadā |

diśāntaragatā lakṣmīḥ samāyāti na saṃśayaḥ || 10-603 ||

trailokye ca mahādevyaḥ sundaryāḥ kāmamohitāḥ (17) |

mugdhāḥ svayaṃ samāyānti vaiṣṇavaṃ ca bhajantitāḥ || 10-604 ||

gurviṇī janayet putraṃ kanyā vindati satpatim || 10-605 ||

rājāśca vaśyatāṃ yāti kiṃ punarkṣudramānuṣaḥ (19) |

144a) sahasranāmaśravaṇāt paṭhanāt pūjanāt priye || 10-606 ||

dhāraṇāt sarvamāpnoti vaiṣṇavo nātra saṃśayaḥ (20) |

ya paṭhed vaiṣṇavo nityaṃ sayāti harimandiram || 10-608 ||

kṛṣṇe noktaṃ rādhikāyai mepi proktaṃ purāśive (22) |

mayā tvāye varāhohe proktametat sudurlabham (23) |

gopanīyaṃ prayatnena naprakāśaṃ kathaṃ ca na || 10-610 ||

nadātavyaṃ nadātavyaṃ nadātavyaṃ kadācana || 10-611 ||

deyaṃ śāntāpaśiṣyoya viṣṇubhaktiratāya ca (25) |

aśvamedhasahasrasya phalaṃ pāṭhe bhavedrume (26) |

mohanaṃ staṃbhanaṃ caiva māraṇoccāṭanādikam || 10-613 ||

yadyadvāṃcchati citte na tat tat prāpnoti vaiṣṇavaḥ (27) |

ekādaśyāṃ naraḥ snātvā sugaṃdhidravyatailakaiḥ || 10-614 ||

āhāraṃ brāhmaṇo datvā dakṣiṇāṃ tvaṇaṃ bhūṣaṇām (28) |

tata āraṃbhakarttāsau sarvaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ || 10-615 ||

śatāvartaṃ sahasraṃ ca yaḥ paṭhedvaiṣṇavogrataḥ (29) |

śrīvṛndāvanacakrasya prasādāt sarvamāpnuyāt || 10-616 ||

yadgehe pūstakaṃ devi pūjitā caiva tiṣṭhati (32) |

namānī na ca dūrbhikṣaṃ nopasargaṃ bhayaṃ kvacit || 10-617 ||

sarpādibhūtayakṣādyān vaśyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |

śrīgopālo mahādevi vaset tasya gṛhe tathā || 10-618 ||

yatra gehe sahasraṃ ca nāmnī tiṣṭhati pūjitam (33) |


iti śrīsammohanatantre pārvatī hara saṃvāde śrīgopālasahasranāmasamāptam ||

atha gopālastavaṃ likhyate ||


oṃ navīna nīrade śyāmaṃ nīlendīvaralocanam || 10-619 ||

146a) iti kīrītamantreṇa vyāpakaṃ kuryāt ||

iti mantraḥ ||


dviṭhaḥ svāhā |

148a) iti dhyānam ||


oṃ śyāmāṃ vicintrāśukaratnabhūṣāṃ padmāsanā tuṃgapayodharānatām |

11-28 ||

iti dharaṇīmantraḥ ||

namāmyahaṃ mayaṃ prokto mantrarājasuradrumaḥ || 11-32 ||


mantro yathā ||


oṃ ugravīraṃ mahāviṣṇu jvalantaṃ sarvato mukham [bhukham] |

nṛsiṃhaṃ bhīṣaṇaṃ bhadraṃ mṛtyumṛtyaṃ namāmyaham || 11-33 ||


asya mantrarājasya brahmā ṛṣiḥ anuṣṭupacchandaḥ narasiṃho devatā 
dhanādyabhīṣṭasiddhau viniyogaḥ ||


caturbhihṛdayaṃ proktaṃ śirastāvatbhirakṣaraiḥ |

śikhāṣṭabhiḥ samākhyātā ṣaḍbhiḥ kavacamīritam || 11-34 ||

tāvadbhirnayanaṃ proktamantrasyāt karaṇākṣaraiḥ |


karaṇākṣaraiścaturakṣaraiḥ |

ugraṃ vīraṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ |

mahāviṣṇuṃ śirase svāhā |

nṛsiṃhaṃ bhīṣaṇaṃ kavacāya hum |

bhadraṃ mṛtyumṛtyuṃ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ |

namāmyahamastrāya phaṭ |

ityaṅgamantraḥ |

atrāpi vimalotkarṣiṇī jñānā kriyā yogā prahvīsatyā īśānā 
anugrahāpīṭhaśaktayaḥ |

oṃ namo bhagavate viṣṇave sarvabhūtātmane vāsudevāya sarvātma 
saṃyogapadmapīṭhātmane namaḥ |

iti mantra || dhyānaṃ yathā ||


māṇikyādisamaprabhaṃ nijarucā santrastarakṣogaṇaṃ 

jānunyastakarāṃvujaṃ trinayanaṃ ratnāllasad bhūṣaṇam |

iti dhyātvā pūjayet ||


atroparihastayoḥ śaṃkhacakre adho hastau jānunyastau āvaraṇa pūjāyām |

148b) agnyādikoṇakesareṣu hṛdayaśiraḥ kavacāni agre netrāṇi dikṣvastram |

asya mantrasya ||

149b) trailokyaṃ rakṣa rakṣa hūṃ phaṭ |

150a) asya mantrasya śrīnārada ṛṣiḥ gāyatrī cchandaḥ śrīviṣṇurdevatā klī/

150b) caturlakṣaṃ japet ||

asya mantrasya nārāyaṇa ṛṣiḥ śrīgāyatrī cchandaḥ śrīmukuṃdo devatā

ai/ klī/ kṛṣṇāya govindāya śrī/ gopījanavallabhāya svāhā sauḥ ||

151a) daśākṣaraḥ sa evādosyāccecchaktiramānvitḥ |

151b) caturdaśasvaropetaḥ śuklastu sa ca mūrddhataḥ |

kecit tu tadūrdhvatasteṣāṃ varṇānāmupari prathamata ityāhuḥ |

mantro yathā ||

ai/ klī/ kṛṣṇāya hrī/ govindāya śrīgopījanavallabhāya svāhā sauḥ |

iti mantracaturdaśāsvaraḥ aukāraḥ śuklaḥ sakāraḥ |

asya mantrasya nārada [nāra] ṛṣiḥ gāyatrīcchandaḥ śrīkṛṣṇā devatā klīvījaṃ 
svāhāśaktiḥ kṛṣṇa prakṛtidūrgādhiṣṭhā tṛdevatā vidyādyabhīṣṭau 
viniyogaḥ ||

dhunyat sarvaṃ maṃgamantradhyāne vihāya viṃśatyarṇavadvidheyam ||

aṅgamantrām |

klī/ kṛṣṇāya hṛdayāya (1) ||

govindāya śirase svāhā |

gopījana naśikhāyai vauṣaṭ |

vallabhāya kavacāya hūm |

svāhā astrāya phaṭ |

dhyānam ||

vāmārdhvahaste ityādi ||


caturlakṣaṃ japen mantramimaṃ mantrīsusaṃyutaḥ |

palāśapuṣpaiḥ sucajaiścatvāriṃśat sahasrakam || 11-81 ||

hūhūyāt karmaṇānena manuḥ sidvā bhaved dhruvam |


iti śrī viṣṇumantraḥ ||

śrīkṛṣṇa jayakṛṣṇa jaya jaya kṛṣṇati bhabhadram ||

śrīśivāya nama |

atha śrīśivamantraḥ |

oṃ namaḥ śivāya |


sātaḥ satyāntasaṃyuktā vinduṃ bhūṣitamastakaḥ || 11-82 ||

prāsāhākhyo manuḥ prokto bhajatāṃ sarvasiddhida |


sāntāhakāraḥ satyānta aukāraḥ tadyuktaḥ vindūranusvāraḥ ||

tadyukta it mantro bhavati || hau/ ||


asya pūjāvidhiṃ vakṣye sarvakāryārtha siddhidām || 11-83 ||


tadyathā ||

brāhme muhurtte cāttha svaśirasi guruṃ dhyāyet |

prātaḥ śirasi śukle kṛ dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ gurum || 11-84 ||

varābhayakaraṃ śāntaṃ smaret tannāmapūrvakam |


iti dhyātvā mānasopacārairārādhya ca saskuryāt anenaṣu 
jñānatimirāndhasyati tataḥ sve hṛtyatmānaṃ tadrūpaṃ 
vibhāvyāvaśyakārthaṃ vahirgacchet ||

iti prātaḥ kṛtyam ||

tato madhyāhnakāle snānārthaṃ nadyādau gatvā yathā vidhisnātvā 
mantrasnānaṃ kuryāt ||

152a) yathā ||

ādau prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā kalāṅganyāsaṃ kṛtvā jale paṃcakoṇacakraṃ 
vilikhya aṃkuśamudrayā sūryamaṇḍalāt ||

gaṃge ca yamune caivetyanenatīrthamāvāhya mūlamantramaṣṭadhā japtvā 
śivarūpaṃ vibhāvya kuṃbhamudrayā svaśirasi saptavāraṃ jalāṃjaliṃ dadyāt 
||

tato vāratrayaṃ nimaṣṭhyotthāyācamya pūnarnimajjyatarpaṇaṃ ca kṛtvā 
jalādutthāya dhaute vāsasaparidhāyācamyamūlamaṃsudhni trivāraṃ jalaṃ 
datvā sadāśiva urvārāmīti triḥ santarpyagāyatrīṃ japtvā sūryārghaṃ datvā 
śivagāyatrīṃ yathāśakti japet ||

yāyatrī yathā ||

oṃ sadāśivāya vidmahe tryaṃvakāya dhīmahi tannaḥ śivaḥ pracodayāt ||

iti gāyatrī japtvā tāmrapātre jalaṃ gṛhītvā raktapuṣpaṃ ca datvā hastābhyāṃ 
gṛhītvā sūryāya śivatejase prakāśaśasihitāyaiṣogho namaḥ ||

ityarghandatvā namaskṛtya vā ghrā? gacchet ||

tataḥ pūjāgṛhadvāre gatvā hastapādau ca prakṣālya dvāradevatābhyo namaḥ |

iti dvāradevatāṃ saṃpūjya gṛhātyantare praviśya apasarpaṃ tu ye bhūtā iti 
paṭhitvākṣatānvikiret |

āsanayogya merupṛṣṭhaṛṣiḥ sutalaṃ cchandaḥ kūrmo devatā āsanayoge 
viniyogaḥ ||

pṛthvi tvayā dhṛtā loka iti paṭhitvā ||

ādhāraśakti kamalāsanāya nama ityāsanaṃ saṃsṛjya āsane upaviśya |

vāme gurubhyo namaḥ |

dakṣiṇe gaṇapataya namaḥ |

madhye sadāśivāya namaḥ |

tato strāya phaṭ |

iti gandhapuṣpābhyāṃ karau sammārjya tālatrayaṃ datvā digvandhanaṃ kuryāt 
||

tato bhūtaśuddhiṃ kuryāt |

sohamiti mantreṇa jīvānmānaṃ pradīpakalikākāra hṛdayāta sirasi 
sahasradalakamale niyojyayamiti vāyuvījena śarīraṃ śuṣkaṃ vicintya ramiti 
vahnivījena śarīraṃ dagdhvāyamiti vāyuvījena bhasma pronsārya ṭhamiti 
candravījenāmṛtavṛṣṭiṃ kṛtvā tayā śarīramutpādya lamiti pṛthivī vījena 
dṛḍhīkṛtya tadrūpamātmānaṃ cintayet |

152b) tato jīvātmakaṃ hṛdi samānayet ||

atha mātṛkānyāsaḥ ||

brahmā-ṛṣiḥ gāyatrīcchandaḥ sarasvatī devatā mātṛkā nyāse viniyogaḥ ||

a/ namo lalāṭe |

ā/ namo mukhavṛtte |

iṃ namo dakṣiṇacakṣuṣi |

ī/ namo vāmacakṣuṣi |

u/ namo dakṣakarṇe |

ū/ vāmakarṇe |

ṛ/ dakṣanāsāyām |

ḹ/ namo vāmagaṇḍe |

ai/ adhare |

o/ nama ūrdhvadante |

au/ nama dhadhodante |

aṃ nama mūrdhni |

dhaṃ aṃgulimūle |
