unmūle tve naiva bhairavyādīnāmapi tathā hvetām |

evaṃ vījaistribhiriti prapaṃcasārothe tatpara eva sādhāraṇa "abdāt [śakṣyat]? |

yatra vāmaṃ draped vījaṃ tatparaṃ vāgbhavādi paṃkama viśaṣāt |


vāme pāṇitale caikaṃ dakṣipāṇi tale tathā || 12-330 ||

ubhayoḥ saṃpuṭe vidyā trivījaṃ vinyase cheve | evam ||

cādyaṃ dvitīyaṃ karayostāstīyamubhayo nyaset || 12-331 ||

mūrddhādhāre hṛdi nyasya bhūyo vījatrayaṃ kramāt |


iti nivandhe vvayameva kramoddhavya |

ekavākyatā valān |

tathā pramāṇakaumudyām | 


vidyā nyāsaṃ tata kuryā tribhirvīja kramā chive || 12-332 ||

karayo vinyased ādau maṇivandhe tale grake |

dakṣe vāme ca vinyasya kakṣakūrparapāṇiṣu || 12-333 ||

punardakṣa tu vāme tu pādayośva tathā nyaset |

nādānte hṛdaye liṃge nyasa devi tataḥ param || 12-334 ||

ete saṃgeṣu deveśi saṃhārakramato nyaset |

vidyā sṛṣṭikrameṇaiva jānī hi parameśvari || 12-335 ||

navayonyātmake nyāsaṃ kuryād vījaistribhistataḥ |

karṇayościvuke bhūyo gaṇḍayorvadane punaḥ || 12-336 ||

netrayo nasi vinysed vāhuyugme hṛdaṃvuje |

tataḥ pūrparayoḥ kṣaujānunordhvajamūrdhani || 12-337 ||

pādayo guhyadeśe ca pārśvayo hṛ stanadvaye |

kaṭhe ca tava yonyākhyaṃ nyased vījatrayaṃ kramāt || 12-338 ||


vāhuyugā ityatra aṃsayorjaṭhare punariti nivedheṣu pāṭha |

202b) karṇādau vāmadakṣiṇakrameṇa nyāsa |

hasta nyāse tathā prāgdarśanāt |

vījaistribhirnyased vastatale ca śatryadakṣadvayaṃ dvitamaṣyubhayo tṛtīyamiti 
prapaṃceṣṭyabhidhāntan |

ekatra nirṇita iti nyāyāt |

kiṃ ca atrāpi nyāse savye kṣaṇayo lalāṭe tato vudhaḥ karṇayuge cakre ceti tripurā 
sārābhidhānāt |

savyapadasya dakṣepi nyāsektā tvāt |

dakṣādirevātra krama iti prāṃca |

atha yantroddhāra |


tato taryajanaṃ kṛtvā vāhyanhapūjāṃ samācaret |

tatra prāṅmukha śrāsīno yantroddhāraṃ prakalpayet || 12-339 ||

iśānādagni paryantaṃ ṛjurekhā samālikhet |

iśādagnestadagrābhyāṃ rekhe ākṛṣya daiśikaḥ || 12-340 ||

ekī kṛtya ca vāruṇyāṃ śaktirekhā parāśive |

trikoṇākāra phukapaṃ tu śaktidvayamidaṃ śive || 12-341 ||

pūrvaśakyagrabhāve tu maṇiyaṣṭivadālikhet |

rekhāntu parameśāni vāyurākṣasakoṇagāt || 12-342 ||

sandhibhedakrameṇaiva tayoḥ śaknostataḥ param |

rekhe ākṛṣya kośābhyāṃ udayāt pūrvagān kuru || 12-343 ||

vahnimaṇḍalametat syāt pūrvograṃ suravandite |

navayoniviśobhāḍhyaṃ caturaśraṃ likhe chive || 12-344 ||

caturdvāraviśobhāḍhyaṃ sarvānandakaraṃ sadā |

vilikhya pūjayed yantraṃ hamarūpyādi yadṛke || 12-345 ||

bhūmau candanapīṭheṣu kuṃkumāguruvandanai |

sindūrarajasā vāpi kastūrī ghusṛṇādibhiḥ || 12-346 ||

suvarṇaratnarekhe nyā kalpitaṃ mṛṇamayethavā |

vilikhya yantraṃ deveśi pūjā dravyāṇi sādhayet || 12-347 ||

atha pūjāṃ vidhiṃ vakṣye sarvatantreṣu gopitām |

brāhme muhūrtte cotthāya khaśirasi guruṃ dhyāyet || 12-348 ||

203a) prātaḥ śirasi śukrevje dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ gurum |

varābhayakaraṃ śāntaṃ smaretannāmapūrvakam || 12-349 ||


mānasaupacārairārādhya mūlavidyāṃ yathāśakti jāpatvā pūrvavat 
sandhyākarma kṛtvā krāmityaṃ kuśamudrayām |


vrahmāṇḍodaratīthānikaraiḥ sṛṣṭāniterake |

tena satye name deva tīrtha dehi divākaraḥ || 12-350 ||


oṃ gaṃge ca yamune cetyādi savibhṛmaṇḍalān tīrthamāvāhya trirnimakṣyotthāya 
mūlamantrajapan kuṃbhamudrayā śrītripurabhairavīmabhiṣaṃcāmi nama iti 
vāratrayaṃ mūrdhni datvācamet |


prathamakūṭamucāryātmatatvāya svāhā |

dvitiyakūṭamuccārya vidyātattvāya svāhā |

tṛtīyakūṭamucārya śivatattvāya svāhā |

ityācamya yathā sthānāni spṛṣṭvā nimajyotthāya mārjanaṃ kṛtvā saṃdhyā 
pṛ | tṛtarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā mūlamuccaran |

śrītripurabhairavī tarpayāmi svāhā |

iti triḥsantarpya jalādutthāya vāsasī paridhāyo paviśyācamya mūlavidyāṃ 
japan |

kuśāgreṇa vāratrayamūrdhni jalaṃ datvā gāyatrī japet |

japaṃ samāpyararaktaṃ candanākṣatajale kusumaistāmrapātramāpūrya 
hastābhyā gṛhitvā lalāṭaṃ spṛśan hrī/ hrī/ śaḥ kulamārtāḍabhairavāya 
prakāśaśaktisahitāya svāhā namaśca iti sūryāya namaskuryāt |

tasmin jale bhairavīcakraṃ dhyātvā hastābhyāṃ gṛhītvā lalāṭaṃ spṛśan |

ai/ tripurāyai vidmahe bhairavyai dhīmahi tanno devī pracodayāt (3) ||

iti japan ||

pūrvavadarghaṃ devyai dadyāt |

mantroddhāra māha |


203b) tripurā ca tathā ṅetaṃ vidmahe padamuddharet |

bhairavyaiśca tataścoktā dhīmahiti tato vadet || 12-351 ||

tato devī padaṃ coktā pracodayād udīrayet |

gāyatrīpurī khyātā vāgādyena samuddharet || 12-352 ||


tato maunī |

ayogā saṃbhāṣaṇa parāṅmukhaḥ vāḍhyāṃ gachet |

tāmrapātre sāmānyārghyaṃ saṃsthāpyārghajalena dvāraṃ prokṣya |

dvāradakṣiṇe gaṇeśāya namaḥ |

vāma kṣetrapālāya namaḥ |

tayoḥ pārśvayuge gaṃgāyai namaḥ |

yamunāyai namaḥ draparidvāra śrīyai namaḥ |

adho devyai namaḥ |

tatodgamaṃ kocya gṛhaṃ praviśyārghajale na prokṣya gṛhābhyantaram |

oṃ brahmeṇe namaḥ |

oṃ vāstu puruṣāya namaḥ |

iti saṃūjya bhūmau trikoṇaṃ vilikhya hrī/ iti pṛthivī saṃśodhya kaṃvalā yā 
sanamāstīryarghyajale na prokṣya dhyātvā paṭhet |

merupṛṣṭa ṛṣirityādi pṛthvitvayeti hrī/ mādhāraśakti kamalāsanāya namaḥ |

ityāsanaṃ pūjya prāṅmukha udaṅmukho cā upaviśet |

parāvījaṃ samucārya tataścādhārapūrakam |

śaktiti padamālikhya kamalāsanamālikhet |

oṃ taṃ namaḥ padaṃ coktā āsanasya manuḥ priye |

akṣatamādāya apasarpa tu iti paṭhitvākṣatānvikīrya astrāya phaṭ iti 
vāmapādedyate nabhau tālatrayaṃ ṇāntarikṣagān vā layā samudaṃ 
vinavaktraṃ ūrdhvaṃ ṭhaṣṭyā divyān iti trividhavighnān nirasya vāme gurubhyo 
namaḥ |

dakṣiṇe gaṇeśāya namaḥ |

madhya tripurabhairavyai namaḥ |

ityurdhordhvaṃ tālatrayaṃ pūrvoktavat |

204a) gandhapuṣpābhyāṃ karau saṃśodhya kṛtājali |

asya bhairavī mantrasya dakṣiṇāmūrtti ṛṣipaktiḥ chandaḥ tripurabhairavī devatā 
ādyaṃ vījaṃ atyaṃśaktiḥ rephaḥ kīlakaṃ vidyādibhīṣṭasiddhau viniyogaḥ |

śirasi dakṣiṇāmūti ṛṣaye namaḥ |

mukhe paktichandase |

hṛdi tripurabhairavyai naḥ |

kṛtāṃjali mama vidyādyabhīṣṭasiddhyarthe viniyogaḥ |

tato bhūtaśuddhiṃ kuryāt |

śarīrākārabhūtānāṃ bhūtānāṃ yad viśodhanam |

advaya brahmasaṃyogā bhūtaśuddhiriyaṃ matā |

tvāṅge uttānahastau kṛtvā sohamiti mantraṇa pradīpakalikākāraṃ hṛdayasthaṃ 
jīvātmānaṃ mūlādhārasthaṃ kuḍalinyā saha sirastha sahasradalakamala 
karṇikāntargata paramātmanilīnaṃ vibhāvya pṛthivī manmunau vahnau vahni 
vāyau samīraṇaṃ pravilāpya tathā kāśe ākāśaṃ prakṛtau tataḥ ||

aparaṃbrahmarūpāṃ tāṃ māyāśakti parātmani |

iti samastadehāmi prapaṃcaṃ paramātmani pravilāpya puruṣākāra bhāganādi 
bhava saṃcitaṃ brahmahatyā śiraskamityādi pūrvoktavan |

tata iḍā vaktre vā manasi dhūmravarṇaṃ yamiti vāyuvīja ṣoḍaśavāraṃ 
vāmanāsāpuṭa na vāyunādehamāpurya punarvyamiti catuḥ ṣaṣṭivāraṃ 
kuṃbhakurvan dehaṃ saṃśodhya punaryamiti dvātriṃśadvāraṃ 
dakṣanāṭāpuṭena vāyuṃ recaye |

tatoruṇa vāyu saṃvaddhe naramiti vahni vījena ṣoḍaśavāra japtena 
dakṣanāsāpuṭena dehamāpūye catuḥ ṣaṣṭivārajaptena kuṃbhakaṃ kurvan |

204b) tad vaśībhūtamiti vicintya raimiti dvātriṃśadvāraṃ japan vāmanāsayā 
bhasmanā saha vāyu recayet |

tata ṭha iti candravījaṃ prakṛtyātmakaṃ vāmanāsāpuṭena puruṣātmakalalāṭa 
candraṃ ṣoḍaśavāre japan na yet |

tato va miti pūrvoktavat |

lalāṭa candrā dādikṣāntamakṣaramayī vṛṣṭiṃ nipātya tayā devyāpāsana 
yogyaṃ sakalaṃ vapuḥ kurvan |

lamiti dvātriṃśadvāraṃ japan sakaśarīraṃ dṛḍhībhūtaṃ vicintya 
dakṣiṇāsāpuṭena vāyuṃ recayet |

punarutpādaye dehamiti ārabhya bhutaśuddhirihoditā |

iti yāvat pūrvoktakrameṇa nāsāpuṭo dhṛtvā vāyumavaṣṭatyahaṃ sa iti 
sohamavatara 2 prajvala 2 haṃsaṃ sohaṃ svāhā ||

tataḥ pṛthivyādīni tattvāni iti yathā |

āṃ sohaṃ mama prāṇā iti |

yathā | soha mama prāṇā iha prāṇā | iti |

ā sohaṃ mama jīvā iti |

evaṃ mama sarvendriyāṇi vāṅmana iti sukhaṃ tiṣṭaṃ tu svāhā |

iti vījaṃ nyaset |

prāṇapratiṣṭā mantro yathā |



mukhavṛttaṃ samuccārya haṃsaśca viparītataḥ |

uccarebhyadamīśāni vidyeya ākṣarī bhavet || 12-353 ||

prāṇapratiṣṭāmantro ye sarvakarmaṇo sādhayet |


mukhavṛttimākāra ||

savinduriti prāṃcaḥ tena āṃ viparitaṃ sohamityarthaḥ |

tena ā sohamiti mantra |

etat karaṇā śakrānāṃ etat pāṭhamātreṇaiva bhūtaśuddhiḥ syāt |

iti tataḥ prāṇāyāmaṃ kuryāt ||

mūlamantreṇa vā layā vā kuryāt |

yathā ||

mūlamantraṃ ṣoḍaśavāraṃ catuṣaṣṭivāraṃ pūrvoktakrameṇeti |

athāntarmātṛkā nyāsaḥ |


dvyaṣṭayatvāṃvuje kaṇṭhe kharān ṣoḍaśavinyaset || 12-354 ||

205a) dvādaśachada hṛtpadme kādīn dvādaśa vinyaset |

daśapatrāṃvuje nābhau uakārādītrase daśa || 12-355 ||

ṣaṭ ṣatne liṃgamadhyasthe vakārādī nyaset ṣaṭ |

ādhāre caturo varṇān ulādīse caturdale || 12-356 ||

bhakṣau bhumadhye gepadme dvidale vinyase chive |

ityantarmātṛkāṃ nyasya sarvāṅganyāsamācaret || 12-357 ||


atha mātṛkānyāsa |

mūrdhā ca mukhavṛttaṃ ca netrakaṇṭheṣu pārvati |

nāsāgaṇḍoṣṭadanteṣu mūrddhāsyeṣu pravinyaset || 12-358 ||

pāṇipādayugasyāntaḥ sandhyāgraṣu tathā śive |

pārśvadvaye pṛṣṭanābhijaṭhareṣu kramā nyaset || 12-359 ||

yādīn sadhā tu kān prāṇajīvabrahmāṇi tā set |

hṛdaye vāhumūle ca tathā paragale śive || 12-360 ||

kakṣāyāṃ hṛdaya prānte pāṇipādayuge tathā |

japarātalayordevi vyāpakaṃ cakraṃ mānyaset || 12-361 ||


vyācakamiti hṛdayamārabhya uttamāṅgavyāpakamityartha |

hṛdayaprānte yāṇyādi viśeṣaṇam |

pra vāhulābhāya evaṃ hṛdayāmārabhya pāṇipādayugate tataḥ |

iti tadeyamevārthaḥ | yathā ||

aṃ namo lalāṭe | iṃ cakṣuṣi | ī/ vāmacakṣuṣi | u/ dakṣakarṇe |

ū/ vāme | ṛ/ dakṣanāsāyām | ṝ/ vāmanāsāyām | ḷ/ dakṣagaṇḍe |

ḹ/ vāmagaṇḍe | ai/ oṣṭe | ai/ adhare | o/ ūrdhvadante | au/ adhodante |

aṃ mūrdhniḥ | a mukhe | kaṃ namo dakṣavāhu mūle | kha/ kūrpare |

ga/ maṇivandhe | gha/ magulimūle | ṅa/ maṃgulyagre | ca/ vāmavāhumūle |

cha/ kūrpare | ja/ maṇivandhe | jha/ aṃgulimūle | ña/ maṃgulyagre | 

ṭa/ dakṣorumūle |

205b) ṭhaṃ jānuni | ḍaṃ gulphe | ḍha/ ma/gulimūle | ṇa/ a/gulyagre | 

ta/ vāmorumūle | tha/ jānuni | da/ gulphe | dha/ aṃgulimūle | na/ maṅgulyagre | 

pa/ dakṣapāśve | pha/ namo vāmapārśve | va/ pṛṣṭe | bha/ udare | 

ya/ tvagāmini hṛdaya | ra/ asṛgātmani dakṣavāhumūle | 

la/ māṃsātmani ghāṭāyām | va/ medātmane vāmakumūle | 

śa/ asthātmane hṛdayādi | dakṣiṇākare | ṣa/ hṛadyādi vāmakare | 

sa/ śukrātmane hṛdayādi dakṣe pāde | ha/ namaḥ prāṇātmane hṛdayādi 
vāmapāde | 

la/ jīvātmane hṛdayādyudare | kṣa/ namaḥ paramātmane hṛdayādi mukhe | 

atha śrīkaṇṭhādi nyāsaḥ |

śrīvāsudeva ṛṣiḥ paktichanda īśāno devatā nyāsakarmaṇi viniyogaḥ |

hrā/ hṛdajaiḥ tribhirnyaset sthāneṣṭeṣu viśeṣata | mūlādhāre | 

ai/ ratyai namaḥ hṛdi | klī/ prītyaiṃ namaḥ | bhruvormadhyeḥ 

sau/ manodbhavāyai | amṛteśī ca yogeśī/ viśvayo/nikramādimā | 

vilomavījairvinyasya mūrttinyase samācaret | 

vilimavijairiti sau ādivāsā vījairityartha | 

mūlādhāre sau/ amṛteśyai namaḥ | hṛdi |

klī/ yogeśyai namaḥ | bhrūmadhye | 

ai/ viśvayonyai namaḥ |

tato mūrttinyāsaṃ kuryāt | 

mastaka | saharoṃ īśānāya namo manobhavasahitāya namaḥ | 

hṛdi | saharū/ aghorāya kumārakandarpasahitāya huhye |

sāhariṃ vāmadevāya manobhava sahitāya |

pādayo saharuṃ sadyojātāya kāmadeva sahitāya |

ūrdhvaprāgdakṣiṇodī paścimeṣu mukheṣu tāt |


206a) pravinyased yathā pūrvaṃ bhṛguvyomāgni saṃyute |

satyādi paṃcahrasvādyo vījameṣāṃ samīritam || 12-362 ||



bhṛguḥ sakāraḥ vyomahakāra | 

agnireḥpha satya okāraḥ tadādi caṃkam |

u e ū ī aṃkāra svarūpaṃ svasvavījeti saharau sahare saharūṃ sahariṃ saharam |

ūrddhamukhe maharoṃ īśānāya manobhava sahitāya namaḥ pūrvamukha saharai 
tat puruṣāya makaradhvajāya pūrvavat |

dakiṇamukhaṃ saharūṃ uttaramukhe sahariṃ paścimamukhe saharam |

iti dikcatupeṣu caturmukha nyāsaḥ |

athakaranyāsaḥ |

vad dīrghayukte nādyena vījenādga kriyā matā |

hsro/ aṃguṣṭābhyāṃ nama ārabhya |

hsrau/ hsrū/ hsrai/ hsraṃ(ḥ) karatalapṛṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ | tāvat | 

karanyāsa | evaṃ prakāreṇāṅganyāsaḥ |

hsro/ hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādi astrāya phaṭ |

tālatrayaṃ pūrvavadatvā daśadiśo vadhniyāt |

athaṃ paṃcavānanyāsaḥ |

paṃcavāṇā krameṇaiva karāṅguliṣu vi hṛdayāya namaḥ |

ityādi astrāya phaṭ |

tālatrayaṃ pūrvavadantvā daśadiśo vadhnīyāt |

atha paṃcavāṇa nyāsaḥ |


paṃcavāṇā krameṇaiva karāṅguliṣu vinyaset |

aṃguṣṭādikaniṣṭāntaṃ krameṇa parameśvarī || 12-363 ||


drā/ drī/ klī/ plu/ iti pro candra nasargavān bhavet |


paṃcavāṇā ime devi nāmāni śṛṇu pārvati |

kṣobhano drāvaṇo devi tathā karṣaṇasaṃjñaka || 12-364 ||

vaśyonmāda krameṇaiva kathitāni carānane |

kāmastatraiva vijñeyāsteṣāṃ vījāni saṃ śṛṇu || 12-365 ||

parā vījaṃ madhyavāṇaṃ vāgbhavaṃ parameśvari |

206b) turyavāṇaṃ tataścaiva strīvījaṃ tat kramāt priye || 12-366 ||

paṃcakāma ime devi nāmāni śṛṇu vallabhe |

kāmamanmathakaṃdarpa makaradhvajasaṃjñakaḥ || 12-367 ||

mīnaketurmaheśāni paṃcamaḥ samudīritaḥ |



catuḥ paraḥ sakāra drā/ drī/ klī/ plū/ sa iti paṃcavāṇā ||

hrī/ klī/ ai/ vlu/ strī/ iti paṃcakāmaḥ drā/ kṣobhanāya nama ityādi krameṇa 
aṃguṣṭādi nyaset |

krī/ kāmāya namaḥ ityādi krameṇaṃ aṃguṣṭādiṣu nyaset |

anantaraṃ mūrddhādiṣu ca |


paṃcavāṇāstato nyaya mantrī trailokyamohanāt || 12-368 ||

drāmaṃ dyādrāviśī mūrddhni drīmārdyāṃ kṣobhinī pade |

klī/ vaśīkaraṇaṃ vaktre guhya vlu/ vījapūrvikām || 12-369 ||

ākarṣa hṛdi punaḥ sargānto bhṛgusaṃyutaḥ |

saṃhohinikramā devī vāṇanyāso yamiritaḥ || 12-370 ||


nyāso yathā |

mūrddhni drāṃ drāvinyai | pādayo |

drī/ kṣobhinyai | mukhe |

vlu/ vaśīkariṇyai | guhye |

vlu/ ākarṣaṇyai namaḥ | hṛdi |

saḥ samobhitryai sargānto bhṛgu saṃyuta iti |

bhṛgusakāraḥ sargo visarga tena ma iti bhavati |

atha śubhagādi nyāsaḥ |

vālabrumayādi hṛdayāya nama ityādi nyasya dhāyet |



oṃ vaṃdhūkā kāṃcananibhaṃ rucirākṣamālāṃ 

pāśāṃkuśau ca varadanti javāhudaraiḥ |

vibhrāṇaṃ induśakalā bharaṇa trinetraṃ 

ardhāṃvikuśamaniśaṃ vapurāśrayām || 12-371 ||


iti dhyātvā mātṛkā sthāneṣu nyaset |

atha śrīkaṇṭhāya pūrṇādarpai namo lalāṭe |

ā/ anatāya virayābhyāṃ | mukhe |

i sukṣmā śātyai | ī trimūrtayalolākṣyai |

oṃ amareśvarāya vatulākṣyai |

207a) ū/ arghyeśāya dīrghaghoṇāyai |

ṛ/ bhārabhūtāya dīrghamukhyai |

ṝ/ atithīśāya yogamukhyai |

ḷ/ sthāṇukāya dīrghajihvāyai |

ḷ/ harāya kuṇḍodayai |

ai/ ṛṃ ṭheṃśāya ūrdhvakaśyai |

ai/ bhautikāya vikṛtamukhyai |

o/ sadyojātāya jvālāmukhyai |

au/ anugrahāye ulkāmukhai |

a/ akrūrāya śrīmukhyaiḥ |

a mahāsenāya vidyāyai namaḥ |

ka/ krodheśāya mahākālyai namaḥ |

kha/ caḍiśāya sarasvatyai |

ga/ paṃcāntakāyai gauryai namaḥ |

gha/ śivottamāya trailokyavidyāyai |

ṅa/ ekā mātrāya mantraśaktyai |

ca/ kūrmāya ātmaśaktyai |

cha/ ekanetrāya bhūtamātryai |

ja/ caturānanāya laṃvodaryai |

jha/ ajeśāya drāviṇyai |

ña/ sarveśāya nāgaryai |

ṭa/ someśāya khecaryai |

ṭha/ lā/galine maṃjaryai |

ḍa/ dārukāya rūpiṇyai |

ḍha/ ardhanārīśvarāya vīriṇyai |

ṇa/ umākāntāya kākodaryai |

ta/ āṣāḍhine pūtanāyai |

tha/ da/ḍite bhadrakālyai |

da/ adreśāya yoginyai |

dhamīneśāya vi/khinyai |

na/ meṣāya garjinyai |

pa/ lohitāya kālarātryai |

pha/ śikheśāya kubjinyai |

va/ chagalatuṃḍāya kapardinyai |

bha/ duruḍeśāya kapālinyai |

ma/ mahakāleśāya jayāyai |

ya/ tvāgātmane vāliśāya sumukhīśvayai |

ca/ amṛgātmane bhujaṃgeśāya revatyaiḥ |

la/ mā/sātmane pinākīśāya mādhavyai/ |

va/ medātmane khaḍgīśāya vāruṇyai |

śa/ āsthātmane va keśāya vāyavyaiḥ |

ṣaṃ majātmane śveteśāya rakṣo vidāriṇyaiḥ |

sa/ śukrātmane bhṛgvīśāya sahajāyaiḥ |

207b) ha/ prāṇātmane nakulīśāya mahālakṣmyai namaḥ |

la/ śaktyātmane śiveśāya vyāpinyai namaḥ |

kṣa/ paramātmane saṃvartteśāya mahāmāyāyai namaḥ |

atha mantra nyāsa |


nābherācaraṇaṃ nyasya galadānābhi cāparam |

mūrdhādi galaparyanta tryaṃkṣaraṃ vinyaset kramāt || 12-372 ||


iti vālāmantra nyāsa |

e veṃ bhairavī mantra pakṣepi |


nabherācaraṇaṃ nyasya vāgbhavaṃ mantravittamaḥ |

hṛdayānnābhiparyantaṃ kāmarājaṃ pravinyaset || 12-373 ||

mūrdhādi hṛdayāntaṃ ca śaktivīje pravinyaset | yathā |


nābherācaraṇaṃ yāvat |

hsrai namaḥ hṛdayādinābhiparyantam |

hasakalaṃrī namaḥ |

brahmarandhrādi hṛdayaṃ yāvat |

hsrau/ namaḥ | atha karanyāsa |


ṣāmapāṇitale caikaṃ dakṣapāṇitale tathā || 12-374 ||

ubhayoḥ sapuṭī vidyā strīvījaṃ vinyase chive |


bhairavī mantrapakṣe |


ādyaṃ dvitīyaṃ karayostārtīyamubhayornyaset || 12-375 ||


dakṣakrameṇa |

hsrai/ namo dakṣakare |

hasakalarīṃ vāmakare |

hsrau/ namaḥ karatalapṛṣṭayo |

mūrdhādhāre hṛdi nyasya bhūyo vījatrayaṃ kramāt | sirasi |

hsrai/ namaḥ hṛdi | hasarī/ namaḥ | mūlādhāre | 

hsrau/ namaḥ | atha navayo nyāsa |

dakṣakarṇai hsrai/ |

vāmakarṇe hasakalarīṃ namaḥ |

civuke | hsrau/ namaḥ | gaṇḍayo |

vadane ca | netrayo | nāsikāyām | 

a/sayo | jaṭhare | kūrpayoḥ | kukṣau | 

jānuno | liṅge | pādayoḥ | guhye pārśvayoḥ hṛdi | 

stanayoḥ | kaṇṭhe | iti sava | 


mūleratiṃ hṛdi prītiṃ bhruvormadhye manobhavām |

208a) vālāvīdhyavadanakaṇṭhikā kaṇṭhahṛtsucam || 12-376 ||

nābhyadhiṣṭānayoḥ paṃcatārādyāḥ śubhagādikāḥ |

nyastavyā vidhinā devyo mantriṇā śubhagā bhagāḥ || 12-377 ||

bhagasarpiṇātha parābhagamālinyanantaram |

anaṃgā raṃgakusumā bhūyāścanaṃgamekhalāḥ || 12-378 ||

anaṃgamadanāḥ sarvā madavibhramamantharā |

vākvāma vlu/ punaḥ strīsastārāḥ paṃcodinā amī || 12-379 ||

nyāsakuryād bhuṣaṣākhya tataḥ sādhakasattamaḥ |


nyāso yathā |

ai/ klī/ vlu/ strī/ saḥ śubhagāyai lalāṭe | bhramadhye |

ai/ klī/ vlu/ strī/ saḥ bhagāyai namo mukhe |

bhagasarpiṇo kaṇṭhamadhya 5 bhagamālinyai kaṇṭhe 5 anaṃgāyai hṛdi |

5 anaṃgakusumāyai nābhau | 5 anaṃgamekhalāyai svādhiṣṭāne |

liṃge 5 anaṃgamadanāyai namaḥ | iti bhūṣaṇa nyāsaḥ |



nyese chirasi bhālabhrukarṇākṣi yugale nasī || 12-380 ||

gaṇḍayo roṣṭayordantapaktyorāsya nyaset svarāt |

civuke thagale kaṇṭhe pārśvayā stanayugmake || 12-381 ||

donmūlayo kūpareyoḥ pāṇyostat pṛṣṭadeśayoḥ |

nābhau guhye punaścorvān jānuno jaṃghayostathā || 12-382 ||

sphicostatalayopaścā vāraṇāṅguṣṭayostathā |

kādirān nyased varṇāt sthāneṣveṣu samāhitaḥ || 12-383 ||

kācyāṃgraiveyake paścāt kaṭake hṛdi guhyake |

karṇā kuṇḍalayo mūlo vadanānuśakṣasārṣahau || 12-384 ||

aṣṭāvimāt pravinyasya devi daiśikasattamaḥ |

kāṃcyādipadaṃ tatra tsthānopalakṣakam || 12-385 ||



bhrukarṇādiṣu dvi dvi varṇanyāsa iti |

nyāso yathā |

śiraśi | aṃ namaḥ | 

lalāṭe ām | bhruyuge iṃ īm |

karṇayuge uṃ ūm | akṣiyuge ṛṃ ṝm |

208b) nāsāyā/ śṛ/ gaṇḍayo ḷ/ | oṣṭayoḥ ai/ au/ |

dantapaktyoḥ au am | mukhe a namaḥ | 

civuke kam | gale kha/ | kaṇṭhe ga/ | pārśvayoḥ gha/ ṅa/ |

stanayo ca/ cha/ | donmūle | ja/ jha/ namaḥ | pūrvarayoḥ ña/ ṭha/ | 

pāṇyau/(ḥ) ṭha/ ḍa/ | tat pṛṣṭayoḥ | nābhau tam | guhye tha/ | 

ūrvoḥ da/ dha/ | jātvoḥ na/ ya/ | jaṃghayoḥ pha/ va/ |

spitvā ūrumūlayo bha/ ma/ | tat talayoḥ pa/ ra/ | caraṇādguṣṭayo/ la/ va/ | 

kaṭyāṃ śa/ | grīvāyāṃ ṣa/ | kaṭakai valayasthāne sa/ | hṛdi ha/ namaḥ | 

guhya la/ karṇayo maulo kṣa/ namaḥ | jñānārṇave |


evaṃ nyasta śarirosau samāhita manastataḥ |

paramānanda sandoha mānandaścitayet parām || 12-386 ||


tato hṛtpadmamadhye divī dhyātvāt |

tato hṛtpadmamadhye devīṃ dhyāyet |



oṃ udyadbhānu sahasrākānti maruṇā kṣaumāṃ śiro mālikāṃ 

raktā liptapayodharāṃ japavaṃṭī vidyāmabhītivaram |

hastāvraidadhatīṃ trinetravilasad vatkrāravindaśriyaṃ 

devī vaddhahimāṃśu ratnamukuṭāṃ vande samaṃ dasmitām || 12-387 ||


iti dhyātvā mānasopacārairārādhya yonimudrāṃ vaddhādaya sarojamukha 
bhrumadhyalalāṭaṃ brahmarandhreṣu namaskuryāt | tadyathā |

yonyākhyā hṛdi vikhyātā trailokṣaṇā bhavet |


trailokya drāvaṇāṃ nāma mukhasthā parameśvarī |

bhrumadhye sthā maheśāni trailokyākarṣiṇī bhavet || 12-388 ||

lalāṭasthā maheśāni trailokya vaśāriṇī |

brahmarandhra sthitā saiva sarvāhlādakarī parā || 12-389 ||

paṃcamudrā mayī devi yonimudrāṃ pradarśayet |


iti namaskutyārghapātraṃ sthāpayet | yathā |

209a) svavāme | īkārargabham trikoṇaṃ vilikhya tadupari ṣaṭkoṇaṃ caturaśraṃ 
kṛtvā tadupari triparidikāmāropya |

maṃ vahnimaṇḍalāya dhūmrārcirādi daśakalātmane nama iti tripadi kāsaṃ 
pūjya phaḍiti śaṃkhaṃ prakṣālya tadupari saṃsthāpya aṃ sūryamaṇḍalāya 
tapinyādi dvādaśakalātmane pūrva va chaṃkhaṃ saṃpūjya 
śuddhatalamāpūrya dralomamaṇḍalāyāmṛtādi ṣoḍaśakalātmane nama iti 
saṃpūjya tajjale ṣaḍaṃganābhyarcya hasakṣamalavarayū/ ānandabhairavāya 
vauṣaṭ namaḥ |

evamānandabhairavyai vauṣaṭ namaḥ |

iti jalaṃ saṃpūjya jalaṃ spṛśan mūlamaṣṭadhā japtvā gandhavyākṣatā 
tripyāstreṇa sarakṣaṃ kavacenāvaguṇṭhya dhenumudrayāmṛtī kṛtya 
yonimudrāṃ pradarśya tasmin jale trikoṇaṃ vilikhya dikṣumapya paṃcaratnāni 
pūjayet |


gamau ca yavalaḥ paścād indrasthā kramata śive || 12-390 ||

vāmakarṇasamāyuktā nādavinduvibhūṣitā |

vījapaṃcakamadhye tu paṃcaratnāni sundari || 12-391 ||


glū/ gagaratnāya namaḥ |

slū/ martyaratnāya namaḥ |

plū/ svargaratnāya namaḥ |

vlū/ pātālaratnāya namaḥ |

mlū/ nāgaratnāya namaḥ |

gamu ceti takāramakārau yakāra vakāralakārāścetyartha |

indro lakāraḥ | vāmakarṇaḥ ūkāraḥ | 

iti paṃcaratnai saṃpūjyaḥ |

oṃ gaṃge ca yamune caityādinā savitṛmaṇḍalā tīrthamāvāhya tadakṣiṇe 
prokṣaśīpātra kiṃcijjalaṃ nikṣipya tenodakenātmānaṃ pūjyopakaraṇaṃ 
cābhyukṣya yantroparipīṭhān pūjayet |

karṇikāyām |

209b) maḍūkāya namaḥ |

kālāgnirudrāya namaḥ |

oṃ kachapāya namaḥ |

oṃ pṛthivyai namaḥ |

oṃ kṣīrasamudrāya namaḥ |

oṃ ratnadvīpāya namaḥ |

oṃ kadaṃvavanāya namaḥ |

oṃ varṇamayakalpavṛkṣāya namaḥ |

oṃ maṇimaṇḍapāya namaḥ |

oṃ ratnasiṃhāsanāya namaḥ |

āgneṣādi koṇeṣu |

oṃ dharmāya namaḥ |

oṃ jñānāya namaḥ |

oṃ vairāgyāya namaḥ |

ai/ śvaryāya namaḥ |

pūrvādi dikṣu |

adharmāya namaḥ |

ajñānāya namaḥ |

advairāgyāya namaḥ |

anaiśvaryāya namaḥ |

punakarṇikāyāṃ uparyuparikrameṇa |

aṃ sūryamaṇḍalāya dvādaśakalātmane namaḥ |

oṃ somamaṇḍalāya ṣoḍaśakalātmane namaḥ |

tataḥ keśareṣu navaśaktī pūjayet |

oṃ ichāyai namaḥ | jñānāyai namaḥ | kriyāyai namaḥ | kāminyai namaḥ |

kāmadāyinyai namaḥ | ratyai namaḥ | priyāyai namaḥ |

nandāyai namaḥ | madhye | manonmanyai namaḥ | ai/ parāyai namaḥ |

aparāyai namaḥ | parāparāyai namaḥ | prāgyaunyantarāle | śrīgurubhyo namaḥ | 

hsau/ sadāśiva mahāpretapadmāya nāya namaḥ | 

tato dinakarakiraṇāruṇa kusumāṃjalau vahannāsāpuṭena svahṛyā devī 
samānīya paṃcabhiḥ praṇavai mūrtti tasyāmāvāhya pujayet |

tato vākśakti kamalā hasakhaphre/ hasrau/ puna ityāsama mantrādau viśeṣaḥ |

oṃ ai/ hrī/ śrī/ hasakhaphre/ sau/ | iti mantra |

mūlamucārya śrīmahātripurabhairavīmamṛtacaitanya mūrtti kalpayāmi namaḥ |

iti mūrtti saṃkalpa yatre saṃsthāpya hastaṃ datvā prāṇapratiṣṭāṃ kuryāt | 
yathā |

śrā/ hrī/ kro/ ha/ saḥ |

210a) śrītripurabhairavyāḥ prāṇā iha prāṇā |

evaṃ vījam (5) ||

śrītripurabhairavyā jīva iha sthitā evam ||

sarvendriyāṇi vāṅmana ityādi ihāgatya sukhaciraṃ tiṣṭantu svāhā ||

iti prāṇapratiṣṭāpya āvāhanādi mudrā |

pradarśya ṣaḍaṃgaṃ vinyasya punarvyātvā ṣoḍaśopacāraiḥ pūjayet | yathā |

āsanaṃ svāganaṃ vāryamitirghyaṃ ṣoḍaśopacārakrameṇa idamārānam |

svāgatamiti kuśalapraṣṇam | idamarghyam | idaṃ pādyam |

ācamanīyam | eṣa madhuparkaḥ | idamācamanīyam |

idastonīyam | etāni paṭṭacastrāṇi |

ida svarṇābharaṇam | eṣa gadha | 

idaṃ puṣpam | eṣa dhūpadīpa | 

tato naivedyaṃ purataḥ samānīya astrāya phaṭ |

iti jalena prokṣya hūmiti vāmatarjnyā vaguṇṭhya ityādi idaṃ naivedyaṃ 
śrītripurabhairavyai namaḥ |

ityutsṛjya jalaṃ gṛhītvā amṛtopastaraṇamasi ityādi vāṇāya svāhetyidinā 
paṃcaprāṇāhutīrhutvā svīkāraṃ vibhāvyāmṛtapidhānamasi svāhā |

punarayośānandatvā iti etāni tāṃvalāniyuta |

puṣpāṃjali tradhaṇasaṃpūjya śrītripurabhairavāṃ taryamāsīti devyā mukhe 
trivāraṃ santarpa ṣaḍaṃgena pūjayet | yathā |

hastrāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ |

hasrīṃ śirase svāhā |

hasrūṃ śikhāyai vaṣaṭ |

hsrai kavacāya hu/ |

hasrau netratrayāya vauṣaṭ |

sahaḥ astrāya phatrinipūrvādidikṣusaṃpūjya tripurabhairavyai namaskṛtya 
śrāvaṇān pūjayet ||

yathāmadhye trikoṇe | 

aya koṇe | ai/ ratyai namaḥ |

dakṣiṇakoṇe |

ai/ prītyai namaḥ |

vāmakoṇe | ai/ manobhavāyai nama |

tatoṣṭayoniṣu |

aṣṭaśaktī pūyet |

210b) ai/ klī/ klu/ strī/ maśubhagāyai namaḥ (5) ||

bhagasarpiṇai namaḥ (5) ||

bhagamālinyai namaḥ (5) ||

anaṃgāyai namaḥ ||

anaṃgakusumāyai namaḥ (5) ||

anaṃgamekhalāyai namaḥ ||

anaṃgamadanāyai namaḥ ||

tataḥ kesareṣu ||

pūrvādikrameṇa brahmādiśaktīḥ pūjayet ||

brahmyai ityādi mahālakṣyai tāvat ||

namaḥ paṃcāgreṣu asitāṅgāya | rukhe ||

caṇḍāya krodhāya unmattāya kapāline ||

bhīṣaṇāya saṃhārāya tad vāhye ||

indrādīn pūjayet ||

tad vahisteṣāmastrī pūjayet ||

tataḥ puṣpāṃjali traveṇa devī saṃpūjya prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā gurubhyo 
namaskṛtya japet ||

jayaṃ samāpya punaḥ prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvārghodakena |


guhyāti guhyagoptri tvaṃ gṛhāṇāsmat kṛtaṃ jayī |

siddhirbhavatu [-ntu] me devi tvat prasādān maheśvarī || 12-392 ||


ityādi devyā vāmahaste tejomayajapaṃ samarpa parameśvaryā parigṛhītaṃ 
vibhāvya mūlena tāṃvūlaṃ nivedya punargandhādibhiḥ saṃpūjya 
yonimudrāṃ pradarśya daṇḍapaviśya tataḥ pūrvaṃ 
prāṇavuddhidehadharmādhikārato jāgratsvapnādi manasā vācādi yat bhyā 
sudareṇadi yat kṛtaṃ yaktamityādi tat sarvaṃ brahmārpaṇaṃ bhavā tu svāhā |

māṃ madīyaṃ ca sakalamityādi ityātmānaṃ samarpya namaskṛtya 
punarācamanīyam |

iti mūlamuccara bhagavati tripurabhairavi pūjitāsi kṣamasveti saṃhāramudrayā 
svahṛdi samānīya prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvātmānaṃ tad rupaṃ vibhāvya nirmālya 
puṣpaṃ gṛhitvā nirmālya vāsinyai namaḥ | iti aiśānyāṃ kṣipet |
tataścaraṇodakaṃ pitvā naivedyaṃ svīkṛtya yathā sukhaṃ viharet |

211a) iti śrītripurabhairavī pūjāpaddhatiḥ samāptaḥ ||

atha tripurabhairavī kavacam ||

oṃ nama tripurabhairavyaiḥ |


śrīpārvatyuvāca 

deva deva mahādeva sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ |

kṛpāṃ kuru jagannārtha dharmajñosi mahāmate |

sundari parapurā proktā vidyā tripurapūrikā |

tasyāstu kavacaṃ divyaṃ mahya kathaya tattvataḥ (2) || 12-393 ||

tasyāstad vacanaṃ śrūtvā jagādajagadvīśvara |

adbhutaṃ kavacaṃ devyā sundaryādi virūpi vai (3) || 12-394 ||

śrī-īśvara uvāca

kathayāmi mahāvidyā kavacaṃ sarvadurllabham |

śṛṇuṣva tva vidhinā śrūtvā gopyaṃ tvayāpi tam (14) || 12-395 ||

yasya prasādāt sakalaṃ vibharmi bhuvanatrayam |

yasyāḥ sarvaṃ samutpannaṃ yasyā madyāpi tiṣṭati || 12-396 ||

mātā pitā jaganmānyā jagadakṣā svarūpiṇi |

siddhidātrī ca siddhā * duṣṭaṃ jantuṣu * * * (6) || 12-397 ||

sarvabhūtahitakari sarvabhūtasvarūpiṇī |

kakāre pātu māṃ devī kāminī kāmadāyinī (7) || 12-398 ||

ai/kārī pātu māṃ devī mūlādhārasvarūpiṇī |

ikārāṃ paramāṃ devī rati sarvastuska pradā (8) || 12-399 ||

lakārī pātu māṃ devī indrāṇī varavallabhā |

hrī/kārī pātu māṃ devī sarvatra śaṃbhu sundari (9) || 12-400 ||

etairvarṇamahāmāyā śāṃbhavī pātu mastakam |

kakāre pātu māṃ devī śarvāṇī haragehini || 12-401 ||

makāre pātu māṃ devī sarvapāpapraṇāśini |

kakāre pātu māṃ devī kāmarūpadharā sadā || 12-402 ||

kakāre pātu māṃ devī śavarāri priyā sadā |

lakārī pātu māṃ devī dharādharaṇirūpa dhṛt (12) || 12-403 ||

hrī/kārī pātu māṃ devī śaṃkarārdhaśarīriṇī |

211b) etairvarṇai mahāmāyā kāmarāja priyā va tu || 12-404 ||

makārī pātu māṃ devī savitrī sarvatrakamalā svarūpiṇī (14) |

lakārī pātu māṃ devī lakṣmī sarvasulakṣaṇā || 12-405 ||

hrī/kārī pātu sarvatra devi tribhuvaneśvari (15) |

etairvarṇai mahāmāyā vā bhuśakti svarūpiṇī || 12-406 ||

vāgbhavaṃ mastakaṃ pātu vadanaṃ kāmarājikā (16) |

śaktisvarūpiṇī pātu hṛdayaṃ mātra siddhidā || 12-407 ||

sundarī sarvadā pātu sundarī  * pariratu (17) |

raktavarṇā sadā pātu sundarī sarvadāyi || 12-408 ||

nānālaṃkārasaṃyuktā sundī pātu sarvadā (18) |

sarvāṅgasundarī pātu sarvatra śivadāyinī || 12-409 ||

jagadā hlādajananī śaṃbhurūpā ca māṃ sadā (19) |

sarvamantramani pātu sarvasaubhāgya dāyinī || 12-410 ||

nānālaṃkārasaṃyuktā sundarī pātu sarvadā |

sarvalakṣmī mayi devī paramānanda dāyinī (20) || 12-411 ||

pātu māṃ sarvadā devī nānāśaṃkhanidhiḥ śivā |

tataḥ padmanidhirdevī śarvāṇī sarvadāyinī (21) || 12-412 ||

dakṣiṇīmūrtti mā tu ṛṣiḥ sarvatra mastake |

paktiḥ chandasvarūpā tu sarvākarṣaṇakāriṇī || 12-413 ||

sarvā hlādakarī pātu sarvasaṃkṣobhiṇī sadā (24) |

sarvasiddhi pradā pātu bhagāṃ māṃ sarvadā va tu (26) || 12-414 ||

māheśvarī sadā pātu kaumārī sarvadā va tu |

sarvā hlādanakārī māṃ pātu sarvavaśaṃ karī (27) || 12-415 ||

kṣemaṃ karī sadā pātu sarvāṅgasundarī sadā |

sarvāṅgayuvatiḥ pātu sarvasaubhāgya dāyinī (28) || 12-416 ||

vāgdevī sarvadā pātu vāṇinī sarvadā va tu |

vaśinī sarvadā pātu mahāsiddhi pradā sadā (29) || 12-417 ||

sarvavidrāviṇī pātu gaṇanāthaḥ sadā va tu |

212a) durgā devī sadā pātu vaṭukaḥ sarvadā va tu (30) || 12-418 ||

kṣetrapālaḥ sadā pātu pātu mādhāraśaktikā |

anantasarvadā pātu varāhaḥ sarvadā va tuḥ (31) || 12-419 ||

pṛthivī sarvadā pātu varṇasiṃhāsanaṃ tathā |

raktāmṛtaṃ ca samataṃtaṃ pātu māṃ sarvakālataḥ (32) || 12-420 ||

sudhārṇava sadā pātu kalpavṛkṣaḥ sadā va tu [ṭu] |

śvetachastraṃ sadā pātu ratnadīpaḥ sadā va tu (33) || 12-421 ||

naṃ sanodyānasatataṃ * * * sarvasiddhaye |

dikpālāḥ sarvadā pātu indrādyā sakalāstathā (34) || 12-422 ||

vāhanāni sadā pātu astrāṇi pātu sarvadā |

śastrāṇi sarvadā pātu yoginyaḥ pātu sarvadā (35) || 12-423 ||

siddhā pātu sadā devī sarvasiddhipradā va tu |

sarvāṃgasundarī devi sarvatrasarvadā va tu [ṭu] || 12-424 ||

ānandarūpiṇī devi citsvarūpā cidātmikā |

sarvāṅgasundari pātu sundarī bhava sundarī (37) || 12-425 ||

pṛghagdevālaye ghore saṃkaṭe durgame girau |

araṇyaprāntare vāpi pātu māṃ sundari śivo (38) || 12-426 ||

indrakavacamityuktaṃ mantroddhāraśca pārvati |

yaḥ paṭhet prayato bhūtvā trisandhyaṃ niyataḥ śuciḥ (39) || 12-427 ||

tasya sarvārthasiddhiḥ syād yan manasi vattate |

gorocanā kuṃkumena raktacandana kena vā (40) || 12-428 ||

svayaṃbhūḥ kusumai śuklai bhūmiputrai śanaiścare |

śmaśāne prāntare vāpi śūnyāgāre śivālaye (41) || 12-429 ||

saśaktyā guruṇā yantraṃ pūjayitvā kumārikā |

tanmanuṃ pūjayitvā ca gurupakti viśeṣata (42) || 12-430 ||

pūjayitvā yathāśaktyā stutvā va vidhinā munā |

dhārayed guruṇā yantraṃ pūjayitvā kumārikā (43) || 12-431 ||

tad yonau pūjayitvā ca gurupaktiṃ tathaiva ca |

devyai valaṃ nivedyātha naraṃ mārjāramūkaraiḥ (44) || 12-432 ||

212b) nakulairmahiṣairmeṣaiḥ pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ |

dhṛtvā suvarṇamadhyasthaṃ kaṭhe vā mastake bhuje (45) || 12-433 ||

sutithau śubhanakṣatre sūryasyodayane tathā |

dhārayitvā ca kavacaṃ sarvasiddhiṃ labhennaraḥ || 12-434 ||

kavacasya mahātmyaṃ nāṃ havarṣaśatairapi |

śaptomi tu mahesāni vaktuṃ tasya phalaṃ ca yat (47) || 12-435 ||

nāsti caurabhayaṃ tasya na tatra vyādhipīḍitam |

nadurbhikṣaya yaṃ tatra na cāpi pīḍanaṃ sadā (48) || 12-436 ||

vighnapraśamanaṃ sarva sarvavyādhi vināśanam |

sarvarakṣākaraṃ jantoścaturvarga phalapradam (49) || 12-437 ||

yatra tatra na vaktavyaṃ nadātavyaṃ kadācana |

mantra prāpya vidhānena pūjayet satataṃ sudhīḥ (50) || 12-438 ||

tatrāpi durllabhaṃ manya kavacaṃ deva rūpiṇi |

guro prasādamāsādya vidyā prāpya sugopitām (51) || 12-439 ||

tatrāpi kavacaṃ divyaṃ durllabhaṃ bhuvanatrayam |

ślokaṃ vāstave menaṃ vāyaḥ paṭhet prayataḥ śuciḥ (52) || 12-440 ||

tasya sarvārthasiddhi syā chaṃkareṇa prabhāṣitam |

gururdevo haraḥ sākṣāt patni tasya ca pārvatī (53) |

abhedena yajed yastu tasya siddhi na dūrata || 12-441 ||


iti viśvasāratantre tripurabhairavī kavacaṃ samāptam |


atha tripurabhairavī sahasranāmam ||


oṃ nama tripurabhairavyai ||

ahaṃ rudretyaṣṭamantrasya vāk staṃbhana ṛṣiranuṣṭupchandaḥ vāk staṃbhana 
devatā durgā prītaye viniyogaḥ ||


oṃ madhye sudhābdhi maṇimaṇḍapa madhyavedyāṃ 

siṃhāsanā parigatāṃ parigatāṃ paripītavarṇām |

pītāṃvarā bharaṇabhūṣaṇamālyaśobhāṃ 

devī bhajāmi dhṛta mudgaravairijihvām || 12-442 ||

abdhavakṣye maheśāni devyā nāmasahasrakam |

yat prasādān maheśāni sarvavargaphalaṃ labhet (1) || 12-443 ||

213a) sarvarogaprasamanaṃ sarvamṛtyu vināśanam |

sarvaśāntikarastotraṃ śakareṇa bhāṣitam || 12-444 ||

nātaparataṃ raṃ stotraṃ nātaḥ parataraṃ tapaḥ |

nātaḥ paratarā vidyā tīrthaṃ nātaḥ paraṃ param || 12-445 ||

yasyāṃ sarvasamutpunnaṃ yasyāmadyāpi tiṣṭati |

layameṣyati tat sarvaṃ layakāle maheśvari (4) || 12-446 ||


asya śrītripurabhairavī sahasranāma stotrasya bhagavān śivaṛṣi paktichanda ādyā 
śakti bhagavati tripurabhairavī devatā dharmārthakāmamokṣārthe viniyogaḥ ||


oṃ tripurā parameśāni yogasiddhi nivāsinī |

sarvamantramayi devī sarvaśakti samanvitaḥ (5) || 12-447 ||

sarvadhārayī devī sarvasaṃpat pradā śubhā |

yoginī yogamātā ca yogasiṃdhu pravarttinī (6) || 12-448 ||

yogi dhyeyā yogi mātā yogabhogavilāsini |

līlā helā tathā krīḍā kālarūpavarttinī (7) || 12-449 ||

kālarūpā kālarātri kālī kamalavāsini |

kamalā kāntirūpā ca kāmarājeśvarī kriyā (8) || 12-450 ||

kuhuḥ kapaṭaveśā ca kapaṭā kupaṭā kupaṭā kṛti |

kumudā carcitā kāntā kālarātriḥ kriyāspadaḥ (9) || 12-451 ||

ghorarāvā ghorarūpā ghorarāvā ghṛṇā pahā |

ghargharā ghariṇī cūrṇā ghoranāda priyā sadā (10) || 12-452 ||

ghorākārā ghoratarā gharmā gharmā pradā rati |

ghaṃṭā ghargharanādā ca ghaṇṭā nāda priyā sadā (11) || 12-453 ||

sūkṣmā sūkṣmatarā sthūlā ati sthūlā mahāmati |

kṣamā bhāmā tathā bhīmā bhīmanāda pravarttinī (12) || 12-454 ||

aparūpā bhayaharā bhayadā bhaya nāśini |

śmaśānavāsinī devī śmaśānālaya vāsinī (13) || 12-455 ||

śavāsanā śavavahā śavarehā śavā savā |

śavahārā kaṇṭhadeśe śavakaṃkadhāriṇī (14) || 12-456 ||

213b) ranturā sudatī satyā satyā sukeśavāśinī |

satyadehā satyahaṃrā satyavādavilāśini || 12-457 ||

satyadehā satyaharā satyavādavilāsinī (15) |

satyālayā satyasaṃgā satyā satyavihāriṇī || 12-458 ||

asaṃgā ca susaṃgā ca satsaṃgā saṃgamohinī (16) |

māyāpatirmahāvidyā mahāmadyavilāśini || 12-459 ||

madyahārā madhyapānā madyaniṃdakanāśini (17) |

galadudhiramālā ca sṛkvidvayanivāsinī || 12-460 ||

mahāmadya madāsaktā māṃsapāśāsrumāṃsagā (18) |

māṃsāharā māsadharā māṃsāśīnāṃ sabhakṣaṇā || 12-461 ||

raktapānā raktaruciraktāsyā raktalocanā (19) |

raktāhārā raktapriyā raktanindakanāśinī || 12-462 ||

raktapāṇī raktaruciraktadehā suraktakā (20) |

svayaṃbhū kuṇḍama svāhā svayaṃbhū kuṇḍamotsukā || 12-463 ||

svayaṃbhū kuṇḍamāhārā svayaṃbhū nidakāśakā (21) |

svayaṃbhū puṣpa saṃprītā svayaṃ prītā svayaṃbhū janmasaṃbhavā || 12-464 
||

svayaṃbhu puṣpakāhārā svayaṃbhū nidakātakā (22) |

kuṇḍagolavilāsā ca kuṇḍagolasadā rati || 12-465 ||

kuṇḍagola priyakarī kuṇḍagolasamudbhavā (23) |

śukrāsikā śukrakarā suśukrā caiva śukriṇī || 12-466 ||

śukra pūjana pūjyā ca śukranindaka nindakā (24) |

raktamālyā raktapuṣpā raktapuṣpakapuṣpikā || 12-467 ||

raktacandana siktāṅgī raktacandana vandakā (25) |

matsyā matsya priyā mātsye matsya bhakṣyā mahodayā || 12-468 ||

matsya kāmā matsya hārā matsya nindakanāśini (26) |

kekarākṣī tathā krūrā krūrasatva nivāsini || 12-469 ||

krūrāntā kuliśāṅgi ca vajjīdgīvajrasaṃbhavā (27) |

vajradehā vajradehā vajrakaṃkālahāriṇī || 12-470 ||

214a) nimvanābhibhītiharā bhayadā bhayahāriṇi (28) |

bhayapradā bhayamāṃ mā abhīmā bhīmanādini || 12-471 ||

sundarī śobhanā satyā kṣamā kṣamaṃ karī tathā (29) |

sindūrārcitasīmantā sindūrasadṛśākṛti || 12-472 ||

muktāraṃjitanāsā ca sunāsā snigdhanāsikā (30) |

kharvā laṃvodarī dīrghā dīrghorā mahatkavā || 12-473 ||

kuṭilā carcikā caṇḍi caṇḍanādāti caṇḍikā (31) |

aci caṇḍā mahācaṇḍā pracaṇḍā caṇḍaveginī || 12-474 ||

caṇḍālī caṇḍikā caṇḍaśabdarūpā mahācalā (32) |

caṃpā caṃpāvatī cokṣā tikṣṇātīkṣṇa priyā kṛti || 12-475 ||

jaladā yadā yogā jagadānandakāriṇī (33) |

jagaddhaṃdyā jaganmātā jagatī jagataḥ kṣamā || 12-476 ||

jayā jayā jayitrī ca jayadā jayinī tathā (34) |

jananī ca jagaddhātrī jayākhyā jayamātā ca jayā ca vijayā janā || 12-477 ||

ṣaḍginī ṣaḍgarūpā ca sukhaḍgāṣaḍgadhāriṇī (36) |

khaṃgarūpāṃ ṣaṃgakarā ṣaṃgāgī ṣaṃgavallabhā || 12-478 ||

ṣaṃgadā khaṃgamāyā ca ṣaginī ṣaṃgamaṇḍalī (37) |

śaṃkhinī vāpinī devī vajriṇī musulīmati || 12-479 ||

vāṇinī bhiṇḍipāli ca pāśe caivāṅkuśeśvarī (38) |

dhanuṣī ṭaṃkinī ca mikuntī ca karṇanāsikā || 12-480 ||

mudgarī halarūpāsra samudbhavā |

lakṣyā lakṣyaharā lākṣā sulābhā lābharūpiṇī (41) || 12-481 ||

dhanitrī dhanadā dhanyā dhanyarūpadharā dhanuḥ |

dhanvanī dhanuśavdā ca dhanurmānī dharīdharā (42) || 12-482 ||

dhīraśabdā dharā mānyā dhanāṅgī dhanavāsinī |

dhanadā dhanalābhā ca dhanalabhyā mahādhanu (43) || 12-483 ||

aśāntā śāntarūpā ca śvāsamārganivāsinī |

gaganāgaṇasevyā ca gaṇāṃ vā gaṇavallabhā (44) || 12-484 ||

214b) gaṇadā gaṇahā gaṇyā gatyā gamananandini |

gamya cāgamanā śā ca gadahā gadavardhinī (45) || 12-485 ||

priyahā priyabhavyā ca priyā premā priyā tanuḥ |

priyadā priyabhāvyā ca priyasya bhavane sthitā (46) || 12-486 ||

musthirā sthirarūpā ca sthiradāsthairyavarddhinī |

caṃcalā capalā cālā capalāṅganivāsini (47) || 12-487 ||

gaurī kārī tathā chinnā māyāmyā stutaṃ prabhā |

sundari tripurā bhavyā tripureśvara vāsini (48) || 12-488 ||

tripurā trāsanī devī tripurā prāṇahāriṇī |

bhairavī bhairavastā ca bhairasya priyā tanuḥ (49) || 12-489 ||

bhairavādgī bhayakarī bhairavī priya vallabhā |

kāladā kalarātri ca bhadrakātyāyanī kriyā (50) || 12-490 ||

krayadā krayahā krevyā krayaprāṇakriyā tathā |

klī/ kālī kamalā lakṣmī śaktisvāhā vibhuprabhuḥ (51) || 12-491 ||

prakṛtiḥ pruṣaścaiva puṃnāmnī puruṣākṛti |

puruṣāṅgo puruṣī ca tathā prakṛti rūpadhṛt (52) || 12-492 ||

paramaḥ puruṣaścaiva māyānārāyaṇī mati |

brāhmī māheśvarī caiva kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā (53) || 12-493 ||

vārāhī caiva cāmuṇḍā rudrāṇī haravallabhāḥ |

kauveri śāṃbhavī kṛṣṇā kātyāyanyapi pūjanā (54) || 12-494 ||

rākṣasī ḍākinī citro vicitrā citkramā tathā |

haṃsinī rākinī tīkṣṇā gāṃdhā vigandhavāhanā (55) || 12-495 ||

kekarī koṭarākṣī ca nirmo sā ca kṣamāṃsikā |

lalajihvā sujihvā ca vadanāvanadāyinī (56) || 12-496 ||

candrā candraprabhā cāṇḍī candrakātiṣu tatparā |

amṛtā pādanā pūṣā tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭiratirdhṛtiḥ (57) || 12-497 ||

śaśani caṇḍikā kānti jyotsnā śrīḥ prītiraṃgadā |

pūrṇā pūrṇāmṛtā kalpalatikā [+kalpalatikā] kalpadānahā (58) || 12-498 ||

215a) svākalpākalpahastā ca kalpakṣayakarī ta tu |

kalpākhyā kalpabhavyā ca kalpavandakavandakā (59) || 12-499 ||

śucimukhī pretamukhī ulkāmukhī mahāmukhī |

sumukhyūlūkavadanā kākāsyā vikaṭānanā (60) || 12-500 ||

kṛkilā sā ca sapāsyārtkuṭalī śarabhānanā |

nānāmukhī nānarūpadhāriṇī dharaṇīdharā (61) || 12-501 ||

viśvavandyā viśvamātā viśvākhyā viśvabhāvini |

sūryā sūryaprabhā śobhā sūryamaṇḍalasaṃsthitā (62) || 12-502 ||

sūryakāntiḥ sūryakalā sūryākhyā sūryabhāvanā |

naṣinī tāpinī dhrūmrā marīcirjvālinī ruci (63) || 12-503 ||

suṣumnā bhogadā viśvā vodhinī dhāriṇī kṣamā |

yugadā yugahā yogyā yogṭhādā yogyavarddhinī | 504 ||

vahnimaṇḍalasaṃsthā ca vahnimaṇḍalasadmakā |

vahnipriyā vahnivagā vapnkāhārasurakriyākā (65) || 12-505 ||

vahnā tejā vahnirāgā vahnidā vahnināśini |

vahnipriyā vahnikarā kalāvahnau sthitā sadā (66) || 12-506 ||

dhrumrārci ūṣmā jvālinī jvālinī visphuliṅginī |

svaḥ strīsvarūpā kapilā havyakavyavahā tathā (67) || 12-507 ||

nānātejasvinī devī paraṃbrahmakuṭuṃvinī |

jyotirbrahmamayī devī parabrahmasvarūpiṇī (68) || 12-508 ||

paramātmā parā puṇyā puṇyadā puṇyavarddhinī |

puṇyahā puṇyanāmnī ca puṇyavandhu priyā tanuḥ (69) || 12-509 ||

puṇyanāmnī puṇyamānyā puruṣāṅgavivarddhinī |

puṇyadehā puṇyakarī puṇyanindakanindakā (70) || 12-510 ||

puṇyakālaharā puṇyā supuṇyā puṇyamālikā |

puṇyaśīlā puṇyakīliḥ puṇyanāma samāparā (71) || 12-511 ||

puṇyasevā puṇyasūkṣmā purāṇapuravallabhā |

215b) puruṣā puruṣaprāṇā prasaṣānma svarūpiṇī (72) || 12-512 ||

puruṣāṅgī puruṣā ca puruṣasya priyā tanuḥ |

suṣumṇā puṣpakaprāṇā puṣpahā puṣpavallabhā (73) || 12-513 ||

puṣpapriyā puṣpahārā puṣpanandakanandakā |

puṣpaḍhyā puṣpamālyā ca puṣpanindakanāśinī (74) || 12-514 ||

gāndhārī gandhapūjyā va sugandhā premagandhikā |

durgandhā pūtigandhā ca sugandhāti sugandhikā (85) || 12-515 ||

mahāgandhā sugandhārhā gandhanāśakanāśini |

padmā padmāvatī premā padmagandhasugandhikā (86) || 12-516 ||

padmālayā padmakarā padmavāhana vāhanā |

padmasthā padmavaktrā ca padmakrīḍā suvahnikā (87) || 12-517 ||

padmāntakā padmavahā padmaprāsādaśaṃkiri |

padmanindakanindā ca padmasantoṣavāhanā (88) || 12-518 ||

raktotpaladharā devī raktotpala priyā sadā |

raktotpalasugandhā ca raktotpalanivāsinī (89) || 12-519 ||

raktotpalagṛhā rāmā raktotpalamanoharā |

suraktotpalanetrā ca suraktotpalarūpadhṛk (90) || 12-520 ||

nārī nārāyaṇīśānā nārāyaṇagṛhapriyā |

nārāyaṇasya dehasthā nārāyaṇamanoharā (91) || 12-521 ||

nārāyaṇasya dehasthā nārāyaṇanamaskṛtā |

nārāyaṇādga saṃbhūtā nārāyaṇapriyā tanuḥ (92) || 12-522 ||

vaiṣṇavī viṣṇupūjyā ca vaiṣṇavāṅganivāsinī |

viṣṇu pūjakapūjyā ca vaiṣṇavī saṃsthitā tanuḥ (93) || 12-523 ||

hara pūjyā haraśreṣṭā harasya vallabhā kṣayā |

saṃhāre hara dehasthā harapūjana tatparā (94) || 12-524 ||

haradehasamudbhūtā harāṅgavāsinī dahuḥ |

harapūjaka pūjyā ca haranandana tatparā (95) || 12-525 ||

haradehasamārūḍhā harakrīḍā sadā ratiḥ |

susaṃgasaṃgarahitā asaṃgasaṃganāśini (96) || 12-526 ||

nirjanā vijayā durgā durgadeha vivardhinī (97) |

216a) durgadehāntakā durmarūpiṇī durgavāriṇī || 12-527 ||

pretapriyā pretakarā pretadehasamudbhavā (98) |

prītāṅgavāsinī prītā pretadehavimardakā || 12-528 ||

ḍākinī yoginī devī kālarātri priyā sadā (99) |

kālarātriharā kālā kṛṣṇadehā mahātapu || 12-529 ||

kṛśāṅgī kuliśāṅgī ca vajrāṅgī vajrarūpadhṛt (100) |

nānādehadharā dhanyā ṣaṭcakrakramavāsini || 12-530 ||

mūlādhāranivāsā ca mūlādhāra sthitā sadā (101) |

vāyūrūpā mahāmāyā vāyumārganivāsinī || 12-531 ||

vāyuyuktā vāyukarā vāyupūrakapūrakā (102) |

vāyurūpadharā devī suṣumnā mārgagāmini || 12-532 ||

dehasthā deharūpā ca dehadheyā sudehakā (103) |

nāḍīrūpā ca nāḍīsthāna vīnanalinānaṇā || 12-533 ||

iḍā ca piṅgalā caiva suṣumnā ca sarasvatī (104) |

sadāśivapriyakarī mūlaprakṛti rūpadhṛk || 12-534 ||

amṛteśī maheśāni śṛṅgārāṅganivāsini (105) |

utpattisthtisaṃhartī pralayā padavāsinī || 12-535 ||

mahāpralaya yuktā ca sṛṣṭisaṃhārakāriṇī (106) |

svadhā svāhā havyavahā havyā havya priyā sadā || 12-536 ||

havasthā havyabhakṣyā ca havyadehasamudbhavā (107) |

havyakrīḍā kāmadhenu svarūpā rūpasaṃbhavā || 12-537 ||

surabhinandinī puṇyā yajñāṅgī yajñavallabhā (108) |

yajñasthā yajñadehā ca yoginī yonivāhini || 12-538 ||

ayonijā satī satyā asanī kulaṭā tanu (109) |

ahalyā gomatī gamyā videhanāśini || 12-539 ||

gandhārī draupadī dūti śivādūti śivapriyā (110) |

tripureśī paṃcadaśī paurṇamāsī caturdaśī || 12-540 ||

paṃcamī ca tathā ṣaṣṭi navamī cāṣṭamī tathā (111) |

ekādaśī dvādaśī ca vāyurūpā bhayapradā || 12-541 ||

saṃkrānti māṃsarūpā ca ṛturūpā ṛtupriyā (112) |

216b) paramātmādi rūpā ca ghaṭikā daṇḍarūpadhṛk || 12-542 ||

yāmarūpā mahākālī sandhyā susaṃganāśinī (113) |

triyāmāsāmarūpā ca yamakanyā yamī yamā || 12-543 ||

mātaṃgīlarūpā ca kulīnā kulanāśini (114) |

kulakāntā kṛśākumbhā kumbhākumbhadehavivardhakā || 12-544 ||

tvaritā kulavṛddhā ca saturā aruṇaprabhā (115) |

nadī sāgaragāśāntiḥ śāntirūpā suśāntikā || 12-545 ||

āśā tṛṣṇā kṣudhā kṣobhā kṣobharūpanivāsini (116) |

nadīśā gṛhaśāntiśca śruti smṛti dharā na hi || 12-546 ||

divārātriḥ paṃcabhūtadehā caiva sudehakā (117) |

kuru kulyā chinnamastā nāgayajño pavītinī || 12-547 ||

varṇinī ḍāginiśaktiḥ kuru kuṣṇyā sukṛllikā (118) |

aparājitā ca pratyagī rajitā jayanāśani || 12-548 ||

jayā ca vijayā caiva kauravyaḥ parikīrttitā (122) |


sahasranāma 1000 aṣṭādhikasahasraṃ tu devyā nāmānukīrttanam |


mahāpātakayuktopi mucyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ (123) || 12-549 ||

brahmahatyā surāpānaṃsteyaṃ gurvaṃganāṅgama |

mahāpātaka koṭi ca tathaiva copapātakam (124) || 12-550 ||

gale dhṛte na stotreṇa sarvaṃ naśyati tatkṣaṇāt |

sarvaṃ vā ślokamekaṃ vā prapaṭhatvā smarannapi (125) || 12-551 ||

paṭhed vā pāṭhayed vāpi sadyo mucyate vandhanāt |

rājadvāre bhaye durge saṃkaṭe giridurgama (126) || 12-552 ||

prātare parvate caiva naukāyāṃ vā maheśvarī |

vahnidurgo bhayaṃ prāpte siṃhavyāghrabhayākule (127) || 12-553 ||

prapaṭhan nā smaran martya sarvasaṃkaṭanāśana |

aputro labhate putraṃ daridro dhanamāpnuyāt (128) || 12-554 ||

sarvaśāstraparo vipraḥ sarvayajñaphalaṃ labhet |

vādī mūko bave chreṣṭā rājā ca sevakā yate (129) || 12-555 ||

217a) golocanā kuṃkumena likhe stotramananyadhī |

guruṇā vaiṣṇavāpi sarvasiddhi phalaṃ labhet (130) || 12-556 ||

dhṛtvā suvarṇamadhyasthaṃ sarvakāmaṃ tato labhat |

striyā vāmabhuje dhāryaṃ puṃsāṃ vāmakare tathā (131) || 12-557 ||

ādityamaṅgaladine guro vāpi maheśvarī |

śanaiścara dine vāpi sarvasiddhi phalaṃ bhavet (132) || 12-558 ||

prātare va śmaśāne vā niśāyāmardharātrake |

śūnyā vāse gṛhe vāpi likhed yatnena daiśikaḥ (133) || 12-559 ||

rajasvalā bhaya dṛṣṭvā tat spṛṣṭvātha likhet sadā |

svayabhūḥ kusumaiḥ śukraḥ sugandhigandhacandanai (134) || 12-560 ||

mṛganābhiraśākteśca likhitvā yatnapūrvakam |

likhitvā caina māpnoti dhanaiśvaryamananyadhī (135) || 12-561 ||

kumāryaḥ pūjayitvā ca nāryaścaiva viśeṣataḥ |

pūjayitvā sukusumairgandhacandanavastrakai (136) || 12-562 ||

sindūrairaktakusumaiḥ pūjayed bhaktiyogata |

aṭhyadevyaḥ kumāryā vā daśamyaṃ taṃ prapūjayet (137) || 12-563 ||

sarvābhīṣṭaphalaṃ sopi labhate tatkṣaṇādapi |

nātrasiddhādya pekṣāsti navamittrāridūṣaṇam (138) || 12-564 ||

navā prayāsavāhulyaṃ kāyaklervaliṃ dadyā divā niśam (140) || 12-565 ||

pūjayet pāvatiṃ devī bhairavī tripurānvitām |

brāhmaṇān bhojayannityaṃ daśakaṃ dvādaśaṃ tathā (141) || 12-566 ||

māsamekaṃ paṭhed yastu trisaṃdhyaṃ vivināmunā |

aputro labhate putramadhano dhanavān bhavet (142) || 12-567 ||

māsadvaye na vidhinā tathā māsatraya na ca |

kṛtīrājyamavāpnoti tāthā māsacatuṣṭayaiḥ (143) || 12-568 ||

dīrgharogīt pramucyata paṃcamekavirāṭ bhavet |

sarveśvaryaṃ labhe dīno māsaṣaṣṭa tathaiva ca (144) || 12-569 ||

217b) saptamekhe ca ratvaṃ ca aṣṭame vākpati bhavet |

navame sarvasiddhiśca daśame lokapūjita (145) || 12-570 ||

ekādaśe rājavaśyo dvādaśe bhūpurandaraḥ |

varamekaṃ paṭhed yastu sapūjyaptalamāpnuyāt (146) || 12-571 ||

sanyagvyā ślokamekaṃ vāyaḥ paṭhet prayataḥ śuci |

sapūjā phalamāpnoti bhairaveṇa ca bhāṣitam (147) || 12-572 ||

āyuṣmān prītiyoge ca brāhmaṇaiśca viśeṣataḥ |

saubhāgya siddhiyoge ca saubhanaṃ ca maheśvari (148) || 12-573 ||

paṃcamyāṃ vā tathā ṣaṣṭyāṃ yatra kutrāpi tiṣṭati |

natraśaṃkā na vidyeta na ce mātrādi dūṣaṇam |

kimanyad vahunā devi sarvābhīṣṭaphalaṃ labhet (150) || 12-574 ||


iti viśvasāratantre śrītripurabhairavī sahasranāma stotraṃ saṃpūrṇasamāptam ||


atha tripurabhairavī strotram ||


oṃ brahmādayaḥ stuti śatairapi sūkṣmarūpāṃ 

jānanti naiva jagadādimanādi mūrttim |

tasmā hṛyaṃku ca natāṃ tava kuṃkumābhyām 

sthūlāṃstumaḥ sakalavāṅmaya mātrarūpām (1) || 12-575 ||

sadyaḥ samudyatasahasradivākarābhāṃ 

vidyākṣasūtra varadābhayacihnahastām |

netrotpalau stribhiralaṃkṛta vastrapadmāṃ 

tvāṃ hārabhārarucirāṃ tripure bhajām || 12-576 ||

sindūrapūraruciraṃ kucasāranamraṃ 

janmātare prakṛtapuṇyaphalaikagamyam |

anyonya bhedakalahā kulamānabhedair

jānanti tikījaḍādhiyastava rūpamaṃ ca (3) || 12-577 ||

sthūlāṃ vadanti munayaḥ śrutayo gṛṇanti 

sūkṣmāṃ vadanti va ca sāmradhi vāsamanye |

tvāṃ mūlamāhuraparejagatāṃ bhavānim

anyā mahe vayapārakṛpāṃ kurāśim (4) || 12-578 ||

candrāvataṃ sakalītāṃ śaradinduśubhrāṃ 

paṃcāśadakṣaramaṃdi hṛdi bhāvayati |

218a) tvāṃ pustakaṃ japavaṭīmamṛtāḍhyakuṃbhaṃ 

vyākhyāṃ ca hastakamalairdadhatāṃ trinetrām (5) || 12-579 ||

śaṃbhustvamadritanayāṃ kalitārdhabhāgo 

viṣṇustvameva kamalā parinaddhadeha |

yajñopavastvamasi vā gadhivāsabhūmis

teṣāṃ kriyāśca jagati tripure tvameva (6) || 12-580 ||

āśritya vāgbhava bhavāṃścaturaḥ parādīn

bhāvānyed eṣu vihitān samudārayantitam |

karṇādibhiśca karaṇai paradevatāṃ tvāṃ 

saṃvinmayī hṛdi kadāpi na vismarāmi ( 7) || 12-581 ||

āṃkuṃcya vāyumabhijitya ca vairiṣṭkaṃ 

mālokya niścaladhiyā nijanāsikāgram |

dhyāyanti mūrddhni kalitendu kalāvataṃsaṃ 

tvad rūpamaṃ vakṛtinastaruṇārkamitram (8) || 12-582 ||

tvāṃ prāpya manmatha riporvapurardhabhāgaṃ 

sṛṣṭiṃ karopi jagatāmiti veda vāda |

satyaṃ tadadritanaye jagadekamātar

moceda śeṣajagataḥ sthitireva nasyāt (9) || 12-583 ||

pūjā vidhāya kusumaiḥ surapādapānāṃ 

pīṭhe tavāṃ vakanakā calakandareṣu |

gāyanti siddhacanitasmaha kinnarībhir

āsvāditā savarasāruṇa netrapadmāḥ (10) || 12-584 ||

vidyudvilāsavapuṣaḥ śriyamāvahantī

yatiṃ svavāsabhavanā chivarājadhānīm |

saundaryamārgakamalāni vikāsayanti 

devī bhajeta paramāmṛtasikta gātrīm (11) || 12-585 ||

jājanmajanmabhavanaṃ bhavanaṃ śrutināṃ 

caitanya mātratanumaṃ vatavā śruyāmi |

brahmeśa viṣṇubhirūpā sitapādapadmāṃ 

saubhāgyajanmavasatiṃ tripure yathā vat (12) || 12-586 ||

śabdārthabhāvi bhuvanaṃ sṛjatendurūpā 

yā taddhibharti punarakatanuḥ svaśaktyā |

218b) vahnyātmikā harati tatsakalaṃ yugānte 

nāṃ śāradāṃ manasiyā tu na vismarāmi (13) || 12-587 ||

nārāyaṇīti narakārṇavatāriṇīti 

gauriti khedaśamanīti sarasvatīti |

jñānapradeti nayanatrayabhūṣite 

nitvā madrirājatanayeti vurvuvati (14) || 12-588 ||

yastuvanti jaganmātaḥ ślokairdvādaśabhi kramāt |

tvāmanuprāpya vāk siddhi prāpnuyustaparāṃ śriyam (15) || 12-589 ||


iti śrītripurabhairavī stotraṃ saṃpūrṇa ||


pūjā nantaraṃ nitya homaṃ kuryāt |



pariṣicya tato devī nitya homaṃ samācaret |

mūlamantreṇa deveśi hunet paṃcāhutīḥ kramāt || 12-590 ||

prāṇā pāṇau tathā vyānamudāna ca samānakam |

etat svarūpāṃ jāniyād āhutīnāṃ ca paṃcakam || 12-591 ||

ṣaḍāhutiḥ ṣaḍaṃgeṣu nityahomo yamīritaḥ |

nityahomavidhiṃ kṛtvā validānavidhiṃ caret || 12-592 ||

īśānādiṣu koṇeṣu maṇḍalānāṃ catuṣṭayam |

śrīcakramabhito devi trikoṇaṃ vyoma cālikhet || 12-593 ||

athavā vāmabhāge tu maṇḍalaṃ caikamālikhet |


vyomavyāpakaṃ maṇḍalaṃ vetyarthaḥ |
athaveti samu ca pārtha validānasya tatra vidhanāt | vikalpa eva |
aśaktau tatraiva sakalavalidānamiti kaścit |


pūrvamantraiḥ samabhyarcya vaṭukādikamadrije || 12-594 ||

ehye hi devi putrāṃ te vaṭukānte tu nātha tu |

kapilānta jaṭābhāra bhāsvarānte trinetra ca || 12-595 ||

jvālāmukhe tu sarvā te vighnaṃ nāśayanāya |

sarvapacārasahitaṃ valiṃ gṛhna dvidhā padam || 12-596 ||

vahnijāyānvito mantro vaṭukasya samīritaḥ |


anena vidhinā devi vaṭukasya valiharet |


oṃ ehyehi deviputra vaṭukanātha kapilajaṭā bhārabhāsvara trinetra jvālāmukha 
sarvavighnaṃ nāśaya 2 sarvopacārasahitaṃ valiṃ gṛhna gṛhna svāhā ||

iti mantra ||


ūrdhvabrahmāḍato vā divigagaṇatale vā pātāle vā vane vā salilapavane 
yorṣatra kutra sthitā vā | kṣetre pīṭhopapīṭhādiṣu ca kṛtapadā dhūpadīpādike 
ne prītā devyā sadānaḥ śubhavalividhinā pātu devendri vandyā |
etadante maheśāni yāṃ vīyogini tataḥ |


bhyaḥ svāhā sarvamantrānte yoginipadamālikhet || 12-597 ||

kavacaṃ cāstu mālikhya vahnijāyāṃ punarlikhet |

anena manurā devī yogini valimāharet || 12-598 ||


devendra vayānantaram | yo yoginībhyaḥ svāhā |

yoginī huṃ phaṭ svāhā taṃ mantraḥ |


ṣaddīrghasvarasaṃyuktaṃ kṣakāraṃ punarālikhet |


kṣakāre vindurācārā dakṣiṇā mūrttisaṃhitāyām |

yoginīnāṃ tu kathyate |


punaḥ kṣetrapadaṃ pālaṃ dhūpadīpādi cālikhet || 12-599 ||

vahnijāyānvito mantra kṣetrapālasya sundari |

anena sanunā devi kṣetrapālavaliṃ haret || 12-600 ||


kṣā/ kṣī/ kṣū/ kṣai/ kṣau/ kṣaḥ kṣetrapāladhūpadīpādi sahitaṃ valiṃ gṛhna 
gṛhna svāhā ||

iti mantra ||


gā/ gī/ gū/ trayapālikhya tretaṃ gaṇapatiṃ vadet |

varāntaṃ varadā te ca sarvāṃ te janamālikhet || 12-601 ||

meva śaṃ cānayet yuktāṃ valigṛhnā dvidhā padam |

vahnijāyānvito mantro gāṇapatya valiṃ haret || 12-602 ||



gā/ gī/ gū/ gaṇapataye e varavarada sarvajanaṃ meva śamānaya valiṃ gṛhna 
gṛhna svāhā | iti mantra ||


vāmāṅguṣṭā nāmikābhyāṃ vaṭukasya valiṃ bhavet |

aṃguṣṭā madhyamā nāmā yonyākāreṇa yojayet || 12-603 ||

219b) vāmapuṣṭividhā pādau tarjanī saralāṃ kuru |

ata yā mudrayā devi kṣetrapāla valirbhavet || 12-604 ||

tathā muṣṭimadhyasthāmaṃguliṃ saralāṃ kuru |

gajadanta mahāmudrā gāṇapatya valirbhavet || 12-605 ||

anena vidhinā devi pūjayet parameśvari |


puraścaraṇaṃ varṇalakṣaṃ janmeṃtraṃ tad daśāṃśaṃ hune chive |


arpaṇaṃ tu tataḥ kuryāt sarvasaubhāgya bhāgbhavet || 12-606 ||


nivandheṣu |

tattvalakṣyaṃ japed ityarthād uktam |

bhairavī japād yati deśāt |

tatra tattvāni daśāntaraṃ gatvāt |

śaktitattvānāṃ daśasaṃkhyatvāditi vastuta dvādaśatattvāni tattvā vakṣyā va 
dhīti prapaṃcasārasāt |

anacavirodha |

asya yugabheda paratvāt |

kalausaṃkhyā caturguṇā ityabhidhānād ityarthaḥ |

atha kāmyāni |


evaṃ ya kurute devyā vilyapatreṇa mānavaḥ |

yaṃ yaṃ pārthayate kāmī sarvaṃ sarvamavāpnuyāt || 12-607 ||

lulāvasya ca meṣasya saromaṃ māṃsameva vā |

hunetrimasaṃyuktaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ sapāyasaṃ || 12-608 ||

panasā prayutaṃ kṛtvā śrīmanayāyinī |

dūrvayādhanadhānyaṃ ca putralābhā bhaven nṛṇām || 12-609 ||

lulāvo mahiṣa |

aṣṭamī dve caturdaśyāṃ navamī dve tathaiva ca |

rātrau saṃpūjayed yasmān mahāvibhavavistare || 12-610 ||

palāśakuśakāśaiśca tulasīṃ ca vivarjayet |

pūjanāt pātakī bhūyā lugne cāpi śriyeṃ haret || 12-611 ||

tathā nivandhe ||

evaṃ siddhamanurmantrī sādhayed iṣṭamātmana |

eve kṛte bhave śyora māyā ca satirbharvat || 12-612 ||

raktotpalaistrimadhvaktairarūṇairvāhapārijaiḥ |

puṣpaipapātraiḥ saghṛtairhomād viśvaṃ vaśaṃ nayet || 12-613 ||


hayāriḥ karavīra payonnaṃ pāyasam |


vāksiddhi labhate mantrī palāśakusumānvitaiḥ |

220a) juhuyād aruṇyaṃ bhojairaduṣṭairmadhurānvitaiḥ || 12-614 ||

lakṣasaṃkhyaṃ tadardhaṃ vā pratyahaṃ bhojaye dvijāt |

vanitā yuvatī ramyāḥ prīṇaye devatādhiyā || 12-615 ||

homānte dhanadhānyādyostoṣayeṭ gurumātmanaḥ |

karpūrāgurusaṃyuktaṃ guṃguluṃ juhuyāt sudhī || 12-616 ||

jñānaṃ divyamavāpnoti te naiva sāmavet kaviḥ |

kṣīrāktairamṛtā khaṇḍai homaiḥ sarvāpamṛtyujit || 12-617 ||

durvābhirāyuvo homaḥ kṣīrāktābhirdinatrayam |

girikarṇibhavaiḥ puṣpai brāhmaṇān vaśamānayet || 12-618 ||


girikarṇī aparājitā |


kalhāraiḥ pārthivaiḥ puṣpaistad va bhūḥ karṇikārajaiḥ |

mallikākusumairhutvā rājaputrānvaśaṃ nayet || 12-619 ||

koraṃṭhakusumairvaśyān vṛṣalāt pāṭalābhavai |


koraṃṭhakiṃṭhiti prasiddhaḥ |


anulomavilomāntaḥ sthitasādhyā hvayānvitam || 12-620 ||

mantramucāryajuhuyān mantrī madhuraloḍitai |

sarpibhirmadhusammiśrairvaśayen pārthivāt kṣaṇāt || 12-621 ||

anenaiva vidhānena tat patnī tat sutā napi |

evaṃ vilvabhavaiḥ puṣpairmadhuratrayasaṃyutaiḥ || 12-622 ||

naranārī tapatīrhomato vaśamānayet |

mālatī vakulodbhūtaiḥ puṣpaicandanaloḍitaiḥ || 12-623 ||

juhuyāt kavitā mantrī labhata vatsarāntare |

madhuratrayasaṃyuktaiḥ phalaivilvasamudbhavaiḥ || 12-624 ||

juhuyād vaśayaṃllokaṃ śriyamāpnoti saṃcitām |

ṣāṭhalaiḥ kusumaiḥ kuṇḍairutmalairnāgacaṇḍupakai || 12-625 ||

naṃdyāvartte vikasitaiḥ kṛta mālai juhoti yaḥ |

jāyate vatsarā darvā kṛśriyā vijitapārthivam || 12-626 ||


naṃdyāvartastagara |


sājyamantraṃ prajuhuyād bhaved atra samṛddhimān |

kasturī kuṃkumopetaṃ karpūrajuhuyād vaśī || 12-627 ||

220b) kandarpādadhikaṃ sadya saudaryadhigachati |

lājān prajuhuyān matri dadhikṣīramadhuplutān || 12-628 ||

vijitya lokānakhilān sajīve charadāṃ śatam |

pādadvayaṃ malayajaṃ pādaṃ kuṃkumakasanam || 12-629 ||

pādaṃ gorocanaṃ caiva trīṇipiṣṭvādi śāṃbhasā |

vidadyā tilakaṃ bhāle yā nu paśyed yana vikṣyate || 12-630 ||

yānu dṛśya dṛśyate yaurvārvaśyāsyustasya te cirāt |

karpūrakapicaurāṇi samabhāgāni kalpayet || 12-631 ||

caturbhāgajaṭāmāṃsī tāvatīrocanāmarā |

kuṃkumaṃ saptabhāgaṃ syān nibhāgaṃ candanaṃ matam || 12-632 ||

agururnavabhāgaḥ syād iti bhāgā kra tu |

himādbhiḥ kalpakāpiṣṭametat sarvaṃ su sādhitam || 12-633 ||

dāya tilakaṃ bhāle kuryād bhabhipatīn narāt |

vanitāmadagarvādyā madonmattān mataṃ gajān || 12-634 ||

siṃhān vyāghrān mahāsarpān bhūtavetālarākṣasān |

darśanād eva vā śaye tilakaṃ dhārayan naraḥ || 12-635 ||

ityāgamakalpalatāyāṃ śrītripurabhairavī samarpayāvidhirakonaviṃśatipaṭalaḥ
