vuhimālā ca vuhā ca vanamālā valīvarā |

varadā vā bhaṇī vīṇā vīṇavādana tatparā || 18-228 ||

virodini vinodasthā vaiṣṇavī vismavallabhā |

vaidyā vaidyaciphitmā ca vivaśāviśvaviskutā || 18-229 ||

vidyau ca ṣitkulāvelā vitaṃjñāṣi gatajvalā |

virā vā vividhākārā vimvoṣṭī viṃvavatsalā || 18-230 ||

viṃdyāsthā vīravandyā ca vīrapānaparā ca vit |

vedā taṃ vedyā vedyā ca vīrapānaparā ca vit || 18-231 ||

vedāntavedyā vedyā ca vīrapānaparā ca vit |

vijayā vijaya pradā * * * * * * * * || 18-232 ||

virodhavarddhinī vandyā vandhyā vandhanivāriṇi |

bhagini bhagamālā ca bhavāni bhavabhāvini || 18-233 ||

bhīmā bhīmānanāmemī bhaṃgurā bhīmadarśanā |

miktībhaktadharā bhīma bherudābhibhavā pahā || 18-234 ||

bhagasarpiṇāpi bhagā bhagarūpā bhagālayā |

bhagāsanā bhagāmādā bharībhaṃkāraraṃjitā || 18-235 ||

bhīṣaṇā bhīṣaṇārāvā bhagavatya hi bhūṣaṇā |

bhāradvājā bhogadānnimavaghnībhūtabhūṣaṇā || 18-236 ||

bhūtidā bhūmidāgrī ca bhūpati tva pradāyinī |

bhramarī bhrāmarī bhīmā bhūpālamukuṭi sthitā || 18-237 ||

mantāmattoharā matā mānini mohinī mahī |

291b) mahālakṣmirmadakṣī ca madirā madirālayā || 18-238 ||

madoddhatā mataṃgasthā mādhavī madhumardinī |

medhā medhyākarī medhyā madhyā madhyavaya sthitā || 18-239 ||

madhyayāmāṃ malā matsyā mādinī maithunodvatā |

mahāmujñavatī mātrādhvī mahiṣamardinā || 18-240 ||

mahāmāyā mahāvidyā mahāmārī maheśvarī |

mahādeva vadhūmānyā ṣathurāmerumaṇḍalā || 18-241 ||

medasvinī milaṃda śrīrmahiṣāsuramardini |

maṇḍalasthā maṭhasthā ca madirāmagarditā || 18-242 ||

mokṣadā muṇḍamālā ca mālāmuṇḍavilāśini |

mātaṃginiṃ ca mātagi mataṃ na * *  yāmi ca || 18-243 ||

yamaghnī yame kanyā ca yajamānasvarūpiṇi |

yajñā ījyāpi yakṣa ca yaśoniṣkarakāriṇī || 18-244 ||

yakṣiṇi yakṣajananī yaśodā yaśadhāriṇī |

yajñasūtra pradā jyeṣṭā yajñakarmakarī tathā || 18-245 ||

yaśasvinī pakārasthā pūpamnaṃbhanivāsinī |

raṃjitā rājapatni ca ramārekhā parī vaṇā? || 18-246 ||

rajo va nirajaścintā rajanī rajanī pati |

dāgjiṇī rajinī rājyā rājyadā rājyavarddhinī || 18-247 ||

rājanvanī rājanītistathā janavāsini |

ramaṇī ramaṇīyā ca dāmā rāmādatī rati || 18-248 ||

retoṣatī ratonsādā * dihṛyogakāriṇī |

raṃgā raṃgāvati raṃgā rāgajñā rāgakṛrduṣā || 18-249 ||

rantikā raṃjikā raṃjā raṃjinī raktalocanā |

raktacarmadharā raṃbhī raṃgasthā raktavāhinī || 18-250 ||

raṃbhā raṃbhaphaphalaprīti raṃbhorūrā ca va priyā |

292a) raṃgami iṃgamadhūrā rodaśī rodaśī gṛhā || 18-251 ||

rodhakṛyodhaharti ca rūpabhijñā gaṇāyinī |

vaṃdivaṃdistu ṣaṃ dhūrvardhūṃkakusumāṃvarā || 18-252 ||

vaṃdinā va nāmānī ca vaṃdhurā vaidhavī vidhā |

piṃkivihvā phalaṃ vihvā vihvasthā vihvavatsalā || 18-253 ||

vedirvignā vilagnā ca vidhirvidhikari vidhā |

śaṃkhinī śaṃkhavalayā śaṃkhamālā vaniśami || 18-254 ||

śaṃkhapānnāśiniśaṃkha svanāśaṃkhagalāśaśī |

"avarī śāṃkarī śaṃvū śavukīśavarāśini || 18-255 ||

śaṃvīśarāvati śyāmā śyāmāṅgī śyāmalocalā |

śmaśānasthā śmaśānā ca śmaśānasthalabhūṣaṇī || 18-256 ||

śamadāśamahartrī ca śaṃkhinī śaṃdhuśekharā |

śāntiḥ śāntipradā śeṣā śoṣaśopinī || 18-257 ||

śṛgiṇī śṛṃgīpala tu kuśāṃkarākarī ca śam |

śaṃkāśaṃ kāpahā saṃsthā śāsvatitalā śivā || 18-258 ||

śavasthā śavatukuśā vā śāvakarmaśavodari |

śāpini śayatāśikhyā śiṃśaṃ pāśiṃ śapāpani || 18-259 ||

śaivakuṇḍalini śaivā śīkarāśiśirāsirā |

śavakāṃcī prāvaśrīkā śavamālā śivākṛti || 18-260 ||

śavatā śaṃkuvasaniḥ śrīladā * * *  yini |

sidhu sārasvatī sidhuḥ sundari sundarānanā || 18-261 ||

sādhu siddhi siddhidātri siddhāsiddhasarasvatī |

santatiśca tathā saṃpat maṃ pannidāyinī || 18-262 ||

sapatnisarasā sārā sārasvatakari svadhā |

saraḥ samāsamānā ca samārādhyā samastadā || 18-263 ||

292b) samidā saṃmadā saumyā sarmohā samadarśanā |

sīmati samadā sīmā sāvitri savidhāsani || 18-264 ||

savanāvasanādhārā sāvanā sāmarāsamī |

sṛmarā satataṃ sādhyā dardhīvīnāsahāriṇi || 18-265 ||

haṃsihaṃsagatirhisi hasaujvalani caula puk |

halinī haladā hālā halaśrīrhalavallabhaḥ || 18-266 ||

helā helāvatī heṣā heṣā heṣasthavarddhinī |

hantā hantirhayā hā hā hā tāhāhantikāriṇi || 18-267 ||

haṃkāri haṃvṛtirhaṃkā hi hi hānā hanā hanā |

hetirhemapradā hāravantī hārītasaṃmatā || 18-268 ||

horā hotrī holikā ca homyā homā havirhari |

hariṇi hariṇīnetrā himācalanivāsinī || 18-269 ||

laṃvodarī laṃvakarmā laṃvikā laṃvavigrahā |

līlā līlāvati lolā lalanā lālitārasā || 18-270 ||

lalāmatomalo ca nālocyā lālākṣī lakṣaṇā lalā |

laṃpanī laṃpatī laṃpā lopāmudrā lalaṃvini || 18-271 ||

latikālaṃvini laṃvā laghimā laghumadhyamā |

labdhīyasī yalaghudarpā lūtālūtanivāriṇi || 18-272 ||

lāmatṛllomalomnī ca lulantī lurluluṃpini |

lupasthā ca lahari lavoṃkapuraṃdarā || 18-273 ||

kṣapā kṣapākarī kṣārā kṣīradākṣīrasāgarā |

kṣemakarī kṣayakarī kṣahṛtkṣaṇa pradākṣani || 18-274 ||

kṣīranti kṣudrikā kṣudrā kṣut kṣarā kṣīṇapātakā (1000) |

umā tuḥ sahasranāmedaṃ sumukhyo siddhidāyakam || 18-275 ||

yaḥ paṭhet prātarutthāya sa evasyān maheśvaraḥ |

293a) janācāntaḥ paṭhennityaṃ daridro dhanado bhavet || 18-276 ||

mukhaḥ syād vākupatirdevi rāgīnāṃ rogatāṃ labhet |

aputrī putramāpnoti triṣuloke vidhiśrutam || 18-277 ||

vaṃdyāyi sūte tema yāt durdharyān sarcato guṇau |

saśyaṃ ca vahudhā bhūyād gāvaḥ syurvajjadugdhadā || 18-278 ||

rājāna pādanamrāsyuḥ stasya dāsā iva sphuṭam |

arayaḥ saṃkṣayaṃ yāti manasā saspṛnā api || 18-279 ||

darśanād eva jāyate narānāyopi taṭcaṇā |

karttā harttā svayaṃ vīrau jāyate nātra saṃśaya || 18-280 ||

yaṃ yaṃ kāmayate kāmaṃ taṃ taṃ prāpnoti niścitam |

duritaṃ na ca tasyā sve naiva śokaḥ kadācanaḥ || 18-281 ||

catuṣpathe va rātrau ca yaḥ paṭhet sādhakontamaḥ |

ekā kīrtti bhayārvīro daśāvarttastavauntamam || 18-282 ||

mana sacintitaṃ kārya tasyā siddhyaratya saṃśayaḥ |

vinā sahasranāmnā yo japen mantraṃ kadācanaḥ || 18-283 ||

na siddhirjāyate tasya mantrakalpaśatairapi |

jajavāre śmaśāne vā madhyāhne pau japet sadā || 18-284 ||

śatā cartryā jāyate sakartā harttā nṛṇāmiha |

rogārrordhaniśāyāṃ cet paṭhatye bhasisaṃsthitaḥ || 18-285 ||

savonīraugatāmeti yadi syān nibhayaḥ sadāḥ |

ardharātre śmaśāne vā śanivāre japen manum || 18-286 ||

aṣṭauntare sahasraṃ tu daśavārapaṭhen tataḥ |

sahasranāma caitaddhi tadā yāti svayaṃ śivā || 18-287 ||

mahāmavena rūpeṇa ghoragomāpunādinī |

tadā yadi na bhītiḥ syān tato dehiti vāgbhavet || 18-288 ||

293b) tadā paśuvaliṃ dadyāt svayaṃ gṛhaṇāni caṇḍikā |

paṭheṣṭaṃ ca varaṃ datvā pāti śrīsumukhī śivā || 18-289 ||

rocanāgurukasyūrī karpūramadacandanai |

kumkume prathamārbhyā tu likhitaṃ bhūrjapatrake || 18-290 ||

śubhanakṣatrayogena kṛta mārutasatkriyam |

kṛtasaṃ paṭhana vidhirdhāraya dekṣiṇe kare || 18-291 ||

sahasranāmasvarṇasthaṃ kavecāpi jitendriyaḥ |

tadyasmai praṇamen mantrī kuddhaḥ sastriya te naraḥ || 18-292 ||

yasmai dadāti ca svasti phabhaved dhanadopam |

aṣṭasvāyadaṃ jabhūnāṃ nabhīḥ kutrāpi jāyate || 18-293 ||

vālakāmāmiyaṃ rakṣā garbhinī nāmapi śruvam |

mohanastaṃbhanākarṣa māraṇocāṭanāni ca || 18-294 ||

patraṃ dhāramato nūnaṃ jāyate sādhakasya tu |

nīlavastrai vilikhitaṃ dhvajāyāṃ yadi tiṣṭati || 18-295 ||

tadā naṣṭā bhavatyaiva pracaṇḍāpyarivāhinī |

etajjaptaṃ mahābhasma lalāṭe yadi dhārayet || 18-296 ||

tad darśana bha evasyu prāṇinastasya kiṃkara |

rājapatnyaupivaśagaḥ kimanya purayoṣitaḥ || 18-297 ||

etajjaptaṃ pivat stotraṃ māsaikena mahākaviḥ |

paṇḍitaśca mahāvādi jāyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ || 18-298 ||

ayutaṃ ca paṭhet stotraṃ puraścaraṇasiddhayaḥ |

daśasāhasrakamalairjuhuyā trimaciśritaiḥ || 18-299 ||

svayamāyāti kamalā sahavāṇyā tadālaye |

mantrāniskilatāmeti sumukhī * * * * bhavet || 18-300 ||

anantaṃ ca bhavet puṇyamapuṇyaṃ ca kṣayaṃ vrajet |

294a) puṣkarādiṣu tīrtheṣu snāna tā yat phalaṃ bhavet || 18-301 ||

tat phalaṃ labhate jantuḥ sumukhāstautravācanāt |

etad uktaṃ rahasyante sva sarvasvaṃ varānane || 18-302 ||

na prakāśyaṃ tvayā devi yadi sisiddhi mavāsyasi |

prakāśānādasiddhiḥ syāt kupitā sumukhi bhavet || 18-303 ||

nātaparaḥ stavo loke siddhidaḥ prāṇināmihe |

tasmāt sarvaprayatnena gopanīyaḥ stavontamaḥ || 18-304 ||


oṃ vande śrīsumukhiṃ prasaṃnamanasaṃpūrṇenduviṃvānanā 
sindūrāṅkitamastakāṃ madālolāṃ samuktāvalīm |

śyāmākajjalitāṃ karāṃvujagatāṃ vādhyāpayanti 
śukāṃguṃjāpuṃjavibhūṣaṇam | śukarūṇi māmuktaveṇī latām |


iti śrīnandyāvanto uttarakhaṇḍe umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde tvaritaphaladāyinī 
sumukhī sahasranāma samāptam |


atha sumukhī stava |


śrībhairava uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi stotraṃ mātaṃgini * * priyam |

yaṃ paṭhitvā paṭhed bhūmau sādhakau bhairavopama || 18-305 ||


asya śrīsumukhī paṃcāṅga stotrasya mataṃgaṛṣiḥ anuṣṭupchandaḥ 
śrīsumukhī devatā ai/ vījaṃ klī/ śaktiḥ klī/ kīlakaṃ śrīdharmārtha 
kāmamokṣārthe jape pāṭhe viniyogaḥ |


atha dhyānam |

vālāṃ ṣoḍaśavārṣikī śavagatā mādhvī rasā ghūrṇitāṇyamāṅgīm 
aruṇāṃ varāṃ pṛthak cāṃgujāvalī śobhitām |

294b) hastābhyāṃ dadhatiṃ kapālamamalaṃ tījñāṃ tathā kartṛkāṃ dhyāye 
mātasapaṃkajaṃ bhavatīmuchiṣṭacāṇḍālinīm || 18-306 ||

vāgbhūvataśive priya vījamantra maulimamṛtaikanidhyātam |

yo japed raṣasivārdharānake sasvayaṃ śivabhavāṣṭasiddhibhāk (2) || 18-307 ||

kāmarājānavāṃ ca dimāna saṃjape chavahetha mandire |

tasya kāmaśagā bhave śakṣāṇādurvaśāṃ śāśitibhānanāśive (3) || 18-308 ||

uchiṣṭacaṇḍālinimantrarājaṃ jape chive yā ripuvargataptaḥ |

savairisaṃvān puṣṭi jeṣyeti drāṣkābhotirājye nṛpacakravektā (4) || 18-309 ||

yat stvan manomadhye gataṃ nāma jape chive itkāmaleti mūḍhaḥ |

vādbhiḥ sudhājñaminiśāṃ nilāṃke japaṃ suśīghraṃ kavicakravatri (5) || 18-
310 ||

mahāpiśācini nāmaṃntāyaṃ japataṃ hṛdi |

tasya brahmādayo devāḥ śāsanā vaśavarttinaḥ (6) || 18-311 ||

māyāvījaṃ cāṃ japen mantra dhye kāmārtāvāsā komau purāte |

raṃbhā laṃvonta sapuṣpāpi nityaṃ vatryā tasya svarganākāsarūpā (7) || 18-312 
||

paṃkajannapamidaṃ maturūpaṃ yo japed bhagavati priyavījam |

bhūmi pātu raṇabhuvi pramathya saṃkṣoṇipo bhavati yāti ca svargamu (8) || 18-
313 ||

mantrāntayā dahanavatitāṃ saṃjaped ardharātre māta prānaḥ sakalajanani 
meracakre pramataḥ |

sasvarvākarakisalayo vīkṣyamānāṃ vineyāti svargaṃ tridaśapatinā 
pūjāmānāḥ smareṇa (9) || 18-314 ||

295a) abhaṇārkanibhāṃ trilocanāṃ dvibhujādyāṃ ca śavāsanasthitam |

madhupānaparāṃ paraṃ parāṃ hṛdi śrīcakragatāṃ piśācinīm (10) || 18-315 ||

iti manumayastotraṃ puṇyā paṭed yadi sādhakāraṇa bhuvi rano prāvarmāte 
japāpasare śivi |

iha bhuvi ciraṃ bhutkā bhogān sāmaṃ sutapautrakairvrajati divaṃ paścān mukta 
sasarvamure svāra (11) || 18-316 ||

iti stotraṃ manumayaṃ tu mukhyāḥ sāramuntamam |

paṃcāṅgaḥ bhūtaṃ deveśi mutyaṃgoptaṃ gopayet || 18-317 ||

iti śrīsumukhī devyāṃ pacāṅgadeva durllabham |

aprakādya madātavyaṃ gopanīyaṃ svayonivat (11) || 18-318 ||


iti śrībhadrayāmale guhyakālītantre pacāṅgabhūtasumukhī stava samāptaḥ |


śrīdevyuvāca

sumukhī stotraṃ pravakṣyāmi devi sadyamukha pradam |

yasya pradato nṛṇāṃ jāyante sarvasaṃpadaḥ (2) || 18-319 ||

śrīśiva uvāca

amṛtaṃ sumukhī devyā uchiṣṭabhayavarjitām |

sadā hasanmukhī bhakterjanannāsakṣayaṃ karī (3) || 18-320 ||

śivapriyāṃ śivā rūpāṃ śiva prīti pradāyinīm |

gujāhāra dharā gujā koṭisūryasamaprabhām (4) || 18-321 ||

samājanīla sadvāmā pāṇipadmavirāmatām |

guṃjāhārābhilāṣādyāṃ pāramānhādarūpiṇīm (5) || 18-322 ||

sadotkāsamayī devī * salāvaṇyavigraha |

sadābhaktajanannāma dantaścintāsuntamām (6) || 18-323 ||

sadābhojanabhāvasthāṃ sadāmanthāragāminim |

295b) sadābhaṇatarākṣīṃ ca sadonmannobhabhāṣiṇim (7) || 18-324 ||

krī/kārakṛta vāsā ca vahni māyā kṛtālayām |

vadehaṃ sumukhī devīṃ vedināṃ tṛmurāmuraiḥ (8) || 18-325 ||

ityeṣṭakaṃ ya prayatāḥ paṭhanti paramāpadaḥ |

praṇaśyaṃ nidhruvaṃ tasya satyaṃ satyaṃ ca pārvati |

ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā teṣāṃ siddhi pade pade (9) || 18-326 ||


iti bhadryāmale sumukhyaṣṭaṃkaṃ saṃpūrṇam |


śrīdevyuvāca

namāmi viṃśatik vrūhiṃ sumukhyā siddhidāyinī |

yenācireṇa kāleyaṃ jāyate sarvasaṃpadaḥ || 18-327 ||

iśvara uvāca

namāmi viṃśanika devi stavarājamanoharam |

anuvakṣyāmite sehā yan mayā na prakāśinam (2) || 18-328 ||

sumukhī sundarī rāmā surasyāraṇadūrmadā |

japaṃ nījayanīyamyā yāmyā muktiratāṃ vara (3) || 18-329 ||

bhuktimukti pradā vāṇā vīṇāvādavilāsinī |

vāmāṃgī vāmanā vāmā * * * rgavicakṣaṇā || 18-330 ||

vijñānaśīlāsakalaiḥ mevitaprapaṭedidam |

stavarājaṃ vilālākṣī siddhistasyā napāyinī (5) || 18-331 ||

vardhate saṃpadaḥ pūjyāḥ paramānanda dāyikā |

ardharātre kṛtā hāro paduchiṣṭapaṭhedidam (6) || 18-332 ||

acireṇaiva kālena śriyājinadhaneśvarā |

bhavatyaiva varārohe śaṃkarasevamājñayā (7) || 18-333 ||

anena sadṛśaṃ ponya stotraṃ bhūyād yathādhama |

padyā na pāpasadṛśaṃ nasya pāpaṃ prajāyate (8) || 18-334 ||


iti śrībhadrayāmale sumukhī stavarājaṃ saṃpūrṇasamāptam |


296a) atha maṃjughoṣamantra |


merupṛṣṭe sukhāsīnaṃ deva deva jagat gurum |

śaṃkaraṃ paripapracha pārvatī parameśvarīm || 18-335 ||

śrīpārvatyuvāca

bhagavan sarvadharmajña sarvaśāstrā gamādiṣu |

vāṃchitārthaṃ pradaṃ loke maṃjughoṣaṃ vravīṣime || 18-336 ||

śrīśakara uvāca 

śrṇu devi mahāmantraṃ sādhakānāṃ sukhāvaham |

yajñātvā jaḍadhī prāyo vācaspati samo bhavet || 18-337 ||

aṃga nyāsa karaṃ nyāsa vahirnyāsa samanvitam |

japāt siddhi prado mantro vimāho mārcanādibhi || 18-338 ||

japed vā jāpayed vāpi sādhake vidhi pūrvakam |

sarvajñatvamavāpnoti satyaṃ satyaṃ hi pārvati || 18-339 ||

kārtikeya mukhaṃ yāvat tā vallabhaṃ japen manu |

sarvaśāstreṣu cairvācaspati samo bhavet || 18-340 ||

vṛhadāraṇyako nāma ṛṣiḥ chando virāṭ smṛtam |

devatāṃ maṃjughoṣākhyā bhuktidā caiva muktidā || 18-341 ||

japet siddhi pradaṃ sadyo vaiṣṇavaṃ sānvikārmakam |

te va siddhi pradaṃ sadyastāmasaḥ samudāhṛtam || 18-342 ||


mantrodhāramāha |


viṣṇugnipāsi śaśiyukucadhīśca ṣaṭvarṇamantro jagatāṃ pradiṣṭa |

sarvajñatāṃ sadaśivāk prasūte vedāntavedaniratasya vasu pradasyāt || 18-343 ||


296b) viṣṇu akāra agni repha pāśī vakāra śāśīśakāraḥ |
dhīriti svarūpaṃ te na aravaśvadhītiti mantra |
ādya mantraṃ japen mantrī yadi śādhaka |
vali naivedya bhukusākṣā vṛhaspatirivāpara |


māsatreye śayaḥ kuryāt puraścaryāṃ paraṃ naraḥ |

tasyāpi vadanād vāṇī nistāred varavarṇinī || 18-344 ||

māsatrayeṇaśatataṃ kavirava na saṃśayaḥ |

gomuḍe gavipṛṣṭe ca patre māpi ca gomaye || 18-345 ||

yantre mantraṃ likhitvādau paścān mantraṃ likhet punaḥ |

dhyānamātraṃ vidhāyādau bhāvayitvā ciraṃ sudhi || 18-346 ||

nirjanasthānamāsādya japenmantramadhomukhaḥ |

paurṇamāsī samāsādya kuṇḍasya kusumaiḥ śalaiḥ || 18-347 ||

aṣṭāvikaiśca saṃpūjya japenmantraṃ catuṣpathe |

trimuṇḍārohanaṃ kṛtvā niṇīthe muktakuntalaḥ || 18-348 ||

ṣaṇmāsamātraṃ japati yadi mantrī vidhānavit |

vṛhaspati samovaktā nātrakāryā vicāraṇe || 18-349 ||

kukkurasya ca muṇḍaikamuṇḍaṃ kroṣṭarvṛṣasya ca |

trimuṇḍameta tad vikhyātaṃ sādhakānāṃ sukhāvaham || 18-350 ||

āsanaṃ caiva gomuṇḍe vāme kukku ca muṇḍakam |

dakṣiṇe ca śivā muṇḍe kṛtvā pūjāṃ samācaret || 18-351 ||

arddhacandrākṛti paścād vāme candrākṛtisphuṭam |

tatra mantraṃ likhet pūjāṃ kudasya kusumena ca || 18-352 ||


savyena pāṇikamalena jayādi pūjā śṛṅgāraśīlana vikhaṇḍadakṣiṇe naḥ |
kāvudhākaramarīci tuṣārahārigauraṃ dhyātvā catuṣpatha 
taṭavṛṣamastakastham | saṃcitya kukkuraśichirasādhirūḍha kundena sādhakatamo 
japati prakāśam |


297a) gocarmamātraracitaṃ rasakoṇamātraṃ cakraṃ natopi na ca 
kuṃkumarocanābhi |

nirmāya savyavidhinā vijena śmaśāne saṃpūjya yetat 
kṣatajapadmadalāyatākṣam || 18-353 ||

ariṣṭagehe niśitailameva mādāya yatnāt karapallavenaḥ |

tenāṃcitaṃ kācanapuṣpameva nivedya tasmai japati prakāsam || 18-354 ||

aśokaśāko tatarāśca mūle viliṣya pādau vadanāmṛtena |


trimuṇḍamātraṃ sthita eva rātrau japed yathāśakti ca paurṇamāsyām |


laku ca vipaṭamadhye muḍamātraikarūḍho himakarakaragauraṃ cintayitvā niṣithe |

yadi japati taḍātmā lakṣamekaṃ traipakṣaṃ bhavati jagati sākṣātīrpati nātra 
cintam || 18-355 ||

bhūktātrāmanakadalītarupuṣpajāle muṇḍatrayo trayaviracitā sanasaṃniviṣṭaḥ |

rākāvidhūdayamapetyakaroti pūjāṃ jasopi mūrṣa iva cākupatirīśvarasyāt || 
18-356 ||


jihvāṃ visṛjya nijapāṇisaroruhābhyāṃ vāṇā prasūnaśatakai paripūjya goyo 
vai japed anudinaṃ rasalakṣamātraṃ iśam japat kimuta cākupatim-
ekavaktramātram |


vāṇī pāṭhi |

sthtvā niṣithasamaye raja yasya kāṣṭe khaṅgānvito jayati kanyayi paurṇamāsyam |

saṃpūrṇamāsamathavā tarasāpi mantrī vaktrādvinismarati gismṛnāpamānā || 
18-357 ||

yo dantadhāraṇa kṛtaiśca karaṃjakāṣṭaistasmāpi gīṣpanivaco niyatasūlabhyam |


297b) tilatailena maniśāṃ kudakairavapuṣpakai |

yatnato juhuyān mantrī sarvasiddhimupālabhet || 18-358 ||

maṃjiṣṭanāpa suvacāsitabhānupuṣai sipāṅgaśoṇitayutaiḥ samakuṇṭhakaiśca |

kṛtvā lalāṭaphalake tilakaṃ japastho vidyā virodhaviṣayena ca gīṣpatiḥ syāt || 18-
359 ||

yoya māraṇālamiti khyātaṃ sitavādhaścetārthakuṣṭaṃ kudu iti prasiddham |

mantrotaramāha |

śṛṇu devī mahāmantraṃ saptārṇātmaṣaḍarṇakam |

sakṛd ucāraṇā devi mukto bhavati paṇḍitaḥ || 18-360 ||

ravivindu samāyukto jāto rātogni śāntiyuk |

kṣakāra pṛthivī cāgnirvinduḥ śānti uddhṛtaḥ || 18-361 ||

saptavaṇamanuḥ prokto mantriṇāṃ vitārakaḥ |

yasma saṃkīrttanān mantrī vācaspatirivā paraḥ || 18-362 ||

śiśutaravarakāti kanti nīlāṃvudābhaṃ vikacasaramijābhyāṃ yustakaṃ 
karparaṃ ca |

smita suviṣadacakraṃ paṃcacūḍaṃ trinetraṃ kumati dahanadakṣaṃ majughoṣaṃ 
namāmi || 18-363 ||


vad dīrghabhājā vījena kuryādaṃgāni sādhakaḥ |


ravirmakāraḥ jāntā rukāraḥ rāntolakāraḥ śāntiḥ īkāraḥ pṛthivī lakāraḥ |
maṃrulaṃ rīkṣalarīm |

iti saptavarṇātmake mantra | maṃḍularīkṣalarī | ṣaṭvarṇātmako mantraḥ | iti |
nivandhāntarāt | tathā |


evaṃ dhyātvā jape devi va lakṣasamāhitam |

tad daśāṃśa ca juhuyāt sarṣaperloṇamiśritaiḥ || 18-364 ||

ṛṣiḥ kaṇo varṣirāḍūchando devatā vaṭukaḥ smṛtāḥ |

mātṛkokte yaje devī vāgīśaṃ deva manvaham || 18-365 ||

298a) ādāvaṃgāni saṃpūjya paścā chaktīrimāyajet |

yogā satyā ca vimalā jñānā vuddhiḥ smṛtiḥ puna || 18-366 ||

midhā prajñā ca yatneṣu mudrā pustakadhāriṇī |

lokapālā vahiḥ pūjāsteṣāmastrāṇi taddhahiḥ || 18-367 ||

pitvā tan mantritaṃ toyaṃ sahasraṃ pratyahaṃ japet |

mahākavirbhaven mantrī māsena tu na saṃśayaḥ || 18-368 ||

ācaret kutsitā cāraṃ bhakṣayed vāmavāhunā |

pradīpochiṣṭatailena viliṃ pedgātramanvaharam || 18-369 ||

paridyānandā kuryāt kevalaṃ rātri vāsasā |

niśiniśivalimasmai bhuktamatraṃ pradadyāddhṛdaya manuṃ galāṃ narmaṃ ca 
jāpaṃ citanvat |

bhavati nṛpati pūjyo yoṣitāṃ premapātraṃ bhavati khalukavīṇāmagraṇīḥ 
paṇḍitaśca || 18-370 ||

sakṛt sthāne sadā kuryāt pūjana japanaṃ hi tat |

kāvyārthi kurute kāvyaṃ dhanārthī prāpnuyād dhanam || 18-371 ||

kavitāmāvahet puṃsā māmardana vijṛṃbhinīm |

sarvavaidāgamādīnāṃ vyākhyātā jāyate cirāt || 18-372 ||

manorasya sakolasthi jñānaiścarya prado puraḥ |

tattvavodhe |

śraddhayā jāyate siddhiriha loke paratra ca || 18-373 ||

śraddhāhīne na deveśi ddhirjanmaśatairapi |

etāsāmapi vidyānāṃ jape home ca tarpaṇe || 18-374 ||

svechā viracitaṃ vat tva vodhānnivodhayet |

tattvavodhasya hṛdaye yadyat saṃpratipadyate || 18-375 ||

tattvadeva vijānīyad eva tayā śrīkīrttitam |


iti śrībhairavatantre maṃjughoṣaprakaraṇam |

atha varadagaṇapati paṃcārā likhyate |


śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ |

merukuṭhe mahādevaṃ sukhīsīnaṃ trilocanam || 18-376 ||

surāsuragaṇairyuktaṃ brahmācyutanamaskṛtam |

siddhakinnaragandharvai yakṣabūtapiśācakaiḥ || 18-377 ||

toṣitaṃ natibhistotraiḥ pārvatī sahaitaṃ śivam |

sasmitaṃ bhāṣamāṇaṃ ca brahmaṇā hariṇā saha || 18-378 ||

pārvatī praṇatā bhūtvā vacanaṃ samabhāṣitam |

śrīdevyuvāca 

bhagavan sarvatattvajña sarvadharmaprakāśakaḥ || 18-379 ||

kāryasma devatānāṃ ca manuṣyāṇāṃ tathaiva ca |

vighnaḥ kena bhave deva saṃśayaṃ chidiśaṃkara || 18-380 ||

bhairava uvāca

devi vakṣo rahasyante praṣṇottaramidaṃ mahat |

avaktavyamabhaktebhyo bhuktimuktyośca sādhanam || 18-381 ||

vināyako mahādevi gaṇeśa iti yaḥ prabhuḥ |

vighnakarttā jagat tratā vighnahartā ca viśrūtaḥ || 18-382 ||

tasya bhaktasya deveśi martyasyāpi maheśvarī |

vighnonavādhate jātu yastaṃ saṃpūjayet sadā || 18-383 ||

yojaye devi tan mantraṃ kavacaṃ tasya dhārayet |

paṭhen nāmnāṃ sahasraṃ tu stotraṃ mūlaikasādhanam || 18-384 ||

vighnona vādhate tasya kāryahāni na vā bhavet |

devyuvāca

deva deva mahādeva gaṇeśasya jagatpate || 18-385 ||

mantraṃ yantraṃ stavaṃ varma nāmnāṃ sahasramuttamam |

pūjāṃśyo tu mahantvat to vāṃchāmi parameśvara || 18-386 ||

bhairava uvāca

yo deva devo varado gaṇeśo vighnanāyakaḥ |

gajānano gaṇādhyakṣo vināyaka iti śrutaḥ || 18-387 ||

tasya paṃcāṅgamakhilaṃ vakṣyāmi tava prītaye |

299a) tatrādau te pravakṣyāmi mantroddhāraṃ maheśvari || 18-388 ||

yatroddhāraṃ layāṅgaṃ ca mahāgaṇapatiḥ śivaḥ |

atha mantroddhāraḥ |

tāraṃ māsakalā smaro maṭhaśivau nāmnā vareti dvayaṃ saryaḥ 
sarvamatojanaṃdulilate jīmutaśarmākṣaram |

bhekīkhaṃ lavaṇaṃ samīrahasitaṃ mantro cale ṭhadvayaṃ hyaṣṭāviṃśati 
varṇako nigadito vaināyakoya manuḥ || 18-389 ||

iti mūlaṃ tattvalakṣaṃ puraścaryā prakīrttitā |

vaṭe catuṣpathe śūnyagehe pretālaye japet || 18-390 ||

nāsya vighno na vā śocaṃ na vā mantraṃ viparyayaḥ |

nakleśo na ca durvuddhi namoho na ca śokatā || 18-391 ||

sarvavyā siddhamantroyaṃ mahāgaṇapateḥ śive |

yantroddhāraṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvavyā paripūrakaḥ || 18-392 ||

sarvasaṃmohanaṃ cakraṃ gaṇeśasya jagatpate |

trikoṇaṃ khayuktaṃ daśāraṃ maheśi suvṛttaṃ tathā nāgapatraṃ kalāram |

suvṛttaṃ caturdvārayuktaṃ prasiddhaṃ gaṇeśasya śrīcakrametat praguktam || 18-
393 ||

atha dhyānaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvasiddhi pradāyakam |

yena smaraṇamātreṇa sarvavighnaḥ palāyate || 18-394 ||

udyakoṭi divākaraṃ śusadṛśaṃ akṣakirītojalaṃ 
pātraṃ modakapūrṇamidyakakalaśaṃ padmākṣasūtre bhujaiḥ |

vibhrantaṃ śucināgasaṃbhava lasat kāyaṃ bhujaṃgojjvalaṃ 
dhyāye sihayugāsanaṃ gaṇapatiṃ trailokya cintāmaṇim || 18-395 ||

iti dhyānam |

layāṅga sadyavakṣyohaṃ yantrasya parameśvarī |

299b) pūjanaṃ sarvatantreṣu gopitaṃ śarajanmanmaḥ || 18-396 ||

indradharmeśavaruṇāḥ kuverasahitāstathā |

pūrvapaścimayoḥ pūjyā dakṣiṇottarayostathā || 18-397 ||

nandivīreśasaubhāgya bhrṃgirīṭivirāṭakā |

puṣpadantavikārttātpa cittalohitasundarā || 18-398 ||

nūpurāghavicārogra suptabhūpavinarttakā |

gaṇāḥ ṣoḍaśasaṃpūjyā ṣoḍaśāreṣu sundari || 18-399 ||

karālo vikarālaśca saṃhāco rurubhairavaḥ |

mahākālaśca kālāgniḥ sitāsyopyasitānmakaḥ || 18-400 ||

aṣṭapatreṣu deveśi pūjayed aṣṭabhairavān |

vināyakaṃ vighnarājaṃ gaṇādhyakṣaṃ gajānanam || 18-401 ||

heramvaṃ modakāhāra dvaimāturanadiṃ damam |

ekadantaṃ vaktratundaṃ śāreṣu ca pūjayet || 18-402 ||

kumāraṃ ca jayaṃta ca pradyumnaṃ surasundari |

trikoṇe pūjayed vindau mahāgaṇapatiṃ prabhum || 18-403 ||

gaṇeśaṃ caiva gāṃgeya siṃhāsanamataḥ param |

modakāṅkitaṃ pātraṃ ca kṛlaśaṃ paraśuṃ tathā || 18-404 ||

śaṃkhaṃ saṃpūja vindau lapāṅgamidaṃ mucyate |

mantrasmāsya gaṇeśasya ṛṣirbrarhmā prakīrttitaḥ || 18-405 ||

gāyatraṃ chidadratyuktaṃ devo gaṇapati smṛtaḥ |

śivavījaṃ tu vījaṃ syān māyāśaktirudāhṛtā || 18-406 ||

kuru dvayaṃ kālakaṃ syāt sarvaṃ mantraikasādhanam |

trailokya vijaye devi viniyogaḥ prakīrttitaḥ || 18-407 ||

ayutaṃ ca japen mūla vaṭe hutvā citānale |

staṃbhayed akhilāṃ lokān vādidasyu valāni ca || 18-408 ||

ayutaṃ ca japen mūlaṃ hutyāsarpiryanāṃkurāt |

300a) mohaye parameśānaṃ mārayed ripumaṇḍalam || 18-409 ||

nadyā sādhaka evāśu jayed ayutasaṃkhyayā |

juhuyād ibhaviṃ māṃsaṃ ripumuccāṭaye dhruvam || 18-410 ||

home nānena vidhinā japed ayutasaṃkhyayā |

vidveṣaye ca brahmāṇaṃ girije nātrasaṃśayaḥ || 18-411 ||

vane vidurasaṃhutvā japtvā mūlamathāyutam |

vaśayedapi rājānamākarṣayati yoṣitaḥ || 18-412 ||

ityeṣa paṭalo guyhyo mahogaṇapate śiveḥ |

sarva tatraika sarvasvaṃ gopaniyo viśeṣataḥ || 18-413 ||


iti śrīrudrayāmale tantre mantroddhāravidhirnāmapaṭalaḥ |


athāhaṃ paddhatiṃ cakṣyo mahāgaṇapateḥ śiveḥ |

gadyaikasārasarvasvām ānandaikataraṃgiṇim || 18-414 ||


sādhako rātri śeṣa uttthāya vaddhapadmāsanaṃ mūlena trirācamya svaśirasi 
sahasradala mahāpadmakeśano jalakarṇikāntargataṃ śuklālaṃkāraṃ nijaguru 
dhyātvā tachāsavamādāya vahirāgatya malāpakarṣaṇaṃ snānaṃ vidhāya 
mūlenāṃkuśamudrayā tatra bhrikoṇaṃ likhitvā | 

gaṃge ca yamune caiva godāvarisarasvati |

narmade sindhukoveri jalesmin saṃnidhi kuru || 18-415 ||

iti tīrthamāvāhya devaṃ saśivaṃ mūle nāvāhya mūle na sukhaṃ triḥ prokṣa 
trīrānmāna payasi pronmantraḥ hrī/ haṃsaḥ śrīsūryāya eṣa terdyo namaḥ |
ityarghatrayaṃ datvā jalādavaruhya sandhyāṃ kuryāt |

300b) nato vā kare nalaṃ dhṛtvā varmaṇā va guṇṭhya mūlena 
saptadhābhimantrya tad gati todakavinduḥ bhiḥ prokṣya i upāntarnītvā 
vāmanāsayā viricya suṣumnāyā dakṣahaste dhṛtvā phaḍiti mantreṇa vāme 
nāgastha sitvā yāmā sphālayed ityaghamarṣaṇam | tataḥ prāṇāyāmaṃ tra 
kuryāt | puraka (16) | kuṃbhakam (32) | recakam (64) | evaṃ vidhāya gāyatrīṃ 
japet |

oṃ hrī/ vaktratuṇḍāya vidmahe śrī/ klī/ ekadaṃṣṭrāya dhīmahe glau/ ga/ tannaḥ 
gaṇapatiḥ pracodayāt |

iti yathāśaktyā japtā goyratryā devī devayorarghyatrayaṃ datvā mūlena samasta 
bhuvanāntarāpaharaṇaṃ haraputraṃ gaṇagandharvasiddha vandinataṃdi prakaṭaṃ 
vikaṭānanaṃ parāpararūpaṃ gaṇādhyakṣaṃ gajānanaṃ mahāgaṇapatiṃ 
tarpayāmi namaḥ |

iti triḥsantarpya saharamudrayā tatra devau visṛjya yāgamaṇḍapamāgachediti 
sandhyā vidhiḥ |

yogamaṃdupatyesa |

hrā/ śrī/ klī/ ga/ gaṃgāyai namaḥ pūrve (3) |

yaṃ yamunāyai namaḥ | uttare (3) |

saṃ sarasvatyai nama dakṣo (3) |

yaṃ yoginībhyo namaḥ paścime (3) |

kṣā/ kṣetrapālāya namaḥ | madhye 

iti dvāradevatāḥ saṃpūjya gṛhāntaḥ praviśyāsanaṃ śodhayet |

āsanaśuddhimantrasya meruṣṭaṛṣiḥ sutalaṃ chandaḥ kurmo devatā 
āsanamantreṇa viniyogaḥ |

ai/ pṛthvivyaiḥ namaḥ | e/ aṃ anantāya namaḥ |
vaṃ vasudharāyai namaḥ | 


pṛthvi tvayā dhṛtā lokāstvaṃ devi viṣṇunā dhṛtā |

301) tvaṃ ca dhāraya māṃ devi pavitraṃ kuru cāsanam || 18-416 ||


oṃ hrī/ śrī/ klī/ padmāsanāya namaḥ (3) | si/hāsanāya namaḥ |
bhadrāsanāya namaḥ | iti |


apasarpantu te bhūtā ye bhūtā bhūvisaṃsthitā |

ye bhūtā vighnakarttāraste naśyantu śivājñayā || 18-417 ||


iti tālatrayaṃ datvā nārāca mudrāṃ pradarśya guruṃ dhyāyet |


akhaṇḍamaṇḍalākāraṃ vyāptaṃ yena carācaram |

tanyadaṃ darśite yena tasmai śrīguruve nama || 18-418 ||


iti guruṃ dhyātvācamet |

gau/ ātmatattvaṃ śodhayāmi svāhā | vidyātattva śodhayāmi svāhā (2) |

śivatattvaṃ śodhayāmi svāhā | śivaśaktitattvaṃ śodhayāmi svāhā |

ityācamya pūrvoktaṃ prāṇā vāyuṃ prokṣa oṃ klī/ tatrādhiṣṭānamānīya 
svaśarīraṃ bhasmībhūta vicintyaṃ yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ brahma 
yathāntarjīvātmānaṃ viciṃtma tatra haṃsa iti sudhāmānīyatayā saṃplāvya 
vigatakalmaṣaṃ dehaṃ vibhāvyasthā tajīvamānīya prāṇān pratiṣṭāpayed iti 
bhūtaśuddhiḥ |

ā/ hrī/ kro/ ya/ ra/ la/ va/ śa/ ṣa/ sa/ ha/ so/ ha/ saḥ mama prāṇa (16) |

iha mama jīva iha sthitaḥ mama sarvendriyāṇi vān manacakṣuḥ śrīttra jihvā 
ghrāṇā prāṇā ihaivāgatya sukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭantu svāhā |

iti prāṇapratiṣṭā |

tataḥ punarācamya prāṇāyāmaṃ vidhāya sapūrvakaṃ saṃkalpaṃ kuryāt |

asya śrīvaradagaṇeśamantrasya brahmā ṛṣi gāyatraṃ chandaḥ 
śrīgaṇapatirdevatā gaṃ vījaṃ hrī/ śakti kuru kuru kīlakaṃ śrīmahāgaṇa 
prītyarthaṃ jape viniyogaḥ |

301b) brahmaṛṣaye namaḥ śirasiḥ | gāyatraṃ chandase namo mukhe |

mahāgaṇapati devatāyai namaḥ hṛdaye | gaṃ vījāya namo nābhau |

hrī/ śaktaye namaḥ guhye | kuru kuru kīlāya namaḥ pādayo |

trivagasiddhyarthe jape viniyogā namaḥ | sarvāṅgeṣu |

gā/ hṛdayāya namaḥ | gī/ śirase svāhā | gu/ śikhāyai vaṣaṭ |

gai kavacāya hum | gau netrebhyo vauṣaṭ | gaḥ astrāya phaṭ |

iti hṛdayā nyāsaḥ | atha karanyāsāḥ |

gā/ aṃguṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ | gī/ tarjanībhyā/ namaḥ |

grū/ madhyamābhya/ namaḥ | gai anāmikābhyā/ namaḥ |

gau kaniṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ | ga karatalapṛṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ |

iti karanyāsaḥ |

ātmatattvaṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ | vidyātattvaṃ śirase svāhā |

śivatattvaṃ śikhāya vaṣaṭ | gurutattvaṃ kavacāya hum |

yogatattvaṃ netrebhyo vauṣaṭ | śivatattvaṃ astrāya phaṭ |

ityaṃga nyāsaḥ | evaṃ karanyāsaḥ | iti tattvanyāsaḥ |

aṃ kaṃ khaṃ gaṃ ghaṃ ṅaṃ ā/ a/guṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ |

iṃ caṃ chaṃ jaṃ jhaṃ ñaṃ iti tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ |

uṃ ṭa/ ṭha/ ḍa/ ḍha/ ṇa/ ū/ madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ |

e/ taṃ tha/ da/ dha/ na/ ai/ anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ |

o/ paṃ phaṃ baṃ bhaṃ maṃ o/ kaniṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ |

aṃ yaṃ raṃ laṃ vaṃ śaṃ ṣaṃ saṃ haṃ laṃ kṣaṃ(ḥ) | aḥ 
karatalapṛṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ |

evaṃ hṛ nyāsasaḥ |

302a) gaṃ kāśīpīṭhāya namaḥ | hṛdaye |

gaṃ kāṃcīpurīpīṭhāya namaḥ śirasi |

gaṃ madhupurīpiṭhāya namaḥ | śikhāyām |

gaṃ uḍḍāyānapīṭhāya namaḥ | kavace |

gaṃ ayodhyāpīṭhāya namaḥ netre |

gaṃ jālaṃdharapīṭhāya namaḥ astre |

iti pīṭhanyāsaḥ |

oṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ | śrīmahāgataye śirase svāhā |

kāṃ varavaradaśikhāyai vaṣaṭ | klī/ sarvaṃ janaṃ mekavacāya hum |

glau vaśamānaye netrebhyo ṣaṭ | gaṃ svāhā astrāya phaṭ |

evaṃ karaṃ nyāsaḥ |

evaṃ nyāsaṃ vidhāya śrīcakraṃ chyātvā caturaśraṃ ṣoḍaśadalaṃ 
vasudalaṃ daśāraṃ trikoṇaṃ vinduṃ vicintyaṃ bhadrapīṭhoparimūlena 
saṃsthāpya yogapīṭha pūjāṃ kuryāt |

hrī/ maṃḍūkāya namaḥ | kālāgni rudradrāya namaḥ |

mūlaprakṛtyai namaḥ (1) | ādhāraśaktyai namaḥ |

kurmāya namaḥ | anantāya namaḥ (1) | varāhāya namaḥ (1) |

pṛthivyai namaḥ | urpyuparisaṃpūjya tanmadhye sudhārṇavāya namaḥ |

macyaṃ na ca ratnavirājita navakhaṇḍamayeścetahīpāya namaḥ |

tatre śānādisraṣṭasu prādakṣiṇyena navaratnakhaṇḍebhyo namaḥ |

etan madhye padmarāgakhaṇḍāya namaḥ |

madhye suvarṇaparvatāya namaḥ | madhye nandanodmānāya namaḥ |

madhye valā valāvanāya namaḥ | madhyanandanoghānāṃya namaḥ |

madhye sarovarāya namaḥ | madhye padmavanāya namaḥ |

madhye vicitraratnakhacitabhūmikāyai namaḥ |

302b) madhe sahasrastaṃbhānvitamadhyastaṃbha vivarjita catuṣpaṃcāśad 
yojanavistīrṇa nānāratnakhakhitacintāmaṇimaṇḍapāya namaḥ |

madhye navaratnakhacita ratnamayavedikāyai namaḥ |

upari ratnasiṃhāsanāya namaḥ upari uccaiḥ śvetā chatrāya namaḥ |

pāṭhe smakalvitāgnikoṇādiṣu dharmādibhyo namaḥ |

kalpita pūrvādiṣu adharmādibhyo namaḥ | madhya |

ānandakandāya saṃcinnalāya namaḥ | sahasradalakamalāsanāya namaḥ |

prakṛtimaye sarebhyo nama | vikṛtimaya patnebhyo namaḥ |

paṃcāśadvarṇavījāḍhya sarvatattvarūpāyai karṇikāyai namaḥ |

upari aṃ arkamaṇḍalāya namaḥ | saṃ somamaṇḍalāya namaḥ |

raṃ agnimaṇḍalāya namaḥ | taṃ tamase namaḥ | raṃ jajase namaḥ |

saṃ satvāya namaḥ hrī/ ga/ hrī/ sarvamantrāsanāya namaḥ |

oṃ amityā dikṣātamucārya śivaśakti sadāveśvara mahāgaṇapatī sakalātmane 
namaḥ saṃsatvāya namaḥ |

iti yogapīṭhapūjāṃ kṛtvā yātrāṇi sthāpayet |

svavāmabhāgatrikoṇa ṣaṭkoṇa vṛtta caturaśrarūpaṃ yantraṃ vilikhya 
śaṃkhamudrāṃ pradarśyagāmiti ṣaḍaṃgamantraiḥ saṃpūjya astrāya phaḍīti 
pādikāṃ prakṣālya mūlena saṃsthāpya raṃ vahni maṇḍalāya daśakalātmane 
namaḥ |

iti mahāgaṇapati pātrāsanāya namaḥ |

iti gaṃdhākṣatairabhyarcya |

303a) tadupari pātraṃ saṃsthāpya |

sūryamaṇḍalāya dvādaśakalātmane namaḥ |

mahāgaṇapatyaghapātrāya namaḥ |

iti gaṃdhākṣatairatyarbhya tato kuśamudrayā gaṃgeti tīryanyāvāhya 
mūlāṣṭabhirmantraṣaḍaṃgai svaparipūjya matsyamudrayā chādya phaḍiti 
choḍiti choṭikābhiḥ saṃrabhyadgati tyavaguṇṭharamamityamṛtī kṛtya 
yoniśaṃkhacakramusulamudrāḥ pradarśva yāgava smṛti tena saṃprokṣa 
mūlaparamāmṛta dūpeṇa mahāgaṇapati caṇḍalani vā sāyaṃ cadrāmṛte na 
pūraya pūraya dravyaṃ pūraya pūrayaḥ amṛti kuru kuru au/ hau/ ju/ saḥ svāhā |

anena sarvaprokṣeyet |

taduttare pādyācamanīya madhuparkapātrāṇi saṃsthāpya saṃpūjyātmānaṃ 
tanmayaṃ vibhāvya |

o/ namo bhagavate sakalaguṇātmaśaktiyuktā yānantāya yogapīṭhātmane namaḥ |

iti saṃpūjya saśaktikaṃ devaṃ mūle nāvāhya mūlena navamudrāḥ pradarśya 
mūlaṣaḍaṅga kṛtvā prāṇān pratiṣṭā svāgatamityudīrya mūlena 
pājyārghyaṃ datvā evaṃ pādyārghyācanīya madhuparkasnānādi vidhāya 
mātṛkākṣarairabhyarcyatai dhūrpadīpanaivedyādīn nidyapuṣpāṃjalitrayaṃ 
datvā |

oṃ hrī/ śrī/ indrāya namaḥ | pūrve (3) |

vahaṇāya namaḥ | paścime |

dharmarājāya namaḥ dakṣiṇe (3) |

kuverāya namaḥ | uttare |

iti gaṃdhākṣata dūrvābhirabhyarcya |

prathamāvaraṇam |

mūlena puṣpāṃjaliṃ datvā dakṣāvarttena ṣoḍaśavāraṃ pūjayet |

303b) oṃ hrī/ śrī/ mandine namaḥ | vīreśāya namaḥ (3) | saubhāgyāya namaḥ |

bhṛṃgirīḍāya namaḥ (3) | viṇṭhakāya namaḥ (3) | puṣpadantāya namaḥ |

vikarttanāya namaḥ (3) | aṃbhyagaṇeśāya namaḥ | cintarāṇāya namaḥ (3) |

lohitāya namaḥ (3) | sundarāya namaḥ (3) | nupurāḍhyāya namaḥ (3) |

vicārāya namaḥ | ugrahayāya namaḥ (3) | suptāya namaḥ (3) |

vinartakāya namaḥ (3) |

iti gandhākṣatairabhyarcya dvitīyā caraṇam (3) |

mūlena puṣpāṃjali datvā |

oṃ śrī/ hrī/ karālāya namaḥ (3) | vikarālāya namaḥ | saṃhārāya namaḥ |

ru ru bhairavāya namaḥ (3) | mahākālāya namaḥ (3) | kālāgnaye namaḥ (3) |

sitānmane namaḥ | ahitātmane namaḥ | 

iti tṛtīyāvaraṇam (3) |

o/ hrī/ śrī/ vināyakāya namaḥ (2) | vighnarājāya namaḥ |

gaṇādhyakṣāya namaḥ | gajānanāya namaḥ (3) |

heraṃbhāya namaḥ | mohakāhārāya namaḥ (3) |

dvaimāturāya namaḥ (3) | arindamārāya namaḥ |

ekadanttāya nama (3) | vakratuṇḍāya namaḥ |

iti gandhākṣatadūrvābhiḥ saṃpūjya dakṣavarttenā caturthāvaraṇam (4) |

daśāreṣu mūlena puṃṣpāṃjaliṃ datvā |

o/ śrī/ hrī/ kumārāya namaḥ (3) | agre jayantāya namaḥ (3) |

dakṣe pradyumnāya namaḥ | 

iti gandhākṣatadūrvābhiḥ trikoṇaṃ saṃpūjya pañcamāvaraṇam (5) |

304a) mūlena puṣpāṃjaliṃ datvā mūlavidyāmucārya |

hrī/ svarākrisahitāya gaṇapataye namaḥ |


iti saptadhā saṃpūjya ṣaṣṭāvaraṇam |


puṣpāṃjaliṃ datvā mūlam | 

gāṃgeyāya namaḥ | mūlasihāsanāya namaḥ |

mūlaṃ vighnāntakāya namaḥ | mūlaḥ ākhuvāhanāya namaḥ | mūla |

tripuratnakāya nama |

ityuparigandhākṣata dūrvābhiḥ saṃpūjya saptamāvaraṇam |

puṣpāṃjaliṃ datvā uparimūlaṃ śatayatvāya namaḥ |

datnamālāyai namaḥ | sudhākalaśāya namaḥ | paraśave namaḥ (1) |

śaṃkhāya namaḥ | paṃcajanyāya namaḥ |

darśanāpi śaṃkhasya kiṃ punaḥ sparśanena ca | vilayaṃ yānti pāpāni 
himavahāskarodaye |

iti saptadhā saṃpūjyāṣṭamāvaraṇam (8) |

tato mūlamucārya gandhākṣatadūrvābhiḥ puṣpadhūpadīpanaivedyocamanīya 
tāṃvūlachatracāmarāt saṃsthāpya devāgre ṣaḍaṅga pūrvanyāsaṃ vidhāya 
deya devaṃ dhyātvā mūlena mālāmabhyarcya yathāśaktyā mūlaṃ ḍaga 
pūrva nyāsaṃ vidhāya devaṃ dhyātvā mūlena mālāmabhyarcya 
yathāśaktyā mūlaṃ japan |

tato mūlamuṃcārya davā yajayaṃ samarpya kavaca sahasranāmastava pāṭhaṃ 
devāgre kṛtvā |


prātaḥ prabhṛti sā yātaṃ sā yādi prātaraṃ tataḥ |

yat karomi jagaṃnnātha tadastu tava pūjanam || 18-419 ||


iti saṃhāramudrayā saśaktikaṃ devaṃ visarjjayet |

iti śrīnitya pūjāyā paddhatiṃ gadyarūpiṇīm |

mahāgaṇapaterdivyāṃ sarvathā devi gopayet |

iti rudrayāmalatantre śrīgaṇapati paddhati saṃpūrṇam |


śrībharava uvāca

mahādevi gaṇeśasya varadasya mahātmana |

kavacante pravakṣyāmi vajrapaṃjarakābhidham || 18-420 ||


asya śrīmahāgaṇapati vajrapaṃjarakavacasya śrībhairavaṛṣiḥ gāyatrī chandaḥ 
śrīmahāgaṇapatirdevatā gaṃ vījaṃ hrī/ śaktiḥ kuru kīlakaṃ vajravidyādi 
siddhyarthaṃ mahāgaṇapati vajrapaṃjara kavace pāṭhe viniyogaḥ |


oṃ śrī hrī/ ga/ śiraḥ pātu mahāgaṇapatiḥ prabhuḥ |

vināyako lalāṭe vighnarājo phavau mama || 18-421 ||

yātutre gaṇādhyakṣo nāsīkāṃ megajānataḥ |

stutīmeva tu heraṃbho gaṇumemodakaśana || 18-422 ||

dvai māturo mukhaṃ pātu cādharopātvaridama |

dantān mamaikadante vyādvakutuṇḍe vatādrasām || 18-423 ||

gāṃgeyo megalaṃ pātu skandhau siṃhāsano va tu |

vighnāntako tujau pātu hastau mūṣakavānaḥ || 18-424 ||

uro mamāvatān nityaṃ devaḥ tripuratāpanaḥ |

hṛdayaṃ me kumāro syā japantaḥ pārśvayugmakam || 18-425 ||

pradyumno mevatāt pṛṣṭaṃ nābhiṃ śaṃkaranandana |

kaṭinandigaṇaḥ pātu śiśraṃ vīreśvarova tu || 18-426 ||

medre meva tu saubhāgyo tṛṅgirīṭī ca guhyakam |

vināyako vatādūrūjānu me puṣpadantaka || 18-427 ||

jaṃghe mama vikarttovyāṅgulphāvanya gaṇāva tu |

305a) pādau cittagaṇaḥ pātu pādādo lohito va tu || 18-428 ||

pādapṛṣṭaṃ sundaro vyānnupurādaro va yurmamaḥ |

vismāritaṃ ca yatsthānaṃ gaṇeśastat sadāva tu || 18-429 ||

pūrve māṃ hrī/karā lovyādāgne ye vikarālakaḥ |

dakṣiṇe pātu me skaṃdau naite ru ru bhairavaḥ || 18-430 ||

uttare māsadāsyo vyādaiśānyā masitātmakaḥ |

prabhāte śatapavovyāt sahasrārastu madhyate || 18-431 ||

dantamālādināṃ te vyān niśipātraṃ sadāva tu |

kalaśo māṃ niśīthe vyān niśāṃnte paraśustathā || 18-432 ||

sarvatra sarvadā pātu śaṃkhayugmaṃ ca madvayuḥ |


o/ au/ rājakule harāraṇabhaye hrī/ hrī/ kudyūtevatām |

śrī/ śrī/ śakragṛhe śaśau jalabhaye klī/ klī/ vanāṃsevatu |

glau/ gaṃ glai/ gusatvabhītiṣu mahavyādhārttiṣu glau/ ga gāṃ nityaṃ 
yakṣyapiśācabhūtaphaṇiṣu glau/ ga gaṇeśova tu |


itidaṃ kavacaṃ guhya sarvatantreṣu gopitam || 18-433 ||

vajrapaṃjaranāmai tad gaṇeśasya mahātmanaḥ |

aṃgabhūtaṃ tatra mayaṃ sarvācāraikasādhanam || 18-434 ||

vinānena siddhih syāt pūjanasya jayasya ca |

tasmā tu kavacaṃ puṇyaṃ paṭhed vāradhārayet sadā || 18-435 ||

tasya siddhirmahādevi karasyā pālalaukikī |

yaṃ yaṃ kāmayate kāmaṃ taṃ taṃ prāpnoti pāṭataḥ || 18-436 ||

ardharātraṃ paṭhennityaṃ sarvābhiṣṭaphalaṃ labhet |

iti mūjraṃsukavacaṃ mahāgapatepi ye |

sarvasiddhi mayaṃ pūjyaṃ gopayen parameśvarī || 18-437 ||


305b) iti śrīrudrayāmale tantre mahāgaṇapate gaṇeśakavacaṃ sampūrṇam |

atha gaṇeśasahasranāma |


bhairava uvāca 

śṛṇu devī rahasyaṃ me yan mayā parikīrttitam |

tava bhaktyā gaṇeśasya vakṣyaināmasahasrakam || 18-438 ||

śrīdevyuvāca

bhagavan gaṇanāthāya varadasya mahātmana |

śrotuṃ nāmasahasraṃ me hṛdayaṃ cotsukāyate || 18-439 ||

bhairava uvāca

prā'metripuranāśa tu jātā vighnakulāḥ | śive |

mohena muhyatennante so sarve valadarpitā || 18-440 ||

tadā prabhuṃ gaṇādhyakṣaṃ stutvā nāmasahasrakaiḥ |

tadadya tava bhaktyāhaṃ sādhakānāṃ hitāya ca || 18-441 ||


asya śrīvaradagaṇeśa sahasranāma stotrasya śrībhairavaṛṣi gāyatrichandaḥ 
śrīmahāgaṇapatirdevatā gaṃ vījaṃ hrī/ śakti kuru kuru kīlakaṃ 
trivargasiddhyarthe pāṭhe viniyogaḥ |


o/ śrī/ hrī/ klī/ gaṇādhyakṣo glau/ gaṃ gaṇapatirguṇaḥ |

guṇāḍhyoti gurṇā goptā gajavaktro vibhāvasuḥ || 18-442 ||

viśveśvarī vibhā dīpto dīpanordhā va rodhanī |

sadīśānto jagattrātā viśvakseno vibhākaraḥ || 18-443 ||

visraṃbho vijayo vaidyo vārāṃ nidhiranuttamaḥ |

aṇīyānvibhavā śreṣṭohyeṣṭo gāthā priyo guruḥ || 18-444 ||

sṛṣṭigarbhā jagaddhartā viśvabharttā jagatpatiḥ |

patiḥ pītaṃ vibhūṣāṅgo raktākṣo lohitsavaraḥ || 18-445 ||

virūpākṣā vimānastho vinayaḥ sunayaḥ sukhī |

306a) svarūpasātvi va satyaḥ śuddhaḥ śaṃkaranandanaḥ || 18-446 ||

nadiśvaro mahānandi vadistutyo vicakṣaṇa |

daityamardi sadākṣīvo madirārūṇalocana || 18-447 ||

mārātmā viśvadhāri ca viśvakoro vilepana |

paraṃbrahma paraṃjyoti sākṣitryakṣo vikarttanaḥ || 18-448 ||

vīreśvaro vīrahantā saubhāgyabhogavarddhana |

bhṛṃgirīḍhī bhṛṃgimāli bhṛṃgikrūji tanādita || 18-449 ||

vivartako vinītāpi vinatānandaḥ tā citi |

vainate yo vinagnāṃgo viśvanāyakanāyaka || 18-450 ||

virāṭako viṛāṭaśca vipadho vidhirātmabhū |

puṣpadantaḥ puṣpahāri puṣpamālyavibhūṣaṇa || 18-451 ||

puṣpeṣu mathanaḥ praṣṭo vikarttā karttarī karaḥ |

abhyāntako vittagaṇacittavittāpahāraka || 18-452 ||

acintyā cintyarūpāśca pedanākulamaṇḍalā |

lohito lopito lupto lohitākṣo vilobhakaḥ || 18-453 ||

lubdhā śayo lobharato lobhadolaṃghya gātrakaḥ |

sundaraḥ sundarī putraḥ samastā suraghātakaḥ || 18-454 ||

nūpurādyo vibhavado naranārāyaṇo ravi |

vicāro vāntado vāgni vicāri vijayeśvaraḥ || 18-455 ||

supto vuddhaḥ sadārūpaḥ sukhadaḥ sukhasevitaḥ |

vikartṛno viyacārī vinado naṭanartṛkaḥ || 18-456 ||

naṭo nāṭyapriyo nādo nandonantaḥ guṇātmaka |

vibhūśvasūrviśvaghātī ca vinatāsyo vinarttaka || 18-457 ||

karālaḥ kāmadaḥ kāmadaḥ kāntaḥ kamanīyaḥ kalādharaḥ |

kāruṇya rūpaḥ kuṭilaḥ kulacārī kuleśvarā || 18-458 ||

vikarāro raṇaśreṣṭaḥ saṃhāro hārabhūṣaṇaḥ |

unmattarūpakālāgniragnisūryendu locanaḥ || 18-459 ||

sitāsya sitamālyaścaḥ sitadantaḥ sitāṃśumān |

āsitātmā bhairave saubhāgyavān bhagavānbhaga || 18-460 ||

bhargātmajo bhagāvāso bhogadā bhavavarddhana |

śubhaṃkaraḥ śuciḥ śānta śreṣṭaśravyaḥ śacīpati || 18-461 ||

vedādyo vedakarttā ca vedavaidyaḥ sanātanaḥ |

vidyā prado vedaraso vaidiko vedapāragaḥ || 18-462 ||

vedadhvanirato vīro vedāgamārtha pāravit |

tatvajñaḥ sarvagaḥ sādhuḥ sadasatmayaḥ vigraha || 18-463 ||

nirāmaya yonirākāro nirbhayotit parūpabhṛt |

nirvairo vaurividhvaṃsī mattavāraṇasaṃnibhaḥ || 18-464 ||

śivakaraḥ śivasutaḥ śivānandavivardhanam |

śītaḥ śvetāśritamukho mugdho modakabhojanaḥ || 18-465 ||

deva devo dinakaro dhṛti mānudyutimādhavaḥ |

śuddhātmā śuddhamatimānu śuddhadīptiśucivrataḥ || 18-466 ||

śaraṇyaśaunakaḥ śūla śaradaṃ bhojadārakaḥ |

dārakaḥ śiśuratyugraḥ śitaśaṃkarapūjitaḥ || 18-467 ||

śaṃkaronirbhayo vasyo bhayakṛllāsya tatparaḥ |

lūtolīlā rasollāsī vilāśivibhramo bhramaḥ || 18-468 ||

bhramaṇaḥ śaśibhūḥ sūryaḥ śanirdharasminandanaḥ |

vadho vivudhasevyaśca vudharājo va dharaḥ || 18-469 ||

jīvo jīvaprado jaitraḥ stutyo nutyo nati priyaḥ |

janakotina mārgajño jainamārganivarttakaḥ || 18-470 ||

gaurīsuto gururavo gaurāṅgo gajapūjitaḥ |

307a) paraṃ paraṃ paraṃhāma paramātmā kaviḥ kujaḥ || 18-471 ||

rāhurdaitya śirachedī ketuḥ kanakakuṇḍalaḥ |

grahendro grāhito grāhyo guṇī ghurghuranāditaḥ || 18-472 ||

parjanya pīvanaḥ potrī pīnavakṣo parājitaḥ |

vanecaro ca na patirvanavāsmī smaro paraḥ || 18-473 ||

puṇyaḥ pūtaḥ pavintradeva parātmā pūrṇavigraha |

pūrṇendurākarā kāro māraḥ pūrṇamanorathaḥ || 18-474 ||

yogyātmā yogabhṛdyajvā yājñiko yajñavatsalaḥ |

yaśasvī yajamāneṣṭo vajrabhṛdvajrapaṃjaraḥ || 18-475 ||

maṇibhadro naṇimayo mānyomīnadhvajāśritaḥ |

mīnadhvajo manohāri yogajño yogavarddhanaḥ || 18-476 ||

drasṭā sraṣṭā tapasvī ca vigrahī tāpasapriyaḥ |

tayomayastapamūtistapanaśca tapodhanaḥ || 18-477 ||

rūcako mocako bhraṣṭastuṣṭastomaraghātakaḥ |

daṇḍijaṃghasuravyaktaḥ kamaṇḍaludharo naghaḥ || 18-478 ||

kāmikarmarataḥ kālakelyā kraṃditadika tataḥ |

bhrāmako janapūjyaśca jādyahā jaḍasūdanaḥ || 18-479 ||

jāladharo jagadhāsī hāsya kṛddhavalo hari |

haviṣmān havyavāhākhyo hāṭako hāṭakāṃgada || 18-480 ||

sumeru himavānhotā haraputro halaṃkaṣa |

hālā priyo hṛdisthaśca kāntā hṛdayapoṣaṇaḥ || 18-481 ||

śoṣaṇaḥ kleśahā krūraḥ kaṭhorakaṭhina kṛti |

kuvaro dhīmayo dhyetādhyayo dhīmāndayā vidhi || 18-482 ||

daviṣṭo madanobhyasto dātā trātā sitasamaḥ |

nirgato naigamāgasyo nirjano jaṭilojanaḥ || 18-483 ||

307b) jananīvo jitārātirjagadvyāyī jaganmayaḥ |

cāmīkarabhibhonādyo nalinā yata locana || 18-484 ||

rocanā mocanī mantri mantrakoṭisamāśritaḥ |

paṃcabhūtātmakaḥ pacasāyakaḥ pacavaktrakaḥ || 18-485 ||

paṃcamaḥ paścimapūrvakīrṇalakaḥ kuṇi |

kacārahṛdayo grīvā laṃkṛto lalitāśayaḥ || 18-486 ||

lolacitto vṛhannāso māsapakṣartu bhūpavān |

dhruvo dhruvagatirdharmyo dharmīnāda priyo na saḥ || 18-487 ||

agastyograstabhuvano bhuvanaikamalāpahaḥ |

sāgaraḥ sugatisvakṣaḥ sānandaḥ sādhudhūjitaḥ || 18-488 ||

satīpatiḥ samarasaḥ sanakaḥ saralaḥ suraḥ |

surapriyo vasupatirvāsavo vasupūjitaḥ || 18-489 ||

vistado vittanāthasya dhanināṃ dhanadāyakaḥ |

rājirājivanayanaḥ smṛtidaḥ kṛtikāṃvadaḥ || 18-490 ||

aśvinośvimukhaḥ śubhro bharaṇobharaṇapriyaḥ |

kṛtikāsanakaḥ kolo rohi rohanayā dukaḥ || 18-491 ||

vibhaveṣṭorimardī ca rohiṇī mohanomṛta |

mṛgarājā mṛgaśirā mādhavo madhuro dhvani || 18-492 ||

ārdrākaro mahāvuddhirmahovuddhirmahoragavibhūṣiṇa |

bhūkṣepadantavibhavo bhūkarālapunarvasuḥ || 18-493 ||

tittiristimiketuśca timitārakaghātakaḥ |

tiṣyastulādharo jeyo viṣṇe ṣośeṣadevarāṭ || 18-494 ||

mānado mādhavo māgho vācālo macavo mataḥ |

māgho maghā priyo mogho mahāmuṇḍo mahābhujaḥ || 18-495 ||

pūrvaphālguṇikaḥ sphītaḥ phalgurūttara phālguṇaḥ |

pheṇilo brahmado brahmā saptatantu samāśraya || 18-496 ||

308a) ghonahastaścaturhasto hastivaktro halā yudhaḥ |

citrāṃvarorcitapadaḥ śubhadaḥ svātivigraha || 18-497 ||

śikhākhaśikhise dhvajasahodaraḥ-------------- |

aṇureṇuḥ kaṇasphāro rurureṇu sutonaraḥ || 18-498 ||

anūrādhā priyo rādhaḥ śrīmānuśukraśucismitaḥ |

śucipūrvastathā ṣāḍho hyutarāṣāḍha eva ca || 18-499 ||

śravaṇaḥ śrotādhanī dhanyo dhaniṣṭakaḥ |

śātātapaḥ śātakaumbhaśatijyoti śataṃbhiṣak || 18-500 ||

pūrvo bhādra uttarobhādro revatī pratipāditaḥ |

reṇukā tanayo rāme rivatī ramaṇorakaḥ || 18-501 ||

rājyado bhiṣagātmīyastāraśastārakākṛti |

pratitaḥ prodyataḥ protaḥ pārasaḥ paramohitaḥ || 18-502 ||

parahā pañcabhūpañca vāyupūjyaḥ paraṃmaha |

purāṇāgamavadyojño māhiṣo rāsabhogragaḥ || 18-503 ||

graho meṣā vṛṣo maṃdo manmatho mithunārcitaḥ |

kalkabhūḥ karkaṭo dīno markaṭaḥ karkaṭāghṛṇī || 18-504 ||

kukkuṭo vanajo haṃsaḥ paraṃhaṃsaḥ sṛgālakaḥ |

siṃhaḥ siṃhāsano bhūṣī mohimuṣakavāhanaḥ || 18-505 ||

putrado narakatrātā kanyā prītaḥ kulodvahaḥ |

atulya bhūyo varadastulābhṛtulyasākṣikaḥ || 18-506 ||

alicāpadharo pajvī kachayo makaromaśi |

kuṃbha kṛt kṛlaśī kubjo mīnamāṃsa sutarpitaḥ || 18-507 ||

rāśitārā grahamayaṃ tithibhūyo jagatprabhuḥ |

pratāpī pratiyat preyo dvitīyo dvaitaniścitaḥ || 18-508 ||


trirūpaśca tṛtīyāgvistrayī tanu caturthī vallabho deva pāragaḥ pacamīravaḥ |


308b) ṣadrasā svādako jñota ṣaṣṭiṣaṣṭikavatsala |

saptārṇavagatisāraḥ saptamīśvara īśvaraḥ || 18-509 ||

aṣṭamī nandanonanto navamī bhaktipūjitaḥ |

daśadikapati pūjyataśca daśamī drohiṇo mataḥ || 18-510 ||

ekādaśā sā gaṇayo dvādaśīya va carcitaḥ |

trayodaśamanustulyaścaturdaśasvarapriye || 18-511 ||

caturthaśendrasantulyaḥ pūrṇimā nanivigrahaḥ |

daśodarśo darśanaśca vānaprastho munīśvaraḥ || 18-512 ||

maunī madhuravāgmūla mūrttimānmeghavāhanaḥ |

mahāgajo jitakrodho jitaśakrajapāśraya || 18-513 ||

raudro rudrapriyo rukmoruputrodyatāpanaḥ |

bhava priyo bhavānīṣṭo bhārabhṛdbhūtavāhanaḥ || 18-514 ||


gāndharvakuśalakuṇṭho vaikuṇṭho sevita vṛtuhā vighnahāsīraḥ 
samastaduritāpaha |


maṃjaro mārjaromatto durgāputro durālasaḥ |

anantavit sudhā sāro vīravīryaikavīryadaḥ || 18-515 ||

sāsvanmukuṭamāṇikya kūbjatkiṃkiṇijālakaḥ |

śuṇḍādhārī cañcalaśca kuṇḍālī muṇḍamālikaḥ || 18-516 ||

padmākṣapadmahastaśca padmanāmasamarcitaḥ |

udgīto naradantāḍhya mālābhūṣaṇabhūṣitāḥ || 18-517 ||

nārado varaṇolāla śravaṇaḥ śūnyakarṇakaḥ |

vṛhadullāsanā sāgra vyāptatrailokyamaṇḍalaḥ || 18-518 ||

iḍāmaṇḍalasaṃbhrānta kṛtā tyugraraṇānandarasālayaḥ |

vṛhaddhasta dhṛtā śeṣā yudhanirjitadānavaḥ || 18-519 ||

309a) sphurat siṃdūravadanaḥ sphuratnejognilocanaḥ |

uddīpitaṃ maṇisphurjayūgrarādhvaninādinaḥ || 18-520 || 18-(?)

calatoya pravyahāṭya nadījalakaṇākaraḥ |

bhramat kujarasaṃghāta vanditāṅghrisaroruhaḥ || 18-521 ||

brahmācyutamahārudraḥ puraḥsara surārcite |

anantaśeṣaprabhṛti vyālajālopasevitaḥ || 18-522 ||

gūrjatyaṃ cānanārāva prāptākāśadharānala |

hā hā hū hū kṛtātyugra svaravibhrāntamānasaḥ || 18-523 ||

paṃcāśadvarṇavījāḍhya mantramantritavigrahaḥ |

vedānta śāstrapīyuṣadhārā plāvitabhūtala || 18-524 ||

saṃṣadhvani samākrānta pātālādi nabhastalaḥ |

cintāmaṇi mahāmallo bhallahasto valipriyaḥ || 18-525 ||

kṛta tretā yugollāsi bhāsamānajagattraya |

dvāparaḥ paralokaika karmadhvānta sudhākaraḥ || 18-526 ||

sudhāśiktavapurvyāpta brahmāṇḍādi kaṭāhakaḥ |

akārādikṣakārāntaḥ varṇapakti samujjvalaḥ || 18-527 ||

ākarākaraḥ proṅgīta tāranādavināditaḥ |

īkāre karamantrādya mālābhramaṇalālasaḥ || 18-528 ||

ukārokārarūpādyo ghoranāgopavītakaḥ |

ṛvarṇāṅkita jhṛkārapadmadvayasamujjvalaḥ || 18-529 ||

mṛkālayugaḷkāra śaṃkhapūrṇadigaṃntaraḥ |

ekāraikāragirijā stanapānavicakṣaṇa || 18-530 ||

okārokāraviśvādi kṛtasṛṣṭikramālasaḥ |

aṃ avarṇāvalī vyāpta pādādiśirṣamaṇḍala || 18-531 ||

karṇanālākṛtātyugra vāyuvījitanirjaraḥ |

309b) khaḍgeśadhvasaratnāṅva kiriṭārūṇapādakaḥ || 18-532 ||

garvitā śeṣagandharva gānatatpara śrotrakaḥ |

ghanavāhanavāgīśa puraścarasurārcitaḥ || 18-533 ||

ṅavarṇācyutadhārādhya śobhamānaikadantakaḥ |

candrakuṃkumajavālaliptasuravigraha || 18-534 ||

chatracāmaratnā ca mukuṭālaṃkṛtānanaḥ |

jaṭāvaddha mahānargha maṇipaktivirājitaḥ || 18-535 ||

jhaṃkāramadhupavrāta gītavādanināditaḥ |

ñavarṇi kṛta saṃhāra daityāsṛk pūrṇamudgaraḥ || 18-536 ||

ṭaṃkārukaphalāmvā davepitā śeṣaśūbhramam |

ṭhakārādya ṅakārānta ḍhakārānandatoṣitā || 18-537 ||

ṇavarṇāṅgitapīyuṣadhārā dharamudhādhara |

tāmrasindūrapūjāḍhya lalāṭaphalakachavi || 18-538 ||

thakāradhanapaktyātasatopi taddhijavrataḥ |

dayāmaya hṛdaṃbhoja dhūtalaukya? maṇḍale || 18-539 ||

dhanadādi mahāyakṣa saṃsevita padāṃvujaḥ |

namitā śeṣa devaurghya kirīṭamaṇiraṃjitaḥ || 18-540 ||

paraṃ vargāpavargādi marmachedanadakṣakaḥ |

phaṇicakrasamākrānta galamaṇḍalamaṇḍitaḥ || 18-541 ||

vaddhabhūyagabhīmogra santarjitasurāsuraḥ |

bhavānī hṛdayānanda vardhanaikaniśākara || 18-542 ||

madirākalasasphīta karālaikakarāṃvujaḥ |

yaṃ jñāntarāya saṃghāta sajjī kṛtā yudhastathā || 18-543 ||

ratnākarasutākānta kāntikīrtti vivardhana |

laṃvodara mahābhīma vapurdonī kṛtā suraḥ || 18-544 ||

310a) varuṇādidibhīśāna racitārcana carcitaḥ |

śakāraikapriyo bhauma nayanānandavarddhanaḥ || 18-545 ||

ṣoḍaśaśvaritālāya gītagānavicakṣaṇa |

samastadurgati sarinnāraṇakodrupaḥ || 18-546 ||

harādi brahmavaikuṇṭha vrahmagītādipāṭhakaḥ |

kṣamāpūritavat padma saṃrakṣita carācaraḥ || 18-547 ||

tārāṭkavarṇamantrāṃka vigraho jvalavigraha |

akārādikṣarānta vidyābhūṣitabhūṣaṇa || 18-548 ||

o/ śrī/ vināyaka au/ hrī/ vighnādhyakṣo gaṇādhipaḥ |

heraṃvo modakāhāro vakratuṇḍo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 18-549 ||

vedāntagīto vidyārthī siddhamantraḥ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ |

gaṇeśo varado devo dvādaśākṣaramantrinaḥ || 18-550 ||

saptakoṭi mahāmantramantritā śeṣavigraha |

gāṃroyo gaṇaśevyaśca au/ śrī/ dvaimāturaśivaḥ || 18-551 ||

o/ śrī/ hrī/ klī/ glau/ gaṃ devo mahāgaṇapati prabhuḥ (1000) |

idaṃ nāprā sahasraṃ tu mahāgaṇapate smṛtaḥ || 18-552 ||

guhyaṃ gopyatamaṃ gopyaṃ sarvatantreṣu gopitam |

sarvamantranidhiṃ divyaṃ sarvavighnavināśanam || 18-553 ||

grahatārāmayaṃ rāśi varṇapaktisamuṃcalam |

sarvavighnāmayaṃ brahma sādhanaṃ sādhaka priyam || 18-554 ||

gaṇeśaśya ca sarvaśvaṃ rahasyaṃ tridivaukasām |

yatheṣṭaphaladaṃ loke masorathaprapūraṇam || 18-555 ||

aṣṭasiddhimayaṃ sādhya sādhakānaṃ jana pradam |

vinārcanaṃ vināhomaṃ vinānyāsaṃ vināvalim || 18-556 ||

aṇimādyaṣṭasiddhināṃ sādhanaṃ smṛtimātrataḥ |

310b) caturthīmarddharātre tu paṭhen matrī catuṣpathe || 18-557 ||

likhet bhūrje mahādevi puṇyaṃ nāsahasrakam |

dhārayet ta catudarśyāṃ madhyānhe mūdhni vā bhuje || 18-558 ||

staṃbhayedapi brahmāṇaṃ mohayedapi śaṃkaram |

vaśayedapi trailokya mārayed valinoripun || 18-559 ||

uccāṭaye *gīvāṃṇāṃ tu śamaye ca dhanaṃ japan |

vandhyā putraṃ labhe chīghraṃ nirdhanodhanamāpnuyāt || 18-560 ||

trivāraḥ yaḥ paṭhed rātrau gaṇeśyaśya puraḥ śive |

nagnaśaktiyuto devī bhutkā bhogā nyasepsitān || 18-561 ||

pratyakṣaṃ varadaṃ paśved gaṇeśaṃ varadottama |

e enaṃ paṭhate nityaṃ sahasraṃ bhuktipūrvakam || 18-562 ||

tasya vittādivi bhavodārādyā saṃpadastathā |

raṇerājabhayedyūte paṭhen nāmasahasrakam |

mahāgaṇapatergopyaṃ gopanīyaṃ svayonivat || 18-563 ||


iti śrībhadrayāmalatantre mahāgaṇapati nāmasahasraṃ stvarājasaṃpūrṇaḥ |


śrīdevyuvāca

bhagavan parameśāna mahāgaṇapate vibho |

storarājādhipaṃ vrūhi mūlamantrasya tattvataḥ || 18-564 ||

bhairava uvāca

mahāgaṇapate vakṣye stotraṃstatvanirūpaṇam |

devasyāgre kalosīghraṃ jīvanmuktipradāyakam || 18-565 ||


asya śrīmahāgaṇapateḥ stotrasya śrībhairavaṛṣi gāyatrī chanda 
śrīgaṇapatirdevatā gaṃ vījaṃ hrī/ śaktiḥ kuru kuru kīlakaṃ jīvanmukti 
prāptyartha stotra pāṭhe viniyogaḥ |


311a) dvicaturdaśavarṇabhūṣitāṅgaṃ śaśipūryāgnivilocanaṃ sureśam |

ahibhūṣitakaṇṭhamakṣasūtraṃ bhramayaṃtaṃ hṛdaye smare gaṇeśam || 18-566 ||

tāraṃ tāradagītamādimaniśaṃ yaḥ sādhakendro jayed rātrā vahni niśāmukhe 
śivamahe |
śūnyālaye vā prabho tasya smare mukhāṃvujā pratidinaṃ lāvaṇya darvyojvalā 
|| 18-267 ||

rveśvāśca vaśī bhavantini dive bhūmau samastā nṛpaḥ |

sakalī sakalāṃ japed yadā gaṇanātha priyamantrameva ca || 18-568 ||

sakalākṣmāpatimūrdhani spharacaraṇā ko bhuvi śobhate ca saḥ |

kamalāṃ kamalāspṛhī ca yo hṛdaye dhyāyeti deva madhyagām || 18-569 ||

tava mantraprabhātatastadā kamalāsadmani tasya niścalā |

kamalā jamaniśaṃsmarāturo yo japed vadi gaṇaśatāvakam || 18-570 ||

ūrvaśī vadanapadmaṣaṭpado dhīyate dharaparāgakāmitām |

maṭhaṃ japed yo niśi śunyagehe vimuktakeśo gaṇanāthabhaktyā || 18-571 ||

raṇe ripunindra samānvijitya prāpnoti rājyaṃ nṛpacakravartti |

śivārṇamannaryadi sādhako japed araṇyabhūmau śavasannidhau vidhau || 18-572 ||

carācareṣu pracare dvimārījagatyaśeṣāmaracakravarttī |

gaṇapataye iti mūlamantrayo japate bhajate paṭhate || 18-573 ||

gaṇagaṇakinnara siddhasurāsuranamito labhate gāṇapatyaṃ saḥ |

varavaradeti ca saṃnnamimaṃ yo nayati niśi vādivamepyaniśam || 18-574 ||

sadate savate vadanasate hṛdi vāk kamalā tava bhaktirapi |

311b) sarvajanaṃ ma iti mantrarājaṃ japati ye bhojanakāvasāne || 18-575 ||

bhajanti te nandanacandanādi vṛkṣeṣu līlāṃ surasundarībhiḥ |

vaśamānayeti bhagavan yadi japati smara smarataṃ hṛdayaśca || 18-576 ||

vaśamati kinnarasutapyurvaśīsatataṃ suranatadakṣā |

svāheti manvākṣara vyugmametajjaye dināntepyaśanādi kāle || 18-577 ||

rogā aśeṣāḥ pralayaṃ yānti tamāṃsisūrye bhyudite yathā vā |

padmahastamamṛtaṃ | śusaṃnibhaṃ śaṃkhayūgmamaparaṃ ca dadhānam || 18-
578 ||

tryakṣamagnisadṛśaṃ smareti ye te prayānti tridivaṃ vimānagā |

vindutryaśradaśāravāsaṃ va kalā vedāśramadhye vibhoyo dhyāyanti 
bhavantamīḍya vapuṣaṃ trailokyarakṣā param |

jitvā bhūmimalpakalpanivahā dhukkāsahauyaryutā paśvāpyānti 
gaṇeśatāvakapadaṃ devāsūrai durllabham || 18-579 ||

iti gaṇapateḥ stotraṃ matrātmakaṃ paramaṃ japet paṭhati śṛṇuyād bhaktyā 
nitya samastarahasyakam |

iha dhanapatirlakṣmī sādhuḥ śubhāṃ labhate cirād vrajati tridivaṃ jīvanmuktaḥ 
paratra sasādhakaḥ || 18-580 ||

iti stavottamaṃ devi mahāgaṇapateḥ param |

gopyaṃ guptam sadā gopyaṃ paṃcāṅgasundari || 18-581 ||

paṃcāṅgaṃ ca gaṇeśasya rahasyaṃ mama pārvatī |

vināśiṣyāya no dadyāt sādhakāya vinā tathā || 18-582 ||

vinādīnaṃ tadā tavyaṃ vinādānaṃ na grāhayet |

anyathā siddhihāniḥ syāt sarvathā dānamācaret || 18-583 ||

paṭalaṃ paddhatiṃ varṇā mantranāmasahasrakam |

stotraṃ paṃcāṅgamakhilaṃ gopanīyaṃ svayonivat || 18-584 ||


312a) iti śrīrudrayāmale tantre mahārahasye mahāgaṇapati stotraṃ samāptam |

atha phetkāriṇitantre jvālāmukhimantra |


namo bhagavatet pratkā jvālāmālinyataḥ param | 

gaddhagaṇaparivṛte dviṭhānnomanurīritaḥ || 18-585 ||


tena | 

namo bhagavati jvālā mālinī gṛdgagaṇaparivṛte svāhā |

iti mantra |

oṃ namo hṛdaya proktaṃ śiraḥ smṛtam |

jvālāmālini ni ca śikhāgṛddhagapavṛte |

varmasvāhāstramityetajjātiyuktaṃ nyasen namau |

abhutvā niyataṃ caitaj jayamantraṃ japejjayī |

hrī/ jvālāmukhi svāhā |


rudrāṅganāgni jāyābhyāṃ rudrajvālāmukhītyapi |

mantrāntaraṃ samākhyātaṃ jvālāmukhyā anataram || 18-586 ||

dīpatailākapādodharātre guru dinā dinaḥ |

japedaṣṭasahasraṃ tu trayoviprāti vā sarāt || 18-587 ||

pratyahaṃ palaṣaṭkaṃ ca dadātiti na saṃśayaḥ |

nārāṇīya kathitaṃ magastyaiḥ kathitaṃ punaḥ || 18-588 ||

susiddhimikṣatā gopyo nāmāpina samīrayet |

putrasya nṛpateścāpi dhārmikasya viśeṣataḥ || 18-589 ||

navadurgā vidhānena darśayet kāryagauravāt |

nṛpopi śamyagācāro mantrajño vividhairdhanaiḥ || 18-590 ||

nivedya vidhivat paścāt mantravarṇenadirdahet |

smṛtimātreṇa vai varṇā tripunsarvānvināśayet || 18-591 ||

pradadyā dakṣiṇāṃ tasya sahasraṃ śatameva ca |

gavāṃ suvarṇaniskāṇāṃ bhūmiṃ vāsasya śālinīm |

sarvaśāntimavāpnoti sarvarakṣākṛtirbhavet || 18-592 ||


312b) niskamaṃlaṃkāraḥ |

iti jvālāmukhi prakaraṇam |

atha piśāvinimantra |


kahakahe kāliki gṛhna gṛhna piśāci svāhā |


kahayugmaṃ kālike ca gṛhnayugmaṃ tathaiva ca |

piṇḍaṃ piśāci svāheti nṛpārṇā kathito manuḥ || 18-593 ||

dhyāyet piśācī calaśūlahastāṃ bhyāmāṃsudīrghā yuvati trinetrām |

kurddhā vivarṇāṃ tanuvṛttamadhyaṃ gujāphalaiḥ katpitahārapaṣṭhiya || 18-594 ||

ekaviśādinaṃ yāva idapāstamayaṃ jayet |

nityaṃ sāyaṃ svamāhāraṃ piṇḍaṃ haryoparikṣipet || 18-595 ||

trimadhyahā catuṣṭā sā śayyāṃ gatvā piśācinī |

paṃcaviṃśati deśānaṃ kathāṃ ca kathayatyapi || 18-596 ||

triṃśaddinaiśca deveśi jaṃvudvīpakathānayet |

dvitriṃśatāyukarṇe tat pavarttāṃ prakāśayet || 18-597 ||

dvistriṃśatā tathā gauri trailokyaṃ śrāvaye dhruvam |

trisvāduvalidānena sarvaṃ vadati tatkṣaṇāt || 18-598 ||

sabhāyāṃ yadi saṃghāte dyūtecari prasaṃsadi |

sarvakathayati kṣipraṃ tūnaṃ tuṣṭā piśācinī |

kenāpi naivedya ca śvā sādhakastāṃ prapaṣyati || 18-599 ||


iti karṇapiśācinī prakaraṇam |

atha bhūtinī mantraḥ |

atha phetkāriṇitantre |


o/ hrī/ hrī/ phaṭ vibhiṣikā hū/ hū/ |


adyā vibhūṣaṇī devī parākuṇḍaladhāriṇī |

hāriṇī siṃhanī caiva haṃsini tu tato nadī || 18-600 ||

devī kāmeśvarī caiva proktā devī ratipriyā |

krodhāstradvayamāyānte bhūtinī ca trikurcataḥ || 18-601 ||

313a) tārālajjā dvayādastrād vayānte ca vibhīṣikā |

krodhadvayānto mantro yaṃ vibhūṣirācāḍadīritaḥ || 18-602 ||


krodho hu/ hu/ kurcopi sa eva krom iti kaścit |


karti caiva kare naraśirastuṃgākṛti bhūṣaṇādantāntargata māṃsaśoṇitavasā 
saṃyukta bhūtavratā |

kālavyālakarālasātvavikaṭavyālaṃ vipṛṣṭakṣamā yasyāḥ keśakulā 
kulākhila mahiśailāstu sā rakṣatu || 18-603 ||

ekavṛkṣataṭaṃ gatvā yāmin prāṃ pravasaṃstryadam |

japedaṣṭasahasraṃ tadhā yatra vyagramānasaḥ || 18-604 ||

japānte pūjanaṃ nāmnā guḍadhūpaṃ nivedayet |

prakuped adharātre tu samāyāti vibhūṣaṇī || 18-605 ||

candanodakamiśreṇa dattārvātuṣpati dhruvam |

mātā vā bhaginī bhāryā hṛṣṭā bhagavatikāmitā || 18-606 ||

karamelakṛtvā mātā bhūtvā jagattayam |

śatāṣṭaparivārasya sadābhyaṃjanabhūṣaṇam || 18-607 ||

bhagini cen mahāyogin sahasraṃ yojanādapi |

dadāti striyamānīya divyaṃ rasarasāyanam || 18-608 ||

bhāryā yet pṛṣṭamāropya svarganayati tatkṣaṇāt |

dīnārāṇāṃ sahasraṃ tu rasa caiva rasāyatam || 18-609 ||

sarvā śā pūrayatyeva sadā devī vibhūṣaṇī |

dhruvamāyāstrayugalā proktā kuṇḍaladhāriṇī || 18-610 ||

karṇe kuṇḍaladhāriṇī śaśikuṇḍaladhāriṇi |

āyati rudhireṇādyo deyāstraṣṭā vadatyapi || 18-611 ||

kiṃ kartavyamayāvat samātīti bhava sādhaka |

paṃcāśātaṃ dinācāpi trailokyamapi dāsyati || 18-612 ||

313b) krodhadvayātayugataḥ siṃdūrariṇīpadaṃ 

kurcatrayāstrasaṃprokta sindhūrahāriṇimanuḥ |

svarṇālaṃkṛti hāriṇī caladalavyālolaśākhāṃ varā

sotkaṇṭhītagātrakam aśubhagāśubhrāṃśu candrasmitā || 18-613 ||

annaḥ santatakāntidaṃ tamalina trailokyaśobhā sadā |

pāyāduddhala vā śūlalanikā sindūrahāriṇyabho || 18-614 ||

śūnyaṃ devālayaṃ gatvā japedaṣṭasahasrakam |

śīghramāyā tyāgatā sā bhāryā bhavati kāmini || 18-615 ||

paṃcaviṃśati dinānivastrālaṃkārabhūṣaṇam |

rakṣati dvādaśadinaṃ devi sidūrahāriṇī || 18-616 ||

lajjādvayāstrayugalaṃ siṃhinīti pannataḥ |

krodhavījatrayād yasta kīrttitasiṃhinī manuḥ || 18-617 ||

śailāgraduḥkhasvargadantavadane durgatadaitya sthitā 

keśā asthisamastaviṣṇucaraṇa prabhraṣṭamantrāsini |

yā dattāṃ taralaṃ vidhāraṇarāṇat prakṣa va siddhāṅganā

seyaṃ pātu samastakakulavyākaṃpi mālākulā || 18-618 ||

gatvaikaliṃgaṃ yā mindāṃ prajapedataṃ manum |

tato hṛṣṭāti varadā siṃhinyā yāti pūjitā || 18-619 ||

kiṃ kiṃ karomi va datyeva bhāryā bhavati kāmitā |

dīnāravastrayugalaṃ dadyād rasarasāyanam || 18-620 ||

tāratrikuryāstra yugāddhaṃ sītijūtinī tataḥ |

kūcatrayāntaḥ kathitaṃ haṃsinī marutaḥ || 18-621 ||


śubhrāśubhrasarojatulya nayanā dugdhāsave nāmvitā 

prāravdhanimudhvaṃ māvavadhūmaṃ sevitāsādaram |

314a) kiṃci tu gavilolayāṅgavalitavyā mugdhamīlanmukhī 

muktāyuktavilolahāralalitā śrīhaṃsini pātu na || 18-622 ||

vajrapāṇi gṛhaṃ gatvā pratimāṃ śobhatāṃ likhet |

hayamāreṇa saṃpūjya japed ayutasaṃkhyakam || 18-623 ||

yāvad ardhaniśāṃ devi haṃsinyā yātiścitam |

candanārgha pradānena hṛṣṭā bhavati sādhakam || 18-624 ||

kiṃ mayāte prakarttavyaṃ kiṃkarī bhavasādhaka |

vastrālaṃkārabhojyādyaṃ vyayārthaṃ saṃprayachati || 18-625 ||

tadaśaṣavya yā bhāvān nadadāti prakuṣpati |

tārakrodhāstrayugato nadīti padamuccaret || 18-626 ||

kurcarvayānto ṅektoyaṃ kathito pakṣiṇī manu |

phullendī varasundaridharamukhi muttuṃgakuṃbhastanī 

khelat vaṃjanayugmamaṃju nayanāmānandakṣayi smitām |

kṣīrāṃbho nidhikūlavalitā mānīya vailāsiniṃ 

vīṇā śrāvaṇaśālinī malikulavyāhāravākcāturīm || 18-627 ||

nīcagā saṃga kṛtvā prāptāhaṃ japa pūjane |

kṛtvārkāstamane rūpaṃ candanena japed apiḥ || 18-628 ||

kāmabhorā pradā śrīssā bhāryā bhavati narttakī |

suvarṇapalamekaṃ tu vyayārthaṃ tyajya gachati || 18-629 ||

dine dine naṭī devī sthāpinī bhavati dhruvam |

tārā kūrce yugaṃ cāstra yugmaṃ ceṭī tataḥ param || 18-630 ||

ṅentaṃ krodhayugaṃ coktā ceṭīyugmaṃ trottayottamaḥ |

gachantī mitabhāṣiṇī śaśimukhi bhraṣṭottarīyā || 18-631 ||

ṣarā vibhrantī kalaśaṃ sarojamamalaṃ svaṇḍāntasīmantinī |

314b) śrīkhaṇḍāditi lepanāmalavapuḥ saurabhyasaṃbhāvitā || 18-632 ||

kiṃci dharmasamārjitārpita karapāyādipaṃceṭikā |

kutrāpi bhīṣaṇasthāne nāmocāraṇamātrataḥ || 18-633 ||

dhruvaṃ ceṭī samāgatyaṃ ceṭī karmakarotyapi |


athavā svagṛhe ahaṃ rātrau japaṃ caret |


āgatya niyataṃ devī ceṭī karmakaroti ca || 18-634 ||

tārakūrcāstrayuktā meśvarī bhūtinyataḥ param |

dventaṃ krodhatrayaṃ kāmeśvarī mantra-udāhṛtaḥ || 18-635 ||

kāmaskandha samāgatā madhuratā vāsanti puṣpānvitā

gāyantī madhurādharasmitamukhī vīṇāvatī caṃcalā |

rakāṃ bhojavilocanā madhumadairyutā samatāṃ dipaṃ 

pādyāt pravyadhanurdharā madhumatī kāmeśvarī bhūtinī || 18-636 ||

mātṛsthānaṃ samāgatya kṛtvā māṃsasya bhakṣaṇam |

māṃsādina valiṃ datvā sahasraṃ saptavāsarān || 18-637 ||

japān niyatamāyāti rudhirārdyati vedayet |


kāmeśvarī bhavet tuṣṭā bhāryā bhāryā bhavati kāmitā |


sarvāśāḥ pūrayetyeva rājyaṃ pakrati niścitam || 18-638 ||

tāraṃ lajjā ca kūrcāstra dvayayuktā rati priyā |

krodhatrayāstu yuktā meśvarī mantroktavaskriyā || 18-639 ||

hemaprākāramadhye suraviṭapitaṭe himayī ṭhāhi rūḍhā 

yakṣīmāvisarpanpari malakusumodbhāsidaṃ millabhārā |

pīnotuṃgastadvyā kuvala yana nāraktavarṇā karābhyām 

bhrāmyad raktotpalābhāsa kalasurayutā divyabhūṣāṅgarāgā || 18-640 ||

krodhadvayāstrayugato deviti padamuccaret |

315a) ṅentaṃ krodhayugaṃ gujyaṃ bhāryā bhavati sādhitā || 18-641 ||

rātro deva gṛhaṃ gatvā tatra śayyāṃ prakalpayet |

sitavastraṃ candranaṃjātī puṣpaṃ pradāpayet || 18-642 ||

dhūpaṃ tu gugguluṃ datvāṣṭasahasraṃ japenmanum |

japāte nitya māyāti cuṃvanā ligatādibhiḥ || 18-643 ||

kāmitā jāyate bhāryā satyaṃ devī kumārikā |

dadyādaṣṭau dinārāśi divyaṃ vastrayugaṃ tataḥ || 18-644 ||

kāmikāṃ bhojanaṃ divyaṃ parivārasya dāsyati |

anyadvaiśvona gṛhādravyamānipayachati || 18-645 ||

sahasrametāni japepānte siddhyati dhruvam |

muhūrmuhurjapen mantraṃ mityā hṛkrodhabhūpati || 18-646 ||

krodhādhipavyomavaktaṃ vajrapāṇi surāntakam |

kali kali kalpatabhūṃ natvā bhūtinī siddhirarpate || 18-647 ||

anyatra valahavāḥ santi atra kiṃcin nibhūpitam |

ityāgamakalpalatāyāṃ bhūtinī prakaraṇaṃ nāma catuviṃśati patalaḥ |



rudrayāmale (29) |

athāta saṃpravakṣyāmi mantraṃ parimanātmakam |

yena vijñānamātreṇa nāsādyaṃ bhuvanatrayam || 19-1 ||

rudrajāyā mahāyogi nyato gauripadaṃ vadet |

bhuvanabhayaṃ kariti vamāstraṃ coddharet || 19-2 ||


rudrajāyā hrī/ tena hrī/ mahāyogini gauri bhuvanabhayaṃkari hū/ phaṭ | tathā |


ṣaddirghayuktavījena mantrārddhe nātra yogini |

hṛdayaṃ ca tamordhena varmāt tena śiraḥ kramāt || 19-3 ||

śikhā kavacanetrāstram evat pāsakramaṃ tataḥ |


315b) evaṃ madhārdhakrameṇa |


tathāṅgirāṛṣirdevī gāyatrī chanda īritam || 19-4 ||

siṃhavaktrā ca bhūtānāṃ bhayaṃkariṇtataḥ param |

mahākṛtyā devatā cetyuttkā dhyānamathā caret || 19-5 ||

siṃhāsyāṃ kṛṣṇavaranāṃ laṃvamāvapayodharām |

daṃṣṭrākarālavadanāṃ trinetrāṃ sarvato jvalām || 19-6 ||

kṛṣṇakaṃcukasaṃvītāṃ cidhūmāgni samaprabhām |

triśūlacakracaṣakakhaṭvāṃgakārayaṃ kajām || 19-7 ||

lelidāna mahājihvāṃ vidyūt prekṣaṇabhīṣaṇām |

dhyātvā kṛbhyāṃ vidhanena pūjayen matravittamaḥ || 19-8 ||

dhyātvā kṛtyāṃ samarceta raktapuṣpairaraṇyakaiḥ |

kṛṣṇāṃ māraṇa kṛtyeṣu māṃsaraktāsavaistathā || 19-9 ||

kṛtyāṃ ca madanāṃ sarvai kanyāmuchādaṃni tathā |

bhīṣaṇaṃ srīmati caiva pratiṣṭā ca tataḥ param || 19-10 ||

vidyāmabhyarcayed aṣṭapatteṣu kramataḥ sudhīḥ |

sarve tu śāṃkarimāsa śukravarṇā manānosmām || 19-11 ||

dvibhujāṃ saumyavadanā pāśāṅkuśadharāṃ śivām |

dakṣiṇe paracaturbhujāṃ siṃhanādajvalachūlakapālinīm || 19-12 ||

śūlakhaḍgakartṛhastāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām |

paścime vāruṇi nāma svarṇo hasanmukhīm || 19-13 ||

suvarṇamālikāṃ śubhraṃ daṣṭrāmabhayaṃ dā śivām |

uttare bhīṣikāṃ nāma caturvarṇabhayaṃkarim || 19-14 ||

pūjayitvā japen * * * ṣṭottaraśatam |

athāsya viniyogastu kartavyo mantriṇā sadā || 19-15 ||

kālaviditvā pratimāṃ madhūchiṣṭena kārayet |

dvādaśāṅgulakaṃ śatrornakhalomasamanvitam || 19-16 ||

hṛdayaṃ nāmadheyaṃ ca phaṭkāraṃ sarvakarmake |

tataḥ prāṇān pratiṣṭāpya maricairlapayet || 19-17 ||

mṛtavrāhmaṇacaṇḍālakeśābhyāṃ pādayo pṛthak |

vaddhākāraskaramaye toraṇe vāpyato mukhī || 19-18 ||

tasyādhā mekhalāyuktaṃ trikoṇaṃ vahnikuṇḍakam |

tatra śāvaṃ vidhāyāgri paristīryaśaraistṛṇaiḥ || 19-19 ||

vibhītako paviṣṭeśva kalpayed yasya māraṇam |

juhuyān nivatau lāktai kākolukākapakṣakaiḥ || 19-20 ||

dhārayainaṃ śoṣayainaṃ mārayatyabhidhāya ca |

aṣṭottaraśarai naiva anayā juhuyād vudhaḥ || 19-21 ||

homānte vidhivat kṛbhya mārādhyāgnaśca sannidhau |

yome kaṇṭhagrahorātirdūrasthā vārtikepi ca || 19-22 ||

pivatūrmāmasṛktasya vahne cotkā nivedayet |

saṃrakṣāgni vidhānena navarātriṃ samācaret || 19-23 ||

mṛtastiṣṭeti hutvaivaṃ bhave tasya ripormṛti |


śāraṃśarākāṣṭabhavaṃ śāvātiti yā ṭhe śmasānāgnimityarthaḥ |


ripoḥ pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā śiro nirmāya kūpake || 19-24 ||

kṣiyet kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ japtvā mantrasahasraśaḥ |

sadyo va gāhādanaṃ kṛtvā kuryād yasyātmarakṣaṇāt || 19-25 ||

mantra japtā vidhānena bhadeyen siṃhamudrayā |

ṣaṇmāsān mriyate kruddha sandhyayorubhayorapi || 19-26 ||

vā prāṃsamuddhṛtaṃ patraṃ puṣkaṃ vai bhītakaṃ tataḥ |

gṛhītvā ca rikhen matrī kṛṣṇasarpasya śorṇite || 19-27 ||

nāmadhedyoriporantaṃ vāyuvījaṃ prayo japet |

svanet tasyāpi gehe tu kākavadbhrabhramati dhruvam || 19-28 ||

316b) vātoddhataiḥ śuśrupatreḥ kāṣṭairaśanipātitaiḥ |

uṣṭrāṣṭināvacāṅgāraiḥ śakroruccāṭanaṃ bhavet || 19-29 ||


iti dhūmāvatī prakaraṇam |


iti rudrāyāmale bhairavī tatrokta vagalāmukhi mantra |


praṇavaṃ pūrvamucārya sthiramāyāmathācaret |

saṃvodhanaṃ padaṃ coktā tataḥ śrīvagalāmukhi || 19-30 ||

tataśca sarvaduṣṭānāṃ tato vācaṃ mukhaṃ padam |

staṃbhayeti padaṃ cottkā kīlayeti pada * yam || 19-31 ||

vuddhiṃ nāśayati datvā sthiramāyāṃ punarlikhet |

likhe ca punaroṃkāra svāheti padamuccaret || 19-32 ||

ṣaṭtriśadakṣarā vidyā devānāmapi durllabhā |

mantro yathā |

o/ klī/ hrī/ śrī/ vagalāmukhi sarvaduṣṭānāṃ vācaṃ mukhaṃ sthaṃbhaya 
kīlaya vuddhināśaya klī/ hrī/ oṃ svāhā |

iti mantra sthireti cindracaturthasvarayuktaṃ lakārastha hakārayukta mityartha |
tena hrī/ iti bhavati |


trailokyaṃ staṃbhinī ni vidyā vijñedyā vagalāmukhi || 19-33 ||

ṛṣirnārāyaṇastriṣṭupdevatā va * lāmukhi |

hrī/ vīja ca dviṭhaṃ śakti mudrā rādiṣu vinyaset || 19-34 ||

yugmaraseṣu saptartu daśārṇeśca mamudbhaveḥ |

bhrāmaṇī mandagamanā bhogasyā caiva bhogini || 19-35 ||

bhogāḥ ṣoḍaśapatreṣu gandhapuṣpākṣatairyajet |

ṣoḍaśaśvarasaṃyuktā saṃpradāyakulāgaye || 19-36 ||

etan madhyeṣṭapīṭheṣu kalpayedaṣṭapatrakam |

brahmāṇyādhyāśca pūrvādau bhairavāṣṭaka saṃyutā || 19-37 ||

sthiramāyādikāḥ sarvāḥ paripūjyāḥ kulodbhave |

yadi hṛnnāḥ ṣaḍaṃgeṣu saptadhā dhātumātṛkā || 19-38 ||

317a) yognimadhe mūlavidyā tribhiraṃ jalibhiryajet |

puṣpadhūpādinaivedya gandhatāṃvūladīpakaiḥ || 19-39 ||

nīrājavidhināpaścājjapasaṃkhyā nivedayet |

valitrayaṃ tato devi pūjanādi mahotsavaiḥ || 19-40 ||

evaṃ tu kathitaṃ devi gurorājñāṃ ca pālayet |

jayamālā * * * ca deveśi haridrāgrathisaṃbhavaḥ || 19-41 ||

pīthāsanasamārūḍha pitadhyānaparāyaṇam |

pītapuṣparcanaṃ nityaṃ mayutaṃ japamācaret || 19-42 ||

taddaśāṃśakṛto domaḥ pītadravyai suśobhanaiḥ |

saṃjñāmucāraye sādhya nāma * * * bhavet || 19-43 ||

gatistaṃbhakarī vidyāṃ  śtrustaṃ bhavet |

madhurāṅgatinānyena naranāmāni sādhayet || 19-44 ||

tathā karmaṇakarmāṇi lodhratrimadhurānvitam |

nimvapatrāṇi tailāktaṃ vidveṣaṇarahaparam || 19-45 ||

hṛditālaṃ haridrāṃ ca lavaṇe na ca saṃyutam |

tavane viddhi deveśi prajñā caiva gatirmatiḥ || 19-46 ||

lājāṃstrimadhu saṃyuktān sarvarogāṃpaśāntaye |

lakṣamekaṃ japen mantraṃ brahmacāre dṛḍhavrataḥ || 19-47 ||

parvatāgre mahāraṇye siddherśevālaye tathā |

saṃgame ca mahānadyā vaiśikāḥ sādhayet svayam || 19-48 ||

śvetabrahmataro mūle pādukā caiva raṃjayet |

alaktakena rāgeṇa raṃjitā navamudrayā || 19-49 ||

ṣaṭtriṃśadakṣarā vidyā lakṣamāṇe na lakṣike tasamanvitam |

japen matraṃ lakṣamekaṃ sarvāṅge lepanaṃ kṛtam || 19-50 ||

adṛśyakāraṇaṃ devi viloke ca mahadbhatam |

rasaḥ pāradaṃ tālaṃ haritālaṃ mākṣike madhu || 19-51 ||

317b) ekavarṇaṃ gavā caiva dāroṣṇakṣī ramāhṛret |

śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktaṃ triṃśatermantritaṃ priye || 19-52 ||

pitvā ca harate devi viṣasthā varajaṃgamam | tathā |

māridyamocanaṃ devi lakṣamānaṃ japet naraḥ || 19-53 ||

daśāṃśai nāyuto homa ebhidravyaiḥ kṛtākhaluḥ |

lakṣmī yukto bhave devi rāridyaṃ ca vināśayat || 19-54 ||

tantrāntare |

viddhihīnenhu yugmā yā vagalāmukhi sarvayuk |

draṣṭānāṃ vācamityuttkām usvastaṃbhayakīrttayet || 19-55 ||

jijvāṃ kīlaya vuddhiṃ ca vināśaya padaṃ vadet |

purnacījaṃ tatastāraṃ vahnijāvadhirbhavet |

tārādhikā catustriṃśad akṣarā vagalāmukhi || 19-56 ||


mantro yathā |

o/ klī/ vagalāmukhi sarvaduṣṭānāṃ vācaṃ mukhaṃ sthaṃbhaya jihvā kīlaya 
vuddhi nārāya klī/ oṃ svāhā |

iti mantra |

asya vagalāmukhi mantrasya śrīnāradaṛṣiḥ paktichanda śrīvagalāmukhi devatā 
klī/ vījaṃ svāhāśakti samastaśatrumukhastaṃbhavane viniyogaḥ |

atra sthiramāyā saṃjñake ta vījena ṣaḍaṃganyāse |

karanyāṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmaṃ kuryāt |

tato dhyāyet |


āṃ madhyaśudhādvi maṇimaṇḍaparatnavedi 
siṃhāsano parigatāṃ paripītavarṇām |

pītāṃvarāṃ kanake bhūṣaṇamālyaśobhyāṃ
devi bhajāmi dhṛta mudgaraveri jihvām || 19-57 ||

jihvāgramādāyakareṇa devī vāme tana śatrun paripīḍayantim |

parābhighātena ca dakṣiṇena pītāṃvarāṃdyāṃ dvibhujāṃ namāmi || 19-58 ||


318a) iti rudrayāmale tantre śrīpītāṃvarāpaṭalaṃ saṃpūrṇasamāptam |

atha pītāṃvarāpaddhatiḥ |


śrīyātāṃvarāyai namaḥ |

prātaḥ śirasi śukrekre dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ gurum |

varābhayakaraṃ śāntaṃ smaret tan nāmapūrvakam || 19-59 ||


gurunādyalalitāṃ vā śaktiyukta śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmi namaḥ |

iti vrahmarandhra śrīgurudhyātvā namaskuryāt |

śaucavidhiṃ kṛtvā dantadhya ca nādikaṃ kṛtvā tīrthe gatvā pādau 
prakṣālyācamya |

mūlamantreṇa | ātmatattvaṃ śodhayāmi namaḥ | mūlenaḥ |

vidyā tattvaṃ śodhayāmi namaḥ | mūlenaḥ |

śivatattvaṃ śodhayāmi namaḥ | mūlena |

sarvatattvaṃ śodhayāmi namaḥ | ityācamana catuṣṭayaṃ kṛtvā nābhidāghne 
jalamadhya praviśet |

jalamadhya trikoṇaṃ caturastraṃ kṛtvā tanmadhye pārthivavījaṃ dhyātvā 
sūryamaṇḍalā tīrthānāvāhya aṃkuśamudrayā |

gaṃge ca yamunā caiva godāvari sarasvati |

narmade sindhukāveri jalesmin sannidhikuru |


iti smṛtvā devī mayo bhūtvā snānaṃ kuryāt |
pūrvoktācamanacatuṣṭayaṃ kṛtvā gāyatrī japtvā saṃkalpyaṃ kṛtvā 
sūryārghyaṃ datvāt |

hrī/ haṃsaḥ iti mūlamantreṇa yathāśakti japtvā saṃkalpaṃ kṛtvā 
tīrthamaṇḍalaṃ mārttāṇḍaṃ maṇḍale nidhāya devīṃ hṛdi smaret |

jānāmidharmena ca me pravṛrtirjānāmya dharme na ca me pravṛttiḥ 

tvayā hṛṣikeśa hṛdi sthitena yathā niyuktosmi tathā karomi |

318b) iti smṛtvā svayadadṛṣṭibhātramavalokya gṛdamāgachedaḥ |

pādau prakṣālyacamya yāgamaṇḍapamāgatya |

gaṃgāyai namaḥ | dvādasyo pari pari | viṃ vidhātre namaḥ dvārādhaḥ |

daṃ dvāraśrīyai namaḥ | dvāramadhye | de dehalyai namaḥ | 

vaṃ vāstupuruṣāya namaḥ |

iti puṣpacandanākṣataiḥ saṃpūjya pūjāvedikāmāgatya yathā 
kāmanāpūrvakamātmāsanaṃ sthāpyasau 

ādhāraśakti kamalāsanāya namaḥ |

iti mantreṇa svāsane upaviśva bhūtāni mantrāsya pādyātakarā sphoṭe astrāya 
phaṭ |

iti mantreṇa bhūtatrāsanaṃ kuryāt guraṃ śirasi smṛtvā bhūtaśuddhiṃ kuryāt |

prāṇāyamakrameṇa svādhiṣṭānākhya ṣaṭkoṇād āgataṃ vāyuvījaṃ yamiti 
saṃciṃbhyaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇa te nu śoṣayet |

samyagāsīna śrāsani dakṣiṇanāsāpuṭena pūrakaṃ kṛtvā trirākṛtyā 
kudbhakaṃ nāsāpuṭadvayadhāraṇarūpaṃ kṛtvā dakṣiṇe na recayet |

kaniṣṭānāmikāṅkaṣṭairnāsāpuṭadhāraṇaṃ prāṇāyāmaḥ |

tanu śoṣaṇe vāmakukṣau pāpapuruṣaṃ vicintayet |

kajjāllābhaṃ brahmahṛtyā śiraskaṃ varṇasteya bhujayugalaṃ surāpāna hṛdaye 
abhakṣabhakṣaṇādyupapātakāṅgapraṃbhyagakaṃ gurutalpa 
kaṭikharḍgacarmadharaṃ kruddhaṃ 
iti saṃcintya tadu sthite na vāyunā pracaṇḍā na pāpapuruṣasahita tanoḥ 
śoṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā sakṛdāvṛtyā vāmanāsāpuṭena recayet |

319a) tato niṣpāyantaṃ vamityamṛtamantreṇāmṛtāplāvanaṃ kṛtvā 
tenāmṛtenotyantre dehamāplācayet |

iti bhūtaśuddhiḥ |

atha prāṇapratiṣṭā |

ā/ hrī/ kro/ ya/ ra/ la/ va/ śa/ ṣa/ ha/ sa/ ha/ sa/ḥ moha vagalāmukhi va iha sthitaḥ |

ā/ hrī/ kro/ 22 vagalāmukhi sarvendriyāṇi |

ā/ hrī/ krau/ 22 vagalāmukhiṃ vāṅmanaḥ śrotraghrāṇaprāṇā ihaivāgatya 
mukhaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭu tu svāhā |

tatastanuṃ svāyāṃ sthirāṃ dṛḍhāṃ bhāvayet |

pūrvoktarityāha |

iti prāṇapratiṣṭa |

atha mātṛkā nyāsa |

asya śrīmātṛkā mantrasya brahmā ṛṣiḥ śirasi |

devī gāyatri chanda mukheḥ |

mātṛkā devatā hṛdaye |

vyaṃjanāni vījāni guhye svarāśaktayaḥ pādayo |

atha mātṛkā ṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ |

a/ ka/ kha/ ga/ gha/ ṅa/ ā/ hṛdayā namaḥ |

ai/ ta/ tha/ da/ dha/ na/ ai/ kavacāya hū/ |

iti mātṛkā ṣaḍaṃganyāsa sarvapāpaharaḥ |

a/ ā/ i/ ī/ u/ ū/ ṛ/ ṝ/ ḷ/ ḹ/ ai/ ai/ o/ au/ a/ aḥ |

naubhaudaprālepadme |

319b) ādhāre caturdale |

va/ śa/ ṣa/ sa/ vinyeset |

bhūmadhye dvidalakamalapadme |

ha/ kṣa/ vinyaset |

ityannarmātṛkā nyāsa atha mātṛkā dhyānam |


paṃcāśadvarṇarūpā śaśikalita jaṭā sphāṭikasyaṣṭavarṇā 
śuddhakṣaumāḍhyaśobhā vividhamaṇigaṇai kajjale nāpi bhūṣā |

dakṣe vāme ca yugme karasarasiruhe jāpya mālā varādyāṃ 
saudhaṃ pātraṃ ca pūrṇatilasatuhṛdaye mātṛkā saṃvahanti || 19-60 ||


iti dhyātvā nyase |

aṃ murddhni āṃ mukhavṛtte | i/ ī/ netreyo |

mūlasandhyagreṣṭa |

ṭaṃ ṭhaṃ ḍaṃ ḍhaṃ ṇaṃ taṃ thaṃ daṃ dhaṃ naṃ dakṣivāmapādamūla 
saṃdhyagreṣṭa |

yaṃ tvaci hṛdaye | raṃ raktadakṣavāhūmūle | 

laṃ māṃse vāmavāhumūle | vaṃ medasi galāparabhāge |

śaṃ aṣṭidakṣakakṣe | ṣaṃ majjāyāṃ vāmakakṣe |

saṃ śukre hṛdayādi pāṇiyuge | haṃ ātmani hṛdayādi pāṇe yuge | 

haṃ ātmani hṛdayādi pādayādi pādayuge mukhe |

laṃ jīvātmani jaṭhare vyāpaka/ | kṣaṃ paramātmani āsanavyāpaka/ |

iti va hi mātṛkā nyāsaḥ |

śrīvaglāmukhyai namaḥ |


brahmāstrapūjanavidhiṃ pra * kṣyāmi samāsataḥ |

sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya staṃbhanāya ca vairiṇām || 19-61 ||


kṛtanitya kriyaḥ |

320a) prāṇāyamaṃ sthalaśuddhi bhūtaśuddhi prāṇapratiṣṭā mātṛkā nyāsa 
prāṇāyāmapūrvakaṃ gurucaraṇāravindaṃ śirasimabhyarcya |

asyāḥ śrīvagalāmukhi vidyā yā |

brahmā ṛṣiḥ | śirasi | devī gāyatri chandaḥ mukhe |

śrīvagalāmukhi devatādaye |

klī/ vījaṃ dakṣastene svāhāśaktiḥ vāmastane | o/ kīlakaṃ nābhau |

mama caturvidhapuruṣārtha siddhārthe jape viniyoga |

ityaṃguliṃ vaddhā namaskuryāt |

athavā cūlakena jalaṃ bhūmau nikṣipet |

atha karanyāsaḥ |

o/ ai/ hlā/ vagalāmukhi aṃguṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ |

au/ ai/ hā/ vagalāmukhi tarjanībhyā/ namaḥ |

au/ ai/ hlā/ vagalāmukhi madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ |

o/ ai/ hlā/ vagalāmukhi anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ |

o/ ai/ hlā/ vagalāmukhi kaniṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ |

kṣo/ ai/ hlā/ vagalāmukhi karatalapṛṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ |

ā/ hlā/ aṃguṣṭābhyāṃ namaḥ | oṃ hlī/ tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ |

o/ hlū/ madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ | au/ hlai/ anāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ |

o/ hlau/ kaniṣṭābhyā/ namaḥ | oṃ hlaḥ karatalakarapṛṣṭābhyā namaḥ |

vagalāmukhi tarjanibhyā/ namaḥ | sarvaduṣṭānāṃ madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ |

320b) o/ ai/ hlā/ vagalāmukhi śikhāyai vaṣaṭ | o/ ai/ hlā/ vagalāmukhi kavacāya 
hū/ |

o/ hlā/ hṛdayā namaḥ | vagalāmukhi śirasi pūrvavat |

iti ṣaḍaṃganyāsaḥ |

tato mūlavidyāmucārya | ātmatattvavyāpini vagalāmukhyaṃ pūjayāmi hṛdaye |

mūlena tatvavyāpini vagalāmukhi vā śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmi śirasi |

pūjayāmi | brahmarandhre |

atha dhyānam |

madhye sudhābdhimaṇimaṇḍaparatnavidyāṃ siṃhāsano parigatāṃ 
paripītavarṇām |

pītāṃvarābharaṇamālya vibhūṣitāṅgi devī smarāmi dhṛta 
mudgaravairijihvām || 19-62 ||

jihvāgrasādāya kareṇa devīṃ vāmena śatrun paripīḍayantim |

gadābhighātena ca dakṣiṇena pītāmvarāḍhyāṃ dvibhujāṃ namāmi || 19-63 ||

triśuladhāriṇī devīṃ sarvasiddhi pradāyinim |

sarvaśṛṃgāraveśāḍhyām iti dhyātvā prapūjayet || 19-64 ||

gaṃbhīrāṃ ca madonmattāṃ taptakāṃcana saṃnibhām |

catubhujāṃ trinayanāṃ kamalāsana saṃsthitam || 19-65 ||

mudgaraṃ dakṣiṇe vā śaṃ vāme jihvāṃ ca vajrakam |

pītāṃvaradharāṃ sādra dṛḍhapīnapayodharā |

hemakuṇḍalabhūṣāṃ ca pītacandrārdhaśekharām || 19-67 ||

pītabhūṣaṇabhūṣāṅgi svarṇasiṃhāsana sthitām |

321a) trinetro dvibhujāṃ ramyāṃ pītālaṃkālabhūṣitām || 19-68 ||

pītamudgarasaṃyuktāṃ duṣṭakaṣṭanivāsiṇim |


pītavarṇā pītagandhānulepanām |

vahiryajanamārabhat |

kamalāt |

parameśvarīmāvāhayāmi namaḥ |

ityāvāhayet |

321a) śrīvagalāmukhī idaṃ madhuparka adyantāssanasahitaṃ 
madhuparkasamarpayāmi namaḥ |

tataḥ puratro candanādināṣṭāṅgulaṃ caturaśraṃ maṇḍalaṃ tan madhye 
ṣaṭkoṇaṃ kṛtvā tadupari rakṣālitāṃ triyādikāṃ saṃsthāpya tasyopari

vaṃdhūmrārciṣe namaḥ laṃ kapilāyai namaḥ |

vaṃ visphaliṃginyai namaḥ | śaṃ jvālāmālinyai namaḥ |

ṣaṃ arcipmatyai namaḥ | saṃ havyavarāhāyai namaḥ |

haṃ rauḍyai namaḥ | laṃ kṣaṃ sāriṇyai namaḥ |

ityagnikalā saṃpūjya tatorghyaṃ pātrakarpūrādi vāsitaṃ jalena vilomamātṛkā 
mūlavidyāṃ japan tat pātre |

arkamaṇḍalaṃ dvādaśakalā pūjāyet | tadyathā |

kaṃ vaṃ tapinyai namaḥ | khaṃ bhaṃ tāpinyai namaḥ |

ḍhaṃ jyeṣṭāyai namaḥ | ḍhaṃ ḍaṃ hiraṇyadāyai namaḥ |

ityarkakalāḥ saṃpūjayet |

322a) mūlavidyāṣṭākṣata candrana puṣpāni dadyāt |

tato dhenumudrāṃ yonimudrāṃ ca darśayet |

śa śīṃ śuṃ śe śau śa śukraśāya vimocinyai svāhā 

ityabhimanya amṛtaṃ śī smṛtvā ai/ klī/ śau amṛte atodbhave amṛteśvari 
amṛtavarṣiṇi amṛtaṃ srāvaya srāvaya sāṃ ju juṃ saḥ | amṛteśvaryai svāhā |

ityamṛte śvatvā dhenumudrā yonimudrā darśayet |

anandabhairava surādevyai śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmi tarpayāmi namaḥ |

iti candanagandhapuṣpākṣatātini savye vāme dvitīye mikṣipya pātrāṇi pūrayet |
ādau kuṃbhaṃ tata śaṃkhaṃ śrīpātraṃ śaktipātrakam |
valipātraṃ bhogapātrakam |

tarpaṇaṃ kuryāt |

mūlena saptavāraṃ gāvaraṇaṃ tarjanyaguṣṭayogena santarpya tripuṣpāṃjali 
vidhāya |

gāṃ gaṇeśāya namaḥ | pūrva | vaṃ vaṭukāya namaḥ | dakṣiṇadvāre |

daṃ durgāyai namaḥ | paścimadvāre | kṣaṃ kṣetrapālāya namaḥ | uttaradvāre |

iśānyādi va sainyāyāṃ gurupaṃktiṃ prapūjya |

śrīpūrṇanāthānandanāthāya lalitāṃ vā śaktiyuktāya śrīpādukāṃ 
pūjayami tarpayāmi namāḥ |

śrīgaganānandanāthāya cichakti aṃvāsahitāya śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmi

323a) ityaṣṭapatrāṇi saṃpūjya ṣaṭpatrāṇi pūjayet

o/ hlī/ bhagasiddhā vā śrī (4) | hlī/ bhagapatityaṃcā śrī (5) |

hlī/ bhagamālinyaṃvā śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmi namaḥ |

itāḥ sarvāḥ prapūjyaṣaṭkoṇakrameṇa | tataḥ trikoṇapūjā |

aparā vagalāmukhyaṃ vā śrīpādukām |

parā vagalāmukhyaṃvā śrīpādukāṃ pūjayāmi tarpayāmi namaḥ |

mūlena |

karpūrādivāsitaṃ jalaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ | mūlena |

323b) tāṃvūlaṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ | mūle nārī rātrikāṃ kuryāt |

japaṃ saṃkhyā nivadayet |

aṣṭottaraśataṃ vā sahasraparimitaṃ kṛtam |

parā vagalāmukhyāṃ puṣpāṃjaliṃ samarpayāmi namaḥ |

iti puṣpāṃjaliṃ trayaṃ dadyāt | stotrādikaṃ paṭhet |



paṭhattarehaṇā devi madanasya yathāvidhiḥ |

svāhā |

eva mevā parāparetyādi iti vargalatrayaṃ dadyāt |

tato vārāgre lakuṭakharparadharaṃ bhairavaṃ saṃpūjya |

ai/ hrī/ śrī/ a/ caṇḍabhaira kṣetrapālā ehe hi mama vighnaṃ bhaṃjaya 
amukaduṣṭaṃ bhaṃjaya khādaya khādaya hrī/ amukasya mukhaṃ staṃbhaya 2 
mamepsitaṃ kuru kuru imāṃ vali gṛhna 2 svāhā |

valipūjānena metraṇa kartyavyā svapātre śrīguruṃ siraśi santarpa gurave namaḥ 
pātram |

śaktyai pātraṃ sādhakebhyaḥ pātraṃ sadakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt |


evaṃ yaḥ pūjed bhaktyā siddhistasya prajāyate |

agneyaṃ caiva śaktiyaṃ śāṃbhavaṃ ca padaṃ labhet || 19-73 ||

324a) evaṃ tu kathitaṃ bhadre gurvājñā leṃghayet tu yaḥ |

|
