śrīmahāgratārovāca


rājñāṃ maṇḍalakādīni malopai sarpatām |

sarvāgaṇāṃ mahādeva dhanavikṛta cetasām || 19-75 ||

gajasarpādi jaṃbhūnāṃ staṃbhanaṃ vada śaṃkaraḥ |

śrībhaiva uvāca

purā kṛta yuge devi vātakṣobhamupasthite || 19-76 ||

saptārṇavānāme tvaṃ gatakṣobhaṃ yayusurāḥ |

aindrāsma viṣṇvaḥ sarve sabhayā māmupasthitāḥ || 19-77 ||

devā ucuḥ 

śivaśaṃkabhūdreśa rakṣā smā śaraṇāgatam |

mahāvātādi vikṣobha kṣavādasmān mahārṇavāt || 19-78 ||

tachṛtvā haṃ sureśāni kavacaṃ pūrvanirmitam |

dattavān sarvavedebhyo mahābhayanikṛntanam || 19-79 ||

kavacaṃ vagalākhyāṃ śiva prāṇapradaṃ mahat |

pūrvaṃ bhasmāsura trāsād bhayavihvālitaḥ svayam || 19-80 ||

paṭhe te tenamaprāṇān rakṣa parameśvarī |

śiva prāṇapradaṃ tasmād viśutaṃ rakṣakaṃ param || 19-81 ||


asya śrīvagalāmukhi kavacasya śrībhairava ṛṣiḥ uṣṇikachanda advaitarūpiṇi 
mahāstaṃbhanakāriṇi śrīpitāṃvarā devatā sthira māyā vījaṃ svāhāśakti 
praṇavaḥ kīlakaṃ mama dūrasthānāsatipasthānā sarvaduṣṭānāṃ vākpada 
gati mukhastaṃbhanārthaṃ parasainyamantra yantroṣadhastaṃbhanārthaṃ 
sarvarājakulamohanārthaṃ parā śrīvagalāmukhi prītyarthaṃ jape viniyogaḥ |

atha nyāsaḥ |

oṃ hrā/ hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ hlī/ śirase svāhā | o/ hlu/ śikhāyai vauṣaṭ |

o/ hlai/ kavacāya hum | o/ hlau/ netrebhyo vauṣaṭ | o/ hliḥ karatala astrāya phaṭ |

evaṃ maguṣṭādikaratalāntaṃ vinyaset |

o/ hlī/ hṛdayāye namaḥ | vagalāmukhi śirase svāhā |

sarvaduṣṭānāṃ śikhāyai vaṣaṭ | vācaṃ mukhapadaṃ stambhaya kavacāya hum 
|

jihvāṃ kīlaya vuddhiṃ vināsaya hlī/ netratrayāya vauṣaṭ |

o/ svarahā astrāya phaṭ |


dhyānam |

madhe sudhābdhi maṇimaṇḍaparatnavedyāṃ siṃhāsano parigatāṃ 
paripītavarṇām |

pītāṃvarā bharaṇamālyavibhūṣitāṅgi deviṃ namāmi 
dhṛtamudgaravairijihvām || 19-82 ||

ṣaṭtriṃśadakṣarāmevyādvagalā paramāśira |

sahasrāre gurupātu śaktyā paramayo * la |

vidrūpā paramāśaktirlalāṭaśivagehi bhūyugaṃ 

me mahākāli netrayugmaṃ pareśvarī || 19-83 ||

pītapuṣpārcitā kaṇṭhaṃ skandhau skandastana prabhā |

pītāṃvarā dakṣabhujaṃ vāmaṃ vāmāṅguhāriṇī || 19-84 ||

325a) hṛdayaṃ sastanadvandvaṃ bhānuvimvamukhaikadhṛk |

pṛṣṭaṃ kāṇḍadharaṃ me vyāt kaṭikandarpa rūpadhṛk || 19-85 ||

hṛtpaṃkajaṃ śaivaśaktirviśuddhaṃ jīvarūpiṇī |

ājñācakro vidurūpā parabrahma kuṭaṃvini || 19-86 ||

brahmarandhrasahasrāra paṃkajākhya svarūpadhṛk |

pātu māṃ paramāśaktiñcamarī kuṭilālakā || 19-87 ||

ṣaṭtriṃśadakṣarā vidyā pītāpitavapudharā |

pītapuṣpārcitā pita naivedyādi valipriyā || 19-88 || 
 
sarvāga me śubhā pātu vairistaṃbhanakāriṇī |

brahmarandhreṣu parmā saṃsārārsāvatāriṇi || 19-89 ||

tārayen mahādevī manovāchitadāyinī |

ajñācakre gurorūpā mahābhayavināśini || 19-90 ||

sarvavidyā parā pātu jihvāṃ śāstravayurdharā |

viśuddhau ṣoḍaśadale jīveśvaravayurdharā || 19-91 ||

ṣaṭtriṃśattattvarūpākhyā kaṇṭhasthā māṃ sadā va tu |

hṛtpaṃkaje bhavānīśa rūpārūpaparātmikā || 19-92 ||

indriyādi mahāśatrun vidāraya tu dārayet |

maṇipūre daśadale mahāviṣṇu svarūpadhṛk || 19-93 ||

dārayan nakhilān mohān māmā yā sā parā va tu |

prajāpati svarūpe yaṃ svādhiṣṭāne ṣaḍṛlenaḥ || 19-94 ||

sṛṣṭisthiti tā yarā vidyā māṃso hāddhagalā va tu |

gaṇaśarūpadhak pātu mādhāre caturdare || 19-95 ||

ṣoḍaśasvarapuruye yaṃ pātu māṃ paramākalā |

ka/ kha/ ga/gha/ tathā ṅa/ ca/ cha/ ja/ jha/ ña/ tathā va tu || 19-96 ||

ṭa/ ṭha/ ḍa/ ḍha/ tvaṃ mākāga ṇa/ ta/ tha/ da/ tathā va tu |

dha/ ta/ pa/ pha/ tu śatrubhyaḥ pātu māṃ dhyāna tatparam || 19-97 ||

325b) va/ bha/ ma/ ya/ vidāryārtha śatrūn māṃ vāk svarūpiṇi |

ra/ la/ va/ śa/ tathā ṣa/ sa/ śatrū jihvā/ vidārya tu || 19-98 ||

mevā caṃ mu mukhaṃ jihvāṃ sadārakṣatu rakṣatu |

ha/ la/ kṣa sakalaṃ pātu rakṣe me paramaṃ yaśaḥ || 19-99 ||

praṇavaṃ sthiramādyā ca tṛtīyaṃ kāntitayaṃ tathā |

yā tṛtīyaṃ svarādyugmaṃ pādādya svarapaṃcamam || 19-100 ||

kā dvitīyaṃ dakṣanetra sarvaduṣṭavahusmṛtam |

ṣaṣṭarāvācaṃ sukhapadaṃ staṃbhayāthāpi jihvāyā || 19-101 ||

dvitīya yā kīlayanti tathā vuddhiṃ vināśayaḥ |

sthiramāyā tathā tāraṃ cānte vahni vadhūpirye || 19-102 ||

eṣā vidyā mahāvidyā śatrūstaṃbhanakāriṇī |

tridhāmantraṃ samucārya savāṅgavyāpakaṃ kuru || 19-103 ||

japatyeva na saṃdehaḥ śatrūśūrān mahāhere |

kulālamṛttikāṃ gṛhya vāmahastena dīpakam || 19-104 ||

kṛtvā prajvālya tailena vāme sunapyamanuṃ japa |

trirātraṃ staṃbhayabhye va śatrūsenā mahāhave || 19-105 ||

gauravarṇā pītapuṣpairvālāṃ saṃpūjya bhāvata |

nyāsān vidhāya dehena bhajanyogī japen manum || 19-106 ||

pītopacārestāṃ śaktiṃ santoṣya paricāraṇān |

vaśayabheva lokeśaṃ kiṃ punakṣudramānuṣam || 19-107 ||

vācaḥ staṃbhanakāmastu vīraśaktiṃ śubhānanām |

ṣoḍaśābdāṃ suvarṇādyairalaṃkṛtya bhajanpriye || 19-108 ||

sahasraṃ mūlaṃ mantraṃ tu japtvā cācaspate rapi |

staṃbhayetyeva paramāṃ cā caṃ kikṣudramānuṣam || 19-109 ||

vaśikaraṇakāmastu vaddhā parikaraṃ dṛḍham |

śaktiṃ liṃgoparisthāpya trisahasraṃ japen manum || 19-110 ||

326a) indrāṇīṃ vaśayatyeva kiṃ punaḥ kṣudramānuṣim |

maṇḍalidhīśa vaśakṛduttarābhimukhaṃ sthitaḥ || 19-111 ||

dakṣānanāṃ sudaṃritu ṣoḍaśābdāṃ sucismitām |

kucayostu karau dantvā pītvādyāṃ hasitānanām || 19-112 ||

liṃge sṃsthāpya suṃcuṃvan muṃkhaṃ pītāmanuṃ japan |

trisahasraṃ mahārāja vaśayatyeva nānyathā || 19-113 ||

yena kenāpyupāyena vagalābhakti talparaḥ |

staṃbhayet sakalāṃ lokān vaśayatyevaśaṃkaram || 19-114 ||

māraye chatrusaṃghātamucāṭayati parvatām |

devānāṃ sthapatiṃ cadraṃ ghātayat kiṃ paraṃ janam || 19-115 ||

yasya pārvati māhātmyaṃ kavacasya kva varṇitam |

mahādridāruṇācānte pralayād āvupasthite || 19-116 ||

staṃvitaṃ caikadā devi cātasaṃghaṃ sakṛjjayāt |

eṣā sā paramā vidyā mahāśatruvidāriṇī || 19-117 ||

nāstike taskare duṣṭe mūḍhaṃ paṇḍitamanini |

nadeyā hṛdayā vidyā dadaṃbhayamavāpnuyāt || 19-118 ||

suśīlāya sudāntāya gurubhakti parāya ca |

viśuddhāya mahāmantraṃ jāpine deyamuttamam || 19-119 ||

kṛtvā purā sukavacaṃ japen mantraṃ samāhitaḥ |

nānyathā staṃbhinī vidyā siddhāsyāditiścayaḥ || 19-120 ||


iti śrī-ekavīrātatre mahogratārāsaṃvāde ṣaṭtriṃśarakṣarā vidyāratnaprastā 
ve vagalāmukhi kavacaṃ saṃpūrṇam |

satha sahasranāmaḥ |

oṃ surālaye pradhāne tu deva devaṃ maheśvaram |

326b) nānāsahasraṃ vagalāmukhyāḥ kathayavallabhaḥ || 19-121 ||

īśvara uvāca

śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi nāmnāṃ devyāḥ sahasrakam |

parabrahmāstravidyā yā caturvargaṃ phalapradam || 19-122 ||

guhyā guhyataraṃ devī sarvasiddhaikavanditaḥ |

atiguhyatalā vidyā sarvatatreṣu popitā || 19-123 ||

virodhinī vighnasaṃghānāṃ mohini parayoṣitā |

staṃvani rājasainyānāṃ vādini paravādinām || 19-124 ||

purā caikārṇave ghore kāle paramabhairave |

sundarī sahito deva keśavaḥ kleśanāśanaḥ || 19-125 ||

uragāsanamāsīno yoganidrāmupāgamam |

nidrākāle citkelaśi mayā proktā sanātanaḥ || 19-126 ||

mahāstaṃbhakaraṃ deva stotraṃ vā śanāmakam |

sahasranāmaparamaṃ vada devasya kasya kasyacit || 19-127 ||

śrībhagavānuvāca

śṛṇu śaṃkara deveśa paramātirahasyakam |

ajohaṃ yat prasādena viṣṇuḥ sarveśvaraḥśvari |

gopaniyaṃ prayatnena prakāśāt siddhihāni kṛt || 19-128 ||


asya śrīpitāṃvarā sahasranāma stotrasya śrībhagavān sadāśiva ṛṣi 
anuṣṭupchadaḥ śrījagadvasikari pītāṃvarā devatā sarvābhiṣṭa siddhyarthe jape 
viniyogaḥ |

atha dhyānam |


pitāṃvaraparīdhānāṃ pīnonnatapayodharān |

jaṭāmukuṭaśobhāḍhyāṃ pitṛpisukhāvahām || 19-129 ||

śatrājjihvā mudgaraṃ ca vibhratīṃ paramokalām |

sarvāgamapurāṇeṣu vikhyātā bhuvanatraye || 19-130 ||

327a) sṛṣṭisthiti vinaśānāmādibhūtāṃ maheśvarīm |

gopyaṃ sarvaprayatnena śṛṇutāṃ kathayāmi te || 19-131 ||

jagadvidhvaṃsini devīmajarāmarakāriṇīm |

to namāmi mahāmāyā mahadaiśvarya dāyinim || 19-132 ||

praṇavapūvamudhṛtya sthiramāyāṃ tato vadet |

vagalāmukhi sarveti duṣṭānāṃ vācameva ca || 19-133 ||

mukhaṃ pada staṃbhayeti jihvāṃ kīlaya siddhimat |

vināśayeti tāraṃ ca sthiramāyāṃ tato vadet || 19-134 ||

vahni priyānvito mantraścaturvargaphalapradaḥ |

atha nāma |

brahmāstrā brahmavidyā ca brahmabhūtā sanātani || 19-135 ||

brahmeśrīvamalā vagalā brahmacāriṇi |

nityānandā nityasiddhā nityarūpā nirāmayām || 19-136 ||

saṃdhāriṇī mahāmāyā kaṭākṣakṣepakāriṇi |

kamalā vikalā nīlā ratnatā bhṛgu ṇāsitā || 19-137 ||

maṃgalā vijayā jāyā sarvamaṃgalakāriṇi |

yāmini kāmini kānyā kāmukā kāmacāriṇī || 19-138 ||

kāmapriyā kāmaratā kāmakāmasvarūpiṇi |

kāmākhyā kāmavījasthā kāmapīṭhanivāsini || 19-139 ||

kāmadā kāmahā kāli kapālikarālikalā |

kaṃsārikamalā kāli kailāseśvaravallabhā || 19-140 ||

kātyāyani keśavā ca karuṇākāmakīlayuk |

kriyā kīrttikartṛkā ca kāśī kāmāturā śiva || 19-141 ||

kāmaṃghnī kālikākārā tārā tā dhavāmalā |

327b) khecarī ca khamūttiśca kṣudrākṣudradharāvarā || 19-142 ||

kharḍgahastā kharatarā khaḍginikharpara priyā |

gaṃgā gauri gāmini ca gītā gotravivarddhini || 19-143 ||

godhārā gaukarā gopyā gandharvapuravāsini |

gāndharvi gandharvakalā gopalī garuḍāsanā || 19-144 ||

govindābhāgovindā gādhāri gandhamādini |

gaurāṃgi gopikā mūrtirgopagoṣṭanivāsini || 19-145 ||

gandhā gajendragamanī gaṃgādhara priyā gada |

ghanāghorā ghorarūpa naśreṇī ghanaprabhā || 19-146 ||

daityandra pravalāghaṇṭā nādini ghoraniḥsvana |

ḍākinyumā upendrā ca urvaśī uragāsanā || 19-147 ||

uttamā unna unnā uttamasthāna vāsini |

cāmuṇḍā caṇḍikā caṇḍī caṇḍadarpahareti || 19-148 ||

ugracaṇḍā caṇḍaghaṇṭā caṇḍadaityavināśini |

caṇḍarūpā pracaṇḍā ca candrā caṇḍaśaririṇī || 19-149 ||

caturbhujā pracaṇḍāra carācaranivāsinī |

catraprāyaṃ śiro vāhau chalachalatarāchali || 19-150 ||

chalarūpā chaladharā kṣatriyā kṣayakāriṇī |

jayā ca jayadurgā ca jayanti jayadā parā || 19-151 ||

jathinī jaṭinī jyotsnā jaṭādhāra priyā jitā |

jitendriyā jitakrodha japamālā jaleśvarī || 19-152 ||

jitamṛtyu jarātītā jāhnavījanakātmakā |

jhaṃkāri jharjharājhaṇḍā jhajhājhaṃkāraśobhini || 19-153 ||

jharjharajhamurājhāri jharājharatarā parā |

ña śābhrametā ñakārī ñaṇākalyāṇadāyiṇī || 19-154 ||

328a) amena manasā nityā ñayuktā śa.kara priyā |

ṭokā ṭopā ṭakayatī ṭāminī ṭamanapriyā || 19-155 ||

ṭhakāradhārṇī ṭhīvā ṭhakārā ṭhīrakarapriyā |

ṭhekanāṃ sā ṭhakavatī ṭhāmini ṭhamana priyā || 19-156 ||

ḍarahā ḍākini ḍārāmarā ḍāmara priyā |

ḍaṃgini ḍarāṅyuktā ca ḍamarūḍaravallabhā || 19-157 ||

ṭhakkāḍakkāṭokanā hāṭolaśabdapravodhinī |

ḍhāmini ḍhamanapritā ḍhagatantraprakāśitā || 19-158 ||

anekarūpiṇī ānā aṇimā siddhidāyini |

āmatriṇi ākāri ca ānamadbhānu saṃstutā || 19-159 ||

tārātantra va bhītārtā tantvarūpā tapasvini |

taraṃgiṇi tantva parā tantrikātantravigrahā || 19-160 ||

tattvarūpā tantvadhātri tantvavratapradarśinī |

tantrayatrārya na parātalātalavilāsinī || 19-161 ||

talpadā kalpadā kāmyā sthirā sthiratarādayā |

rūpādāvāgni * * * * * * dāminī dayā || 19-162 ||

durgā durgaparā devi duṣṭadaitya vināśinī |

madanapradā daityahā dayā na parāyaṇā || 19-163 ||

durgatināśini dattādābhirānanda varjitā |

digaṃvarapriyā dānā daityadaṃbhavidāriṇī || 19-164 ||

damanā daśasaudaryā dānavendravināśini |

dayādharā ca dapini darbhayatra vilāsinī || 19-165 ||

dharaṇī dhīraṇī dhātri dharādhara dharapriyā |

dharmajña dhavalā dhūlī dhanadā dhanavardhinī || 19-166 ||

dharā dharā sutā devī sudharmādharmāvāriṇī |

dhīrā dhīrādhīrasiddhi pradā yaśī || 19-167 ||

caṃ * lari dharadharādhayā dhātu svarūpiṇi |

328b) nārāyaṇī nārasiṃhi nityānandānaromā || 19-168 ||

naktāṃ naktavati nityā nīlajīmulasannidhi |

nīlāṅgī nīlavastrā ca nīlaparvatavāsinī || 19-169 ||

munīlā puṣpakhacitā nīlaṃvujasamaprabhā |

nityakhyā ṣoḍaśī vidyā nityānitya sukhāvahā || 19-170 ||

narmadā nandahā sunandacarcinī |

yaśodānanda nayanā nandanodyānavāsinī || 19-171 ||

nagāntakā nāgavṛddhā nāgapatni ca nāgini |

namitā śoṣajanitā namaskāravatī namaḥ || 19-172 ||

pītāṃvarā pārvatī ca pitāṃvaraṇabhūṣitā |

pitamātyāṃvaradhari pitāṃ piṅgalamurdhajā || 19-173 ||

pītapuṣpācanatarā pītapuṣpasamarcitā |

paraprabhā pitṛpatiḥ parasainya vināśini || 19-174 ||

paramā paratatrā ca paramantrā parātparā |

parāvidyā parāsiddhiḥ parasthāna pradāyinī || 19-175 ||

puṣpā puṣpavati puṣpā puṣpamālyā vibhūṣitā |

pūrātanā pūrvaparāparasiddhi pradāyini || 19-176 ||

pitānītaṃ vinipītā pīnonnatapanastanī |

premā pramadhyamā śeṣā padmapatravilāsinī || 19-177 ||

padmāvati padmanetrā padmā padmamukhī parā |

admāsanā padmapriyā padmarāgasvarūpiṇi || 19-178 ||

pāvanālikāpātrā paradāra śivā parā |

pretasaṃsthā parānandā parabrahmasvarūpiṇi || 19-179 ||

jiveśvarapriyā devī paśuraktaratipriyā |

paśumāṃsaṃ priyā parṇā parāmṛtaparāyaṇa || 19-180 ||

329a) pāśini pāśikā pānā pasavī paśubhakiṇī |

parānanda pradā devi paśupāśavināśini || 19-181 ||

phullā ravindanā phullotpalaśaririṇī |

phutkārāha parāphārṇā phullendivaralocalocanā || 19-182 ||

phaṇmantrā sphāṭikī svāhā sphotasphotā phasvarūpiṇi |

sphuṭikā guṭhikā dhīrā sphāṭikādi svarūpini || 19-183 ||

varāṅganā varadharā vārā hi vā sulivarā |

vindusthā vindinī vāmā vinducakranivāsini || 19-184 ||

vidyādhari viśālākṣī vāsijana priyā |

vedavidyā virūpākṣī viśvayugvadgabhūpini || 19-185 ||

paṃcaśaktirviśaktiḥ paṃcavaktrā śiva priyā |

vaikuṇṭhavāsini devi vaikuṇṭheśvaravallabhā || 19-186 ||

vaikuṇṭharūpi rūpiṇī devī vaikuṇṭhaparadāyiṇī |

brahmarūpā viṣṇurāpā parabrahmamaheśini || 19-187 ||

bhavapriyā bhavodbhāvā bhavā rūpā bhavodbhavā |

bhavapārā bhavā dhārā bhāgyavat priyakāriṇi || 19-188 ||

bhadrā subhadrā bhavadā bhavadaityavināśinī |

bhavāni bhairavā bhīmā bhadrakāli śubhadrikā || 19-189 ||

bhagini bhagarūpāśca bhagamālā bhagāttamā |

bhagavati bhagavāsā bhagavāsā bhagākārā || 19-190 ||

bhagaśṛṣṭā bhagāvati bhagarūpā bhagāsini |

bhagaliṅgapriyā devi bhagaligaparāyaṇā || 19-191 ||

bhagaliṃgasvarūpā ca bhagali * vinodini |

bhagaliṃgagatā devi bhagaliṃgavilāsini || 19-192 ||

bhagamālā bhagakalā bhagākārā bhagāṃvarā |

329b) bhagavegā bhagabhūṣā bhagendrā bhāgyarūpiṇī || 19-193 ||

bhagaliṅgāṅgasaṃbhogā bhagaliṃgaṃ samāvahā |

bhagaliṃga samādhuryā bhagaliṃganiṣevitā || 19-194 ||

bhagaliṃga supūjyā ca bhagaliṃgasukhānvitā |

bhagaliṃgaviraktā ca bhagaliṃgasamāvṛtā || 19-195 ||

mādhavī mādhavā mānyā madhurāmadhumālini |

maṃdahā mā mahāmāyā mohini mahattamā || 19-196 ||

mahāmohā mahāvidyā mahāghorā mahāsmṛti |

manasvini mānaratā mohinī madhurānanaḥ || 19-197 ||

menikā māninī saunāmaṇiratnavibhūṣiṇā |

mallikā maulikāmālā mālādharabhadottamā || 19-198 ||

māttasiṃhaṃ samollāsa mattasiṃhamahāsanāḥ |

madanā sundarī medhā madhumattā madhupriyā || 19-199 ||

mahendravallabhāsīnā śailā ca midhunātmajā |

mahāmālyā mahākāli manovuddhirmahotkaṭā || 19-200 ||

maheśvarī mahāmāyā mahiṣāsuramardini |

madhurā kīrttikā mattā mattamātaṅgagāmini || 19-201 ||

madapriyā mānsaratā mattayuktā makāriṇī |

midhunā vallabhā devī mahānandā mahotsavā || 19-202 ||

māricirmaricirmānā manovuddhipradāyini |

mahāmokṣā mahālakṣmīrmahatpadapradāyini || 19-203 ||

yamarūpā ca yamunā jayanti ca yaśaḥ pradā |

yāmyā yāmavati yuddhā pādoḥ kulavivardhinī || 19-204 ||

ramā rāmā rāmapatni ratnamālā ratipriyā |

ratnasiṃhāsanasthā ca ratnībharaṇamaṇḍitā || 19-205 ||

330a) ramaṇiramaṇiyā ca ratiramadhparāyaṇā |

ratānandā ratavati raghunāṃ kulavarddhini || 19-206 ||

ramaṇā raṃkiniratyā raidhārādhikaratnajā |

rantrirasasvarūpā ca rātriranasukhāvahā || 19-207 ||

ritujā ṛdidāridyā riturūpā ṛtupiyā |

raktapriyā raktaratā raṃginiraktadantikā || 19-208 ||

lakṣmīllājjācalati kāli līlolālatākṣiṇi |

lilāvati lomaharṣā lalāṭapadikā sthitā || 19-209 ||

brahmarūpā ca brahmajña vedamātā ca vaidīki |

brahmodbhavā brahmakalā brahmaṇi brahmavādini || 19-210 ||

vedāṃganā vedarūpā vanitā ca nitā ca sā |

vindhyasthā vindhyavāmāravindhya vā ca vindhyarūpyavibhūṣikā |


vidyāvati vedadhari vyāpikā rūpiṇikalā |

śivāśiva pri śuddhāśuddhādgī śuklavarṇikā || 19-214 ||

śuklapuṣpa priyā śuklā śiva dharmaparāyaṇā |

śukrasthā śukraṇī śukrā śukrarūpā ca śukriyā || 19-215 ||

śukramālyāṃvaradharā śuklaya svarūpinī |

ṣadāmūrttiścaṃṣagā ṣaṭcakravinivāsini || 19-216 ||

ṣaḍgaṃthiyuktā ṣoḍā ca parāmātrā ca ṣaḍātmikā |

ṣaḍaṃgayuvatī de devī ṣaḍaṃgaprakṛtiśca sā || 19-217 ||

ṣaḍānanā ṣaḍusā ca ṣaṣṭiṣaṣṭaśvara priyā |

ṣaṭsaṃvādo ṣoḍaśī ca ṣoḍhānyāsa svarūpini || 19-218 ||

ṣaṭcakrabhedanakari ṣaṭcakra svasvarūpiṇi |

ṣoḍaśasvararūpā ca ṣaḍamukhi ṣaṭbhujānvitā || 19-219 ||

sanakādi svarūpā ca viṣṇudharmaparāyaṇā |

sitasaptasvari śuddhā surupā ca tāmurottamā || 19-220 ||

siddhividyā siddhimātā siddhidā siddhirūpiṇi |

harāharipiyā hārā hariṇi hāriyuk tathā || 19-221 ||

hari bhūpā haridharā hariṇākṣi haraprabhā |

hetu piyā heturatā hitabhāhitarūpiṇi || 19-222 ||

harāhari piyā hārā hariṇi hāri yuk tathā |

harirūpā haridharā hariṇākṣī hara prabhā || 19-223 ||

hetu priyā hetu ratā hitabhā hitarūpiṇī |

kṣamākṣamāvati kṣāti kṣudraghaṇṭā vibhūṣiṇā || 19-224 ||

kṣayakāriṇi kṣitiśākṣī ramadhye suśobhanā |

ajānantā avarṇā ca akrayā śeṣaśāyini || 19-225 ||

śāntargatā sādhunī ca na tarānantarūpiṇi |

arūpā amalā āryā anantaguṇaśāliṇi || 19-226 ||

savidyā vidakā vidyā aradhinvavirocanā |

aparājitā cāti jāra ajeyā amalāvati || 19-227 ||

alpā analpā āḍhyā ca ādisiddhi pradāyini |

ādividyā ādibhūtā ādisiddhi pradāyiṇī || 19-228 ||

satkārarūpiṇī devī sarvāsanavibhūṣitā |

331a) indripriyā indrāṇi candrayatnatavāsinī || 19-229 ||

indrākṣī indravajrā ca indramukhyai kṣiṇī tathā |

ilākāgranivāsā ra īśvarīśvaravallabhā || 19-230 ||

jananī yā surādīnāṃ meghā ceśvarakarmakṛt |

umā kātyākāṇi ūrdhvamenā cottaravāsini || 19-231 ||

ṛṣiputrī ṛṣichandā siddhi siddhi pradāyiṇī || 19-232 ||

utsavotsavasīmantā kālī kālī guṇānvitā |

eṣā ikāravidyā ca eṇīvidyādharā tathā || 19-233 ||

ai/kāra pralayā ye tā aiṃkāraparamākalā |

praṇavoṃkorarūpā ca praṇavocārabhāk punaḥ || 19-237 ||

hrī/kārarūpā hrī/kāri vāgvījākṣā bhūṣaṇā |

hṛllekhā siddhiyogo ca hṛtpadmāsana sasthitā || 19-238 ||

vījākhyā netra hṛdayā hrī/ vījā bhuvaneśvarī |

klī/ klī/ klī/ rūpikā devī krī/ krī/ krī/ nāmadhāriṇī |

kamalāśaktivījā ca pāśāṅkuśavibhūṣitā || 19-240 ||

śrīśrīkārā mahāvidyā śraddhāśraddhāvatī tadā |

331a) o/ ai/ hrī/ hrī/ śrīparā ca hrī/ kālī paramālā || 19-241 ||

krī/ śrīkārasvarūpā ca sarvakarmaphalapradā |

sarvādyā sa devī sarvasiddhiśvarī tathā || 19-242 ||

sarvajña sarvaśaktiśca vāgvirūti pradāyiṇī |

sarvamokṣapradā devī sarvabhogapradāyinī || 19-243 ||

guṇandrevallabhatarā sarvaśakti pradāyini |

sarvacakreśvarī devi sarvasiddhiśvarī tathā || 19-244 ||

sarvapriyaṃkari caiva sarvatrasukhadāyiṇī |

sarvanindā pradā devī brahmānanda parāyiṇī || 19-245 ||

manovāṃchitadā caiva manovuddhirsamarcitā |

akārādi kṣakārādikṣantā durgā durgatināśini || 19-246 ||

padmā hastā sunetrā ca svadhā svahā ca ṣṭkarā |

ka ca vargā ṭavargā ca pavargā ca samanvitā || 19-247 ||

antasthā coṣmarūpā ca navadurgā narottamā |

navasiddhi pradā nīlā tathā nīlapatākini || 19-248 ||

nityarūpā niśikari staṃbhanī mehinipi ca |

vaśīkari tathoccāṭī umādinikarṣiṇiti ca || 19-249 ||

mātaṃgi madhumattā ca aṇimā laghimā tathā |

tathā mokṣapradā nityā nityānandā pradāyinī || 19-250 ||

raktāṃgi raktanetrā raktacandanabhūṣitā |

svalpasiddhiḥ śalyakalpā divyavīrayaśukrayāṃ || 19-251 ||

saṃkrānti sarvavidyā ca saptavāsarabhūṣitā |

prathamā ca dvittīyā tṛtīyā ca caturthikā || 19-252 ||

paṃcamī caiva ṣaṣṭi ca viśuddhā saptamī tathā |

aṣṭami navami caiva daśamekādaśī tathā || 19-253 ||

332a) dvādaśī ca cuturdaśyā * * * * thapūrṇimā |

āmāvāśyā tathā pūrvā uttarā paripūrṇikā || 19-254 ||

ṣaṅgini cakriṇighorā gadiniśūli *  tathā |

cāpiṇi vānabhūṣaṇḍi sarvāyudhavibhūṣaṇā || 19-255 ||

kuleśvarī kulācāla parāyaṇā |

kularkarmasuraktā ca kulācāra pravodhinī || 19-256 ||

kīrttiḥ śrīśvaramārāmā dharmārye śatataṃ namaḥ |

kṣamā dhṛtiḥ smṛtirmedhā kalpavṛkṣaphalāsini || 19-257 ||

ugrā ugra prabhavati deva vidyā pravodhinī |

sādhisiddhā susiddhā ca ripurūpā tathaiva ca || 19-258 ||

kālī karāli kātyā ca kāladaityavināśini |

kaulini kulinī caiva kaphavargā ratāpi ca || 19-259 ||

jayati jaya puṣpā ca jayadā jṛṃbhini tathā |

śrīvinidrāvini devī bhemaṇḍā vahnivāsini || 19-260 ||

jyotirūpā ca jayadā jvālāmālā samākulā |

bhinnābhinnaprakāśā ca vihavā bhinnarūpiṇī || 19-261 ||

kaḍuśca vinatākhyātā ditiśvāditireva ca || 19-262 ||

dakṣiṇā cottarā pūrvā paścimādika tathaiva ca |

āgneyī naiṛti yāmyā vāyavyā dik tathā smṛtā || 19-264 ||

ūrdhvagā dhoratā khyātā kṛṣṇā raktā ca pītikā |

catuturyugā catuvarṇā caturpāmā catuṣkṛtā || 19-265 ||

332b) caturmukhi catrvedā cartuvidhā caturmukhā |

caturguṇā caturmānā caturvarṇaphalapradā || 19-266 ||

dhātrividhātrimithunā nārī nāyakavāsini |

surā sudāmudavati medanimedakātsajā || 19-267 ||

arddhakāli siddhikāli dakṣiṇākālikā śivā |

nīlasarasvati tārā vagalā chinnamastakā || 19-268 ||

sarvaśvarī siddhividyā parāparamadevatā |

hiṃgulāhiṃgulāṅgī ca hiṃgulādharavāsinī || 19-269 ||

hiṃgurottamavarṇābhā hiṃgulāraṇi priyā |

jagatī ca jaganmātā jagadīśvaravallabhā || 19-270 ||

jñanadā kari vidyā jānakijanakī priyā |

jayatāṃ japadā nityaṃ jvaladagnisamaprabhā || 19-272 ||

viṃvodharā ca vimvauṣṭi kailāsā calavāsini |

vibhāvā vaibhavamaṭā agnihotraphalapradā || 19-273 ||

mantrarūpā paro devi tatheva gururūpinī |

gayā gaṃgā gomatī ca prabhā sā puṣkarāpi ca || 19-274 ||

vindhyacalā ratā devi vindhyācaranīvāsini |

vahuvāhuḥ sundari ca kaṃsāsuravināśini || 19-275 ||

śuliniśūlahastā ca vajrāvajradharāpi ca |

durgā śivā śāntikari brāhmaṇi brahmaṇa priyā || 19-276 ||

sarvalokapraṇetrī ca sarvalogaharāpi ca |

maṃgalā śobhanā śuddhā niṣkalā paramākulā || 19-277 ||

333a) sadāśivā umā kṣemā caṇḍikā caṇḍivikramā || 19-278 ||

sarvadevamayi devi sarvāgabhayāpahā |

brahma śaviṣṇunamitā sarvakalyāṇakāriṇā || 19-279 ||

yoginī yogamāyā ca yogindra hṛdaya sthitā |

yogijā yā yogavati yogindrā yogini * * || 19-280 ||

indrādi namitā devī iśvarī ceśvara priyā |

viśuddhidā bhayaharā bhaktadveṣabhayaṃkari || 19-281 ||

bhavaveṣā kāmini ca bhīrundrābha ca kāriṇī |

siṃhavāhāhya māhyā ca mohapāśavināśini |

sarveśvarī yakṣiṇī ca kirākṣasi tathā |

mahābhogakarī devī mahāmokṣapradāyini |

viśvahartti viśvarūpā viśvasaṃhārakāriṇi || 19-287 ||

dhātri ca sarvalokānāṃ devānāṃ hitakāriṇī |

kāmini kamalā sūkṣmā devī vedhyā mahāvalā || 19-288 ||

surendra pūjitā saṃdhyāmatejavatiti ca |

parā bhūpavatī devī trailokyā kaṣarūpiṇī || 19-289 ||

sahasra 1000 |

iti te kathitaṃ devī pītanāmasahasrakam |

paṭhed vā pāṭhayed vāpi sarvasiddhirbhavet priyā || 19-291 ||

333b) itibhyāṃ viṣṇunā proktāṃ mahāstaṃbhaikaraṃ param |

prātaḥkāle ca madhyāhne saṃdhyākālerapārvati || 19-291 ||

ekaritta paṭhed etat sarvaṃ siddhiṃ bhaviṣyati |

ekavāraṃ paṭhyed yastu sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet || 19-292 ||

yugmavāraṃ paṭhed yastu gaṇeśasadṛśo bhavet |

trivārapaṭhanārdevi sarvasiddhyanti nānyathā || 19-293 ||

stavasyāsya prabhācena mokṣo bhavati suvrate |

mokṣārthī labhate mokṣaṃ dhanārthi labhate dhanam || 19-294 ||

vidyārthi labhate vidyā tarkavidyā karaṇānvitam |

sahipatirvat sarāsyā chatruhāni prajāyate || 19-295 ||

kṣoṇīpatirvaśastasya smareṇa sadṛśo bhetaḥ |

yaḥ paṭhet sarvadā bhaktyā śrīyukto bhavati priye || 19-296 ||

gaṇādhyakṣya pratinidhiḥ kaviḥ kāvya ivāparaḥ |

gopanīyo prayatnena jananī jāravat sadā || 19-297 ||

hetu yuktā bhaven nityaṃ śaktiyuktaḥ sadā bhavet |

yaḥ idaṃ paṭhate nityaṃ śivena sarvaśo bhavet || 19-298 ||

jīvadharmārthabhogisyāt mṛto mokṣapatirbhavet |

satyaṃ satyaṃ mahādevī satyaṃ satyaṃ maheśvarī || 19-299 ||

tavastastya prabhāvena devena sahamodate |

stambhitāścāsurāssarve stavarājasya kīrttanām || 19-300 ||


iti śrī-utkaṇṭhāt saṃvatsare nāgendraprayāṇatantre ṣoḍaśasāhasre 
viṣṇuśaṃkarasaṃvāde pitāmvarasahasranāmastotraṃ samāpta |


pītāmvaraparīdhānāṃ pītagandhānulepinā |

paramodaya kartti syāt pathanāt surasundarī || 19-301 ||


atha pītāṃvarā stotram |

334a) śrīpitāmvarāyai namaḥ |


oṃkārasya dvayasaṃpuṭītarapaṭaṃ māyāsirādvadvitaṃ 

tanmadhye galāmukhiti vimalaṃ samvodhanaṃ sarvataḥ |

tuṣṭā nāmapi vā ca māśucamukhaṃ sastaṃvayetyakṣaraṃ 

jihvāṃ kīlayaṃ kalayeti ca likhed vuddhiṃ tathā nāśayaḥ || 19-302 ||

brahmastraṃ sakalārtha siddhijananaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśadagākṣaraṃ 

proktaṃ padmabhuvā jitāyajagatāṃ yatrāradāge purā |

jīvaṃ muktipade vibhāṃ tisradhīyo yeṣāṃ mukhe bhāsate 

tirdvaṃdvāmṛtasāgarendu kiraṇā hārāścakorāstu te (2) || 19-303 ||

drormityādi svarūpaṃ jayati tva śive śabdatanmātragarbhā 

vāco yasmāt parārthaṃ prakaṭitapaṭavarṇarūpā niripra brahmādyaiḥ |

paṃcatattvairapi śritamanaghaṃ citpravodhādigamyaṃ durjñeyaṃ yogayukteḥ 
kathamapi manasā yogibhi gṭahyamānam (3) || 19-304 ||

hrī/ vījaṃ hṛdi yasya bhāti vimale lakṣmī sthirā tad gṛhai 
dhairyaṃ tasya kalavarepi bhajate dīrgho yuṣo bhūtale |

kalpātedyapi vṛddhimeti vimalā tad vaṃśavallītarā 

śauryasthairyamupaiti tasya purastasyanti vādhi carāḥ (4) || 19-305 ||

vadhucāridhimudyato janakajā nāthopi pītāṃvare tvāṃ 

dhyātvā navaśoṣaṇe kṛtam itissetuṃ pracakre bhṛśanti |

jitvāravaṇamugraśatrumavalānvandīmuvyāmarāṭ 

kīrttilokasukhodayāṃ vyara ca yat kalpasthirāmaṃvike (5) || 19-306 ||

śīrṣikharvati raṃkati kṣitirmūkāyate vāk pati 

vahniśītatī durjjanaḥ sujanate puṣpāyate vāsuki |

334b) śrīnitya va mele tavākṣarapadairyantrī kṛtā

yantritāḥ kekainīti patati mukuṭaścedrārkatulyā api (6) || 19-307 ||

lāvaṇyā mṛtapūrite tava kṛpā yāṅgani magnā nari 

brahmendrādidigīśabhūtamapi te jānanti muṃjopamam |

yeṣāṃ cetasi sasthitā śivagale te viśvarakṣā kṣamā 

prālabdhaṃ dṛḍhayanti satvārataraṃ vighnairavighni kṛtā (7) || 19-308 ||

mukhyatvaṃ samupaiti saṃsadi tavā pāṅgāvalokanaraḥ 

kitaccītramahosvayaṃ prabhavate śṛṣṭipathatidhvasame |

yaccintena bhāti māmaka iti tvarddarśanaṃ yasya vāte 

sarvahyaṇimādayopyatirāmārādhayante dhruvam (8) || 19-309 ||

khinnānāṃ varadāyinī jalanidha yo tasthito nāvikāṃ 

tatrāsthā ghanakuṇḍagahvaragiri vyāghrādi bhīteṣvapi |

tvāṃ pītāṃvaradhāriṇīṃ paraśivāṃ candrāvaṇo

dyaḍgadā hastāṃ vāmakare pratiparasanāmunmūlayanti bhaje (9) || 19-310 ||

svechaṃ pe praṇamantti pādayugalaṃ pītāṃvare tāvakaṃ 

te vāchādhikamāpyasakalāṃ siddhiṃ bhajate punaḥ |

yat tat kartṛmucī karoti vagale tvat sāṃdhakotrā dhunā

tat saṃjñātamivekṣate tava kapāpāṅgāvalokekṣaṇam (10) || 19-311 ||

vāṇīṃ mūktisudhārasadravamayī sā laṃkṛtā tanmukhe 

śāpānuga kāriṇī kavijanā naṃdaikasaṃvandhini |

vyākaṃrtukṣamate viśālamati yāṃstvat sevako vāḍyayaṃ 

kiṃcit taṃ yadi śṛṣṭimāsuracate brahmāṇḍakoṭyālaye (11) || 19-312 ||

335a) devi tvadbhaktidṛṣtarātu hinagirimukhaḥ parvatāḥ pāṃśutulyā 

kālā mālāśca candrāmṛtakarasadṛśāḥ puṣpatāyāṃ nnināgā |

mūkatvaṃ vāk patindrāḥ sarasisamatulāmāśrayante 

samudrā rājā noraṃkabhāvaṃ raṇa tu viripavo vidravate visastā (16) || 19-313 ||

dṛṣṭvāgpraṃtava mantravījamamalaṃ duṣṭaughasaṃstabhanavaśyākarṣaṇā 
māraṇa prathamana prakṣobhanocāṭane |

vyaktaṃ vajamivā padaṃ hṛdimukhe jāgarttimasyāgrateḥ 

pādāstāḥ parisaṃcarantiripavo ya saptadvīpeśvarā (13) || 19-314 ||

nānāratnavibhūṣitāmalamaṇi dvīpe sudhā sāgare 

kalpānau kṛ hṛkānanāntaragatā yā ratnadevī para |

tantrakāritapaṃcapretakamale siṃhāsane saṃsthitāṃ 

dhyāyehaṃkaruṇākarāṃ hariharāṃ rādhyāmaśaṣārthadām (14) || 19-315 ||

vāgdevi vadane va satyaviratantre ca lakṣmikared 

ānandā nakṛpācutā ca hṛdi ca vīratvamājau sadā |

tvad bhaktasya bhavādhvipārataraṇe tattvodayo jāyate 

ye nedaṃ nalini dalo parijalākāraṃ jagaddhāsane (15) || 19-316 ||

caṃvat kāṃcanatulya pītavasanāṃ candrāvataṃsojvalāṃ 

keyūrādgadahārakuṇḍaladharāṃ bhaktodayo yoktatām |

tvāṃ dhyāyāmi catubhujāṃ trinayanāmugrāsya jihvāṃ 

kurākarṣatī mahimaṃ va pā hi ca gale trāṇaṃ tvame vā sime (16) || 19-317 ||

335b) mātaste mahimānamugramadhikaṃ proktaṃ svayaṃ 

mānavairvākyai saktite śrameṇa yadi vā śakyāguṇāṃ bhodhe |

noti śeṣatayā mururaividiśā tasya padmālaye tasyāt 

sarvagatā tvameva sadasada sadrūpā sadā gīyate (17) || 19-318 ||

khajantārkṣa samodyasaṃ prakurute tākṣaṃ ca khaṃjādhikaṃ 

vātastaṃbhaya te jalāgni śayane yā vyaktiśaktiḥ śiveḥ |

tadvījaṃ vagaleti mastulasanā lagnaṃ sadaivāmalaṃ 

yaṃ brahmādikadūrllabhaṃ tu vitalaiḥ saprāktanaurlabhyate (18) || 19-319 ||

staṃbhatvaṃ pavanopi yātirtaktasya pītāṃvare 

kicit taṃ yadi vāridhiḥ ṅālapadaṃ merūstu māṣāmayaḥ |

kalpanau kahakāmadhanupramukhai ratnairaliṃda sthitair

vādyārghādhikadānamāśu karute dīne svadīne svapi (19) || 19-320 ||

bhāgyādyasya mukhe vibhāti vimalā vidyādiśoṣādikāa 

ṣaṭ triṃśadbhirathoditā vahuguṇai vījaistu sarvārthadā |

te sarve praṇamannimāna namisendrāḥ surā bhūsurā 

phrāntā śeṣamahodayaṃ svakalanā krāntaṃ trilokālayet (20) || 19-321 ||

yatkiṃcid bhuvane vibhāti vimalaṃ ratnamahānandanaṃ 

dadyā yā vṛtti rudāragā janayate yadyatparaṃ sundaram |

yatkiṃcit vathavā tu bhūtamahaśabde napat kiryate 

tat sarvaṃ tava rūpameva vagale saṃsāratāra prade (21) || 19-322 ||

jāgrat pūrṇakṛpā mṛtāṃ ya bharite śrīmatkaṭākṣe kṣaṇe 

sarvārtha pratipādaka vratadhiyo yaṃ ye nignānarā |

336a) teṣāṃ bhāgyamatindriyaṃ nigadituṃ brahmādayo 

nakṣamāye saṃkalpavikalpamātraracanistrāṇātya ye hetu vaḥ (22) || 19-323 ||

haste saṃgṛhya cāpaṃ śarabharanikale yat kiṃ rātaṃ mahājau 

pāthau brahmāstravidyā bhyasatapaṭmatirdvadvayuddhe tu toṣa |

tat sarvaṃ devavṛndairartharipulivahairvījitaṃ siddhalokairgheyaṃ śauryaṃ ca 
sarvaṃ tava rajani taṃ bhāti pītāṃvare stram (23) || 19-324 ||

karṇālaṃvitalolakuṇḍalayugalāścetendu maulau kare 
keyurāṅgadapāśamudgaragadā vajodikāṃ dhubhatim |

deviṃ pītavibhūṣaṇā marikuladhvasādhyato ye narā dhyāyaṃtyāśubhavanti 
siddhimatulā kevāliśāścotkaṭāt (24) || 19-325 ||

laddhārāntaraśeṣakānti bhavitā naraṃ kṛpā vīkṣaṇaṃ varṣiyānapi mohītu 
prabhavate strīvṛndamunmūlane |

kintaccimanekadhā dhanapate daṣṭrā trilokimimāṃ sūryaṃ dustanadhāriṇīṃ 
kṣaṇayā kaṃdarpadarpādhikaḥ (25) || 19-326 ||

yatraṃ jaitramanekaduḥkhaśamaṇaṃ tītāṃvare tāvakamokāradvyasaṃṣudastha 
sakalābhīṣṭaṃ tridhā veṣṭitam |

tadvāhyeṣṭiramāya yāṣṭapuṭitaṃ pāśāṃkuśābhyā vṛtaṃ yeṣāṃ te 
tasibhāgyato nivasate te viśvasargakṣamā (26) || 19-327 ||

karpūrāgurucandanai mṛmadairgorocanā kesaraistat pādāṃ kujamarcayanti vagale 
ye pratyahaṃ mānavā |

te labdhā śriyamdbhutāmapi ciraṃ bhogāśca tutkāvanau sā yujyā lamā viśanti 
paramā nekṣestiyatrādhikaḥ (27) || 19-328 ||

336b) labdhvā pāṭayuge ratiṃ tava śive kṣudropi devendratā māsādyā 
marasundarīramalairbhogaidivi krīḍati |

ye hitvā tava bhaktimatyavacanā nandāściratne narā bhraṣṭā 
dharmaparāṅmukhātramadhiyau bhāraṃ vahante tu vi (28) || 19-329 ||

yāmārādhyaharo haratva bhajadviṣṇustu viśvātmatā cakre mṛṣṭimajepya vo ca 
dakhilaṃ vedāddhimadvāṅmayam |

dhyātvāpī tamaśeṣamāśu harate sūryopi pītāṃvare śāpāntaṃ 
kamapācakārarajani nāthopi cūḍāśritaḥ (29) || 19-330 ||

viddhiṃ nāśaṣakīraya svarasanāmaṃghrergatiṃ staṃbhaya duṣṭāndrāvaya 
mārayasya piśūnān sādhuṃściraṃ pālayaḥ |

itthaṃ ye vagalāmukhi padagatiṃ ladhvā paṭhiṣyanti ye 
yatrārūḍhamavārivṛndamakhilaṃ kartusamarthāḥ sadā (30) || 19-331 ||

tvāṃ dhyātvāṃ vagale purāṣiri sutācakre ca śiveśvaraḥ proktāmarpayate 
valiṃ nipatitāṃ saṃkalpanāgnau tadā |

tpattkāgniṃ galitā valā girisutāt yatkāgalasthā sukhāt tasmāt tvaṃ 
vagalāmukhiti gaditā nityā parā yogini (31) || 19-332 ||

nāgendrai deva siddhai ṛṣivaranivahai dānavai rākṣasendrai dikpālaidikkarindrai 
dinakarayumukhai saṅgaistālakādyaiḥ |

brahmādyaiḥ sthūlasūkṣmairamuditā tvaṃ parā conmanī tvaṃ nityaṃ 
pītāmvarā tvaripubhayaśamanī bhaktacittāsanasthā (32) || 19-333 ||

337a) śaṃbhūryaṭguṇagānanoghanamatirnādyotsave tāṇḍave cakre 
candramayūkhakaṃpana chalānnīṃ rājanā pādayo |

hemāṃbhojadalaijahā jalabharairānanditairmonibhiḥ pūjāṃ pratyadamānanoti 
namanāka sāhasratālādibhiḥ (33) || 19-334 ||

yāṃ dadha caturānanopi vadane cittā ravinda sthitāṃ yāṃ vakṣasulasasthitāṃ 
harirajāmāliṃgya pītāṃvarām |

yardehārdhamurī cakārapurajit saudaryāmārādhikāṃ ṣaṭcakrākṣararūpiṇi 
bhajamukhe devī jagatpālikām (34) || 19-335 ||

haste bhāti gadā sadārti samanī ratnāvalītvaṅguje yā de 
nūpuramosamaulimaṇibhirnīrājitaṃ rāte |

nāṭhaṃkaṃ śravaṇekta copari sadākastūrikā lepanaṃ 
vāmmi?radramaṃgalāgamadhikaṃ pītachacintanvate (35) || 19-336 ||

śrokāradvayasaṃpuṭe na puṭitāṃ vidyāgamasthe sthitāṃ 
ṣaṭcakrākṣaravījasāraraciti ṣaṭtriṃśadarṇātmikām |

ye jananti yajanti santatamabhidhyāyati gāyatti vāte vandyā vivuddhaiścarati 
bhuvane siddhā vitāṃ siddhaye (36) || 19-337 ||

svāhārūpa śivāsane tava ṛṣiḥ śrīnārado devatā nibhyā śrīvagalāmukhi 
nigaditā chandobhave triṣṭubham |

viratusthilamāyayā viracitaṃ nānāvidhastaṃbhane proktaṃ padmatuvāvibhrāti 
viniyogopyucaraṃ kārak (37) || 19-338 ||

hṛdyaṃ survasureśvaraiśca ṛṣibhirduṣyāyame vādbhutaṃ stotraṃ gopyamataḥ 
svabhāgyavaśataḥ prāptopathivyanti ye |

337b) sūktyā deva guruṃ dhanena dhanadaṃ jitvā ciraṃ jivitā ṣaṇmāsān 
śivasaṃgaraiḥ śivasamāḥ krīḍākariṣyati te (38) || 19-339 ||

devī svapnagatā svanāmalikhitaṃ mahyaṃ dadā vadbhutaṃ divyāṃstraṃ 
purataḥ patat sumamalaṃ sindūravarṇaikaraiḥ |

romājjāṃkitaharṣavālyalulite raṃgaiḥ pathatputtaro prāptohaṃ paramo dayaṃ 
paramidaṃ jñānaṃ kavindrādidam (39) || 19-340 ||

prāptā śrīvagalāmukhi vadanataḥ statye svavidyā mayā ṣaṭtriṃśad 
bharimaikavitvaprathimaiḥ sadvījaratnāvalī |

yeṣāṃ kaṇṭhe gatā vibhāti jagatī pāthepayogakṣamā 
vaśyākarṣaṇamohamāraṇavidhau staṃbhe tathodvāṭale (40) || 19-341 ||


iti śrīpitāmvara tnāvalistotraṃ saṃpūrṇa samāptam 


calatkanakakuṇḍalol lāsitarcarugaṇḍasthaliṃ 

lasatkanakacampakadyuti virāji candrānanām |

gadā hatavipakṣakāṃ kalitalāṃlajihvāṃ calā 
smarāmi vagalāmukhiṃ vimukhavāṅmaya staṃbhinim || 19-342 ||

śrīpitāṃvarāya namaḥ |

oṃ asya śrīvagalāmukhi stotramantrasya śrībhagavān nārāya ṛṣiḥ 
śrīvagalāmukhi devatā vṛhatī chandaḥ samasaṃnihitāṃnāṃ duṣṭānāṃ 
dūrasthānāṃ virodhināṃ padamukhajihvāṃ staṃbhanārthe mama vāṇi 
siddhyarthaṃ ca virodhināṃ vināśāya śrīvagalāmukhi prītyarthe stotramantra 
jape viniyogaḥ (1) |


338a) o/ pītavastrāṃ sunetrā ca dvibhūjāṃ dā hṛṃkojvalā |

śilāparvatahastāṃ ca smaret tāṃ vagalāmukhim (2) || 19-343 ||

madhye sudhāddhi maṇimaṇḍaparatnavedyāṃ siṃhāsano parigatāṃ 
paripītavarṇām |

pītāṃvarābharaṇamālyavibhūṣitāṃgi devinnāmāmi dhṛtamuṭ ra vairijihvām 
(3) || 19-344 ||

triśūladhārinīmaṃvā sarvasaubhāgya dāyinīm |

sarvaśṛṃgāraveśāṣyāṃ devī dhyātvā prapūjayet (4) || 19-345 ||

calat kanakakuṇḍalo lasitacārugaṇḍasthalāṃ lasat kanakacampakadyutim adinduṃ 
viṃvānanām |

gadā hata vipakṣakāṃ karitalolajihvāṃ calā smarāmi vagalāmukhi 
vimukhasaṃmukhastaṃbhani (5) || 19-346 ||

pīyūṣo dadhimadhyacāruvilasad rastojvale maṇḍale tat siṃhāsanamolipāṭhitaripu 
pretāsanādhyāsini |

svarṇābhāṃ karapīḍitārirasanāṃ bhrāmyad gadā vibhramā tayastvāṃ 
yaṣyati tasya pānti vilayaṃ sadyopi sarvopadaḥ (6) || 19-347 ||

devi tva caraṇāṃvuje vitanu te yaḥ pītapuṣpāṃjaliṃ mudrāṃ vāmakare nidhīya 
ca punarmatrī manojñakṣaram |

pitadhyāna paropakuṃbhakavaśād vīja smaret porthivaṃ tasyā mitramukhaṃsya 
vāga ghṛdayago staṃbho bhaveta kṣaṇāt (7) || 19-348 ||

mantrasthāvadalaṃ vipakṣadalane stotraṃ pavitraṃ ca te yantraṃ vādini yatrināṃ 
trijagatāṃ yantraṃ pavitraṃ ca te |

mātaḥ śrīvagaleti nāmalalitaṃ yaḥ sthūyate te mukhe tannāmagrahaṇena 
sampadi mukhaṃ staṃbho bhaved vādinam (8) || 19-349 ||

338b) vādi mūkati raṃkati kṣitipatirveśvātara śrīnatikrodhiśāntitī durjanaḥ 
sujanatiṃ kṣipramukhaḥ khaṃjatiḥ |

garvirkharvati sarvajaḍati tvadyanniṇā yantrita śrinitye vagalāmukhi pratidinaṃ 
kalpāni tubhyaṃ namaḥ (9) || 19-350 ||

duṣṭastaṃbhanamugravighnaśamanaṃ dāridya vidhvaṃsanaṃ 
bhūrmṛtsaṅgamanañca yan mṛgadṛśī cet samākarṣaṇam |

saubhāgyaikaniketanaṃ mama dṛśoḥ kāruṇyapūrṇekṣaṇaṃ mṛtyo 
māraṇamāvirastu purato mātastvadiyaṃ vapuḥ (10) || 19-351 ||

tvaṃ vidyā paramā trilokajanani vighnaughasaṃchedini yoṣākarṇakariṇi ca 
saṃmuhadvaṃdvaika saṃchedani |

duṣṭācāṭanakāriṇī janamanaḥ saṃmohasaṃdhāyani jihvā kīlana 
vairbharvavījayata brahmāstravidyā parā (11) || 19-352 ||

māta staṃbhaya madvipakṣavadanaṃ jihvāṃ calaṃ kīlaya brāhmī 
mudrayamudrayā sudhiṣaṇāṃ vahnergati staṃbhaya |

śatrūścūrṇaya cūrṇayāśu madayā gaurāṅgiṃ pītāṃvare vaghnaudhaṃ 
vale harapratidinaṃ kāruṇyapūrṇekṣaṇe (12) || 19-353 ||

mātabhairavi bhadrakāli vijaye vārāhi viśvāśraye śrīnitye samaye maheśi 
vagale kāme śivā me rame |

mātaṃgi tripure parātparatare svargāpavargaprade dosohaṃ ra śaṇāgataṃ 
karuṇayā viśveśvarī trāhi mām (13) || 19-354 ||

sāktameḍhye caurasaṃghe praharaṇasamaye vadhane vārimadhye vahnau vāde 
vivāde prakupitanṛpataudikāle niśāyām |

339a) vācyatve staṃbhane vā ripuvadhasamaye nirviṣatve raṇe vā gachastiṣṭaṃ 
strikālaṃ stavapaṭhanimidaṃ kāryasiddhi pradaṃ ca (14) || 19-355 ||

mātaryastu naraḥ sadā stavamidaṃ devyāḥ paṭhet sādaraṃ dhṛtvā yantramidaṃ 
tathai samaye vāhau kare vā gale |

rājāno varayoṣito vikariṇaḥ sarpāmṛgendrāḥ khalā staṃbhaṃ yānti niyoḥ 
vitaṃ ripu gatā lakṣmīryathā siddhyati (15) || 19-356 ||

anudinamabhirāmaṃ sādhako yaṃ trikālaṃ paṭhati trituvaneśaḥ pūte 
devavargaiḥ |

bhavati sakala kṛtyaṃ tasya dṛṣṭo valoke bhavati paramasiddhau lokamānya 
yāstathaiva (16) || 19-357 ||

iti śrīnāradaviracitaṃ pītāṃvarā stotraṃ saṃpūrṇam |

ityāgamakalpalatāyāṃ vagalāmukhivarṇānānāṃma paṃcaviṃśaḥ patalaḥ 
(25) |



taduktaṃ kulacūḍāmaṇau 

kulācārakulāṣṭamyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |

yogini pūjanaṃ tatra pradhānaṃ kulapūjanam || 20-1 ||

yathā viṣṇu tithau viṣṇuḥ pūjito vikrana pradaḥ |

tathā kulatithau durgo pūjitā varadāyini || 20-2 ||

kulavārādi yamo yathā |

tadukta rudrayāmale 

raviścandro guruḥ sauriścatvāraścālādume |

bhaumaśukrau kulākhyau tu vudhavārāḥ kulākulaḥ || 20-3 ||

tṛtīyā daśamī ṣaṣṭī kulākulamudā hṛtam |

339b) pratipatyaścamī navamyaikādaśī paurṇamāsya kulatithayaḥ || 20-4 ||

dvitīyā caturthyaṣṭamī dvādaśī cardaśī kulatithayaḥ |

viṣamāścākulāḥ sarvā śeṣaśca tithayaḥ kulā || 20-5 ||

varuṇādrābhijinmūlaṃ kulākumudāhṛtam |

kulāni samayugmāni śeṣabhānyakulāni ca || 20-6 ||

tithivāre ca nakṣatre akulasthāpino jayaḥ |

kulākhye jayino nityaṃ sāmyaṃ caiva kulākuleśravam || 20-7 ||

kulayārādikaṃ jñatvā mā sādhakakarma cācaret |

karmāraṃbhasya pūrvedyuḥ śivābhyo valimaharet || 20-8 ||


śivā vali prakāramāha |

taduktaṃ kulacūḍāmaṇau |

vilvamūle śantare vā ityādi śivāyai namo namaḥ |

ityanna śivā vali vidhāne nīlasarasvatī prakaraṇe likhimasti athābhidhāne 
tatresmin samyak ṣaṭkarmalakṣaṇam |


sarvatantrānusāreṇa prayogaphalasiddhidam |

śānti vasya staṃbhanādi vidvecaṭanaṃ tataḥ || 20-9 ||

māraṇāmnāniśaṃ santi ṣaṭkarmāṇi maniṣiṇaḥ |

rogakṛtyā grahādīnāṃ nirāśāśātiriritā || 20-10 ||

vaśyaṃ janānāṃ sarveṣāṃ vidheye tvamudiritam |

pravṛtti vodhaḥ sarve staṃbhanaṃ samudāhṛtam || 20-11 ||

stigdhānāmudvījajananaṃ mithoviddheṣaṇaṃ matam |

uccā naṃ svadeśā debhraṃśanaṃ parikīrttita || 20-12 ||

prāṇināṃ prāṇiharaṇaṃ samudāhṛtam |

stigdhānāmudvījajananaṃ mithovidveṣaṇaṃ matam || 20-13 ||

340a) svadevatā dikrālādīn jñatvā karmaṇi sādhayet |

gatirvāṇi ramājyaṃṣṭā durgā kāli tathā kramāt || 20-14 ||

ṣaṭkarmadavatā proktaḥ karmadaunāḥ prapūjayet |

īśacandrendranirṛti vāyvagnīnāṃ diśo matā || 20-15 ||

śśānādidiśiṃ śāntiṃ kuryāditi krameṇa vā |

sūryodayaṃ samārabhya dyaṭikādeśakaṃ kramāt || 20-16 ||

ṛtavaḥ syurvasantādyā ahorātradine dine |

ghaṭikā daśakaikasmin karmāṇi daśatrā bhavati || 20-17 ||

ekasmine vāhorātre ṣaṭ ṛtavaḥ daśa daśaśuvibhāgena |

vasantagrīvarṣākhyaṃ śararddhematraśiśirāḥ || 20-18 ||

hemanta śātrike prokto vasannāvaśyakarmaṇi |

śiśirastaṃbhane jñeyo dvidvepetriṣma iritaḥ || 20-19 ||

prāvṛhaccāṭane jñeyā śaranmāraṇakarmaṇi |


meṣavyāghragajāṣṭrarukṣatu ragatvagbhistu ṣaṭkarmapratyekaṃ vividhāsaṇāṇi 
kuruto mantri sadā siddhaye |


padmākṣaṃ svastikaṃ bhūyo vikaṭaṃ kukkuṭaṃ punaḥ || 20-20 ||

vajraṃ bhadrakamityāddha rāmanānimaṇiṣina |

śānti puṣṭi vaśā dveṣe uccāṭane ca māraṇe || 20-21 ||

svāhā svadhā vaṣaṭ huṃ ca vauṣaṭ phatkārakaṃ kramāt |

paṇyudrāḥ kramato jñeyā padmapāśagaḍāchaya || 20-22 ||

musalāśani khaḍgākhya sāntikādiṣu karmasu |


ṣaṭkarmasukhameṇḍalāni kārjyāni janaṃ śāntividhau śastavaśya 
vahnirudāhṛta |


staṃbhane pṛthivīśastā vidveṣe vyomakīrtitam || 20-23 ||

uccāṭane mṛto vāyurbhūyognirmāraṇe mataḥ |

340b) tat tad bhūtodaye sasyāt tat tan maṇḍalasaṃyutam || 20-24 ||

tatakarmavidhātavyaṃ matriṇāniścitānmanā |

śītaṃ susulilakṣoṇī vyomavāyuyakāraḥ || 20-25 ||

davirtukureka |

grathanaṃ ca virbhaśca saṃyuto rodhanaṃ tathā |

yogaḥllava itye te vinyāsaḥ ṣaṭsukarmasu || 20-26 ||

matrāṇāṃ taritā kuryān nāmavaṇīnyathā vidhi |

grathanaṃtadvijāniyāt praśastaśānnikarmarṇā || 20-27 ||

matrārṇa dvaṃdva madhyastaṃ sādhyanāmākṣaraṃ likhet |

vidarbha eṣa vijñayo mantribhirvaśya karmaṇi (2) || 20-28 ||

ādācante ca matrasyān nāmnosau saṃpuṭaḥ smṛtaḥ |

eṣa saṃstambhane śastadutyukto matravedibhiḥ || 20-29 ||

nāmnā ādyantamadhyeṣu mantrasyādrodhanaṃ manam |

vidveṣeṇa vidhāneṣu praśastamidamiritam || 20-30 ||

mantrasyāte savyatāmayogaḥ procāṭane manaḥ |

annenāmno bhavenmatraḥ pallavā māraṇe mataḥ || 20-31 ||

sitaraktapītami akṛṣṇadhūmrā prakīrttitā |

varṇamantra saṃproktā devatāṣaṭsu karmasu || 20-32 ||

mantrāṇāṃ lekhanadravyaṃ candranaṃ locanā niśā |

gṛhadhūmatacitaṅgāro māraṇeṣu viṣāṇi ca || 20-33 ||

śvetāgni lonapittāṇi dattukarasaṃ vṛta |

gṛhadhūmastrikaṭukaṃ viṣāṣṭakamudrā kṛtam || 20-34 ||


niśā haridrāḥ gṛhadhūmahā yā prasiddhāśye na prasiddhāḥ agniścitrakaṃ 
loṇapittātilavaṇamalaṃ trikaṭukaṃ śuṃṭhipippalimaricati |


devatā kālamudrādīn sasyagjñatvā vicakṣaṇa |

ṣaṭkarmaṇi prayujīta yathokta phalasiddhaye || 20-35 ||

341a) pauṣṭikādīnikarmāṇi yadvāratithiyogataḥ |

prayoktavyāni vidhinā ca saṃprotedhunā || 20-36 ||

paṃcamī ca dvitiyā ca tṛtīyā saptamī tathā |

vudhavāra samāyuktā śāntikarmarṇiye jitā || 20-37 ||

navamī pauṣṭike śastā aṣṭami daśami tathā |

daśamyekādaśī caiva bhānusaṃkrānti saṃyutā || 20-38 ||

ākarṣaṇemya māvāsyā navamī pratipantathā |

paurṇemāsī tathā bhānu yuktā vidveṣakarmaṇi || 20-39 ||

kṛṣnā caturdaśī tadvad aṣṭamī maṃdanārakā |

uccāṭene tithiḥ śastā pradoṣe ca viśeṣataḥ || 20-40 ||

caturdaśyāṣṭamī kṛṣṇā amāvāsyā viśeṣataḥ |

maṃdabhaumadino pītāḥ śastā māraṇakarmaṇi || 20-41 ||

vudhacaṃdradino petā paṃcamī daśamī tathā |

paurṇamāsī ca vijñeyā tithistaṃbhanakarmaṇi || 20-42 ||

śubhagraho hodaye kuryā dhumāni ca śubhādaye |

raudrakarmāṇiriktārko mṛtyuyoge ca māraṇam || 20-43 ||

atha śāntiṃ pravakṣyāmi nānātantrānusāriṇī |


tadukta śāradātilake |

tvaritā mantra prayogamāha |

mantro yathā |

oṃ hrī/ hū/ kheca dekṣa hrī/ phaṭ |

ayutaṃ hāmasaṃkhyā japasnā vān prakīrttita |

yāmi kuṇṭhaṃ prakalpātra homaṃ kuṇṭhaṃ prakalpa u homaṃ kuryāt |

nijeṣkṛyā |


kṛtvāṃ ḍāhādiśamaṃ na palāśakusumairhutam || 20-44 ||

tajjaptaṃ cuṃlakaṃ toyaṃ mukhakṣiptiṃ viṣāpaham |

ārtāyaiṃ neṭhājaṃ dadyān mantriṇānaṃ namuntritam || 20-45 ||

341b) sanavedvyādhini mukto mantrasyāsya prabhāvataḥ | 
 

atha durgāmaṃḍa prayogaḥ |

oṃ hrī/ duṃ durgāyai namaḥ |


vasulakṣaṃ japan mantra tataḥ prayāgamārabhet || 20-46 ||

kṛtvā na ca yadānmānaṃ maṇḍalaṃ yantrabhūṣitam |

abhiṣekaṃ prakurvīta vidhinā sarvasiddhaye || 20-47 ||

kaluṣāsthāpayateṣu padeṣu śubhalakṣaṇāt |

candanā lipta sarvāgaindurvākṣatasamanvitān || 20-48 ||

ugūlaveṣṭinā netāt pūrayantirthavāribhi |

navaratnasamāvaddhaṃ karṣakāṃcanakalpitam || 20-49 ||

madhyakuṃbhe kṣipet padmaṃ mantrādyaṃ daiśikottamaḥ |

candanāśīrakarpūra jātikakkolakuṃkuṃmam || 20-50 ||

kuṣyagurutamālailāyutaṃ saṃpiśpabhāgate |

vilodya sarvakumbheṣu ratnānyapi vinikṣipet || 20-51 ||

lakṣī dūrvā sadābhadrā mahādevī madhuvratā |

mumulī śakuvallā ca krāntāpāmārgapadmaka || 20-52 ||

priyaṃgamudbhagodhūma vriṃhiśca matilānyavān |

śālitaṃ dulamārṣāśca prakṣālyai teṣu nikṣipet || 20-53 ||

dhātrikulavitvānāṃ kadalinārikelayo |

phalānyapi vinikṣipya puṣpāṇye tāni nikṣipet || 20-54 ||

padmaṃ saugandhikaṃ jāniṃ mallikāṃ ca kulaṃ tathā |

caṇḍapakāśokaputrāṃ tulaśīketako bhavam || 20-55 ||

pallavāṇi vaṭāśvattha plakṣo duṃvaraśākhinām |

brahmakurcaṃ ca nikṣipya caṣakaiḥ saphalākṣataiḥ || 20-56 ||

pipāya kuṃbhavaktrāṇi kṣaumarā chādayan tataḥ |

342a) ādyāṭṭāmadhvakalaśe devī saṃpūjya yatnataḥ || 20-57 ||

itareṣu ca saṃpūjya prayatnena japādhikā |

jayā ca vijayā kīrtti prītipaṃcāt prabhā punaḥ || 20-58 ||

śraddhā saurā śrutiḥ proktā svanāmādyakṣarādikā |


vijayā yai nama ityādi | 


saṃpūjya gandhapuṣpādyairabhiṣiṃcennarādhipam || 20-59 ||

rājā vijayate śatrūn yo dhakovijaye chriyam |

prāpnoti rogadīrghāyu sarvavyādhivivarjitaḥ || 20-60 ||

saṃdhyābhiṣiktavidhinā labhate tana yaṃ varam |

mantreṇānyena saṃjaptamājyaṃ kṣudragrahāpaham || 20-61 ||

garbhiṇīnāṃ viśeṣeṇa gabhe rakṣākaraṃ tathā |


iti mahiṣamardinya prayoga |

mantrā yathā |

mahiṣamardini svāhā |


aṣṭalakṣaṃ munuṃ japtvā prayogaṃ tu samācaret || 20-62 ||

siddhārthe juhuyān mantrī rāgān mucyata tatkṣaṇāt |

padmai hutvā jayachatrun dūrvābhiḥ śāntimāpnuyāt || 20-63 ||


atha śūlinyā matraṃ prayogaḥ |

mantro yathā |


jvala jvala śūlini dṛṣṭagrahaṃ huṃ phaṭ svāhā |

paṃcarakṣamanuṃ japtvā prayogaṃ kārayet sudhi || 20-64 ||

tadyadā yudhahastā tāṃ devī kālaghanapradām |

dhyātvātmānaṃ japen mantraṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ mucati graha || 20-65 ||

sarveṣu vṛścikādīnāṃ viṣamāśuvināśayet |


atha navadurgā mantra prayoga |

mantro yathā |


hū/ uttiṣṭapuruṣī kintyapi vibhayaṃ me mamupasthitam |

yadi śakyamasakyaṃ vā bhavati sama svāhā || 20-66 ||


342b) saptatriṃśadakṣarāṇi caturlakṣaṃ manujaptvā prayogānācaret sudhīḥ |


ayutaṃ vaṭavṛkṣotthaiḥ saśūgaiḥ rarcite nale |

homaṃ samidvarai kuryān nāśayedāpadākulam || 20-67 ||

dūrābhicārān dūtādin samaye vidhināmunā |

apāmārga sasidbhirvā tilairvā kānanodbhavaiḥ || 20-68 ||

sarṣapairvā kṛto maḥ kṣudrā yasmāranāśanaḥ |

dvāraro grahe sarpa tajatyā saṃspṛśan japet || 20-69 ||

spṛṣṭvā śūlakarī devī tatkṣaṇā devatāṃ haret |

kārayedbrahmavṛkṣeṇa śilpinā yudhayaṃ ca kam || 20-70 ||

śaṃkhacakrarathāṅgāni śāṅgakaumodakīṃ gadām |

paṃcagavyeṣu nikṣiṣya tāni spṛṣṭvā japen manum || 20-71 ||

saṃmyak paṃcasahasrāṇi teṣu saṃpādyat punaḥ |

tā vakṣyena juhuyā mantraiḥ svai pūjayat kramāt || 20-72 ||


śaṃkhamantro yathā |

jaladharāya svāhā |

khaḍgamantro yathā |

gati kṣṛ chiṃda chiṃda khaṇḍa khaṇḍa |

cakramantro yathā |

sudarśana mahācakrarājāya svāhā |

sarvaduṣṭabhayaṃkara chinda vidāraya vidāraya paramatrāṇa grasaka kṣaya 
kakṣaya trāśaya 2 hū/ hū/ phaṭ cakrāya namaḥ |

śāṅgamatro yathā |

śāṅgāya saśarāya svāhā |

kaumodakī mahāvale sarvā surāntakī prasīda prasīda phaṭ svāhā |


uddhṛtya paṃcagavye tya pūrvavat pūjayedimāt |

avataṃ paṃcanigvane dikṣamadhyādi yuktamāt || 20-73 ||


343a) atha tān gartān sva vaṭe śre pūrṇapuṣaṃ paṃcagavyena sādhakaḥ |


āyudhāni prajaptāni paṃcaghoṣapuraḥ saram |

vinyese teṣu madhyādi pūjāṃ kuryadyathā purā || 20-74 ||

vālukābhiḥ samāpūryaṃ yathā purā |

vālukābhiḥ samāpūryaṃ mṛdbhikuryāt samaṃ sulam || 20-75 ||

valiṃ ca vikiret tatra teṣā mantrairyathā kramam |

dikpattibhyo valiṃ datvā brāhmaṇā bhojayat tataḥ || 20-76 ||

dīnāṃdhakṛpanādīśca toṣayed bhojanādibhiḥ |

dakṣiṇāṃ gurave dadyād ātmavittānusārataḥ || 20-77 ||

yantre yaṃ vihitā vidyā deśe vā nagare pure |

grāme gṛhethavā tatra varddhante sarvasampadaḥ || 20-78 ||

agnyutpātādayo doṣā bhutapretādi saṃyutā |

abhicāra kṛtā kṛtyā ripurāgādyupadravā || 20-79 ||

nakṣatre todiśaṃ bhītyā tarjitā devatā jñeyā |


atha mātaṃgi prayogaḥ |

mantro yathā |

ai/ hrī/ śrī/ o/ namo bhagavati mātaṃgeśvari sarvajanamanohari sarvamukhaṃ 
raṃjani sarvarājavaśaṃkari sarvastrīpuruṣaṃ vaśakari 
sarvaduṣṭamṛgavaśaṃkari sarvalokamamukaṃ vaśamānayet svāhā |

aṣṭārśītyakṣaro mantra |


pratijaptvā yutaṃ mantrṃ tata prayogamācaret || 20-80 ||

vaṃjurasya samiddho mo dṛṣṭiṃ vitanute cirāt |


kṣīrāktai smṛtā khaṇḍairhomo nāśayati jvalaṃ amṛtā trūci |


nirguṇḍī mūlahomena nigatān mucyate nara || 20-81 ||


nirguṇḍī saṃbhālo iti prasiddhaḥ |

athāgni mantra prayoyogaḥ |


343b) puruṣa haripiṃgalo lohi me dadāpaya svāhā |

prātasnāna gato matri sahasra yojayen manum || 20-82 ||

jitvā rogā sukhaṃ jīvaṃ chiyāvarṣaśataṃ nara |

hṛtpramāṇajale sthitvā bhānumālokya saṃyutam || 20-83 ||

catusahasraṃ japennityaṃ naraḥ santat sarāvadhi |

apamṛtyubhayaṃ roga kṛtyā dāridya saṃbhavā || 20-84 ||

kleśān nihatya tejasvī jīved varṣaśataṃ sukham |

taile jyotiṣmati bhūtairiyoragre yajen nṛpa || 20-85 ||


jyotiṣmatī mālakaṃgunī iti |


aśvatthasamidho meṣi ghṛtāktā kuhuyānnara |

kanyāmaṣṭābhavāpnoti sāpi tat prāpnuyāt patim || 20-86 ||

śuddhā yena kṛto homo jvaranāśakaraḥ paraḥ |


atha śrīnṛsiṃhamantra prayo |

ugravīramityādi mantra |

saliladevamabhyarya gaṃdhapuṣpādibhi śubhaiḥ dūvābhistatra juhuyān 
nityamaṣṭottaraśatam |


uapasargā vinaśyanti kṣudrabhūtarādibhiḥ || 20-87 ||


mahenti śeṣa |


duḥsvapneniśi saṃjāte snātvā mantra mamujapet |

anidro mantravityamaṣṭośvāt susvapnasya jāyata || 20-88 ||

vyāghracauramṛgādibhyo mahārasa ye bhayākule |

lakṣen marayajaptvo bhayaṣvanyeṣu mantriṇam || 20-89 ||

ane mantritaṃ bhasma viṣagrahamahābhayām |

nāśayed acirād eva mantrasyāsya prabhāvata || 20-90 ||

ghorābhicāretmā deva mahotpāte mahābhaye |

japen matraṃ smaren devaṃ duḥkhān muktā bhavennaraḥ || 20-91 ||

kṛtvā navayadānmānaṃ maṇḍalaṃ mantrasaṃyutam |

344a) asmin saṃnyāpayen mantri kalaśānnavaśobhanām || 20-92 ||

kaṣāyatoyaṃ saṃpūrṇā tvastu ratnādi saṃyutān |

madhyasaṃpūjaye devaṃ nṛsiṃhaṃ śātavigraham || 20-93 ||

tārkṣadin pūjayen mantrī pūrvādiṣu yathākramam |

iti saṃśādhitaistauyairabhiṣiṃcet priyattaram || 20-94 ||

abhicāragrahonmādā vinaśyastyaribhismaha |

abhiṣiktastadā viśrān pūjaye devatā dhiyā || 20-95 ||

yathāvat pūjayet paścāt bhaktyā gurumaraṃ jayet |

rājā vijayamāpnoti yuddheṣu vidhināmunā || 20-96 ||

śālmalirvadarī jamvurāmraśca vakulastathā |

kaṣāyāḥ paṃcasadiṣṭā daiśikaistatra vedibhiḥ || 20-97 ||


āṃ hrī/ kṣauṃ krau/ phaṭ |


ṛtulakṣaṃ manuṃ japtvā prayogānācaret sudhiḥ |

dūrvāpayo ghṛtā tyaktā hunedaśottaraṃ sudhi || 20-98 ||

anvahaṃ juyāt samyadīrghamāyuravāpnuyāt |

tasya rohaḥ praṇaśyanti kṛtyād rohādibhiḥ saha || 20-99 ||

sapaṃcagavyaṃ kuhuyādapāmāsya maṃjarim |

nityaṃ sahasramānena saptāhaṃ vijitendriyaḥ || 20-100 ||

homoyaṃ sarvasarvathā bhūta kṛtyā rogāt pranāśayet |

payoktairamṛtākhaṇḍaistrisahasraṃ caturdinam || 20-101 ||

anena vihito homo bhramajvalavināśanaḥ |


atha sudarśanamantraprayogaḥ |


arkalakṣe manuṃ japtvā prayogānācaret sudhīḥ || 20-102 ||

dinatrayaṃ yojuhuti gulitā pūranirmitā |

aṣṭottarasahasreṇa sajayennirivalā padaḥ || 20-103 ||

udumvara samiddho ma putradāyi bhave nṛṇām |

344b) śaracandranibhaṃ sārṇaṃ sudhādhārābhirvarṣiṇam || 20-104 ||

smaren mūrddhaniyasyāsau sajīved vigatāmaya |

mumtāśuṇṭhiniśāvahmī lailāyaṣṭirvacāvṛṣā || 20-105 ||

pāṭhāvitra samaṃjiṣṭā drākṣādirvicarohiṇi |

phalatraya ca taikaktaiḥ paṃcagavyena sarpiṣām || 20-106 ||

prastaṃ pacaidyathā nyāyaṃ matreṇānena sādhitam |

dvātriṃśatyakaṃ prasthaṃ svayamuktaṃ svayaṃ tu vā || 20-107 ||

kāntidaṃ śubhaṃ daṃstrīṇāṃ bhūtapratā mayāpaham |

gabhārakṣākaraṃ śuddha paṃcagavyaghṛtaṃ vidu || 20-108 ||


atha paṃcagavyāni |


palamātraṃ dugdhabhāgaṃ gomūlaṃ tāvadiṣyate |

ghṛtaṃ ca palapātrasyā gomayatolakadvayam || 20-109 ||

dadhipraṃsṛnamātraṃ syāt paṃcagavyamidaṃ smṛtam |


atha mṛtyuṃjayamantra prayogaḥ |


lakṣatraya manuṃ japtvā prayogāniti pūrvavat padam || 20-110 ||

dugdhakhaṇḍe mantri māṣaṃ sahasrakam |

ārādhitegnau vidhivajuhuyād vijitendriyaḥ || 20-111 ||

santuṣṭaḥ śaṃkarastena sudhāhyāvitavigraha |

āyurārogya saṃpatti paśaḥ putrānvivardhayet || 20-112 ||

svadhāvaṭau tilādūrvāpayaḥ sarpiḥ payo haviḥ |


ityuktaḥ |

saptabhirdravyai juhuyāt saptavāsare |


kramādaśaśataṃ nityaṃ maṣṭottaramataṃdritaḥ || 20-113 ||

saptādhikā dvijānnityaṃ bhojayet sadhurānvitam |

vikārānnaguṇaṃ matri varayed vomavāsarāt || 20-114 ||

hotṛbhyo dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād ahṛṇā gāpayasvinī |

guruṃ prapūjayet paścād vanādyai devatādhiyā || 20-115 ||

345a) anena vidhinā sāvya kṛtvādrāhaṃ jvarādibhiḥ |

vimuktaḥ suciraṃ jīve cchavadāṃ śabhamaṃjasā || 20-116 ||

abhicāreśvare brahma gurūtmādeśirogade |

asādhyaroge kṣeḍārtai mohe dāha mahābhaye || 20-117 ||

homoyaṃ śāntida proktāḥ sarvasaṃpat pradāyakaḥ |

dravyairetai prajuhuyānnijanmasu yathā vidhi || 20-118 ||

bhojayen madhurairbhojyairbrahmaṇā vedapāragāt |

dīrghamāyuravāpnoti vāṃchitāṃ vindati śriyam || 20-119 ||

ekādaśāhutirnityaṃ dūrvābhi * huyāt tataḥ |

apamṛtyu doṣasya āyurārogyavarddhanaḥ || 20-120 ||

trijanmani sudhāvalli kāsmirivaphulodbhavai |

samihṛraiḥ kṛto homa sarvamṛtyugadāpaha || 20-121 ||

siddhānnaivihito homo mahājvalavināśanaḥ |


apāmārga samiddhomaḥ |

sarvāmaya nisudanaḥ |

atha mahiṣasya cintāmaṇi mantramayutaṃ rogaśāntaye |


surāvṛtamidaṃ vījaṃ vilalparamāmṛtam || 20-122 ||

candravimvamidaṃ mūdhni dhyānacheḍagadāpaham |

pratiromasvarāvījaṃ vījaṃ vahni gṛhasthite || 20-123 ||

raphādivyaṃ jatollāsi ṣaṭkoṇena vṛtaṃ vahi |

raphādītilakaṣamaraya ityarthaḥ pratiloma svaretiḥ || 20-124 ||

aḥ a/ au/ o/ ai/ e/ ḷ/ ḹ/ ṝ/ ṛ/ ū/ u/ ī/ i/ ā/ a/ iti yāvat |

bhūtānnamyaṃ smṛtaṃ mūrdhni bhūtamāśuvināśayet || 20-125 ||

vījacandragataṃ vījaṃ smaraḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ kramāt |

galat parasvadhāpūraṃ netre dhyātaṃ rūjāpaham || 20-126 ||

345b) evameva smṛti vījaṃ kukṣau śūlādiroganut |

sphoṭe jvale viṣe dāhe mohe śīrṣagade bhrame || 20-127 ||

vījametaṃ tathā dhyataṃ tanttat kreśāṃnvitāśayet |

vījatāṃvūlapatrasthaṃ prajaptaṃ manunāmunā || 20-128 ||

bhakṣitaṃ nāśayen samyak śirorogā nātra saṃśayaḥ |

saṃpat pradā bhairavī mantra prayogaḥ || 20-129 ||

tattva laṃ manu japtvā prayogānācaret sudhīḥ |

kṣīrākairamṛtā khaṇḍai homasarvāṣamṛtyujit || 20-130 ||

dūrvābhirāpuṣo homaḥ kṣīrāktābhirdinatrayam |

lājān prajuhuyān matrī dadhikṣīrasamadbhutām || 20-131 ||

vijityalokānakhilāt sajīva chakāṃśatam |


atha śrīvidyā mantra prayoga |


lakṣaṃ saṃjapya vidhināmaprayogā canmucasudhiḥ || 20-132 ||

lakṣahomā labhed rājyaṃ dāridribhayapiṇḍitaḥ |

purgopasargaśamanaṃ pralāṃ ūmadhuhomataḥ || 20-133 ||

khecaro jāyate rātrau kṛtvā homaṃ catuṣpathe |


palāṇḍu prāṇa iti |


kṣīraṃ dadhimadhughṛtaṃ pṛthakkṛtvā varānane || 20-134 ||

āyurvalavadārogyaṃ samṛddhijāyate nṛṇām |

gutvātrapalamadhvannaṃ mantraṃ smṛtvamaheśvarī || 20-135 ||

khecaro jāyate devī rātraugatvā catuṣpathe |

homo dadhimadhukṣīra lājābhirvīravandite || 20-136 ||

rogahantā kālahartā mṛtyu hantā  na saṃśayaḥ |

kamalairaruṇairhomaḥ samyak saṃpatdāyaka || 20-137 ||

gugulo khaluhomena sarvaduḥkhavīnāśayet |

346a) tilataṃdulahomena śāntirbhavati maṃdire || 20-138 ||

sopaskarastu vaṭukairūpasargānvināśayet |

śāntikārye tathā kṛṣ'u caturasraṃ tu kuṇḍakam |

bhuktau muktau tathā devi dīrghakuṇḍasamācaret || 20-139 ||


iti śrī-āgamakalpalaṭāyāṃ ṣaḍtriṃśapaṭala |



vaśyaprayo pravakṣyāmi nānātantrānusārataḥ |


atha bhuvaneśvarī mantra prayogaḥ |


tattvalakṣaṃ manuṃ japtvā prayogānācaret sudhī || 21-1 ||

karpūrāgurusaṃyuktā kuṃkumaṃ sādhusādhitā |

gṛhitvā tilakaṃ kuryād rājā ca śyamanuttamam || 21-2 ||

śālipṛṣṭamayi kṛtvā puṃjalimadhurānvitam |

japāpratiṣṭitaprāṇāṃ bhakṣayed ravivāsaret || 21-3 ||

vasanayati rājānaṃ nāri vā narameva vā |

likhitvā bhasmanā māyāṃ susādhvāṃ phalakādiṣu || 21-4 ||

tatkālaṃ darśanmantraṃ sukhaṃ sūryatagabhini |

sarṣapai loṇasaṃmiśrai rājyākaijuhuyānniśi || 21-5 ||

rājāṃ ca na vayen sadyustat patnīmapi sādhakaḥ |


atha tvaritā mantra prayogaḥ |


ayutaṃ tu manujaptvā prayogānini * * *  || 21-6 ||

ayutaṃ homasaṃkhyāsyā japastāvān prakīrttitaḥ |

yonikuṇḍaṃ prakalpāḍa homaṃ kuryā trije chayā || 21-7 ||

mallikākusmai homā vaśayedakhilaṃ jagat |

mantrī nīlotpalairhutvā nṛpapatnī vaśaṃ nayet || 21-8 ||

pravuddhaiḥ paṃkajairhutvā vaśaṃmapatidainī |


atha mahiṣamardinī prayoga |

346b) aṣṭalakṣamanujaptveti |

vaśayen tilahomena narānnarapratinapi |


athaśūlini mantra prayogaḥ |

aśvārthākasamiti bhirvā tilairvā madhurojitaiḥ || 21-9 ||

homā vaśayati kṣipramīpsitān mantriṇo narān |


atha vanadurgā mantra prayogaḥ |

mantro yathā |


utiṣṭe pāti catulakṣaṃ manuṃ japtvet yādi ca || 21-10 ||

kusumairvaṛśayed viprān nṛpatiṃ padmahomataḥ |

tat patnī rutpalaiḥ phulai varopekalhārahomataḥ || 21-11 ||

śūdrāṃ llakuṇahomena jātipuṣpai sabhāṃ vudhaḥ |


atha satpadā bhairavī mantra prayogaḥ |


tattvalaṃkṣaṃ manuṃ japtvā prayogāmācaret sudhī || 21-12 ||

manutvimavilomāta sthita sādhyā kṛyānvita |

manumucārya juhuyā mantrimamadhuraloḍitaiḥ || 21-13 ||

sarṣapai paṭsamiśrairvaśayat pārthivān kṣaṇāt |

anenaiva vidhānena tat patnī tat sutāmapi || 21-14 ||


pādadvayaṃ layajaṃ pādaṃ kuṃkumakesaraṃ pādayo rocanaṃ caiva triṇi 
piṣṭvā himāṃbhasā |


vidadyā tilakaṃ bhāle pānyaśyai dvai vilikyate |

yān nṛśye spṛśyate yairvā vaśyāsyustate cirāt || 21-15 ||

karpūrakapicaurāṇi samabhāgāni kalpayet |

caturbhāgā jaṭāmāṃsī tāvatirocaṇāmatā || 21-16 ||

kuṃkumaṃ saptabhāgaṃ syā dvibhāgaṃ candanaṃ matam |

agurrunavabhāgasyād iti bhāgaḥ krameṇa ca || 21-17 ||

himādbhikanyakā pṛṣṭametat sarvasusādhita |

347a) ādāya tilakaṃ bhāle kuryād bhūmipatin narāt || 21-18 ||

varnitāmadagarvādyā madoṃmartamarāṃgajān |

siṃhānvyāghrān mahāsarpā bhūtavetālarākṣasān || 21-19 ||

darśnād eva vaśaye tilakaṃ dhārayannara |


atha mātaṃgini prayoga |


prajaptvā prayutaṃ mantri tataḥ prayogamācaret || 21-20 ||

candanāgurukarpūra rocanā kuṃkuṃmādibhiḥ |

vaśyāya juhuyāt matrī vasatayedakhikhaṃ jagat || 21-21 ||

etāni japtvā nilakaṃ kuryāllokapriyo bhavet |


phalai vilvamūlasaṃmudbhūtaistat putraistasya traivāhutaṃ bhavet |


rājaputrasya rājyāgni paṃkajaiḥ śivamāpnuyāt || 21-22 ||


atha śrīgaṇapatimantra prayogaḥ |


vedalakṣaṃ manujaptveti śukrapratipadamārabhya dinaśaḥ sudhīḥ |


caturthyantaṃ nārikelasaktulātilai kramāt |

catuśataṃ pradjuhuyād vaśyāḥ syuḥ sarvajantavaḥ || 21-23 ||

lājaistimadhurāpetairdomakanyāṃ prayachati |

anena vidhinā kanyā varamāpnoti vāchitam || 21-24 ||

dadhnā viloditai lauṇairhomāniśi caturdinam |

saṃprītiṃ kurute tadvad vaśyaṃ vi tanute sadā || 21-25 ||

śvetāke bhavamūlena raktacadanadāruṇā |

ibhagnena vimvena dantidatte na vā kṛtam || 21-26 ||

vighneśvaraṃ samabhyarcya śītāṃśugrahaṇe jayat |

spṛṣṭvā mantrinirāhārastaṃ śikhāyāṃ samudvaham || 21-27 ||

yuddheṣu vyavahāradau japaṃ ca śriyamāpnuyāt |

347b) metreṇānena saṃjaptā rocanā madhusaṃyutā || 21-28 ||

tilakanniyayā sarvān vaśa tayatimānavān |

anulomastha vīryeṇa rasamālikhet || 21-29 ||

navatīte samabhyarśca spṛṣṭvā prāṇamanuñjapet |

aṣṭottaraśataṃ bhūyo mūlamantra jape tataḥ || 21-30 ||

bhakṣayet mautamāsthāya yā mityāṃ saptavāsaram |

savaścāyate śrīghraṃ sādhakasya na saṃśayaḥ || 21-31 ||


atha mahāgaṇapati mantraprayogaḥ |


oṃ hrī/ śrī/ klī/ glau/ gaṃ gaṇapataye varavarada sarvajanaṃ meva śamāmaya 
svāhā | caturlakṣaṃ manuṃ japtveti |


padmahomena bhūpālāstasya traiḥ śubhaiḥ |

mantriṇaḥ kumudai phulairviprān pippalasaṃbhave || 21-32 ||


plakṣairvaśyātvatodgataiḥ |

śraddhāt matrī vaśaṃ na yat |


madhunā svarṇalābhasyād godugdhena labhe tagāḥ |

ājyaṃ homena mahati śrīmāpnoti mānavaḥ || 21-33 ||

dagdhā sarvasamṛddhi syād annairnarapatirbhavet |


atra mantrāntarā prayoga |


lakṣatrayaṃ manuṃ japtvā prayogānācaret sudhī || 21-34 ||

hutvā pūpaitrimadhvāktai vaśayadbhavi pārthivān |

lavaṇairmadhusaṃyuktairvaśayad vanitājanabha || 21-35 ||


mantrāntara prayogaḥ |


lakṣamekaṃ manuṃ japtvā prayogāniti |

ājyadomena vaśayet prāṇinaḥ sakalat sudhī || 21-36 ||

nārikelaphalaṃ paktaṃ lāṣṭacarmasamanvitam |

348a) huhvayā pratyahaṃ mantri maṇḍalāt siddhimāpnuyāt || 21-37 ||

juhuyādaṣṭabhidravyairmadhuratrayasaṃyutaḥ |

vaśayet pārthivāt sarvāstat patnīrdhidhināmunā || 21-38 ||


aṣṭadravyārāpāha |


modakeḥ pṛthukairlājaiḥ satubhiḥ sekṣaparvabhiḥ |

nālikelaistilaiśuddhaiḥ sapaktai kadalī phalai || 21-39 ||

aṣṭadravyāni vighnasya kathitāni maṇiṣibhī |


atha śrinarasiṃhamantraprayogaḥ |

ṛtulakṣamanuṃ japtvā yogāniti |


snātāṃstrimadhuro petāt prāptaḥ kāleṣu juhutaḥ || 21-40 ||

kanyā siddhi bhavet paścāt katyāyāḥ sadvaro bhavet |

atha maheśasya cintāmaṇi mantra prayogaḥ || 21-41 ||


lakṣamekaṃ miti pūrvoktavat |


kuṃkumābhatidaṃ vījantrikaṃṇagatamujvalam |

yasya mūrdhni smantri savaśyo jāyate cirāt || 21-42 ||

ūrdhvarephaśya sādhyākhyaṃ vījaṃ vahni gṛhasthitam |

vahni geha dvaye nāmni yuktoṇena ca saṃsthitām || 21-43 ||

pratilimasurācītaṃ culle sthāne nivesitam |

vaśīkarītyāmaraṇādacirīd eva dāsavat || 21-44 ||

madhuratrayayuktena śālipiṣṭena puttalīm |

kṛtvā pratiṣṭita prāṇyaṃ vibhakṣya juhuyādvaśī || 21-45 ||

trivāsaramanai navasādhyastasya vaśyā bhavet |


atha vaṭukabhairavamantra prayogaḥ |

varṇalakṣaṃ manu japtvā prayogāniti |


vaśyāya juhuyādikṣu śalakairvaśayarjjanān || 21-46 ||

348b) juhuyāt putralābhāya praphullairkairavaiḥ śubhaiḥ |

lavaṇaimadhusaṃmiśrairvaśayed vanitājanām || 21-47 ||

vacācūṇaṃ palaṃ japtaṃ gavyenājyeḍitam |

vibhajyabhakṣayed vandhyā maṇḍalaṃ putrakāṃkṣiṇī || 21-48 ||

viṇitaṃ putramāpnoti medhārogya valānvita |

ādāvante prayogasya vaṭukāvaliṃ haret || 21-49 ||

mudgapāya sasaṃyuktaṃ madhulatrayaloḍitam |

uśīlaṃ candanaṃ kuṣṭaṃ dyanasāracakuṃkumam || 21-50 ||

śvetārkamūlaṃ vārāhi lakṣmīkṣīramahinṛhān |

tvavorṣilvanaromūlaṃ śoṣayitvāśucūrṇayet || 21-51 ||

cūrṇavyomni gṛhinagomayena vimiśrita |

kṛtvā piṇḍāya saṃśocya saṃskṛte havyavāhane || 21-52 ||

mūlena dagdhā tadbhasma śuddhapātre vinikṣipet |

kaṭekī mālatīpuṣpairvāśayed bhasmasādhitam || 21-53 ||

ayutaṃ prajapen mantraṃ dṛṣṭvā bhasmasupūjitam |

etadādāya dinaśaḥ prāṇaḥ puṇḍaṃ karoniyaḥ || 21-54 ||

tasya rogā praṇaśyanti kṛtyādohāmayagrahaḥ |

ripucauramṛgādibhyo bhayamasya na jāyate || 21-55 ||

varddhante samyada sarvā pūjyate sakalairjanaiḥ |

rājā vaśyā bhavet tasya sāmānya saparichadaḥ || 21-56 ||


atha bhairavamantra prayogaḥ |

tattvalakṣamanuṃ japtvā prayogāniti |


raktopalaistu madhyāktai ruruṇervāhapārijaiḥ |

mallikā kusumai hutvā rājaputrātvaṃśaṃ nayat || 21-57 ||

349a) evavillubhavaiḥ puṣpai madhuratrayasaṃyutai |

naranārī naraṃ pate demito vasamānayet || 21-58 ||

pādahṛyaṃ malapajaṃ pādaṃ kukumakeśare |

pādaṃ gorocanaṃ caiva trīṇipiṣṭvāhimām || 21-59 ||

vidadyā bhilaṃkaṃ bhāle yāna paśyed yena vikṣyateḥ |

yāt spṛśat smṛśyate yai vācasyāḥ syustasya te cirāt || 21-60 ||

karpūrakapicaurāṇi samābhāgāni kalpayet |

caturbhāgaṃ jaṭāmāṃsi tāvatīrocanā manā || 21-61 ||

kuṃkumaṃ saptabhāgaṃ syāt tibhāgaṃ candranamanam |

agururnavabhāgasyād iti bhāgakramena ca || 21-62 ||

himādbhiḥ kanyakāpiṣṭametat sarvesumādhitam |

ādāya tikaṃ bhāle kuryād bhūmipatinnarān || 21-63 ||

vanitāmadagarghādyā madonmabhāmataṃ gajān |

siṃhavyāghrān mahāsarpān bhūtavetālarākṣasān || 21-64 ||

darśānād eva vaśaye tilakaṃ dhārayaṃ naraḥ |


atha śrīvidyāmantra prayogaḥ |

lakṣamekaṃ manuṃ japtvā prayogāniti |


mahākāmakalā dhyāna yogāśya suravandite || 21-65 ||

kṣobhavet svargabhūloka pātālatalayoṣitā |

rocanā bhāgamekaikaṃ ca kuṃkumam || 21-66 ||

atha bhāgadvayaṃ devi candanaṃ mardayet samam |

ekatratitttvakaṃ kuryā trailokya vasakāraṇam || 21-67 ||

aṣṭottaraśatāvṛtthā mantrayitvā vaśaṃ nayet |


iti vaśya prakaraṇam |


staṃbhanaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi nānā nā tatrānusārataḥ || 21-68 ||


349b) atha tvaritā mantra |


ayutaṃ hāmasakhyāsyājjapastāvan prakīrttitaḥ |

yonikuṇḍaprakalpyātha homaṃ kuryān nijechayā || 21-69 ||

māsahomena prakasyād unmatau kṣairbhavedari |


atha śūlinī mantra prayogaḥ |


varṇalakṣaṃ manu japtvā prayogaṃ sādhayat sudhī || 21-70 ||

gṛhitvā gomayaṃ vyogni trisahasraṃ jape tataḥ |

gatvā ca tad dvāradeśe nikhātaṃ staṃbhanaṃ bhavet || 21-71 ||


atha vanadurgā |


catullakṣaṃ manujaptvā prayogāniti |


senā saṃstaṃbhane mantri kāraskarasamudbhaivaiḥ |

puṣpaṃ sahasraṃ juhuyāt tat patyai stāṃ nivarttayet || 21-72 ||


kāraskarakucila iti prasiddha |

atha mātaṃgyāḥ |


prajaptvā cāyutaṃ mantraṃ tataḥ prayogamācare |

haridrācūrṇasaṃmiśrerlakṣaṇaistaṃbhayelparāt || 21-73 ||


atha nṛsiṃhasya |

varṇalakṣaṃ manuṃ japtveti |


yayataṃ kurvita vidhivat chatrusenā nivāraṇe |

vibhītakāṣṭai jvalite pāvake ripumardanam || 21-74 ||

vicintya devaṃ nṛhariṃ saṃpūjya kusumādibhi |

samūlai juhuyān nityaṃ śalairdaśaśanaṃ pṛthak || 21-75 ||

ṛpuṃ khādan nivajad vāhni bhinikṣipet |

hutvā saptadinaṃ senāṃ miṣṭāṃ mantrimahipate || 21-76 ||

sthāpaye chubhe labhe laglapen rāṣṭrajape chrayā |

tasyāḥ purastāntu harinighnaṃ taṃ ripumaṇḍalam || 21-77 ||

spṛtvā prayogaṃ kurvīta yāvadāpāti sā punaḥ |

vijitya nikhilān śatrut sahavīraśriyāṃ sukhāt || 21-78 ||

350a) āgatya vijayi rājā grāmakṣetradharmadibhi |


prīṇayen matriṇe samyak vibhavaiḥ pretamānasa mantriya dina santuṣyedaṃ narthe 
syān mahipate |

atha vaṭukasya |

varṇalakṣaṃ manuṃ japtveti |


juhuyāt kevalairloṇaiḥ prayutaṃ staṃbhane chayā || 21-79 ||

nigatrādivimokṣāya prayogo ya mudīrin |


atha śrīvidyā prayogamāha |


lakṣamekaṃ manuṃ japtvā prayogānācaret sudhiḥ || 21-80 ||

pitadravyaiḥ samālikhya pītapuṣpai samarpayet |

pūrvāśābhimukho bhūtvā staṃbhayet sarvavādinaḥ || 21-81 ||

sahasravadano devi muko bhavati tatkṣaṇāt |

yamā śābhimukhi bhūtvā cakraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ yadārpayet || 21-82 ||

yasya nāmāṃkitaṃ tasya mantrahāni prajāyate |

caṃpakai patalai hutvā śriyaṃ prāpnotyanuttamām || 21-83 ||

prāpnoti matri mahanti staṃbhe ye jagatimimām |

sitayā ke calo homo vairistabhana nakāka || 21-84 ||


atha chinnamastāyā |


palalaṃ māhiṣaṃ hutvā madhunā sahasundari |

staṃbhayed āśuśatrūṇāṃ yvalaṃdūrepi sasthitam || 21-85 ||

caṃpakaṃ ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ hutvā nagarātmaje |

ripusainyaṃ mahāghoraṃ staṃbhayed acirā chive || 21-86 ||


iti staṃbhanaprakaraṇam |


vidveṣa saṃpravakṣyāmi nāgnārtatrānusārataḥ |


tvaritāmantraprayogamāha |


ayutaṃ homa saṃkhyā syā japastāvān prakīrttita || 21-87 ||

350b) yonikuṇḍaṃ prakalpyātha homakuryān nijechayā |

ko dravyaiḥ khaṇḍite tadvan nivaivirdvaṣayen mitha || 21-88 ||


atha mahiṣamarddinyā |


vasulakṣaiḥ kṛto homo dveṣaṃ vitanu te nṛṇām |


atha śūlinyā |

varṇalakṣaṃ manuṃ japtvā iti |


ūlikā gomayotpannā hutvāṣṭaśatasaṃkhyayā || 21-89 ||

saptāhāt kurute matri vidveṣasnigdhayormitha |


atha vanadurgā yā |

caturakṣaṃ manujaptveti |


agākhāre kulike japtvā bhasmacittodbhavam || 21-90 ||

vinikṣipen mūdhni riporvidviṣṭo deśato vrajet |


atha śrīvidyāyā |


lakṣaṃ japtvā ca vidhivat prayogaṃ kurute naraḥ || 21-91 ||

mahiṣāśvapuriṣābhyāṃ rasamākṛṣya śailaje |

gomūtreṇa saṃlikhya nāmasaṃdarbhya pūrvavat || 21-92 ||

kṛtvā lavaṇamadhyasthaṃ vidveṣaṇakaraṃ bhavet |

lavaṇeti kṣiptvā āraṇālamadhyasthamiti catuḥśatyām || 21-93 ||


āraṇālaṃkācikam |


telasthaṃ cakrarājaṃ tu vidveṣaṇakaraṃ bhavet |


rocaṇā likhitaṃ yantra rājamitiśeṣaḥ |

iti vidveṣa prakaraṇam |


uccāṭanaṃ pravakṣyāmi nānātantrānusārataḥ || 21-94 ||


atha tvaritā mantraprayogaḥ |


ayutaṃ homasaṃkhyā japastāvān prakīrttitaḥ |

yonikuṇḍaṃ prakalyātra homaṃ kuryān nijechayā || 21-95 ||

patrai va kulajai hutvā śīghramuccāṭayed arīt |


vanadurgā yā |

caturlakṣaṃ manuṃ japtvā iti |


351a) cāpavāṇadharo bhīmāṃ sihasthā jvalanopamom || 21-96 ||

śṛjanti vāṇanivahā dhāvatintāṃvatiripum |

dhyātvā japet manumi mama yutaṃ toyutaṃ toyamadhyaga || 21-97 ||

ripuṃ ca parame nāṃ ca drutamuccāṭaye dhruvam |


atha śrīnarasiṃhasya |


varsālakṣaṃ manujatveti siṃharūpaṃ mahābhīmaṃ nakhadaṃṣṭāti bhīṣaṇam 
|

smṛtvātmānaṃ ripuṃ paścād vyayena mṛgaśirāṃ ripum || 21-98 ||

gṛhi gatvā galadeśe taṃ punardikṣa diyet tataḥ |

putramitrakalattrādyairucāṭau jāyate ripo || 21-99 ||

pūrvamṛtyupade sādhya nāmakṛtvā svayaṃ hari |

niśatai nakhaṃdraṃṣṭrādyai khaṇḍamānaṃ ripusmaram || 21-100 ||

nityamaṣṭottaraśataṃ japen matraṃ mayedritaḥ |

jāyate maṇḍalādarvā kaśatrave vasvatapriyaḥ || 21-101 ||


śrīvidyā yā |

lakṣamekaṃ manuṃ japtvati |


kṛtvā rocanayā nāmamadhyasthaṃ kākamadhyagam |

śavamānaṃ tadā kāśe śatruccāṭanakārakam || 21-102 ||

gomūtramadhyagaṃ samyak śatrucātakaraṃ bhavet |


atha chinnamastā yā |


kākapakṣairkṛto homo datūrāgnau samāhitaḥ || 21-103 ||

āloḍhyakaṭutailena sadyocāṭanamāraṇe |

uccāṭane makuṇḍaṃ syād vartulaṃ ca sukṣubhonam || 21-104 ||


atha nīlasarasvatyā |


śmaśānagāramādāya niśimatalavāśare |

kṛṣṇavastreṇa saṃvyavdya vadhnīyād uktataṃ tu nā || 21-105 ||

śanābhimantritacca prakṣipe vairamandire |

351b) saptāhābhyantare teṣāmuccāṭanamidaṃ bhavet || 21-106 ||


ityucāṭanaprakaraṇam |


māraṇaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi nāmastrānusārataḥ |


atha tvaritā yā |


ayutaṃ japasaṃkhyā japaptvā vāt prakīrttitaḥ || 21-107 ||


yonikuṇḍaṃ prakalpātra homaṃ kuryāt ti ochayā kurvita sarṣaye homarica homān 
maraṇaripurāpnoti sarvayā |


bhūtāri caurabhūtādyā dhānā devi nāśayet |


atha śūlinyā |

atha vanadurgā yā |

catulakṣamanumiti |


marunniyāti taiḥ yatraikāraktarasamudbhavaiḥ || 21-108 ||

tasya pādarajā hotamaṃ nirnāśayed arin |

kāraskāramayiṃ kṛtvā pratimāṃ ca suśobhanām || 21-109 ||


japtvā pratiṣṭita prāṇāṃ bhedayedaṅgasa na varṇalakṣaṃ manumiti |


juhuyāt tilasiddhādyai lakṣamekaṃ yathāvidhi |

nāmayuktaṃ japen matraṃ yasyā sau mṛtimepyati || 21-110 ||


atha vanadurgā yā |

catulakṣamanumiti |


maruṃti pātiteḥ patrai kārastu rasamudbhavai |

tasya pādarajāktairhomaṃ nirnāśayedarin || 21-111 ||

kārastāramyīṃ kṛtvā pratimāṃ ca śuśobhanām |

japtvāṃ pratiṣṭita prāṇāṃ bhedayedaṅgaśa punaḥ || 21-112 ||

kākolūkavasā yuktamaṣṭottarasahasrakam |

kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyā śmaśāne havyavāhane || 21-113 ||

juhuyāt priyate rātri revameva dinatrayāt |

utmattasamidho homān mattaḥ syuḥ śatavakṣaṇāt || 21-114 ||


ulūkakākayośanam |

atha narasiṃhasya |


352a) varṇalakṣaṃ manumiti pūrvamṛtyu padeśā dhyanāmakṛtvāyaṃ hari |


niśitairnakhadaṣṭrādyaiḥ |

khaṇḍamāṇaṃ ripusmaran |


nityamaṣṭottaraśataṃ japet mantramantadritaḥ |

jāyate maṇḍalādarvāk śatru vaivasvata priyaḥ || 21-115 ||


atha sudarśanāstrasya |

arkalakṣaṃ manuṃ japtveti |


pulayāgni samacakraṃ yasya mūrdhni cintaye |

sakāhāra mūrchātto maṇḍalān mṛtyumeti saḥ || 21-116 ||

maṇḍalād ekonapaṃcāśadivaśātaḥ |

vagnau dagdhā maheśāni mārayet vairiṇaṃ śiva || 21-117 ||

jvarajvareti madhyasthaṃ śatraurnāśakaraṃ bhavet |

japtvā matrihutaṃ rātrau rāṣṭraṃ ca vinipātayet || 21-118 ||


kokirā sthiti liyo juhoti citānale |


samārayati śatrunāṃ valaṃ dūrepi saṃsthitam |

māraṇe vartulai caiva kuryāt kuṇḍaṃ vicakṣaṇa || 21-119 ||


atha nīlasarasvatyā |


narāsthini likhet mantraṃ kṣārayuktaṃ haridrayā |

sahasraparijapyā tu triśāra vivāsaret || 21-120 ||

nikṣipyate gṛhe yasya mṛtyustasya trimāsataḥ |


iti māraṇa prakaraṇam |


ityāgamakalpalatāyāṃ saptaviṃśapallavaḥ ||


atha kulasnānam |

kulacūḍāmaṇau |


ācāntakuladarbheṇa sadarbhakulapuṇḍakaḥ || 21-121 ||

kulapātre sadurvaṃ ca samilaṃ sajantataḥ |

gṛhitvā kuladevyāstu prītaye snānamācaret || 21-122 ||

kṛtasaṃkalpa evā so kulacakraṃ jale nyaset |


kuladevī nāda |


somasūryagni rūpāsyur varṇālāhatrayastathā || 21-123 ||

352b) raupyamindu smṛto hema sūryāstāsraṃ hutāśataḥ |

lohabhāgāḥ samudiṣṭā smādyakṣarasaṃkhyayā || 21-124 ||

tailaṃ haiḥ kāreyen mudrā masaṃkalita saṃgatān |

sāgraṃ sahasraṃ japya pṛṣṭvā tāṃ juhuyaṃ tataḥ || 21-125 ||

tasyāṃ saṃpātayet mastrī sarṣiṣā pūrvasakhyayā |

niśipya kuṃbhe tāṃ mudrāmabhiṣekoktavatsatā || 21-126 ||

āvāhya pūjaye devī mupecāraiḥ samāhitaiḥ |

abhiṣicya vinitāpa dadyān nāmudrikāṃ guro || 21-127 ||

drūpaṃ rakṣā kṣadurogaviṣajvalavināśi |

vyālacauramṛgādibhyo rakṣāṃ kuryād viśeṣataḥ || 21-128 ||

yuddhe vijayamāpnoti dhārayet manujeśvaraḥ |


lohabhāga iti |


ṣoḍaśabhāgā rajastasya pacaviṃśabhibhāgā hesradaśabhāgā * * sya ṣoḍaśa 
paṃcaviṃśatirdaśa |


asaṃkalinaṃ anekatrīkṛte |


kulasnāna samānīya kulamudrāṃ kuśena ca || 21-129 ||

kulatīrghāni tatraiva samāvāhya śivātmakam |

tattoyaṃ ca vidhāyitvā tridhā ca prokṣaṇaṃ tano || 21-130 ||


taduktaṃ tantrāntare |


mūlanyathanmūrdhnitoyaṃ mudrayā kuṃbhasaṃjñayā |

kṣiptvā vāratraya devi ācamet sādhakāgrasit || 21-131 ||


ātmavidyāni vaistatonya ca samācaret |

taduktaṃ jñānārṇave |


kulasūryāya devā ācamet sādhakāgrasit || 21-132 ||

ātmavidyāni vaistatai statonya ca samācaret |


taduktaṃ jñānārṇave |


kulasūryāya devāya trirargha tu pradāpayet || 21-133 ||

353a) devān liṣin pitru caiva tarpagne deva vāriṇā |


atha kulācāraḥ |

taduktṃ bhadrakālītantre |


ācāraṃ ca pravakṣyāmi ya kṛte mṛtamaśrute || 21-134 ||

sarvabhūtahite yuktaḥ samayācārapālaka |

anityakarma santyāgi nityānuṣṭāna tatparaḥ || 21-135 ||

mantrārādhanamātraṇa śivabhāvaṇa tatparaḥ |

paramyāṃ devatāyāṃ tu sarvakarmanivedakaḥ || 21-136 ||

anya mantrārcana śraddhā anyamantra prapūjanam |

kulastrī vīraniṃdāśca tadrunyamayasāraṇam || 21-137 ||

strīṣuroṣaprahāraṃ ca varjrayat sadā |

mayaṃ ca jagatsarvaṃ bhāvayed vidhinā yunaḥ || 21-138 ||

peyaṃ cavyaṃ tathā coṣyaṃ bhekṣyaṃ tahyaṃ gṛhaṃ sukha |

sarvaṃ ca yuvatirūpaṃ bhāvayed yata mānasa || 21-139 ||

kulajā yuvantī vīkṣya namaskuryā samāhitaḥ |

tadeva mānasiṃ pūjāṃ tatra tāṃ sāṃ prakalpayet || 21-140 ||

vālāṃ vādyau vanonmatāṃ vṛddhāṃ vā sundari tathā |

kutsitāṃ rāmahāduṣṭāṃ namakṛtya vibhāvayet || 21-141 ||

tāsāṃ prahāsaṃ nindāṃ ca kauṭilyamapriyaṃ tathā |


sarvathā navakarttavya manyathā |

siddhirodha kṛt |


striyo devā striyaprāṇā striye vahimūṣaṇam || 21-142 ||

striyo doṣo nakarttavyo viśayāt pūjanaṃ striyaḥ |


taduktaṃ vīratantre |


dikkālaniyamo nātra sthitvādi niyamo nahi || 21-143 ||

jayana kālaniyamo nārcādiṣu valiṣyapi |

svechā niyama uktotra mahāmantrasya sādhane || 21-144 ||

vastrāsanasthana gahe dehasparśādi vāyunā |

353b) śuddhiṃ na vāre tatra nirvikalpaṃ naśaret || 21-145 ||

svechācārotra gaḍitaḥ pracaradaṣṭamānasaḥ |

vyathā kālaṃ va gamaye tadyutakriḍādināsudhā || 21-146 ||

gamaye devatā pūjā japayāgastadādibhi |

vīrāṇāṃ ya pa yajñastu sarvākāle praśasyate || 21-147 ||

sarvadeśe sarvapīṭhe karttavyo nātrasaṃśayaḥ |


taduktaṃ śivāgame |

śaktiḥ śivaḥ śakti brahmājanārdana |


śaktirindrā raviśaktiḥ śaktiścandro grahādhruvam || 21-148 ||

śaktirūpaṃ jagatsarvaṃ yonajānāti nāraki |


vīratantre |


snānādi mānasaśaucaṃ mānasaḥ pravaro jayaḥ || 21-149 ||

pūjanaṃ mānasaṃ divyaṃ mānasaṃ trpaṇādikam |

sarva eva śubhaḥ kālo nāśubho vidyate kvacit || 21-150 ||

nāviśeṣā divā rātrau na saṃdhyāyāṃ mahāniśi |

sarvadā pūjaya devīmasnāna snāna eva vā || 21-151 ||

mahāniśi śucaudeśe valimantreṇa dāpayet |

rātrā ve va mahāpūjā karttavyā vīravandite || 21-152 ||

nadine sarvathā kāryā śāsanā mama suvrate |


mahāniśā tu |


ardharātrāt paraṃ yacca mūrtadvayameva ca || 21-153 ||

sā mahārātriruddiṣṭā tad dantamakṣayaṃ bhavet |


taduktaṃ gandharvatatro pṛthvīṃ mṛdumati vīkṣya sahasraṃ padanityasaḥ |


tadā vādhāt svasiddhānta hataḥ kṣititalaṃ vrajet || 21-154 ||

parvato hastamāropya nirbhayo yatamānasa |

kavitālate sopi amṛtaṃ tvaṃ ca gachati || 21-155 ||


354a) māmyamāha |


kulāgāramaraṇye tu dṛṣṭvā yojayate naraḥ |

ayukaika pramāṇena mādhaka sthiramānasaḥ || 21-156 ||

atha syānena mantreṇa mantrimukhavāpnuyāt |

ghoradaṃṣṭre karālāsye kiti śabda praṇāditi || 21-157 ||

ghora ghora racā sphāle namaste citi vāsini |

raktavastraṃ tathā puṣpaṃ vilokya tripurārvikā || 21-158 ||

praṇamya daṇḍavad bhūmā vimaṃmatraṃ japennara |

vadhukapuṣpasaṃkāśe tripure bhayanāsini || 21-159 ||

bhāgyo vaya samutpanne namaste varavarṇini |

kṛṣṇavastraṃ tathā puṣpaṃ rājanaṃ rājaputraka || 21-160 ||

hastyaśvarathavastrāṇi phalake cirapuruṣā |

mahiṣaṃ kuladevaśca dṛṣṭā mahiṣamardinim || 21-161 ||

praṇamya jayadurgāṃ vā samā ca vighnau naliṣyate |

jaya devi jagaddhātri tripurā detri daivate || 21-162 ||

bhaktebhyo varade devī mahiṣaghnī namostu te |

annabhāṇḍaṃ samālokya matsyamānsaṃ varastriyam || 21-163 ||

dṛṣṭvā ca bhairavaṃ devi praṇamya vimṛṣaṃ manum |

ghoravighnavināśāya kulācārasamṛddhaye || 21-164 ||

namāmi varare devī muṇḍamālā vibhūṣite |

raktadhārā samākīrṇe vadane tvāṃ namāmyaham || 21-165 ||

sarvavighna hare devi namaste haravallabham |

eteṣāṃ darśanaṃ yadi naivaṃ prakurvate || 21-166 ||

śaktimantraṃ puraskṛtya tasya siddhina jāyate |

atha bhāvāt pravakṣyāmi sādhakābhīstasiddhidā || 21-167 ||


354b) taduktaṃ bhāvacūḍāmaṇau |


śrībhairava uvāca

samayācārasaṃketaṃ bhāvasaṃketameva ca |

sucitaṃ tu mayā devi kathayasvānukampayā || 21-168 ||

śrī devyuvāca 

bhāvastu trividivya vīrayaśukramāt |

guravastu tridhā cātra tathaiva mantradevatā || 21-169 ||

ayabhāvo mahādevaḥ śrayot sarvasamṛddhidaḥ |

dviyo madhye mamaścaiva va tṛtīyaḥ sarvaninditaḥ || 21-170 ||

kiṃ vīrasādhanai lakṣaiḥ kimākṛṣṭi kulākulaiḥ |

akulaṃ śivamityāhu kulaṃ tu yarameśvarī || 21-171 ||

kiṃcid indriyabhāvena kiṃ kulācārakarmaṇā |

yadi bhāvaviśuddhātmā nasyāt kulaparāyaṇa || 21-172 ||

bhāvena labhate muktiṃ bhāvena kulavardhanam |

bhāvena gotravṛddhi syād bhavena kulaśodhanam || 21-173 ||

kiṃ nyāsavistareṇaiva kibhūtaśuddhi vistaraiḥ |

kiṃ tathā pujanai naivedyadibhāvo na vidyate || 21-174 ||

vahujā tathā homāt kāyakleśādi vistarāt |

nabhāvai navitā deva mantravidyā phalapradā || 21-175 ||

phalābhāvā śvamantrāṇāṃ bhāvābhāvannajāyate |

etad rahasyaṃ kathane śaṃketa namānasam || 21-176 ||

trilokī matsaraprāyā sarvatra tat samāgataḥ |

nirikṣanirjunaṃ deka payiṣyāmi te kathāt || 21-177 ||


śrīdevyuvāca 


prathamaṃ divyabhāvaśca kathyate śṛṇu sādaram |

355a) yadvarṇa devatā yatra tat tejapuja * puri || 21-178 ||

tejomayaṃ jagatsarvaṃ vibhāvya mūrtikalpanam |

ātmānaṃ tat mayaṃ kṛtvā sarvabhāva tathaiva ca || 21-179 ||

tat sarvaṃ yoṣini dhyātvā cintaye dhanamānasaḥ |

aśeṣakulasaṃpannā nājāti kulodbhavā || 21-180 ||

nānādeśodbhavā vāpi guṇalāvaṇya saṃyutā |

dvitīyā sarādūrdhvaṃ yāvat syād aṣṭamāvdikā || 21-181 ||

tāvat kumārī vijñayā mantravidyā phalaṃ pradā |

kumārī pūjanāścaiva kumārī bhojanādapi || 21-182 ||

kevalaṃ guptabhāvena satu vidyānidhi bhavet |

athavā muktakeśaśca haviṣyaṃ bhakṣayen naraḥ || 21-183 ||

prajapyād ayutaṃ tasya hyeṣa pratinidhiḥ smṛtiḥ |

mahācīna drumalatā majjābhirvilvapatrakam || 21-184 ||

sahasradevi manyaśca śmaśāne sādhakottamaḥ |

tadīrājyamavāpnoti yadi sanaparāyate || 21-185 ||


vākucūḍāmaṇau |


ekākī nirjanadeśe śmaśāne vijane vane |

śūnyāgāre nadītīre nismaṃgo viharessadā || 21-186 ||

mahācinadrume devi dhyātvā tatra prapūjayat |

tadramodbhavapuṣpeṇa pūjayed bhaktibhāvataḥ || 21-187 ||

sabhayaṃ kaladevaśca kulakramagataḥ śuci |

brahmataro mahāpadme devīṃ dhyātvā yathāvidhi || 21-188 ||

so tantrokravidhānena tarpayet mātṛkānane |

rātrau yadi japen mantraṃ saivakalpalatā bhavet || 21-189 ||

aṣṭamyāṃ caṃcaturdaśyāṃ dviguṇaṃ yadi japyate |

tadeva teṣā siddhiḥ syā devānāmapi durllabhā || 21-190 ||


kulacūḍāmaṇau |


śṛṇu putrarahasyaṃ me samayācārasaṃbhavān |

yena hīnāta siddhyanti janmakoṭi sahasrasaḥ || 21-191 ||

mānavaḥ kulaśāstrānāṃ kulacaryānucāriṇām |

udācacittaḥ sarvatraḥ vaiṣṇavā cā tatpara || 21-192 ||

paranindasahistusyād upakārarataṃ sadā |

parvato cipininnitye śunyamaṇḍaye || 21-193 ||

catuṣpathe jale śūnya yadi devā cibhi bhavet |

kṣaṇāṃ dhyātvā manuṃ japtvā natvā gached yathā mukhe || 21-194 ||

gṛddhraṃ vikṣamahākālī namaskuryād alakṣitaḥ |

kṣemaṃkarīṃ tathā vikṣya jaṃvukīṃ yamadūtikām || 21-195 ||

kukhaśūlabhūṣakākau kṛṣṇamājīlameva ca |

kṛśodarī mahācaṇḍe keśivali priyā || 21-196 ||

kulācārapraśannāsye namaskeśaṃ karapriye |

śmaśāne ca śacaṃ dṛṣṭvā pradakṣiṇamanuvrajan || 21-197 ||

aśeṣamantravidyānāṃ phaladā nātrasaṃśayaḥ |

tebhyaḥ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ cāpi anulepādikaṃ tathā || 21-198 ||

valiśrīyaṃ cainaivedyaṃ datvā tad bhāvapūritaḥ |

ati priyakathālāya krīḍākaubhūharādikam || 21-199 ||

yathā tathā tat priya kṛt kṛtvā siddheśvaro bhavat |

rakṣatavyā prayatnena nakṣatā tatra kārayet || 21-200 ||

pūjayet tāṃ prayatnena yaṃ tato naiva saṃspṛśet |

sparśe ca siddhihāni syārśaca saraṇaṃ dhruvam || 21-201 ||

356) naviṣṇumnuhariśaṃ turnadevī nāpi ṣoḍaśī |

naca mṛtyuṃjayo vāpi tathā nārāyaṇaḥ svayam || 21-202 ||


iti śrīyadunāthacakravartti viracitā yām āgamakalpalatāyāṃ ṣṭaviṃśaḥ 
pallavaḥ samāptam |


iti saṃvat 1955 sālamiti jyeṣṭa śudi 8 roja 7 tadine likhitam saṃpūrṇam | śubham |
